prathamo 'dhyāyaḥ
svasti śrīgaṇādhipataye namaḥ | avighnam astu | hariḥ om |
praśāsitāraṃ paramaṃ pareṣām anādim ādiṃ bhuvanatrayasya |
hiraṇyagarbhaṃ praṇipatya mūrddhnā smṛter ahaṃ vyākaraṇaṃ kariṣye ||1||
svāyaṃbhuvānuśiṣṭaṃ ca manuṃ svāyambhuvaṃ tathā |
yasya prasādāś loko (dāc chloko) 'yaṃ dharmacakṣur ajāyata || 2 ||
tcchiṣtañ ca bhṛguṃ yena vyākhyātam idam āditaḥ |
anujñātena manunā sarvapratyakṣadarśinā || 3 ||
tacchiṣyaśiṣyān anyāṃś ca sarvaśāstrakṛtaśramān |
yair iyaṃ vedaśāstrajñair vyākhyātā karuṇātmabhiḥ || 4 ||
avijñānād avijñāya dharmaśāstraṃ mahodayam |
ṛndhe(ṛjve?)tad iti vā kṛtvā durbodham iti vā punaḥ || 5 ||
dṛṣṭibhedena vā bhedaṃ(cedaṃ?) vyākhyātaṃ yair apaṇḍitaiḥ |
samyag aśrutaśāstrārthair arthavādavimohitaiḥ || 6 ||
pratyādeśāya sarveṣāṃ teṣām unmārgagāminām |
dharmaprītyā ca satatam idaṃ vakṣyāmi tattvataḥ || 7 ||
ramaṇīyavicāraṃ ca vicitrārthaṃ nirāulam |
satataṃ ko na vi(nir)brūyāt padavākyapramāṇavat || 8 ||
ko 'syābhisaṃbandhaḥ? ayam ucyate |
******ṣṭāniṣṭopabhogaprayojanaiḥ śarīrendriyaviṣayaiḥ saṃyogaviyogarūpo janmamaraṇaprabandhaparyāyaḥ ******** nimittaḥ nānājñānādhikārahetukaḥ | yathā kecid āhuḥ — tau ca punar dharmādharmāv abhipretānabhipreta ****hetutvāt puruṣsyopādānaparivarjanārhau nityaṃ bhavataḥ | yathā loke sragādayo viṣādayaś ca *****niścayajñānaṃ ca vedasmṛtiśāstrapramāṇaṃ yathā viṣayaṃ na pratyakṣānumānapramāṇam anyāgamapramāṇaṃ vā | evaṃ ca sati yathaiva vedāt puruṣasya śreyo 'gnihotrādi tadarthānuṣṭhānadvāreṇety ato [2] vedāgamair yatnaḥ śreyo'rthibhiḥ kriyate | evaṃ smṛtito 'py aṣṭakādikarmācārānuṣṭānāt tasyotkarṣo yataḥ tadadhiṣṭā[nam ā]gamo 'py ādaraṇīyaḥ | idaṃ dharmaśāstram aṣṭakādikarmācārānuṣṭhānapratipattikāraṇam ārabhyate |
manum ekāgram āsīnam abhigamya maharṣayaḥ |
pratipūjya yathānyāyam idaṃ vacanam abruvan || 1.1 ||
tatra manur nāma viśiṣṭasthānādhikārakāryanivṛttisamarthaḥ puruṣaviśeṣaḥ svāyambhuvo nānyaḥ | tathā ca viśeṣayiṣyate [viśeṣa iṣyate] "idaṃ śāstraṃ tu kṛtvāsau" ityevaṃ ca smṛtyantarāṇi | "manuḥ svāyambhuvo 'bravīt" iti | tatra ca manusaṃjñayā [a]nvarthitāṃ darśayati yasmād ayam abhihitasarvārthadarśanaśaktiḥsarvārthān yathecchaṃ viśeṣato vijñātum vaktuṃ ca śaknoti tasmān mananān manuḥ | yadi cāyam itthaṃbhūto vaktā bhavati tataḥ paramasūkṣmān dharmān praṣṭuṃ yujyate | nānitthaṃbhūtas tasya viśeṣaṇam ekāgram iti | akarmāttamana (akarmātmatā?) ucyate | arthagrahaṇe 'gragāmitvād ekasmin paramārtham anusandhāya yatane 'ntarhṛdayaikadeśākhye 'graṃ mano yasya so 'yam ekāgraḥ tam ekāgram avikṣiptamanasaṃ parasmortha(?)sambodhanābhimukhībhūtāntaḥ karaṇam ity arthaḥ | dharmo(rma)praśnopalabdhipraṇihitamanasaṃ vā | ekāgram idaparaṃ(?) tadviśeṣaṇam | āsīnam upaviṣṭaṃ na śayānaṃ tiṣṭhantaṃ vā | yady api śayānas tiṣṭhan vā śaknoti dharmān vaktuṃ tathāpy ācārapipālayiṣayā "yad yad ācarati śreṣṭha" (BhG 3.21) ity anena darśanena mā bhūt pareṣām itthaṃbhūte(ta)maryā[dā]cāraparilopa iti paracittajño nāgam ṛṣibhyo darmapraśnānuyogaṃ buddhvaivam babhūva | nāśaśre(śro)tṛsambodhā(mbandhā)nurodhena vābhigmanapraśnakriyayor manuḥ karma yatas tatra dvitīyaṃ | evaṃ ca viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyapadānāṃ trayāṇām apy ekavibhaktitvaṃ "manum ekāgram āsīnam" iti | abhigamya maharṣayaḥ abhyupasamādhoni[1 akṣ missing]te bhāgaprasādapuraḥsaraṃ vinayapraśanyor upagṛhītaṃ manuprasādanārthaṃ gamanaṃ kṛtvā taṃ ca te anatrdhānādiprakārayā yogadhyāye(ne) [na] ṛṣayaś ca ṛgyajussāmadarśaneneti | kim akurvann abhigamanānantaraṃ ta iti śabdanyāyaprāptaṃ kriyāntaraṃ pṛcchati | tad idam ucyate | idaṃ vacanam ābruvan (anubruvan) (ya) dvitīyaślokaṃ yathānyāyaṃ yathāśāstraṃ pūrvam ṛṣayaḥ paścād idaṃ vacanam abruvan vakyamāṇa(ḥ) dharmapraśnākhyaṃ nānācāreṇa cocyate | yadi vakṣyamāṇaṃ kathaṃ tarhīdaṃ vyapadiśati vartamānasāmīpyād atha vā buddhyākhyam ātmasaṃsthaṃ pratyakṣam ity ucyate | bhaviṣyacchabdākhyaḥ pratyakṣatvād vakṣyamāṇaṃ sarvadharmāpāyaṃ pratyakṣe ca parokṣe cety evamādi punaḥ
bhavan sarvavarṇānāṃ yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ |
antaraprabhavānāṃ ca dharmān me vaktum arhasi || 1.2 ||
bhavacchabdaḥ samagraiśvaryavacanaḥ | evaṃ tadvantaṃ manum āmantritaśabdenābhimukhīkurvanti praśnaśravaṇārtham | bhagavan dharmān no vaktum arhasīti pradhānapadena sambandhas tasya viśeṣaṇaṃ sarvavarṇānām [3] advitīyajātīnām eva | yataḥ śūdra(rā)varodhārtham idam | yathāvat tat punaḥ anupūrvabrāhmaṇādivarṇakrameṇa kiṃ teṣām evam antaraprabhavānāṃ ca antaraṃ bhaṇa | me(e)ṣ[ā]m eva na, antaraprabhāvānāṃ ca | antaraṃ brāhmaṇādivarṇamadhye tatra prabhavaḥ yeṣāṃ tadantaraprabhavā anulomapratilomavarṇebhyo jātyantaraprabhūtā aśvataravat | yathā aśvarāsabhayor bījamiśrībhāvād aśvataro jātyantara evaṃ varṇānām apy anulomapratilomābhiḥ strībhiḥ samparkād antaraprabhavā jātyantarabhūtāḥ pṛthaṅnirdiśyante | reviṣāṃ (eteṣāṃ) sāmānyāt vaiśeṣikāṃś ca dharmān vyavasthārūpān no 'smākaṃ vaktuam arhasi | nanv adhikṛtā evāntaraprabhavā na sarve | kiṃ tarhi | pratilomāḥ, anulomās tv adhikṛtā eva | yatas tān apekṣyaitad uktam antaraprabhavānāṃ ceti | tathā pratilomā apy adhikṛtā eva puruṣadharmais tathā ca vakṣyati "gavārthe brāhmaṇārthe vā" (11.79) ityevamādi | vṛttiniyamaś ca teṣām upadekṣyate "matsyaghāto niṣādānām" (10.48) ityevamādi | yathā kṛtsnadharmānadhikārād anadhikṛtās te | evam itaratrāpi samānam | yena na kaścid ekas sarvadharmair adhikriyate | yugapat krameṇa vā upanayanasampatpratiṣedhena caitad upadiṣṭaṃ gautamīye "pratilomās tu (māt tu) dharmahīnā" (GDh 4.25) iti | tat sarvadharmānadhikārād iti | yatas tatraiva sthitāḥ dharmān no vaktum arhasīty atra kāraṇam āhur maharṣayaḥ | yena,
tvam eko 'py asya sarvasya vidhānasya svayaṃbhuvaḥ |
acintyasyāprameyasya kāryatattvārthavit prabho || 1.3 ||
ekaśabdaḥ stutyartho yathā ca loke na saṃkhyārthaḥ | hiśabdo 'vadhāraṇe | asya sarvasyety abhinayena vidhānaṃ nirdiśyate vidhānasya śāstrasya kathaṃ punar vidhānam? śāstraṃ vidhatta iti "kṛtyaluṭo bahulam" (Pāṇ 3.3.113) iti kartari lyuṭaḥ svātantryavivakṣāyāṃ ca kartṛtvam | vidhatte(r) hitāhitaprāptiparihārau pradarśayatīty arthaḥ | bhāvo 'yaṃ vidhir vidhānam upadeśaḥ | karṇādhikaraṇayor vā vidhīyante 'nenāsmin vopadiśyata ity arthaḥ | evaṃ vidhānaṃ śāstraṃ tasya viśeṣaṇam | svayaṃbhuvaḥ nityasyety arthaḥ | svayaṃbhuvaḥ nityasyety arthaḥ | evaṃ ca nedaṃ manukartṛkam | kim atra pramāṇam? manvapy(bhy)udayasya karyatvaṃ taddharmas ca śāstrāt prāptir evaṃ cāsti punastād api manujanmanaḥ śāstraṃ taddharmānuśsanam | evaṃ tarhi na śāstrāt tasya dharmo 'bhyudayahetur bhaviṣyati | evam anyeṣām apīti śāstram ānarthakyaṃ hetvabhāvaś ca tathānyaśāstrāt tasya dharma evam anyeṣām apīti śāstrānarthakyam | pratijñāmātram cāthavā vinā dharmā[t] tasyaiśvaryaṃ [na] | kiṃ tarhi? svābhāvikam anyeṣām apīti śāstrānarthakaṃ hetvabhāvaś cānyeṣāṃ śāstratas tasyāntareṇa śāstraṃ svabhāvata iti vaiśeṣikatvahetvabhāvaś ca | etena hiraṇyagarbho vyākhyātaḥ | evaṃ nityaṃ vidhānaṃ ta[t] tarhi kathaṃ yad vakxyāmaḥ | stutyarthaṃ vā tat tasya śāstrasya idaṃ cānyad viśeṣaṇam acintyasya | acintyaṃ hetutaḥ apratiṣedhyaṃ yenānumānato dharmādharmavyavasthā neṣyate | udāharaṇaṃ yāvat kiṃcid abhojyam [4] abhihitaṃ bhāvataḥ puruṣapurīṣādi | tat sarvaṃ bhojyaṃ bhautikatvā dadhyādivat | yāvatyaś ca kāścid agamyā abhimatā bhaginyādistriyaḥ sarvārthāgamyāstrīndriyayogāt veśyāstrīvat evam aniṣṭasparśane 'py udāharaṇīyam etac ca śāstrāgamabalīyastvāt tadviruddham anumānam atrāpramāṇam | bhautikatve sati medhyāmedhyavibhāgas tv indriyayoge ca gamyāgamyavibhāgaḥ | śāstrapramāṇam eva (evaṃ) yato 'cintyam idaṃ hetutaḥ aprameyaṃ ca phalataḥ satyavacanādibhir brahmalokaprāptyādividyām stutyartham idaṃ viśeṣaṇatrayam | asyetthaṃbhūtasya vidhānasyaikaḥ "kāryatattvārthavit prabho" vidhānasya kāryatvaśāstracoditaṃ karma yena śāstrād dhi tatpuruṣeṇopalabhyaṃ kriyate tasya tattvaṃ viśiṣṭakartṛpuruṣasambandhaḥ phalaprāptiyogyatārūpaḥ | cikitsāvad idaṃ karmaānenaiva kartṛviśeṣeṇa sambandhaṃ phalāya nānyeneti | kāryasya tattvaṃ kāryatattvam | pūrvapadārthapradhānṃ (naḥ) ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣaḥ, odanapākavat | yathodanāt pākavataḥ phalaṃ na ta (1 akṣ unclear) pākād evaṃ ca tatra viśiṣṭāt karmaṇaḥ phalaṃ na ta mātrād iti kāryatattvasyārthaḥ prayojanaṃ adhikāraviśiṣṭasya karmaṇaḥ viśvavikalpas tatphalaṃ puruṣārthaḥ sa ca bhinnaḥ kasycit karmaṇo dharmiṇaḥ kasyacit pratyavāyānutpattimātraṃ kasyacid ubhayaṃ kasyacin niṣkṛtiḥ kasyacid dharmasya niṣkṛtiś ca eva kāryatattvārthaḥ taṃ vettīti (?) kāryatattvarthavit | atha vā vidhānasya karma kāryo dhamas tasya tattvaṃ na kriyā mātratvam | kiṃ tarhi? tadvyatiriktasya sataḥ kālāntarāvasthāyitvātmaguṇatvakarmaphaladāyitvādikāryatattvasya dharmasyārthaḥ phalam abhyudayo viśvavikalpaḥ | taṃ vettīti yo yasmāt karmaṇo bhavati tathā ceṣṭāniṣṭaphalavipākaḥ phalanimittaṃ karma nimittaṃ vakṣyati | evaṃ cānena śāstrapraṇrtuḥ sādhutvam uktaṃ bhavati | yo hi śāstrasya kāryattatarthavit evam svadharmān śaknoti vaktuṃ nānyaḥ evaṃ cātra prabhutve saty asya "prabho" ityāmantraṇaṇ yuktaṃ codyate avāptadharmā caraṇaprayojanās te sākṣātkṛtadharmāṇaś ca maharṣayaḥ santo na dharmāt(n) praṣṭum arhanty atha vā pṛṣṭavanto na tarhi maharṣayaḥ | atra samādhim ākhyāyikādharmeṇa prakalpya maharṣīn dharmapraṣṭṝn idaṃ śāstrastutyarthaṃ vacanam | yathā vedaśāstrārthavādākhyāyikārūpeṇa katham evaṃ nāmedaṃ dharmas tattvaṃ paramasūkṣmatvād duravbodhatamaṃ yena maharṣīn api praṣṭṝn arhati kiṃ punar itarān? saiṣā śāstrastutir adhyayanādiprarocikā sampadyate | atha vā maharṣitvaprāptisādha[na]sya sarvasya svīkaraṇopayamārgasthā manunā pratipṛccha(ya)tā subhāṣitavacanena dharmapraśnākhyena maharṣayo yūyam ity evam uktā smṛtypanibaddhagranthakāreṇa "paścād evaṃ vijānatā(?)" iti vṛttanukathanaṃ śāstre 'sminn uktās ta eva maharṣaya iti | tathā ca dharmacyākhyānapratiśravaśloke caturthe vakṣyaty enam artham "pratyuvācārcya tān sarvān maharṣīn śrūyatām iti" | na ca manusakāśād anyābhivādanādi teṣāṃ pūjanam uktaṃ śiṣyatvād ṛte 'smāt svaparigrahottarakālayogyāc ca prītihetor maharṣiśabdād ity adhyayanavijñānaprayojanena vā viśiṣṭaśaktisambandhena teṣāṃ pūrvavan maharṣiśabdo yojanīyaḥ [5] bhaviṣyadvṛttyā vā maharṣayo bhavitāro maharṣiśabdenocyante guṇavṛttyeti parārthaś caiṣāṃ maharṣīṇāṃ satāṃ dharmapraśno bhaviṣyati yathā loka ity evaṃ triślokīdharmapraśnārthoktā | prativacanārthas tūttaraślokaḥ prakriyate |
sa vai pṛṣṭas tathā samyag amitaujā mahātmabhiḥ |
pratyuvācārcya tān sarvān maharṣīñ chrūyatām iti || 1.4 ||
sa manus tair maharṣibhiḥ pṛṣṭaḥ kiṃ dharmān yathāpomān ma (?) "sarvavarṇānāṃ yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ, antaraprabhavānāṃ ca" (1.2) iti | samyag iti praśnaviśeṣaṇam | samyak pṛṣṭo nānācāreṇa | kathaṃlakṣaṇaḥ | yatas tasya vaktuḥ prāmāṇyaṃ darśayati — amitaujāḥ ojaḥ sarvārtho dharmaḥ tadbījaṃ vā vijñānam apratihataṃ sarvārtheṣv asya soya[ma]mitaujāḥ [so 'mitaujāḥ] | mahātmabhiḥ mahātmatvaṃ caiṣāṃ vyākhyātam | pratyuvāca | kiṃ kṛtvā | [ā]rcyvākāt [arcya tān] maharṣīn ity uktamānda(tvāda)tropaśleṣo (akārapraśleṣo?) draṣṭavyaḥ | saṃhitāpasyaḥ (?) padāvagraheṇa arcayitvā | kṛtasamāsasya lubyā(lyabā)deśasya ārcyeti rūpa[m] | (1 akṣ missing) "phalāgha (athārca?)yitvā tān" iti spaṣṭataraḥ pāṭhaḥ | maharṣīn kimiti pratyuvāca, yat pṛṣṭo 'haṃ bhavadbhis tac chrūyatām iti evaṃ cocyate | nanu manuprokte śāstre "manum ekāgram āsīnam" (1.1) ityevamādiparāpa(ravya?)deśas tasya dṛṣṭiviruddho 'smin prāpnoti manukartṛke śāstre | syād ayaṃ doṣaḥ | na cānyakartṛke | kas tarhy asyārthaḥ | kartā kaścic chiṣṭaḥ sarvasmṛtidharmāṇāṃ pratyakṣadarśī sarvavedaśākhānugatamantrabrāhmaṇaliṅgas teṣāṃ granthānāṃ [granthakānāṃ] smṛtidharmāṇām api smaraṇārthān tān gṛhyenopadidikṣur "manur vai yat kiṃcicāvadat tad bheṣajam" bheṣajākhyaṃ [bhṛgvākhyaṃ] puruṣaviśeṣaṃ śāstrasya vaktāraṃ prakalpya maharṣīṃś ca śrotṝn | śāstrastutyarthaḥ smaraṇopanibandhanaṃ kāruṇyāt granthamātraṃ cakāra | evaṃ ca paropadeśo nāsya kartur yuktaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi | mānavam iti | vyapadeśo 'sya yady anyakarṛkaṃ (kaḥ) tatsmaraṇe tenopabandhanāt tadarthaṃ tacchabdaṃ bhavati yathā taṇḍulārthaṃ varṣaṃ taṇḍulo nocyate | bhūtavṛttānukathanapakṣe 'pi ca śiṣṭakṛte granthe na virodhaḥ tadarthe | iyāṃs tu viśeṣaḥ | ekatra prakalpya vādo 'nyatra bhūtānukathanam | tathā ca paurāṇikāḥ:
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni mantrā ātharvaṇāś ca ye |
saptarṣibhis tu tat proktaṃ smārtaṃ tu manur abravīt ||
evaṃ ca tatraiva sthitam "pratyuvācārcya tān sarvān maharṣīñ chrūyatām iti |
[6]
āsīd idaṃ tamobhūtam aprajñātam alakṣaṇam |
apratarkyam avijñeyaṃ prasuptam iva sarvataḥ || 1.5 ||
idam ayuktaṃ vartate dharmān pṛṣṭenāvyaktatvām jagataḥ pradarśya tadutpattisthitipralayā apṛṣṭāḥ kathyanta ity evaṃ ca saniḥśokamātram (?) anārabhyam utpattyādi | yatatvā (yad vā?) n (na) vyākhyānaṃ vā jagataḥ | kiṃ tarhi | kṛtsna evādhyāyas tathā ca pṛṣṭaprativanārtham arthasambandhaṃ dvitīyāt prabhṛti śāstraṃ vaktavyam | anyathā pratijāpramādadoṣa ācāryasya yad bhavati yat pṛṣṭaṃ ca dharmaṃ yathoktaviśeṣaṇaṃ na prativakti | praśnānantaram aprṣṭaṃ ca purāṇaśāstraviṣayam anenāsambandha (saṃbaddhaṃ) jagadutpattyādipadārthaṃ vyākaroti | sa eṣa doṣo mā bhūd iti yata idam anantaram arthasambandhaṃ (-baddhaṃ) tatprativacanaṃ vaktavyam | "vidvadbhiḥ sevitaḥ sadbhiḥ" (2.1) ityevamādi | atra brūmaḥ | śāstrasya sādhutvāt vyākhyānārtho 'yam arthavādaḥ jagadutpattipralayasatattvavyākaraṇaprapañcam | kathaṃ kṛtvā | dehendriyamanobuddhyādivyatiriktasyātmano nityasya sataḥ kartur bhoktuś ceṣṭāniṣṭaiḥ śarīrendriyaviṣayaiḥ saṃyogaviyogarūpo 'nukrāntaḥ saṃsāra jagadutpattyādipratīte
nai(e)tadantās tu gatayo brahmādyāḥ samudāhṛtāḥ |
ghore 'smin bhūtasaṃsāre nityaṃ saṃtatayāyini || [1.50]
sarvadharmādharmanimitta uktas tathā ca vakyati |
evam etair idaṃ sarvaṃ manniyogān mahātmabhiḥ |
yathākarma tapoyogāt sṛṣṭaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam || [1.41]
tathā ca dvādaśādhyāye vakṣyati karmata eva dehādisargaiḥ (sargaṃ) puruṣasya,
etā dṛṣṭvāsya jīvasya gatiṃ svenaiva cetasā |
dharmato 'dharmataś caiva dharme dadyāt sadā manaḥ || [12.23]
tayoś cāyam abhipretā na hi pretopabhoga sādhanayor dharmādharmayoḥ prāṣṭiparivarjane śāstrapramāṇe | yenātaḥ tatputryarthaṃ jagatsargādayaḥ purāṇaśāstranirdiṣṭā iha tu samāsato 'nūdyante svārtham | tathā ca śāstrārambhasāmarthyopapattyāṃ satyām adhyayanaśravaṇānuvādanāsāmarthyaṃ (?) kalpitaṃ mānyaṃ bhavati — idam adhyetavyaṃ śreyo 'rthibhiḥ śrotavyaś ceti | jāgratsvapnādinirdeśaprakaraṇavac caina[du]pariṣṭān ya(da)pi brāhmaṇastutiprakaraṇādīni prasaktānuprasaktāni taccheṣatayaiva draṣṭavyāni | tathā ca darśayiṣyāmas tad evam | "āsīd idaṃ tamobhūtam" (1.5) ity evamādiḥ ślokasandarbhaḥ | ā adhyāyaparisamāpteḥ śāstrārambhasāmarthyapradarśanārtho draṣṭavyaḥ | yathā vyākaraṇādiṣu nonmattavākyavad asamīkṣitārtha iti | śloka idānīṃ vivriyate | tatra tāvad utpattyādibhyo jagaddharmebhyaḥ sarvakālabhāvinīm avyaktāvasthām anabhidhāya jagatutpattyādayo 'bhidhātum anyāya ity atas tasyāvyaktāvasthaiva tāvad ebhyaḥ pūrvam ucyate | āsīd idaṃ tamobhūtaṃ jagat | kena prakāreṇa tamobhūtam | tama iva na tu tama eva avidyamānasadbhāvam | kathaṃ tarhi | tamaḥśabdena tamobhūtaṃ labhate yena prajñātaṃ prajñātatvasāmānyādijagat tasyām avasthāyāṃ tamobhūtam ity ucyate aprajñātañ ca | kutaḥ | pramāṇābhāvāt | tathā ca tadabhāvaṃ darśayati | alakṣaṇam ity anena pratyakṣasyāpratarkyam [7] ity anumānasyāvijñeyam ity āgamasya | evaṃ ca tadabhāvād asattvam asya prāptaṃ nivartayati "prasuptam iva sarvataḥ" ity etad vakṣyāmaḥ |
atha vā āsīd idaṃ jagat | kena prakāreṇa | tad darśayati | tamobhūtam anabhivyaktaviśeṣam ataś cāprajñātaṃ pratyakṣato 'nu ('tha) vā kena prakāreṇa yathedam āsīd iti | na cātītaviṣayaṃ pratyakṣaṃ sūkṣmārthaṃ viṣayaṃ vā anumānena tarhi bhaviṣyatīty ata āha: alakṣaṇam | lakṣyate yena tallakṣaṇam | nāsya lakṣaṇaṃ vidyate tad idam alakṣaṇaṃ dṛṣṭaliṅgānumānapratiṣedhaḥ | sāmānyatodṛṣṭaṃ tarhi bhaviṣyatīty ata āha: apratarkyam | sāmānyatodṛṣṭād apy anumānāt tathedaṃ tarkayitum aśakyaṃ yathedam āsīd itīdam ayuktaṃ yena śakyate bhāvo dharmāntarayuktaḥ pūrvadharmeṇaivam āsīd ity anumātuṃ yathā tathā jagad apy evaṃ syād iti | satyam etad evaṃ saukṣmyātiśayapradarśanaparatve tata(tada)pratarkyapratyākhyānam | āgamastutiparaṃ ca | āgamo yaḥ stutyarthāpetam | nanv ayam "āsīd idaṃ tamobhūtam" ity atītāvasthā pratipādako jagato yataś caitad eva mataṃ avijñeyam | tathā jagadāgamānta (?) pramāṇāntarataḥ | kutas tarhi tad vijñātuṃ śakyam | āgamād ity uktam | saiṣāgamapramāṇastutiḥ | evaṃ ca yatrāgamā (ye āgamā) bhāvam api varṇayanti te pūrvāparaviruddhaṃ varṇartayanto (varṇayanto?) na pramāṇībhavanti | katham | "āsīd idaṃ tamobhūtam" iti vā(cā)cakṣate āgamapratiṣedhaṃ ca kurvantīti na yuktam | idānīm āgamopadiṣṭam arthaṃ dṛṣṭāntena pratipādayati: prasuptam iva sarvataḥ | anena hi jāgatsvapnavṛttiḥ (vṛttīḥ) parityajya puruṣasya suṣuptāvasthocyate dṛṣṭāntatvena | yathāyaṃ puruṣo yasyām asvasthāyāṃ vartamāno niḥsambodhatve kleśapratyastam iti viśeṣatayā śānta evam idam | jagad asyām avasthāyām avyaktaḥ vyatyastam iti viśeṣatayā śāntam iti | ayam upamārthaḥ | prasuptam ivety asya sarvata iti | na ekadeśavikāro 'ntarā pralayaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | kṛtsnasya jagato mahāpralayād ūrdhvam avyākṛtākhya evaṃ ca pravibhaktapadārthasya śokasya ye samānārthapadatvam āhus te asaṃskṛtaprajñatayā śāstradveṣād vā | samānārthatve 'pi vā saty etad ucyate | anuvādo 'yaṃ na punaruktaḥ | kaḥ punar anayor viśeṣaḥ | śabdābhyāsasamāne 'rthavad abhyāso 'nuvādaḥ | gamanopadeśavad anarthakaṃ tu punaruktaḥ śabdābhyāsasāmānye 'pi | yady evaṃ ko 'trābhyāsārthaḥ | saukṣṃyātiśayapradarśanam avyaktasya kathaṃbhūtaviśeṣākhyaḥ aviśeṣās tanmātrākhyāḥ sūkṣmās tebhyo 'py ahaṅkāraḥ sūkṣmataraḥ | ahaṅkārān mahān | mahato 'vyaktasya niratiśa(ra?)yasūkṣmatvaṃ pradarśaya(1 akṣ unclear) cchet mā (?) | "āsīd idaṃ tamobhūtam" ityevamādidṛṣṭāntas tu laukiko vacanīya iti |
tataḥ svayambhūr bhagavān avyakto vyañjayann idam |
mahābhūtādivṛttaujāḥ prādur āsīt tamonudaḥ || 1.6 ||
tata ity ānantarye avyākṛtāvasthāyā anantaram | svayṃ bhavatīti svayambhūḥ | yatheṣṭaṃ kāryakāraṇaṃ (ṇe) gṛhṇann ucyate | cetanācetanajagatpraśāsitā jagatkāraṇapuruṣaḥ [8] paramātmā | na tu saṃsāryātmaiva kaścit, tasyāsvatantratvāt | tathā ca gītāślokaḥ:
ajo 'pi sann avyayātmā bhūtānām īśvaro 'pi san |
prakṛtiṃ svām avaṣṭabhya saṃbhavāmy ātmamāyayā || [BhG 4.6]
kasmāt punar ātmavikalpaḥ | sarvopaniṣacchāstrebhyaḥ dharmādharmavyavasthāprāmāṇyasaṃniyogapratiṣedho ye cātrāvacanīyau yathābhūtārthaviśeṣatvāt pramāṇam indriyavat | tathā ca vakṣyati |
praśāsitāraṃ sarveṣām aṇīyāṃsam aṇor api |
rukmābhaṃ svapnadhīgamyaṃ vidyāt taṃ puruṣaṃ param || iti | [12.122]
punaś ca gītāślokaḥ |
uttamaḥ puruṣas tv anyaḥ paramātmety udāhṛtaḥ |
yo lokatrayam āviśya bibharty avyaya īśvaraḥ || [BhG 15.17]
anumānaṃ cātra viśiṣṭavijñānakartṛpuruṣavyāpāropagrahavaśena puruṣārthakriyāsamarthānāṃ kāryāṇām abhinivṛttiṃ dṛṣṭvā pratipadyāmahe | idam api jagat prajñāprayatnavyāpāro 'tha(pa)grahavaśena svatas tasya kascid asaṃsāriṇaḥ puruṣasya kāryaṃ bhavitum arhatīty evaṃ cānumānato 'py asti pradhānapuruṣor vyatirikta īśvaraḥ paramātmanā upāsatā (-ātmānam upāsataḥ?) karmabhūto na kevalam āgamataḥ | evaṃ ca sarvārthe yā dharmaśaktyā sa eva bhagavān avyakto 'pi san akṣayyakāraṇo (-karaṇo?) 'pi san aviratasāmarthyo draṣṭavyaḥ | vyañjayann iva mahadādibhāvenāviṣkurvan mahābhūtādiṣu vṛttaujaḥ mahadāditattveṣu vṛttaṃ jātam ojo vīryaṃ payaso 'yaṃ mahābhūtādivṛttaujāḥ | atha vā pāṭhāntaram asminn arthe mahābhūtatadvṛttenaujaḥ (mahābhūtānuvṛttaujāḥ?) mahābhūtāni yāvad anuvṛttam ojo yasya so 'yaṃ mahābhūtānuvṛttaujāḥ | prādur āsīt | prāduḥśabdaḥ prakāśe | prakāśa āsīt pradhānapravartayitṛrūpatvena | evaṃ ca tamonuda ity uktaṃ tamo 'vyaktāvasthāṃ tām evaṃ nudan tamonuda ity ucyate | mahadāditattvāviṣkaraṇena yathāvocāma evam ayaṃ ślokaḥ |
yo 'sāv atīndriyagrāhyaḥ sūkṣmo 'vyaktaḥ sanātanaḥ |
sarvabhūtamayo 'cintyaḥ sa eva svayam udbabhau || 1.7 ||
atīndriyaṃ manaḥ | atikrāntam indriyāṇi kathaṃ deśakālakāryatas tena gṛhyate ca yasmād ato 'tīndriyagrāhya ucyate | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
naivāsau cakṣuṣā grāhyo na ca śiṣṭair atīndriyaiḥ |
manasātiprasannena gṛhyate sūkṣmadarśibhiḥ ||
evaṃ ca sati sūkṣma ity ucyate (2 akṣ missing) | tasya paryāyaḥ avyaktaḥ | prakṛtiśarīro mahaccharīro vā | tathā ca paurāṇikāḥ |
sa ma(mā)hātmyaśarīras tu yatraivāgre hy avartata |
tatraivāvasthito vidvān dvāraśālāmukhe prabhuḥ ||
sanātanaṃ nityam eva | evaṃ yathopadiṣṭaḥ | anyathā sarva evātmanaḥ satattvavyapekṣayā sūkṣmā ity aviśeṣaṇam idaṃ tasya syāt | sarvabhūtamaya iti | sarvabhūtavinivṛttyupāyavijñānabhāvitā sarvabhūtamayaḥ sāmarthyād eva draṣṭavyam | mayaḍ bhāvanāyām | yathā mṛnmaya iti | acintyaprabhāvatvād acintyaḥ viśiṣṭaśaktisamāgamād ity uktam | ataś ca sa eva svayam udbabhau | apare punaḥ saṃsāryātmana eva sato hiraṇyagarbhasya [9] bhoktur kartuś ca pradhānaprayojakatvena varṇayanti yathā vatsasya bhogāya kṣīram acetanaṃ pravartate na mām aptnīrato (?) | na paramātmā nāma kaścid asti | ṣaḍviṃśakaṃ tattvam iti | jñāpakaṃ cātrāhuḥ | "etadantās tu gatayaḥ (1.50) iti | tad etad avirodhena prakaraṇasya vyākhyānīyam | samāsena cotpattir brahmaṇaḥ ślokadvayenoktā | katham asau prādurbhūta ity evaṃ pṛṣṭo viśeṣa prakriyārtham āha |
so 'bhidhyāya śarīrāt svāt sisṛkṣur vividhāḥ prajāḥ |
apa eva sasarjādau tāsu vīryam avāsṛjat || 1.8 ||
so 'bhidyāya kim ātmāhuḥ śarīrotpattiḥ katham aṇḍotpattikrameṇa kim arthaṃ yena tataḥ śarīrāt svāt sisṛkṣuḥ vividhā prajāḥ kiṃ tasya  śarīram antaraṇḍasya saṃsāramaṇḍalasyāpi prāpāpatyaḥ kāyas tasmāt sisṛkṣuḥ pradhānād vā śarīraṃ sṛṣṭam eṣaṇa(vaṃ)śīlaḥ kiṃ tad vividhāḥ prajāś caturdaśasaṃsthaṃ jagat | tathā cārthavādaḥ | paurāṇikāś ca prajāpatiśarīrād evādau prajāsargam evam aṇḍāt svaśarīram ut[pi]pādayiṣuḥ prajāsargārthaṃ kiṃ kṛtavān | aṇḍādhārabhūtā "apa eva sasarjādau" sṛjyamānānāmapadaṃ parmatvād dvitīyānirdeśaḥ | apaḥ sasarjādau | tasyāṇḍotpatter jagato vā tāsu vīryam avāsṛjat | vīryam aṇḍotpattiśaktir apām | evaṃ ca tad vīryam apāṃ pariṇāmāt prajāpaīcchayā | tatkrameṇa
tad aṇḍam abhavad dhaimaṃ sahasrāṃśusamaprabham |
yasmin jajñe svayaṃ brahmā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ || 1.9 ||
tad vīryam apāṃ pariṇāmāt prajāpatīcchayā tatkrameṇāṇḍam abhavad dhaimam haimam iva haimam upamanon (upamānārthas?) tadguṇaśabdaḥ antareṇāpi taddhitārthaḥ | tad yathā hāridrau kukkuṭasya pādau kāṣāyau gardabhasya karṇāv iti tat punar upamānārthaḥ | adbhir hi saṃśo(śle)ṣāt asaṃkocāc ca haimam ivocyate tattvaśuddher vā | yena tat sahasrāṃśusamaprabham | ato vā tad dhaimam upacayapakṣeṇa vā idam ucyate | yasmin jātavān svayaṃ brahmā ātmābhiprāyeṇa na parecchayā itarātmavat na tv asya prāk śarīrotpatter eva | tad ayuktam (?) | yata āha sarvalokapitāmahaḥ | yenāyaṃ sarvalokotpādayitā āgamopapattibhyām ukto netaraḥ evam asyai vātmecchayā svaśarīragrahaṇasāmarthyaṃ saṃsāryātmanām |etām evaṃ ca yathopadiṣṭām adhunā prakriyāṃ nāmadheyena samarthayati ||
āpo nārā iti proktā āpo vai narasūnavaḥ |
tā yad asyāyanaṃ pūrvaṃ tena nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 1.10 ||
āpo nārā ity ucyate (-ante) | yenaitā nareṇa brahmākhyenāṇḍotpattyarthaṃ sṛṣṭās tā yat yena kāraṇenāsyāyanaṃ gamanam āśrayaḥ aṇḍāntarbhūtaśarīrasya pūrvaṃ bhuvanasṛṣṭes tena kāraṇenāyaṃ brahmā [10] nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ | nārā āpaḥ ayanam asya so 'yaṃ nārāyaṇaḥ | tathā ca paurāṇikāḥ:
sahasraśīrṣaḥ puruṣo rukavarṇas tv atīndriyaḥ |
brahmā nārāyaṇākhyas tu sṛṣṭopasalile (?) tadā || iti |
yat tat kāraṇam avyaktaṃ nityaṃ sadasadātmakam |
tadvisṛṣṭaḥ sa puruṣo loke brahmeti kīrtyate || 1.11 ||
yat tad iti sārvanāmikāv uddeśanirdeśaśabdau sāmānyataḥ kāraṇaṃ pradhānapuruṣavyatiriktam ācakṣate | kasya | jagataḥ | pradhānapravartayitur dvāreṇa | kīdṛśam | avyaktam | atrāpiśabdo lopa(ḥ) sāmarthyāt | avyaktam api vyaktam api | saśarīram aśarīraṃ cety arthaḥ | nityam iti tadviśeṣaṇam | nityaṃ kāraṇaṃ na kadācid akāraṇam ekadeśasya vā sadasadātmakam iti sadasadbhāvaṃ sadātmakaṃ kāryadarśanāt | asadātmakaṃ cātyantaparokṣatvāc chaśaviṣāṇavad upacaryate paraṃ brahma | tadvisṛṣṭaḥ sa puruṣo bhūtamayaśarīrākhyo loke brahmeti kīrtyate brahmaprabhavatvāt | pradhānaviṣayo 'yaṃ śloko vartani(rṇṇnī)yaḥ | apare tu paramātmaviṣayam etam ācakṣate | evam asāv aśarīro 'pi sarvasākṣiī bhagavān viśiṣṭaśarīragarbham aṇḍaṃ nirmame ||
tasminn aṇḍe sa bhagavān uṣitvā parivatsaram |
svayam evātmano dhyānāt tad aṇḍam akarod dvidhā || 1.12 ||
tasminn aṇḍe sa bhagavān uṣitvā [2 akṣ missing] yakaṃ kālaṃ parivatsaram | parivatsaraśabdo garbhavṛṣṭikālapradarśanārthaḥ | netarapuruṣavat parādhīnaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | svayam eva svecchayety arthaḥ | tato 'nantaraṃ garbhavāsakāle parisamāpte tad aṇḍam akarod dvidhā | katham | ātmano dhyānāt | ātmanas tatrāvasthānakālaparisamāptim arthalakṣaṇād buddhvā tad aṇḍam autarādharyeṇa dviśakalam akarot |
tābhyāṃ sa śakalābhyāṃ tu divaṃ bhūmiṃ ca nirmame |
madhye vyoma diśaś cāṣṭau apāṃ sthānaṃ ca śāsvatam || 1.13 ||
jagadādhārasamudraṃ pṛthivyābhūtaṃ vā saptadhā niviṣṭaṃ mahadādis tattvasṛṣṭau ca satyāṃ mahābhūtāṇḍasṛṣṭir na ca mahadāditattvasarga ukto yatas tadvivakṣayedam ārabhyate | pūrvam eva ca mahābhūtāṇḍasvaśarīrasargāt |
udbabarhātmanaś caiva manaḥ sadasadātmakam |
manasaś cāpy ahaṃkāram abhimantāram īśvaram || 1.14 ||
udbabarha uddhṛtavān | kutaḥ | ātmanaḥ kāraṇāt pradhānāt krameṇa kiṃ tan manaḥ kīdṛśaṃ sadasadātmakam ity etad vyākhyātaṃ manasaś ceti | tañ ca bhūtendriyasargaṃ darśayati | manasaś cāpi [11] pūrvam ahaṃkāraṃ tatkāraṇṃ ca | evaṃ tarhīti vartate | kas tasya dharmo yata āha | abhimantāram asmin pratyayamātraṃ tasya vṛttir īśvaram | kasya | svavikārasya bhūtendriyalakṣaṇasya | utpannagrahābhyām ahaṅkāraṃ ca pūrvataram |
manāntam eva cātmānaṃ
tatkāraṇamusupañcabarheti (tatkāraṇam uktam udbabarheti) vartate | ātmaśabdas tattvaparyāyo vastu vātmārthatvāddhyā (?) mahān ucyate | tathā puruṣārthatvāc charīraṃ puruṣaḥ
sarvāṇi triguṇāni ca |
tanmātrāṇām ucyante | yad ayam āha
viṣayāṇāṃ grahītṝṇi śanaiḥ
tārkikābhyanujñātena kremenety arthaḥ |
pañcendriyāṇi ca || 1.15 ||
pañca buddhilakṣaṇāni manasā saha | caśabdāt karmendriyāṇi cety arthaḥ ||
teṣāṃ tv avayavān sūkṣmān ṣaṇṇām apy amitaujasām |
saṃniveśyātmamātrāsu sarvabhūtāni nirmame || 1.16 ||
ṣaṇṇām iti ṣaḍviśeṣā ucyante | pañcatanmātrāṇy ahaṃkāraś ceti | ebhiḥ sarvam idaṃ sṛjyate krameṇa | katham | tanmātrābhyo bhūtāni, ahaṃkārād indiryāṇi | anye [tu] ṣaṇṇām ity anena manaḥṣaṣṭhānīndriyāṇy ācakṣate |
yan mūrtyavayavāḥ sūkṣamās tasyemāny āśrayanti ṣaṭ |
tasmāc charīram ity āhus tasya mūrtiṃ manīṣiṇaḥ || 1.17 ||
bhūtasūkṣmāśrayaṇāc charīram iti śarīranirvacanaṃ pūrvaprakriyāsiddhyartham |
tatrāviśanti bhūtāni mahānti saha karmabhiḥ |
manaś cāvayavaiḥ sūkṣmaiḥ sarvabhūtakṛd avyayaḥ || 1.18 ||
bhūtasūkṣmapariveṣṭitaṃ liṅgaṃ tad āviśati bhūtāni mahānti sthūlāni saha karmabhiḥ saha karmendriyaiḥ smṛtisaṃgraha vakti (?) bhu(ma)hāvakāśadā[ne]naiva svadharmair manaś cāvayavaiḥ sūkṣmair manasaḥ saṅkalpābhi (?) mano 'dhyavasāyair avayavāḥ śubhāśubhasaṅkalpā vā anantāḥ sarvabhūtakṛc ca mano yena tac chubhāśubhasaṅkalpaprabhavaṃ karma sarvajagadbījam | [11]
teṣām idaṃ tu saptānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ mahaujasām |
sūkṣmābhyo mūrtimātrābhyaḥ saṃbhavanty avyayād vyayam || 1.19||
saptānām iti | mahān atra saptamaḥ parigṛhyate | tadarthaṃ ca tanmātrāhaṃkārāṇām api punar grahaṇa** *** tattvānāṃ sūkṣmābhyāṃ(bhyo) mātrābhyaḥ sāmānyānavasthitābhyaḥ sarvam idaṃ vyaktaṃ saṃbhavaty avyayād vyayayaṃ (?) nityād anityaṃ saṃbhavati puruṣaśabdaḥ caiṣāṃ puruṣārthatvād veditavyaḥ | atha vā manaḥ ṣaṣṭhānām indriyāṇāṃ bhūtasaptanām anirdeśaḥ puruṣaśabdaś ca puruṣārthatvād ity uktam |
ādyādyasya guṇaṃ teṣām avāpnoti paraḥ paraḥ |
yo yo yāvitithaś caiṣāṃ sa sa tāvadguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 1.20 ||
neha pṛthivyādbhūtānāṃ kramo 'bhipretaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | ākāśādi | vakṣyati hi "ākāśāj (ākāśaṃ) jāyate tasmāt" (1.75) ity evam | ākāśam ādyaṃ tasya śabdo guṇaḥ tasmāt paro vāyuḥ sparśaguṇas tasyāpi śabdaḥ sāmsargikas tathā ca dviguṇo vāyur evaṃ yo yo yāvatāḥ(tāṃ) pūraṇo yāvatithaḥ dvitīyas tṛtīyo vā bhūtaviśeṣaḥ bhūtātmā vā sa tāvadguṇaḥ dviguṇas triguṇaś caturguṇaḥ pañcaguṇo vā | evaṃ ca bhūtānām ekaikaguṇatve sati saṃsargādikam anekaguṇagrahaṇaṃ vijñeyam |
arthanivṛttim uktvā saṃjñādivṛttim idānīm āha |
sarveṣāṃ tu sa nāmāni karmāṇi ca pṛthak pṛthak |
vedaśabdebhya evādau pṛthak saṃsthāś ca nirmame || 1.21 ||
sarveṣāṃ tu nāmānīti brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśya ity evamādīni śāstravati(vanti?) netarāṇi yāny anāryair bhāṣyante śaktijāni ca nābhyādīni āryoktāny api santi | karmāṇi ca yajanādīni pṛthak pṛthak viśiṣṭāni ca sā(kā)myāni ca vedaśabdebhyaḥ eva mantrārthavādarūpebhyaḥ sṛṣṭyādau nirmitavān karmānurūpyeṇa | tathā ca paurāṇikāḥ |
ārṣāṇi (caiva) nāmāni yāś ca vedeṣu dṛṣṭayaḥ |
śarvaryante ********************** ||
nāmarūpaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ sthitīnāṃ ca prapañcanam |
evaitā nirmita (?) ***  śabdebhya īśvaraḥ || iti |
saṃsthāś cāsya pṛthak smārtā laukikyo vā — "idam anena kartavyam anyaiś (?) ca palāyanānveneti (pālanīyam eva" iti ) |
karmātmanāṃ ca devānāṃ so 'sṛjat prāṇināṃ prabhuḥ |
sādhyānāṃ ca gaṇaṃ sūkṣmaṃ yajñaṃ caiva sanātanam || 1.22 ||
karmātmanaś ca devāḥ karmāṅgadevatāḥ teṣāṃ sa(saṃ)sarjakasya te karmātmānaḥ prāṇināṃ sādhyānāṃ ca gaṇam | karmātmanām iti vartate | atiśayārthaś ca punar nirdeśaḥ | tathā ca sūkṣmam ity āha | yajñaṃ caiva bāhyam ādhyātmikaṃ cety arthaḥ | sanātanam ā(ma)nādikālapravṛttyā (?) nityam | [13]
agnivāyuravibhyas tu trayaṃ brahma sanātanam |
dudoha yajñasiddhyartham ṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇam || 1.23 ||
brāhmavāde āgneyaḥ ṛgvedo, vāyavye yajurvedaḥ, sauraḥ sāmavedaḥ iti | kenacid vṛttisāmānyena eṣv avasthitānāṃ vedānāṃ tebhyo dohanam ity ucyate |
kālaṃ kālavibhaktaṃ(ktīś) ca nakṣatrāṇi grahāṃs tathā |
saritas sāgarāñ chailān samāni viṣamāṇi ca || 1.24 ||
kālādiṣu kānicid yajñasiddhyarthāni kānicit | sukhānubhavaprayojanāni kānicid ubhayārthāni veditavyāni |
tapo vācaṃ ratiñ caiva kāmaṃ ca krodham eva ca |
sṛṣṭiṃ sasarja caivemāṃ sraṣṭum icchan imāḥ prajāḥ || 1.25 ||
tapaś cānnayaṇādi (cāndrāyaṇādi?) prasiddhaṃ karmendriyakāryaḥ (kāryam) | śabdaḥ priyahitarūpaḥ | nendriyam uktatvāt | rati(r) tapasi prītir na kāmaḥ | tathā ca punar vakti — kāmaṃ ca krodhaṃ eva (ca) | etat pratyanīkaḥ saṃsāryātmā darmasṛjyamānānāṃ sa karmāśrayanimitto 'yaṃ sṛṣṭau draṣṭavyaḥ | sṛṣṭiṃ sasarja ca | evamādirūpāṃ sṛṣṭim icchan imāḥ prajāḥ |
karmaṇāṃ ca vivekāya (-kārthaṃ) dharmādharmau vyavecayat |
dvandvair ayojayac cemāḥ sukhaduḥkādibhiḥ prajāḥ || 1.26 ||
karmaṇāṃ cātmasamavāyinām abhyudayapratyutthāyahetūnāṃ pṛthak karaṇāya dharmādharmau tadarthau prayogau taccabde nocyayete | sāmarthāt tau vyavecayat | katham | ayaṃ prayogo dharmāyāyaṃ ca punas tadviparīto 'dharmāya | tābhyāṃ cāyaṃ sukhaduḥkhādibhiḥ ayojayad imāḥ prajāḥ | mahadādi tatra prasaṅgenedaṃ bahvabhidhāya tām evopasaṃharati bhūtaviśeṣantām |
aṇvyo mātrā vināśinyo daśārdhānāṃ tu yāḥ smṛtāḥ |
tābhiḥ sārdham idaṃ sarvaṃ saṃbhavaty anupūrvaśaḥ || 1.27 ||
idānīṃ karmatve nimittatve saty evaṃ saṃsārasyānāditvād yatra kutracid utpannasyātmanaḥ sarvaprāṇabhṛj jātidharmaṃ (maḥ) pūrvābhyāsāt prātaḥ sa pratiṣidhyate |
yas (yṃ) tu karmaṇi yasmin sa nyayuṅkta prathamaṃ prabhuḥ |
sa tad eva svayaṃ bheje sṛjyamānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ || 1.28 ||
pūrvābhyāsapratibandhe saty ayaṃ puruṣaḥ karmajātisāmarthyād vartamānajātidharmam eva pratipadyate, [14] tatpratipattikarmopabhogavaśena vā kevalena | yathā matsyo jalataraṇaśayanaprasūtyanimeṣādīny eva jātyantaradharmam abhyastam apy evaṃ gavādayo 'pi draṣṭavyāḥ | kiṃ punas tat karma yato yasya pradarśanārtham ayaṃ teṣāṃ karma pratipattiprapañcaḥ prakriyate |
hiṃsāhiṃsre mṛdukrūre dharmādharmāv ṛtānṛte |
yad yasya so 'dadhāt sarge tat tat syā(svā)śayam āviśat || 1.29 ||
himsrāhiṃsre mṛdukrūre ṛtānṛte cetyevamādi yat karma dvandvaśaḥ prasiddhaṃ samānārthaṃ prāṇinikāyabhedāśrayaṃ tat teṣāṃ dharmādharmāv upādāya bhavati, nākasmikam | tathā ca – nirbījaḥ karma pratipattiprapañcaḥ prāṇinām | evam ayaṃ śloko vyatyastapadārthasambandhāc ca praguṇīkṛtyādhyāhāreṇa vyāghātaḥ prakṛtārthānugrahāya | kiṃ punar atrodāharaṇam idam [itīdam] ucyate |
yathartuliṅgāny ṛtavaḥ svayam evartuparyaye |
svāni svāny abhipadyante tathā karmāṇi dehinām(naḥ) || 1.30 ||
yathartuliṅgāni cūtamañjaryādi(dī)ni | ṛtavo vasantādayaḥ | svayam eva parivartamānāḥ krameṇa svāni svāny abhipadyante kartuṃ cūtamañjaryādīni liṅgāni kāryāṇīty arthaḥ | evaṃ tatra tatra prāṇi(ni)kāyebhyaḥ utpadyamānāḥ karmavaśāt prāṇino 'pi hiṃsrāhimsrādi kāryakāraṇabhūtān svayam eva pratipadyata iti tattvasṛṣṭyupasaṃhārānantaram idānīm anekaprayojanātmā sṛṣṭiṃ pradarśayiṣyann imāṃ varṇasṛṣṭiṃ tāvad āha – uttarārtham |
lokānāṃ tu vivṛddhyarthaṃ mukhabāhūrupādataḥ |
brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyaṃ vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ ca niravartayat || 1.31 ||
vivṛddhiṃ vṛṣṭibāhulyaṃ vā | etac cobhayaṃ karmataḥ | karma ca dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaṃ brāhmaṇādisaṃśritam | yato 'yamotarādharyya(muttarādharya?)vivakṣayopakāraviśeṣapradarśanasambandhena vā mukhādiṣv agnyādisambandhāpekṣayā vā mukhādikarmayogād vā tatprabhāvo guṇapravṛttyocyate | varṇānāṃ tathā ca kṛtvā mithunasaṃprayogadvāreṇemāṃ sṛṣṭim ācakṣate |
dvidhā kṛtvātmano deham ardhena puruṣo 'bhavat |
ardhena nārī tasyāṃ ca virājam asṛjat prabhuḥ || 1.32 ||
bahūpakārasambandhā dharmāpatyadhaneṣu cā (vā) | vibhāgaṃ nāryā svadehārdhavādo guṇavād iti vṛttānukathanaṃ vedaṃ yady āgamopapattibhyām aviruddhavaṃsoparaṃ(?) nāryā saha virājaṃ dīptimantam ātmavidham evāsṛjat | [15]
tapas taptvāsṛjad yaṃ tu sa svayaṃ puruṣo virāṭ |
taṃ mā(ṃ) vittāsya sarvasya sraṣṭāraṃ dvijasattamāḥ || 1.33 ||
teṣāṃ maharṣīṇāṃ manur bhagavān sarvasya jagataḥ saṃbhavanty ākhyāya bhūyaḥ sukhapratipattaye dṛṣṭāntatvena taṃ 'mā(ṃ) vitta" ityātmānam eva pradarśayati | tathā ca paurāṇikāḥ |
virājam asṛjat brahmā so 'sṛjat puruṣaṃ virāṭ |
puruṣaṃ taṃ manuṃ viddhi mānavyā yasya tāḥ prajāḥ ||
ahaṃ prajāḥ sisṛkṣus tu tapas taptvā suduścaram |
patīn prajānām asṛjaṃ maharṣīn ādito daśa || 1.34 ||
uttaraprakaraṇābhisambandhārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
marīcim atryaṅgirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratum |
prācetasaṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ ca nāradaṃ bhṛgum eva ca || 1.35 ||
ete maharṣayo viśeṣanāmabhir abhidhīyante | veśeṣaprakriyārtham ṛṣipūjāyām etā tannāmabhir ete yatho(thā) pūjyeran | tathā coktaṃ devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam |
ete manūṃs tu saptānyān sasṛjur bhūritejasaḥ |
devān devanikāyāṃś ca maharṣīṃś cāmitaujasaḥ || 1.36 ||
manvantarāṇāṃ nāmabhiḥ saptānāṃ smaraṇāt mantrāṇāṃ saptatocyate | mantrāṇāṃ devanindādīn, ye ca gaṇaśaḥ ākhyāyante vasvādayaḥ | etāṃs tu karmasiddhyartham asṛjat | tathodakadānādikarmasiddhyartham ṛṣi(ṣya)bhidhānān devān asṛjat |
yakṣo(kṣa)rakṣaḥpiśācāṃś ca gandharvāpsaraso 'surān |
nāgān sarpān suparṇāṃś ca pitṝṇāṃ ca pṛthaggaṇān || 1.37 ||
karmārtham ātmabhogasiddhyarthaṃ ca etān asṛjat | marīcyādīnāṃ ca prajāpatisambandhe naitad eṣāṃ punaḥ sṛṣṭyābhidhānam anuktaṃ ca kiṃcit | (?)
vidyuto 'śanimeghāṃś ca rohitendradhanūṃṣi ca |
ulkānirghātaketūṃś ca jyotīṃṣy uccāvacāni ca || 1.38 ||
vidyudādīny autpātikasya karmaṇaḥ pradarśanārthāni | kiṃ cānyat |
kiṃnarān vānarān matsyān vividhāṃś ca vihaṅgamān |
paśūn mṛgān manuṣyāṃś ca vyālāṃś cobhayatodataḥ || 1.39 ||
[16]
kṛmikīṭapataṅgāṃś ca yūkāmakṣikamatkuṇān |
sarvaṃ ca daṃśamaśakaṃ sthāvaraṃ ca pṛthagvidham || 1.40 ||
uktaprayojanaiṣā sṛṣṭiḥ |
evam etair idaṃ sarvaṃ manniyogān mahātmabhiḥ |
yathākarma tapoyogāt sṛṣṭaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam || 1.41 ||
evaṃ ca karmanimittaḥ saṃsāro trāsito(darśito?) bhavati | marīcyādiprajāpatisṛṣṭer ayam upasaṃhārārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
yeṣāṃ tu yādṛśaṃ karma bhūtānām iha kīrtitam |
tat tathā vo 'bhidhāsyāmi kramayogaṃ ca janmani || 1.42 ||
paśavaś ca mṛgāś caiva vyālāś cobhayatodataḥ |
rakṣāṃsi ca piśācāṃś ca mānuṣāṃś ca jarāyujāḥ || 1.43 ||
aṇḍajāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ sarpāḥ nakrā matyāś ca kacchapāḥ |
yāni caivaṃ prakārāṇi sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca || 1.44 ||
svedajaṃ daṃśamaśakaṃ yūkāmakṣikamatkuṇān |
ūṣmaṇaś copajāyante yac cānyat kiṃcid īdṛśaḥ (śam?) || 1.45 ||
udbhijjāḥ sthāvarāḥ sarve bījakāṇḍaprarohiṇaḥ |
oṣadhyaḥ phalapākāntā bahupuṣpaphalopagāḥ || 1.46 ||
uktānāṃ pūrvam atra ca prakaraṇe "yeṣāṃ tu yādṛśaṃ karma bhūtānām iha kīrtitam" iti nāmadheyanirdeśād evaitat | karma kīrtitaṃ karma teṣāṃ karmajatvān nāmnas tad yathā devanād dyotanād dyusthānād vā devā, ṛṣayo darśanād, yakṣaṇād yakṣāḥ, piśitāśanāt piśācāḥ, gaṃ(?) gamanāt gandharvāḥ, apa iva surasaḥ apsarasaḥ amṛtākhyāyaḥ(?) surā vā, alābhād asurāḥ, nāsty eṣām agam iti nāgāḥ, sarpaṇāt sarpāḥ, śobhanapatanāt suparṇāḥ, pātratvāt pitaraḥ, nānādyotanād vidyutaḥ, atyarthaśātādaśaniḥ, sekāt meghāḥ, rohitatvād rohitaḥ, indrajālapadayathātthadarśanād indradhanuḥ, ūrdhvakṛtatvād ulkāḥ, miśrībhāvād yūkāḥ, śarīrabhakṣaṇān matkuṇaḥ, ityevamādi |
apuṣpāḥ phalavanto ye te vanaspatayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
puṣpiṇaḥ phalinaś caiva vṛkṣās tūbhayataḥ smṛtāḥ || 1.47 ||
[17]
vṛkṣā vanaspataya iti nedaṃ vyavasthārtham ucyate | kiṃ tarhi | ubhayam ubhayatra | vṛkṣā vanaspatayo vanaspatayo vṛkṣāḥ | evaṃ ca sati vṛkṣagrahaṇena vanaspatigrahaṇaṃ sarvavanaspatīnām iṣṭo (?) yathārtha iti vṛkṣe vanaspatiśabdo dṛṣṭaḥ | ayaṃ ca vakṣyati | "vanaspatīnāṃ sarveṣām upabhogo yathā yathā" (8.285) iti | na ca tatrāmrādivṛkṣaparyudāsaḥ śakyo vijñātuṃ śāstrāntarābhāvāt samānakāryatvāc ca |
gucchagulmaṃ tu vividhaṃ tathaiva tṛṇajātayaḥ |
bījakāṇḍaruhāṇy eva pratānā vallya eva ca || 1.48 ||
vṛkṣādisthāvarātmāno hi sarva evaite |
tamasā bahurūpeṇa veṣṭitāḥ karmahetunā |
antaḥsajñā bhavanty ete sukhaduḥkhasamanvitāḥ || 1.49 ||
tamasā dharmeṇa bahurūpeṇānekaprakāreṇa veṣṭitāḥ sambandhāḥ śiṣṭākaraṇapratiṣiddhasevanārūpenāsatkarmahetunā antaḥsaṃjñā bhavanty ete sarva eva sthāvarātmānaḥ yathā jale bāleva (?) puruṣe vidyamānāv indriyavṛttidoṣaprasaṅgād anyato vā kāraṇān nāvirbhavati evaṃ vṛkṣādiṣu vidyamānāpīndriyavṛttiḥ prāṇādivṛttiś ca jātiprabandhān nāvirbhavati | karmataḥ ubhābhyāṃ sukhaduḥkābhyāṃ śa[1 akṣ unclear]nitās tu caitanyāvabhavati (?) | tathā ca śrutiḥ "sa yāṃ śākho(khāṃ) jīvo jahāti atha sā suṣyati" iti | nyāyaś ca tadviruddho na pramāṇam ity uktam | evaṃ ca sati,
etadantās tu gatayo brahmādyāḥ samudāhṛtāḥ |
ghore 'smin bhūtasaṃsāre nityaṃ santatayāyini || 1.50 ||
tathā | dharmādharmanimittatvaṃ saṃsārasyāśritatvaṃ bhavaty uta evaṃ ca vyāsaḥ |
ūrdhvamūlam adhaḥśākham aśvatthaṃ prāhur avyayam |
chandāṃsi yasya parṇāni yas taṃ veda sa vedavit || [BhG 15.1]
ityevamādinā karmato jagatutpattipradarśanānantaram idānīṃ tasyopasaṃhāraṃ pralayākhyaṃ darśayati |
evaṃ sarvaṃ sa sṛṣṭvedaṃ māṃ cācintyaparākramaḥ |
ātmany antardadhe bhūyaḥ kālaṃ kālena pīḍayan || 1.51 ||
bhogasthitikālam upasaṃhārakālena tiraskurvan yatheha puruṣo jāgradvṛttyā tiraskaroti evam ihādhikasya punar hetor yena iti | yena,
yadā sa devo jāgarti tadedaṃ ceṣṭate jagat |
yadā svapiti śāntātmā tadā sarvaṃ nimīlati || 1.52 ||
dṛṣṭo hi loke vyāpāro paramaḥ svāpakāle puruṣasya | evaṃ ca,
tasmin svapiti tu svasthe karmātmānaḥ śarīriṇaḥ |
svakarmabhyo nivartante manaś ca mlānim ṛcchati || 1.53 ||
yathā tathehāpīti |
yugapat tu pralīyante yadā tasmin mahātmani |
tadāyaṃ sarvabhūtātmā sukhaṃ svapiti nirvṛtaḥ || 1.54 ||
hiraṇyagarbhajagadupasaṃhārapradarśanaprasaṅgenādhunā saṃsāryātmana utkrāntiṃ darśayati |
tamo 'yaṃ tu samāśritya ciraṃ tiṣṭhanti sendriyaḥ |
na yaḥ svaṃ kurute karma tadotkrāmati mūrtitaḥ || 1.55 ||
yadāṇumātriko bhūtvā bījaṃ sthāsnu cariṣṇu ca |
samāviśati saṃsṛṣṭas tadā mūrtiṃ vimuñcati || 1.56 ||
evaṃ sa jāgratsvapnābhyām idaṃ sarvaṃ carācaram |
saṃjīvayati cājasraṃ pramāpayati cāvyayaḥ || 1.57 ||
prakṛtārthopasaṃhārārthaḥ ślokaḥ utpattipralayāv asya jagato 'bhidhāyedānīṃ sthitikālapradarśanaprakaraṇārthopanyāsārtham idaṃ prastauti |
idaṃ śāstraṃ tu kṛtvāsau mām eva svayam āditaḥ |
vidhivad grāhayāmāsa marīcyādīṃs tv ahaṃ munīn || 1.58 ||
śāstrakaraṇāl liṅgāc chāstraṃ kurvan | na tu dharmaṃ yena manor api hy anyata evāgamadharmāvagamaṃ syāt (?) tathā cānāditām asya dharmasya vyākaroti śāstrasya sādhutvākhyāpanāya marīcyādīṃś codāharati | tad evam idaṃ svāyaṃbhuvaṃ śāstraṃ darśayitvāntaraśeṣatayā bhagavān manur idam āha |
etad vo 'yaṃ bhṛguḥ śāstraṃ śrāvayiṣyaty aśeṣataḥ |
etad dhi matto 'dhijage sarvam eṣo 'khilaṃ muniḥ || 1.59 ||
tatas tathā sa tenokto maharṣir manunā bhṛguḥ |
arthato na tu śabdataḥ |
tān abravīd ṛṣīn sarvān prītātmā śrūyatām iti || 1.60 ||
prītātmatvaṃ ca pravacanaśaktisaṃbhāvanayā guruniyogād yuktam anekaśiṣyasaṃnidhau bhṛguniyogārthaḥ ślokaḥ | svagotraprītyā ca bhṛguḥ kalpito granthakāreṇa yathā | bhūtānukathāṃ vā idānīṃ sthitikālaṃ manvantākhyaṃ jagato vakṣyāmīty anenābhiprāyeṇedam āha | [19]
svāyaṃbhuvāsyāsya manoḥ ṣaḍvaṃśyā manavo 'pare |
sṛṣṭavantaḥ prajāḥ svāḥ svāḥ mahātmāno mahaujasaḥ || 1.61 ||
tān manuputrān (?) viśeṣanāmabhiḥ pradarśayati vyavahārtham |
svārociṣaś cottamaś ca tāpaso raivatas tathā |
cākṣuṣaś ca mahātejā vivasvatsuta eva ca (?) || 1.62 ||
tulyaśaktiprabhāvatvam eṣāṃ darśayati |
svāyaṃbhuvādyāḥ (khyaḥ) saptaite manavo bhūritejasaḥ |
sve sve 'ntare sarvam idam utpādyāpuś carācaram || 1.63 ||
āpuḥ pālitavantas te | evam ete manavaḥ samānabalavardhayor vijñeyaḥ (vṛddhayo vijñeyāḥ?) viśiṣṭakālāni manvantarāṇi tāni caibhiḥ samākhyāyante | yavarttano (?) manvādibhiḥ manvantare kālaparimānāvadhāraṇāyedam adhunocyate |
nimeṣā daśa cāṣṭau ca kāṣṭhās triṃśat tu tāḥ kalā |
triṃśatkālā muhūrtaṃ syād ahorātraṃ tu tāvatā (taḥ) || 1.64 ||
ahorātre vibhajate sūryo mānuṣalaukike |
rātriḥ svapnāya bhūtānāṃ ceṣṭāyai (?) karmaṇām ahaḥ || 1.65 ||
pitrye rātryahnī māsaḥ pravibhāgas tu pakṣayoḥ |
karmaceṣṭāsv ahaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ śukla¥ svapnāya śarvarī || 1.66 ||
daive rātryahnī varṣaṃ pravibhāgas tayoḥ punaḥ |
ahas tatrodagayanaṃ (?) rātriḥ syād dakṣiṇāyanam || 1.67 ||
brāhmasya tu kṣapāhasya yat pramāṇaṃ samāsataḥ |
ekaikaśo yugānāṃ ca kramaśas tan nibodhata || 1.68 ||
catvāry āhuḥ sahasrāṇi varṣāṇāṃ tat kṛtaṃ yugam |
tasya tavacchatī saṃdhyā saṃdhyāṃśaś ca tathāvidhaḥ ||1.69 ||
itareṣu sasaṃdhyeṣu sasaṃdhyāṃśeṣu ca triṣu |
ekāpāyena vartante sahasrāṇi śatāni ca || 1.70 ||
[20]        yad etat parisaṃkhyāta ādāv eva caturyugam |
etad dvādaśasāhasraṃ devānāṃ yugam ucyate || 1.71 ||
daivikānāṃ yugānāṃ tu sahasraparisaṃkhyayā |
brāhmam ekam ahar jñeyaṃ tāvatīṃ rātrim eva ca || 1.72 ||
tad vai (tato) yugasahasrāntaṃ brāhmaṃ puṇyam ahar viduḥ |
rātriṃ ca tāvatīm eva te 'horātravido janāḥ || 1.73 ||
saṃstutir darśanārthānāṃ hy akasmāt stutiḥ pravartata iti kṛtvā,
tasya so 'harniśasyānte prasuptaḥ pratibudhyate |
pratibuddhaś ca sṛjati manuḥ sadasadātmakam || 1.74 ||
antarālapralayānantaraṃ pratibuddhasya hiraṇyagarbhasyāyaṃ piṇḍasṛṣṭyartho nirdeśaḥ | atha vā mahāpralayakālaparisamāptyanantaraṃ paramātmanaḥ | tathā ca vakṣyati: "ity eṣāṃ sṛṣṭir āditaḥ" (1.78) iti |
manaḥsṛṣṭir vikurute codyamānaḥ(naṃ) sisṛkṣayā |
hiraṇyagarbhapakṣe manas tadantaḥkaraṇaṃ piṇḍasṛṣṭtiṃ vikurute | paramātmapakṣe tu mahatattvam ādhyaṃ manas tathā ca paurāṇikāḥ |
buddhir mano 'tha vijñānaṃ vikāraḥ prathamaṃ tathā |
paryāyavācakāḥ śabdāḥ mahataḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
prapañcatanmano mahatsṛṣṭiṃ vikurute | svavikārasyāhaṃkārasya codyamānam | kena |
paramātmanaḥ sisṛkṣayā |
ākāśaṃ jāyate tasmāt tasya śabdaṃ guṇaṃ viduḥ || 1.75 |
ākāśāt tu vikurvāṇāt sarvagandhavahaḥ śuciḥ |
balavān jāyate vāyuḥ sa vai sparśaguṇo mataḥ || 1.76 ||
vayor api vikurvāṇād virociṣṇu tamonudam |
jyotir utpadyate bhāsvat tad rūpaguṇam ucyate || 1.77 ||
jytotiṣaś ca vikurvāṇād āpo rasaguṇātmikāḥ [-guṇāḥ smṛtāḥ] |
adbhyo gandhaguṇā bhūmir ity eṣā sṛṣṭir āditaḥ || 1.78 ||
ebhiḥ ślokair imāṃ mahābhūtasṛṣṭiṃ sāmānyata uktvā viśeṣato vyācaṣṭe | ekaprakaraṇārthaṃ ca tvanyan (?)mahadādi tat sarvaṃ punas tathaivotpadyata iti draṣṭavyam evamādyaṃ na tv asṛṣṭiprakriyāśeṣam abhidhāyedaṃ prakriy[ā]m adhikṛtyocyate |
tat prāg dvādaśasāhasram uditaṃ daivikaṃ yugam |
[21]        tad ekasaptatiguṇaṃ manvantaram ihocyate || 1.79 ||
manvantrakālapariṇāmāvadhāraṇārthaḥ ślokaḥ | evam,
manvantarāṇy asaṃkhyāni sargaḥ saṃhāra eva ca |
krīḍann ivaitat kurute parameṣṭhī punaḥ punaḥ || 1.80 ||
kṛtayugādisvabhāvaḥ idānīṃ nirdiśyate prakaraṇaprāptaḥ |
catuṣpāt sakalo dharmaḥ satyaṃ caiva kṛte yuge |
nādharmeṇāgamaḥ kaścin manuṣyān upavartate || 1.81 ||
yo 'yaṃ varṇadharmaś caturvidhaḥ catuṣpād eṣa sakalaḥ samagra api guṇo yathopadeśam anuṣṭhīyate 'dhikṛtaḥ puruṣaiḥ kṛtayuge kasya hetoḥ | yena "satyaṃ caive kṛte yuge" iti samānam ubhayor viśeṣaṇam | caśabdaḥ samuccayābhāvāt pādapūraṇārthaḥ śraddhāsamuccayārtho vā | evaśabdo 'vadhāraṇārthaḥ | satyam eva tadātiśayavataḥ śraddhāsahitaṃ na tadviparyayeṇāto bhavaty ete tad evaṃ tathā ca tretādiṣu dharmasaṅkocāvahe tu manṛtādipadārthaṃ (tv anṛtādi?) vakṣyati "corikānṛtamāyābhir dharmaś cāpaiti pādaśaḥ" (1.82) iti | yataś caitad evam ataḥ śraddhāsāmarthād ayaṃ catuṣpād varṇadharmas tadānīm asau satk[ā]lo (sakalo?) bhavati evaṃ ca kṛtvā yuktam asattvasya hetvarthaṃ pṛthagvacanam | yataś caitad evam athaḥ | nādharmeṇa vyavasthā tānovadhigamaḥ (?) kaścid vedaśāstrasya smṛter vā manuṣyān upavartate | kāraṇābhāvād dhetvarthanigamanārthaḥ uttaraślokārdhaḥ | atha vā saṃ caivety etad anyathocyate (?) | katham | sattvaṃ hi yathābhyupagamanānuṣṭhānam api guṇatve 'pi sati padārthāntarārthād vaktavyam asya pradarśanam | yathā "māsam imaṃ śrāddhaṃ kariṣye" ity abhyupagamyāviguṇaṃ ca tat karoty athābhyupagataṃ kālam | evam anyad api (?) ca kartavyam udāharaṇīyaṃ ca | tathā ca yuktopadeśam idaṃ pṛthak pṛthak pūrvasmād itaratra satyavacanasākālyāntarbhāvād avacanīyam asminn arthe | tathā ca padārthabhedād uktasamuccayārthaś ca śabdo yukto 'smin pakṣa ity eva śabdaś cobhayāvadhāraṇārthaḥ | anyat samānam | pūrveṇāthavā guṇaśabdo 'yaṃ catuṣpād iva catuṣpād ayaṃ dharmas tasmin kāle yathā loke śavādipadārthaś catuṣpād eva dharmo 'pi tadguṇayogāc catuṣpād uparyate tadguṇayogaś ca sakalatvam avaiguṇyaṃ ca tadānīṃ dharmasya yato 'yam api cec catuṣpād ucyate | guṇatve (?) | satsv api catuṣpādeṣu kṛtayugasvābhāvyāt | "satyaṃ caiva kṛte yuge" ityevamādi samānaṃ pūrveṇa | apare tu caturaḥ pādān ācakṣate | tapojñānayajñadānāni vakṣyamāṇāni "tapaḥ paraṃ kṛtayuge" (1.86) ityevamādīni | tad ayuktaṃ pramāṇābhāvād anyaparatvocya(?) tadabhidhānasyāśramāc ca (?) teṣām apy etat brāhmaṇādivarṇadharmaikadeśobhidhānāt tad ayuktaś catuṣpācchabde 'nyakṛtsattvād dhetvabhāvāc ca |
itareṣv āgamād dharmaḥ pādaśas tv avaropitaḥ |
[22]        caurikānṛtamāyābhir dharmaś cāpaiti pādaśaḥ || 1.82 ||
itareṣu tretādiṣu āgamāc chāstrād dharmavyavasthārūpo yaḥ asau pādaśo 'varopitaḥ pratiyugaṃ tasya pādaḥ pādaśaḥ | kena | caurikānṛtamāyābhiḥ | nātra yathāsaṃkhyaṃ caurikādīnāṃ tretādiṣu dṛṣṭi(ṣṭa?)virodhāt | kiṃ tarhi | sarvāny evaitāni tretādiṣu vidyamānāni dharmasaṃkocahetutvenopadiśyante vikalpitāni bahvalpatayā tāratamyam āśritya | evaṃ ca sati "dharmaś cāpaiti pādaśaḥ" iti yad idaṃ dvitīyaṃ dharmagrahaṇam etanmukhyasyābhyudayahetor vyavasthākāryasya dharmasyābhāvaṃ darśayati kāraṇābhāvāt | yataś caitad evam ataḥ,
arogāḥ sarvasiiddhārthāś caturvarṣaśataṃ yugāḥ (śatāyuṣaḥ) |
kṛte tretādiṣu tv eṣāṃ vayo hrasati (?) pādaśaḥ || 1.83 ||
arogā rogakaraṇābhāvāt tadānīṃ kāmyakarmasāphalyāc ca sarvasiddhārthās caturvarṣaśatāyuṣa ity atra śataśabdo bahvartho na gaṇanārthaśāstravirodhād etad vakṣyāmaḥ | evaṃ drāghiṣṭhakālāyuṣaś caturvarṇaśatāyuṣo manuṣyāḥ kṛtayugasvabhāvato dharmānugraheṇa cedānīṃtanebhyo dīrghāyuṣas tretādiṣv eṣāṃ yathā vayo hrasati pādaśaḥ evaṃ siddhyārogye api hrasataḥ pradarśanārthatvāt vayograhaṇasya | yuktaś ca tayoḥ prakṛtatvādigrahaṇaṃ satāṃ nārthatvād yugsvabhāvānuvidhānena caivaṃ vā,
vedoktam āyur martyānām āśiṣaś caiva karmaṇām |
phalanty anuyugaṃ loke prabhavāś ca śarīriṇām || 1.84 ||
tatra vedoktaṃ nāmāyuṣaḥ sahasrasaṃvatsarādiṣu tadanuṣṭhatṝṇāṃ yadarthaṃ gṛhītaṃ tan na | "śatāyur vai puruṣaḥ" ityevamādiśāstravirodhād ayam apy [1 akṣ unclear]ad ahnāṃ vā tadaśakyatvāt" (?) iti sūtrakāraiḥ kalpyānta(kalpyate?) | tad api pradhānakalpanāyām asaṃbhavatyāṃ(ntyāṃ) guṇādy (?) āśrayituṃ yuktaṃ syād yathā ca pradhānakalpanoparodhanyāyonta(?) na kalpyate | tathā ca vakṣyāmaḥ | athāyurvede yad uktaṃ tat paribhaktaṃ (?) sahasrasaṃvatsaraprāpakaṃ karma tatsaṃvivakṣite idam ucyate | tathā ca śiṣṭamūrtiḥ (śiṣṭair uktam?) "daśavarṣsahasrāṇi rāmo rājyam akārayat" | asmin sahasrasaṃvatsarādiṣu prabhāvantas tena karmaṇādhikriyante aśvamedhādhikāravat | na caikadā śrutyarthādarśanād asāmarthyaṃ vedaśāstrākhyā(trāṇya)numātuṃ śakyam aśvamedhavad a(ya)m eva āśiṣaḥ śrautakarmaṇāṃ kāryāṇāṃ pravartitāḥ(tikāḥ) putrādiprayojanāḥ phalanty anuyugaṃ loke kṛtādiyugasvabhāvānuvidhānena karmavaiguṇye sati | satyaṃ tasya phalapradhānapratibandhābhāvāt | prabhāvaś ca śarīriṇām | na śrautasmārtakarmādhikārapuruṣāṇām antardhānāveśākāśam anabhicārābhiśāpādi | "phalanty anuyugaṃ loke" iti vartate | evaṃ ca saty uktasaṃbandhārthāpekṣayedam ucyate |
anye kṛtayuge dharmāś tretāyāṃ dvāpare pare |
[23]        anye kaliyuge nṝṇāṃ yugahrāsānurūpataḥ || 1.85 ||
yugahrāsānurū [20 akṣ missing] rtho 'nuvadam idam anyad yugasvabhāvānuvidhānaṃ prakaraṇe taddharmāṇāṃ tadanuvidhānaṃ vaktavyatām apaṃ(?) na śiṣyate |
tapaḥ paraṃ kṛtayuge tretāyāṃ jñānam ucyate |
dvāpare jñānam evāhur dānam eva kalau yuge || 1.86 ||
sarvayugeṣu sarvaprakāro dharma ācāryamāṇa upalabhyate | prādhānyaṃ tv eṣāṃ yugasvabhāvānuvidhānena vivakṣitatven(edam) uktaṃ puruṣ [12 akṣ. missing] nenaiṣāṃ prāyo vṛttir evam imaṃ svabhāvabhinnakṛtayugādikālaviśeṣaṃ manuśāstrād upalabhya vismayān mīmāṃsakaḥ codayāmāsa: "sarvam evaitad atyantā pratyakṣatvād aśraddheyam | ato yādṛśa ** dīnāṃ kālas tādṛk sarvade [1 akṣ unclear]na santi kṛtayugādayaḥ kālavibhāgās taddharmāś ca" | yata idam atrocyate | asmād apratyakṣato 'py asti devasaṃvatsaraś caturyugādi manuṣyakālaṃ smṛtyanvākhyānasāmarthād yatheha dharmādharmaprāptivyavahāravyavasthā idaṃśāstraprāmāṇyād astīti gamyate, evam atra pramāṇābhāve 'pi smṛtiśāstreṇa caturyugādir apy adhyavaseyaḥ kālaḥ | atha vābhyupagamyate | asau kartavyākartavyavyavasthāpi tarhi nāsmāt pratipādanīyā, viśeṣo vā vacanīyaḥ | yat sāmarthyagṛhītasyāsya kuśalākuśalakarmavyavasthāsādhutvam eva bhavati | netaratra | sa vaikapramāṇaśastrākyaḥ tayoḥ padārthayoḥ sattā vāsattā vā pratipādako viśeṣo durvacaḥ | na cāntareṇa tadviśeṣavacanam ayaṃ karmakālavyavasthāyāḥ sadasadvibhāgo vacanīyo na yuktaḥ pratipattum | niṣkāraṇe ca tadvibhāgapratipattinirbandho viparyayaiṣamikatve (?) api prāpnuyātām | te cāniṣṭatvāt svapakṣoparodhinī | mārutām iti | yataḥ kuśalākuśalakarmavyavasthāpa(va)d avaśyānujñeyā | yato na pramāṇādi (ni)yamaḥ | laukikī kṛtayugādikālavyava (1 akṣ unclear) [sthā ?] bhavatīti | tathā dṛṣṭasyāhorātrapakṣamāsartvayanasaṃvatsaralakṣaṇasya kālātmano vidyamānasyānvākhyānaṃ smṛtyā tathaivādṛṣṭasyāpi devadevasaṃvatsaracaturyugādeḥ svata eva tadanvākhyānaṃ bhavitum arhati nāsataḥ | na caikāgamapramāṇasadbhāvopanibandhanayoḥ kālayeśca (?) tulyapramāṇatvāt sadasadbhāvaḥ śakyaḥ kalpayitum asati viśeṣe | na ca tadviśeṣapramāṇaṃ śāstragataṃ kiṃcid upalabhāmahe | yenaitat pratipādyāmahe | na ca lokasaṃpratipattipramāṇāntarānugrahalakṣaṇo viśeṣo 'sya bhede samarthaḥ kṛto nirapekṣatvād alaukikārthapratipattau smṛtiśāstrasya | yathā hīdam alaukikasya dharmasya dharmādharmasādhanayor hetuphalasaṃpratipādane samarthatvān na pramāṇāntaram apekṣate, evaṃ sarvārtheṣv alaukikeṣu | tasmād ayam aviśeṣasmṛtyanujñāyaitaṃ(?) viśeṣam ucyate [smṛtyanujñāya cainaṃ viśeṣam ucyate] | evaṃ hi kalpyamāne yatra yatra nyāyānugrahaḥ śāstrasya, tatra tatra prāmānyaṃ syād vā | anyatraivaṃ ca sāpekṣatvādisamartham | dharmādharmaprāpti[11 akṣ. missing]padyeta | na ca nyāyapramāṇagamyā karmavyavastheṣyate | aprāpte ca [24] śāstram arthavad bhavati, na ca pramāṇāntaraprāpte | yato na kiṃcid etattulyaś ca pramāṇāntarānugrahe [19 akṣ missing]khyānaśāstraṃ pratyakṣapramāṇāntarānugṛhītatvāt yathā bhūtārthavacanaṃ kalpyate evaṃ devasaṃvatsaracaturyugādikālāt kathanaśāstram api pramāṇa[19 akṣ missing]māṇam uktam | yathāyam atyantālaukikayor dharmādharmasādhanayoḥ hetuphalasambandhavyavasthā padārthaḥ pratyakṣānumānayoḥ pramāṇayor aviṣayabhūtasmṛtiśāstrapramāṇadṛṣṭam ubhayam apy alaukakaḥ kālaḥ avihitasarvātīndriyārthadarśanasamarthena smṛtiśāstreṇānvākhyātam om iti pratipādanīyaḥ | na hy ekadeśe tatpramāṇam apramāṇaṃ caikādaśa iti yuktam evaṃ pratipattum kāraṇābhāvādṛṣṭena cāhorātrādikālavibhāge 'yaṃ dṛṣṭaḥ kālaprapañcaḥ (?) saṃgacchateti virodhena yato 'yam anumānāgamapramāṇānugṛhītasadbhāvas tadavirodhī na ca yuktaḥ pratyākhyātum | na ca rājavṛttamātram eva kṛtayugādayaḥ | paramārthayugeṣu hiyasu(?) tat saty upapatteḥ | tulyaśāstrapramāṇānāṃ ca padārthānāṃ dṛṣṭavad adṛṣṭasiddhir dṛṣṭāntaḥ | tad yathā jambudvīpamaṇḍalasya prasiddhasya pramāṇaśāstraprapañcasya himavadarvāg vā prapañcadṛṣṭyā purāṇaśāstrāt tatparabhāgo 'pi samānaśāstras tathārūpo 'stīti pratīyata, evaṃ jyotiśśāstropadiṣṭasya padārtharāśer ekadeśagrahaṇena candrasūryoparāgādinā pariśiṣṭasyāpi yātrālagnāder yathāśāstram astitvānumānam api pratiṣiddhaṃ vaidikeṣu ca padārtheṣv evam eva mantravidhyutpannaḥ putrādipadārthaḥ sākṣāt kṛtvo(tvā) dṛṣṭasya svargāder anumānaṃ yathaivam ihāpi dṛṣṭādṛṣṭayoḥ kālavyavasthayor ekaśāstrapramāṇatvāt dṛṣṭapa(va)d adṛṣṭasiddhyā bhavitavyaṃ yadi caitad astitvaṃ vedaśāstrasmṛtipramāṇādivirodhe varteta tato na pratipadye hi tadasadbhāvaḥ (?) | yathā puruṣaudāsīnyaṃ katarttatva(?)vacanavirodhena vartamānaṃ nābhyanujñāyate evam ihāpi syān na ca mānapye(vye)va (?) kevalā smṛtir adṛṣṭkālābhidhāyinī yatas tadadhigataṃ pratyekatvād āśakyeta (?) purāṇakālajñāne | abhihitam arthaṃ vistareṇācakṣate purāṇaśāstraṃ tāvat
sargaś c pratisargaś ca vaśo manvantarāṇi ca |
vaṃśānucaritaṃ caiva purāṇaṃ pañcalakṣaṇam ||
ityevamādi | tac cānekaliṅgānugatapramāṇabhāve satyatāpramāṇam ity aśakyaṃ kalpayitum | tāni liṅgāni te vā atharvāṅgirasa etad itihāsapurāṇam abhyacapa(vada?)t "itihāsapurāṇaṃ pañcamaṃ vedānām" iti ca | dvitīyaṃ vedaliṅgam itihāsapurāṇaṃ vidyād upaniṣad iti | maivam | āditṛtīyaṃ kālajñānaṃ tu vedaśāstrasyāvyavahitopakāravṛttitvena gṛhyamāṇārthatayā cāprasiddhatarapramāṇābhāvaṃ sutarām iva pratyutsāryam evaṃ ca sati yathartulakṣaṇakālavibhāgāśritātmasvabhāvabhedas tu san sthāvaraśarīrāśrayeṇa tatkāryaviśeṣeṇa saha dṛṣṭa evaṃ kṛtatretādvāparakalikālavibhāgo 'py aṅgīkṛtayugasvabhāvo bhedo manuṣyādyāśrayeṇa siddhyārogyāyuḥ pravṛttatvāt tatkāryaviśeṣeṇa saha yathāśāstraṃ bhavitum arhati | evaṃ ca vedakalpapramāṇaśāstratrayaṃ siddham adṛṣṭaṃ devāhotrātrādikālaṃ kevalavaiyatyām na pratipadyase | tanmānaṃ cātra pramāṇaśaraṇaṃ yuktā(yuktyā?) vā tatra pratipattir bhavataḥ | kevalamīmāṃsakasyāna(bhi)hitav(tva?)gocarānyaśāstrāntaraviṣayo darśapūrṇamāsavadarūpa[25]prakriyādharmas tvayā śakyaḥ prajñāpayitum | yathaivam ayam apy adṛṣṭakālaḥ kṛtayugādis tadaviṣayatvān mīmāṃsādhyetṛbhir durvijñeyaḥ ataś ca svaviṣayadarśanavyavasthānātikramenendriyabahutvavac chāstrabahutvaṃ kutrai(kutra vai?)yarthyaṃ satatvapradarśanaśeṣam upapādye | tathā ca sarvavidyaḥ sarvaṃ veditum arhati nālpavidyo samagrakaraṇavad evaṃ ca purāṇaś ca sarvadharmasūtrakārāṇām avibhāgena svaśāstreṣu sarvavidyādhigamopadeśau bahuśruti(ḥ) smṛtiś ca taddhetūnām
itihāsapurāṇābhyāṃ vedaṃ samupabṛṃhayet |
bibhety alpaśrutād vedo mām ayaṃ prapa(ha)riṣyati ||
iti purāṇaślokaḥ | tathā,
buddhivṛddhikarāṇy āśu dhanyāni ca hitāni ca |
nityaṃ śāstrāṇy avekṣeta nigamāṃś caiva vaidikān ||
yathā yathā hi puruṣaḥ śāstraṃ samadhigacchati |
tathā tathā vijānāti vijñānaṃ cāsya rocate || iti | (MDh 4.19–20)
"mānavaḥ sa eṣa bahuśruto bhavati, loko vedavedākgavid vā, vākovākyetihāsapurāṇakuśalaḥ, tadapekṣas tadvṛttiḥ" iti gautamīyam (GDh 8.4–7) | śaṅkhalikhito (tābhyāṃ) vivṛtameyocaratra (vivṛtam etac cottaratra) "purāṇadharmaśāstranyāyo mīmāṃsā catvāro vedāḥ ṣaḍaṅgāni caturdaśavidyāsthānāny etāni brāhmaṇān adhikuruta" iti | evaṃ ca darśanāntaravika(re 'pi ki)lāsyabhavato yad dṛṣṭakālaviṣayaṃ samyag darśanaṃ nāsti sarvatraividyavṛddhānujñātaviṣayaṃ tad yuktaṃ ca yatra sādhanābhāvād iti | yat kiṃcij jaimineḥ kṛtayugādikālasadbhāvapratyākhyānādhikaraṇam ārabhya pādavihitam abhaviṣyat, pratipadyemahi tadabhāvakṛtakoṭibhavadāsabhāvyaṃ (?) vā na cāsti yato 'pramāṇābhivyavahāro bhavata unmārgagāmina eva tasmād asti yathāśāstram anvākhyāto jagadutpattisthitipralayakālo devasaṃvatsarakālaś ca | tathā ca mantrārthavādaliṅgāni "tad vetad ataś ca vyākṛtam āsīt | tan nāmarūpābhyām eva vyākriyate asad vā idam agre āsīt tad āhuḥ kiṃ tad āsīt" iti ca | brāhmaṇamantras tu "hiraṇyagarbhaḥ samavatratāgre" itievamādi | na ca liṅgasya prāmāṇyaṃ śaktaṃ kalpayitum | tathā hi saṃdigdheṣu vākyaśeṣād evamarthaviśeṣapratipattir udāharaṇam uktaḥ "śarkarā upadadhāti" ityevamādi yathā, evam anyatrāpi yathā syād iti | yataś caitad evam ato na jagat svābhāvikam, kiṃ tarhi | hiraṇyagarbhaprāṇijanatvāt puruṣakarmapratibhavam evam ayam api saptadvīpasamudro lokasaṃniveśaḥ sarvaprāṇyabhyudayapratyavāyopabhogāyatana-bhūtas tatkarmabhya evotpattum arhaty anagna(?)svābhāvikam idaṃ yaś caitaṃ necchanti tasya śarīrādy api sukhaduḥkhopabhogāyanakarmanimittaṃ prāpnoti | om iti bruvato 'numānāgamavirodhaḥ | na cārdhaja(ra?)tīyaṃ yuktam abhyupagantum | puruṣasyeṣṭāna(ni?)ṣṭopabhogopasarjanaṃ bhūtānāṃ bāhyādhyātmikānāṃ tulyalakṣaṇatvād ekatra svābhāvikatvam anyatra ca [26] karmopādānatvam ity aṇḍa(?)mahatoś cānityatvāt sthūlam imaṃ mahāṇubhūtamaṇḍalam utpādavināśaghaṭam udbhavitum arhaty ataś ca jagadutpattisthitipralayakṛtamad(?) dhiraṇyagarbhāhorātrākyā yathāśāstraṃ bhavitum arhanti evam etasmin vyavasthite prakṛtam adhikṛtyaitam (tad?) ucyate |
sarvasyāsya tu sargasya guptyarthaṃ sa mahādyutiḥ |
mukhabāhūrupajjānāṃ pṛthak karmāṇy akalpayat || 1.87 ||
sargasṛṣṭiḥ sahacaritaṃ jagatsāhacaryavad ucyate | guptir anucchitiḥ | sthitiś ca tadarthaṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ vakṣyamāṇāni pṛthak karmāṇy akalpayat dharmavṛttyarthāni | tāni ca,
adhyāpanam adhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ yājanaṃ tathā |
dānaṃ pratigrahaṃ caiva brāhmaṇānām akalpayat || 1.88 ||
prajānāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ dānam ijyādhyayanam eva ca |
viṣayeṣv aprasaktiṃ ca kṣatriyasya samādadhat (diśat?) || 1.89 ||
paśūnāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ dānam ijyādhyayanam eva ca |
vaṇikpathaṃ kusīdaṃ ca vaiśyasya kṛṣim eva ca || 1.90 ||
ekam eva tu śūdrasya prabhuḥ karma samādiśat |
eteṣām eva varṇānāṃ śuśrūṣām anasūyayā || 1.91 ||
yena brāhmaṇānāṃ tad ijyāṅgabhūtāñ ca devādīnāṃ parasparopakārasambandhenāvṛtti-karmabhiḥ pratigrahādibhir ijyādibhiś ca dharmaprayo(ja)nair jagatsthitir abhivṛddhiś cāta eṣāṃ upadeśo 'smin prakaraṇe | katham | brāhmaṇas tāvat pratigṛhṇan kṣatriyavaiśābhyāṃ tayor upakaroti | tau ca tadu(?) | tau brāhmaṇasya | evaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ śūdrasya ca yojyam | tathā brāhmaṇādayo yajamānā(nā)m evamādīnām | devādayaś ca brāhmaṇādīnām | tathā vakṣyati:
agnau prāstāhutiḥ samyak ādityam upatiṣṭhate | (MDh 3.76)
ityevamādi | tad evam eṣāṃ parasparopakārasambandhena jagataḥ karmanimittā sthitir abhivṛddhiś ca yathā, evaṃ tadutpattir apīti | saiṣā śāstrastutir anena prakāreṇa tasya sādhutvakhyāpanāya tadupadeśārthānuṣṭhānanimittatvāt kuśalakarmaṇo dharmākhyasya jagatutpattihetor evaṃ ca saty arthavādārtham evāyam upadeśaḥ karmaṇāṃ na vidhyarthe | vidhis tūttaratra vakṣyate | evam ayaṃ dharmādharmanimittaḥ saṃsāro jagadutpattyādidvāreṇānukrāntaḥ śāstrārambhasāmarthyapradarśanārthaḥ | kathaṃ kṛtvā | ayam asmād eṣāṃ puruṣāṇām abhyudayapratyavāyahetor dharmādharmayor idaṃ tatpratiprayojanaparihāraprayojanam ataḥ puruṣārthakāritvād etan mānavaṃ dharmaśāstram adhyetavyam | adhītya ca śrotavyam | śrutvā ca samyak kāruṇyād yathādhikāram arthibhyo [27] 'nuvaaktavyam iti | idānīṃ brahmaṇaḥ stutidvāreṇa punaḥ puruṣārthakriyāyāṃ dharmaśāstrasya sādhutvapradarśanārtham idaṃ pūrvasmāt prakaraṇāntaram ārabhyate |
ūrdhvaṃ nābher medhyataraḥ puruṣaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
tasmān medhyatamaṃ cāsya mukham uktaṃ svayambhyvā || 1.92 ||
sāmānyanirdeśe 'pi sati asmin yaḥ prajāpateḥ kālavibhāgāśrayān mukh(y)asya medhyatvena saṃstutir uttaratra brāhmaṇotpattistutyartham | tathā ca darśayati |
uttamāṅgodbhavād jyaiṣṭhyāt brahmaṇaś caiva dhāraṇāt |
sarvasyaivāsya sargasya dharmato brāhmaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 1.93 ||
uttamāṅgodbhavatvaṃ jyaiṣṭhyatvaṃ ca sādhāraṇaṃ brāhmaṇasya | tadbhavapratapas (?) tapyate | bahūni varṣasahasrāṇy antaradevātī(?) ityevamādi | evaṃ ca sarvabhūtānāṃ sthāvarasarīsṛpamṛgapaśumanuṣyāṇāṃ brāhmaṇāḥ viśiṣṭāḥ | kiṃ bahunā |
utpattir eva viprasya mūrtir dharmasya śāśvatī |
sa hi dharmārtham utpanno brahmabhūyāya kalpate || [MDh 1.98]
utpattimātram eva vināpi saṃskārair brāhmaṇasya mūrtir dharmasya śāśvatī saṃskāra[1 akṣ missing]tvākhyāne brāhmaṇas tu trayyākhyasya dharaṇaṃ sādhāraṇam api sa(?)pūrvābhyāṃ hetubhyām upasaṃhṛtam | brāhmaṇastutau sampadyate kāraṇam | pravacanātiśayāpekṣayā vedam asādhāraṇaṃ bhavati | sarvasyaivāsya sargasya dharmato 'nuśāsitṛtvena brāhmaṇaḥ prabhur nānyathā brāhmaṇastutiprayojanaṃ ca prakaraṇaṃ te [prakaraṇānte?] darśayiṣyāma iti yataś caitad eva | tasmāt
taṃ hi svayambhūḥ svād āsyāt tapas taptvādito 'sṛjat |
havyakavyābivāhyāya sarvasyāsya ca guptaye || 1.94 ||
uttamāṅgodbhavatvaviśeṣaṇaparopāyam asyādarārthaḥ ārambhaḥ | taṃ hi svayambhūḥ brāhmaṇaṃ hiraṇyagarbho nānyaprajāpatis tadadhikṛtaḥ prajāsṛṣṭau svād āsyād asṛjat | tapaś ca taptvā nāntareṇātmāsaṃyamam avajñāyātatpadārthāgauravāt | āditaś ca | anyasṛṣṭer na | paścād anādareṇa | evam idam ādarotpādanapradarśanārthaṃ viśeṣaṇatrayaṃ prakṛtārthastutyai sampadyate | kasya punar hetoḥ | ayaṃ hiraṇyagarbha evam ādaraṃ cakāra | brāhmaṇasṛṣṭau havyakavyābhivāhyāya tathātithīnāṃ pratyakṣatvāt taddānaṃ sākṣāc chakyam anuṣṭhātum, evaṃ devapitaro na pratyakṣā yena tebhyaḥ sākṣād dīyata etat tayos tatprāptisādhanatvān mahāprayojanatvaṃ brāhmaṇasṛṣṭeḥ | kiṃ ca, sarvasyāsya ca guptaye brāhmaṇo hi juhvad āhuter ādityatṛptidvāreṇa sarvalokānugrahī ca sampadyata ity uktam | yasmād ataś cāyaṃ mahāprayojano yuktaṃ evammariṇotpādayitum (?) | evaṃ caitad ataḥ.
yasyāsyena sadāśnanti havyāni tridivaukasaḥ |
[28]        kavyāni caiva pitaraḥ kiṃ bhūtam adhikaṃ tataḥ || 1.95 ||
anyaprāptyupāyāsaṃbhavād dhavyakavyānāṃ devapitṛbhir yuktam idaṃ vismayāt tatstutivacanaṃ "kiṃ bhūtam adhikaṃ tataḥ" iti tajjagdhānnāśanena vā devapitṝṇāṃ prakalpya, pādeneyaṃ stutiḥ | asmin pakṣe stutyabrāhmaṇaviśeṣanārtham idam adhunā slokadvayam ārabhyate | evaṃ ca na jātimātrāpekṣeyam asmin prakaraṇe brāhmaṇastutiḥ, kiṃ tarhi | yādṛggunaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vakṣyāmas tadapekṣeyaṃ brāhmaṇapraśaṃsā |
bhūtānāṃ prāṇinaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ prāṇināṃ buddhijīvinaḥ |
buddhimatsu narāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ nareṣu brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || 1.96 ||
brāhmaṇeṣu ca vidvāṃso vidvatsu kṛtabuddhayaḥ |
kṛtabuddhiṣu kartāraḥ kartṛṣu brahmavedinaḥ || 1.97 ||
neyaṃ ca stutir brāhmaṇasya jātimātrāpekṣayaivātha tu saṃskārān(t) prāpnoty atho dharmārtham utpannaḥ saṃskṛteḥ śarīram,
utpattir eva viprasya mūrtir dharmasya śāsvatī |
sa hi dharmārtham utpanno brahmabhūyāya
brahmatvāya
kalpate || 1.98 ||
yataś caitad evam ataḥ |
brāhmaṇo jāyamāno hi pṛthivyām adhijāyate |
īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ dharmakośasya guptaye || 1.99 ||
brāhmaṇo jāyamāna eva | hiśabdo 'vadhāraṇārthaḥ | kva | pṛthivyām | upari bhavati | kasya | sarvabhūtānāṃ iti vyavahitenābhisambandhaḥ sāmarthād vijñeyaḥ | yena kāraṇena ye na (?) paraḥ | teṣāṃ kiṃ rājatvam | nety āha | kathaṃ tarhi | dharmakośagoptṛtvāt | etac cāsyādhyāpanānuvacanayājanakṛtaṃ veditavyam | tathā ca vakṣyati |
sarveṣāṃ brāhmaṇo vidyād vṛttyupāyān svakarmasu |
prabrūyād itarebhyaś ca svayaṃ caiva tathā bhavet || [MDh 10.2]
ity evaṃ ca dharmakośagoptur itthaṃbhūtasya |
sarvaṃ svaṃ brāhmaṇasyedaṃ yat kiṃcij jagatīgatam |
śraiṣṭhyenābhijanenedaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ sarvam arhati || 1.100 ||
sarvaṃ svaṃ brāhmaṇasyedam | svam iva svam | kathaṃ gamyate ''syaivaṃ luptopam[ā]tvam | stutiprakaraṇasāmarthyāt neya[m] | rājadaṇḍaprāyaścittopadeśasāmarthyebhyaś cāyam evārtho gamyate (?) | yathā [29] hi pūrvottaravirodhād ubhayaśāstraṃ prasjyeta yat kiṃ ca jagatīgatam aanyadīyam api yad vā hiraṇyādi | na kevalaṃ yājanādhyapanapratigrahādibhir āgataṃ guṇavantaṃ ca praty eṣā stutiḥ | yad ayam āha — śraiṣṭhyainābhijanenedaṃ mukhodbhavatvena etac cetanayor api sāhacaryāt | brāhmaṇaviśeṣas tu tair eva | ataś caivaṃvidho brāḥmaṇaḥ sarvam aarhati | arhatiśabdāc ca stutiparam etat pratīyate yathāvocāma, na tu svasvāmisambandho ppadeśaparam | tathā ca sati stutyā guṇavṛttyā cedaṃ śakyam evaṃ vaktum |
svam eva brāhmaṇo bhuṅkte svaṃ vaste svaṃ dadāti ca |
ānṛśaṃsyād brāhmaṇasya bhuñjate hītare janāḥ || 1.101 ||
yathā nṛpavat kriyayā svaṃ bhujyate eva manuḥ(naḥ) prakṛtyaiva pitrādiṣu brāhmaṇo bhuṅkte 'tithitvena vivāhādiṣu cāvaṣṭabhyaiva pratigṛhṇāti dānakriyāṃś copadiśan na svāmivad dāpayati tatprayuktatvāc ca dāpayan dadātīty ucyate guṇataḥ ebhiḥ karaṇaiḥ "svam eva brāhmaṇo bhuṅkte svaṃ vaste svaṃ dadāti ca" ityuktiś ca pūrvaśloka upaśabdalo(lakṣaṇo)panyāsaśabdārtha-vyavasthāhetoḥ | evaṃ vyavasthite mā (ā)nṛśaṃsyāt brāhmaṇasya sāṅgopaniṣadvedaśāstrādhyetus tadvijñātuś cādhikārapuruṣānuśāsanādhikṛtasya śāstreṇa bhuñjate hītare janāḥ | śāstracoditānuṣṭhānaphalaṃ dṛṣṭam adṛṣṭaṃ ca yadā hītthaṃbhūto vaktādhikṛtaśāstreṇa yathādhikāram arthibhyo nānubrūyāt grantham arthaṃ ca | syāt (?) | tathā ca śrutiḥ "yo ha vai vidyāmedhīty ārthinau sa pradadyāt sa kāryahānyāt sa śreyasā dvāram āvṛṇuyād adhyāpayen mahad etad yaśas svavāco vikārayat kavayo vadanty asmin yoge sarvam idaṃ pratiṣṭhitam | ya evaṃ vidur amṛtās te bhavanti" iti | sarvathaiva tatprakaraṇasāmarthyād brāhmaṇastutiparam eva veditavyam | yataś caitd āha (?) |
tasya karmavivekārthaṃ śeṣāṇāṃ cānupūrvaśaḥ |
svāyambhuvo manur dhīmān idaṃ śāstram akalpayat || 1.102 ||
anuśāsitabrāhmaṇasyādhikṛtapuruṣānuśāsane stutiprakaraṇasāmarthyād iti | anyas tv āha brāhmaṇapraśaṃsāyāḥ prayojanas tv eko 'yaṃ kāraṇānupadeśād asaṃbhavāc cāsya prayojanaślokaś copariṣṭāt |sa cāyam ucyate sa,
viduṣā brāhmaṇeṇedam adhyetavyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
śiṣyebhyaś ca pravaktavyaṃ samyaṅ nānyena kenacit || 1.103 ||
ubhayathāpīyaṃ svaparaśāstraṃ sambandheṣvidvattāna (vidvadbrāhmaṇena?) smṛtiśāstrādhyayane kāraṇam arthavirodhād anyatrādarśanāc ca | na ca śāstram evaitat kalpayituṃ yuktam | viśeṣānupadeśād vedaśāstradarśanāñ ca | na ca vidvadbrahmaṇādhyayanam asya stutau sava(sarva)nniyamaparisaṃkhyopamaṃ tathā yathā [yathā tathā?] vakṣyāmaḥ | yato 'smāt sāmarthyād iyam adhyayanacodanā śāstraśravaṇārthā draṣṭavyā | katham | [30] adhyāhāreṇa padavākyapramāṇaśāstraviduṣā brāhmaṇenedam adhyetavyaṃ śrotavyam ity arthaḥ | yasmād iha vidhipratiṣedhapratiprasavaniyamaparisaṃkhyāpunarvacanārthavādaś ca yathā tathā śrutapramāṇagrāhiṇo viduṣaḥ paramārtha hanti (?) hetavas tasmāt paramadurvijñānatayaivaṃ paṇḍitair upavedanīyam etat smṛtiśāstraṃ viditvāryeṇa kṛtam idaṃ viśeṣaṇam | viduṣā brāhmaṇenedam adhyetavyaṃ śrotavyam ity arthaḥ | prayatnataḥ samyak śiṣyebhyaś ca pravaktavyaṃ cety evam eva samyak śabdāv ādaro yuktāv ubhayaviśeṣaṇe draṣṭavyau | tathā ca vakṣyati |
yaṃ vadanti tamobhūtā mūrkhā dharmāṃś ca tadviduḥ |
tat pāpaṃ śatadhā bhūtvā tān vaktṝn adhigacchati || [MDh 12.115]
anyaśāstreṣu tenaivam anyathā vacano(naṃ) vidhiḥ śāśtratvāt teṣām | ato na yatnataḥ kriyate puruṣārtho 'vyavahitopakāravṛttitvād asyeti | atha vānadhikāraśāstratvād asyātantram adhyayanam | evaṃ ca sati pravacanārthādeśa (?) asya hi nirdeśaḥ | aviśeṣaṇasya śāstrastutyai pūrvavad vyākhyeyam | tathā ca kṛtvāyaṃ kaiścid ucyate:
śiṣyebhyaś ca pravaktavyaṃ samyaṅ nānyena kenacit |
iti | asya pratiṣedhasya pravacanenābhisambandho nādhyayanenety etad asamīkṣitābhidhānam, yenātra pakṣadvaye 'py adhyayanacodanā nāsti | tathā coktam evam | ayaṃ brāhmaṇastutiprayojanaślokaḥ | tasya karmavivekārtham atha vā saviśeṣaṇam | idam atra brāhmaṇagrahaṇam adhyayanapravacanayoḥ kāryayoḥ na niyamārtham asya varṇatrayādhikārād adhyayanasyāsmiṃc chāstre "prabrūyād brāhmaṇas teṣām" (MDh 10.1] iti cānena pravacanasya siddhatvāt | kimarthaṃ tarhi | smṛtiśāśtrastutaye | yathā, loka "itthaṃguṇam idaṃ vastrayugaṃ rājñāṃ paridhatavyaṃ nānyena" iti vastrayugapraśaṃsārtham | rājagrahaṇaṃ saṃniyogato rājānaṃ niyamayaty evam ihāpīti | tathedam anyad apy atrodāharaṇaṃ vacanīyam rājopabāhyo 'yam aśvo hastīty evamaditye evam idaṃ vidvadbrāhmaṇasambandhena śāstram adhyayanādiprayojanārthaṃ punaḥ stutvā sākṣāt stauti |
idaṃ śāstram adhīyāno
jānānas sāmarthyāḍ ity uktam |
brāhmaṇaḥ saṃśitavrataḥ |
śiṣṭānuṣṭhānato pratiṣiddhavarjakaś ca
manovāgdehajair nityaṃ karmadoṣair na lipyate || 1.104 ||
nityaṃ tatkāraṇābhāvād(?) evaṃ ca śāstrastho (?) 'sau |
punāti paṅktiṃ vaṃśyāṃś ca sapta sapta parāvarān |
pṛthivīm api caivemāṃ kṛtsnam eko 'pi so 'rhati || 1.105 ||
pratigṛhītur alipyamānaḥ pratigrahadoṣeṇa | prakṛtārthastutiparam etat kiṃcānyat |
idaṃ svastyayanaṃ śreṣṭham idaṃ buddhivivardhanam |
[31]        idaṃ yaśasyaṃ satatam idaṃ niḥśreyasaṃ param || 1.106 ||
svastīty utkarṣas tatprāptyartham ayaṃ [ayanam?] mārgaḥ panthāḥ | idam śāstraṃ kṛtayet [kīrtayet?] | na tadarthānuṣṭhānadvāreṇedaṃ niḥśreyasaṃ param | niḥśreyasārthatvān naiśreyasaṃ vā sataddhitam | ko 'yaṃ nirdeśas tadarthavivakṣayātha vā svastīty avināśaḥ | ayanaṃ mārgaḥ | svastyartham ayanam anarthoparamas tasya sādhanaṃ svatyayanam | niḥśreyasam utkarṣo 'bhyudayo mokṣākhyaśāstraṃ tadarthaṃ tacchabdaṃ bhavati | taddhitapratyaye 'py (?) ayam evārthaḥ | idānīm anantaraślokatrayopadiṣṭārthakāraṇaṃ vakti | yasmāt,
asmin dharmo 'khilenokto guṇadoṣau ca karmaṇām |
caturṇām api varṇānām ācāraś caiva śāśvataḥ || 1.107 ||
asmin dharmo 'khilenoktaḥ kartavyaḥ parihartavyaś ca | na tu yathā buddhādivacanādiṣu parānuśāsanāpekṣatvam (?) akṛtsnārthatvāt teṣām evātra nirapekṣatvād asya guṇadoṣau ca karmaṇāṃ phalasyeṣṭāniṣṭatvasya prarocanāvidveṣaṇārtho (?) 'pi hitapratiṣiddhayoś caturṇām api varṇānām api śabdāntaraprabhāvānāṃ coktatvāt (?) teṣām ācāraś caiva śāśvataḥ puruṣadharmākhas tatsaṃnidhau ca karmāṣṭakādismārtaṃ vijñeyam | atha vā karmaśabdagṛhīto 'py ācāram uttarārtho nirdiśyate | tathā cedam āha |
ācāraḥ paramo dharmaḥ śrutyuktaḥ smārta eva ca |
svastutyartho dṛṣṭāntārthavādaḥ | yathā śrutykto 'gnihotrādiḥ smārthaś cāṣṭakādiḥ paramo dharmaḥ, evam ācāro 'pi | tasmād asmin sadāyukta ity etasmin arthaṃ (?) evaṃ vā lokanyāyastutyaikatvād yadā punar ayam anyaḥ pāṭhaḥ "ācāraḥ paramo dharmaḥ śrtuyktaḥ smārta eva ca" iti, tadā yuktas trayaḥ (tv ayam?) pāṭhaḥ:
tasmād asmin traye(sadā) yukto nityaṃ syād ātmavān dvijaḥ || 1.108 ||
iti | tathā vyāsaślokaḥ |
śrautakarma na cec chaktaḥ kartuṃ smārtaṃ samācaret |
tasyāpy aśaktaḥ karaṇe samā(dā)cāre vahed budhaḥ || iti |
yadāpi "ācāraparamo dharmaḥ" ity ayaṃ bahuvrīhisamāsanirdeśaḥ kriyate tadāpy ayam eva paṭhitvavyaḥ ślokaḥ: "tasmād asmin samā(sadā) yukto nityaṃ syād ātmavān dvijaḥ", ity ācārasyetaradharmābhyāṃ paramatvena śavaṇāt | yataś caitad evam athaḥ:
ācārād vicyuto vipro na vedaphalam aśnute |
ity anena jñāyate evācārasya śrautasmārtakarmādhi(di)kāraṇatvāt | tad dhi na saphalaṃ tatkarmavaiguṇyāt
ācāreṇa tu saṃyuktaḥ saṃpūrṇaphalabhāgataḥ (bhāg bhavet || 1.109 ||
[32]
ity anena punar jāyate viguṇād api karmaṇo 'nityād asti phalam iti | yad atrārthatvaṃ tadvidvāṃso jñātum arhaty ubhayatra dṛṣṭāntopapateḥ |
evam ācārato dṛṣṭvā dharmasya munayo gatim |
sarvasya jatago (tapaso) mūlam ācāraṃ jagṛhuḥ param || 1.110 ||
uttaratrācārastutis tadvidhyanuṣṭhān (?) prarocanārthā | iha tu śāstrastutyarthatvena uktānuvādaḥ prakaraṇasāmarthād vijñeyam(yaḥ) | evam imāṃ brāhmaṇastutidvāreṇa vyavahitāṃ śāstrastutim uktvā sākṣāc cedānīm anukramaṇīs tatstu(tya)rtham evārabhate —
jagataś ca samutpattim saṃskāravidhim eva ca |
vratacaryopacāraṃ ca snānasya ca paraṃ vidhim || 1.111 ||
dārādhigamanaṃ caiva vivāhānāṃ ca lakṣaṇam |
mahāyajñavidhānaṃ ca śrāddhakalpaṃ ca śāśvatam || 1.112 ||
vṛttīnāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ caiva snātakasya vratāni ca |
bhakṣyābhakṣyaṃ ca śaucaṃ ca dravyāṇāṃ śuddhim eva ca || 1.113 ||
strīdharmayogaṃ tāpasyaṃ mokṣaṃ saṃnyāsam eva ca |
rājñaś ca dharmam akhilaṃ kāryāṇāṃ ca vinirṇayam || 1.114 ||
sākṣipraśnavidhānaṃ ca dharmaṃ strīpuṃsayor api |
vibhāgadharmaṃ dyūtaṃ ca kaṇṭakānāṃ ca śodhanam || 1.115 ||
vaiśyaśūdropacāraṃ ca saṃkīrtṇānāṃ ca saṃbhavam |
āpaddharmaṃ ca varṇānāṃ prāyaścittavidhiṃ tathā || 1.116 ||
saṃsāragamanaṃ caiva trividhaṃ karmasaṃbhavam |
niḥśreyasaṃ karmaṇāṃ ca guṇadoṣaparīkṣaṇam || 1.117 ||
jātidharmān kuladharmān deśadharmān śāśvatān |
pāṣaṇḍagaṇadharmāṃś ca śāstre 'smin uktavān manuḥ || 1.118 ||
jagataś ca samutpattim ity ata ārabhyānukramaṇīś ślokāḥ | śāstrasāmarthyapradarśanārtham | katham evam idaṃ bahuvṛttāntatvād adhyetavyam ity atha vā pradarśitaśāāstrātmanaḥ śrotuḥ [33] pūrvaṃ saṃkṣepābhidhānenānukramaṇyākhyena paścād vistarābhidhānād asaṃmohena pratipattir yathā syāt sa ca puruṣārtha iti | atas tadartham evāsya saṃkṣepavistarābhyām upadeśo yuktaḥ | tathā ca vyāsaślokaḥ |
vistīrya hi mahāgrantham ṛṣiḥ saṃkṣepam abravīt |
iṣṭaṃ hi viduṣāṃ loke samāsavyāsadhāraṇam || iti |
yathedam uktavāṇ chāstraṃ purā pṛṣṭo manur mayā |
tathedaṃ yūyam apy adya matsakāśān nibodhata || 1.119 ||
dvitīyādhyāyābhisambandhārthaḥ ślokaḥ | śrīḥ śrīḥ śrīḥ |
iti ślāghyasmṛtisāyujyācāryaguror ānirnīyasya ṛjuvimalakṛtau
manuśāstravivaraṇasmṛtimīmāṃsāyāṃ prathamo 'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ |
dvitīyo 'dhyāyaḥ
evaṃ tāvac chāstrārambhasāmarthyapradarśanārthas tricaturmātrārthavādaḥ prakaraṇaḥ prathamo 'nukrāntaḥ, adhyayanavijñānānuvacanakriyāśeṣaśāstrasya "āsīd idaṃ tamobhūtam" (MDh 1.5) ity ata ārabhya | idānīṃ tatpratijñā[tā]rthānukramaṇāyedam ā[ra]bhyate —
vidvadbhiḥ sevitaḥ sadbhir nityam adveṣarāgibhiḥ |
hṛdayenābhyanujñāto yo dharmas taṃ nibodhata || 2.1 ||
((asya ślokasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ 2.1 saṃkhyākasya ślokasya vyākhyāyām uddhṛtaḥ | tatra "varṇadharmān nibodhata" iti pāṭhaḥ))
tatra sarvavedaśāstrārthvido vidvāṃso bhṛgvādayaḥ śiṣṭās tatsambandhasāmarthyād asya nānyaśāstravido vidvāṃso 'sambandhāt | tathā ca vakṣyati yaḥ | kecit kṛtasya viddharmo(?), "eko 'pi vedavid dharmam" (MDh 12.113), "teṣāṃ vedavido brūyuḥ" (MDh 11.85) iti ca | tair vidvadbhiḥ sevito 'nuṣṭhito yaḥ padārthaḥ kartrādikārakavyāpārānuṣṭheyaḥ sa dharmo dharmārthatvād yataś caiva tad evam ataḥ kṛtsnena śāstreṇa vakṣyamāṇaṃ vyavasya(?) taddharmārthena nobodhateti pāṭḥāntarasyedam anuṣṭhātṛviśiṣtapuruṣadvāreṇāparaṃ viśeṣaṇam ucyate sadbhir iti | yady api ca sacchabdaḥ sadbhāvasādhubhāvayoḥ prayuktas tathāpi hi sādhubhāve sāmarthād draṣṭavyaḥ | taiḥ sadbhiḥ samastavedaśāstrārthajñair(?) hy ubhayathā dṛṣṭaḥ santaś cāsantaśca yat tvaṃ caitad viśeṣaṇopādānam | sadbhir itīdaṃ cāparaṃ viśeṣaṇam | nityam iti vidvadbhir anabhibhūtaparamārthavijñānaiḥ | karmāviśeṣaṇaṃ vedam | yo hi vidvadbhiḥ sevito nityaṃ sa dharmo, na kadācin na hy apratirūpaḥ padārtha evaṃvidhair mahātmabhiḥ nityam āsevituṃ yuktaḥ | saty apīndriyārthopalabdhyabhibhave | sattvaviśeṣaṇaṃ vā nityagrahaṇam | vidvadbhiḥ sevitaḥ sadbhir śāstranigṛhītātmabhiḥ rāgadveṣābhāvaviśeṣaṇaṃ nityam adveṣarāgibhir ity etad atra pradhānaṃ tat pra(ni)rūpyate | katham | vidvāṃso nityam | kathaṃ ca ca (?) santapte (?) bhavanti | nityam | kathaṃ ca sevante | nityam | yata idaṃ sarvaśeṣatvāt pradhānaṃ smṛtidharmalakṣaṇam upādīyate | nityam adveṣarāgibhir iti codyate | karaṇalakṣaṇavato hi puruṣasyeṣṭāniṣṭaviṣayopalabdhisukhaduḥkhavinmūlava[1 akṣ unclear]bhāvinau rāgadveṣau tadarthatvāt tasya | tathā ca brāhmaṇam: "sa(na) ((ha)) vai saśarīrasya sataḥ priyāpriyayor apahatir [35] asti" iti | yatra ca priyāpriye tatrāvaśyaṃbhāvitvena tanmūlau rāgadveṣāv iti satyam etat | tatra tu tau rāgadveṣāv utpannau kaścin niyantuṃ śaknoty akāryataḥ yaḥ śuddhābhiviniveśābhyāṃ paramārthadarśanābhyāṃ saṃbhāvitaprasaṃkhyāno viśiṣṭadharmānugṛhītāntarātmā | na ca sarvaḥ | yato rāgadveṣakāryābhāvāt nityam adveṣarāgibhiḥ ity evaṃ samarthatvād yuktam idaṃ viśeṣaṇam upādātum | yadi caitad evaṃ bhavati, tato yogamokṣopadeśaśāstrāṇy arthavanti bhavanti, nānyathā | tasmāt yuktam idaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ nityam adveṣarāgibhir iti | hṛdayenābhyanujñāta ity etad aparaṃ smārtadharmaviśeṣaṇam | katham | hṛdayātmapradeśasamaveto mantrabrāhmaṇādibhāvanākhyo hṛdayaṃ talliṅgābhyanujñāt ity ucyate | atha vā sarvakarmānuṣṭhānasahakāribhāvaprasāda ātmatuṣṭiśraddhādiparyāyaḥ śiṣṭapuruṣadharmaḥ pūrvavad dhṛdayāyatano hṛdayaṃ tenānugraho yenābhyanujñātaḥ viśiṣṭadharmānugṛhītānāṃ vā śiṣṭānāṃ bahu(ku)tsiteṣu kartavyapadārtheṣu yathecchaṃ pratyakṣapramāṇakalpaṃ pratibhāpramāṇaṃ hṛdayābhyanujñānubandhaliṅgānāṃ vā buddhipūrvavyavahāriṇāṃ sati śāstravittve satpratyayapadārtheṣu vṛttihṛdayābhyanujñā | evaṃ ca, sthitam etad hṛdayenābhyanujñāta iti yo dharmas tan nibodhateti | etad vyākhyātam | evaṃ ca yatheha loke āyurvedaśāstravidā pe(pre?)kṣāpūrvakāraṇāc ca satā manuṣyeṇa pittādhikaprakṛtinā pratyākhyātapittaghnadravyopayogenājñātavīryam api sad anyair dravyaṃ tenopayujyamānaṃ dṛṣṭvā pittaghan ity avaśī(dhī)ryate, evam ihāpi vedaśāstrārthajñair upahatarāgadveṣaprapañcadoṣair atyantadṛṣṭadoṣahānyā niḥsaṃdigdhasādhubhiḥ śiṣṭair bhṛgvādibhir anuṣṭhānāt smṛtidharmasya sādhutvam anāmnātasyāpi sākṣād vedaśāstre 'numātavyaṃ prayojanam | katham | puruṣaviṣayaṃ dha[rma]smaraṇam | itareṣām ācārād iti | kimarthaṃ punar idam ārabhyate | sarvasmṛtidharmāṇāṃ sāmānyalakṣaṇasiddhyartham | tatsiddhiprayojanaṃ tarhi vaktavyam | idam ucyate | pauruṣeyāpauruṣeyāsmaraṇāvibhāgapratipattiḥ (?) prayojanaṃ katham | puruṣaviṣayaṃ hi smaraṇam itareṇa miśraṃ durvijñeyaṃ syād antareṇedaṃ smṛtisāmānyalakṣaṇam | asmiṃs tu sati vibhāgaḥ suvijñāto bhavati | idam apauruṣeyaṃ śāstuḥ smaraṇam adṛṣṭasya prayojanam | idaṃ ca pauruṣeyam aśāstrasmaraṇaṃ dṛṣṭārtham iti | sa ca puruṣārthas tasmād yukto 'syārambhaḥ, pratipattilāghavārthaṃ vā | etad dhi sarvasmṛtidharmāṇāṃ sāmānyalakṣaṇam asya pradarśanāya śāstram idaṃ vartayiṣyate |
kāmātmatā na praśastā na caivehāsty akāmatā |
kāmyo hi vedādhigamaḥ karmayogaś ca vaidikaḥ || 2.2 ||
yad yac chāstreṇopadekṣyate karma tat tan (?) niṣkāmenānuṣṭheyam iti paribhāṣārthaślokārambhaḥ | kāmātmatā phalabhogābhiprāyeṇānuṣṭhā(nā)nāṃ nityānāṃ rasā(?) na praśastā | nindauṣā tasyāḥ | evaṃ ca ninditā na samācared ity etasmāt sāmarthāt pratiṣedhamātravākyānumeyaḥ kīdṛśaṃ phalaṃ prāptābhiprāyeṇānuṣṭheyam ity etat | kasmāt | śāstropadeśasāmarthyād anityatvaprasaṅgāc ca | karmānuṣṭhānasya yasyaiva hi [36] phalaprāptyabhiprāyaḥ, kartus tasyaiva tatsādhanakriyānuṣṭhānaṃ kriyā prāpnoti | laukikavan nānyasya kriyāprayau (?) niṣkāmasya karmānuṣṭhānasya nityatvaṃ sidhyati | yata evamartheyaṃ paribhāṣā | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
akṛtsnatvāt sādhanānāṃ maurkhyāt kartṛkṛtas tathā |
phalasyānabhisambandhā daṣavitropadhiḥ (?) smṛtaḥ ||
vyāsaś caivam āha: "mā karmaphalahetur bhūḥ" (BhG 2.47) | apare tu, dṛṣṭaprayojanābhisambandhapratiṣedhārtham idam ācakṣate, "kāmātmatā na praśastā" iti, teṣāṃ punar uktadoṣaḥ | vakṣyati "arthakāmeṣv asaktānām" (2.13) iti | tasmād yathokta evārthaḥ | evaṃ ca sarvaśāstrakriyāsu tatpratiṣedhe prāpte idam ārabhyate: "na caivehāsty akāmatā" | kathaṃ gamyate | yad ayam āha "kāmyo hi vedādhigamaḥ" vedādhyayanavijñānaprāptir vedādhigamaḥ | sa ca prārthanīyaḥ | na hy asā cendhavad (?) anityaṃ nyāsavad yadṛsā(?'dhigantuṃ śakyaḥ | "karmayogaś ca vaidikaḥ" agnihotrādikarmānuṣṭhānaṃ caivam eva vyākhyeyam | evaṃ ca tatkaraṇākaraṇayoḥ vibhāgenāśakyatāyām (?) idaṃ nivṛttyartham ārabhyate |
saṃkalpamūlaḥ kāmo vai yajñāḥ saṃkalpasaṃbhavāḥ |
vratāni yamadharmāś ca sarve saṃkalpajāḥ smṛtāḥ || 2.3 ||
indriyārthopabhogecchālakṣaṇasya kāmasya mūlaṃ tat saṃkalpaḥ | kaḥ punar asau (?) guṇavato viṣayasyābhidhā | na tasyeha grahaṇam | kiṃ tarhi | kartavyapadārthāvadhāraṇam iha saṃkalpaḥ | sa mūlaṃ kāmasya kartavyecchāyāḥ | evaṃ yajñāḥ śrautasmārtā ijyādirūpāḥ saṃkalpasaṃbhavās tatkartavyecchāprayatnadvāreṇocyanta evaṃ yajñā evaṃvṛttādikā niyamānuṣṭhānalakṣaṇāḥ niyamadharmāś ca nivṛttirūpā evaṃ ca kriyāphalaṃ na saṃkalpiṣyate | kiṃ saṃkalpābhāvāc ca nityakarmānuṣṭhānataḥ kāmātmatā na bhaviṣyati yajñādisaṃkalpāc ca tadanuṣṭhānecchāprayatnadvāreṇa "kāmyo hi vedādhigamaḥ karmayogaś ca vaidikaḥ" (2.2)  ity etat dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
akāmasya kriyā kācid dṛśyate neha kasyacit |
yad yad dhi kurute kiṃcit tat tat kāmasya ceṣṭitam || 2.4 ||
kāmārthaḥ saṃkalpaḥ kāmaśabdenocyate | tatprakaraṇasāmarthād evaṃ cākāmasya saṃkapasyecchā kāryā kriyā kācin na dṛśyate tad upapannam | tadā devo vāma (?) "yad yad dhi kurute kiṃcit tat tat kāmasya ceṣṭitam" ity upasaṃhāraḥ prakṛtasya | evaṃ ca |
teṣu samyag vartamāno gacchaty amaralokatām |
yathāsaṃkalpitāṃś ceha sarvān kāmān samaśnute || 2.5 ||
[37]
kāḥ punas teṣu samyag vṛttir nityānāṃ karmaṇāṃ phalābhisambandhatyāgenānuṣṭhānaṃ kāmyānāṃ ca parityāgena taṃ tathā teṣāṃ śrutatvād devādhigamakarmayogau ca kāmyān evāpratiṣedhāt (?) | anyathā ca tada(nu)papatteḥ | evaṃ ca teṣu yathāśāstraṃ vartamāno vidvān gacchaty amaralokatām | kriyāpadārthasvābhāvyena śāstralakṣaṇād upakarṣaprāptyupāyaḥ syānirdiṣṭaphalānāṃ (?) ca karmaṇāṃ svargaḥ phalam ity āhur ācāryāḥ | "yathāsaṃkalpitāṃś ceha sarvān kāmān samaśnute" iti | anena śāstrapratyakṣaṃ sarvakāmaprayojanatvaṃ (?) karmaṇo 'ntadyate (?) parā paribhāṣā |
vedo 'khilo dharmamūlaṃ smṛtiśīle ca tadvidām |
ācāraś caiva sādhūnām ātmanas tuṣṭir eva ca || 2.6 ||
prāmāṇyāya kathaṃ kṛtvā | vedaśāstramūlatvād smṛtiprāmānyasya | tathā ca vakṣyati "śrutiprāmāṇyato vidvān svadharme niviśeta vai" (2.8) | vyavahārārthaiveyaṃ (?) śāstre | vakṣyati "eko 'pi (?) vedavid dharmam" (12.113) ity evamādismṛtyupalakṣaṇārthā ca paribhāṣā | vakṣyati hi "smṛtiśīle ca tadvidām" iti | athedānīṃ vivriyate (?) | vida jñāne | jñāyate 'nena karaṇabhūte nāsmin vādhikārabhuktāni tāni kriyādharmaśreyāṃsīti vedaḥ | atha vā vidur (vidtṛ?) lābhe labhyate nāsmin pūrvoktāni kriyānivedādyatha(artha?)vādasattāyārmanvantyane(?) nāsmin pūrvoktānīti vedaḥ | katham anekāni santi na
