śabdārthasya sattvaṃ yuktaṃ śabdapramāṇatvāt teṣāṃ kriyādīnām | tatas tāni santīty ucyate | guṇa vida vicāraṇe vicāryante 'nenārthādyaṅgair asmin vā ni vāyunta[1 akṣ unclear] iti vedaḥ | mantrabrāhmaṇākhyam etāvatā purbhā(ma?)rthopasiddheḥ | sa punas triprakāraḥ | ṛgayajuḥsāmalakṣaṇā | kiṃ nātharvavedaḥ | om ity ucyate | teṣu kriyāsamāptes tathā ca brāhmaṇam "bhūr ity ṛgvedād bhuva iti yajurvedāt svar iti sāmavedāt tathā tad ṛgvedenaiva hotraṃ kurvate yajurvedenādhvaryavaṃ sāmavedenodgīthaṃ yady evaṃ tayaiva vidyāyai śukraṃ tena brāhmaṇatvam" iti ca | tathā "iyam aparā trayī vai hy arcaḥ sāmāni yajūṃṣi ca" iti | trivedasaṃyogāc ca brahmatvaśrutes tathā "yad ṛcā hotraṃ kriyate yajuṣādhvaryavaṃ sāmnā vodgītham" | atha kena brahmatvam iti praśne "trayyā vidyayā" iti prativacanopapatter brahmatve 'py atharvavede vyāpārān na (?) tasya grahaṇam | vedaśabdo neti | tathā ca sūtrakāraḥ "trivedasaṃyogāc ca" iti brahmatvaprakaraṇe ti(tri) śruteḥ (?) pratiṣedhaṃ karoti | tasmād ātharvaṇena saṃpraśyāt iti | kiṃ tad vaidikaṃ karmeti prakaraṇād jñāyate kathaṃ tarhy ātharvaṇe vedaśabdaḥ itihāsapurāṇāyurvedavad iti ṛṅmātrasamūhatvāc ca (?) tasya svastyayanādiprayogasya vedāviśeṣāt vedaśabda ekavacanam | samudāyaikatvāpekṣaṃ vacanavat (?) trayī cet kriyāprayojaneti kṛtvā tadekatvāpekṣam ekavacanam | tadupadeśaśāstrabahutve 'pīty evaṃ ca naikavedāpekṣaṃ bhūtapūrvatvāj (?) jātyapekṣatvāt [38] tatraikavacanam ubhayabhāvād iti vedavad arthaṃ ca sākāṅkṣam | yataḥ samāsapadena nirākāṅkṣīkariṣyati | kathaṃ vedaḥ kiṃ dharmamūlam iti vakṣyamānenāsya sambandha evaṃ darśayiṣyati | kiṃ tu vedapadaviśeṣaṇārtham idaṃ tāvad ārabhyate | akhilaḥ kṛtsnaḥ dharmamūlaṃ codyate | nanu ca vidhimātravākyasya phaladharmakriyopadeśatvād yuktaṃ ca dharmamūlatvān nārthavādanāmadheyamantrāṇām anupadeśarūpatvād ity asya parihāro yady apy avidhāyakārthavādās tathā vidhivihitam arthaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ kecit vadanti | kecit vihitānuṣṭhānābhimukhyakaraṇāyārthaṃ vadantīty arthavādās tad evaṃ vidhivākyaśeṣatādarthavādanāṃ (?) vidhiśabdenaikavākyatve sati yuktaṃ dharmamūlatvaṃ ca nāmadheyam | "śaradi vājapeyena yajet" iyaṃ(idaṃ?) nāmadheyāśrayaṃ śaratkālaguṇavidhānaṃ tathā "annādyakāmo vājapeyena yajeta" ayam api kriyāphalopadeśo nāmadheyāśraya eva | tathā ca viśeṣatvād asyā(anyā)? pranāḍikayā dharmamūlatvam | mantrāṇāṃ tu karmāṅgatvād ayatnasiddhaṃ dharmamūlatvam | evaṃ ca codyānavakāśaḥ |
"dharmamūlam" ity etad adhunocyate | atra kecit tāvat "dhatte puruṣaprītim ity ato dhā(dā)nārthī" iti dānād dharmaśabdam āhuḥ | mīmāṃsādhyāyā(ye?) bhavadāsastu (?) dhāraṇakriyātaḥ kathayati | trayo lokā anena dhriyanta iti dharmatvābhisambandhād dharma iti vaiśeṣikāḥ | jātinimitta etat | kiṃ na etena śabdavyutpattikāraṇānveṣaṇena (?) bhavatu caivaṃ dharmo mā vā bhūt | sarvathā tāvad evaṃ kartur abhyudayaniśreyasahetuṃ kriyāvyatiriktam ā phalapradānavasthāyinaṃ (?) padārtham ācaṣṭa iti mūlaśabdaḥ kāraṇaparyāyaḥ dharmasya mulam uktam | [pūrva]padārthapradhānaḥ ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣaḥ | evaṃ cāsya vedaśabdena vividhapadārthaviśeṣaṇena (?) sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ dharmamūlaṃ kintad vedaḥ | yathā rājapuruṣodaśarīra (?) iti | yady api ca sada(?)śabdopa[ja]nitakriyā dharmamūlaṃ na sākṣād vedas tathādhikāraṇe kāranasadṛpa(rūpa?)pāreṇai(?)tad uktam "vedo 'khilo dharmamūlam" iti | atha dā(?)[agni]hotradivyapanathādharmo dharmārthatvāt tasyāśva (??) vedamūlatvāt | upapannam etad vedaśabdapramāṇā cākriyāsāpekṣaśabdenocyate (?) sāhacaryāt | tasya dharmamūlatvād upapanneyaṃ guṇavṛttir na ca gauṇo daṇḍapādito (daṇḍāpūpikayā?) vyavahāraḥ | atha cobhayaṃ śāstraṃ codyake 'gnihotrādikriyādhyayanaṃ ca | tasmāt "svādhyāyo 'dhyetavyaḥ" ity anena ity anena etasyāṃ ca kalpanāyā dupavṛttyeva (?) "vedo 'khilo dharmamūlam" iti vedasyākhilasya dharmamūltvenopadiṣṭe 'nyasya prāpnoti tadupadeśābhāvād iṣyate cānyasyāpi dharmamūlatvam |
tac cāntareṇa śāstrārambhayatnaṃ na labhyata ity atas tadartham idam ārabhyate | "smṛtiśīle pa(pi) tadvidām" | dharmamūlam iti vartate | smṛtir nāma śiṣṭapuruṣadharmaḥ | pūrvaśiṣṭopadeśapratyayabāvanām (?) jape(upa)labdhārthaviṣayaḥ (?) bhāvanākhyo vidhipratiṣedhavākyasvarūpo 'ṣṭakādikarmācārānuṣṭhānanimittaṃ mānavādiśāstropanibandhanaṃ padārthasmaraṇaṃ smṛtibhāvanākārakam | kecit tu tām ahuḥ smaryate | ity evaṃ cānuṣṭhātavyaṃ padārthaḥ prāpnoti (??) | yad aniyamakarmānuṣṭhānaṃ [39] hi śiṣṭaiḥ smaryate | bhavatv evam, ko doṣaḥ | nanv idaṃ pramāṇaṃ tadanuṣṭhānaṃ prāpnoti atha smṛtipramāṇaṃ tadanuṣṭhānaṃ manye puruṣadharmas tarhi sa karmānuṣṭhāny āha (?) kratusmaraṇaṃ (?) smṛtinā smaryamāṇaṃ karma | tat pañca | yathāgnihotrādikarmānuṣṭhānaṃ vedapramāṇaṃ na vedaḥ | tat pañca(?)karmācārānuṣṭhānaṃ smṛtipradhānaṃ na smṛtir iti | tāṃ ca tair vidhavinya(?) vṛddhāḥ | kecinṛtsaṅgaśāpāpūrikādi(??) karmācārādipratipattikāraṇaṃ smṛtiṃ manyante | na cāpastambavad (?) eṣāṃ mukhaṃ na pāṭhād aprayogād anumīyanta iti | anye mantrārthatvādaliṅgaprabhavād āhuḥ | apare punar anādivāraṃ parva sampradāyāvicchedaṃ nityārthaṃ svatantrāyāḥ smṛter ācakṣate | tarkakhinno (?) 'pi pratipattyā yathā tathāpi bhavatv iyaṃ sarvathottarapadasyāvedavit saṃsvanya (syāvedavitsu | anye??) dharmamūlatvaṃ nānyad athaiṣā smṛtiśīle rāgadveṣaprabhyāna(hāṇa)m ucyate | śīlasamadhau pakṣīṇarāgadveṣaś ca samādhistho bhavati rūḍhito vā | vigatarāgadveṣaḥ puruṣaḥ śīlavān ity ucyate | dveṣarāgavān [ca] duḥśīlaḥ | ata ete smṛtiśīle dharmamūlam iti | caśabda uktasamuccaye | vedaś ca smṛtiśīle ca idānīṃ sarvayoḥ smṛtiśīle te dharmamūlaṃ bhavataḥ nāṇye |
kim etāvad eva dharmamūlam | nety ucyate: ācāraś caiva sādhūnām | ācāra kautukamaṅgalādiḥ sādhuvivedabhavanāṃ (?) vedavidām | nanu cācāraḥ (?) smṛtir eva | om ity ucyate | kathaṃ tarhi pṛthaṅ nirdeśas tasya | smṛtitaḥ brāhmaṇaparivrājakanyāyena yukto kṣatrabrāhmaṇatvāviśeṣāpi pṛthag upadeśaḥ | parivrājakasyādareṇa liṅgasambandhād adhikāreṇa vā kāraṇāntarato vā tad idaṃ samāsaṃ(sād?sena?) vaktavyaṃ tad dhy ācārasya smṛtitaḥ pṛthagupadeśakāraṇam upanibaddhā smṛtir anupanibandhaḥ (baddhaḥ) śiṣṭācāra ity etāvatā viśeṣeṇa pṛthagvacanam upanibandhānupanibandhena ca punar asmaraṇasya kasmāt kautukamaṅgalayos tāvad ānantyā aśeṣata upanibandho(ddho) 'śkyaḥ kartur viśeṣataḥ svarūpato deśajātikuladharmāṇām ānantyāt tathā tithyādīnām anuvṛttir yat kāraṇaṃ vacaneṣu hi prativacanāni | yathā prativilokayātrāyām (?) | na ca tāni granthenopadeṣṭuṃ śakyante, puruṣasya bhāvabhedāt | syāt tat na punas tatprativacanaprāyā lokayātrā yathā jñāpyā, evaṃ bhavatām eva tapogṛham iti caivamādir ato 'yaṃ na dharma iti | naivaṃ, paritoṣaṇaparatvād asya vacaso 'dṛṣṭaprayojanaphalābhāsovadhaṃ (?) kriyate | asmṛtir evāsau | yathā na dharmāpety (?) atha vā taṃ nāśrayai(?) tattvād yathā pradhānaṃ yathā sthūlaṃ copanibaddho viparyayād anupanibaddhatey atha vā yad anācāryamāṇaḥ(ṇaṃ) karmapratyayād ācāryamāṇaṃ cābhyudayāya tad avaśyānuṣṭheyeṣu smṛtitvam anācāryamāṇam atyayāya | ācāryamāṇatvābhyudayāyaiva | yathaiv(?)ordhvabāhvavasthādināvaśyānuṣṭheyaṃ sa ścāro 'nenaikaviśeṣaṇasmṛitataḥ pṛthag ucyate śīlatvāt karmarūpatvāt sarvārthatvāc ca |
ātmastutir bhāvaprasādaśraddhāparyāyaḥ | smṛtyaviruddhaṃ [40] vihitakriyātirekakaraṇam ātmatuṣṭir ity apare | evaṃ ca śīlātmatvāc śīlasmaraṇam (?) ātmatuṣṭim api (?) caiveme draṣṭavye | tathā ca vedasmṛtiprakaraṇam anugṛhītaṃ bhavati, smṛtiśīlācāratuṣṭīnāṃ ca dharmamūlatvam | vedavad uktaparihāraṃ draṣṭavyam (?) | smaraṇaprapañcaprayogavākyam itīdaṃ kartavyam idam akartavyam ity evaṃvidhaṃ smaraṇasya prayogārthatvaṃ darśayati, vijñānasvarūpatvāc ca |
śabdasyety aparaḥ pāṭho 'rthatva (vān?) "vedo 'khilo dharmamūlaṃ smṛtiśīle ca tadvidām" | arthas tu śīlam anuṣṭhānam aṣṭakādeḥ karmaṇas tatra tu yadanuṣṭhānasmaraṇaṃ vedavidāṃ tac ca dharmamūlaṃ vedaś ca | kiṃ cānyad ācāraś caiva sādhūnām | ācāro yamaniyamasamūhas tadanuṣṭhānasmaraṇaṃ dharmamūlaṃ pūrvoktaṃ cety atas tasmād (?) evācāropadeśasāmarthyāc chīlaśabdenāṣṭakādikarmamātraṃ pratīyate, na yamaniyamasamūhaḥ sarvo vā smṛtidharma ātmatuṣṭir eva cāstikyaṃ (?) vā gṛhecchā ca (?) sarvakarmānuṣṭhānasahakāribhāvaprasāda ātmastutis tadanuṣṭhānasmaraṇaṃ ca yad vedavidāṃ tac ca dharmamūlaṃ pūrvoktaṃ cety evaṃ smṛteḥ śīlaviśeṣaṇatvādirūpadharmasmaraṇaṃ (?) śīlācārātmatuṣṭyarthaṃ vidheyam iti |
kasmāt punar vedavitsambandhena smṛter dharmamūlatvam ucyate yena |
yaḥ kaścit kasyacid dharmo manunā parikīrtitaḥ |
sa sarvo 'bhihito vede sarvajñānamayo hi saḥ || 2.7 ||
yasmād ato 'syāḥ pauruṣeyād yuktaṃ vedavitsambandhena prāmāṇyam | evaṃ tarhi na vedasmṛtiḥ pramāṇāntaraṃ bhavatu, ko doṣaḥ | na, vedavitsambhandhe nāsyāḥ prāmāṇyaṃ vacanīyam, vedaprāmāṇyenaiva |
nanv asyāḥ prāmāṇyaṃ tadarthāntarābhāvāt siddham | atrottaram | sa sarvo 'bhihito vede (?) vedaliṅgabhāveneti brūmaḥ | kim evaṃ parihṛtaṃ codyaṃ bhavati | om ity ucyate | katham | yathā pratyakṣaśiṣṭā agnihotrādayaḥ padārthā vedena tathā smārthāḥ | kiṃ tarhi | liṅgānumitā evaṃ ca pramāṇāntaratvaṃ smṛteḥ, lokavat | yathā lokapratyakṣād dhūmād apratyakṣasyāgner adhigamaḥ pramāṇāntaram anumānam evaṃ pratyakṣād vedaliṅgād apratyakṣāṇām aṣṭakādikarmayamaniyamānām adhigame pramāṇaṃ smṛtiśāstram anumānapadārthāntaravat padārthāntaraṃ pratyakṣād vedaśāstrād bhavitum arhati | evaṃ ca yad avocāmaḥ "sa sarvo 'bhihito vede" (?) vedaliṅgato na pratyakṣataḥ agnihotrādikarmavad iti | tatsambandha udāharaṇaṃ kartavyākartavyasmaraṇayor vedaliṅgaprabhavayor ucyate  | "dhanvann iva prapā asi" ity etat prapākartavyasmaraṇasya | kathaṃ nu nāma ta (?) eva | janayitṛtvāt (?) saṃbhavatīty evaṃ sakṛdakartavyasya |  evaṃ sarvatra draṣṭavyam | vedaliṅgaprabhavatvaṃ (?) smṛter adarśanāt sarvatrāyuktam iti ced, darśane yatnaḥ kriyatām | dṛṣṭāc cādṛṣṭāc ca adṛṣṭam apy anumātuṃ śakyam | na tv adṛṣṭāc cādṛṣṭam apy anumātuṃ śakyam | na tv adṛṣṭād dṛṣṭahāniḥ | sarvavedaśāstrārthavidaḥ pratyakṣaṃ ca vedaliṅgaṃ sarvasmṛtidharmāṇāṃ trivedādhyetur api gamyam, kiṃ punar ekavedādhyetuḥ iti | tathā cāha: "sarvavedamayo hi saḥ" | vedaḥ kaḥ | manur vā sarvavedaśāstrārthajñānabhāvitasarvajñānamayaḥ [41] śaknoti sarvasmṛtidharmān vaktum | tatsamo nānyaḥ |
kathaṃ (?) ca kṛtvā |
sarvaṃ samavekṣyedaṃ nikhilaṃ (?) jñānacakṣuṣā |
śrutiprāmāṇyato vidvān svadharma niviśeta vai || 2.8 ||
sarvaṃ samavekṣyedaṃ nikhila(ṃ) ? karma smārtam | nikhilaśabdaḥ sarvaśabdasya mukhyārthavāditvaṃ darśayaty ubhayathā tatprayojanadarśanāt (?) | śloke (-darśanāḷ loke) tat samavekṣyam (kṣya?) kāla (?) jñānacakṣuṣā vedena | katham | yathāvocāma pūrvaśloke | evaṃ sati śrutiprāmāṇyato vidvān jānāno (?) vedaśāstravit sve sve dharme niviśeta na puruṣaprāmāṇyataḥ | na hi tasya vedasambandham uktvānyataḥ prāmāṇyaṃ bhavitum arhati | tathā coktam "vidvadbhiḥ sevitaḥ sadbhiḥ" (MDh 2.1) iti |
evaṃ ca smṛtiprāmāṇye tatstutyartham idam ucyate |
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ dharmam anutiṣṭhan hi mānavaḥ |
iha kīrtim avāpnoti pretya cānuttamāṃ gatim || 2.9 ||
graham atrādhikṛtā smṛtye (?) stutyartham | katham | yathaiva śrutyuditadharmam anutiṣṭhan kīrtyā yujyate evaṃ smṛtyuditam apīti bhūtārthavādena prakṛtārthastutiprakaraṇasāmarthāt draṣṭavyā | evaṃ ca na śruteḥ svārtha upadeśaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | smṛtistutyarthaḥ |
śrutis tu vedo vijñeyo dharmaśāstraṃ tu vai smṛtiḥ |
te sarvārtheṣv amīmāṃsye tābhyāṃ dharmo hi nirbabau || 2.10 ||
agniñchaddhā (?) śaṅkāparihārārtho 'yam ārabhyate stutyarthaṃ ceti vartate | yad yad dharmasādhanaṃ śiṣṭasamācaraṇe na nibaddhākṣarayā smṛtyā pratīyate (?), tat sarvam aviśeṣeṇa smṛtir, nānayor viśeṣo 'sti | nibaddhānibaddhayor ubhayor api hi prāmāṇye śiṣṭasmaraṇam evṃ cāvalmbya (?) vedānugrahthasaṃdarbhaś cāpi smaraṇārtha uktam (?) | te sarvārtheṣv evāmīmāṃsye na pratiṣeddhye | yathaiva vedasmṛtiśāstrasiddho medhyāmedhyādivibhāgo na vicāryate darśaṇāntarasthaiḥ(?), evaṃ vihitakarmaviṣayo 'pi paśvādivadho 'tra na vicāraṇīyaḥ | yena "tābhyāṃ dharmo hi nirbabhau" | katham | yathaiva pramāṇāntareṇāprāptau dharmo vedātmānaṃ pratilebhe 'gnihotrādi (?) evaṃ smṛtito 'py aṣṭakādir iti śrutivad vā smṛtir apy aṣṭakāpratiṣedhyety ayaṃ cānyo vākyārtho 'pi | saiṣobhayathā smṛtistutiprakaraṇād iyaṃ ca dṛṣṭā parā |
yo 'vamanyeta te tūbhe (?) hetuśāśtrāśrayād dvijaḥ |
sa sādhubhir bahiṣkāryo nāstiko vedanindakaḥ || 2.11 ||
[42]
aśabdatvād aprāmāṇyaśaṅkayā śrutyā samānīkaroti yā ca manye krayas (?) te pratiṣedhayan (?) ubhe dvijas san vedaśāstrānuśiṣṭaḥ hetuśāstrāśrayād iti vedasmṛtyāgamavirodhinas tarkaśāstrasyāvaṣṭabdhaha (-dhasya?) na tasya nyāyābhāsaśāstravyavahāriṇo vedasmṛtyāgamapratiṣedhaḥ | puruṣasyeyaṃ nindā | sa sādhubhir bahiṣkāryaḥ ātithyādikriyābhyaḥ | yena nāstiko 'sau vedanindakatvāt | trayīśāstrapramāṇaṃ hi dvijatvam | tatrasthasya na ca yuktā nindā | yata evaṃ trayyarthavirodhābhidhāne pravṛtto nindyate dvijaḥ | vedagrahaṇaṃ cātra smṛtyartham api draṣṭavyam uktanyāyasāmarthyāt | evaṃ ca kṛtvā yathā hetuśāstrāśrayād apratiṣedhyo vedo dharmamūlatvāt, evaṃ smṛtir apīti smṛtiparam etat prakaraṇasāmarthyāddiyuktam (-marthād ity uktam?) |
vedaḥ smṛtiḥ sadācāraḥ svasya ca priyam ātmanaḥ |
etac caturvidhaṃ prāhuḥ sākṣād dharmasya lakṣaṇam || 2.12 ||
ayam api prapañcārtham evāpareṇa prakāreṇa ślokaḥ | katham | yathaiva hi vedaḥ sākṣād apy avahito dharmamulam evaṃ codanavākyasvarūpā smṛtir api dharmasyānuṣṭhātavyasya sākṣān mūlaṃ na punar adhikaṃ (?) yā vedasambhavā (d) | atha vā (?) smṛtistutyupasaṃhārārthaḥ ślokaḥ | tadupasaṃhāraś ca pūrvaprakāravyavacchedena paribhāṣāntaropanyāsābhiprāyaḥ matāntarapradarśanārthaḥ | vedaṃ vākyaṃ kecit tacchīlasyāpāṭhenaiva tredhā smṛtiṃ manyante vedam (?) | ci(ta)tpāṭhena (?) saha caturdhā | yathoktam "smṛtiśīle ca tadvidām | ācāraś caiva sādhūnām" (2.6) iti | gautamas tu dvedhā: "tadvidāṃ ca smṛtiśīle" (GDh 1.2) iti | tad evaṃ smṛtibhedābhidhānam etad (?) gautamācāryasya manor api vivakṣitam | smṛtipadārthabhedasyaikaiva smṛtiḥ | tathā coktam anenānyatra (?) "dharmaśāstraṃ tu vai smṛtiḥ" (MDh 2.10) iti, "śrutismṛtyuditaṃ dharmam" (MDh 2.9) iti ca | ekam (evam?) eva smṛtim avocad yenātas tad vijñāpayati | nāyaṃ smṛtibhedo mukhyaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | kāraṇāntarato gauṇaḥ | idānīṃ vedasmṛtyor dharmamūlatvāt tadarthānuṣṭhātuḥ puruṣasyopadhasyānupadhasya vānuṣṭhānakriyā padārthasambandhaḥ svābhāvyād (?) āturacikitsād abhyudayotpattau prayuktāyām (?) idaṃ tatpratiṣedhādayaḥ pratyakṣa upalabhyate | tathā [ca?] vakṣyati: "svāni karmāṇi kurvāṇāḥ" (MDh 8.42] yogna(?)niyamārthaṃ (?) śāstram ārabhyate |
arthakāmeṣv asaktānāṃ dharmajñānaṃ vidhīyate |
dharmaṃ jijñāsamānānāṃ pramāṇaṃ paramaṃ śrutiḥ || 2.13 ||
puruṣasya śāstrānuṣṭhātur anuṣṭhānakriyāyāṃ svabhāvānuṣaktāv arthakramo dṛṣṭapuruṣārthaḥ pratyakṣat upalabhyate | tathā [ca?] vakṣyati: "svāni karmāṇi kurvāṇā dūre santo 'pi mānavāḥ" [MDh 8.42] iti | evaṃ ca vajasaneyi(ka)brāhmaṇaṃ lokavartī svādhyāyābhyāsasya dṛṣṭaṃ prayojanam uktvāha "lokaḥ pacyamānaś caturbhir dharmair brāhmaṇaṃ bhunakty arcayā dānena jñānena tathā cāvadhyatayā ca" iti | [43] tathā ca smṛtyantaram:
yathekṣuhetor iha secitaṃ payas tṛṇāni vallīr api ca prasiñcati |
tathā naro dharmapatheṣu saṃcaran yaśaś ca kāmāni vasūni cāśnute || iti |
bhūtārthānuvāda eva teṣām anuṣṭhātṛpuruṣāṇām arthakāmabhisaṃdhityāgena śāstrārtham anutiṣṭhatāṃ dharmajñānaṃ vidhīyate | dharmajñānaṃ śāstraṃ kathaṃ jñāyate | teneti jñānamūlaṃ deśavākyam ity arthaḥ | dharmasya jñānaṃ dharmajñānaṃ tadartho vā prayogo jñāyate 'sminn iti vā jānam ity adhikaraṇe kārake guṇavṛttyā dharmajñānasya śāstrasyārthakriyā dharmajñānaśabdenocyate | evaṃ ca tacchāstram atha vā dharmajñānaṃ vidhīyate upadiśyate | kaiḥ | śiṣṭaiḥ śāstreṇa vā | kiṃ kāraṇaṃ yena dharmajñānam icchatā kenopāyena dharmo bhavatīty evaṃ "pramāṇaṃ paramaṃ śrutiḥ" | kutaḥ | anumānāt | tac coktam | sā ca śrutir anupakakṣyamānāpi asminn arthe mṛgyā | śiṣṭebhyaḥ liṅgaprabhavayā smṛtyā, na cānumeyā (?) |
yasyehārthaṃ karma vidyā tapo vā nāmutrārthaṃ tasya maphalaṃ(?) vadanti |
yat tat phalaṃ divyam anantakalpaṃ tat tasya lobhād itareṣu naṣṭam |
tathā ca kadā (?) bhṛguvākyam |
sarvakarmānuṣṭhānaprayoganiyamaśāstrānuvādād idaṃ bhavati |
dṛṣṭahānād dṛṣṭaprayojanānāṃ dṛṣṭābhāve prayoge 'bhyudayāya ||
iti | evaṃ ca, yathaiva na sarvasmāt puruṣakarmaṇāṃ dharmāḥ | kiṃ tarhi | śāstropadiṣṭād eva | evaṃ na sarvasya śāstrānuṣṭhātur dharmaḥ tathā śāstraṃ nirupadhasya (?) | evaṃ ca kriyā padārthasambandhasvābhāvyaṃ dharmotpattau sarvāvasthasya (?) śāstrānuṣṭhātuḥ parityaktaṃ bhavati | anayā paribhāṣayā virodhād idaṃ vāparaṃ śakyasamuccayakriyānivṛttyarthaṃ paribhāṣāntaram ārabhate |
śrutidvaidhaṃ tu yatra syāt tatra dharmāv ubhau smṛtau |
ubhāv api tau dharmau samyag uktau manīṣibhiḥ || 2.14 ||
śrutigrahaṇam atrādhikṛtāyāḥ smṛteḥ (?) virodhe sati vikalpaprasiddhyarthā | na svārtham, aprakaraṇāt | atra rūpo (?) dāharaṇasyārthaṃ śloko bhavati |
udite 'nudite caive samayādhyuṣite tathā |
sarvathā vartate yajña itīyaṃ vaidikī śrutiḥ || 2.15 ||
ya evaṃ ca yathā śrauteṣu padartheṣv anyonyaviruddheṣu śakyasamuccayeṣv api satsv anyataraśāstrānuṣṭhānenāpi kṛtaḥ śāstrārthaḥ sampadyate | yathādhyayanam avirodhe (?) yaḥ kṛto bhavati | evaṃ sṃrāv api draṣṭavyam udāharaṇam | "nityam abhojyaṃ keśakīṭāvapannam" iti gautamaḥ | "mṛtprakṣepākhyasaṃskāreṇa bhojyam" iti manuḥ | evaṃ tulyabalānāṃ smṛtipramāṇānāṃ parasparavirodhe vikalpaḥ | anyatarānuṣṭhānaṃ veditavyam | yathā śraute juhotīty anumānam iti |
niṣekādiśmaśānānto mantrair yasyodito vidhiḥ |
[44]                tasya śāstre 'dhikāro 'smin jñeyo nānyasya kasyacit || 2.16 ||
dvijātīnāṃ smṛtyadhyayanādhikārārthaḥ ślokaḥ | kathaṃ niṣekaśmaśānalakṣaṇasaṃskārayor ādyantayor grahaṇe sati tanmadhye patitānāṃ sarvasaṃskārāṇāṃ grahaṇaṃ dvijopalakṣaṇārthaṃ vinaiva sāmarthāḥ (?) lakṣaṇaṃ ca dvijātīnāṃ smṛtyadhyayanādhikārthaṃ tathā caiva yasaitat saṃskārakarma puruṣasya tasya smṛtiśāstre 'dhikāre 'smin jñeyaḥ, na tu śūdrasyādhyayanam | na tasya dvijātisaṃskaro 'sti | tasmād asya smṛtyadhyayanādhikārapratiṣedhas tadupadeśasāmarthān na ca karmādhikārāt tacchāstrādhyayanādhikāro yuktaḥ strīvad aśvamedhādiśāstravad vikalpaḥ | atha vā śāstrasāmarthyād yathopadeśaṃ śāstrārthapratipattiḥ syān na tu nyāyān na tu ca "viduṣā brāhmaṇenedam adhyetavyaṃ prayatnataḥ" (MDh 1.103) (?) ity ayam ukta eva smṛtyadhayanādhikāraḥ dvijātīnam ity ubhayathaitad vākyaṃ smṛtiśāstrastutiparaṃ tatroktam ato nāyaṃ smṛtyadhikāras teṣām iti "adhīyīraṃs trayo varṇāḥ" (MDh 10.1) ity ayam api cānuvāda evopanayanādiprakāreṇopadiṣṭavedādhyayanavidher na tu smṛtyadhyayanādhikāraḥ | kecit tu bruvate "atra śāstre 'dhikāro 'smin nitya(?) śāsttraṃ vedo mukhyas tatprakaraṇasāmarthyapariśeṣai [1 akṣ unclear] smṛtiḥ" iti | tad ayuktam | upanayanādhikāre hi kṛtopanayanānām ādiṣṭavedavratānāṃ ca gurūpasadanānantaraṃ vedagrahaṇavidhīm ma(dharma)hatā (?) granthasaṃdarbheṇānukrāntas tathā cedaṃ tatprakaraṇa evābhihitam | "brāhmaṇaḥ praṇavaṃ kuryād ādāv ante ca sarvadā" (2.74) iti |
oṃkārapūrvikās tisro mahāvyāhṛtayo 'vyayāḥ |
tripadā caiva sāvitrī vijñeyā brahmaṇo mukham || [MDh 2.81]
iti caivamādi | tasmān nāyaṃ vedādhikāraḥ | kiṃ tarhi | smṛter eva | nanu ca śabdakriyā vā da(a)dhipūrvasyendro(sya kṛ)dhyā(dhā)tor yathoktaviśeṣaṇe yaḥ smṛtāv ayam adhyayanādhikāro virudhyata iti samaraṇopanibandhanārthe granthe eṣo 'shikāhaḥ syāt | yathā cāsmadādīnāṃ (?) dharmāṇām iti | aparas tv āha "tasya karmavivekārthaṃ śeṣāṇāṃ cānupūrvaśaḥ" (MDh 1.102) iti vacanād asyaiva smṛtyadhyanādhikāraḥ śūdrasyeti sa imaṃ śāstrārtham | yathābhihitam evaṃ bodhayitavyaṃ karmavivekārthatvaṃ ca śāstrādāv uktam (?) atra nādhyayanādhikāraḥ | idaṃ tarhi kathaṃ "svaṃ svaṃ caritraṃ śikṣeran pṛthivyāṃ sarvamānavāḥ" (MDh 2.20) iti sarvagrahaṇam atroktaparihāraḥ (?) | agrasthaḥ kaś cāyam upadeśena nādhyayanādhikāraḥ | tathā ca "dharmapravaktā nṛpater na tu śūdraḥ kathaṃcana" (MDh 8.20) ity etad api brāhmaṇābhāve kṣatriyavaiśyapratiprasavārthaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ | yato nāyaṃ pratiṣedhaprāpter liṅgam uktaṃ cātra "dharmopadeśaṃ darpeṇa viprāṇām asya kurvataḥ" (MDh 8.272) ity etad api na śāstraprāpter liṅgam | kiṃ tarhi | arthaprāpteḥ | prāptasya liṅgaṃ dyotakaṃ bhavati, nāprāptasya prāpakam | "na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyāt" (MDh 4.80) ity atra śloke sphuṭīkariṣyāmaḥ | tasmān nāsty atrānupanītasya smṛtyadhyayanādhikāraḥ śūdrasya | [45]
sarasvatīdṛṣadvatyor devanadyor yad antaram |
taṃ devanirmitaṃ deśaṃ brahmāvartaṃ pracakṣate || 2.17 ||
devasaṃparigrahād devanirmitatvaṃ ca deśastutyarthaṃ brahma cotpannaṃ (?) maharṣīṇām atra kila pratibhātam iti brahmāvarto deśaḥ arthasaṃjñayā stūyate |
yasmin deśe ya ācāraḥ pāraṃparyakramāgataḥ |
varṇānāṃ sāntarālānāṃ sa sadācāra ucyate || 2.18 ||
ayam api deśastutyarthaḥ ślokaḥ | evaṃ ca nānyatra deśe asadācāratoktā na bhavati (?) |
kurukṣetraṃ ca matyāś ca pāñcālāḥ śūrasenakāḥ |
eṣa brahmarṣideśo vai brahmāvartād anantaraḥ || 2.19 ||
brahmāvartād anantaro brahmarṣideśa itīyam api deśasaṃjñā deśastutyarthaiva | brahmāvartād anantaraḥ īṣadūnastutyarthaḥ | etac cānantaragrahaṇaṃ sarvasmin deśe 'nuvartate | pūrvasmāt pūrvasmād deśād anantara uttarottara īṣadūno bhavati | evaṃ ca deśakramābhidhānam anugṛhītaṃ bhavati |
etaddeśaprasūtasya sakāśād agrajanmanaḥ |
svaṃ svaṃ caritraṃ śikṣante (ṣeran) pṛthivyāṃ sarvamānavaḥ || 2.20 ||
ayam api brahmarṣideśārthavādaḥ | evaṃ ca nānyatrāgrajanmanaḥ sakāśāc caritraśikṣaṇapratiṣedhaḥ |
himavadvindhyayor madhyaṃ yat prāg vinaśanād api |
pratyag eva prayāgāc ca madhyadeśaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 2.21 ||
atra deśastutyarthavādenoktaḥ prakaraṇāt tu draṣṭavyaḥ | evam uttareṣv api ślokeṣv etad boddhavyam utkṛṣṭadeśopavarṇanaprakaraṇamadhyābhidhānāc cāsya madhyadeśaṃ draṣṭavyam anvartham |
ā samudrāt tu vai pūrvād ā samudrāt tu paścimāt |
tayor evāntaraṃ giryor (?) āryāvartaṃ pracakṣate (vidur budhāḥ) || 2.22 ||
āryā vartante 'sminn ity āryasaṃjñayaivaṃ deśastutiḥ |
kṛṣṇasāras tu carati mṛgo yatra svabhāvataḥ |
sa jñeyo yajñiyo deśo mlecchadeśas tv ataḥ paraḥ || 2.23 ||
mlecchadeśa ity anadhikṛtapuruṣadeśo na yajñānām adhikaraṇatvam arhatīty arthaḥ | evaṃ ca tatra nivāsapratiṣedho 'vyartham ukta eva draṣṭavyaḥ |
etān dvijātayo deśān saṃśrayeran prayatnataḥ |
[46]        śūdras tu yasmin kasmin vā nivased vṛttikarśitaḥ || 2.24 ||
deśavideśaviśeṣopavarṇanāt pūrvasmin pūrvasmin niyamena vaset | prayatnaviśeṣāt phalaviśeṣo darśito yathā syāt, aśiṣṭadeśatyāgaś ca | śūdrasyāpi dvijātiśuśrūṣor etad deśaṃ ca nivāsaniyamaḥ tatsāhacaryād arthasiddhaḥ, saty api dvijātigrahaṇe | ayaṃ cāsya tebhyo viśeṣaḥ | śūdro vṛttyasambhavakarśito yasmin kasmin vā nivasej jīvataś ca śilpair dvijātayaś ca kṣīṇavṛttayo 'pi nānyatra nivaseyur asatyām āpadi nivāsapratiṣedhāc ca | vṛttyarthaṃ teṣām anyadeśagamanaṃ kadācit tan na pratiṣidhyate | nivāsas tu nāsti |
eṣā dharmasya yo yoniḥ samāsena prakīrtitā |
sambhavaś cāsya sarvasya varṇadharmān nibodhata || 2.25 ||
eṣā varṇāśramavyavasthā dharmasyābhyudayahetor āphalapradātā(tra?)vasthāyino mukhyasya yoniḥ kāraṇa eva bhavatāṃ samāsenābhihitā mayā "vidvadbhiḥ sevitaḥ sadbhiḥ" (MDh 2.1) ity atra śloke tatprasaṅge | na ca śāstraparibhāṣā "kāmātmatā na praśastā" (MDh 2.2) ityevamādyā | yāvad ayaṃ ślokam "sambhavaś cāsya sarvasya" jagataḥ sthitipralayābhyāṃ saha tatsamānārthāni cānyāny api brāhmaṇastutiprakaraṇādīni prathame 'dhyāye śāstrārambhasāmarthyadarśanārthāny uktāni santi | punar ihānuktāni teṣām apy upasaṃhārārtham | upasaṃhāraś ca vakṣyamāṇaprakaraṇopanyāsārthaḥ | tasya cāsaṃmohaḥ prayojanaṃ śrotuḥ | sa ca puruṣārthaḥ yathaitatsamupasaṃhṛtya pratijñātārthābhidhitsayācārya idam āha: "varṇadharmān nibodhata" | kimartham āśramadharmā noktā yasmāt te 'pi varṇadharmā evaṃ ca tat sarvaṃ niyogaśiṣṭatvāt teṣām | evaṃ ca ye varṇadharmāḥ kecit sarvāvasthā ācamanādayaḥ avasthādharmāś cāśramadharmā avasthāntaradharmebhyo viśiṣṭāḥ | evaṃ ca sati yāvanto 'tra dharmā ucyante sarve te varṇadharmā yāvad varṇabhāvi (?) yena jāter anapāyād iti |
vaidikaiḥ karmabhiḥ puṇyair niṣekādir dvijanmanām |
kāryaḥ śarīrasaṃskāraḥ pāvanaḥ pretya ceha ca || 2.26 ||
etāvad upasampannaḥ śrutipūrvikām aṣṭakādiśrutiṃ manyante (?) | tatpakṣeṇedaṃ yuktam eva vaidikatvaṃ garbhādhānādīnāṃ saṃskārakarmaṇāṃ yeṣām api pramāṇāntaraṃ smṛtis teṣām api vaidikamantrasādhyatvān naiṣāṃ vaidikatvam upacaryate | vaidikakarmaśeṣatvād vā vaidikatvam | stutiparaṃ cedaṃ vaidikatvam | vaidikair iva vaidikaiḥ karmabhiḥ garbhādhānādibhiḥ | puṇyair ity arthavādaḥ | te taṃ (?) niṣekādibhāvidvijanmanāṃ kāryaḥ śarīrasaṃskāra iti pitur ayam upadeśaḥ | kasya punar hetoḥ | yenāsau (?) pāvanaḥ pretya [48] cotkarṣahetoḥ | darmasyāpratibandheneha ca kuṣṭhādiśarīropaghātahetuprabandhena | so 'yam ubhayārthavādaḥ | puṇyasambandhena pāvanatvena ca kāryaḥ (?) śarīrasaṃskāra iti ayaṃ vidhiḥ | sāmānyasarvasaṃskārakarmaṇāṃ smārtānām anuṣṭheyakarmaṇām ity ayaṃ cānyas taccheṣ eva |
gārbhair homair jātakarmacaulamauñjīnibandhanaiḥ |
baijikaṃ (?) gārbhikaṃ caino dvijānām apamṛjyate (?) || 2.27 ||
garbhasaṃskāraprayojanās tatrabhavā vā gārbhaiś taiḥ gārbhair homair iti | homagrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham | kasya | mantrakriyauṣadhaprayogāṇāṃ gṛhyoktānām | evaṃ ca kṛtvedam (maṃ) vādaṃ vākyam īdṛśaṃ bhavati | homamantrakriyauṣadhaprayogair homaiḥ | kasmāt | etad dhomagrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārtham evaṃ kalpata iti ced yena trayāṇāṃ garbhādhānapuṃsavanasīmantonnayanānāṃ dvāv ādyau saṃskārau mantrauṣadhaprayogamāse dvāv homakau pratigarbhaṃ ca bhavataḥ | tṛtīyas tu sahomakaḥ | sīmantonnayanaṃ prathamagarbhaviṣaya evātra saṃskāro na pratigarbhaṃ pūrvasaṃskāravad ato nidarśanārthaṃ homagrahaṇaṃ kalpitam | ādiśabdalopo vātra draṣṭavyaḥ | homādibhiḥ homair upalakṣaṇārthaṃ ca homagrahaṇam | sakarmakasya garbhasaṃskārakarmaṇaḥ jātakarmacaulamauñjīnibandhanās tu saṃskārā abhihitā evaṃ sākṣād upadiśyante samāsataddhitābhyām | atha vā homagrahaṇaṃ pratipadaḥ sthāne (?) 'pi draṣṭavyam, na pūrvaviṣayam eva | ataś ca gārbhair homair jātakarmahomaiś caulaiḥ homair mauñjīnibandhanaiś ca homais teṣāṃ mauñjī | (?) kāmātur (?) asya puruṣacintanena śoṇitaṃ duṣyati | tathā ca vakṣyati "yan me mātā pralulubhe" (MDh 9.20) iti | pituś ca pratiṣiddhamaithunasaṃkalpābhyāsau śuklam evaṃ tannimittaṃ garbhasya yad enas tad baijikam | atha vā vinaivāśubhacintanena mātāpitror lohitaretasī aśucinī svabhāvata eva | yatas tatsambandhanimittam eno garbhasya baijikaṃ gārbhikam apy (?) aśicisthānābhābhyāṃ tad (?) enas tadbījnām apamṛjyate garbhādhānādisaṃskāraiḥ | ity evaṃ ca kṛtvaiṣo 'rthavādo yathākathaṃcit prakṛtavidhistutyarthaḥ na kāraṇopadeśaḥ pramāṇaśāstravirodhād ity evaṃ cāparas taccheṣa evārthavādaḥ |
svādhyāyena vratair homais traividyenejyayā sutaiḥ |
mahāyajñaiś ca yajñaiś ca brāhmīyaṃ kriyate tanuḥ || 2.28 ||
svādhyāyeneti praṇavavyāhṛtisāvitryadhyaynenopanayanaśeṣeṇa | kasmāt | punar vedādhyayanena cety (?) anādiṣṭavedavratasya vedādhyayanānadhikārāt | tathā ca vakṣyati |
kṛtopanayanasyāsya vratādeśanam iṣyate |
brahmaṇo grahaṇaṃ caiva krameṇa vidhipūrvakam || (MDh 2.173)
vratair ity atra ṛgvedādigrahaṇaprayojanaiḥ prativedaniyatair atha vā sāvitrādibhir homair ity agnikāryaviṣayaiḥ samidādhānādi vidyeneti (?) trivedādhyayanena ca yathā vedavratam ijyā | ye guruśuśrūṣayā, ye 'pi tapaḥ caranti teṣām api guruśuśrūṣayaiva tapo (48) vijñeyam | snānaṃ cānupadiṣṭam api tatsahacaritasyādhyayanāder brahmacāridharmasyopadeśād artha(ṃ)gṛhītam | udvāhaś ca sutagrahaṇād vijñeyaḥ | sutair iti dharmaprajotpattyā | mahāyajñair pañcabhiḥ smārtair hutayajñādibhir yajñaiś ca jyotiṣṭomādibhiś śrautair brāhmīyaṃ kriyate tanuḥ | brahma prāptyarthādhānā(?) brāhmī seyaṃ smārtasaṃskāryavivekaśuddhiprakarṣakāṣṭhā tadanuṣṭātur ucyate sarvasmārtadharmastuyarthā | katham etad dhi smārtasaṃskārakarmaṇo māhātmyaṃ yenāyam īdṛśaḥ kāyo hy atyantāśuddhaḥ (?) san brahmaprāptyarhaḥ kriyate | evaṃ tarhi śrautayajñābhidhān ayuktam, asyāṃ śrauto jyotiṣṭomādibhir yajñair "brāhmīyaṃ kriyate tanuḥ" evaṃ smārtair api sarvasaṃskārakarmabhiḥ | tathā ca śrautasmārtakarmasamuccayasādhyaḥ paraḥ puruṣārtho brahmatvaprāptaye niyukto bhavati na śrutivāddānuṣṭhānamātrasādhyaḥ, evaṃ ca prakaraṇasāmarthyāt smṛtiśāstrastutir aparityaktā bhavati | aparaḥ punar āha: "anena ślokatrayeṇa garbhādiśuddhir ucyate | patitotpannasya saṃskāraprāptyarthaṃ tasya hi śuddhiar arthavatī, tenetarasya" | atrocyate | evaṃ hi sati patitotpādo garbhasaṃskārāṇāṃ prayojakaḥ syāt | tathā ca naimittikatvād anityatvaṃ teṣāṃ prāpnuyāt | prāyaścittārthatvaṃ ca | na caitad iṣṭam aprakaraṇatvān nityatvāc caiṣām | yac coktaṃ tasya hi śuddhistutir arthavatī netarasyeti | atroktam "kāryaḥ śarīrasaṃkāraḥ" (MDh 2.26) ity asya vihitārthānuṣṭānasya prarocanaiṣā stutir nānarthikā (?) | tathā sarvārthavādavihitānuṣṭhānasstutyarthaiva | yadi cāsau patanīyenāyogānaivaina svī (??) | idaṃ tarhi vaktavyam | bhavatā ślokatraye garbhādānādiśuddhir ucyata iti | tasmād yathokta eva ślokatrayārthaḥ |
prāṅnābhivardhanāt puṃso jātakarma vidhīyate |
mantravat prāśanaṃ cāsya hiraṇyamadhusarpiṣām || 2.29 ||
prāṅnābhivardhanād iti uktajātakarmakālopalakṣaṇam etat | puṃgrahaṇaṃ tu napuṃsakanivṛttyartham | strīṇām amantrakam avabhṛtaṃ (??) vakṣyati | atha vā puṃgrahaṇam avivakṣitam | jāter adhikārāt jvaritaṃ jvaram uktaṃ vā (?) | dinānte bhojayen naram iti | yad vā mantram arhatīti mantravat | kiṃ tat | prāśanam | kasya | kumārasya | keṣām | hiraṇyamadhusarpiṣām | ghṛtasyaikasyaivety eke | itaḥ prabhṛti (?) gṛhyoktānāṃ vidhīnāṃ sāmānyānukramaṇaṃ prāmāṇyārtham āmnātānāṃ (?) ca prāptyartham iṣyate 'bhyadhikavidhyupasaṃgrahaḥ śākhāntarāviruddhaḥ śrutivad atra |
nāmadheyaṃ daśamyāṃ tu dvādaśyāṃ vāsya kārayet |
puṇye ('hani) tithau muhūrte vā nakṣatre vā guṇānvite || 2.30 ||
[49]
svārthe dheyapratyayaḥ | evaṃ ca nāmaiva nāmadheyam | prakṛtasya kumārasya kārayet | pitur ayam upadeśaḥ | yo vā yatreṣṭe | kasmin kāle | daśamyām | janmādhikāra ucyate | ta(ta) ? eva prabhṛti daśamī draṣṭavyā | na candratithiḥ, sāmarthyāt | etena dvādaśī vyākhyātā | puṇyaśabda ubhayaviśeṣaṇaḥ | puṇyayos tithimuhūrtayoḥ samuccayaś cātra, na vikalpaḥ | nakṣatraguṇānvayastutacandratārābalaṃ ca gṛhyate | evaṃ ca sati daśamyāṃ tithau grahaviṣkambhavyatīpātavaidhṛtatāracandradoṣopahatāyāṃ dvādaśyāṃ ca doṣavarjitāyām asambhavanāmadheyaṃ tithyādiguṇāpekṣayottarasmin svakāle vaikalpikam, sāmarthyāt tad bhavet | punaḥ,
maṅgalyaṃ brāhmaṇasya syāt kṣatriyasya balānvitam |
vaiśyasya dhana(puṣṭi)saṃyuktaṃ śūdrasya tu jugupsitam || 2.31 ||
maṅgalaṃ dharmas tatprayojanaṃ maṅgalyaṃ nāma brāhmaṇasya syāt | rūpo(nāmo?)dāharaṇaṃ tu śatāyuḥ, nandano, dharmaḥ devo, yajñaḥ satyaḥ, śāntiḥ ityevamādi | kṣatriyasya balānvitam | śatruṃtapaḥ, dviṣantapaḥ, paraṃtapaḥ, bhīṣmo, yudhiṣṭhiro, vijayo, duryodhana ityevamādi | viśyasya dhanasaṃyuktam – aśvapatiḥ, dhanapatiḥ, gopatiḥ, paśupatiḥ ityevamādi | śūdrasya tu jugupsitam | karmakāraḥ, śabarako, lubdhako, dhīvarakaḥ, niṣādaḥ, kaivartikaḥ ityevamādīni | pratilomavarṇanāmāni | tac cāpy amaṅgalyaṃ nāma |
śarmavad brāhmaṇasya syād
śarmaśabdavad viṣṇuśarmā, yajñaśarmā, somaśarmety evamādi | atha vā śarmapadaṃ maṅgalyaṃ syād iti | kuta etat | ārambhasāmarthyāt | itarathā hi janārambhasamaṃ prasajyeta | "maṅgalyaṃ brāhmaṇasya syāt" (MDh 2.31) ity etenaiva siddhatvāt | tac cāmaṅgalyaṃ dviprakāram | yac ca maṅgalārthaṃ na bhavati, amaṅgalārthaṃ vāpi | tad yathā kapilaḥ, piṅgala ityevamādi | yat tac charmavat syād iti | etad ārambhasāmarthyād ity arthagrahaṇaṃ vaitad yathottaratra tat kathayati |
balānvitaṃ vā yathoktam,
rājño rakṣāsamanvitam |
vā | yathā pālako rakṣakaḥ, śarakṣas (tārakas) trāyako, nāthaka ityevamādi |
tathā dhanasaṃyuktaṃ vā yathoktam,
vaiśyasya puṣṭisaṃyuktam
vā | yathā svāmī, āḍhyaḥ(?), īśvaro, gomān ityevamādy eva |
jugupsitaṃ vā nāma,
śūdrasya
[50]
yathoktam
preṣyasaṃyutam ||
vā | yathā dāsako, modakaḥ (?), kiṃkaro vā |
atra brāhmaṇanāmnāṃ pūrvaśloke 'rthagrahaṇam uttaratra śabdagrahaṇaṃ śarmavat | brāhmaṇasya syād itīdaṃ yatas tatra pākṣikaḥ samuccayo 'sti tathā ca kṛtvopavarṇitam etat | katriyādīnāṃ pūrvoktaślokayor arthagrahaṇam eva | yenātas tatra samuccayo nāsti | athāparaṃ (?) paraṃtapaḥ, dviṣantapaḥ śatruṃtapa ity evamādiṣu nāmasu samuccaya iti | tathā ca gṛhyasmṛtiśāstravirodhaḥ syāt | tatra hy uktam: "pitā nāma kalpayati (?) dvyakṣaraṃ caturakṣaraṃ vā" (?) | na cedaṃ (?) tathā | tasmād ayam anarthaḥ | na ca samuccayaḥ sarvanāmasu sambhavati | rakṣārthaṃ vāpi nāmavatvāt (?) na hi yathā balārthavādināṃ bahutvam evam itaratra | tasmād yathokta evārthaḥ | vaiśaśūdranāmnā evaṃ nyāyo veditavyaḥ | ye tv āhuḥ: "śarmavad brāhmaṇasy nāma maṅgalyaṃ vā" iti, teṣāṃ vikalpārthāsaṃbhavād ayam avāras (?) tadasambhavaś ca sāmarthyād draṣṭavyaḥ | dharmaśabde hi vaikalpikenāpy ucyamāne (?) vyavahārābhāvaḥ syāt |
atha vā, anyaḥ ślokārthaḥ | śarmavad brāhmaṇasya syād ity etad yathoktaṃ rājño rakṣāsamanvitam iti | idaṃ brāhmaṇanāmavad vyākhyāyate | katham | rakṣārthaṃ varma | tadvato rakṣakasyaivaṃ (?) cārthagrahaṇe tacchabdagrahaṇaṃ vijñeyam | arthe kāryasyāsambhavād upacārata ekanāmatvād vā śabdārthayoḥ | evaṃ ca rakṣāśabdena varmaśabdagrahaṇād yat pūrvaśloke kṣatriyasya balānvitaṃ nāmoktaṃ tad idaṃ varmavad draṣṭavyam | anena bhīmanyāyāyena (?) bhīmavarmā bhīṣmavarmety evamādi | tathā vaiśyasya puṣṭisaṃyuktaṃ puṣṭir vṛddhis tadarthā guptiḥ puṣṭiśabdenocyate | guptibhāvayogāc ca | tadvato guptasya grahaṇaṃ vijñeyam | yathā pākayogāt pakvasya bhūtakālāpekṣayā | evaṃ ca pūrvaśloke yad vaiśyasya dhanasaṃyuktaṃ nāmoktam | iha guptaśabdavad draṣṭavyam | dhanaguptajña(1 akṣ lacuna)gupta ity evamādi | yathāvad brāhmanād vā dvijātisaṃniyogena śarmavarmaparigrahād dvijātināmasūktāḥ gṛhyasmṛtau | yathā kālasaṃniyoge na dattapade vādiśabdaparigrahaḥ nāmasv evam ihāpīti | tathā ca vakṣyati "yad veṣṭaṃ maṅgalaṃ kule" (MDh 2.34) iti | nanv evaṃ dattadiparigrahaṇād asyāh smṛter virodha iti | yady evam, kutaḥ tarhīyaṃ paribhāṣārthavatī "tulyabalavirodhe vikalpaḥ" iti | tasmād ubhayaṃ smṛtyarthas tayoś ca virodhād vikalpa ity evaṃ śūdrasya preṣyasaṃyuktaṃ vyākhyeyam iti |
puruṣāṇāṃ nāmoktvedam ucyate |
strīṇāṃ sukhodyam akrūraṃ vispaṣṭārthaṃ manoharam |
maṅgalyaṃ dīrghavarṇāntam āśīrvādābhidhānavat || 2.33 ||
sukhoccāraṇārhaṃ sukhodyam | yathā sunāthā, vasunāthā, vasudā, vasumatī, yaśodā, ityevamādyam sukhodyam | kṣmā, kṣoṇī, vṛṣākapāyī | akrūrārtham akrūram | yathodāhṛtaṃ krūratvam | sumālinī, [51] gandhamālinī, papabhayaṅkarī | vispaṣṭārtham avyākhyānagamyam | rākā, sinīvālī, kuhūḥ | manoharaṃ śrutisukham | cārumatī, candralekhā ityevamādyam | manoharaṃ tu śa[1 akṣ unclear]veti | maṅgalyaṃ maṅgalārthavāci | yathā viśavdinī, svastimatī, puṇyodayā | viparītaṃ śāstraprasiddhaṃ vakṣyati: "nakṣatravṛkṣanadīnāmnī" (MDh 3.9) iti | atha vā dāsī gaṇikā vā | dīrgho varṇo 'nto yasya nāmnas tad idaṃ dīrghavarṇāntam | uktam udāharaṇam | viparītaṃ tu medyaṃ yathācāra (yathā cāruḥ?) prakṛtiśāntir ityādyāḥ | śiṣṭo vādaḥ (?) | yena yasmin vābhidhāne tad idam āśīrvādābhidhāna tad yasmin [vāstvi??] nāmni vidyate tad idam āśīrvādābhidhānavat | udāharaṇaṃ dhanadā yaśodā dharmadā iti | viparītaṃ tu kṛṣṇā hrasvā dīrghety evam idaṃ sapta viśeṣaṇaṃ yathā sambhavati veśeṣaṇaṃ vā strīṇāṃ nāmoktam |
idānīṃ dārakasya sāmatthyapikṣayā [sāmarthyāpekṣayā?] mātāpitror vābhiprāyeṇa niṣkramaṇānnaprāśanyor aniyamena prāptayor niyamakāri śāstram ārabhyate |
māse caturthe kartavyaṃ śiśor niṣkramaṇam gṛhāt |
ṣaṣṭhe 'nnaprāśanaṃ māsi yad veṣṭaṃ maṅgalaṃ kule || 2.34 ||
niṣkrāmaṇānnaprāśanayoḥ kṛtsnasya vidher viśeṣaśāstre gṛhyasmṛtyā vādiṣṭatvād ihābhidhānaṃ sāmānyasmṛtitvād asyeṣṭaṃ maṅgalam ity etat sarvasmin saṃskāraprakaraṇe 'dhas cordhvaṃ ca śeṣabhūtaṃ draṣṭavyam | na kevalam ata eva hi nāmasu dattādayaḥ parigrāhyā (?) anyac ca kuladharmā hi śāstrāviruddhā siddhā bhavanti |
cūḍākarma dvijātīnāṃ sarveṣām eva dharmataḥ |
prathame 'bde tṛtīye vā kartavyaṃ śruticodanāt || 2.35 ||
asyādipūrvavad arthaprāptasya niyamārtham (?) āha | cūḍārthasaṃskāraś cūḍākarm ca (?) sadharmakaṃ gṛhyaśāstroktam | tad iha na vyākhyāyate granthagauravabhayāt | ekad (tad?) eva ca prayojanaṃ sarvasaṃskārāṇāṃ kṛtsnasya guṇavidher avyākhyāne | uktaṃ cātra sāmānyasmṛtitvād asyeti | mṛ(2 akṣ lacuna) deśabhedaś ca gotraviśeṣeṇa smaryate | dakṣiṇataś cūḍāvaśiṣṭānām, vāmato (?) bharadvājānām, ubhayato 'tra kāśyapānām, ūrdhvato (?) gālavānāṃ pañcacūḍā aṅgirasām | ṛddhā bhṛgavacca (vṛddhā bhṛgūnāṃ ca?) | tad dvijātigrahaṇaṃ ca śūdraparyudāsārtham | evaṃ ca tasya tad aniyatakālaṃ vijñeyam | "prathame 'bde tṛtīye vā" iti dārakasāmarthyāpekṣas tasya vikalpaḥ | nakṣatragrahasausthityāpekṣaḥ kuladharmāpekṣo vā | kartavyam iti vidhiḥ śruticodanāc chrutiliṅgād ity arthaḥ | tac ca liṅgam "kumārān viśikhā iva" (RV 6.75.17) ity anena ca jñāyate sarvasmṛtidharmā mantrabrāhmaṇaliṅgaprabhavā iti | [52]
garbhāṣṭame 'bde kurvīta brāhmaṇasyopanāyanam |
garbhād ekādaśe rājño garbhāt tu dvādaśe viśaḥ || 2.36 ||
kimartham idam | garbhasaṃvatsarāt prabhṛti dvijātīnām upanayanasaṃvatsarasaṃkhyā yathā syāj janmaprabhṛti lokaṃ (?) mā bhūd ity evamartham idam ārabhyate |
garbhasahacarito 'bdo garbhaśabdenocyate | aṣṭānāṃ pūraṇam aṣṭamaḥ | garbhaḥ aṣṭamo yasya so 'yaṃ garbhāṣṭamaḥ samāsārthaḥ | abdas tasmin kurvīta | kim | tadaupanāyanam | asya vyutpattiḥ — guror vrātānāṃ brāhmaṇo vā yamaniyamānāṃ devatānāṃ vā samīpaṃ yena nīyate saṃskāreṇa tad upanayanam | karaṇakārakam | upanayanam aupanāyanam | svārthe taddhitapratyayaś chandaḥ samādhānārthaḥ | gṛhye tu garbhāṣṭamatvaṃ vaikalpikam | tasmāt punar aṣṭama eva | yato liṅgam āha — "gāyatreṇa chandasā brāhmaṇam upanaye 'ṣṭākṣarā vai gāyatrī 'gnis taddevatyam" (??) ato 'traiva prathamakalpaḥ saṃskāraḥ aupanāyanam | anupanītasya brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ kurvan darśayati | nopanayanādisaṃskāranimittaṃ brāhmaṇyaṃ sambhavaty anavasthādoṣaprasaṅgāt kurvataḥ (?) kiṃ tarhy | tad vakṣyati | "sarvavarṇāsu tulyāsu patnīṣv akṣatayoniṣu" (MDh 10.5) ity evaṃ ca jātir adhikārakaraṇaṃ saṃskārāṇāṃ yujyate, na saṃskārebhyo brāhmaṇyam | evaṃ garbhād ekkādaśe rājñaḥ (?) | traiṣṭubhena chandasā | garbhāt tu dvādaśe viśaḥ | jāgatena chandasā | kiṃ tat prakṛtam | "upanayanaṃ sarveṣāṃ vai gāyatrī vikalpena" iti gṛhyakāraḥ |
nitya upanayanakāla uktaḥ | kāmya idānīm ucyate |
brahmavarcasakāmasya kāmyaṃ viprasya pañcame |
rājño balārthinaḥ ṣaṣṭhe vaiśyasyehārthino 'ṣṭame || 2.37 ||
garbhādisaṃkhyā varṣāṇām anuvartate | adhyayanavijñānācārakam anuṣṭhānasampadbhir yat tejo brāhmaṇasya tad brahmavarcasā (?) | tasmin kāmo yasya so 'yaṃ brahmavarcasakāmaḥ | balavarcasya (bālavarṣasya?) viśeṣavijñānābhāvāt pitur ayam upadeśaḥ | viśiṣṭakāle vasajjane (?) ca pradhānakarmābhyupayo(ye)na (yogena?) dṛṣṭaṃ bilam (balam?) anubadhyate (?) | brahmavarcasaṃ guṇasaṃbandhinimittam | tac ca śāstrasāmārthyāt pratīyate | "yavāgvā grāmakāmaḥ," "taṇḍulair balakāmaḥ," "gudghenendriyakāmaḥ," "mādirāc chabalaṃ (?) tu śastrāstraprayatnaprakarṣāgataṃ śarīraṃ kośadaṇḍo bāhyam amātyādiprakṛtisaṃbandhāt | tasmin kāmo yasyeti samānaṃ pūrveṇa" | vaiśyasehārthinaḥ īhāpalārthinaḥ iti yāvat | tac ca kṛṣyādisaṃpadupacayaḥ (?) tasmin kāma iti vyākhyānam | tac caitad upanayanaṃ kecid brāhmaṇāḥ saṃskāram icchanti | tatprāptyanantaraṃ praṇavavyāhṛtisāvitryādibrahmādhyayanāt prati brahmacaryavratānyatvāt vratavad upaśayanam api brahmasaṃskārāyaiva syāt | pare tu puruṣasaṃskārāyaiva syārtham (?) idam āhuḥ | vratādīnīti | nāmadheyādi | idam arthaṃ vijñādhyayate (yajñādhyayane?) tathā puruṣasaṃskāramadhye gautamaḥ pura[skaro]ty enam | pakṣadvaye nyāyenobhayaparaḥ | vayaṃ ta brūmaḥ | sarvathā [53] puruṣasaṃskārāyaivaitat | evaṃ ca saṃskṛtam anena brahma puruṣaṃ saṃskaroti | puruṣārthatvād adhyayanasyaivaṃ vyavadhāne 'pi (?) tadarthaṃ tad ucyate | tathā ca saṃskṛtaḥ tena puruṣo vedādhyayanasamartho bhavati | yathāgnisaṃyogena ghaṭa udāharaṇādibhir adhikṛte ubhayasaṃskārapakṣe 'pi na kaścid doṣaḥ | vyavadhānāvyavadhānābhyām ity uktam |
ā ṣoḍaśād brāhmaṇasya sāvitrī nātivartate |
ā dvāviṃśāt kṣatrabandhor ā caturviṃśater viśaḥ || 2.38 ||
kimartham idaṃ prāthamakalpakopanayanakālavyatirekeṇaiṣām etad anyatra na prāpnotītīṣyate | syād ity ārambhaḥ | kim ayam āṅ (?) abhividhāv atha maryādāyām | kaś cātra viśeṣaḥ | yady abhividhau saha garbhaṣoḍaśena | atha maryādāyām, tad varjayitvābhividhena tarhi gāyatrachando 'nugamād brāhmaṇasya tasyāś ca dvaiguṇyasampādanaṃ ṣoḍaśābde [1 akṣ unclear]naikatra sāvitrī nātivartate | sāvitryanuvacanasādhanam upanayanaṃ sāvitrīty ucyate | gaṇataḥ sā iyaṃ taṃ kālaṃ na jahāti | adhikārayogyām ācaṣṭe | evaṃ dvāviṃśateḥ kṣatrabandhor āgataṃ(?) na paṭhyate pādādhikyabhayāt | tadarthas tu pratīyate prakaraṇasāmarthyāt | ā caturviṃśater viśa (?) ity adhikṛ(?)syāpy āṅaḥ punarāmnānaṃ pādapūraṇārtham |
ata ūrdhvaṃ trayo 'py ete yathākālam asaṃskṛtāḥ |
sāvitrīpatitā vrātyā bhavanty āryavigarhitāḥ || 2.39 ||
ata ūrdhvam, ato 'smāt prāthamikakalpād ānukalpikāc ca kālād ūrdhvaṃ trayo 'py ete yathākālam asaṃkṛtāḥ santaḥ sāvitrīpatitāḥ sāvitryanupasādhanād upanayanāt patitās te saṃbandhā na bhāvapratyayā (?) nāmato bhavanti | saṃjñānirdeśo 'yam | vyavahārārthaśāstra evaṃ ca santo bhavanty ārtyavigarhitā yataś caitad evam ataḥ |
naitair apūtair vidhivad āpady api hi karhicit |
brāhmān yaunāṃś ca sambandhenācared brāhmaṇaiḥ saha || 2.40 ||
śrautena smārtena vā prāyaścittena vāpi brāhmaṇānāṃ ca saṃbandhenācared brāhmaṇaiḥ saha | samācaran (?) | brāhmaṇapadena (?) saṃbandhagrahaṇam | pratigraho jananādi(?)saṃbandhapradarśanārtham api syāt |
ukto brāhmaṇādīnām upanayanakālaḥ | sāmprataṃ saṃbandhanadravyākṛtiniyamavyākhyāpayiṣayedam (?) ārabyate |
kārṣṇarauravabāstāni carmāṇi brahmacāriṇaḥ |
vasīrann ānupūrvyeṇa śāṇakṣaumāvikāni ca || 2.41 ||
[44]
carmāṇy uttarīyāṇi brahmacāriṇām aviśeṣavacane 'pi gṛhyaśāstrāt pratīyate | evaṃ ca śāṇakṣaumāvikāny arthāt paridhānānupūrvagrahaṇaṃ cottarārthaṃ vijñeyam | nanu caitac carmasaṃkhyatvād arthasiddham, gṛhyasmṛtau "śāṇaṃ kṣaumaṃ vā vasanaṃ brāhmaṇasya" ity uktam evaṃ ca saṃkhyābhede sati sarvāṇi sarveṣāṃ mā bhūvan nityatas tadāśaṅkānivṛttyartham ānupūrvyagrahaṇam | katham iti |
mauñjī trivṛt samā ślakṣṇā kāryā viprasya mekhalā |
kṣatriyasya tu maurvī jyā vaiśyasya śaṇatāntavī || 2.42 ||
mauñjyā ity etad viśeṣaṇaṃ trivṛt triguṇā samā samaguṇā | ślakṣṇā parigharṣaṇatanutvaguṇā | kṣatriyamekhalāviśeṣaṇārthaṃ jyāgrahanam | evaṃ ca brāhmaṇamekhalāguṇatrivṛttvaṃ nāsty atra virodhād vaiśyam eva balāguṇānām avirodhāt saṃbhavas trivṛdādīnām (?) |
muñjābhāve tu kartavyā kuśāśmantakabalvajaiḥ |
trivṛtā granthinaikena tribhiḥ pañcabhir eva vā || 2.43 ||
muñjābhāvagrahaṇasāhacaryād itarayor api grāhakaṃ pradarśanārthaṃ vā, ādiśabdalopo vā | muñjādīnām abhāve "muñjābhāve tu kartavyā kuśāśmantakbalvajaiḥ" iti yathāsakhyenaiṣām abhāvavikalpaḥ | "trivṛtā granthinaikena" ity atra trivṛttvaṃ granthiviśeṣaṇam | kevalāyās tu pūrvoktaṃ "tribhiḥ pañcabhir eva vā" iti na granthīnāṃ varṇamekhalāsu yathāsaṃkhyaṃ vacanasāmarthyāt | kiṃ tarhi | (?) sarvāsu vikalpena | syastra (?) caitad abhāvavaikalpikeṣu kevalasūcyamānaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ pūrvāsv api draṣṭavyam |
kārpāsm upavītaṃ syād viprasyordhvavṛtaṃ trivṛt |
śaṇasūtramayaṃ rājño vaiśyasyāvikasautrikam || 2.44 ||
upanī(vī?)tavad upan(vI?)ītam | tathā ca vakṣyati: "uddhṛte dakṣiṇe pāṇau" (MDh 2.63) iti | ūrdhvavṛtaṃ trivṛd dakṣiṇāvṛtāṃ(?) trivṛddhyāsyūtam (??) evaṃ brāhmaṇopavītaviśeṣaṇam itaravarṇopavītayor apy avirodhād draṣṭavyam |
brāhmaṇo bailvapālāśau kṣatriyo vāṭakhādirau |
pailavaudumbarau vaiśyo daṇḍān arhanti dharmataḥ || 2.45 ||
śāstrata ity arthaḥ | samudāye ca bahuvacanam | prativarṇaṃ tu daṇḍadvayaṃ taddhitopaviṣṭaṃ vikalpena | na cobhayapadaprādhānyadvandvasya dṛṣṭārthatvāc | daṇḍadhāraṇasya dravyaniyamavad daṇḍayor [55] yathāśrutaḥ (?) syāt prativarṇam iti | dvandvopadeśābhichando 'nuvidhānenātra | kathaṃ gamyate | gṛhyasmṛtisadā(?)cārābhyāṃ sāmarthāc ca | vikalpaḥ syāc chāstrāntarāt | tathā ca tena tamaḥ tadabhāva ṛtvigācāryāv iti draṣṭavyā niyamavac cādṛṣṭatvam | ekadaṇḍadhāraṇe 'py aviruddhatvam | upariṣṭāc ca daṇḍaguṇavidhiṣv ekavacananirdeśo(śe?) vikalpaḥ |
keśāntiko brāhmaṇasy daṇḍaḥ kāryaḥ pramāṇataḥ |
lalāṭasammito rājñaḥ syāt tu nāsāntiko viśaḥ || 2.46 ||
daṇḍapramāṇaniyamavidhir arthaprāptasya pramāṇasyāntānupadeśe (?) ca kṣatriyasya lalāṭāntasammito daṇḍaḥ sāmarthād draṣṭavyaḥ |
ṛjavas te tu sarve syur avraṇāḥ saumyadarśanāḥ |
anudvegakarā nṝṇāṃ satvaco 'nagnidūṣitāḥ || 2.47 ||
ṛjutvānugato 'pi sarvaśabdo 'vraṇādidaṇḍaguṇaviśeṣaṇārtho draṣṭavyaḥ sāmarthāt |
pratigṛhyepsitaṃ daṇḍam upathāya ca bhāskaram |
pradakṣiṇaṃ parītyāgniṃ cared bhaikṣaṃ yathāvidhi || 2.48 ||
idaṃ bhaikṣakaraṇam upanayanāṅgaṃ tatprakaraṇāt bhuktavataś copaneyatvān na vṛttyartham | ataḥ pakvam āmaṃ vādāyācāryāya nivedayet yathāvidhi | etad uktaṃ yatas tadvidhir ayma ucyate |
bhavatpūrvaṃ cared bhaikṣam upanīto dvijottamaḥ |
bhavanmadhyaṃ tu rājanyo vaiśyas tu bhavaduttaram || 2.49 ||
bhavatiśabdaḥ pūrvo yasya tad idaṃ bhavatpūrvaṃ bahuvrīhiḥ samāsārthaḥ | caraṇaṃ kim | tat bhikṣāprārthanā vājyam (vānnam?) arthagṛhītaṃ bhavati | kathaṃ lakṣaṇam | dehītīdam | bhavatiśabdapūrvasya (?) prayogakāle hrasvatvaṃ ca "ambārthanadyor hrasvaḥ" (Pāṇini 7.3.107) ity anena draṣṭavyam | prakṛtigrahaṇena ca pratyayagrahaṇam | apratyayakasya bhavacchabdasyoccāraṇam anuṣṭupchando'nuvidhānena | (**?) ca bhavatpūrvaṃ cared yāvat | kiṃcid bhaikṣaṃ bhikṣārthaṃ bhikṣaṇaṃ bhikṣāśabdenocyate | tādarthyāt bhikṣāprārthanāvākyaṃ ca bhaikṣam ucyate | yena tadbhikṣāyāṃ bhavaṃ guṇato bhikṣārthaṃ vā | tathā ca gṛhyaśāstre (?) "bhavatpūrvaṃ brāhmaṇo bhikṣeteti paṭhet" iti vāsya prayojanopadeśaḥ | "upanīto dvijottamaḥ" iti copanayanāṅgabhikṣācaraṇavidhir ucyamānaḥ sāmarthāt sārvatrikaḥ pratyetavyaḥ | etena kṣatriyavaiśyayor "bhikṣāṃ dehi" ity etasmin prārthanāvākye madhyāntayor bhavacchabdasya prayogo vyākhyātaḥ | saty api ca puṃsaḥ (?) prādhānye deśasāmarthyaṃ saṃnihitām asaṃnihitāṃ ca striyam evāmantrya bhavatiśabdena bhikṣeta na pumāṃsam | tathā ca tāsām evāyam upadeśaḥ || [56]
mātaraṃ vā svasāraṃ vā mātur vā bhaginīṃ nijām |
bhikṣeta bhikṣāṃ prathamaṃ yā cainaṃ nāvamānayet || 2.50 ||
pratyākhyānāvamānabhiyā ca brahmacāriṇō mātrādi sakāśāt prathamaṃ bhikṣanopadeśo 'bhyudayāya vedaliṅgāt tāṃ bhikṣetety āhuḥ tallaukyam (?) ity etasmād eva (evaṃ?) ca prathamabhikṣaṇe pratyākhyātavyaḥ pratyavāyo 'numīyate |
samāhṛtya tu tad bhakṣaṃ yāvadartham amāyayā |
nivedya gurave 'śnīyād ācamya prāṅmukhaḥ śuciḥ || 2.51 ||
yāvadartham iti tṛptiparimāṇena tato 'dhikaṃ māyayety upadhāparityāgena kadannaṃ dīyamānaṃ (?) sā (?) rogy aham (?) ity evamādinā vyājena pratyācakṣīta | uttarārthaṃ cedam | amāyayā nivedya gurave 'śnīyād ācamya prāṅmukhagrahaṇabhayād bhikṣāṃ pracchādyācāryanivedanaṃ tu (?) saṃskārārthaṃ na dānāya vṛttyarthatvāt tasya | tathā ca tad darśayati "nivedya gurave 'śnīyāt" ity ācamyeti vakṣyamāṇavidhyanuvādaḥ | "prāṅmukhatāniyamo dharmāyāparaḥ punarābhānujñāto (?) bhuñjīta" iti vacanāt | smṛtyantarato 'sti gurvicchayā bhikṣadānam ekadā gurugrahaṇapakṣe ca bhikṣitavyaṃ punas tena "yāvadartham" iti vacanāt | satyam evam etad yad anujñātabhojanaṃ dṛṣṭārthaṃ kalpyetātattve tad adṛṣṭārtham ato na kiṃcid ity etad kāmopasaṃhitaḥ prāṅmukhatāniyamo dharmāyoktaḥ | kāmyaṃ tat |
āyuṣyaṃ prāṅmukho bhuṅkte yaśasyaṃ dakṣināmukhaḥ |
śriyaṃ pratyaṅmukho bhuṅkte ṛtaṃ bhuṅkte udaṅmukhaḥ || 2.52 ||
sta(sna)panamātram anarthakam iti yat kāmyo 'yaṃ vidhir ity eke | anupadeśarūpais tu vidhānād diṅmātrastutir antarālapratiṣedhārtham ity apare | na ca "idaṃ bhaikṣāśanaṃ bhuṅkte" ity ākhyātāntareṇa nirdeśāt bhaikṣāśanāṅgatve hy āyuṣyaṃ prāṅmukho nāśnīyāt (?) iti brūyāt | na tv evam avocad yatas tad ajijñāpeyaṃ (?) na bhikṣāśanāṅgam idam | ataś ca sārvatriko 'yaṃ vidhir ity avagamyate | "nivedya gurave 'śnīyāt" (MDh 2.51) ity uktam | yatas tadvidhir upadiśyat |
upaspṛśya dvijo nityam annam adyāt samāhitaḥ |
bhuktvā copaspṛśet samyag adbhiḥ khāni ca saṃspṛśet || 2.53 ||
dvijagrahaṇaṃ prakṛtatvāt teṣām anuvādaḥ pādapūraṇārthaḥ | nityagrahaṇaṃ sarvāvasthānugatajātidharmaṃ darśayati | apare punar dvijagrahaṇasyedaṃ prayojanam āhur nityagrahaṇaṃ cādarārthaṃ kalpayanti | anye punar idam anarthakaṃ samāhitagrahaṇam, bhuñjānasya cendriyasaṃyamaṃ (?) darśayati | krodhādyasatpuruṣadharmatyāgaṃ (?) vā | tathā cāsyāśnataḥ prasādo 'yaṃ vidhīyate | [57]
pūjayed aśanaṃ nityam adyāc caitad akutsayan |
dṛṣṭvā hṛṣyet prasīdec ca pratinandec ca sarvadā (śaḥ) || 2.54 ||
annasya viśiṣṭadevatātvena saṃsarśane 'nnaṃ pūjāprāṇārthato na | tathā cāgamaḥ:
yo māṃ daśati prāṇāya bhuñjānaḥ śāstravid dvijaḥ |
dhyāyan mama tadarthatvaṃ saṃpūjayati māṃ sadā ||
ity annaṃ kilaivam āheti śrūyate | adyāc caitad akutsayan vāṅmanobhyāṃ kadannatvād iti doṣair bhuñjāno 'pi ca tā dṛṣṭvā manastuṣṭiṃ kurvīta etāha (ity āha?) | sthitihetutvaṃ buddhvānnasya kṣudduḥkhopaghātakatvaṃ (?) ca prasīdec ca | dṛṣṭveti vartate | anyanimitte 'pi ca krodhe tu tyāgena pratinandec ca stūyād avye(pye)na (?) nindyam aśanaṃ sabhūtagaṇā (?) nagakathā (?) | anena nityagrahāc caite 'pi varṇadharmā evāsyāḥ stutir anuṣṭhānāya |
pūjitaṃ hy aśanaṃ nityaṃ balam ūrjaṃ ca yacchati |
apūjitaṃ tu tad bhuktam ubhayaṃ nāśayed idam || 2.55 ||
pūjitaśabdaḥ sāhacaryād itareṣām apy akutsādīnāṃ pradarśanārtham | evaṃ cā(sā?)dhitānāṃ teṣāṃ phalena stutim aviśeṣeṇobhayathā vākyasvabhāvāt bhuñjānasyādhanā(?) brahmacāriṇo 'rthāt prāptaṃ pratiṣidyate )?) |
nocchiṣṭaṃ kasyacit dadyān nādyād etat tathāntarā |
na caivātyaśanaṃ kuryān na cocchiṣṭaḥ kvacid vrajet || 2.56 ||
nocchiṣṭaṃ kascyacid dadyād iti niṣiddher vidhir ārabhyamāṇo 'yaṃ viśeṣārthaḥ saṃpadyate | evaṃ ca śruter apratiṣedhārthaṃ yaṃ yatna(?) śūdrasyānucchiṣṭadānapratiṣedhaṃ vakṣyati: "nādyād etat tathāntarā" iti | arthākṛṣṭatvāt padārthasyātṛptaḥ san kadācid ayam antarāpi pravartate | yatas tatpratiṣedhāya tad ārabhyata iti ya evam āha tena sāyaṃ prātar aśananiyamād arthavacanīyam (?) etat | kas tarhy asya viṣayato na ca mānapyapetam (?) ucchiṣṭam abhojyam atha vāntarapratiṣedhena sāyam api bhaikṣāśanaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ prāpayati kim aprāptaṃ tac chāstrābhāvād aprāptam ity aśanasyādṛṣṭārthasyāpi sataḥ śāstrārthavatvād (?) yā ca dṛṣṭārthatā "na cocchiṣṭaḥ kvacid vrajet" iti varṇadharmapratiṣiddhasya nindārthavādo 'yaṃ vidveṣaṇārthaḥ |
anārogyam anāyuṣyam asvargyaṃ cātibhojanam |
apuṇyaṃ lokavidviṣṭaṃ tasmāt tat parivarjayet || 2.57 ||
bhūrnnā(?) pratiṣedhe saty aśanasya nindārthavādena gurutvaṃ pratīyate | anārogyakāraṇatvād atyaśanam [58] anāyuṣyam asvargyaṃ cāpuṇyatayā | katham | puṇyatāniyamātikramāc chaucādiṣv asāmarthāc cāsya niyamatyāgena | evaṃ cātyaśanasahopadiṣṭānām api pratiṣedhyānāṃ nindārthavādaḥ utprekṣyo 'tidarśanārthatvād asya | idānīṃ brahmacāriṇāṃ varṇāśrayas tīrthopadeśaḥ kriyate 'rthaprāpter nivṛttyarthe |
brāhmeṇa vipras tīrthena nityakālam upaspṛśet |
kāyatraidaśikābhyāṃ vā na pitryeṇa kadācana || 2.58 ||
brāhmeṇeti viśiṣṭadevatāsambandhena tīrthastutiḥ | paropanārthān iti (?) pragra(prakara)nāc ca tasyaivāyaṃ tīrthaniyamāt nityagrahaṇena tadvat kṣatriyavaiśyayor api kalpo 'numīyate | apare 'pi parkaraṇād dvijātipradarśanārthaṃ varṇayanti | nityagrahaṇaṃ ca narṇadharmārthaḥ kāryaḥ prājāpatyaḥ | traidaśikaṃ daivam | brahmābhāvavaikalpike caite vijñeye | pitryapratiṣedho nityānuvādas teṣāṃ lakṣaṇam |
aṅguṣṭhamūlasya tale brāhmaṃ tīrthaṃ pracakṣate |
kāyam aṅgulimūle 'gre daivaṃ pitryaṃ tayor adhaḥ || 2.59 ||
ācamanārthaṃ tīrthalakṣaṇopadeśo 'yam | evaṃ cānena nimitte sati |
trir ācamed apāṃ (apaḥ) pūrvaṃ trir (dviḥ) unmṛjyāt tato mukham |
khāni caiva spṛśed adbhir ātmānaṃ śira eva ca || 2.60 ||
ācamanavidhyarthaḥ ślokaḥ | ātmagrahaṇasiddhyā (?) hṛdayārthas tarhy ātmopacāro brāhmaṇavādeṣu tadguṇasiddhyarthaṃ tv idam upadiśyate |
anuṣṇābhir aphenābhir adbhis tīrthena dharmavit |
śaucepsuḥ sarvadācāmed ekānte prāgudaṅmukhaḥ || 2.61 ||
anuṣṇābhir uṣṇā nety anuṣṇā (?) akvathitāḥ ucyante nādityataptāḥ svabhāvoṣṇavātapodādiṣu (?) smṛtyantarataḥ | "tac ced amiśritābhir anuṣṇābhir (?) aphenābhiḥ" iti śaṅkhalikhitāv ūcatuḥ | upadeśasāmarthāc caivam upapadyate | anyathābhisaratkhīṣvayos (?) tapodādiṣu (?) cāyam asamarthaḥ | pratiṣedhaḥ syād arthi (?) tīrthenety etayor anuvādo gaṇārthaḥ | śaucepsur ātmasaṃskāre saṃskārepsur evaṃ ca nopasparśanād dharma ity uktaṃ bhavati | kiṃ tarhi | ātmasaṃskāramātram adṛṣṭaṃ yathānyeṣu | kriyāgaṇeṣv ājyādiṣu saṃskṛtātmanastuti (?) kriyāntarānuṣṭhānād dharmo 'vaśyabhāvī tatsāmarthyāt sarvadeti varṇadharma ācāmed ity ayaṃ pūrvavidhyanuvādo guṇavidhisaṃbandhe yathā "dadhnā juhoti, payasā juhoti" ity ekāntagrahaṇaṃ (?) śucideśasamparigrahārtham | prāgudaṅmukha iti vikalpārtham | trir ācamed ity apa [59] ity etasmād api śeṣagās tādācamanodakam (?) avikalpataḥ ? pramāṇādīnāṃ śuddhihetuṃ prāptam iṣyate | etat pramāṇaṃ bhedena teṣāṃ śuddhihetuḥ | tac cāntareṇa śuddhiśāstraṃ sidhyati | evam artham idam ārabhyate |
hṛdgābhiḥ śudhyate vipraḥ kaṇṭhagābhis tu bhūmipaḥ |
vaiśyo 'dbhiḥ prāśitābhis tu śūdraḥ spṛṣṭābhir antataḥ || 2.62 ||
hṛdayādīnām avadhitvena nirdeśaḥ ācamanodakasya parimāṇopalakṣaṇārthaḥ | evaṃ cāprāptāv udakasya doṣān ābadhyate (?) krameṇa sāmarthād draṣṭavyaḥ | śūdra(ḥ) spṛṣṭābhir antata iti na hastena | kena tarhi | jihvoṣṭhena | yasmāt tenaiva brāhmaṇādīnām ācamanodakasyāntarhṛdayādipraveśoktisāmarthyāt svahastenaivaṃ (?) ca spṛṣṭābhir antata iti | vacanāt tasyāpi prāśanam anumīyate | varṇavibhāgena vihitalakṣaṇānāṃ dvijātisāmānyo 'yaṃ saṃniveśair anukto yatas tadartham idam ārabhate (?) |
uddhṛte dakṣiṇe pāṇāv upavīty ucyate dvijaḥ |
savye tu prācīnāvītī nivītī kaṇṭhasajjane || 2.63 ||
nivītasya prāsaṃgiko 'nuvādaḥ itarasaṃniveśadvayopalakṣaṇārthaḥ | kathaṃ kaṇṭhāsaktasūtrasya madhyenoddhṛte dakṣiṇe pāṇau pāṇigrahaṇaṃ bāhūpalakṣaṇārtham upavītanāmā tatsaṃniveśo bhavati | so 'syāstīty upavītī | yadā sūtram upavītaśabdenocyate na saṃniveśaḥ "upavītaṃ kamaṇḍalum" (MDh 2.64) ity atra, tadānena nyāyenākāro matvarthīyo (?) 'tra matublopāt sāhacaryād vā | guṇas tācchābdantatā tavañco(?)pavītasaṃniveśavad upavītaṃ guṇataḥ sūtramātram ucyate | tad eva yajñaśeṣatve sati śabdāntaraṃ labhyate | yajñārtham upavītaṃ yajñopavītam | daive karmaṇy ete | tena ca prācīnāvītī vyākhyātaḥ | pitṛkarmaṇi nivītī cābhicāra iti mekhalādīnāṃ dhāraṇam upadiṣṭam | evaṃ ca vinaṣṭānām api te 'py aprāptaṃ(?) na ceṣyate yatas tatpratiṣedhāyedam ārabhyate, tatpratipa(t?)tiniyamārthaṃ ca |
mekhalām ajinaṃ daṇḍam upavītaṃ kamaṇḍalum |
apsu prāsya vinaṣṭāni gṛhṇītānyāni mantravat || 2.64 ||
mantravad grahaṇam eṣāṃ yathāsaṃbhavaṃ vakṣye yat tayor (?) eva grahaṇe mantrajātmānaḥ gṛhyenājinopavītayoḥ saty api vā viśeṣābhidhāne (?) mekhalādīnāṃ sānvaya eva vināśa etad upadiśyate | niranvayavināśe 'rthalopād ubhayo ye deśānarthakyaṃ mekhalājinadaṇḍopavītaiś ca śāstravat grahaṇaiḥ saha pratipattiniyamopadeśāt kamaṇḍalor grahaṇadhāraṇaviniyogād api śāstrata eva vijñeyā | nārthato viśeṣahetvabhāvāt tathā ca vyāsasmaraṇam | **** unclear section
upasparśāpaṭu  **** bhagaṃ labhate, nātra saṃśaya" iti | punaś ca na parīkṣāmahe | "sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum" (MDh 4.36) ity atra kṛtopanayanasya ca vedavratasyāsya snānottarakālam |
keśāntaḥ ṣoḍaśe varṣe brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate |
rājanyabandhor dvāviṃśe vaiśyasya dvyadhike tataḥ || 2.65 ||
keśānto nāma smārtas tasya saṃskāraś cūḍākaraṇādīṣad bhedo gṛhyakārair upadiṣṭaḥ sa cāsmin kāla ekavedabrahmacāriṇo vratasnātasya yujyate | yāvad grahaṇaṃ brahmacāriṇaś ca medhāvina evānyeṣāṃ na caitac chakyate vaktum itareṣām ayaṃ nāstīti nityatvāt saṃskārānto 'nena nyāye naiteṣām api brahmacāriṇāṃ keśāntaḥ samāvṛttyanantaram avaśyaṃ bhāvī prakalpyakālo vijñeya iti | yeṣāṃ tu nāsty evāyam, (?) teṣāṃ smṛtyantaravirodha ity ete catvāriṃśat saṃskārā iti | "prāṅ nābhivardhanāt puṃsaḥ" (MDh 2.29) ity etat puṃgrahaṇaṃ strīnapuṃsakanivṛttyartham uktam | evaṃ ca jātakarmādibhiḥ saṃskāraiḥ strīṇām anadhikāre prapta idaṃ tadadhikārārtham ārabhyate |}
amantrikā tu kāryeyaṃ strīṇām āvṛd aśeṣataḥ |
saṃskārārthaṃ śarīrasya yathākālaṃ yathākramam || 2.66 ||
ya evaṃ jātakarmādīnāṃ puruṣasya kālakramatvād eṣa eva strīṇām api draṣṭavyo mantrahīnaḥ | evaṃ caitad darśitaṃ bhavati | na samaste eva mantrakarmaṇī saṃskārasamarthe | kiṃ tarhi | upadeśasāmarthād āvṛnmātram api (?) strīviṣayam (?) amantrakam iti | napuṃsakasya tv anadhikāra eva | "amantrikā tu kāryeyaṃ strīṇām āvṛd aśetaṣataḥ" iti vacanāj jātakarmādivat ūnavayasām (?) amantrakaṃ prāptaṃ yatas tat pratiṣidhyate |
vaivāhiko vidhiḥ strīṇām aupanāyanikaḥ smṛtaḥ |
patisevā gurau vāso gṛhārtho 'gniparikriyā || 2.67 ||
vaivāhika eva vidhiḥ strīṇāṃ [10 akṣ lacuna] upanayanavad draṣṭavya evaṃ tāsām amantrakam upanayanaṃ na patisevā gṛhasevā sopāsyāḥ (?) | guruśrūṣāgnikāryābhyāṃ brahmacāridharmābhyāṃ tulyau vijñeyau | evaṃ tu samā iti | naitā asaṃstutā (?) na vā mantrakopanayanasambandhibhyaḥ (?) | tathā ca kṛte (3 akṣ lacuna) upanayanasaṃniyoge śiṣṭānāṃ vratādīnām api tadabhāvaḥ (?) siddho 'dhyāyanābhāvaś ca | vivāhasya copanayanasāmyābhidhāne (?) idaṃ paraṃ prayojanaṃ puruṣavat | [61] strīṇām api prāg vivāhāt kāmacāravādabhakṣatvam ūrdhvaṃ cāsmād yamaniyamādhikār ity upasaṃhārāya karmayogopanyāsārthaṃ ca | brahmacāriṇa idam adhunocyate |
eṣa prokto divjātīnām aupanāyaniko vidhiḥ |
utpattivyañjakaḥ puṇyaḥ karmayogaṃ nibodhata || 2.68 ||
aupanāyikagrahaṇaṃ nidarśanārtham | ādiśabdalopo vātra | aupanāyanikādir aupanāyanikaḥ | ādiśabdas tatprakāśārthaḥ | nigṛhītārthaṃ caitat padaṃ tasya viśeṣaṇam | utpattivyañjakaḥ mātṛjanaḥ sāmarthyādhāyakaḥ brahmajanmano vā | ataś ca puṇya ity ucyate | karmayogas tu guror brahmacāriṇa ubhayor vā nobodhateti "kṛtopanayanasyāsya vratādeśanam iṣyate" (MDh 2.173) iti vakṣyati |
evaṃ copanayanānantaram vratādeśe idam ārabhyate |
upanīya guruḥ śiṣyaṃ śikṣayec chaucam āditaḥ |
ācāram (?) agnikāryaṃ ca saṃdhyopāsanam eva ca || 2.69 ||
parijñātaśaucācārāgnikāryasaṃdhyopasano hi vedādhyayanasamarthaḥ | tadarthena vratadarśane 'dhikariṣyate, nopanayanānantaram evodita (?) ity ādarārthaṃ vacanaṃ yuktaṃ caitac chaucasya nityānugatatvād ādarānuvacanam | agnikāryādīni hi svakāla eva bhavanti (?) | na ca saṃbhavantyāṃ prakṛtārthastutāv apūrvasya (?) kalpanā nyāyyā | prakṛtārthadānaṃ hi tathā syād iti guror ayam upadeśaḥ | evaṃ ca guruṇā niyamena śikṣayitavyo (?) nānyeneti gṛhītaśaucārāgnikāryasaṃdhyopāsanaś ca brahmacārī yathāsamādiṣṭavedavratatvād adhikṛtavedādhyayanaḥ san,
adhyeṣyamāṇas tv ācānto yathāśāstram udaṅmukhaḥ |
brahmāñjalikṛto 'dhyāpyo laghuvāsā jitendriyaḥ || 2.70 ||
na anānādiṣṭavedavrataḥ | vakṣyati hi "kṛtopanayanasyāsya vratādeśanam iṣyate" (MDh 2.173) "ācānto yathāśāstram" ity ayam anuvādaḥ | pradhānārthatvāt | (?)gurūpadeśasyodaṅmukhatvabrahmāñjalikaraṇalaghuvāsatvajitendriyatvādīni svādhyāyāṅgāni | ayam aparaḥ ve(upa?)sadanabrahmagrahaṇavidhiḥ |
brahmārambhe 'vasāne ca pādau grāhyau guroḥ sadā |
saṃhatya hastāv adhyeyaṃ sa hi brahmāñjaliḥ smṛtaḥ || 2.71 ||
uttaraślokārthaḥ pūrvaślokābhihitasya brahmāñjaleḥ prarūpaṇārthaḥ | alaukikatvād asya yuktaṃ śāstralakṣaṇatvam | brahmārambhāvasānayoḥ pādopasaṃgrahaṇam uktam | guros tadviśeṣaṇārtho 'yaṃ vidhir ucyate |
vyatyastapāṇinā kāryam upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ sadā |
savyena savyaḥ spraṣṭavyo dakṣiṇena ca dakṣiṇaḥ || 2.72 ||
[62]
atra sadāgrahaṇaṃ varṇadharmārtham adhyayanābhimukhībhūtasya ca gurusaṃnidhau niyamaparaṃ gurutvābhyanjñā svādhyāyāṅgatayocyate |
adhyeṣamāṇaṃ tu guruṃ nityakālam atandritaḥ |
adhīṣva bho iti brūyāt
yadā tadanujñāto 'dhīyīhīti |
virāmo 'stv iti cāramet || 2.73 ||
yadā (?) brūyāt tadā viramed adhyayanāt | evaṃ ca tvaddhāvatāpi (?) vedādhyayanāt sa gurvanuyogo na kartavyaḥ (?) | adhyāpayeti virādo 'stv iti ca svayaṃ kṛtopasadanābhivādanaś cāyam anujñāto guruṇāviṣṭa oṃkārakaraṇāya |
brahmaṇaḥ praṇavaṃ kuryād ādāv ante ca sarvadā |
sravaty anoṃkṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ parastāc ca viśīryati || 2.74 ||
brahmacaryottarakālam apīti sarvadāgrahaṇam api vihitasyādhyāyadharmavidhānāya | oṃkārasya kālavidhānārtham idam ārabhyate |
prākkūlān paryupāsīnaḥ pavitraiś caiva pāvitaḥ |
prāṇāyāmais tribhiḥ pūtas tata oṃkāram arhati || 2.75 ||
kūlaśabdo grahaparyāyaḥ | prākkūlāni eṣāṃ ta ime prākkūlāḥ prāgagrāḥ | ke punas te | darbhāḥ | kṛta (kuta?) etat | smṛtyantarāt | atraiva hi prakaraṇe gautamaḥ: "prāṇopasparśanaṃ darbhaiḥ" (GDh 1.54) ity uktvā "prākkūleṣv āsanaṃ ca" (GDj 1.56) iti | prakaraṇād darbhā eva te bhavanti | evaṃ ca śiṣṭasamācāraḥ tasmāt teṣūpaviṣṭaḥ pavitraiś ca darbhair eva pāvitaḥ bahirhṛdayaspṛgbhiḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "prāṇopasparśanaṃ darbhaiḥ(?)" iti | abhyukṣaṇādīnāṃ cātra sāmarthāsaṃbhava eva prāṇāyāmais tribhiḥ pūtas trigrahaṇaṃ (?) dvyekayoḥ pratiṣedharthaṃ nordhvaṃ sāmarthāt | tato anantaraṃ vihitoccāraṇam oṃkāram arhati asmin kāle kartuṃ pāṭhād evaiṣāṃ dharmāṇām anuṣṭhānakramasiddhiḥ | "brahmaṇaḥ praṇavaṃ kuryāt" (MDh 2.74) ity asya vidher arthavādaḥ |
akāraṃ cāpy ukāraṃ ca makāraṃ ca prajāpatiḥ |
vedatrayān niraduhad bhūr bhuvaḥ svar itīti ca || 2.76 ||
na coṃkāravyāhṛtimātram eva | kiṃ cānyat |
tribhya eva tu vedebhyaḥ pādaṃ pādam adūduhat |
[63]        tad ity ṛco 'syāḥ sāvitryāḥ parameṣṭhī prajāpatiḥ || 2.77 ||
yasmād evaṃ veditavyaṃ niṣkarṣarūpam etat praṇavavyāhṛtisāvitryākhyaṃ trikaṃ hy asmāt brāhmaṇaḥ adhyayanādau praṇavavyāhṛtisāvitrīr uccārayed ante sarvasmin kāle | tathā ca praṇavagrahaṇaṃ nidarśanārtham | ādiśabdalopo vā | pūrvaśloka (?) evām ca sati |
etad akṣaram etāṃ ca japan vyāhṛtipūrvikām |
saṃdhyayor vedavid vipro vedapuṇyena yujyate || 2.78 ||
vakṣyamāṇasaṃdhyopāsanānuvādaḥ prākkṛtavidhistutyarthaḥ | tad uktam "vedavid vipro vedapuṇyena yujyate" iti | vedatrayaniṣkarṣam (?) | idam eva yo veda sa vedavid atrocyate, prakaraṇasāmarthyāt | "vedapuṇyena yujyate" iti vedatrayādhyayanena vedaṃ puṇyeneti etat(?) | evaṃ ca nāyaṃ saṃdhyopāsanavidhiḥ | tathā hi | prakṛtārthoparodhaḥ syāt | aprakaraṇaprakriyā ca na yuktā, prakaraṇoparodhāt (?) tatsadharmakaṃ tadupariṣṭād vakṣyate | punaś ca tatsmṛter evocyate |
sahasrakṛtvas tv abhyasya bahir etat trikaṃ dvijaḥ |
mahato 'py enaso māsāt tvacevāhir vimucyate || 2.79 ||
prakṛtād abhyāsakālāt bahiḥ prāyaścittānuvādo 'yam adhikṛtavidhistutyartha eva | tad uktatvāc ca na bahissaṃdhyopāsanastutir iyaṃ prāyaścittavidhir vā, aprakaraṇāt tasya | iyam aparā stutiḥ |
etayārcā visaṃyuktaḥ kāle ca kriyayā svayā |
viprakṣatriyaviḍyonir garhaṇām yāti sādhuṣu || 2.80 ||
sāvitryā visaṃyuktaḥ yo 'nupanītaḥ vratādeśanakriyayā ca svakāle 'vihitayā dvijātiḥ san garhaṇāṃ yāti sādhuṣu | atha vādhyayanāṅgatvāt sāvitryāḥ sapariṣkāro 'yam (?) tadvihīno 'dhyayanahīna ucyate, prakaraṇāt | anyat samānam | saiṣā trikastutir apareṇa prakāreṇeti | iyaṃ ca pūrvavat | anyā —
oṃkārapūrvikās tisro mahāvyāhṛtayo 'vyayāḥ |
tripādā caiva sāvitrī vijñeyā brahmaṇo mukham || 2.81 ||
prakārāntareṇāyam api pūrvaśeṣa eva na vā prakṛtasya saṃdhyopāsanasyeti brahmaṇo mukhaṃ vedādhigamadvāram | tathā ca vakṣyati:
etad vidanto vidvāmsas trayīniṣkarṣam anvaham |
kramaśaḥ pūrvam abhyasya paścād vedam adhīyate || (MDh 4.125)
atha vā mukhyaṃ prāṇānayanam (?) | mukham iva mukhaṃ luptopamā | ka upamārthaḥ | pavitratvam uttamatvaṃ pradhānatvaṃ vā | ayaṃ cānyo 'dhikṛta japānuṣṭhānasya mahimā (?) |
64]
yo 'dhīte 'hany ahany etāṃ trīṇi varṣāṇy atandritaḥ |
sa brahma param abhyeti vāyubhūtaḥ khamūrtimān || 2.82 ||
vāyubhūta iti bhūtārthavādena prakalpya vādau kāmyavidhyanuvādena vā prakṛtārthastutir etām iti savyāhṛtipraṇavāṃ sāvitrīm āha trīṇy api tāvad varṣāṇi prāg eva mahāntaṃ kālaṃ saiṣā japastutiphalavidhānena phalārthavādena "sa brahma param abhyeti" ity etena parasmin brahmaṇy apyaya(?) tadrūpatvād abhihitaṃ paramātmavat sarvaśaktisaṃpadā yathāpūrvābhiprāyaṃ (?) śarīropabhogo bhavati | yuktaś cāyaṃ praṇavādijapakarmaṇā priyaṃ (?) tava mahimā yasmād etat |
ekākṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma prāṇāyāmaḥ paraṃ tapaḥ |
sāvitryās tu paraṃ nāsti maunāt satyaṃ viśiṣyate || 2.83 ||
svaraḥ kevalo 'kṣaraṃ vyañjanavṛttaṃ ca | yasmād etat svaravyañjanasamudāya ekākṣaraṃ praṇavo 'dhikṛta ucyate | sa paraṃ brahma kartṛsamānajātīyād ṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇāt brahmaṇas tanniṣkarṣatvāt parabrahmatvāt siddhāntatvāc ca | tathā ca brahmanandivākyam: "om ity ātmeti pratiduṣkarmaṇāṃ tādarthyāt" iti | etat prāṇāyāmāt aparaṃ taṃ tapaś cāndrāyaṇādes tapasaḥ adhikṛtaprāṇāyāmatrayakaraṇastutir oṃkārastutir vā | raṃ dṛśonam (?) | ayam oṃkāraḥ yena prāṇāyamais tribhiḥ kartavya (?) evaṃ kartum arhatīty uktam | "sāvitryās tu paraṃ nāsti" japanīyāt brahmaṇo 'ghamarṣaṇādes tathā ca śrutiḥ: "yātayāmānyathā nicchandā(?)khyayā tayā mā gāyatrī vedatrayaniṣkarṣāt"(?) sarveṣāṃ dṛṣṭānto 'rthavādārtham idam ucyate | yathā "maunāt satyaṃ viśiṣyate" ity evam | praṇavādayo 'pi svajātīyebhyaḥ | kathaṃ punar maunāt satyaṃ viśiṣyate | maunavān uta pratyavāyo nāsti satyavacanād abhyudayānupapattiś ca | satye punaḥ sa cānuta (?) pratyavāyo nāśty abhyudayaś ca śāstracodanasāmarthād avaśyaṃbhāvī | yady evam ajyoyasetarastutiḥ (?) na tu (?) dṛṣṭavirodhād ayukteti | evaṃ tarhi maune sarvathā(?) saṃyamaḥ | satyatve tv ekadeśasaṃyamaḥ | ekadeśasaṃyamāc ca sarvasaṃyamena bhavitavyaṃ mahataḥ (?) śāstrayatnasyārthavattvāya | tathā ca dṛṣṭavirodhāsaṃbhave stutitvotpattir yuktasyād (?) iti | atha vaitasmād eva maunena satyavacanasya stutiḥ śāstrān maunasya satyavacanāt gauravamiryate (?), atheti tasyādhikṛtatvāt(?) | oṃkārasāvitrīgrahaṇe sati vyāhṛtigrahaṇaṃ vijñeyam, sāhacaryāt | yathānyatra "yo 'dhīte 'hany ahany etām" (MDh 2.82] iti sāvitrīgrahaṇe praṇavyāhṛtigrahaṇam | evam ihāpīti | atha vā naivedaṃ dṛṣṭāntavacanam | kiṃ tarhi | vyāhṛtistutiḥ praṇavādivat | kathaṃ kṛtvā | smṛtyantare satyavyāhṛtisahitā bhūrādyā vyāhṛtaya upadiṣṭaḥ | evaṃ sati tatsāhacaryād iti tāsām adhikṛtānāṃ tacchabdena grahaṇaṃ guṇato 'pi jñeyaṃ lokavat | tathā ca gautamīyam: "oṃpūrvā vyāhṛtayaḥ pañca satyāntāḥ" | tad ayuktaṃ [65] caitad eva kalpayityṃ praṇavādistutisāhacaryakaraṇasāmarthyāt | itaś ca yukto 'sya japakarmaṇaḥ iyān phalamahimā yena —
kṣaranti sarvā vaidikyo juhotijayatikriyāḥ |
akṣaraṃ tv akṣaraṃ jñeyaṃ brahma caiva prajāpatiḥ || 2.84 ||
durlabhatvāt tadadhikārasya tāḥ kṣarantīty ucyate | tathā cādhikāraśāstram "yajamānartvijāṃ bhavati | ṛtvigbhyorṣayo 'ntamānaḥ | sādhucaraṇo vāg yaṃ tu nāgo (?) nātiriktāṅgo nātiśveto yājyo(?) 'śvaratharmair (?) guṇais tribhir vyākhyātam (??) || vidvān yajate vidvān yajatīti palakṣayat(yāt?) kṣaranty etāḥ | atra ca pramāṇam upādānakṣaye tatsādhyasya prathamo lokavat | tathā ca brāhmaṇaṃ "sakṛd iṣṭir eṣa patatir, dviriṣṭir eṣa patati" iti | nanu caitanyam apy asti tatra "sa tasmāl lokāc cyavate yaḥ strīparijanaḥ" ity atra stutiparam idaṃ vijñeyaṃ karmaphalaprakaṣapiṣayā (?) | kathaṃ gamyate | sakṛd iṣṭer dviriṣṭeś ca pātavacanāt kṛtānāṃ ca kṣayaḥ, darśanāt | yataś ca darśayatisukṛtīni ha vā etāni jyotīṃṣi yās tārakās tāḥ puṇyakṣaye 'py āpādyanta iti na caivaṃ kevalakarmakṛta eva | kiṃ tarhi | jñānakarmasamuccayakāriṇo 'pi tulyapramāṇatvāt samānaṃ kṣaya ekākṣarād iti | trikam akṣaraṃ jñeyam ātmaikasādhatvāt | tasyāviduṣo 'pi cādhikārāñ japena brahmā caiva paramārthalakṣaṇaṃ prajāpatir iti tadabhidhāno yasmād ataś ca taṃ nityatvāt tajjapaḥ syāt (?) kṣaṇaṃ phalato vidhīyate | na cāyaṃ hetutve samānadoṣatvād (?) avacanīyam etat syāt | ataḥ śrutikarmanindayādhikṛtapadārthatrayasādhanajapakarma stūyate vākyasvābhāvyāt lokavat | anyathā hi yathāśrutapramāṇagrāhiṇāṃ śāstropapattivirodhau syātām | tau ca neṣṭau | tasmād ayam arthaḥ | yataś caitad evam ataḥ |
vidhiyajñāj japayajño viśiṣṭo daśabhir guṇaiḥ |
upāṃśuḥ syāc chataguṇaḥ sahasro mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ || 2.85 ||
vidhiyajñaḥ karmayajño 'dhikṛtaḥ pūraśloke jyotiṣṭomādis tasmād oṅkārādijapayajño 'dhiko daśabhir guṇaiḥ | yasmān na hy atra hiṃsāśastralakṣaṇā vidhiyajñavacanārthalakṣaṇā kācid asti nāpi sādhanavaiuṇyādipūrvaślokotko doṣo yatas tasmād ādhikyam | tasmād api śrāvyād upāṃśujapo 'dhikaḥ | sa ca kīdṛśaḥ | yamaparo na śṛṇoti | japaḥ sa eva san dharmaḥ pūrvaḥ syād ātmanigrahaṇam | asmād api mānasaḥ sahasraḥ manaḥsaṃyamātiśayāt tadguṇāntaryogeneṣyate (?) prayatnaviśeṣāc ca phalaviśeṣaḥ | śāstrasāmarthād anumeyo lokavedavat | evaṃ ca sati yathaiva sāmarthāt prakaraṇātikrameṇa sarva (4 akṣ lacuna) pakarma (2 akṣ lacuna) kalpyate evam upāṃśumānasaguṇāv api (?) sarvajapadharmau vijñeyau | tadvidhānaṃ tu [66] stutiprakaraṇopajijīviṣayā | nāsyārtham atha vā prakṛtārthastutidṛṣṭāntārthaviparyayato vāsyārtham evopāṃśumānasaguṇadvayavidhānaprakaraṇāc ca tatsaṃ (6 akṣ lacuna) japakarmana iyam atistutiḥ |
ye pākayajñāś catvāro vidhiyajñasamanvitāḥ |
sarve te japayajñasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || 2.86 ||
śrautasmārtakarmanindayā samasteyam api stutir vedādhyayanānuṣṭhānaprarocanārthatva atistutiś ca vākyasvābhāvyāt | (3 akṣ lacuna) arthatve (?) sati lokavedavad ihāviruddhe smṛtiśāstre | evaṃ ca satyam upāṃśumānasaguṇadvayasaṃbandhinā —
japyenaiva tu saṃsiddhyet brāhmaṇo nātra saṃśayaḥ |
kuryād anyan na vā kuryān maitro brāhmaṇa ucyate || 2.87 ||
japyenaiva tu saṃsidhyet na karmabhyaḥ kadācana | śrautasmārtakarmatyāgeneyaṃ japyastutiḥ prakaraṇād evaṃ ca satīcchayā kuryāt | anyat kuto japyāt | kiṃ tat karma na vā kuryāt | tat karma kasya hetoḥ | yena maitro 'sau brāhmaṇa ucyate śiṣṭair netaraḥ | maitryā bhavo maitraḥ kamaṇi vā ahiṃsāya (5 akṣ lacuna) rthaḥ | karmasthas tu hiṃsraḥ paropaghātasādhanatvād ubhayathā karmaṇā tasmāt etat karmadoṣavad evaṃ buddhvā na vā kuryān niratyayāt tu japyam eva kuryād vedābhyāsārthe 'yam api stutir vaidika (2 akṣ lacuna) karmānuṣṭhānatyāgena praṇavādistutiḥ | anyathā hi śāstrānupapattirvirodhau syātām ity uktanyāyam etat | praṇavādistutiprakaraṇe cāsmin pratiślokam adhikṛtajapastutir apīyaṃ sarvabrāhmaṇyaviṣayā sāmarthyād vijñeyā | na prakṛtividhistutimātraprayojanety ukto brahmacāriṇo vedādhyayanadharmāḥ vratadharmas tv ayaṃ tasya |
indriyāṇāṃ vicaratāṃ viṣayeṣv apahāriṣu |
saṃyame yatnam ātiṣṭhed vidvān yanteva vājinām || 2.88 ||
sarvendriyasāmyam abhidhānārthaḥ | tasyāyam arthavādaḥ ślokaḥ | indriyāṇāṃ cāyam aprakaraṇam api saṃkhyāsvarūpanirdeśas tajjayopāyapradarśanārthaḥ prakriyate |
ekādaśendriyāṇy āhur yāni pūrve maniṣiṇaḥ |
tāni samyak pravakṣyāmi yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ || 2.89 ||
uktasaṃkhyānāṃ cāyaṃ svarūpanirdarśaḥ |
śrotraṃ tvak cakṣuṣī jihvā nāsikā caiva pañcamī |
pāyūpasthaṃ hastapādaṃ vāk caiva daśamī smṛtā || 2.90 ||
[67]
kāryabhedāc ca punas teṣām ayaṃ vargadvayapravibhāgaḥ śiṣyate |
buddhīndriyāṇi pañcaiṣāṃ śrotrādīny anupūrvaśaḥ |
karmendriyāṇi paṇcaiṣāṃ pāyvādīni pracakṣate || 2.91 ||
sarvaviṣayaṃ caiṣām |
ekādaśaṃ mano jñeyaṃ svaguṇenobhayātmakam |
yasmin jite jitāv etau bhavataḥ pañcakau gaṇau || 2.92 ||
svagaṇo manasaḥ saṃkalpaḥ | kenobhayātmakam | śubhāśubhasaṃkalpasya indriyapravṛttihetoḥ kāraṇam, atha ca, indriyapravṛttihetoḥ saṃkalpadvayasya kāraṇam ubhayātmakam | evaṃ kṛtvedam ādarārtham ucyate | yasmin jite jitāv etau bhavataḥ pañcakau gaṇau | indriyāṇāṃ saṃyamaprayojanam uditam | adhunārthavādārtham ucyate | tad ubhayathā yasmāt,
indriyāṇāṃ tu prasaṅgena doṣam ṛcchaty asaṃśayam |
saṃniyamya tu tāny eva tataḥ siddhiṃ niyacchati || 2.93 ||
kasmāt | atimahāprayojanatvād ādaraṇīya indriyasaṃyamaḥ | kasmād | teṣāṃ kṣudha eva pratīkāreṇa viṣayopabhogākhyāne (-svāde?) nivṛttir neṣyate, karmānuṣṭhānasāmārthyārtham iti yasmāt |
na jātu kāmaḥ kāmānām upabhogena śāmyati |
haviṣā kṛṣṇavartmeva bhūya eva hi vardhate (?) || 2.94 ||
idaṃ cānyat prasaṃkhyānaṃ pūrvaviśeṣarū (1 akṣ lacuna) tam |
yaś caitān prāpnuyāt sarvān yaś cainān kevalāṃs tyajet |
prāpaṇāt sarvakāmānāṃ parityāgo viśiṣyate || 2.95 ||
tasmād evaṃ śamasukhadharmārthī prasaṃkhyānaparaḥ syāt | viṣayāsaṃnidhānenaiva saṅgalakṣaṇena saṃyamopadeśe 'rthasiddhiḥ | evaṃ ca kiṃ prasaṃkhyānena kadartheneti, yata idam ārabhyate |
na tathaitāṇi śakyante saṃniyantum asevayā |
viṣayeṣu praduṣṭāni yathā jñānena nityaśaḥ || 2.96 ||
yathopadeśaprasaṃkhyānenety adhikṛtaprasaṃkhyānastutiḥ | tathā cārthavat prasaṃkhyānam anayā stutyā gamyate evaṃ ca satīndriyasaṃyamopadeśasahakāreṇa prasaṃkhyānopadeśasyāpy arthavattvaṃ vijñeyam | [68] kasmāt punar atrevam atimahān prayatnaḥ kriyate, yasmān na kevalam indriyasaṃyamaḥ sākṣād adṛṣṭaprayojanaṃ śāstrasāmarthyād bhavati | kiṃ tarhi | vihitakarmānuṣṭhānasamādhipraṇāḍikayā ca puruṣārthakārī | tathā caitad darśayati |
vedās tyāgaś ca yajñāś ca niyamāś ca tapāṃsi ca |
na vipraduṣṭabhāvasya siddhiṃ gacchanti karhicit || 2.97 ||
asamādhigrastavyeti yatas tatsamādhyartham idam ādaraṇīyam iti ca | saṃyamaḥ svārthaṃ ceti (??) | indriyam adhunā prarūpayati |
śrutvā spṛṣṭvā ca dṛṣṭvā ca bhuktvā ghrātvā ca yo naraḥ |
na hṛṣyati glāyati vā sa vijñeyo jitendriyaḥ || 2.98 ||
tatsaṃyamapadārthaprarūpaṇaṃ sarvendriyaṃ chavārthaṃ (?) vijñeyam | na prasiddhendriyaviṣayamātram eveti | tathā ca kṛtvedam ucyate |
indriyāṇāṃ hi [tu] sarveṣāṃ yady ekaṃ kṣaratīndriyam |
tenāsya kṣarati prajñā dṛteḥ pādād ivodakam || 2.99 ||
sarvendriyasaṃyamastutyartho 'yam arthavādaḥ prakaraṇasāmarthād vijñeyaḥ | tatsaṃyamaś coktaprayojana upasaṃhriyate | evam indriyasaṃyamāt pratilabdhasamādhir brahmacārī |
vaśe kṛtvendriyagrāmaṃ saṃyamya ca manas tathā |
sarvān saṃsādhayed arthān akṣiṇvan yogatas tanum || 2.100 ||
indriyasaṃyamaprayojanopasaṃhāraḥ brahmacāridharmaprakaraṇe cāyam anyas taddharmaḥ |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhet sāvitrīm ārkadarśanāt |
paścimāṃ tu samāsīnaḥ samyag ṛkṣavibhāvanāt || 2.101 ||
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyām ity etad viśeṣaṇam | paścimāṃ tu japaṃs tiṣṭhed iti guṇapradhānāj japakarma tato phalaśrauṣaṃ (?) vedādhyayanadharmavidhyarthavādaḥ | śloke etad akṣametāś cety evaṃ tvat (?) sa tiṣṭhati guṇasaṃbandhaḥ sajjan japakarmātra (??) phalāyopadiśyate | na viparītam | tathā (?) tv īdṛgvākyarūpam atra bhavati | tiṣṭhañ japed iti kasmāt punar idam evaṃ noktam | gadyagranthe etad evoktaṃ codayitṛ | tad ayuktam | padyagranthaś cāyam | tasmāc chandobhaṅgabhayān naivaṃ paṭhitam etat | sāvitrīm iti karmanirdeśo 'yam anyanivṛttyarthaḥ | savitṛdevatyā api ca bahvyarūpaḥ (?) tatsadityṛco  tripādā caiva svāvitrī ityetat [69] pratipadaiveyaṃ prakṛtā sāvitrī gṛhyate | nānyatra tadgrahaṇe ca trigrahaṇam atra vijñeyam | tathā coktam "etad akṣaram etāṃ ca" (MDh 2.78) ity atra saṃdhyopāsanānuvādaśloke | adhunā tajjapasya kālavidhir ayam ucyate | arkadarśanād ity arkadarśanam avidhis tannivṛttyartham evaṃ ca sati prāg arkodayāt tatkarmopakramasiddhiḥ | sa cāyam avyakto 'pīha smṛtyantarād viśiṣṭo boddhavyaḥ "sajyotiṣyājyotiṣo darśanāt" iti | paścimām atyarthaṃ prāptānuvādo 'yam | sadā ity etad āstāṃ tāvat | idaṃ japann iti vartamāne "idam āsīta" ity ucyate | evaṃ ca japann āsīta | tatrāpi pūrvavat vākyaviparyayaprapañco boddhavyaḥ | āsīno japet | asyāpi cāyaṃ japakarmaṇaḥ kālaguṇavidhiḥ samyagṛkṣavidhānāt (vibhāvanāt?) samyakśabdaḥ sadāśabdapa(va)dāstāṃ tāvat (?) | sadāśabdaḥ pūrvaślokārdhe svīkṛto 'nuvartate | evaṃ cārkadarśanād ity ayaṃ tannivṛttyavadhis tadupakramakāsaś cāsmāt pūrvam ity arthasiddham etat | avyaktaś cāyaṃ smṛtyantarāt viśeṣaṇīya ity uktaḥ | sadāsandhyāśabdau (?) cātrobhayasaṃdhyāśeṣau draṣṭavyāv ity asya vidher anuṣṭhānapradarśanaṃ sāmānyaṃ (?) na phalavidhiḥ | phalavidhau hi prāyaścittam etat prasajyeta | tac cāyuktam aprakaraṇāt | prāyaścittaśāstrābhāvaprasaṅgād anityatāprasaṅgāc ca | etenottaraślokārdho vyākhyātaḥ | kiṃ cānyat |
[ pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhet naiśam eno vyapohati |
paścimāṃ tu samāsīnāṃ malaṃ hanti divākṛtam || 2.102 || ]
na tu tiṣṭhati tu yaḥ pūrvām nopāste yaś ca paścimām |
sa śūdravad bahiṣkāryaḥ sarvasmād dvijakarmaṇaḥ || 2.103 ||
ayam aparo 'nanuṣṭhānanindādvāreṇa saṃdhyopāsanapraśaṃsārtho 'rthavādaḥ | vihitārthānuṣṭhāne 'bhiniveśadārḍhyārtho lokavad veditavyaḥ | brahmacāriprakaraṇe yac cānyas taddharmaḥ |
apāṃ samīpe niyato naityakaṃ vidhim āsthitaḥ |
sāvitrīm apy adhīyīta gatvāraṇyaṃ samāhitaḥ || 2.104 ||
sāvitrīgrahaṇam itarapradarśanārtham ity uktam | brahmayajñavidhānaṃ caitat sadharmakaṃ vijñeyam, nanu (?) prakṛtasyāpi saṃdhyopāsanasya deśavidhiḥ | sāvitrīm apīti liṅgād vakṣyamāṇaprakaraṇasāmarthyāc ceti | naityakaś cāyaṃ vidhir brahmayajñākhyaḥ | kasmāt |
[70]
vedopakaraṇe caiva svādhyāye caiva naityake |
nānurodho 'sty anadhyāye homamantreṣu caiva hi || 2.105 ||
"vedaḥ kṛtsno 'dhigantavyaḥ" (MDh 2.165) ity atra vedādhyayanasahaśiṣṭatvād aṅgādhyayanasya vedānadhyāyādhyayanavarjanaṃ (?) teṣām api prāptaṃ yatas tat | pratividhya (?) cedaṃ prakaraṇe kalpādāv aṃśe brahmayajñaprasaṅgena vāyam anadhyāyādhyayanavarjanapratiṣedhas teṣāṃ nasyārthaḥ (?) | dṛṣṭāntārthavādo vāyaṃ brahmayajñārthaḥ | anumānataś ca svārtha iti | anye tu vedopakaraṇe caiva (?) doṣakarmācakṣate | "upākarmaṇi cotsarge trirātraṃ kṣapaṇaṃ smṛtam" (MDh 4.119) ity anadhyāyaṃ vakṣyati | tatrāpi tv adhīyītaiveti | tad ayuktam | uttareṇaiva tatsiddher iti | "svādhyāye caiva naityake" | nitya eva naityako brahmayajñaḥ | nānurodho 'sty anadhyāye | yathānyasminn adhyayane grahaṇadhāraṇārthe vedopakaraṇabrahmayajñābhyāṃ cānyatrāyaṃ vidhir aprāpto yato vidhīyate | "homamantreṣu caiva homamantragrahaṇaṃ ca nidarśanārthaṃ sarvaviśeṣāṇāṃ homabaliyajamānajapabrahmacaraṇamārjanādimantreṣv iti kimarthṃ punar idam uktam anadhyāyādhyayanapratiṣedho viśeṣataḥ sarvādhyayaneṣu prāpto yataḥ brahmayajñādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaviṣaye pratyuddhāraḥ kriyate |
naityake nāsty anadhāyo
yasmād
brahmasattraṃ hi tat smṛtam |
nāmadheyam idam arthavādīkaroti | satataṃ hi satram ucyate, tatra bhavaṃ vā | evaṃ ca nāsty atrānadhyāyaḥ | yataś ca
brahmāhutihutaṃ puṇyam
iti | yat parā stutir brahmaivāhutiḥ, āhutyarthatvāt tatsāhacaryād vā | tad evaṃ ca brahmāhutihutaṃ puṇyam adhītaṃ bhavati |
anadhyāyavaṣaṭkṛtam || 2.106 ||
anadhyāya eva vaṣaṭkārastutyarthā kalpanaiṣobhavaṃ (?) ca tena sambandham anadhyāyavaṣaṭkṛtam ṛtaścānadhyāye 'py adhyetavyam eva brahmāhuter vaṣaṭkāreṇa sambandhārthaḥ puṇyātiśayārthinā svasārdhai (?) tad evaṃ brahmayajñastutiparam evam |
yaḥ svādhyāyam adhīte 'bdam
api, tatra sāmarthyād uktalupto(?) draṣṭavyaḥ | abdam api prāg eva, na yāvajjīvam, jātidharmatvāt | [71] adhyayanaṃ vatsareṇāpi kṛtārtham (?) iti brahmayajñapraśaṃsāpi |
vidhinā
yathoktena
niyato dvijaḥ (?) |
dvijaśabdo 'nuvādaḥ, pādapūraṇārthaḥ |
tasya nityaṃ kṣaty eṣa
brahmayajñaḥ
payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu (?) || 2.107 ||
payaādīni | so 'yaṃ phalavidhiḥ phalārthavādo bādhitavidhistutyarthaḥ | apare tu "payo dharma nyante (?) śuddhisāmānyotmedorthaṃ (?) puṣṭihetusāmānyāt | kāmam (?) ghṛtaṃ snehasārūpyāt | madhya (madhu?) eva go'nnarasaitāt" iti | brahmacāriprakaraṇopadiṣṭās taddharmāḥ prakaraṇasāmarthād anyatra na prāpnuvantīṣyate ca (?) | anyatra teṣāṃ katicic (?) cāntareṇa śāstrārambhayatnaṃ na siddhyatīti yata evamartham idam ārabhyate |
agnīndhanaṃ bhaikṣacaryām adhaḥśayyāṃ guror hitam |
ā samāvartanāt kuryāt kṛtopanayano dvijaḥ || 2.108 ||
apañcāśadevaitavedvam (?) agnīndhanādīnāṃ yāvad brahmacaryabhāvitvapradhānavaśavartitvāt guṇabhāvataḥ (?) siddhe saty ārambhaḥ | tebhyo 'gnīndhanādibhyo 'nyeṣām upasparśanādīnāṃ taddharmāṇām anuvṛttyarthaḥ | kasya snānottarakālāvasathāsv (?) api guros tu yad dhitaṃ samitkuśakāṣṭhodakumbhāharaṇādi (?), tadarthāntadānasamāvartanāt (?) brahmacārī kuryād ity atra tu yāvajjīvam eveti vakṣyati caitam artham |
tayor nityaṃ priyaṃ kuryād ācāryasya ca sarvadā | (MDh 2.228)
iti | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: "uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi" iti | brahmacāry adhyayanaprasaṅgena cedam | anyeṣāṃ tu adhyāpanaṃ śiṣyate |
ācāryaputraḥ śuśrūṣur jñānado dhārmikaḥ śuciḥ |
śakto 'rthado 'rthī svaḥ sādhur adhyāpyā daśa dharmataḥ || 2.109 ||
viniyatam arthaśāstrā [1 akṣ unclear] | eteṣām arthadasya śuśrūṣoś cādhyāpanaṃ dharmāya kāraṇagrahaṇād ekavākyarūḍhatvāt dharmata iti nirdeśaḥ | "śakto 'rthadātāsvaḥ sādhur ity asmin [72] vāre(pāṭhe)na jñāyamāne svatantram ācāryagrahaṇaṃ pūrvasmin pā[1 akṣ unclear] ṭhe(?) punar ācāryagrahaṇaṃ putraviśeṣaṇārthaṃ vijñeyam | daśatvānugraho (?) vāyaṃ (?) caiṣām adhyāpanadharma ucyate |
nāpṛṣṭaḥ kasyacid brūyān na cānyāyena pṛcchataḥ |
jānann api hi medhāvī jaḍaval lokam ācaret || 2.110 ||
eteṣām api pṛṣṭena vaktavyaṃ nāpṛṣṭena (?) | pṛcchato 'pi nānyāyeneti niyamārthaḥ śāstrārambhaḥ | (adharmeṇa?) mām ayaṃ prāyaś cādharmeṇa pṛcchati | tayor anyataraḥ prītiṃ (?) vidveṣaṃ vā nigacchati | vihitānyāyādhyayanādhyāpanasya vyatikramanindāstutyarthā | evaṃ ca yathādhikāram adhikṛtastutiḥ |
dharmārthau yatra na syāyāṃ śuśrūṣā vāpi tadvidhā |
na tatra vidyā vaptavyā śubhaṃ bījam ivoṣare || 2.112 ||
na cāyaṃ bhṛtakādhyāpakaḥ iyad iyateti bhṛteḥ prasiddhaśloke (3.156?) | evaṃ ca saty utpādyādhyāpako bhṛtakādhyāpako vijñeyaḥ upadeśasāmarthyāt | anyathā hi śuśrūṣor arthadasya cādhyāpanenaivopadiśet vipratiṣedhabhayāt | tathā ca nāsyataḥ (?) gurvarthaṃ vakṣyati | vidhyarthaṃ cādhyāpanam |
ṣaṇṇāṃ tu karmaṇām asya trīṇi karmāṇi jīvikāḥ | (MDh 10.76)
iti | evaṃ cānenādhyāpakena nābhāvo (?)
vidyayaiva samaṃ kāmaṃ martavyaṃ brahmavādinā |
āpady api hi kaṣṭāyāṃ na tv enām iriṇe vapet || 2.133 ||
āpadi hi vidyām aprocyamṛtnanya(??) pratyavāyo bhavati | tata etad evaṃ yuktam abhidhānaṃ "vidyayaiva samaṃ kāmaṃ martavyam" iti | tathā loke "kāmaṃ viṣam api bhakṣaya mā cāsya gṛhe bhuṅkṣva" iti | tathā ca brāhmaṇam: "yo ha vai vidyām adhītyārthine na saṃpradadyāt | sa kāryabhā(hā)(?) syāt | adhyāpayen mahad etad yaṃsyaṃ(yaḥ sa??) vācovikāraṃ yat kavayo vadanti | asmin yoge sarvam idaṃ pratiṣṭhitam | ya evaṃ vidur amṛtās te bhavanti" iti | evaṃ ca śrutyarthānuvādinī smṛtir iyatnacchaiṣaiva (??) |
vidyā brāhmaṇam etyāha śevadhiṣṭe 'smi rakṣa mām |
asūyakāya māṃ mā dās tathā syām vīryavattamā || 2.114 ||
asūyakagrahaṇaṃ sarveṣām apātrīkaraṇe hetūnāṃ pradarśanārtham | kva tarhi deyeyam | [72]
yam eva tu śuciṃ vidyāt niyataṃ brahmacāriṇam |
