tasmai māṃ brūhi viprāya nidhipāyāpramādine || 2.115 ||
na kevalaṃ yathoktadoṣābhāve (?) deyeyam (?) | kiṃ tarhi | śaucādiṣu nayo yeṣāṃ vyapekṣitavyaḥ ity evamartham idam antareṇāpi copasadanapraśnaparigrahaṇaṃ guṇavān apy adhītavedaś ca saṃdigdham anāgacchan vā |
brahma yas tva ananujñātam adhīyānād avāpnuyāt |
sa brahmasteyasaṃyukto narakaṃ pratipadyate || 2.116 ||
tasmāt praśnapūrvakm eva saṃdehādimokṣapratyavāyāyācaraṇīyam adhyāpanavidhipraśaṃsārthaḥ ślokāḥ | prakaraṇam upasaṃhṛtyedam anyat taddharmaśāstri (?)
laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ vāpi tathādhyātmikam eva vā |
ādadīta yato jñānaṃ taṃ pūrvam abhivādayet || 2.117 ||
laukikaṃ pauruṣeyatvād annaṃ vijñānavaidikam | mantrabrāhmaṇam ādhyātmikamaunivadantas tattvādarārthaṃ (?) pṛthagvacanam | atha vā vedāvyatiriktāsvādhyātmavidyāsu bhavam ādhyatmikam āyurvedaśāstrātmakaṃ vā kiṃcid jñānaṃ taddhitārthaṃ yas tad ādadīta taṃ pūrvam abhivādayet | kutaḥ | svayonigurubhya evaṃ ca svayoniguruvidyā gurusaṃnidhāv etacchāstram arthavad vijñeyam | anyayothā(?)dhyayanapratiṣedhadvāreṇānyāyādhyayanaṃ vidhāya yat steneti (??) |
sāvitrīmātrasāro 'pi varo vipraḥ suyantritaḥ (?) |
nāyantritas trivedo 'pi sarvāśī sarvavikrayī || 2.118 ||
nyāyataḥ sāvitryadhyayanamātreṇāpi parituṣyet sāmarthye 'py adhyetuṃ brahmacārī na cānyo yena vedatrayam apy adhīyīta | evaṃ ca nyāyādhyayanapraśaṃsaiṣā, na tu sāvitryadhyayanamātropadeśaḥ, kṛtsnavedādhyayananirṇayasmaraṇāt | tathā ca vakṣyati "vedaḥ kṛtsno 'dhigantavyaḥ" (MDh 2.165) iti | sāvitrīgrahaṇaṃ cetareṣu pradarśanārtham uktam | nāyantā ca(?) kṛtvaitat trikaṃ brahmaṇo mukham abhihitam iti | evam adhyayanadharmam abhidhāya brahmacāridharmam anuśāsti (?) |
śayyāsane 'dhyācarite śreyasā na samāviśet (samāraret?) |
śayyāsanasthaś caivainaṃ pratyutthāyābhivādayet || 2.119 ||
vratāvirodhni śayyāsanādhiṣṭhite śreyasā guruṇā tatsamau vānyena | kutaḥ | ātmanaḥ avacanād anyasyeti vijñāyate yenopaviśet tatra svayaṃ ca śayyāsana evam āyāntaṃ sthitaṃ vā dṛṣṭaṃ(ṣṭvā?) [74] pratyutthāyābhivādayet | evaṃ vidhidvayam atra śreyonimittaṃ hi balābalaṃ ca vakṣyati | asya vidher arthavādaḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ prāṇā hy utkrāmanti yūnaḥ sthavira āyati |
pratyutthānābhivādābhyāṃ punas tān pratipadyate || 2.120 ||
śiṣṭasyākriyāyāṃ pratyavāyadarśanena nāntaravidhyanuṣṭhānastutir iyaṃ cāparā |
abhivādanaśīlasya nityaṃ vṛddhopasevinaḥ |
catvāri samyag (?) vardhante āyuḥ prajñā yaśo balam || 2.121 ||
śreyonimittasya sthāviryasya stutir atha vā satsv api śreyonimitteṣv asyātithavireṇābhivādya iti gamyate | "pratyutthāyābhivādayet" (MDh 2.119) ity uktam | yatas tadvidhir ayam evaṃ vaktavyatām āpannaḥ śiṣyate |
abhivādāt paraṃ vipro jyāyāṃsam abhivādayan(?) |
asau nāmāham asmīti svaṃ nāma parikīrtayet || 2.122 ||
jyāyāṃsam abhyivādayan tasmāt paraṃ svanāmoccārayet "asau nāmāham asmi" iti | sarveṣāṃ nāmnāṃ sthāne svarvanāma "asāv aham" iti | viśeṣanāma pradarśayati | abhivādanārtha nāmno 'yam upadeśavikīr(?) abhivādane | idānīṃ sarvābhivādyasaṃnidhau tasya svanāmoccāraṇam aveśeṣeṇa prāptaḥ tatkāraṇāntarato 'podyate (?) |
nāmadheyasya ye kecid abhivādaṃ na jānate |
tān prājño 'ham iti brūyāt striyaḥ sarvās tathaiva ca || 2.123 ||
nāmadheyasya parigrahaṃ kṛtvā ye kecid abhivādyā abhivādayantīty arthaḥ, pratyabhivādasāhacaryāt | iha sāmarthyād abhivādaśabdo na | pratyabhivādam ācaṣṭe - taṃ ye na jānate yathāśāstraṃ prayoktuṃ tān pratyabhivādaprayogāsamarthān aham iti brūyāt | kaḥ | prājñaḥ | evaṃ yathābhihitaṃ śabdasyoccāranopadeśaḥ abhivādanānte svanāmoccāraṇanivṛttyartham | anyathā hi prāptivacanam anarthakaṃ syād iti | striyaś ca pratyabhivādanakuśalā api satya itarāś ceti | sarvagrahaṇaṃ kutaḥ | patadaśaraṃ (abhivādaśabdo) ?? hi sāmarthāt | anyathā hy anārambhasamam idaṃ pūrveṇaiva syād ity evaṃ ca cūrṇikākāraḥ: "pratyabhivāde nāmno 'nte yena klṛptiṃ (?) viduḥ kāmaṃ teṣu hi vipro 'py astrīṣv iva 'ayam aham' iti vadet" iti | aparas tv āha: "śūdro 'pi daśamī gataḥ" (MDh 2.137) ity etasmāc chūdrasyābhivādyatāṃ vakṣyati | evaṃ ca kadācid asāv aprājñatvāt saṃskṛtam ucyamānaṃ nāvabudhyeta yatas tatra "aham" iti brūyāt | atha vā vakyair vartamānādinopanāmakena prasiddhasya gṛhyanāmaparikīrtanaṃ [75] nāvabudhyeta kaścid aśūdro 'pi | yata idam ucyate | ity uccāraṇānuccāraṇapakṣadvaye 'pi nāmnaḥ śeṣavidhyartham idam abhivādasya prativadataḥ |
bhoḥśabdaṃ kīrtayed ante svasya nāmno 'bhivādane |
nāmnāṃ svarūpabhāvo hi bhobhāva ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 2.124 ||
abhivādyānāṃ tannāmagrahaṇam ayuktaṃ śāstrataḥ | na cāgṛhītanāmno 'bhivādyasyābhivādaṃ pratisambodhanābhimukhyam asti | na ca tadanantareṇa pratyabhivādaprayogo 'sya vidyate | yata idam ucyate | bhoḥśabdaṃ kīrtayed ity anenāpy abhivādyasya nāmagrahaṇadoṣaś ca parihṛto bhaviṣyati | saṃbodhanābhimukhyaṃ ca setsyatīty uttaras tu ślokārthaḥ pūrvavidhistutyartho vyākaraṇīyaḥ |
samāpto 'bhivādanavidhiḥ | pratyabhivādanavidhyarthas tv adhunocyate |
āyuṣmān bhava saumyeti vipro vācyo 'bhivādane |
akāraś cāsya nāmno 'nte kāryaḥ pūrvākṣaraḥ plutaḥ || 2.125 ||
āyuṣmān ity etad arthagrahaṇam | apare tu śabdagraṇam āhuḥ | bhavetīdam āśiṣo loṭ madhyamapuruṣaikavacanam | saumyeti cāyuṣmatvād aviśeṣaṇas tasya prītyartham iti | śabda eva śabdārtho vācya iti pratyabhivādanavidhiḥ | vipragrahaṇaṃ ca pradarśanārtham itareṣāṃ pratyabhivādānupadeśād avaśyaṃbhāvitvāc cābhivādasya vipravat | tathā ca vyākaraṇasmṛtiḥ "pratyabhivāde 'śūdre" (Pāṇini 8.2.83) ity etenābhivādanaślokaḥ gataḥ | vipragrahaṇaṃ vyākhyātam | "abhivādāt paraṃ vipraḥ" (MDh 2.122) ity etad evaṃ vipro vācyaḥ | abhivādane kṛte satīti vākyaśeṣaḥ | kiṃ cānyākāraś (akāraś) cāsya nāmno 'nte (?) ity (ā) akāragrahaṇam ikārādisvarapradarśanārthaṃ vijñeyam | niyamaśāstrābhāvavācyaśabdaḥ samuccayārthaḥ | pūrvaṃ ca yad uktaṃ tad vaktavyam idaṃ ca yad vakṣyāmaḥ | asyeti viśeṣaṇaṣaṣṭhī | kasya | abhivādayitur nāmnaḥ | antagrahaṇam (?) ādimadhyapratiṣedhārtham | tathā hi guror (antatantya) (?) atyantasya hy ekaikasya pravācyam iti vācyo 'ntyekāro nāmnaḥ | kīdṛśe | yata (?) idam ucyate | plutas trimātraḥ naikadvimātraḥ | evaṃ ca akāraviśeṣaṇasaṃbandhenaivāyam anuvādo vācya idaṃ tv aparam (?) akāraviśeṣaṇam | pūrvākṣaraḥ pūrvam akṣaram | kutaḥ | prakṛtād akārāt kinta (?) vyañjanam | etac ca guṇato 'kṣaraśabdenocyate | akārākṣarasāhacaryād vyañjane 'kṣaraśabdaḥ | akāre tv ayaṃ pūrvaśabdaviśiṣṭo 'kṣaraśabdo na ghaṭate | tasyānyavidhyabhāvāt parataḥ | tataḥ sāmānyād evaṃ vyañjane 'kṣaraśabdo guṇavṛttyā vijñeyaḥ | evaṃ ca sati pūrvākṣarastho 'kāraḥ pūrvākṣaraśabdasthānād upacaryate māntavat (?) | tathā ca kṛtvedam uktaṃ bhavati | vyañjanenāvirbāvavṛttir ayam akāraḥ parato vaktavyo nāsmād aplutya (?) pṛthaṅ nānyo (?) vā sati pūrvasmin vyañjane 'tha vā pūrvākṣarasametaḥ pūrvākṣara iti | tasmin pakṣe (76) samavetaśabdoktaphalopo (?) vijñeyaḥ | anyat samānam | tathā coktam: "pratyabhivāde 'śūdraḥ" (Pāṇini 8.2.83) iti | manunā (12 akṣ missing) mānteśvarasya plutavṛddhanaṃ lakṣa (?)
** MANUSCRIPT HAS 231 – 241 HERE BEFORE 126 and following. I follow here the normal sequence of Manu
yo na vetty abhivādasya vipraḥ pratyabhivādanam |
nābhivādyaḥ sa viduṣā yathā śūdras tathaiva saḥ || 2.126 ||
abhivādābhimukhaṃ yadā śi [1 akṣ unclear] dānam abhivādayitur asau pratyabhivādo 'bhihitasvarūpaḥ tayor na veda prayoktuṃ yathālakṣaṇaṃ nābhivādaḥ | sa viduṣā (?) kim ayaṃ pratiṣedho 'bhivādanasya | nety ucyate | kiṃ tarhi | pratyabhivādanapraśaṃsaiṣā | dṛṣṭāntāt, nābhidhānasāmarthāt (?) | na tasya pratyabhivādāsamarthasyātyantam anabhivāda ucyate | tathā coktam:
nāmadheyasya ye kecid abhivādaṃ na jānate |
tān prājño 'ham iti brūyāt [striyaḥ sarvās tathaiva ca] || [MDh 2.123]
iti | na ceyatā doṣeṇa śreyāt (?) śūdravad atyantam anabhivādyo bhavitum arhati, brāhmaṇajāter atyantamānyatvāt | atha vā striyām atyantapratiṣedhaḥ, śūdradṛṣṭāntābhidhānasāmarthyāt | evaṃ ca sati vihitapratiṣiddhatvād vikalpaḥ | svanāmagrahaṇena vābhivādyaḥ pratyabhivādanāsamarthaḥ yathāvocāma | atyantaṃ vā | sa cāyaṃ vyavasthitavikalpaḥ ātmanaḥ śreyān jātyādibhir itthaṃbhūto hy abhivādya vinā nāmnā itaras tu na kadācid vināpi nāmneti pratigrahaṇaṃ kṛtavyākhyānam | pratyabhivādaprasaṅgenāyam anyaḥ saṃbhāṣaṇaniyama idānīṃ śiṣyate yo yasyābhivādyaḥ sa tam abhivādayitāram |
brāhmaṇaṃ kuśalaṃ pṛcchet kṣatrabandhum anāmayam |
vaiśyaṃ kṣemaṃ samāgamya śūdram ārogyam eva ca || 2.127 ||
kathaṃ punar etad gamyate abhivādananiyamo 'yaṃ nābhivādayitur iti | pratyabhivādaprakaraṇopadeśasāmarthyād ayaṃ tadabhivyāhāraḥ pratīyate | abhivādananiyamaś cāyam | anyathā hi paunaruktyād avacanīyam etat syāt (?) niyamābhāvāc ca śāstraṃ [apramāṇam?] prasajyeta | samāgamyeti sarvaśeṣaḥ | atha vā śreyaḥsu pūrvo vidhiḥ samāneṣu tv ayam ucyate "brāhmaṇaṃ kuśalaṃ pṛcchet" ityevamādi | samāgamyeti ca liṅgād ayam arthaḥ pratīyate | saṃbhāṣaṇaniyamaprasaṅgād idam anyat ucyate |
avācyo dīkṣito nāmnā yavīyān api yo bhavet |
bhobhavatpūrvakaṃ tv enam abhibhāṣeta dharmavit || 2.128 ||
arthaprāptasya dīkṣitatvena nimittena pratiṣedhaḥ | anantarāśāstrānantarā prāptasya vā (?) yavīyān api iti vacanād daṇḍāpūpikayā samānsyābhijanmajyeṣṭhasyāpi dīkṣitasya guror nāmagrahaṇaṃ (?) pratiṣiddham |
śreyaḥsu guruvad vṛttiṃ nityam eva samācaret | (MDh 2.207)
ity anena kecit apiśabdaṃ saṃbhāvanārthaṃ ca nayanti (?) | yavīyān api prāg eva jyeṣṭhaḥ | tad etad vicāraṇīyaṃ sādhu vā na veti | dīkṣito dvijātir dvijātibhiḥ prakaraṇān nāmnā na vaktavyaḥ | daṇḍāpūpikayā tu śūdreṇāpi | idānīṃ nāmno 'nyatrāniyame prāpte niyamārtham idam ārabhyate | "bhobhavatpūrvakaṃ tv enam" [80] iti | bhośabdaḥ pūrvaṃ yasyābhibhāṣaṇasya bhavacchabdaś ca tad idaṃ bhobhavatpūrvakaṃ śabdadvayaṃ cedaṃ śiṣṭaṃ (śiṣṭāḥ?) smaranti yathā nigṛhītaṃ pūjartham | tathā coktam,
nāmnāṃ svarūpabhāvo hi bhośabda ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtaḥ | (MDh 2.124)
patañjalibhāṣye ca punaḥ punar idam uktam "tatrabhavataḥ pāṇineḥ" iti dīkṣitasaṃbhāṣaṇe ca bhobhavacchabdo vijñeya āmantraṇārthatvāt | asyodāharaṇaṃ "bho dīkṣita yajamāna sunvan (?) satraṃ nityete (?) dīkṣitaśabdasya pratidarśanārthatvād bhośabdapūrvāḥ pratīyante sāmarthyāt | kathaṃ dīkṣitanāmagrahaṇaṃ tāvad atra pratiṣiddham | anyatra bhobhavacchabdayor uttarapadaṃ nopadiṣṭaṃ "bhobhavatpūrvakaṃ cainam" iti cātra bravīti, dīkṣitaśabdaś ceha śrūyate | etad eva pratīyate yathāvocāma | yatas tatsaṃbhāṣaṇāny atra tu dīkṣitasaṃnidhāv asaṃnidhau ca tatrabhavato dīkṣitasyetyevamādi | atha vāyaṃ bhavacchabdo dīkṣitasaṃbhāṣaṇenānyatra ca sāmarthād vijñeyaḥ | "bhavān dīkṣitaḥ prājñaḥ śraddhālur ātrikaś ca" ityevamādi tatsaṃbhāṣaṇa uktam | samastayor api bhobhavacchabdayor dīkṣitasaṃbhāṣaṇe prayogo vijñeyaḥ | "bho bhavān dīkṣitaḥ prājñaḥ" ityādiprakṛtigrahaṇaṃ cedam | prakṛtigrahaṇe ca pratyayagrahaṇam arthasiddham | yata etad evaṃ vyākriyate prakaraṇāc ca | ayam anyatra strīsaṃbhāṣaṇavidhiḥ prakṛtasya brahmacāriṇa ucyate |
parapatnī tu yā strī syād asaṃbandhā ca yonitaḥ |
tāṃ brūyād bhavatīty evaṃ subhage bhaginīti vā || 2.129 ||
parapatnī tu yā strī syāt | kīdṛśī | tāṃ vivṛṇoti | asaṃbandhā ca yonitaḥ | mātāpitroḥ parapatnī seha vijñeyā | tāṃ brūyād ity evaṃ saṃbhāṣaṇaniyamaḥ tatākṣissamatiṣyate (?) brahmacārī kathaṃlakṣaṇo bhavatīty evaṃrūpaḥ | subhage bhaginīti vā | bhavatīti yo 'yam atra itikaraṇaḥ praśliṣṭanirdiṣṭaḥ asau śabdaḥ padārthako vijñeyaḥ pratipadopasthāyī ceti | bhaginīty ayaṃ punar uttaraḥ prakārāvarodhārthaḥ mātaraṃ cety evamādīnām | tathā ca lokaprasiddhiḥ | ayaṃ ca saṃbhāṣya strīṇām ātmanaś ca vayaānurūpyeṇa saṃbhāṣaṇaniyamo vijñeyaḥ | apare tu vyapadeśād (?) vikalpam atra varṇayanti (?) | paraparigṛhītā strī subhageti vācyā | svapatnī kulajā bhaginī saṃdigdhā bhavatīti | tat tv idaṃ syāryyaṃ (syādārthaṃ?) prakaraṇāt | ayam anyo brahmacāridharmaḥ |
mātulāṃś ca pitṛvyāṃś ca śvasurān ṛtvijo gurūn |
asāv aham iti brūyāt pratyutthāya yavīyasaḥ || 2.130 ||
atra guruśabdo 'nuktānāṃ mātṛṣvasṛprabhṛtīnām upasaṃgrahārtho vijñeyaḥ vidyāguruvarjitānām | tasya hi paramagurutvād abhivādanarahitaṃ pratyutthānamātayuktaṃ yavīyaso 'pi sataḥ śāstrāntaravirodhāt | tathā ca śaiśavaṃ vakṣyati | "adhyāpayāmasa pitṝn" (MDh 2.251) ityevamādi | na [81] ca mātulādiviṣayam atrāturagrahaṇaṃ punaruktagurvanupasaṅgaprasaṅgāc ca | udāharaṇābhidhāne ca bahūnām ānarthakyam | yato na mātulādiviṣayo guruśabdaḥ | atha tu bahūnām api pradarśanārthatvaṃ sāmarthyāt kathaṃcid bhavati, tato mātulādīnām anuvādo 'yaṃ guruśabdo yujyate stutyarthaḥ | kathaṃ gurusamarthād ete yavīyāṃso 'pi santaḥ pratyuttheyā ity asāv iti ca sarvanāmnas tathānena coktavidhānam idam asya "asāv aham iti brūyāt" iti | kiṃ tarhi bhavadapratiṣedhaparam (?) | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "ṛtvikśvaśurapitṛvyamātulānāṃ tu yavīyasāṃ pratyutthānam anabhivādāś ca" iti | abhivādanalakṣaṇasāmarthyāc caitad evaṃ pratīyata iti |
mātṛṣvasā mātulānī śvaśrūr ath pitṛśvasā |
sampūjyā gurupatnīvat samās tā gurubhāryayā || 2.131 ||
"saṃpūjyā gurupatnīvat" ity ukte "samās tā gurubhāryayā" ityetadarthaṃ siddhatvād avacanīyaṃ sad ucyate 'rthavācārthaṃ (-dārthaṃ?) yathā āgamataḥ (?) yavīyasyo 'pi satya etā jyeṣṭhānām tadbhartṝṇāṃ saṃpūjyā ity upadeśāt pratyutthāyābhivādyāś ca | tathā ca vakṣyati: "jyāyasyāṃ ca svasary api" (MDh 2.133) ity etasmāl liṅgād asyā gurustriyo jyāyastvam anapekṣyābhivādyā | smṛtyantare 'py uktam: "pativayasya (parivayasyāḥ?) striyaḥ" iti | jyeṣṭhastrīṇāṃ tu pratyutthānaṃ mātulād iva jyeṣṭhabhāryāṇāṃ tūbhayaṃ daṇḍāpūpikayā siddham | atha vā hetur ayam "saṃpūjyā gurupatnīvat" iti | kasya hetoḥ | "samās tā gurubhāryayā" iti | pūjāprakaraṇe ca pratyuttānābhivādanād adhikam idam | anyāsāṃ pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ pūjāhetuḥ saviśeṣaṃ abhidhīyate |
bhrātur bhāryopasaṃgrāhyā savarṇāhany ahany api |
viproṣya tūpasaṃgrāhyā jñātisaṃbandhiyoṣitaḥ || 2.132 ||
jñātiyoṣitsaṃnidhau bhrātṛśabdo 'yam ekamātṛkam | saṃdarbhāntaraṃ darśayati | bhrātur bhāryopasaṃgrāhyā pādayoḥ | smṛtyantarasamācārābhyāṃ guruprakaraṇe 'py (?) ajyeṣṭhasya bhrātus tadviśeṣaṇaṃ sarvarṇāhany ahny api proṣitaṃ pratyāgatena ca jñātisaṃbandhiyoṣitas tu proṣitaṃ pratyāgatenaiva pratyutthānābhivādanaṃ iti vikalpakatvān nityam |
pitur bhaginyāṃ mātuś ca jyāyasyāṃ ca svasary api |
mātṛvad vṛttim ātiṣṭhen mātā tābhyo garīyasī || 2.133 ||
pitṛṣvasṛmātṛśvasṛjyeṣṭhābhaginīnāṃ mātṛvad vṛttyupadeśāt pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ prakaraṇāt nirvikalpakam upadiśyate jyeṣṭhabhrātṛpatnīvat | na tu jñātisaṃbandhayoṣidvat savikalpakam idam | idānīm arthasiddhatvāt kimartham ucyate "mātā tābhyo garīyasī" | ucyate | vikalpārtham | evaṃ cāsāṃ [82] vikalpena prakṛtaṃ pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ veditavyam | proṣitapratyāgatena vā pratyahaṃ vā jñātisaṃbandhayoṣitāt tu viproṣyaiva | jyeṣṭhabhrātṛpatnīnāṃ tu pratyaham eva sati saṃnidhāna iti | atha vā "mātṛvad vṛttim ātiṣṭhed" ity anena sarvasyām ājñākaraṇāyāṃ mātṛvṛttau prāptāyāṃ pratiṣedhārtham ārabhyate | "mātā tābhyo garīyasī" ity arthaḥ kṛtsnātideśanārthaḥ |
daśābdākhyaṃ paurasakhyaṃ pañcābdākhyaṃ kalābhṛtām |
tryabdapūrvaṃ śrotriyāṇāṃ svalpenāpi svayoniṣu || 2.134 ||
ātmano daśavarādhikena saha yad ākhyāyate tad daśākhyābdam | kiṃ tat paurasakhyam | pure bhavaḥ pauraḥ | tasya sakhyaṃ paurasakhyam | puragrahaṇaṃ nidarśanārtham | sakhyaṃ tu pūrvavayāḥ "pratyutthāyābhivādayet" (MDh 2.119) ity etasmāc chāstrāt prātaḥ pratyutthānādir atra pratiṣidhyate | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "bho bhavann iti vayasyaḥ" [GDh 6.14] | mitratvād evaṃ vaktavyaḥ | na tu mānyaḥ nirguṇe caitad ekajātau draṣṭavyam uttaratra guṇopadeśāj jātistutaś cety evaṃ kalābhṛtau iti | guṇādhikyād varṣāvaraḥ (?) | "tryabdapūrvaṃ śrotriyāṇām" itīdaṃ dvijātiliṅgaśrotriyagrahaṇaṃ dvijātigrahaṇam uttaratra ca śūdragrahaṇam etadarthaṃ liṅgayati | alpenāpy adhikasvalpe yo (?) niṣṭhaḥ samānayoniṣṭhaś ca jātiprati | samānaṃ (?) pūrveṇa
brāhmaṇaṃ daśavarṣaṃ tu śatavarṣaṃ tu bhūmipam |
pitāputrau vijānīyād brāhmaṇas tu tayoḥ pitā || 2.135 ||
pitrādiṣu (?) jāte 'nuktatvāt tasya mānyasthānatocyate | evaṃ kṣatriyādiṣv api ca draṣṭavyam | vayaś ca jāteḥ prādhānyavacanād vittabandhubhyām api tasyāḥ prādhānyam anunīyate | karmavidyābhyāṃ na jāteḥ prādhānyam | evaṃ ca pūrvaślokaḥ samānajātiṣv eva draṣṭavyaḥ | ataś ca taccheṣatvād uvayutya (bhayasya?) vittādibhyo jater ihābhidhānaṃ na tajjātir eva mānyasthānam | imāni ca –
vittaṃ bandhu vayaḥ karma vidyā bhavati pañcamī |
etāni mānasthānāni garīyo yad yad uttaram || 2.136 ||
pūjā bhā mānyas triṣu tyasminn iti (??) mānyasthānam | eṣāṃ tānīmāni mānyasthānāni | evaṃ ca vittādīnāṃ mānyatvam arthād uktam | balābalaṃ vaiṣām ucyate | garīyo gurutaraṃ dvaye dvayoḥ sāmānyād veditavyam | kiṃ tanmānyasthānam | yad yad uttaraṃ pūrvasmāt pūrvasmāt arthasiddham | vīpsā sāmarthyād śrutāpi |
pañcānāṃ triṣu varṇeṣu bhūyāṃsi guṇavanti ca |
83]        yatra syuḥ so 'tra mānārhaḥ śūdro 'pi daśamīṃ gataḥ || 2.137 ||
adhikṛtānāṃ pūrvajāhetūnāṃ (?) viśeṣaṇadvayasaṃbandhena stutyartham idam ārabhyate | pañcānāṃ vittādīnāṃ yatra dvijātau hi (?) bhūyāṃsi guṇavanti ca | vittādīni syur ataḥ so 'tra loke cāturvarṇye vā mānārhataro (?) vijñeyaḥ | sāmarthyāc chūdro 'pi prakṛṣṭavayaḥ | pūrve vātra [prāg] vaiśyo 'pi pūrvayoḥ kṣatriyo 'pi pūrvasmāt prāg eva samānajātir utkṛṣṭo veti | "daśamīṃ gataḥ" iti vacanaṃ vittādīnāṃ prakarṣapradarśanārthaṃ vijñeyam | evaṃ sati śūdro 'pīti apir ayaṃ sambhāvanāyam (?) | anyat tu (?) samuccaye | kutaḥ | jāter atyantamānyasthānato 'pi deśā brāhma [3 akṣ misssing] ṇo daśa vā daśavarṣaś ceti śūdrābhivādapratiṣedhāc ca "nābhivādyaḥ sa viduṣā" (MDh 2.126) ity uktam | evaṃ ca yat kaiścid ucyate — anuvādo mā bhūd iti | yato hīnajātiviṣayo 'yam upadeśaḥ prakalpyeta | śūdro 'pi vaiśyo 'pi kṣatriyo 'py evaṃguṇo mānya ity etad ayuktam iti jātivittādīnāṃ mānyahetutvam upadiśet tadvinākṛtānāṃ mānaikadeśo 'pi na prāpta iṣyate tān nirasyāpi ity ata idam ārabyate |
cakriṇo daśamīsthasya rogiṇo bhāriṇaḥ striyāḥ |
snātakasya ca rājñaś ca panthā deyo varasya ca || 2.138 ||
cakravad yānaṃ ca kurvataś cāsya (?) mañcavad upacārāc cakriṇo jātyādihīnasyāpi panthā deyaś cakritvāt | evam uttaratrāpi (?) | kuta etat | ārambhasāmyam arthyāt (?) | daśamīsthasya acakriṇo 'pi | rogiṇaḥ cācakridaśamīsthasyāpi bhāriṇaś cātathābhūtasyāpi | stryāś cāsannaprasavāyāḥ atiparigudupari (?) saraṇatvāt anugrāhyā | yānastrīmātrasya (?) rogyādibhir anugrāhyaiḥ sahopadeśāt | snātakagrahaṇaṃ ca brāhmaṇaviṣayam eva, rājasaṃnidhisāmarthyāt | ayaṃ cādhipatyādiguṇayuktaḥ pārthivo 'pi kṣatriyatvataḥ (?) na kṣatriyamātram | nanv atra snātakagrahaṇam anarthakam uktatvād vācyaṃ samānatvāc ca rogyādibhiḥ | satyam etat | dṛṣṭāntārthas tv ayaṃ vijñeyaḥ nāprāptaprāpaṇārthaḥ | kathaṃ kṛtvā | yathā snātako mānyaḥ evaṃ ced anyo 'pi yathoktamānārhā tūttarārthaṃ ca vakṣyati hi | "teṣāṃ tu samavetānāṃ mānyo snātakapārthivau" (MDh 2.139) ityevamādi | evaṃ kṛtvā rājagrahaṇam apy atra yuktarūpaṃ bhavati | paragrahaṇaṃ ca pūrvavad vyākhyeyam | tasya ca pṛthak śravaṇāt sarvebhyaś cakryādibhyaḥ (?) prādhānyaṃ nirapavādaṃ pratīyate | ebhyaś ca yathāmānāniyamāt (?) anyatra yathākāmam | evam eṣāṃ cakryādīnāṃ mānyasakāśān mānyatvaṃ niyogata upapādya tadviśeṣavivakṣayedam (?) adhunā prārabhyate |
teṣāṃ tu samavetānāṃ mānyau snātakapārthivau |
[84]        rājasnātakayoś caiva snātako nṛpamānabhāk || 2.139 ||
ekamānārhasamavetānāṃ niyamataḥ prakṛtena pathādānena mānyena snātakapārthivena tābhyām anyatra tu pūjāhetutvapakṣayā (?) vikalpa āśrayaṇīyaś cakryādīnām | rājasnātakayos tu saṃgame "snātako nṛpamānabhāk" | vakṣyati hi
vaiśeṣāt prakṛtiśraiṣṭhyāt niyamasya ca dhāraṇāt |
saṃskārasya viśeṣāc ca varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || (MDh 10.3)
ity ato rājñaḥ sakāśāt snātakasya mānyasthānatopadeśo (?) yuktaḥ "uttamāṅgodbhavāj jyaiṣṭhyāt" (MDh 1.96) ityevamādinoktaḥ (?) |
upanīya tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ vedam adhyāpayed dvijaḥ |
sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca tam ācāryaṃ pracakṣate || 2.140 ||
sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ ceti karmaṇy ātmavidyābhyāsahetvarthaḥ | atha vā kalpagrahaṇaṃ sarvāṅgapradarśaṇārtham | rahasyagrahaṇaṃ ca vedārthavijñānapradarśanārtham | tathā ca granthārthavijñānadānād ācāryasya sarvagurubhyaḥ śraiṣṭhyam | kṣatriyasya (?) ca śaiśavākhyam ācāryasya saṃjñopadeśaḥ sokaratva(?) vyavahārārthaḥ | tathā ca vakṣyati "upādhyāyād daśācāryaḥ" (MDh 2.145) ityevamādi | tenopādhyāyādisṃjñā vyākhyātā |
ekadeśaṃ tu vedasya
mantramātram abrāhmanamātraṃ tadavayaṃ ca
vedāṅgāny api vā punaḥ |
vedarahitāni | kutaḥ | etadārambhasāmarthyād asya samastāni svyastāni tadekadeśaṃ vā |
yo 'dhyāpayati vṛttyartham
na dharmārtham | evaṃ cācāryasya cākṛtsnavedadharmādhyāpanābhyāṃ gatvataratvam (?) itarasya vā kṛtsnavedavṛttyadhyāpanābhyām ācāryalāghavam upadiṣṭaṃ śāstre
upādhyāyaḥ sa ucyate || 2.141 ||
ity uktaṃ saṃjñopadeśaprayojanam |
niṣekādīni karmāṇi yaḥ karoti yathāvidhi |
saṃbhāvayati cānnena sa vipro gurur ucyate || 2.142 ||
nopanayanādhyāpanābhyām | ārambhasāmarthyād etad vijñāyate, niṣekagrahaṇāc ca | pitāyam upanayanādhyāpanopakārarahito vijñāyate | tathā ca poṣaṇasahitaṃ niṣekādyupakāramātraṃ [85] darśayati: "saṃbhāvayati cānnena" iti | sa evam aślovakāro (?) | vipragrahaṇam udāharaṇārtham | gurur ucyate | kecit tv etāṃ sāmānyasaṃjñām evāhuḥ | evam ācāryaḥ śreṣṭho gurūṇām ity asya tu viśeṣasaṃjño 'yam | yathā ca vyāsaḥ |
prabhuḥ śarīraprabhavaḥ priyakṛd vṛttido guruḥ |
hitānām upadeṣṭā ca pratyakṣaṃ daivataṃ pitā || iti |
mātary api caivam eva śenarava (?) nirdiṣṭam arthavad vijñeyam | vakṣyati hi: "sahasraṃ tu (?) pitur mātā" (MDh 2.145) iti | atha vāyam api gurur bhavati, kiṃ punaḥ pūrvau brahmopakārakau – ity ācāryopādhyāyastutiḥ |
āgnyādheyaṃ pākayajñān agniṣṭomādimān makhān |
yaḥ karoti vṛto yasya sa tasya ṛtvig ihocyate || 2.143 ||
guruprakaraṇe ṛtviksaṃjño cādeśas teṣāṃ gurutvajñāpanārthaḥ | gurūṇāṃ tad ucyate pratyutthānābhivādanādi, tad eṣām api yathā syād iti | idānīṃ sarvagurubhya ācāryasya jyāyas tv adhyā(jñā)panārtham iyaṃ stutiḥ |
ya āvṛṇoty avitathaṃ brahmaṇā śravaṇāv ubhau |
sa mātā sa pitā jñeyas taṃ na druhyet kadācana || 2.144 ||
āvṛṇoty ācchādayaty api | katham | satyena | kenācchādayati | brahmaṇā vedādhyāpanena tadarthavyākhyānena ubhābhyāṃ vā | kiṃ tat | śravaṇāv ubhau (?) | kasya | śiṣyasya | "sa mātā sa pitā jñeyaḥ" anenopakāraviśeṣeṇaivaṃ ca (?) kṛtvā guṇavadbhyāṃ mātāpiṭbhyām iyam ācāryasya stutismṛtīyasthānopadeśasāmarthyād veditavyā | na nirguṇābhyām | atas taṃ na druhyet kadācana | kasmiṃś cid api kāle kṛtārtho 'pīty arthaḥ | apare tv āhuḥ — ācāryopādhyāyayor iyaṃ stutiḥ, ācāryasyaivottaravivakṣārthā | tat tv idam asamarthāvirodhād iti | tasmāt punar niṣekādyalpopakārābhyāṃ mātāpitṛbhyām ajyāyobhyāṃ mahopakārasya jyāyaḥ sayamaḥ (?) ācāryasya stutiḥ kriyate "sa mātā sa pitā jñeyaḥ" ity atra | ucyate yasmāt |
upādhyāyād daśācārya ācāryāṇāṃ śataṃ pitā |
pitur daśaśataṃ mātā gauraveṇātiricyate || 2.145 ||
yaḥ sarvaguṇa upanayanādhyāpanādisaṃskārahetutvena
sahasraṃ tu pitur mātā gauraveṇātiricyate | [MDh 2.145]
ity etad api vyākhyātam | evaṃ ca kṛtvā guṇavadbhām eva mātāpitṛbhyām ā (3 akṣ missing) cāryasaṃstavo yuktaḥ | nirguṇaḥ pitā utpādakatvenopanayanādisaṃskārahetuḥ (??) | tatra
[86]
utpādakabrahmadātror garīyān brahmadaḥ pitā |
brahmajanma hi viprasya pretya ceha ca śāśvatam || 2.146 ||
vipragrahaṇaṃ darśanārtham | trayāṇāṃ prakṛtatvāt | kiṃ cānyat |
kāmān mātā pitā cainaṃ yad utpādayato mithaḥ |
saṃbhūtiṃ tasya tāṃ vidyād yad yonāv abhijāyate || 2.147 ||
iyaṃ yayā (?) mātāpitṛnindayācāryastutiḥ prakaraṇasāmarthyād veditavyā iyaṃ cā [1 akṣ unclear] avyavahitā (?) |
ācāryas tv asya yāṃ jātiṃ vidhivad vedapāragaḥ |
utpādayati sāvitryā sā satyā sājarāmarā || 2.148 ||
prakaraṇāntareṇeyam anyā prakṛtārthastutiḥ |
alpaṃ vā bahu vā yasya śrutasyopakaroti yaḥ |
tam apīha turuṃ vidyāc chrutopakriyayā tayā || 2.149 ||
ekadeśopakāram api santam upādhyāyasaṃjñaṃ kiṃ punar mahopakāram ācāryam ity ācāryapraśaṃsāpi satī na hy akasmāt praśaṃsā syād ity asyāpy upādhyāyasya gurutvāvagatiḥ | adhunā parakṛter upodghātārtham ayam ārabhyate ślokaḥ prakṛtārthastutyai |
brāhmasya janmanaḥ kartā svadharmasya ca śāsitā |
bālo 'pi vipro vṛddhasya pitā bhavati dharmataḥ (?) || 2.150 ||
na ca janmataḥ (?) pitā | kiṃ tarhi | brahmadaḥ pitety asminn arthe purākṛtir udāhriyate |
adhyāpayāmāsa pitṝñ chiśur āṅgīrasaḥ kaviḥ|
putrakā iti hovāca jñānena parigṛhya tān || 2.151 ||
te tam artham apṛcchanta devān āgatamanyavaḥ |
devāś caitān sametyocur nyāyyaṃ vaḥ śiśur uktavān || 2.152 ||
yasmād iyam atra stutiḥ |
ajño bhavati vai bālaḥ pitā bhavati mantradaḥ |
ajñaṃ hi bālam ity āhuḥ pitety eva tu mantradam || 2.153 ||
[87]
evaṃ ca sati –
na hāyanair na palitair na vitten na bandhubhiḥ |
ṛṣayaś cakrire dharmaṃ yo 'nūcānaḥ sa no mahān || 2.154 ||
tathā ca sati –
viprāṇāṃ jñānato jyaiṣṭhyaṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ tu vīryataḥ |
vaiśyānāṃ dhānyadhanataḥ śūdrāṇām eva janmataḥ || 2.155 ||
kṣatriyādīnāṃ ca vīryādigrahaṇaṃ prakṛtārthastutyartham aprakaraṇād vīryādīnāṃ dṛṣṭāntatvena | evaṃ ca vyavasthite prakaraṇārthe,
na tena sthaviro bhavati yenāsya palitaṃ śiraḥ |
yo vai yuvāpy adhīyānas taṃ devāḥ sthaviraṃ viduḥ || 2.156 ||
evaṃ ca sati –
yathā kāṣṭhamayo hastī yathā carmamayo mṛgaḥ |
yaś ca vipro 'nadhīyānaḥ trayas te nāma bibhrati || 2.157 ||
kiṃ ca –
yathā ṣaṇḍho 'phlaḥ strīṣu yathā gaur gavi cāphalā |
yathā cājñe 'phalaṃ dānaṃ yathā (tathā?) vipro 'nṛco 'phalaḥ || 2.158 ||
** appears that this commentary should go with 2.149 — or is it 145?.
prakaraṇasāmarthād anyādhyarthaṃ (?) śrutayo 'pi santaḥ ācāryapraśaṃsārthakāḥ | upakārātiśayāpy (?) ācāryā gauravaṃ mātāpitṛbhyām udāharaṇaṃ ca bhūtvābhidhānasāmarthyastutyatiśayarūpād veditavyam | ubhayasyopadeśasyārthavatvād yajñācāryapitṛmātṛsamavāye ścāryaṃ pitrā mātrā ca saha vikalpyate 'bhivādanapūjāsu (?) |
idānīṃ brahmacāriṇo 'py adhyayane prasannenāyam adhyāpayitur niyama ucyate –
ahiṃsayaiva bhūtānāṃ
prakaraṇāc chiṣyāṇām,
kāryaṃ śreyo 'nuśāsanam |
vedaśāstrādhyāpanānupavahena (panenānupadena?) saṃbadhyate | arthaprāptānāṃ hiṃsādīnām adharmāṇām ayaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | [88]
vāk caiva madhurā ślakṣṇā prayojyā dharmam icchatā || 2.159 ||
evaṃ cānuśāsananiyamād asmād asti dharmaḥ yathā – yo 'smād eva sarvaniyamaḥ sarvebhyaḥ | evaṃ ceyaṃ prakṛtārthastutiḥ |
yasya vāṅmanasī śuddhe samyaggupte ca sarvadā |
sa vai sarvam āpnoti vedāntādhigataṃ phalam || 2.160 ||
evaṃ ca sati –
nāruntudaḥ syād ārto 'pi na paradrohakarmadhīḥ |
yayāsyodvijate vācā nālokyāṃ tām udīrayet || 2.161 ||
athānyaiś ca yo garveṇa darpeṇācāryaṃ na pūjayed avamānayed (?) vā tathāpy ācāryaś cittasaṃkṣobham ātmanā anena prasaṃkhyānenopāyena pariharet |
saṃmānād brāhmaṇo nityam udvijet viṣād iva |
amṛtasyeva cākāṅkṣed avamānasya sarvataḥ || 2.162 ||
kasya hetoḥ yena (?)
sukhaṃ hy avamataḥ śete sukhaṃ ca pratibudhyate |
sukhaṃ carati loke 'sminn avamantā vinaśyati || 2.163 ||
adhyāpayituś cittasaṃkṣobhahetūpagamanārthaṃ ślokadvayam | jātidharmo vāyam anyaprakaraṇopadiṣta ihādhyāpayitur adhyāpyabrahmacāripraśaṃsayābhihito (?) vijñeyo nāpūrvavidhyarthaḥ | kimartho 'yaṃ brahmacāriṇa iyān yamaniyamakathādeśa ucyate | katham | nāmadheyam |
anena kramayogena saṃskṛtātmā dvijaḥ śanaiḥ |
gurau vasan saṃcinuyād brahmādhigamikaṃ tapaḥ || 2.164 ||
prakṛtārthopsaṃhārārthaḥ ślokaḥ | evaṃ ca saty anena brahmacāriṇaivaṃ saṃskṛtātmanā –
tapoviśeṣair vividhair vrataiś ca vidhicoditaiḥ |
vedaḥ kṛtsno 'dhigantavyaḥ sarahasyo dvijanmanā || 2.165 ||
tapoviśeṣair agnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇādibhiḥ | uktair vakṣyamāṇaiś ca brahmacāriṇaḥ dharmair yamaniyamākhyais tatprakaraṇopadeśasāmarthyād vividhair anekākāraiḥ | tapoviśeṣaṇam idam | vrataiś ca prativedaṃ gṛhyaśāstroktaiḥ saṃskṛtātmanā brahmacāriṇā vedo mantrabrāhmaṇākhyo 'dhigantavya iti vedasvīkaraṇaparyāyaḥ | tasya viśeṣaṇam | kṛtsnaḥ sāṅga ity arthaḥ | kathaṃ punar etat gamyate | evaṃ kṛtsnatvaṃ [89] vedasya na punar brāhmaṇāpekṣasya mantrasyety ucyate | mantrabrāhmaṇayor vedanāmadheyatvād ekadeśatvābhidhānaṃ yuktaṃ vedaśabdenāsati sāmarthye yataś ca sāṅgasya samarthatvaṃ mantrabrāhmaṇarāśer vedākhyasya puruṣārthakriyāyāṃ na kevalasya | ataś ca gamyate | sāṅgaḥ kṛtsno nirapekṣatvād ucyate | tathā padabhāṣyam idaṃ smṛtinibandhanam eva: "brāhmaṇena niṣkāraṇaṃ ṣaḍaṅgo vedo 'dhyetavyaḥ" iti | sarahasya iti tadviśeṣaṇam eva | kiṃ punas tadrahasyam | vedārthakriyāvijñānam | kutaḥ punar etad vijñānasyopaniṣacchabdārthatvam | sāmarthyād rahasyabrāhmaṇasya ca vedaśabdena grahaṇāt | punas tadabhidhānam ayuktam aṅgānāṃ ca kṛtsnagrahaṇenāvirodhād aśāstrārthatvād (?) anyārthasaṃbhavāc ca | rahasyaśabdasya viṣayatvam ayuktam | yato yathokta evāsyārthaś codyate | nanv aṅṅādhigamopadeśād evaitat siddhaṃ sarahasya iti | padārthāntaratvād adhyaynāt tadvijñānādhigamasyāsiddham | ato yatnāntarārtho 'yam upadeśaḥ | viṣayāntarayuktaṃ sarahasya ity abhyanujñāpadena vāyam upadeśaḥ ādarārthakatve (?) karmādhikārasāmarthyād adhītaśrutavedasya śrutiliṅgāni ca "vidvān yajate, vidvān yājayati" iti | saiṣā kṛtsnavedādhyayanasmṛtir ekavedādhyayana eva kṛtārthādhyetuḥ | tathā caikavacanaṃ veda iti | evaṃ ca vakṣyati:
vedān adhītya vedā vā vedaṃ vāpi yathākramam |
aviplutabrahmacaryo gṛhasthāśramam āviśet || [MDh 3.2]
iti | smṛtyantaraṃ caivam eva: "vedasyaikāṃ śākhām adhītya śrotriyo bhavati" iti | prayatnaviśeṣāt phalaviśeṣeṇa phalituṃ yuktaṃ ca | trivedādhyetus tv ayaṃ niyamaḥ | trivedādhyetaiva śrotriyo naikavedādhyetety evaṃ ca | yat kaiścit kṛtsnagrahaṇāt vedayor adhyayanaṃ śabdārtho yena kalpyate, tad anyāyyam iti, yac cedam ucyate vākyārtharūpaḥ (am?) | kaiścid vedagrahaṇena kila mantro gṛhyate, kṛtsnagrahaṇena saha karma, brāhmaṇena mantrasamūhaḥ, rahasyagrahaṇena copaniṣadaḥ – etad apy avadhānam eva veditavyam iti | evaṃ ca krtvā –
vedam eva sadābhyasyet tapas tapsyan dvijottamaḥ |
vedābhyāso hi viprasya tapaḥ param ihocyate || 2.166 ||
"vedaḥ kṛtsno 'dhigantavyaḥ" (MDh 2.165) iti śrutasya vidheḥ stutyarthavādaḥ | prakaraṇāc ca brahmacāriṇo 'yam upadeśaḥ sadāgrahaṇād dvijātidharma eva vijñeyaḥ | nityaprāyaścittārthaṃ vā sadāgrahaṇam | tathā ca vājasaneyakaṃ brāhmaṇam imam evārtham anuvadati |
ā haiva sa nakhāgrebhyaḥ paramaṃ tapyate tapaḥ |
yaḥ sragvy api dvijo 'dhīte svādhyāyaṃ śaktito 'nvaham || 2.167 ||
āndo? (āśabdo?) nakhāgrapade nābhisaṃbandhaḥ | haśabda aitihyārthaḥ (?) | evaśabdo 'vadhāraṇe | vākyārthaṃ brūmaḥ | ā nakhāgrebhyaḥ eṣa tapas tapyate yaḥ svādhyāyam adhīta | vedam [90] abhyasyatīty arthaḥ | evaṃ hi śrūyate svādhyāyapraśaṃsāprakaraṇe: "yadi ha vā apy abhyakto (?) 'laṅkṛtaḥ mukhe śayane śayānaḥ svādhyāyam adhīta āhaiva sa nakhāgrebhyaḥ tapas tapyate | ya evaṃ vidvān svādhyāyam adhīte," "tasmāt svādhyāyo 'dhyetavyaḥ" iti | sa eṣo 'pareṇa prakāreṇa stutyatiśayo veditavyaḥ prakṛtasya vedābhyāsasya | yena vidur indriyatvān nakhāgreṇānnāsti sa tāladukhkhaṃ (?) yaḥ sragvī | vipratiṣiddhā caraṇenāpi vedo 'dhyetavya iti stutiparam etan na vidhyupadeśanidarśanārthaṃ caitat sragvigrahaṇaṃ sarvādhyayanavyatikramāṇāṃ veditavyam | tathā ca brāhmaṇam udāhṛtam | "vedaḥ kṛtsno 'dhigantavyaḥ" (MDh 2.165) ity anena sāṅgavedādhyayanānupūrvam arthāt prāptam idaṃ niyamyate –
yo 'nadhītya dvijo vedam anyatra kurute śramam |
sa jīvann eva śūdratvam āśu gacchati sānvayaḥ || 2.168 ||
upanītasyāyaṃ niyama ucyate | evaṃ sati prāgupanayanād aṅgādhyayanasyāpratiṣedhaṃ kecid āhuḥ | vayaṃ ca (?) "tapoviśeṣaiḥ" (MDh 2.165) ityevamādivacanasāmarthyāt prakaraṇāc copanītasya sataḥ sāṅgavedādhyayane niyamena prāpte 'yaṃ niyama ārabhyate iti manyāmahe | evaṃ ca vyatikramanindaiṣā "sa jīvann eva śūdratvam āśu gacchati sānvayaḥ" iti adhyayanastutyarthā | stutinindābhyāṃ ca vidhipratiṣedhavākyānumānaṃ lokavad atra śāstre veditavyam | vedādhyananādhikārasāmānyāc ca śūdratvam āśu gacchatīty ucyate | sānvaya iti nindātiśayaḥ prakṛtārthastutyatiśayārthāc ca | vedādhyayanādhikāraprayojanam idaṃ brahmajanma stauti |
mātur agre 'dhijananaṃ dvitīyaṃ mauñjibandhane |
tṛtīyaṃ yajñadīkṣāyāṃ dvijasya vidhicoditaḥ (śruticodanāt) || 2.169 ||
mātur agre 'dhijananam itīdaṃ yonijanmanaḥ saṃbhavena mātṛpradarśanān na nindāvacanaṃ brahmajanmastutyarthaṃ prakṛtatvād atra stutiś ca vedādhyaynahetutvād asya | atra dṛṣṭāntam āha: yathā ca yajñadīkṣā janmotkarṣaprayojanaṃ na sattāmātram | evam idam api brahmajanma | ity evaṃ ca pranāḍikayā prakṛtārthastutiḥ | janma ca mātāpitṛkahetukaṃ dṛṣṭam | yato yatra brahmajanmāpi mātāpitarau kalpayati prakṛtasya vyavahitasya vā stutyartham eva | tatra –
tatra yad brahmajanmāsya maujñibandhanavihnitam |
tatrāsya mātā sāvitrī pitā tv ācārya ucyate || 2.170 ||
ādau kāraṇam (?) āha |
[91]
vedapradānād ācāryaṃ pitaraṃ paricakṣate |
sāvitrīṃ ca sādhanasaṃskārabhūtāṃ brahmacāriṇo mātaram evaṃ ca |
na hy asmin yujyate karma kiṃcid ā mauñjibandhanāt || 2.171 ||
adhyayanādi | saiṣā prakṛtārthastutir apareṇa prakāreṇa | tathā cedam āha – svadhābhir vyāhārapratiprasavārtham eva prati sat (evaṃ sati tat – ?) |
nābhivyāhārayed brahma svadhāninayanād ṛte |
nīyate 'nena pitrye karmaṇy udayādi tatsādhanam iti ninayanaṃ svadhāśabda evaṃ ca mantro 'sau pratiprasavasāmarthyāt gamyate | tathā ca kṛtvāyam uditānuvādaḥ kriyate vihitārthastutyai |
śūdreṇa hi samas tāvad yāvad vede na jāyate || 2.172 ||
tathā ca smṛtyantaram: "prāg upanayanāt kāmacāravādabhakṣaḥ" (GDh 2.1) iti | apare (?) "nābhivyāhared" iti prakṛtyantena haratiṃ paṭhanti | tad ayuktaṃ smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt: "na brahmābhivyāhāred anyatra svadhāninayanāt" (GDh 2.9) iti | tasmāt ṇyantena haratir uktaḥ | pitur ayam upadeśaḥ na kumārasya | astu vā kumārasya, prakṛtyantena yadi śāstradoṣo nāsti | idānīṃ "tatra yad brahmajanmāsya mauñjībandhanacihnitam" (MDh 2.170) ityevamādibhyo liṅgebhya upanayanāntaram asya brahmacāriṇā vedādhyayane prāpte idaṃ kramavidhyanuṣṭhānārtham ārabhyate –
kṛtopanayanasyāsya vratādeśanam iṣyate |
brahmaṇo grahaṇaṃ caiva krameṇa vidhipūrvakam || 2.173 ||
[Ed. comment: idaṃ padyaṃ 4.80 saṃkhyākapadyasya vyākhyāyām uddhṛtam | tatra prathamapāde "kṛtopanayanasyāsya" ity atra "na cā[pya?]nupanītasya" iti pāṭhaḥ]
upanayanānantaram asya vratādeśanaṃ yathā vedam ādiṣṭavedavratasya ca yad vedavratācaraṇaṃ tad brahmādhyāyinām ayaṃ krama ācāro vā vidhipūrvam ity etanniyamārtham | adhunā yāny upanayanopadiṣṭāni brahmacārinaś carmasūtramekhalādaṇḍavasanāni teṣāṃ yāvad brahmacaryaṃ dhāraṇe prāpta idam ārabhyate –
yad yasya vihitaṃ carma yat sūtraṃ yā ca mekhalā |
yo daṇḍo yac ca vasanaṃ tat tatd asya vrateṣv api || 2.174 ||
evaṃ ca vratādeśane pūrveṣām utsargaṃ kṛtvā tāny asyā [1 akṣ unclear]pādeyāni | na tu tāny eva dhāryāṇi | pratipattiniyamaś ca teṣāṃ kṛta eva – śāstramekhalā ajinaṃ daṇḍam ityevamādi ca | prakaraṇāc ca [92] brahmacāriṇa idam anyad ucyate yamaniyamopadeśanyāyārtham |
sevetemāṃs tu niyamān brahmacārī gurau vasan |
saṃniyamyendriyagrāmaṃ tapovṛddhyarthaṃ ātmanaḥ || 2.175 ||
niyamagrahaṇa(ṃ)? pradarśanārtham | tathā ca tān api vakṣyati | niyamās tāvad ime tasya bhavanti |
nityaṃ snātvā śuciḥ kuryād devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam |
devatābhyarcanaṃ caiva samidādhānam eva ca || 2.176 ||
nityagrahaṇam ādarānuvādārthaṃ jātidharmārthaṃ vā | kramārthaṃ ca samidādhānasya grahaṇaṃ nāpūrvavidhyartham | niyamās tu –
varjayen madhu māṃsaṃ ca gandhaṃ mālyaṃ rasān striyaḥ |
śuktāni caiva sarvāṇi prāṇināṃ caiva hiṃsanam || 2.177 ||
madhvādīnām atropabhoga[ḥ?] stutyarthaḥ ? | śuktaṃ paruṣavacanam | hiṃsā ca jñāśuṣkasya (?) gatarasasya prakaraṇe pratiṣiddhatvād imāni vacāṃsi pratiyoranne(?) pañcarasaṃ (?) pratiṣedho 'rthatvāt bhavati | tathā ca śrautam (sūtram?): śuktā vācaḥ" (GDh 2.19) iti | kiṃ cānyat –
abhyaṅgam añjanaṃ cākṣnor upānacchatradhāraṇam |
kāmaṃ krodhaṃ ca lobhaṃ ca nartanaṃ gītavādanam || 2.178 ||
abhyaṅgāñjana(?)pratiṣedho 'yaṃ niṣedhād añjanasya sāmarthāt (?) | strīpratiṣedhāt kāmapratiṣedhaḥ | punar anyārtham | arthaprāptānāṃ caiṣāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ |
dyūtaṃ ca janavādaṃ ca parivādaṃ tathānṛtam |
strīṇāṃ ca prekṣaṇālambham upaghātaṃ parasya ca  || 2.179 ||
strīṇāṃ ca prekṣaṇālambham iti bhāvacakṣurnirapātake na (??) sparśane 'sya pratiṣidhyete | ārambhaprayojanam uktam |
ekaḥ śayīta sarvatra
kasya hetoḥ | kathaṃcana ghrāmāpan (?)
na retaḥ skandayet kvacit |
asyārthavādaḥ |
kāmād dhi skandayan reto hinasti vratam ātmanaḥ || 2.180 ||
[93]
asya vyatikramasya prāyaścittam aprakaraṇe 'py ādarārtham |
svapne siktvā brahmacārī dvijaḥ śukram akāmataḥ |
snātvārkam arcayitvā triḥ punar mām ity ṛcaṃ japet || 2.181 ||
brahmacārigrahaṇaṃ prakaraṇāc ca tasyaivedam | vyatikrame prāyaścittaṃ netareṣām | niyamās tu tasya –
udakumbhaṃ sumanaso gośakṛn mṛttikā kuśān |
āhared yāvad arthāni bhakṣaṃ cāhar ahaś caret || 2.182 ||
ācāryārtham ātmārtham ubhayārthaṃ ca vā bhaikṣasya pratyahaṃ grahaṇopadeśaḥ | kālāntarāvasthāyino bhaikṣabhojanasya pratiṣedhārtham uttaravivakṣārthaṃ ca | tebhyaḥ punas tad bhakṣam –
vedayajñair ahīnānāṃ praśastānāṃ svakarmasu |
brahmacāry āhared bhaikṣaṃ gṛhebhyaḥ prayato 'nvaham || 2.183 ||
prayataś cittavākcakṣurbhiḥ | ayaṃ bhaikṣacaraṇasya dvijātibhya ādyaḥ kalpaḥ | tasya viśeṣaṇam |
guroḥ kula na bhikṣeta na jñātikulabandhuṣu |
alābhe tv anyagehānāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ vivarjayet || 2.184 ||
guroḥ kulapratiṣedhaḥ gurukulād apy abhiyācanam arthasiddham | atha vā gurukulam eva gurukulaśabdenābhidhīyate | jñātikulaṃ tu brahmacāriṇaḥ | guror vā sagotrakulaṃ mātṛpitṛtuṣyasaṃbandhānām (?) alābhe 'yagehānām ity asaṃbandhaśrotriyagehānāṃ gurvādīnāṃ pūrvasys pūrvasya varjanam | śāstraprāptasyāyaṃ niyamaḥ | ayaṃ cānukalpaḥ pūrvābhāve |
sarvaṃ vāpi cared grāmaṃ pūrvoktānām asaṃbhave |
niyamya prayto vācam abhiśastāṃs tu varjayet || 2.185 ||
sarvaśabdād ahinustu (?) pratiṣedhāc caturbhyo 'pi varṇebhyo bhaikṣagrahaṇam upadiṣṭam | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "sārvavarṇikaṃ bhaikṣacaraṇam (?) abhiśastapatitavarjam" (GDh 2.35) iti | abhiśaṃsanaṃ copapātakaṃ taduktapratiṣedhe sva(?)mahāpātakibhyo daṇḍāpūpikayā bhavati netarebhyo laghuputvā (?) vāyavyamaru (?) |
dūrād āhṛtya samidhaḥ saṃnidadhyād vihāyasi |
sāyaṃ prātaś ca juhuyāt tābhir agnim atandritaḥ || 2.186 ||
[94]
dūraśabdaḥ (?) parigṛhītārthaḥ sāmarthyāt | saṃnidadhyāt vihāyasīti bhūsaṃsargapratiṣedhārthaḥ | samidhaḥ bahusūkṣmaprāṇyāśrayatvāt | bhuvaḥ prāṇisaṅkrāntisamatsa (?) mā bhūd iti |
akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇam asamidhya ca pāvakam |
anāturaḥ saptarātram avakīrṇivrataṃ caret || 2.187 ||
akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇam utthāyotthāyāvyavahitaṃ saptarātram asamidhya ca pāvakam eva | na sarvāvasthaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | anāturaḥ sann anayor anindhanabhakṣacaraṇayoḥ pratyekavyatikrame saty ayam avakīrṇivrataṃ caret | cittenāpy (?) anākṛta iti kecit | tad etad aprasiddhaṃ vicāryate | kim idaṃ vyatikramaprāyaścittopadeśaparaṃ vākyam, yathā śrūyate, āho svid ayaṃ gauravopadeśanārtham | atrocyate | nedaṃ prāyaścittavidhānam | kiṃ tarhi | saptarātravidhyarho 'yam arthavādaḥ, aprakaraṇāt prāyaścittasya | naitau vyatikramāv upapātakamadhyapaṭhitau | naitāsām (?) anyād idam atra prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃcit kalpyeta | evaṃ ca saty avakīrṇigrahaṇam asya gurutvajñāpanārtham īdṛśam etad akāryaṃ yenāvakīrṇiprāyaścittam atra caritavyam iti prāyaścittapakṣe 'pi saṃdhyopāsanādipratikramaprāyaścittopadeśavad ayam aprakaraṇo 'pi ca tadupadeśo 'yaṃ vijñeyaḥ ādarārthaḥ | asmiṃś ca pakṣe yat kaiścid ucyate – "āturasya sāmānyavihitaṃ prāyaścittam asti | yadi hy ātursaya na kiṃcit prāyaścittaṃ bhaven māṃsabhakṣaṇādiṣv anyatamaṃ (?) na syāt | na caitad iṣṭaṃ niyatasmṛtyā sarvasya sarvadādhikṛtatvāt | nāturavarjam adhikāra upapadyate | na ca nityo 'dhikāram anityam āturaraṃ prekṣya (?) pākṣikaḥ sādhayitum (?) | na cāgnīndhanabhaikṣyacaraṇayoḥ kevalayor āturasyādoṣasmṛtir ākasmikī yuktā" iti | atra brūmaḥ | śastralakṣaṇatvād adhikārasya yathāśāstram adhikāro vijñeyaḥ, nānumānataḥ | yatra hy āturasyādhikārapratiṣedhaśāstram asti, tatrādau nādhikriyate yathānyatra "nāturo na mahāniśi" (MDh 4.129), "anāturaḥ svāni khāni" (MDh 4.144) | kiṃ ca yatra tv asya tan nāsti tatrāsāv adhikāraśāstrād eva tasminn adhikṛto veditavyaḥ | evaṃ ca saty atra nāsti yathoktanyāyāvakāśaḥ | yataś caitad evam ataḥ –
bhaikṣeṇa vartayan nityaṃ naikānnādī bhaved vratī |
bhaikṣeṇa vratino vṛttir upavāsasamā smṛtā || 2.188 ||
atrocyate | "bhaikṣaṃ cāhar ahaś caret" (MDh 2.182) iti bhaikṣaṃ na vṛtteḥ siddhatvāt | nanv idam anārabhyam | niyamārthas tv ārambhaḥ | katham | vidyamānānnasyāpi brahmacāriṇo bhaikṣeṇaiva vṛttir yathā syāt | ārambhaḥ pratigrahādiṣu iti yaḥ śāstrasyāsaṃbhave sati niyamasyāvidyamānatvād athābhāvaḥ arthavādārthaṃ vā punaḥ śrutiḥ: "bhaikṣeṇa vratino vṛttir upavāsasamā smṛtā" iti | bhaikṣavṛttyupasaṃhārārtho [95] vāyam agnīndhanād vā bhaikṣasya gurutvajñāpanārthaḥ punar ārambhaḥ | pratiṣiddhaikānnādanapratiprasvārthasamaṃ vedaṃ punar abhidhānam | tathā ca rabhe (?) |
vratavad devadaivatye pitrye karmaṇy atharṣivat |
kāmam abhyarthino 'śnīyād vratam asya no lupyate || 2.189 ||
vratam arhatīti vratavān | kiṃ tv anyamadhumāṃsavarjitam aśanaṃ tad aśnīyāt | kasmin | devadaivatye | devā devatā yasya tad devadaivatyam abhyudayaśrāddhaṃ tasminn abhyarthitaḥ san mātāmahādīnāṃ "vratastham api dauhitraṃ kāmaṃ śrāddhe niyojayet" (MDh 3.234) ity anena śāstreṇa bhuñjānasyaikānnam api tat parivarjitaṃ vratam asya na hinastīti saṃsṛtiprabhṛtyarthā evaṃ ca grāmāśanam uttaratrāraṇyopadeśād vijñeyam | pitaro devatā yasya tat pitryaṃ karmapiṇḍānvāhāryakādiśrāddham atrarṣivan munyannam āraṇyaṃ yathoktaviśeṣaṇam | evaṃ ca sati na daivapitryaśrāddhāśrayeyaṃ grāmyāraṇyaikānnadānādivyavasthā brahmacāriṇaḥ yena tad ity etad asamartham | neiṣopadeśapakṣe tāpasānnaviśeṣaṇam | atopi jñāyate nedam āraṇyopadeśārthaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ saṃbhavati | evaṃ ca kṛtvā grāmym āraṇyaṃ vā yad vratāviruddhaṃ tat prārthitaḥ śrāddhayor evāśnīyāt ekāhnaṃ nānyatra (?) | evaṃ daivapitryagrahaṇam arthavad bhavati | kecit tu grāmyāraṇyāśane daivapitryor yathoktaviśeṣaṇe viṣayavibhāgena varṇayanti daive grāmyaṃ vratavat pitrye munyannaṃ tat punar yamanyāyam iti vicāritaṃ brahmacāritrayaprakaraṇam | idānīṃ brāhmaṇavat kṣatriyavaiśyayor api brahmacāriṇaḥ śrāddhaikānnabhojane prāpte idaṃ tatpratiṣedhāyārabhyat –
brāhmaṇasyaiva karmaitat upadiṣṭaṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
rājanyavaiśyayos tv evaṃ naitat karma vidhīyate || 2.190 ||
na ca siddham evedaṃ pūrvatra "yasyāsyena sadāśnanti" (MDh 1.95) ityevamādyaiḥ | tadapekṣaṃ hi tad vijñeyaṃ pratiṣedhavidhitvād asya brāhmaṇastutiprakaraṇopadeśasāmarthyāc ca | tasyety upasaṃhrtya brahmacāriṇo vṛttim idam anyat tatprakaraṇa evocyate 'sya |
codito guruṇā nityam apracodita eva vā |
kuryād adhyayane yogam ācāryasya hiteṣu ca || 2.191 ||
viṣayārthaṃ punar ārambhaḥ |
śarīraṃ caiva vācaṃ ca buddhīndriyamanāṃsi ca ||
niyamya prāñjalis tiṣṭhed vīkṣamāṇo guror mukham || 2.192 ||
[96]
ayam adhyayanakāle 'nyadā ca gurusaṃnidhau vidhiḥ | ayaṃ cānyas tatkālaḥ (?) |
nityam uddhṛtapāṇiḥ syāt sādhvācāraḥ susaṃyataḥ |
āsyatām iti coktaḥ sann āsītābhimukhaṃ guroḥ || 2.193 ||
anujñāta upaviśed ity arthaḥ | ayaṃ tv api sārvatrika eva vidhiḥ | idaṃ cānyat – vratadarmam api sat sadāgrahaṇād yāvajjīvaṃ draṣṭavyam |
hīnānnavastraveṣaḥ syāt sarvadā gurusaṃnidhau |
uttiṣṭhet prathamaṃ cāsya caramaṃ caiva saṃviśet || 2.194 ||
suṣupsor anyatra vāyaṃ cānyo vratadharmaḥ |
pratiśravaṇasaṃbhāṣe śayāno na samācaret |
nāsīno na ca bhuñjāno na tiṣṭhan na parāṅmukhaḥ || 2.195 ||
kiṃ tarhi |
āsīnasya sthitaḥ kuryād abhigacchaṃs tu tiṣṭhataḥ |
pratyudgamya tv āvrajataḥ paścād dhāvaṃs tu dhāvataḥ || 2.196 ||
parāṅmukhasyābhimukho dūrasthasyaitya cāntikam |
praṇamya tu śayānasya videśe[nideśe] caiva tiṣṭhataḥ || 2.197 ||
nīcaṃ śayyāsanaṃ cāsya nityaṃ syād gurusaṃnidhau |
guros tu cakṣurviṣaye na yatheṣṭhāsano bhavet || 2.198 ||
nodāhared asya nāma parokṣam api kevalam |
na caivāsyānukurvīta gatibhāṣitaceṣṭitam || 2.199 ||
brahmacāriprakaraṇopadiṣṭā api santa ete yāvajjīvaṃ pratyetavyāḥ |
guror yatra parīvādo nindā vāpi pravartate |
karṇau tatra pidhātavyau gantavyaṃ vā tato 'nyataḥ || 2.200 ||
parivādanindayor guruviṣayayor aśravaṇopadeśaparam idam | "na caivāsyānukurvīta gatibhāṣitaceṣṭtiam" (2.199) ity asya pūrvavidheḥ śeṣaḥ | kathaṃ kṛtvā | yatra guroḥ parivādaśravaṇam api tāvan nānumanyate | tatra gatyādyanukaraṇam ātmanā kṛtaḥ | kaḥ punar anayoḥ parivādanindayor viśeṣaḥ | parivādaḥ prakṛtaśarīraceṣṭānukathanam | nindā tu "asmadguruḥ ajāgarūkaḥ [97] anadhyāyādhyāpanaśīlaḥ tarjanādiduḥkhahetuś ca" iti | atha vā śiṣṭākaraṇasyānukathanaṃ parivādaḥ | nindā pratiṣiddhasevanam | tathā ca śabdārthaḥ | atha evāyaṃ padārtho gurvabhipretetaraḥ aprakṛṣṭaḥ parivādaḥ, prakṛṣṭo nindā (?) | avidyamānadoṣānukathanaṃ vā parivādaḥ | vidyamānadoṣasaṃkīrtanaṃ vā nindā | evam ayaṃ pūrvavidhiviśeṣaḥ ślokaḥ | atha vā guroḥ parivādanindāś ca guṇapratiṣedhārtham anyed eva vidhyantaram | kasya punar hetoḥ | aśravaṇam etayoḥ yataḥ pratiṣedhārthaviśeṣaṇārtham idaṃ satyavratārtham ācaṣṭe —
parivādāt kharo bhavati śvā vai bhavati nindakaḥ |
paribhoktā kṛmir bhavati kīṭo bhavati matsarī || 2.201 ||
śravaṇaṃ guruparivādaḥ pratiṣiddham anyaḥ karoti arthāt nirdiśyate parivādāt kharo bhavatīti | katham idam ucyate | adhyāhāreṇa vyākheyaṃ prakṛtārthānugrahāya | parivādāc chrutāt kharo bhavatīti | evamādi sāmarthyāt | atha vā guroḥ parivādādiśravaṇaṃ sa imā na eṣāṃ kartavyāsu bhavati (??) ity evaṃ vedaṃ vyākhyeyam | paribhogamatsarayos tarhi pūrvatrāpratiṣiddhayoḥ katham idaṃ nindāvacanam ucyate | yathaiva nākasmāt stutiḥ pravartata iti kṛtvā | stutvā vidhir anumīyate | lokaśāstrayor evam asmān nindā na parvartata iti | ihāpi nindayā pratiṣedho 'numātavyaḥ | pūrvatra paribhogamatsarau tatara (?) kriyāpi taddṛṣṭāntārthaṃ vaikayor (?) vacanaṃ yathaiva paribhogamatsarau kuvaṃs tiryakṣūtpadyate evaṃ guroḥ parivādaninde kurvan kharayonim āpadyata ity anyaṃ ca phalārthavādaḥ | yadi śāntyanyayor hy aviruddho 'tha tv asya tābhyām aviruddhas tata eṣa phalavidhir jñeyaḥ | kiṃ ca —
dūrastho nārcayed enaṃ na kruddho nāntike striyāḥ |
gandhamālyādipreṣaṇe | na sāvajñam | kiṃ tarhi | gatvā svayam arcanīyaḥ | na kruddho 'nyasya gurau tu nāstiko(ke) 'dhaḥprāptiḥ śāstrakathanāt tv antike striyāḥ gurupatnyāḥ |
yānāsanasthaś caivainam avaruhyābhivādayet || 2.202 ||
prativāte 'nuvāte ca nāsīta guruṇā saha |
yeneha yathānyasyopaghātaḥ |
asaṃśrave caiva guror
viprakṛṣṭadeśasthaḥ |
na kiṃcid api kīrtayet || 2.203 ||
mā bhūd asya cittasaṃkṣobhaḥ |
[98]
go'śvoṣṭrayānaprāsādaprastareṣu kaṭeṣu ca |
āsīta guruṇā sārdhaṃ śilāphalakanauṣu ca || 2.204 ||
"nāsīta guruṇā saha" (2.203) ity anena pratiṣiddhasya pratyuddhārārtham idam | śiṣyavṛddhyarthā ekādaśaślokā nigadavyākhyātāḥ | adhvaryana(?) saṃbandhe sati kāryasāmānyāt apratino (?) 'pīyaṃ granthārthaḥ śiṣyasya vṛttiḥ syāt | gurau idānīṃ gurvapadiṣṭā viṣttis tat gurau na prāpnoti | śāstrābhāvāt tatre(tro?)hyate ca gurāv api tadvṛttir yata idam ārabhyate |
guror gurau saṃnihite guruvad vṛttim ācaret |
na tu gurumūḍhadatvā (?) guruguror yatra tatrasthasyeti |
na cānisṛṣṭo guruṇā svān gurūn abhivādayet || 2.205 ||
vidyāgurumātṛpitṛsaṃnidhāne vikalpa uktas tena svān gurūn ity etad anyatra mātā pitṛbhyām iti gamyate | pitṛvyādau gurāv iti |
vidyāguruṣv evam eva nityā vṛttiḥ svayoniṣu |
pratiṣedhatsu cādharmād dhitaṃ copadiśatsv api || 2.206 ||
dṛṣṭvā svayoniṣu jñātiṣu pitrādiṣu cānityā vṛttiḥ svābhāvikī vidyāpradānam antareṇāpi syāt | saṃnikarṣaviprakarṣapikṣayā (?) ca vṛttiviśeṣaṇas teṣu parikalpyaḥ | "hitaṃ copadiśatsv api" iti dṛṣṭārthaviṣayasaṃbandhena vedyaviṣayam etat |
śreyaḥsu guruvad vṛttiṃ nityam eva samācaret |
guruputre tathācārye guroś caiva svabandhuṣu || 2.207 ||
kimarthaṃ punar idam asaṃbandheṣu śreyaḥsu guruvad vṛttinā prāpto 'pi śāstrābhāvād iṣyate cānyasyāpīty ārambhaḥ | śreyonimittāni cādhyayanābhijanasaṃskāravijñānācārakarmāṇi |
bālaḥ samānajanmā vā śiṣyo vā yajñakarmaṇi |
adhyāpayan gurusuto guruvan mānam arhati || 2.208 ||
jīvati guruāv etad ucyate | itaratra tu vakṣyati "ācārye tu khalu prete" (MDh 2.247) iti | brahmacāriṇaḥ ātmāvṛddhyapekṣayā (?) bālaḥ samānajanmā cocyate | kaḥ punar asau | gurusutaḥ śiṣyo vā yajñakarmaṇi | atra vāśabdaḥ sāmarthyād uttaratra draṣṭavyaḥ | śiṣyo yajñakarmaṇi cānyasmin yākāraṇādau (?) yajñakarmaguṇedaṃ (?) yajñakarmārthaṃ śāstraṃ yajñavidyocyate tādarthyāt | tuṇato 'py artho vā | pañcāśabdaḥ (?) kathaṃ śiṣyo 'pi san yajñakarmaṇi | atra yathāsthāna eva vāśabdaṃ yajñakarmagrahaṇaṃ [99] ca sarvāṅgapradarśanārtham asmin pakṣe draṣṭavyaḥ | śiṣyo 'pi san yajñakarmaṇy atra cādhyāpayan (?) vedādhyāpanasamarthaś ced adhyāpayatu vā mā vā | anyaṃ sarvathaidadhītavedaṃ (?) copalakṣaṇaparam | tataḥ kim | evaṃguṇo yo gurusutaḥ so 'ṅgavidyāyām etasyānvayo bahvīṣu bādhiṣyopi (?) san gurusaṃmānan aśeṣam arhati na tathānyo guruputra ity etad ārambhasāmarthyād vijñāyate | evaṃ ca viśeṣārthasyārambhaḥ anyanivṛttyartho vā guruputreṣu cācāryeṣv etad iti etasmād vai viśeṣavidhānād idam eva jñāpakaṃ granthaguror arthagurur jyāyān iti | yuktaṃ caitat granthasya tadarthatvāt | evaṃ ca karmasv adhikārasāmarthyaṃ śāstracodanasāmarthyād iti | idānīṃ prakaraṇāt guṇavati guruputre sarvsyāṃ pūjāyāṃ (?) prāptāyām idam ārabhyate |
utsādanaṃ ca gātrāṇāṃ snāpanocchiṣṭabhojane |
na kuryād guruputrasya pādayoś cāvanejanam || 2.209 ||
"gurusuto 'pi guruvan mānam arhati" (MDh 2.208) ity asyāpavādo 'yaṃ vijñeyaḥ | etad eva ca jñāpakam | guror etāni kāryāṇi pūrvaśāstrānupdiṣṭāny api santīti | tathā ca guṇaprabandhābhāvenāpy atyartham idaṃ nivṛttau pradhānataḥ tasya sākṣāc chravaṇād itaratra guṇato 'numānāt | evaṃ ca yat kaiścid ucyate | adhāhārārthaṃ (?) vākyam iti | tadasanāt (?) gurūpadiṣṭaśruśrūṣāvidhir (?) uktavyatiriktam anyatra na prāpnoti | iṣyate ca | syād ity ārambhaḥ |
guruvat pratipūjyāḥ syuḥ savarṇā guruyoṣitaḥ |
asavarṇās tu saṃpūjyāḥ pratyutthānābhivādanaiḥ || 2.210 ||
yathānyāyaṃ vyastasamastaiḥ kanīyasyo 'pi satyā ātmanaḥ prativayasyastriya iti svanā(?) guruvat pratipūjyās tv ity asmāt sarvam upadiṣṭaṃ guruvṛttam āsuprāptaṃ (?) sat pratiṣidhyate |
abhyañjanaṃ snāpanaṃ ca gātrotsādanam eva ca |
gurupatnyā na kāryāṇi keśānāṃ ca prasādhanam || 2.211 ||
caśabdāt taducchiṣṭabhojanaṃ ca pādāvanejanaṃ tad asyāṃ pādasparśanapratiṣedhād evābhivādanakāle so 'pūpiyocāryate (daṇḍāpūpikayocyate??) |
gurupatnī tu yuvatir nābhivādyeha pādayoḥ |
pūrṇaviṃśativarṣeṇa guṇadoṣau vijānatā || 2.212 ||
viśeṣapratiṣedho 'yam | pūrva uttaraś ca pūrṇaviṃśativarṣeṇa guṇadoṣajñena nānyadety arthaḥ | asyārthavādaḥ | [100]
svabhāva eṣa nārīṇāṃ narāṇām iha dūṣaṇam |
ato 'rthān na pramādyanti pramadāsu vipaścitaḥ || 2.213 ||
tatprayatnapūrvakavyāpāro vinyasya saṃyatātmano vyabhicāro mā bhūd ity ayam upadeśaḥ | yena kāreṇena |
avidvāṃsam alaṃ loke vidvāṃsam api vā punaḥ |
pramadā hy utpathaṃ netuṃ kāmakrodhavaśānugam || 2.214 ||
etasmāt kāraṇāt –
mātrā svasrā duhitrā vā na viviktāsano bhavet |
balavān indriyagrāmo vidvāṃsam api kaeṣati || 2.215 ||
etasmād eva kāraṇāt dūrasthaḥ |
kāmaṃ tu gurupatnīnāṃ yuvatīnāṃ yuvā bhuvi |
vidhivad vandanaṃ kuryād asāv aham iti bruvan || 2.216 ||
viproṣya pādagrahaṇam anvahaṃ cābhivādanam |
gurudāreṣu kurvīta satāṃ dharmam anusmaran || 2.217 ||
iyad jānāti viduṣo brahmacāriṇaḥ proṣitapratyāgatasya kadācid aṣṭau (?) ślokā gurudāreṣu śiṣyavṛttyarthā api santaḥ sarvastrīviṣayāḥ pratyetavyāḥ | kimarthaṃ yapana(dupa?)ri guruvat putradāreṣu śuśrūṣopadeśayatnaḥ kriyate, āgamāt | yasmād evam āha |
yathā khanan khanitreṇa naro vāry adhigacchati |
tathā gurugatāṃ vidyāṃ śuśrūṣur adhigacchati || 2.218 ||
gurvādiśuśrūṣāvidhyanuṣṭhānaśeṣaḥ ślokaḥ (4 akṣ lacuna) raś ca |
muṇḍo vā jaṭilo vā syād atha vā syāc chikājaṭaḥ |
brahmacāriputradharmā hy ete yāvadbhāvinaḥ, nopanayanadharmāḥ |
nainaṃ grāme 'bhinimlocat sūryo nābhyudiyāt kvacit || 2.219 ||
grāmasukhāt saṅgo laukiko vā tatra vasanto brahmacāriṇaḥ | sūryo nāstaṃ yāyād vāraṇye tasyodayam astam ayaṃ ca saṃbhāvayed ity evaṃ tarhi niyamagauravārtham upadiśyate, avaktavyam api sad yathāgnīndhanabhaikṣacaraṇe | anyathedaṃ niyamavyatikramānukathanam asati projane nārthakatvaṃ(?) prāpnoti | tathā [101] cāyaṃ prāyaścittopadeśaḥ | aprakaraṇe 'pi tatprayatnagauravapradarśanārtho na svārthaḥ |
taṃ ced abhyudiyāt sūryaḥ śayanaṃ kāmacārataḥ |
nimloced vāpy avijñānāj japann upavased dinam || 2.220 ||
pūrvasaṃdhyāvyatikrame paścimasaṃdhyāvyatikrame rātrau bhuktityāgo japaś ca | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "sūryābhyudito brahmacārī tiṣṭhed ahar bhuñjāno 'bhyastamitaś ca rātriṃ japan sāvitrīm" (GDh 23.21) iti | apare tv āhuḥ | "japan upavased dinam" iti ubhayasaṃdhyāvyatikrame paścimasaṃdhyā vyatikrame 'pi pratyekaṃ tac cānena smṛtyantareṇa na saṃbadhyate | idaṃ cānyat prakṛtārthasāmarthyābhidhānāya tadupo[d]balakaṃ śiṣyate |
suryeṇa hy abhinimluktaḥ śayāno 'bhyuditaś ca yaḥ |
prāyaścittam akurvāṇo yuktaḥ syān manatainasā || 2.221 ||
prāyaścittārthavādo 'yaṃ saṃdhyopāsanasya tadanuṣṭhānābhiniveśaprayojanaḥ yataś caitad evam atas tatparihārārtham |
ācamya prayato nityam ubhe saṃdhye samāhitaḥ |
śucau deśe japañ japyam upāsīta yathāvidhi || 2.222 ||
prakaraṇān niyamagauravārtha evāyaṃ saṃdhyopāsanasya punar upadeśaḥ upasaṃhāro vāyaṃ prakaraṇānte stutirūpaḥ saṃdhypāsananiyamasyānuṣṭhānādarārthaḥ | bahirgrāmadeśagaṇavidhisambandhena vā idam asya punar abhidhānam āśramabrahmacaryārtham | tathā coktam atra: "śucau deśe japañ japyam upāsīta yathāvidhi" iti | vedagrahaṇaṃ brahmacāriṇa eva vā vayovibhāgena sāmathyapikṣayā (sāmarthyāpekṣayā?) tapoviśeṣārthaṃ saṃdhyopāsanopadeśaḥ |
yadi strī yady avarajaḥ śreyaḥ kiṃcit samācaret |
tat sarvam ācared yukto yatra vāsya ramen manaḥ || 2.223 ||
strīśudramantrāpraibaddham api (?) sac chreyo dharmākhyaṃ yadi kiṃcit samācaret kutascid āgamayya agranthakaṃ (?) tatsarvam ācaret ācaritum asya yukto 'sau dharmaśraddhayā kiṃ punaḥ śrutasakāśāt ity adhikāravirodhe sati strīśūdrād apy āgamayya yuktaḥ svadharmo 'nuṣṭhātum | kiṃ punar guror granthakaḥ śiṣṭācārād anyasmād vā iti | smṛtiparam etat, brahmacāridharmāṇām anuktadharmasaṃgrahaparaṃ vā | "yatra vāsya ramen manaḥ" | tad abhyased vihitaṃ svādhyāyaśuśrūṣendriyajayādinā vihitam | viśeṣārthaḥ punar ayam upadeśaḥ | anyathāvihitavidhānād akauśalaprasaṅgaḥ syāt | [102] dharmaśāstraprayoktā | "yadi strī yady avarajaḥ śreyaḥ kiṃcit samācaret" ity atra dharmaḥ śreyān ity uktam | tat kim eṣa eva puruṣārtha iti | neti | ubhe (?) | kiṃ tarhi |
dharmārthāv ucyate śreyaḥ kāmārthau dharma eva ca |
artha eveha vā śreyas trivarga iti tu sthitiḥ || 2.224 ||
arthasukhayor anujñātārtham idam ārabhyate | kathaṃ dharmāvirodhe sati nātyantaṃ niḥsukhaḥ syād evaṃ nātyantam arthatāpekṣyate | parasparāpekṣatvād eteṣām | tathā ca vakṣyati:
parityajed arthakāmau yau syātāṃ dharmavivarjitau |
dharmaṃ cāpy asukhodarkaṃ lokasaṅkruṣṭam eva ca || (MDh 4.176)
iti | saptaprakāraprapañcanidarśanārthaś ca puruṣasya pañcatayopadeśaḥ | ācāryādīnām avamānapratiṣedhārtham idam adhunocyate, śuśrūṣāśeṣatayā svatantraṃ vā |
ācārayaś ca pitā caiva mātā bhrātā ca pūrvajaḥ |
nārtenāpy avamantavyā brāhmaṇena viśeṣataḥ || 2.225 ||
brahmacāriṇām upadiṣṭo 'py ayaṃ dharmaḥ sāmarthyād itareṣām api draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca liṅgāni vakṣyati | nāvamantavyā ity asya vidheḥ stutyartham idam ārabhyate | yasmāt (?) |
ācāryo brahmaṇo mūrtiḥ pitā mūrtiḥ prajāpateḥ |
mātā pṛthvyā mūrtis tu bhrātā svo mūrtir ātmanaḥ || 2.226 ||
yaṃ mātāpitarau kleśaṃ sahete saṃbhave nṛṇām |
na tasya niṣkṛtiḥ śakyā kartuṃ varṣaśatair api || 2.227 ||
sambhavakārye sthitivṛddhisuravā(ṣu aśa?)kayasambhavo guṇataḥ te hi mātāpitroḥ śleṣa(kleśa?)sādhye na sambhavaḥ (?) evam ācāryo viśiṣyānuśāsane kleśānubhavād anupadiṣṭo py atra samānakāryatvāt gṛhyate prakṛtatvād uttaratra varṇācaraṇabhrātā (?) | yataś caitad evam ataḥ |
tayor nityaṃ priyaṃ kuryād ācāryasya ca sarvadā |
na kevalaṃ nāvamantavyā priyaṃ teṣām aparaṃ kartavyam | priyagrahaṇaṃ ca hitadarśanārtham asya stutir uttaratra ślokārthaḥ |
teṣv eva triṣu tuṣṭeṣu tapaḥ sarvaṃ samāpyate || 2.228 ||
yata etad evaṃ kartavyaṃ nānyad ity arthavādaḥ (?) | kiṃ ca teṣāṃ priyaṃ śuśrūṣā | yad ayam āha [103]
teṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ śuśrūṣā paramaṃ tapa ucyate |
na tair anabhyanujñāto dharmam anyaṃ samācaret || 2.229 ||
smṛtimātram etan na tv anyadharmācārapratiṣedhaḥ, tadupadeśasāmarthyāt | yataś caitati –
ta eva hi trayo lokās ta eva traya āśramāḥ |
ta eva hi trayo vedās ta evoktās trayo 'gnayaḥ || 2.230 ||
kiṃcit sāmānyād ayam agnivibhāgaḥ | tatpratiṣedhārtham idam ārabhyate ||

[76]
pitā vai gārhaptyo 'gnir mātāgnir dakṣiṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
gurur āhavanīyas tu sāgitretā garīyasī || 2.231 ||
evaṃ ca sati |
triṣv apramādyann eteṣu trīn lokān vijayed gṛhī |
dīpyamānaḥ svavapuṣā devavad divi modate || 2.232 ||
ayaṃ punar loke vibhāgaḥ kiṃcit sāmānyād eva stutyatiśayārthaḥ |
imaṃ lokaṃ mātṛbhaktyā pitṛbhaktyā tu madhyamam |
guruśuśrūṣayā tv evaṃ brahmalokaṃ samaśnute || 2.233 ||
evaṃ ca śraddadhānasya (?) |
sarve tasyādṛtā dharmā yasyaite traya ādṛtāḥ |
anādṛtās tu yasyaite sarvās tasyāphalāḥ kriyāḥ || 2.234 ||
anyakriyā naiṣphalyābhidhānam idaṃ vidhistutyartham upadeśasāmarthād ity uktam | tathā ca |
yāvat trayas te jīveyus tāvan nānyaṃ samācaret |
teṣv eva nityaṃ śuśrūṣāṃ kuryāt priyahite rataḥ || 2.235 ||
kiṃ cānyat |
teṣām anuparodhena pāratryaṃ yad yad ācaret |
tat tan nivedayet tebhyo manovacanakarmabhiḥ || 2.236 ||
yasmāt –
triṣv eteṣv itikrtyaṃ hi puruṣasya samāpyate |
eṣa dharmaḥ paraḥ sākṣād upadharmo 'nya ucyate || 2.237 ||

stutiparam etat | kiṃ cānyat | pūrvatroktaṃ brahmacāriṇo brāhmaṇasyādhyayanaṃ tat punar uktavyatirekeṇa kṣatriyavaiśyābhyām aprāptaṃ tad vidhīyate |
śraddadhānaḥ śubhāṃ vidyām ādadītāvarād api |
antyād api paraṃ dharmaṃ strīratnaṃ duṣkulād api || 2.238 ||
śraddadhāno 'bhivādādeḥ śreya ity evaṃ sthitaḥ prājñaḥ (?) | śobhate 'nayā ātmeti śubhā vedavidyā | tām ādadītāparād api | kṣatriyād vaiśyād vā | na tu śūdrād iti vakṣyāmaḥ | asya dṛṣṭāntārthavādaḥ | uktaṃ hi purastād "antyād api paraṃ dharmam" ādadīta smārtam ādadīta "strīratnaṃ duṣkulād api" | yasmād ādadīta iti | "yadi strī yady avarajaḥ" (MDh 2.223) ity atra coktam evaitat | yathā strī śūdrād api hīnā smārtadharmopādānam evaṃ "śraddadhānaḥ śubhāṃ vidyām ādadītāvarād api" | kṣatriyād vaiśyād veti vakṣyāmaḥ | atha vā dṛṣṭārthasaṃbandhenedaṃ dṛṣṭāntadvayaṃ pūrvavidher arthavādīkriyate | kuśataḥ (?) antyād api śūdrāt paraṃ dharmaṃ dṛṣṭārthapuruṣārthahetuṃ vyavasthārūpaṃ laukikaṃ strīratnaṃ cāvaidhavyādiguṇahetulakṣaṇasampadā dṛṣṭāntārthavādakaṃ hīnakriyād evādadīta na tu pātakikulādibhyo yathaivaṃ brahmacāry api śraddadhānaḥ prativiśiṣṭam adṛṣṭapuruṣārthasādhanabhūtāṃ vedavidyāṃ hīnād apy (?) ādadīteti vākyārthaḥ | tad vai spaṣṭārtham idam | adhunocyate lokapravādaḥ |
viṣād apy amṛtaṃ grāhyaṃ bālād api subhāṣitam |
amitrād apy sadvṛttam amedhyād api kāñcanam || 2.239 ||
yathāvocāma evaṃ lokapravādo 'py ayaṃ dṛṣṭaḥ | tad api prāyas tv evam: "asataḥ sad upādeyam" iti | evaṃ ca sati |
striyo ratnāny atho vidyā dharmaḥ śaucaṃ subhāṣitam |
vividhāni ca śilpāni samādeyāni sarvataḥ || 2.240 ||
evaṃ prakaraṇārthopasaṃhāraḥ prakṛtārthaśeṣatayā | yatheme pratyakṣaphalatvād asadāśrayā api santa upādīyante, evaṃ vidyāpy adṛṣṭārthatayārthavādo 'yam (?) | na hetuḥ | atra ca viṣādiḥ na vṛttiprayojanād dṛṣṭārtho gṛhyate | dharme stryādibhir dṛṣṭārthair anayoḥ samānādhikaraṇyāt | ratnaśabdaḥ ca strīvidyādharmaśaucasubhāṣitaśilpānāṃ puruṣārthasādhanavidyāpekṣayā stutyartho lokavad vijñeyaḥ | adhunā brāhmaṇābhyām ātmecchayā brahmacāriṇo vidyādhyayane prāpte idaṃ niyamārtham āpatkālaguṇavidhānaṃ (?) pradṛśyate (prastūyat?) |
[78]
abrāhmaṇād adhyayanam āpatkāle vidhīyate |
anuvrajyā ca śuśrūṣā yāvad adhyayanaṃ guroḥ || 2.241 ||
abrāhmaṇād ity ayam anuvādaḥ āpatkālaguṇavidhisaṃbandhena | ko 'yam abrāhmaṇo nāma (1 akṣ lacuna) rnnādeyam | tathā ca cūrṇikākāraḥ: "na yavayuktam anyādhikaraṇe tathā vyartha" iti | śūdrasyāpy adhyayanādhikārād vā svīkaraṇasaṃbhave (?) katham asmād adhyayanam asya nyāyāt | athāsaṃbhave sati tadadhyayanam aviśyaṃ śūdrād bhaviṣyati | evaṃ tarhi, liṅgān nāma śūdrād abrāhmaṇād adhyayanam anujānāti | vakṣyati śrāddhaprakaraṇe "śūdraśiṣyo guruś ca" (MDh 3.156) iti | pāriśeṣyād abrāhmaṇābhyāṃ kṣatriyavaiśyābhyām adhyayanam āpatkāle vidhīyate | avaśayakaraṇīyasya atikrama āpatkālaḥ | tatrāyaṃ vidhir abhāvavaikalpiko draṣṭavyaḥ nātmecchayāpy āpadi | kṣepaṃ paṭhanti | teṣām iyaṃ vyākhyā: "āpadi kalpyate samartho bhavati nānyadā yaḥ sa āpatkalpaḥ | atha vāpadvidhir āpatkalpaḥ | anuvṛddhyā tuṃ (?) śuśrūṣā anyaśuśrūṣāpratiṣedho 'yaṃ prasiddhyapunarvacanāt | na cedaṃ pravāra(pāṭha?)kādhyayanakāle 'nuvrajyājñānaṃ tathārthasiddhatvād avacanīyaṃ (?) syād yatas taduttarakālam idarthavad (?) veditavyam | tathā ca "yāvad adhyayanaṃ guroḥ" ity etat snānottarakālaṃ tannivṛttisamarthaṃ bhavati | na prapāṭhādhyayanakāle | pratyahamadhyayanaśabdena cādhyayanakāryaṃ grahaṇam ucyate | evaṃ ca yad vedagrahaṇaṃ yāvad adhyayanam idaṃ vijñeyam | atha vā "śraddadhānaḥ śubhāṃ vidyām" (MDh 2.238) iti nedaṃ vedavidyāgrahaṇam atra śiṣyate | kiṃ tarhi | viṣayam ahutāśanivyāvṛttimantro (?) dṛṣṭārtha ucyate | evaṃ ca nedaṃ brāhmaṇādhyayanavidhyartham | kiṃ tarhi | vikṣyamāṇayor vidhiśeṣo 'yarthavādaḥ (?) | evam uttarāv api dṛṣṭāntau vijñeyau | dṛṣṭārthasaṃbandhenaiva "antyād api paraṃ dharmaṃ strīratnaṃ duṣkulād api" (MDh 2.238) iti kṛtavyākhyānau dṛṣṭāntau | tathā ca lokavādaḥ: "viṣād apy amṛtaṃ grāhyam" (MDh 2.239) iti | ayaṃ ca ślokokter asaṃbandhārthaḥ (??) | stryodayānyartho (?) vidyeti cārthavādaprakaraṇārtham | adhunā tu nispaṣṭīkaroti | yathā caite evam abrāhmaṇād adhyayanaṃ vidhīyate | evaṃ ca (na?) pūrvoktārthavādam anuvādhaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | vidhiḥ | tathā coktam | tac ca punar adhyayanam asya syād āpatkālenānyatraivam idam ubhayavidhānam adhyayanakriyāyāḥ kālasya ca vākyabhede saty abrāhmaṇād adhyayanān naiṣṭhikasya (?) yāvajjīvam | tatrāpatsthitau (?) prāptāyām idam | na śāstre asya pratyabhivādalakṣaṇasyedaṃ rūpodāharaṇam | āyuṣmān bhava (?) saumya devadatta iti | stutyartham idam adhunādyocyate ||
[back to 103]
nābrāhmaṇe gurau śiṣyo vāsam ātyantikaṃ vaset |
brāhmaṇe vānanūcāne kāṅkṣan gatim anuttamam || 2.242 ||
śāstrād arthāc (?) cāprāptasya vidher ayaṃ (?) pratiṣedhaḥ | ya evaṃ ca naiṣṭhikasya eva duśśuśrūṣugurur (?) brāhmaṇa eva syān na jātimātra evaṃ tarhi abhijanmādiguṇavān iti |
yadi tv ātyantikaṃ vāsaṃ rocayeta guroḥ kule |
uktaḥ paricared enam ā śarīravimokṣaṇāt || 2.243 ||
yadi tv ātyantikam iti vikalpena nirdeśāt naiṣṭhikabrahmacaryam avikalpenoktaṃ bhavati | tac cottaratra pradarśayiṣyāmaḥ | vedaśāstraṃ nyāyavirodhena |
ā samāpteḥ śarīrasya yas tu śuśrūṣate gurum |
sa gacchaty añjasā vipro brahmaṇaḥ sadma śāśvatam || 2.244 ||
naiṣṭhikastutir iyaṃ phalavidhinā |
na pūrvaṃ gurave kiṃcid upakurvīta dharmavit |
snāsyaṃs tu guruṇājñaptaḥ śaktyā gurvartham āharet || 2.245 ||
na pūrvaṃ gurave kiṃcid upakurvīta | duḥkham utpādya iti vākyaśeṣaḥ | evaṃ duḥkham (?) utpādyādhyayanapratiṣedho 'yaṃ na [sarvapratiṣedhaḥ?] | sarvapratiṣedhe tūktaśāstravirodhāh syāt | arthadādhyāpyo "dharmārthau yatra na syātām" [MDh 2.112] iti ca | prakaraṇāc ca śiṣyopadeśo 'py ayam virodhe (?) 'pi yo 'py ayam upadeśo (?) mantavyaḥ | atha vāpi taddānapratiṣedho 'yaṃ gurvarthastutyarthaḥ | uktasāmarthāt | idānīṃ gurvarthaṃ darśayati |
kṣetraṃ hiraṇyaṃ gām aśvaṃ chatropaham antataḥ |
dhānyaṃ vāsāṃsi śākaṃ vā gurave prītim āharet || 2.246 ||
[104]
gurvarthavidhyarthaślokaḥ | kṣetrādīnāṃ grahaṇaṃ sarvadravyapradarśanārtham | saparisaṃkhyaiṣā |
ācārye tu khalu prete guruputre guṇānvite |
gurudāre sapiṇḍe vā guruvad vṛttim ācaret || 2.247 ||
eteṣv avidyamāneṣu sthānāsanavihāravān |
prayuñjāno 'gniśuśrūṣāṃ sādhayed deham ātmanaḥ || 2.248 ||
naiṣṭhikabrahmacaryavidhyarthāv imau ślokau |
evaṃ carati yo vipro brahmacaryam aviplutaḥ |
sa gacchaty uttamasthānaṃ ca cehājāyate punaḥ || 2.249 ||
adhikṛtabrahmacarastutir iyaṃ phalavidhineti ||
śrī śrī śriḥ |
iti manuśāstre bhṛguproktasaṃhitāvivaraṇe bhārucikṛte dvitīyo 'dhyāyaḥ ||
tṛtīyo 'dhyāyaḥ
dvividho brahmacārī pūrvatra prakṛtaḥ – upakurvāṇo naiṣṭhikaś ca | tatra naiṣṭhikasya vihitakālaṃ brahmacaryaṃ śāstrato nāmadheyāc ca | upakurvāṇasyāvihitakālo yas tadvidhitsayedam (?) ārabhyate |
ṣaṭtriṃśadabdikaṃ caryaṃ gurau traivedikaṃ vratam |
tadardhikaṃ pādikaṃ vā grahaṇāntikam eva vā || 3.1 ||
ṣaṭtriṃśad iti dvandvaḥ | ṣaṭtriṃśatām abdānāṃ samāhāraḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadabdam | dvigur ayaṃ samāhārārtho veditavyaḥ | ṣaṭtriṃśadabde bhavat ṣaṭtriśadabdikaṃ tadvrataṃ caryaṃ caritavyam | kva | gurau | karmakavedādhyayanaṃ nāyaṃ (?) kiṃ tarhi | traivedikam | trayāṇāṃ vedānāṃ samāhāras trivedaḥ | tatra bhavaṃ traivedikam | evaṃ ca prativedaṃ dvādaśābdāni bhavanti, upadeśasāmarthyān nityam eva | tasmin brahmacaryakāle prāpta adhyetṛsāmarthyāpekṣayā tatkālavikalpārtham idaṃ prakramyate | tadardham aṣṭādaśāni bhavanti | prativedaṃ vibhāgo draṣṭavyaḥ | pādikaṃ vā grahaṇāntikaṃ veti | abdaṃ pratyavadhāraṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | "caryaṃ gurau traivedikaṃ vratam" iti samānaṃ pūrveṇa | atha kasmād atharvavedaḥ paryudastaḥ | tasyargvedāviśeṣāt | tadvibhāgaś ca śāntyādiprayojanaḥ | atha vā trayyaiva kriyāparisamāpteḥ | tathā ca śrutiḥ, yad "ṛgvedenaiva hautraṃ kurvīta yajurvednādhvaryavaṃ sāmavedenotgīthaṃ yad eva trayyai vidyāyai sūktaṃ tena (?) brahmatvam" iti | yato brahmatve 'py atharvevedasyāvyāpāraḥ trivedasaṃyogāc ca brahmatvaśruteḥ "tad vā yad ṛcā hotrā śrāvayati yajuṣādhvaryavaṃ sāmnodgitham atha kena brahmatvam iti praṇetaryā vidyayā" iti prativacanopapatteḥ | tasmāt paryudastād api (?) karmano 'tharvavedaḥ | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ "trivedasaṃyogād eva brahmatvam" āha | brahmaprakaraṇe ca śrutiḥ pratiṣedhaṃ karoti "tasmād ātharvaṇena na saṃprapaśyāt" iti trayāṇāṃ ca vedānāṃ vedaśabdena sarvatra śrutiṣv abhidhānāt | tarhi ṛgvedāt "bhuva" iti, yajurvedāt "svaḥ" iti, sāmavedāṅgatvenātharvavedasya kutaścid vibhāgam āha "trayīṃ vidyāc ca sāmāni yajūṃṣi thathā ṛcaḥ | yajūṃṣi sāmānīty aṣṭākṣarāni" iti | tathā ca "tebhyo hi tṛptebhyas trayo (?) vedā anvasṛjyanta ṛgveda evāṅgair ajāyata yajurvedo vāyoḥ sāmaveda ādityād iti | tatva (?) vātharvavedaṃ kutaścid āha | tasmāt [106] trayāṇām eva vedatvāt | trivedabrahmacaryam evehocyate | tathā tarhy ātharvaṇe vedaśabda itihāsapurāṇāyurvedavad iti | atha katham idaṃ gṛhyaśāstram "ayaṃ catvāriṃśad varṣāṇi vedabrahmacaryaṃ caret" iti, "ṣoḍaśa ṣoḍaśa bhaviṣyanti" iti, prativedam evaṃ ca na catuḥsaṃkhyānugrahaḥ kuto bhavati | na ca tatra caturgrahaṇam asti, yatas traivedikam etat brahmacaryam | prativedaṃ ṣoḍaśābdo vibhāgena evaṃ kṛtvā dvitīyam idaṃ gṛhyakāras taṃ (?) tad vaikalpikaṃ bhavati dvādaś dvādaśa vā prativedam etac caivaṃ samarthaṃ bhavati yathāvocāmo vayam (?) | itarathā puṛvaśāstraṃ manunārambhasamaṃ prāpnoti yato yukta iha traivedibrahmacaryopadeśaḥ | api ceyam ṛk aṣṭacatvāriṃśatā varṣāṇi vedabrahmacaryaṃ cared iti smṛtiḥ | kṛṣṇakeśavirodhān mīṣāṃ (?) sa tair asmṛtiḥ | kalpitāt tava (?) | idānīm anena śāstreṇa niyamatas trivedādhyayane prāpta idam ucyate |
vedān adhītya vedau vā vedaṃ vāpi yathākramam |
aviplutabrahmacaryo gṛhasthāśramam āvaset || 3.2 ||
atra vedalakṣaṇasyāgnihotrādeḥ pratiśākhabhedenāvasthānāt tannimitto 'pi vedaśabdo na pratiśākham avatiṣṭhate guṇatas tatsamūhe | ato 'nyatamām api vedaśākhām (?) adhītya snānam asy asyād anena śāstreṇa, na svaryā (?) eva | ye tu sarvavedaśākhāvasthitaṃ vedaśabdaṃ manyante teṣām aśakyārthavidhānnopadeśānarthakyam ekasminn eva tāvad vede, kiṃ punas triṣu | evaṃ ca "vedaḥ kṛtsno 'dhigantavyaḥ" (MDh 2.165) ity atra kṛtsnagrahaṇaṃ yathoktaṃ vijñeyam, na tu śākhāsamūhaviṣayaṃ vā samānatvāt | pūrveṇa sāmarthyāpekṣayā ca prayatnaviśeṣāt  phalaviśeṣeṇa bhavitavyam | dvitrivedādhyetur ekavedādhyetuḥ sakāśād ṛgvedaprakramatvād vedānāṃ tatkrameṇa yathāvrataṃ vā yathācāraṃ yathābhijanavetrāviplutabrahmacaryo gṛhathāśramī syād ābhāryopagamakālāt | evaṃ ca tadatikrama upapātakaprāyaścittenādhikriyate | "gṛhasthāśramam āvaset" iti gṛhasthāśramapratipatticodanāiṣā | evaṃ ca vākyadvayam atrārthabedād vijñeyam | tat pratyucyate (?) | vedaṃ vedau vedān adhītya yathāsāmarthyam | gṛhasthāśramam āvased aviplutabrahmacaryaś cety evaṃ ca brahmacaryeṇādhigatavedaśāstraṃ gṛhasthāśramaṃ prati vivitsuṃ snonotarakālam (snānottarakālam?) |
taṃ pratītaṃ svadharmeṇa brahmadāyaharaṃ pituḥ |
sragviṇaṃ talpa āsīnam arhayet prathamaṃ gavā || 3.3 ||
snātakasyaitad (?) yasyārhatocyate (?) | tam adhikṛtaṃ snātakam | pratir ābhimukhye | itaṃ gataṃ gṛhasthāśramam abhimukhībhūtam | katham | svadharmeṇa | pūrvottaraviseṣaṇārtham idaṃ svadharmagrahaṇam | dīyata iti dāyaḥ | brahmaṇo dāyo brahmadāyaḥ | taṃ haratīti brahmadāyaharam | pituḥ sakāśād adhigatavedam ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca saty eṣaḥ prathamaḥ kalpaḥ | yat pitā putram adhyāpayet | tathā ca brāhmaṇam "tasmāt [107] putram anuśiṣṭaṃ lokyam āhuḥ" iti | evam ity asyāpy ayam eva nyāyaḥ | srag asyāstīti sragvī | sraggrahaṇaṃ ca sarvālaṃkārapradarśanārtham | tathā ca gṛhyakāras tān āha ittham "bhūtaṃ cainaṃ talpa āsīnaṃ svastrīr na (?) śayanopaviṣṭaṃ tu pūjayet" | kaḥ | adhikṛtaḥ pitā | prāthamikakalpita (?) ācāryaḥ prathamaṃ vṛtaḥ (?) | "gṛhasthāśramam āvaset" ity ato 'dhikṛtān karmaṇaḥ | kena | gavā | gopradānena tadgrahaṇena pradhānakarmopadiśati madhuparkākhyaṃ pūjāsādhanam iti | evaṃ ca |
guruṇānumataḥ snātvā samāvṛtto yathāvidhi |
udvaheta dvijo bhāryāṃ savarṇāṃ lakṣaṇānvitām || 3.4 ||
satyām api vedavratasmāptau guruṇānumato 'nujñātaḥ snāyāt | gurvabhyanujñātasya snānāṅgatvāt | samāvṛttiś ca yathāvidhi yathāśāstraṃ gurvanujñayaiva | udvaheta dvijo bhāryām | tathā ca gurunānumata iti vartate | udvaheteti vivāhacodanaiṣā | savarṇām iti niyama ucyate | savarṇājātiḥ savarṇāṃ tām ananyajātiṃ lakṣaṇānvitām | ity ayam adṛṣṭā jagata eva śāstrārthavattvād yā dṛṣṭaprayojanaparo niyamaḥ | atha vā vaidhavyaputranapranāḍikayā duṣṭaprayojana eva lakṣaṇānvayo vā deśaḥ | idānīṃ savarṇopadeśād aviśeṣeṇa savarṇastrīmātrodvāhe prāpta idam ārabhyate niyamārtham |
asapiṇḍā ca yā mātur asagotrā ca yā pituḥ |
sā dvijānāṃ praśastā strī dārakarmaṇy amaithinī || 3.5 ||
"sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate" (MDh 5.60) iti vakṣyati | puruṣagrahaṇaṃ ca tryartham api vijñeyam | jñātinirdeśāc chrutau ca prāptipratiṣedhāt | agnicityāṃ hi puruṣarāśir upadadhātīty utpadam āha na vaiśyasya na striyāḥ |(?) mātur grahaṇaṃ cātra mātṛsaṃbandhamātragrāhakaṃ vijñeyam | evaṃ pitṛgrahaṇam api | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "ūrdhvaṃ saptamāt pitṛbandhubhyaḥ, bījinaś ca, mātṛbandhubhyaḥ pañcamāt" (GDh 4.3–5) iti | tad evaṃ vijñāyate yathāvocāmeti sapiṇḍatā ca mātṛbandhūnāṃ bhūtapūrvagatyā vijñeyam | asagotrāyāḥ pituḥ asapiṇḍā cety eka iti vartate | asagotragrahaṇena pitṛvyādisagotrastrīmātra(tṛ?)paryudāsam asiddā ? (paryudāso 'siddho) bhavati | pitṛṣvasur agnaya[ḥ] strīṇās tu (ntu) yathoktād avadheḥ parataḥ prāptaye ca śabdaḥ (??) pravartate (?) | asagotrā yā ca pitur asapiṇḍā yā ca mātuḥ | bhaginyā ity arthaḥ | yady api mātur anyastrīṇāṃ yadā na samakālam eva tad asapiṇḍatvaṃ bhavati | tathā piṇḍagrahaṇaṃ tadanvayastrīṇām upalakṣaṇaṃ bhūtapūrvagatyā vijñeyaṃ sāmarthyāt | tathā ca gautamīyodāharaṇam uktam | evaṃ ca sati yeyam īdṛśī sā hi praśastā strīti niyamas tadantarīyakaḥ (?) | punar dārakarmaṇī kīdṛśe prajāsaṃgatva (santati?) [108] karmopalakṣaṇe evaṃ cāsau śāsvato gṛhyamāṇā amaithunī iva sampadyate | niyamastutimātram idam | tataś ca sarvam api gṛhyamāṇā amaithunīti śakyaṃ vaktum | mithunaprayojanā tatra bhavā maithunī | anyas tv āha vidhavāniyoge jātimātratvā veti vacanāt pitrādibhir utpannā asapiṇḍā api satī mātur asagotrā ca pitur varjyaiva, amaithunīti vacanāt | sā hi pitrādīnāṃ mithunasaṃbhavām aparaḥ dārakamaṇy amaithune vigrahārthau ca pūrvac ca | aparo dārakarmaṇi maithune ca śa(ga?)vādhyāhāreṇaivamarthaṃ darśayati | anye punar dārakarmaṇi maithunīty evaṃ manyante | sarvapāre tv avirodhaḥ smṛtyarthasya | idānīṃ pratiṣiddhaviṣayo 'yam aparo niyama ārabhyate |
manānty api samṛddhāni go'jāvidhanadhānyataḥ |
strīsaṃbandhe daśaitāni kulāni parivarjayet || 3.6 ||
paryupasargapāṭhasāmarthyāc ca na kevalaṃ strīsaṃbandhe, bhojanādiṣv api varjayed iti | tāni punaḥ|
hīnakriyaṃ niṣpuruṣaṃ niśchando romaśārśasam |
kṣayāmayāvyapasmāriśvitrikuṣṭhikulāni ca || 3.7 ||
hīnakriyādīnāṃ yady apy adṛṣṭaṃ pradhānataḥ prayojanaṃ varjane | tathāpi dṛṣṭam upavarṇyate | svabhāvānuṣaktaṃ hīnakriyakulasamparke mātulaprītyā prajāvatīcchacchilā (?) syāt | etena niśchando vyākhyātaḥ | niṣpuruṣaṃ ca santatyabhāvadoṣād romaśaṃ tākṛśasya doṣaśravaṇāt | evaṃ hi śrutiḥ: "nirṛtaye vikṛta dehaṃ romaśam ālabheta | sā hy asya svakā tanuḥ" iti | arśasādikulānāṃ tu saṃkrāntibhayād varjanam | evaṃ tu hy āyurvedakṛt "sarve saṃkramiṇo rogā varjayitvā pravāhikām" iti | vakṣyati ca | "evaṃ karmāvaśeṣeṇa jāyante sadvigarhitāḥ" (MDh 11.53) iti | tataḥ karmadoṣaśeṣeṇāpi varjyaḥ | ayaṃ cānyo niyamaḥ | apratiṣiddhakularogām api ca sakulatayā |
nodvahet kapilāṃ kanyāṃ nādhikāṅgīṃ na rogiṇīm |
nālomikāṃ nātilomāṃ na vācālāṃ na piṅgalām || 3.8 ||
kapilāṃ keśataḥ, piṅgalām akṣitaḥ | ṛjv anyat |
narkṣavṛkṣanadīnāmnīṃ nāntyaparvatanāmikām |
na pakṣyahipreṣyanāmnīṃ na ca bhīṣaṇanāmikām || 3.9 ||
ṛkṣanāmnī kṛttikārohiṇī | vṛkṣanāmnī śiṃśapā kṣīrikā | nadināmnī yamunā narmadā | antyanāmnī ha(śa?)barikā kaivartikā lubdhakā | parvatanāmnī vindhyā malayā | pakṣināmnī śeynā kokilā śārikā | ahināmnī vyālā sarpiṇī | preṣanāmnī dāsī ceṭī [109] kiṃkarī | bhīṣaṇanāmnī rākṣasī piśācī ḍākinī |
avyaṅgāṅgīṃ saumyanāmnīṃ haṃsavāraṇagāminīm |
tanulomakeśadaśanāṃ mṛdvaṅgīm udvahet striyam || 3.10 ||
vidhipratiṣedhayor vidhānād ubhayatrābhyudayaḥ anuvādo vānyatarasya vikalpaḥ | ubhayavarjitānām –
yasyās tu na bhaved bhrātā na vijñāyet vā pitā |
nopayaccheta tāṃ prājñaḥ putrikādharmaśaṅkayā || 3.11 ||
yayorjanyajanakabhāvena pitā na jñāyate bhrātā vā tām api lokavacanāt kevalād upeyāt | atha vā pitṛgrahaṇaṃ kulasaṃvijñānārtham | putrikādharmaś ca tadā nobhayaśeṣaḥ | bhāryopasaṃgrahaprakaraṇāt | idānīm arthaprāptasya savarṇāgrahaṇasya kramavidhir ayaṃ śiṣyat –
savarṇāgre dvijātīnāṃ praśastā dārakarmaṇi |
kāmatas tu pravṛttānām imāḥ syuḥ kramaśo 'varāḥ || 3.12 ||
siddhāpi satī savarṇetaraviprakṣaya ucyate | arthāc ca savarṇāgrahaṇaṃ śāstrāt | ka vidhiḥ (?) | evaṃ ca dvitīyaiṣā smṛtiḥ | pūrvasyā asya stutyartham arthavādaḥ prakriyate |
śūdraiva bhāryā śūdrasya sā ca svā ca viśaḥ smṛte |
te ca svā caiva rājñaś ca tāś ca svā cāgrajanmanaḥ || 3.13 ||
kathaṃ punar ayaṃ ślokaḥ kramavidhistutyai bhavati yenāsavarṇā savarṇayā samānī karoti tathā ca saṃkhyānirdeśaḥ | "tāś ca svā cāgrajanmanaḥ" ity evamādyā evaṃ ca dṛṣṭāntārthaṃ śūdrepādānam | śūdaiva bhāryā śūdrasya yathā tathā dvijātīnām apy etā yathoktā bhavanti evaṃ savarṇāsāmenetarāstutā bhavanti | evaṃ ca svārthaṃ pravṛttāv apratyavāyaḥ nivṛttau vābhyudayaḥ | "nivṛttis tu mahāphalā" (MDh 5.56) iti vacanāt | prathamakalpāsaṃbhave cānukalpa idānīṃm bhāryāgrahaṇasyocyate |
na brāhmaṇakṣatriyayor āpady api hi tiṣṭhatoḥ |
kasmiṃś cid api vṛttānte śūdrā bhāryopadiśyate || 3.14 ||
iyaṃ tṛtīyā smṛtiḥ | kā punar āpad anayoḥ | prakaraṇāt savarṇāsaṃbhavaḥ | asyām āpadi vartamānaḥ yo brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoḥ kasmiṃś cid api vṛttānte anukalpapakṣe 'pīty arthaḥ | śūdrā bhāryopadiśyata ity asya na jñātisaṃbandhaḥ | evaṃ ca savarṇābhāve brāhmaṇasyānantaryās āpatkalpaḥ kṣatriyodvāhanaṃ [1 akṣ unclear] vaiśyayos tadvyavadhānam | tathā cānantaryaṃ kāraṇam anyasminn api āpatkalpe [110] dṛṣṭam | vakṣyati hi "kṣatravṛttyā tu jīveta sā hy asya pratyanantarā" (jīvet kṣatriyadharmaṇa sa hy asya pratyanantaraḥ) (MDh 10.81) iti | evaṃ kṣatriyasyāpi vaiśyodvāhaḥ asyām avasthāyāṃ veditavyaḥ, na tu śūdrayoḥ | uktatvāt | ayaṃ jīvatva(taḥ?) tasyājñayā api vaiśyā brāhmaṇasya uktasya tayā sāmarthyāt pratiṣiddhāsmin pakṣe | tathā ca śudrāgrahaṇam idaṃ brāhmaṇasya vaiśyāpratiśeṣe nidarśanārthaṃ draṣṭavyam | evaṃ ca yathā śūdrā kṣatriyasya pratiṣiddhā, evaṃ vaiśyā brāhmaṇasyeti | brāhmaṇasya tarhi śūdrāpratiṣedha evaṃ na prāpnoti prāpnoty eva (?) | katham | daṇḍāpūpikayā | yatra kṣatriyaḥ śūdrāṃ na labhate tayā sahāvikārābhāvāt tatra brāhmaṇasya sā kathaṃ syāt | samānatvād dhetoḥ | atha vā yatra vaiśyāṃ na labhate brāhmaṇīm ekāntarāṃ yajñiyāṃ ca satīm | tatra katham | śūdrāṃ hy etadantaraṃ (?) ayajñiyāṃ ceti | vaiśyagrahaṇaṃ cātra na kṛtaṃ yenāsya nāsy evāpatkalpaḥ | ānantarye 'pi śūdrayā ayajñiyatvād (?) adhikārābhāve saty atas trayāṇām api brāhmaṇādīnāṃ śūdrāpratiṣedhas tathā cānantaraśloke vakṣyati "hīnajātiṃ striyaṃ mohād udvahanto dvijātayaḥ" (MDh 3.15) iti | atha vāstu brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyābhāve vaiśyā, antyajāpi, sahādhikārād udvāhād gṛhasthasya | nidarśanaṃ ca yathānyasmin āpatkalpe abrāhmaṇād vidyopayoge kṣatriyābhāve vaiśyād api bhavati, evam ihāpi bahavti | yathā brāhaṃasyāpatkalpe kṣatriyavṛttyā hy ājīvato vaiśyavṛttir apratiṣiddhā, evam ihāpy ānantaryasyākaraṇatvād brāhmaṇo yajñārhāṃ vaiśyāṃ kṣatriyāṃ [1 akṣ unclear] va udvahet āpatkalpe(kāle) 'dhikārārtham | atha vā "na brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoḥ" ity asyānyo 'rtha ucyate | vidyamāna etasmin pratiṣedha ūḍhayor api sāvarṇyābhyatikrameṇa kṣatriyāvaiśyayoḥ śūdrānivṛttāv abhyudayo 'stīti gamyate iti | atha (?) hy asaty asmin pratiṣedha ūḍhayor api kṣatriyāvaiśyayoḥ trayā (?) sarvā smṛtir atikrānteti | satyām api śūdrānivṛttāv abhyudayo 'stīti gamyate | itarathā hy asaty asmin pratiṣedhe yathātikrameṇo(cchā)cchāyāṃ kṣatriyāyāṃ vaiśyānivṛttau punaḥ striyam āha | āsāṃ (?) nāsty abhyudaya evam ihāpi syāt | evaṃ ca kṛtvā vaiśyagrahaṇaṃ sa karoti | brāhmaṇakṣatriyayor eva savarṇāsmṛtyatikrame śūdrānivṛttau smṛtiḥ saṃbhavati | na tu vaiśyasya | savarṇātikrame śūdraiś cānantarā | sā ca savarṇāgrahaṇam ity anenaiva pratiṣiddhā na pratiṣedhyābhāvād vaiśyagrahaṇaṃ na karoti | asyedānīm arthavādārtham prakriyate |
hīnajātistriyaṃ mohād udvahanto dvijātayaḥ |
kulāny eva nayanty āśu sasantānāni śūdratām || 3.15 ||
anantarapratiṣedhaśeṣo (?) 'yaṃ nindārthavādaḥ | kramabhedaśeṣo vā | śūdrāyām atyantapratiṣedhaḥ | tathā ca gṛhyakāraḥ: "śūdrām eke mantravarjam" ity ucāva | dvijātigrahaṇasāmarthyāt [111] sarveṣām eva nivṛttir iṣyate na brāhmaṇakṣatriyayor eva śrutatvād dhetoḥ | tathā ca vyākhyātam | evaṃ ca –
śūdrāvedī pataty atrer utathyatanayasya ca |
śaunakasya sutotpattyā tadaptyatayā bhṛgoḥ || 3.16 ||
atreḥ | gautamasya tāvat mataṃ śūdrāpratiṣedhaśeṣatayā vyākhyātaṃ pūrvaślokavat | śaunakasya sutotpattyā | iyam apy aparasmṛtiḥ | ko 'syārthaḥ | "upayann api śūdravṛtto nopeyāt sutotpattibhayāt" iti śaunakaḥ | ṛtāv apy upayann itarāsu jātāpatya upeyād udvāhakrameṇaiva | atha vā śūdrāpatyasyaivāpatyena | vakṣyati "śūdrāpatyaiś ca kevalaiḥ" (MDh 3.68) iti | ime tu smṛtī śūdrānujñānasmṛter (?) avirodhinī, yato manvādibhir anujñāyate śūdraivam | kiṃ cānyat |
śūdrāṃ śayanam āropya brāhmaṇo yāty adhogatim |
etad eva ślokārdhaṃ pūrvavidhitrayaśeṣatayaiva vyākhyeyam | śayanārohaṇaṃ ca vivāhaḥ saṃprayogo vā sāmarthād vijñeyaḥ |
janayitvā sutaṃ tasyāṃ brāhmaṇyād eva hīyate || 3.17 ||
eṣa śaunakasmṛtiśeṣo nindārthavādaḥ | brāhmaṇagrahaṇārthavttvāya (?) ca | sarvā śaunakasmṛtir brāhmaṇaviṣayaiva kalpanīyā | kramodvāhe ca sati –
daivapitryātitheyāni tatpradhānāni yasya tu |
nāśnanti pitṛdevās tan na ca svargaṃ sa gacchati || 3.18 ||
ayam api pūrvavad vibhajya vacanīyaḥ | idaṃ cānyat pratiṣiddhasevanāt |
vṛṣalīphenapītasya niḥśvāsopahatasya ca |
tasyāṃ caiva prasūtasya niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || 3.19 ||
phenaḥ pīto yenāsau phenapītaḥ | vṛṣalyāḥ phenapīto vṛṣalīphenapītaḥ | strīpuṃsayor viśiṣṭasaṃyogopalakṣaṇam eva niḥśvāsopahatasya caikaśayanādibhiḥ | tasyāṃ caiva prasūtasyeti śaunakasmṛtiśeṣo 'rthavādaḥ | niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate kutaścid vyatikrame niṣkṛtir nāsti | idaṃ cedṛśaṃ karma yatra niṣkṛtir nāsti | nindātiśayenaiṣa niṣkṛtipratiṣedhaḥ | pūrvac chloko 'yaṃ vibhajya vacanīyaḥ | uktaṃ savarṇāstrī niyamopadeśadvāreṇa strīgrahaṇagataḥ bahv iti ceti ca (?) | adhunā tu tadvivakṣayedam ucyate –
[112]
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ pretya ceha hitāhitān |
aṣṭāv imān samāsena strivivāhān nibodhata || 3.20 ||
saṃkhyayopadiśyaitān svarūpata idānīm āha |
brāhmo daivas tathaivārṣaḥ prājāpatyas tathāsuraḥ |
gāndharvo rākṣasaś caiva paiśācaś cāṣṭamo 'dhamaḥ || 3.21 ||
brāhmādayo vivāhā devatāsaṃbandhenaivam ucyante, asaty api vā devatāsaṃbandhe stutyartham eṣām etad evaṃ grahaṇam | adhunā tv etad evam –
ye yasya dharmyo varṇasya guṇadoṣau ca yasya yau |
tad vaḥ sarvaṃ pravakṣyāmi prasave ca guṇāguṇān || 3.22 ||
vivakṣitadharmopanyāsārthaḥ ślokaḥ | ye yasya dharmās te ucyante |
ṣaḍ ānupūrvyā viprasya kṣatrasya caturo 'varān |
viṭśūdrayos tu tān eva vidyād dharmyān na rākṣasān || 3.23 ||
iyaṃ ca ṛtvikā(jvarthā) smṛtiḥ | teṣām api ṣaṇṇām |
caturo brāhmaṇasyādyān praśastān kavayo viduḥ |
rākṣasaṃ kṣatriyasyaikam āsuraṃ vaiśyaśudrayoḥ || 3.24 ||
iyam aparā punar abhyudayapradarśanārthā |
pañcānāṃ tu trayo dharmyā dvāv adharmyau smṛtāv iha |
paiśācaś cāsuraś caiva na kartayvau kadācana || 3.25 ||
prājāpatyādayaḥ pañca gṛhyante | paiśācagrahaṇāt jñapakāt teṣāṃ prājāpatyagāndharvarākṣasā dharmyā āsurapaiṣācāv adharmyau | kṣatriyādīnāṃ cāntarādhikārād eṣā smṛtiḥ kṣatriyādīnāṃ trayāṇām iṣyate | na tu brāhmaṇasya | ṣaḍbhir eveti ca brāhmaṇasya niyamāt | tatra "kṣatrasya caturo 'varān" (MDh 3.23) it kṣatrasya gāndharvarākṣasayor iha punar vidhir vaiśyaśūdrayor api tāny eva | vaiśyaśudrabhyām iti prājāpatyarākṣasayor aparo vidhir gāndharvasya punar vidhir ubhe ucyete smṛtī | vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ strīviṣayayor viśeṣābhāvāt neme vaiśyaśūdraviṣaye ity eke manyante | yad iti vivāheṣu svagato viśeṣo 'sti tatas tannimitto 'bhyudayaviśeṣaḥ kalpayitavyaḥ | asti ca viśeṣaḥ | pūrve trayaḥ āsuragāndharvapaiśācāḥ | ime trayaḥ prājāpatyagāndharvarākṣasās teṣāṃ paiśācād rākṣaso viśiṣyate | āsurāt prājāpatyaḥ | gāndharvasyobhayatra viśeṣaḥ | ata eṣā smṛtiḥ pūrvasyāḥ smṛter jyāyasī abhyudayaviśeṣād draṣṭavyā | [113]
pṛthak pṛthag vā miśrau vā vivāhau pūrvacoditau |
