gāndharvo rākṣasaś caiva dharmyau kṣatrasya tau smṛtau || 3.26 ||
punaḥ kṣatriyasya gandharvarākṣasāv ucyete | tayoḥ pṛthag vā samastyor vā kṣatriyasya pravṛttir yathā syād iti rākṣasasya coktasyāpy ekākino gāndharvavidhitsayā punar vidhānam | tatra kalpanā | ṣaṭ catvāro vā brāhmaṇasya | catvāras trayo dvāv eka iti vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ | trayas traya eko vāth caturthakalpeṣu kṣatriyasya | rākṣasāmnāne ko viśeṣaḥ | caturthāvasthitasya caturaḥ karoti rākṣase pravṛttis tathetareṣv api kalpeṣu draṣṭavyam [4 akṣ lacula] syānena prapañcena sarvatrārthakalpanā yojyā | sarvatrāpāpaviṣayatā (?) jyāyasyam | yathā pṛthak pṛthag vā miśrau vānyābhyāṃ saha, na tu gāndharvarākṣasayoḥ samuccitayoḥ miśraśabdena grahaṇam | idānīṃ tadvidhir ayaṃ pradarśyate –
ācchādya cārcayitvā ca śrutaśīlavate svayam |
āhūya dānaṃ kanyāyā brāhmo dharmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 3.27 ||
yajñe tu vitate samyag ṛtvije karma kurvate |
alaṃkṛtya sutādānaṃ daivaṃ dharmaṃ pracakṣate || 3.28 ||
ācchādyety evam anuvartate sarvavivāheṣu |
ekaṃ gomithunaṃ dve vā varād ādāya dharmataḥ |
kanyāpradānaṃ vidhivad ārṣo dharmaḥ sa ucyate || 3.29 |
sahobhau caratāṃ dharmam iti vācānubhāṣya ca |
kanyāpradānam abhyarcya prājāpatyo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 3.30 ||
jñātibhyo draviṇaṃ datvā kanyāyai caiva śaktitaḥ |
kanyāpradānaṃ svācchandyād āsuro dharma ucyate || 3.31 ||
icchayānyonyasaṃyogaḥ kanyāyāś ca varasya ca |
gāndharvaḥ sa tu vijñeyo maithunyaḥ kāmasaṃbhavaḥ || 3.32 ||
hatvā chittvā ca bhittvā ca krośantīṃ rudatīṃ gṛhāt |
prasahya kanyāharaṇaṃ rākṣaso vidhir ucyate || 3.33 ||
suptāṃ mattāṃ pramattāṃ vā raho yatropagacchati |
sa pāpiṣṭho vivāhānāṃ paiśācaḥ prathito 'dhamaḥ || 3.34 ||
[114]
atropagamanaśabdo yadi mukhyārthas tataḥ saṃskāraparilopaḥ | yataḥ kṣatayonyāḥ pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskāro nāsti | vakṣyati hi }
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantrāḥ kanyāsv eva pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
nākanyāsu kvacin nṝṇāṃ luptadharmā vigarhitāḥ || [MDh 8.226]
arthalakṣaṇatvāc ca paiśācasya evaṃ tadupadeśaḥ śāstreṇa prāptaḥ eṣa doṣo mā bhūd iti | yata upagamanaśabdo yathoktanyāyasāmarthyād āliṅganacumbanopagūhanādiṣūpagamanārtheṣu ge'nṛṇo'yaṃ (?) vijñeyaḥ | evaṃ cāgamaś ca śāstre 'rthavān bhavati, na ca saṃskāraparilopaḥ tannindā ca | atrāṇḍayām (?) īṣadupabhogād yuktā yenaiva ca sā īṣad evaṃ bhuktā tasmā eva deyā | akṣatayonyāś ca saṃskārapratiṣedho nāsti | etena gāndharvo vyākhyātaḥ | aṣṭānāṃ ca vivāhānām aṣṭabhiḥ ślokair uktāni lakṣaṇāni ṛjvarthatvā(n?) na vyākhyāyante |
adbhir eva dvijāgryāṇāṃ kanyādānaṃ viśiṣyate |
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānām itaretarakāmyayā || 3.35 ||
evaṃ ca prāg vivāhāt strīvāgdānaṃ sodakaṃ brāhmaṇānām | itareṣāṃ nirudakam | tathā ca vakṣyati "yasyā mriyeta kanyāyā vācā satye kṛte patiḥ" (MDh 9.69) iti |
yo yasyaiṣāṃ vivāhānāṃ manunā kīrtito guṇaḥ |
sarvaṃ śṛṇuta taṃ viprāḥ samyak kīrtayato mama || 3.36 ||
vivāhārthavādopanyāsārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
daśa pūrvān parān vaṃśyān ātmānaṃ caikaviṃśakam |
brāhmīputraḥ sukṛtakṛn mocayaty enasaḥ pitṝn || 3.37 ||
sukṛtakṛd atra jāto mocayaty enasaḥ pitṝn | pūrvāparān | kathaṃ punar utpannān sataḥ mocayati yathaivātītān | sarvathā mocayatu mā vā mūmucat | brāhmīputrasya praśaṃsaiṣocyate | anyathā hi yathāśrutagrāhiṇo 'kṛtābhyāgamakṛtavipraṇāśau vedasmṛtiśāstravirodhinau prasajyeyātām |
daivoḍhājaḥ sutaś caiva sapta sapta parāvarān |
ārṣoḍhājaḥ sutas trīṃs trīn
evam eṣaḥ –
ṣaṭ ṣaṭ kāyoḍhajaḥ sutaḥ || 3.38 ||
itīdam ayuktam | ārṣād guṇavattarasyaiva sataḥ paścād abhidhānam | evaṃ daivād anantaraṃ prājāptyo vaktavyaḥ | tathā ca ṣaṭtvaṃ manugṛhītaṃ bhavati | satyam evam | etad eva vaktavyam āsīt | [115] uttarārthas evm asyopadeśaḥ "pañcānāṃ tu trayo dharmyāḥ" (MDh 3.25) ityādiḥ | kṣatriyādīnāṃ tadapekayā asya prājāpatyasyārṣāt paścād abhidhānam | evam etat pratyekaṃ stutvāpatyataḥ samudāyena punaḥ stauti –
brāhmādiṣu vivāheṣu caturṣv evānupūrvaśaḥ |
brahmavarcasvinaḥ putrā jāyante śiṣṭasaṃnatāḥ || 3.39 ||
rūpasattvaguṇopetā dhanavanto yaśasvinaḥ |
paryāptabhogā dharmiṣṭhā jīvanti ca śataṃ samāḥ || 3.40 ||
padārthasvābhāvyam etad vivāhastutaye 'nūdyate tac ca śāstraṃ pratyakṣaṃ dharmyādharmyavyavasthām anuvadato nātiśaṅkyam |
itareṣu tu śiṣṭeṣu nṛśaṃsānṛtavādinaḥ |
jāyante durvivāheṣu brahmadharmadviṣaḥ sutāḥ || 3.41 ||
brahmadviṣo vedādhyayanadviṣaḥ brāhmaṇadviṣo vā brahmabrāhmaṇayor ekārthatvāt | dharmas tu vihitārthānuṣṭhānadiṣaḥ brāhmādiṣu stutyapekṣam idam aupariṣṭeṣu vivāheṣu nindāvacanam | itarathā hi kṣatriyādidharmāpekṣayā tadvidhānam anarthakaṃ syād āsuragāndharvayos tu brāhmaṇasya vikalpaḥ | ubhayaśāstrānugrahāya kṣatrādīnāṃ ca vivāhapraśaṃsaiṣā | evam eva śāstracodanā sāmaryād vijñeyā | yataś caitad evam ataḥ –
aninditaiḥ strīvivāhair anindyā bhavati prajā |
ninditair ninditā nṛṇāṃ tasmān nindyān vivarjayet || 3.42 ||
vihitastutiparam etat sarvavarṇānām | idānīm adhikāraśāstraṃ praiṣidhyate |
pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskāraḥ savarṇāsūpadiśyate |
asavarṇāsv ayaṃ jñeyo vidhir udvāhakarmaṇi || 3.43 ||
śaraḥ kṣatriyayā grāhyaḥ pratodo vaiśyakanyayā |
brāhmaṇīpāṇiparigṛhītaḥ pāṇigrahaṇasthāne | evaṃ pratodo vaiśyakanyā | brāhmaṇakṣatriyapāṇiparigṛhītaḥ pāṇigrahaṇasthāne grāhya iti vartate |
vasanasya daśā grāhyā śūdrayotkṛṣṭavedane || 3.44 ||
kasyacid dvijāter udvoḍhuḥ pūrvavad utkṛṣṭavedana iti cedaṃ sarvavivāhaśeṣaḥ | kṛtodvāhasyedānīm arthaprāpta upagame tanniyamārtham ārabhyate –
[116]
ṛtukālābhigāmī syāt svadāranirataḥ sadā |
parvavarjaṃ vrajec caināṃ tadvrato ratikāmyayā || 3.45 ||
ṛtur vakṣyamāṇo garbhagrahaṇasamarthaḥ kālaḥ kṛtāpavādo (?) daśa rātrākhyaḥ | ṛtukālābhigamanaṃ prakramo 'sya (?) so 'yam ṛukālābhigāmī yathā sthaṇḍilavartivatenni (?) niveditavyaḥ | syād evaṃ bhaved ity arthaḥ | kim iyaṃ parisaṃkhyā ṛtukāle pratiṣedhārthā nety āha | kiṃ tarhi | niyamaḥ | kaḥ punar anayor viśeṣaḥ | parisaṃkhyānyanivṛttyarthā | vidhiś cāprāptaprāpaṇārthaḥ | niyamas tv arthaprāptasya prāptivacanam | anuṣaṅgataś cānyanivṛttiṃ krtvānugame doṣaḥ prāyaścittasmaraṇād yathāvidhi niyogavat | vakṣyati hi "niyuktas tu yathānyāyam" (MDh 5.35) ity evamādyaṃ ca | "parvavarjaṃ vrajec cainām" ity etad yuktārambhaṃ vākyāntaraṃ bhavati, arthabheedāt | tathā hi "parvavarjaṃ vrajet" iti yad ekavākyaṃ syād arthabhedābhāvāt | tasmād arthaprāptasya niyama ity upapannam | niṛttiś cātra gamyamānā | athānyatra "svadāranirataś ca syāt" iti vartate | ayam arthaprāptasya paradāropagamanasya pratiṣedhaḥ | evaṃ ca sati vākyāntaram idam arthabhedād bhavati | sadeti tadubhayaśeṣaḥ | katham | yady api jātaputrasya dvitīyādiṣu putrajanmanindā arthavādaḥ "kāmajān itarān viduḥ" (MDh 9.107) | tathāpi svadāranirataḥ syāj jātaputro 'pīty arthaḥ | tathā ca mantravarṇaḥ: "daśāsyāṃ putrān ādhehi patim ekādaśaṃ kṛdhi" iti | paradāravarjī sadeti vidhavāniyogapakṣe 'pi jātimātrād vā ity anena | aparas tv āha sajātīya (?) savarṇāyām evartukālābhigāmitvaṃ yuktatvārthatāsaṃbhavāc ca | itarāsu punar yāthātmyam | tat punar nyāyyṃ veti vicāraṇīyam | idānīṃ daśarātrasyartor abhyantare pūrvaśāstreṇa prāptasaptakālopagamaṃ vārayati parvavarjam iti | tathā ca vakṣyati |
amāvāsyām aṣṭamīṃ ca paurṇamāsīṃ caturdaśīm |
brahmacārī bhaven nityam apy ṛtau snātako dvijaḥ|| [MDh 4.128]
evaṃ tarhi punaruktadoṣaḥ | punaruktam anayor yathā na bhavati tathā pradarśayiṣyāms tatraiva | atha cottarārtham asya punarvacanam | parvavarjaṃ vrajec cainām anṛtāv apy etac cainam ārambhasāmarthyād asya pratīyate | nanv etad dṛṣṭārthatvād dṛṣṭārthatā sambhavāc cārambhaḥ | anārambhe ca sati parvavarjaṃ vrajec cainām ity  evam ekavākyatā bhavatu | vākyadvaye nārthaḥ | "ṛtukālābhigāmī syāt," "parvavarjaṃ vrajec cainām" ity ārambhas tasyā saty apy apatyārthatvena tāv api yayoḥ yam enāṃ tadvrato bhūtveti vākyaśeṣaḥ tat syād vrataṃ tadvrataṃ kiṃ tatparapuruṣāsaṃkalpas tadvrataṃ vratam asya so 'yam tadvrataḥ | uṣṭramukhanyāyena ekasya vrataśabdasya lopaḥ | tasyāṃ vā vratam asya so 'yaṃ tadvrataḥ tatsaṃrakṣaṇavrataḥ | sa caivaṃ bhavati yadi kāmam upaity aratikāmyayātmanaḥ nātmopabhogenecchayā | kiṃ tarhi | bhāryānurodhena | akāraś cātra sāṃhittiko vijñeyaḥ | atas tadratyavighātacittasaṃyamādhānena tadvrato bhavati | tathā ca vakṣyati –
[117]        svāṃ prasūtiṃ caritraṃ ca kulam ātmānam eva ca |
svaṃ ca dharmaṃ prayatnena jāyāṃ rakṣaṇ hi rakṣati || [MDh 9.7]
iti | gautamaś caivam eva "prasūtirakṣaṇam asaṃkaro dharmaḥ" (GDh 8.3) ity āha | atha vā pāṭhāntaraṃ "tadvrato ratikāmyayā" tasya eva nātmanaḥ | atha vobhayasaṃbandhī | tacchabdo vacanīyaḥ | tadvratas tadratikāmyayā | triṣv api pāṭheṣu bhāryāprayuktasya pravṛttir anṛtāv api syān nātmopabhogena, icchayā | evam uttaratrāniyamaḥ | ṛtau tv avaśyaṃ gantavyam | yāvatprajādharmasaṃpade paścād ajātaputraḥ putrārthaniyamataḥ pravarteta | jātaputrasya tu dvitīyādiputrajanane pratyavāyaḥ | "kāmajān itarān viduḥ" (MDh 9.107) iti liṅgād ajanane 'bhyudayaḥ | "nivṛttis tu mahāphalā" (MDh 5.56) iti vacanād arthato 'yam arthavādaḥ | "kāmajān itarān viduḥ" (MDh 9.107) iti | tataḥ sarvadāyaṃ niyamo vijñeyaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantram "dharmaprajāsaṃpanne dārenānyāṃ kurvīta" iti |
ṛtuḥ svābhāvikaḥ strīṇāṃ rātrayaḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ |
parvavarjaṃ vrajec caināṃ tadvrato ratikāmyayā || 3.46 ||
svābhāviko nāma ṛtuḥ strīṇāṃ yo na dravyarāgarogaiḥ (?) pravartate caturbhir itaraiḥ prathamair aśucibhiḥ sārdhaṃ sadvigarhitair iti nindārthavādo nivṛttyarthaḥ |
tāsām ādyāś catasras tu ninditaikādaśī ca yā |
trayodaśī ca śeṣās tu praśastā daśa rātrayaḥ || 3.47 ||
tāsāṃ ṣaṇṇitada (?) ṣoṣaśa praśastāḥ ṛtudarśanāt prabhṛti | ekādaśītrayodaśyau vijñeyau | na candratithayas tu daśa praśastās tāsām |
yugmāsu putrā jāyante striyo 'yugmāsu rātriṣu |
tasmād yugmāsu putrārthī saṃviśed ārtave striyam || 3.48 ||
jātaputraḥ śrautadharmādhikārārtham ātmasaṃskārārthaṃ cāyaṃ tāvat sargaḥ (?) | parasya tv apavādārthaḥ |
pumān puṃso 'dhike śukre strī bhavaty adhike striyāḥ |
same 'pumān puṃstriyau vā kṣīṇe 'lpe ca viparyayaḥ || 3.49 ||
pumān bhavati kadācit strī kāle 'pi yadā puṃsaḥ śuklaṃ puṃsaḥ strībījāt | evaṃ strī kadācit bhavati puruṣakāle 'pi yugmāc cādhike strībījaṃ puruṣabījāt | katham etad gamyate | strīpuruṣajanmakālo vāyam ārambhasāmarthye syād asya same 'pumān, bhinne strīpuruṣabīje [118] vāyunā yadi yugmā yady ayugmā tithiḥ puṃstriyo[r] vā viṣamabhede yugapat samabījabhede vā striyau puruṣau vā bhavato daivanipātasāmarthyāt | kṣīṇe 'lpa ity ubhayasmin alpa ityarthaḥ | ayaṃ ced ṛśo (?) nirdeśo 'nuṣṭupchando'nuvidhānena viparyayo napuṃsakajanmoktānuvādaḥ abhāvo vā | garbhasya viparyāyo 'tha vā kṣīṇe parimāṇato 'lpasārata iti | idānīṃ dvitīyasmṛtyupanyāsaḥ "praśastā daśa rātrayaḥ" (MDh 3.47) ity asyāḥ kriyate |
nindyāsv aṣṭāsu cānyāsu striyo rātriṣū varjayan |
brahmacāry eva bhavati yatra tatrāśrame vasan || 3.50 ||
nindyāsu ṣaṭsv aṣṭāsu cāninindyāsv api yāsu kāsucid rātriṣu na krameṇetarayā (?) vā sarvopagamanādhikāro 'nena pratiṣiddhaḥ | arthāt pariśiṣṭe dve rātrī upagacchann api brahmacāry eva bhavati | gārhastyād avasthāntaraṃ prāpto 'pi bhartā yatra tatrāśrame vasan | nanu utpannaviśiṣṭaputrasantativicchedapratyavāye bha (11 akṣ missing)yāt putravataś cet karmāda[r]śanena vidhavāniyogapakṣe vānādeyarad (MDh 8.170?) ity anena śāstreṇa guruvacanād vyāsavan nigṛhītātmā tatputrārtham upagacchan barhmacāry eva bhavati | yatra tatrāśrame vasann iti kecit | tat tv etad ubhayam apy asat | atyantaṃ brahmacaryopadeśāt | teṣu "evaṃ yukto brahmalokam avāpnoti jitendriyaḥ" ity evamādi tadvyatiriktaprāyaścittaviśeṣopadeśāc ca | tasmād rātridvayopagame stutiparam etat | kathaṃ kṛtvā | īdṛśo nāmāyam upagamanakālaḥ praśasto yenātyantabrahmacāriṇaḥ sata āśramāntaraṃ tv asyopagacchataḥ brahmacaryaṃ na khaṇḍyate | tathā ca prakaraṇāvirodhena brahmacaryopadeśaḥ śāstrāntaravirodhaś ca kalpito bhavatīti | kecit tu yatra tatra varṇeṣu sthito gṛhasthāśrame vasan nityam evam āhuḥ | tad etad avākyajñānāṃ vyākhyānam andaraṇīyam | "nindyāsv aṣṭāsu vānyāsu" ity ayam anārṣaḥ pāṭhaḥ | parvabrahmacaryopadeśoparodhadoṣāt | tad etad evaṃ daśānāṃ yathākāmaṃ aṣṭāv (?) utsṛjyante rātrayaḥ, dve parigṛhyete | tayor api putrārthina ekakanyārthino 'parānānuputrārthatvāt (?) pracchannasya (?) kanyārtho 'py upadeśo yuktaḥ | śāstre tasyā dānādiniyamatatpradāne ca | mahāphalas tu viśvajiti san yo (?) yajñaḥ kanyādāne mahāphalaḥ | yathā "nāgnicin narake yāti na satputro na kanyadaḥ" | vājasaneyināṃ copaniṣatsu duhitṛkāmasya karmāmnātam (?) | tac ca nāmnātaṃ syād anabhipretakanyājanane vacanād iti cet, saty avirodhe vacanaṃ yuktam | iha śāstre daśagrahaṇam na kariṣyati vā | evaṃ putrārthās tā evāvakṣyat | putrābhāvapakṣe kanyayā saṃtariṣyante | tathā ca putrikāputrastutiṃ vakṣyati dauhitro 'pi hy amutrainaṃ saṃtārayati yena taratīty (?) evaṃ ca kanyādānādiniyamasya pākṣikatvān na bhaviṣyati tasmāt kanyārthāpi ca pravṛttidharmāya | tathā ca [119] putrārthayor eva ca dvayor anujñātā | na caikaiva (?) rātriḥ putrārthānujñāyate, ubhayoḥ śrutatvāt | iyāṃs tu viśeṣaḥ | jātaputrasya dharmādhikāre sāmarthād ātmādhikārasāmarthāc ca putrotpatter bahuprakārasaṃbandhenānena prathamaṃ niyamataḥ putrārthapravṛttir yuktā | putrārthe niyamena | kanyārthāpi pravṛttir iṣyata evety uktam | kecit tv āhur naitat smṛtyantaram "daśa vā dve vā rātrī" iti | tasmād ekavākyayogo daśānām (?) ekā yā kācid iti | tad ayuktam | asati vākyabhede "praśastā daśarātrayaḥ" (MDh 3.47) itīdaṃ praśaṃsāvacanam ayuktam | ato dve ete smṛtī bhavataḥ | katham | vākyabhedāt | daśa vā praśastā dve vā praśastatare, ātmasaṃyamanasāmarthāt | evaṃ ca sati akṛtārthasya prathamā smṛtiḥ | kṛtārthasya punaruktaṃ vā veditavyam | kecit tv anyathemaṃ ślokaṃ paṭhanti (?):
nindyāsv eva tu sarvāsu striyo rātriṣu varjayan |
brahmacāry eva bhavati yatra tatrāśrame vasan || iti |
arthaṃ cānyataivācakṣate | yāpy anyā śavajananādirātrayas tāḥ sarvagrahaṇena gṛhyante (?) | nigamanaṃ caitat | ṣaṇṇāṃ pratiṣedhārtham | idaṃ nyāyyam veti (?) vicāraṇīyam | vivāhasambandhenedam adhunocyate tadgatam |
na kanyāyāḥ pitā vidvān gṛhṇīyāc chulkam anv api |
gṛhṇan śulkaṃ hi lobhena syān naro 'patyavikrayī || 3.51 ||
āsurasy vaikalpikaśulkagrahaṇapratiṣedho 'yam |
strīdhanāni tu ye mohād upajīvanti bāndhavāḥ |
nārī yānāni vastraṃ vā te pāpā yānty adhogatim || 3.52 ||
tatsaṃbandhā(ddhā?)nām pai doṣaḥ syāt kiṃ punaḥ pitur iti śulkasyāgrahaṇastutir iyaṃ grahaṇanidāyai | tasmāc ca stutyarthād api bandhugrahaṇād idaṃ vijñāyate bandhibhir api na kiṃcid upajīvyam |
ārṣe gomithunam śulkaṃ kecid āhur mṛṣaiva tat |
alpo vāpy evaṃ mahān vāpi tāvān eva sa vikrayaḥ || 3.53 ||
iyam aparā grahaṇe stutyarthā | grahaṇanidāyām |
yāsāṃ nādadate śulkaṃ jñātayo na sa vikrayaḥ |
arhaṇaṃ tat kumārīṇām ānṛśaṃsyaṃ ca kevalam || 3.54 ||
anekatvāt yat kevalaṃ kanyārthaṃ grahaṇe śulkasya doṣo 'sti ūrdhvadānāt (?) |
pitṛbhir bhrātṛbhiś caitāḥ patibhir devarais tathā |
[120]        pūjyā bhūṣayitavyāś ca bahukalyāṇam īpsubhiḥ || 3.55 ||
antaropadiṣṭasya kanyādhānagrahaṇopāyastutir iyam | tasmād eva taddhanaṃ grahītavyaṃ nirupadhena kanyārtham | yena -
yatra nāryas tu pūjyante ramante tatra devatāḥ |
yatraitās tu na pūjyante sarvās tatrāphalāḥ kriyāḥ || 3.56 ||
vihitopāyasya vyatikramanindeyaṃ cānyā |
śocanti jāmayo yatra vinaśyaty āśu tatkulam |
anugṛhyamāṇe tadalaṅkārārthadhane
na śocanti tu yatraitā vardhate tad dhi sarvadā || 3.57 ||
jāmayo yāni gehāni śapanty apratipūjitāḥ |
śvaśurajñātibhiḥ |
tāni kṛtyāhatānīva vinaśyanti samantataḥ || 3.58 ||
tad asaṃskāravardhanena |
tasmād etāḥ sadā pūjyā bhūṣaṇācchādanāśanaiḥ |
bhūtikāmair narair nityaṃ satkāreṣūtsaveṣu ca || 3.59 ||
caśabdād vivāheṣu ca viśeṣata idaṃ kartavyam | kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ bhavet |
saṃtuṣṭo bhāryayā bhartā bhartrā bhāryā tathaiva ca |
dānāt prītir bhavati kanyāyāḥ | bhartuś ca sālaṅkārāyāṃ bhāryāyām | evaṃ ca sati –
yasminn eva kule nityaṃ kalyāṇaṃ tatra vai dhruvam || 3.60 ||
parasparacintānupraveśena jāyāpatyor yathā –
yadi hi strī na roceta pumāṃsaṃ na pramodayet |
apramodāt punaḥ puṃsaḥ prajanaṃ na pravartate || 3.61 ||
yā strī na roceta sālaṅkāratayā niyatam asau bhartāraṃ na pramodayati | apramodāc ca prajananahāniḥ | ato 'parasya manaścakṣurnibandhanī | kanyālaṅkārastutir evaṃ dānālaṅkārādibhir bhavaty aparihāryopamārthaḥ | anupakāriṣv arucir jāyata iti | tasmāt prayatna āstheyas tayā sahāgnihotrādiṣv arucir mā bhūd iti | abhimata(?)vivāhasutir iyaṃ tatsaṃbandhenedam adhunocyat |
[121]
striyāṃ tu rocamānāyāṃ sarvaṃ tad rocate kulam |
tasyāṃ tv arocamānāyāṃ sarvam eva na rocate || 3.62 ||
kuvivāhaiḥ kriyālopair vedānadhyayanena ca |
kulāny āsu vinaśyanti brāhmaṇātikrameṇa ca || 3.63 ||
śilpena vyavahāreṇa śūdrāpatyaiś ca kevalaiḥ |
gobhir aśvaiś ca yānaiś ca kṛṣyā rājopasevayā || 3.64 ||
ayājyayājanaiś caiva nāstikyena ca karmaṇām |
kulāṇy akulatāṃ yānti yāni hīnāti mantrataḥ || 3.65 ||
mantratas tu smṛddhāni kulāny alpadhanāny api |
kulasaṃkhyāṃ ca gacchanti karṣanti ca mahad yaśaḥ || 3.66 ||
catvāraḥ ślokāḥ kulasaṃrakṣaṇaniyamārthāḥ | eṣām atikrame kulasaṃrakṣaṇaniyamo 'py atikranto bhavatīty atra eṣāṃ punaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ | "mantratas tu samṛddhāni" ity atra mantragrahaṇaṃ nidarśanārthaṃ kriyādīnām api draṣṭavyam | evaṃ ca mantrādibhiḥ samṛddhānīti vyākhyeyam | atrāpy eṣa eva nyāyaḥ | atha "yāni hīnāni mantrataḥ" (65) ity atrāpy eṣa eva nyāyaḥ | atha vā vivāhaniyamastutiprakaraṇād itareṣāṃ kriyālopādīnāṃ kuvivāhānāṃ (?) yathā kriyālopādibhiḥ kulāny akulatāṃ yānty evaṃ kuvivāhānāṃ yathā kriyālopādibhiḥ "kulāny akulatāṃ yānti" (65) evaṃ kuvivāhair api | tasmāt taṃ niyamaśāstravihitam ādaraṇīyam ity ayaṃ prakaraṇārthaḥ | kṛtodvāhasyedānīṃ krama uadiśyate | yadi –
vaivāhike 'gnau kurvīta gṛhyaṃ karma yathāvidhi |
pañcayajñavidhānaṃ ca paktiṃ cānvāhikīṃ gṛhī || 3.67 ||
vivāhaḥ prayojanam asya so 'yaṃ vaivāhikaḥ | keṣāṃcit gṛhyakārāṇām āhitaḥ, keṣāṃcid anāhitaḥ | ubhayeṣām apīdaṃ vidhānaṃ tasmin kurvīta gṛhyakarmāṣṭakapārvaṇādi pañcayajñavidhānaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ yat kiṃ ca vaiśvadevārthaṃ vāṣṭakaṃ pratygraham ahaḥ prabhṛti ca | tatra smārtakarmārambhaḥ | kiṃ kāraṇam | etav evaṃ pratyahaṃ karmānuṣṭhīyate | tasmāt –
pañcasūnā gṛhasthasya cullī peṣaṇy upaskaraḥ |
kaṇḍanī codakumbhaś ca badhyate yās tu vāhayan || 3.68 ||
yataś caita evam ataḥ –
[122]
tāsāṃ krameṇa sarvāsāṃ niṣkṛtyarthaṃ maharṣibhiḥ |
pañca kḷptā mahāyajñā pratyahaṃ gṛhamedhinām || 3.69 ||
pañcayajñārthavādārtham (?) idaṃ ślokadvayam | sūnāvacanaṃ ca stutyartham | gārhasthyakarmaṇāṃ stutya etat | turīyaṃ na punar niṣkṛtir eva yathā tathā śruter iti sāmarthyāt karmopadeśaparam idaṃ nityatvāc ca na niṣkṛtyarthaḥ | pañca mahāyajñās teṣāṃ svarūpanirdeśaḥ |
adhyāpanaṃ brahmayajñaḥ pitṛyajñas tu tarpaṇam |
homo daivo balir bhauto nṛyajño 'tithipūjanam || 3.70 ||
adhyāpanaśabdenādhyayanaṃ brahmayajño gṛhyate guṇavṛttyāpi | pitṛbhyo dānaṃ tarpaṇaṃ pitṛyajñaḥ | annahomo devayajñas tato 'nyatra baliharaṇaṃ bhūtayajñaḥ | atithibhyo 'nnadānaṃ manuṣyayajñaḥ | ete brahmayajñādayaḥ prakaraṇāt tad vaiśvadevikāḥ |
pañcaitān yo mahāyajñān na hāpayati śaktitaḥ |
sa gṛhe 'pi vasan nityaṃ sūdādoṣair na lipyate || 3.71 ||
upasaṃhārārtham idaṃ ślokānte 'pi cāhur apunarbhuve viguṇā japyete (?) śaktitaḥ pratyaham anuṣṭheyena mahāsādhanenaiveti | tathā cātra śaktita iti vacanaṃ kasya punar hetoḥ | yena –
devatātithibhṛtyānāṃ pitṝṇām ātmanaś ca yaḥ |
na nirvapati pañcānām ucchvasan na sa jīvati || 3.72 ||
caturthyā saṃpradānārthatayā paṭhitavyo 'yaṃ ślokaḥ | bhṛtyātmā na ca viśvedevāntarbhūtāv eva gṛhyete devatā (???) | evaṃ baliharaṇasaṃbandhād bhūtānīty abhidhīyante 'to devatāgrahaṇena gṛhītatvāt | pṛthaṅ na paryante bhūtāni na śloke "ucchvasan na sa jīvati" ity api evaṃ (?) śruto 'pi stutisāmarthād draṣṭavyaḥ | nirvyāpaś ca devatādibhyo dānam ucyate | brahmadānaṃ ca ṛṣibhyo guṇa āmnātaṃ teṣāṃ saiṣa vidhyartham idaṃ punarvacanam |
ahutaṃ ca hutaṃ caiva tathā prahutam eva ca |
brāhmyaṃ hutaṃ prāśitaṃ ca pañcayajñān pracakṣate || 3.73 ||
eṣām eva mahāyajñānāṃ punarvacanaṃ saṃjñāntarapradarśanārthaṃ śeṣavidhyarthaṃ cāsya ślokasya vivaraṇārthamayam uttaraḥ ślokaḥ |
japo 'huto huto homaḥ prahuto bhaitiko baliḥ |
brāhmyaṃ hutaṃ dvijāgryārcā prāśitaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam || 3.74 ||
[123]
japa iti | brahmayajñaḥ prakaraṇāt | sa vākyam (?) iti abhidhīyate | agnau homo bhūtaḥ sa eva devayajña ity uktaḥ | baliharaṇaṃ prahutaṃ sa eva bhūtayajño 'bhihitaḥ | brāhmaṇāya dānaṃ brāhmyaṃ hutaṃ dvijāgryārcā sā evātithipūjoktā | prāśitaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam udakadānādi śrāddham | evaṃ ta eva vaiśvadevakāḥ pañcamahāyajñāḥ pākayajñaśabdena abhidhīyate | prayojanam asyaivaṃ rūpāḥ | anye 'pi grāhyā vidhayaḥ (?) pākaśabde gṛhyeran | pākayajñavidhiprāptyartham ity asādhano 'pi trayaṃ gṛhastho yatnataḥ |
svādhyāye nityayuktaḥ syād daive caiveha karmaṇi |
asya prayojanaṃ sarvajagadgrahaṇaṃ darśayati gṛhasthasya |
daivakarmaṇi yukto hi bibhartīdaṃ carācaram || 3.75 ||
evaṃ ca mahāyajñaikadeśānuṣṭhānād api sarvalokānugraho yasmāt |
agnau prāstāhutiḥ samyag ādityam upatiṣṭhate |
ādityāj jāyate vṛṣṭir vṛṣṭer annaṃ tataḥ prajāḥ || 3.76 ||
homayajñastutir iyaṃ prakaraṇāt kiṃca nānyat |
yathā mātaram āśritya vartante sarvajantavaḥ |
vartante gṛhiṇaṃ tadvad āśrityetara āśramāḥ || 3.77 ||
[yathā vāyuṃ samāśritya sarve jīvanti jantavaḥ |
tathā gṛhastham āśritya vartanta itarāśramāḥ || ]
evaṃ ca sati,
yasmāt trayo 'py āśramiṇo jñānenānnena cānvaham |
gṛhasthenaiva dhāryante tasmāj jyeṣṭhāśramo gṛhī || 3.78 ||
adhikṛtakarmānuṣṭhānastutiprakaraṇasāmarthād veditavyā yataś caitad eva mahāprayojano gṛhasthāśramaḥ sarvalokānugrāhitayā | ataḥ –
sa saṃdhāryaḥ prayatnena svargam akṣayam icchatā |
sukhaṃ cehecchatātyantaṃ yo 'dhāryo durbalendriyaiḥ || 3.79 ||
phalavidhinā stutiḥ pañcamahāyajñānuṣṭhānasya |
ṛṣayaḥ pitaro devā bhūtāny atithayas tathā |
āśāsate kuṭumbibhyas tebhyaḥ kāryaṃ vijānatā || 3.80 ||
[124]
kiṃ tad idam ucyate |
svādhyāyenārcayetarṣīn homair devān yathāvidhi |
pitṝñ śrāddaiś ca nṝn annair bhūtāni balikarmaṇā. || 3.81 ||
ayam ijyāṅgapradarśanārthaḥ ślokaḥ | vihitakarmānuṣṭhānasādhanāpekṣaṃ tu (?) | kiṃ ca, idam api tāvat,
kuryād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena vā |
payomūlaphalair vāpi pitṛbhyaḥ prītim āvahan || 3.82 ||
tad evaṃ kṛtavaiśvadevo gṛhasthaḥ –
ekam apy āśayed vipraṃ pitryarthaṃ pāñcayajñike |
na caivātrāśrayet kiṃcid vaiśvadevaṃ prati dvijam || 3.83 ||
vaiśvadevād adhikam idaṃ śrāddhakarmaślokadvayenābhidhīyate | asya vikalpena kriyām api vakṣyati |
vaiśvadevasya siddhasya gṛhye 'gnau vidhipūrvakam |
ābhyaḥ kuryād devatābhyo brāhmaṇo homam anvaham || 3.84 ||
vaiśvadevaśabdenātra pāka upadiśyate | siddhasyeti vacanāt viśvedevā devatā yasya so 'yaṃ vaiśvadevaḥ sarvadevārthāt pāka ucyate |
agneḥ somasya caivādau tayoś caiva samastayoḥ |
viśveṣāṃ caiva devānāṃ dhanvantaraya eva ca || 3.85 ||
kuhvai caivānumatyai ca prajāpataya eva ca |
saha dyāvāpṛthivyoś ca tathā sviṣṭakṛte 'ntataḥ || 3.86 ||
evaṃ samyag ghavir hutvā sarvadikṣu pradakṣiṇam |
indrāntakāppatīndubhyaḥ sānugebhyo baliṃ haret || 3.87 ||
marudbhya iti tu dvāri kṣiped apsv adbhya ity api |
vanaspatibhya ity evaṃ musalolūkhale haret || 3.88 ||
ucchīrṣake śriyai kuryād bhadrakālyai ca pādataḥ |
brahmavāstoṣpatibhyāṃ tu vāstumadhye baliṃ haret || 3.89 ||
viśvebhyaś caiva devebhyo balim ākāśa utkṣipet |
[125]        divācarebhyo bhūtebhyo naktaṃcāribhya eva ca || 3.90 ||
pṛṣṭhavāstuni kurvīta baliṃ sarvānnabhūtaye |
pitṛbhyo baliśeṣaṃ tu sarvaṃ dakṣiṇato haret || 3.91 ||
śunāṃ ca patitānāṃ ca śvapacāṃ pāparogiṇām |
vāyasānāṃ kṛmīṇāṃ ca śanakair nirvaped bhuvi || 3.92 ||
evaṃ yaḥ sarvabhūtāni brāhmaṇo nityam arcati |
sa gacchati paraṃ sthānaṃ tajomūrtiḥ patharjunā || 3.93 ||
vaiśvadevaṃ tribhiḥ ślokair avika(vakra?)vidhiprāptyartham |
kṛtvaitad balikarmaivam atithiṃ pūrvam āśayet |
bhikṣāṃ ca bhikṣave dadyād vidhivad brahmacāriṇe || 3.94 ||
yat puṇyaphalam āpnoti gāṃ datvā vidhivad guroḥ |
tat puṇyaphalam āpnoti bhikṣāṃ datvā dvijo gṛhī || 3.95 ||
ślokadvayam ṛjvartham \
bhikṣām apy udapātraṃ vā satkṛtya vidhipūrvakam |
vedatattvārthaviduṣe brāhmaṇāyopapādayet || 3.96 ||
naśyanti havyakavyāni narāṇām avijānatām |
bhasmībhūteṣu vipreṣu mohād dattāni dātṛbhiḥ || 3.97 ||
vidyāpataḥsamṛddheṣu hutaṃ vipramukhāgniṣu |
nistārayati durgāc ca mahataś caiva kilbiṣāt || 3.98 ||
tribhiḥ ślokair atithilakṣaṇam | bhikṣulakṣaṇaṃ cedaṃ sārthavādaṃ draṣṭavyam |
saṃprāptāya tv atithaye pradadyād āsanodake |
annaṃ caiva yathāśakti satkṛtya vidhipūrvakam || 3.99 ||
śilān apy uñchato nityaṃ pañcāgīn api juhvataḥ |
sarvaṃ sukṛtam ādatte brāhmaṇo 'narcito vasan || 3.100 ||
tṛṇāni bhūmir udakaṃ vāk caturthī ca sūnṛtā |
etāny api satāṃ gehe nocchidyante kadācana || 3.101 ||
[126]        ekarātraṃ tu nivasan atithir brāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
anityaṃ hi sthito yasmāt tasmād atithir ucyate || 3.102 ||
idam atithīnāṃ nirvacanam ekāhorātraprativāsino brāhmaṇasyeyaṃ niyamasmṛtir veditavyā | evaṃ ca sati dvitīyādiṣv ahaḥsv anuvṛttim akurvato yathoktapratyavāyābhāvaḥ | anuvṛttena tu satyām ayam abhyudayo yathā pradarśitaḥ syād ity evamartham idaṃ tannirvacanam uktam |
naikagrāmīṇam atithiṃ vipraṃ sāṅgatikaṃ tathā |
upasthitaṃ gṛhe vidyād bhāryā yatrāgnayo 'pi vā || 3.103 ||
ekagrāmaprativāsinaṃ guṇavantam api vipraṃ deśāntarād āgatyākale 'pi svagṛham atikramyāvasthitaṃ gṛhe na vidyād atithi(ṃ?) śāstrāt | prāptasyāyaṃ pratiṣedho niyamadānasya nānyasya sāṅgatikaṃ tathā asamānagrāmam api sāṅgatikam | sahādhyāyī saṅgatyāvalokayātrāyāgacchati sa sākgatikaḥ | vicitrakathāparihāsajīvī yena snātakasya so 'pratiṣiddhā vṛttiḥ "na lokavṛttaṃ varteta vṛttihetoḥ kathaṃcana" (MDh 4.11) iti | sakhātra sāṅgatiko na bhavati tasyottaratra pratiṣedhād "vaiśyaśūdrau sakhā ca" MDh 3.110) iti | na gṛhasthasya grāmāntaragatasyāhyanyonyā (?) pratītir bhavati tatra yenāha "bhāryā yatrāganayo 'pi vā" tatra syād ātithyam | aparas tv āha "bhāryā putrāgnayo 'pi vā" iti | asmin pāṭhe 'rthaḥ – asya ślokapādasya na kevalam ekagrāmavāsy evānatithir bhavati yatra bhāryāputrā nayaty asmin grāme prathamāgatā anyagrāmavāsino 'py avatiṣṭhante sa cānatithir veditavyaḥ | tatraitat punaruktam "naikagrāmīṇam atithim" ity anena | idānīṃ parapākabhojanoddeśenātithīnāṃ pravāsa pratiṣedhartham ayaṃ nindārthavādo 'bhidhīyate |
upāsate gṛhasthā ye parapākam abuddhayaḥ |
tena te pretya paśutāṃ vrajanty annādidāyinām || 3.104 ||
ayaṃ (?) phalavidhiḥ phalārthavādo vā | adhunā tu viśeṣārtham idam asyocyate |
apraṇodyo 'tithiḥ sāyaṃ sūryoḍho gṛhamedhinā |
kāle prāptas tv akāle vā nāsyānaśnan gṛhe vaset || 3.105 ||
pratiṣiddhārho 'yam asyārambhaḥ | kathaṃ vaset tad gamyate | ārambhasāmarthād asyettham | anyathābhihitalakṣaṇasyātitheḥ prātar iva sāyam apraṇodyaviśeṣaṇadāne prāpte gṛhasthasya dvirvaiśvadevavratvād anārambho 'sya prasajyeta | avaśyaṃ (?) tv idam | ato yathokta evārthaḥ ślokaḥ | idānīṃ vivriyate (?) "apraṇodyo 'tithiḥ sāyam" ekagrāmanivāsy api | sāṅgatiko 'py akiṃcanatayā yasmād asau sūryoḍhaḥ sūryāstaṃ gacchatā ūḍhaḥ prāpitaḥ sāyaṃ ca krameṇa [127] pratiṣedhān na proṇodyo 'smin kāle gṛhamedhinā gṛhamedhāḥ pañcamahāyajñā asya santīti gṛhamedhī gṛhasthaḥ | kāle prāptaḥ bhojanasyākāle vā bhojanottarakālam anastamite vā viguṇaḥ īṣadguṇaḥ sarvaguṇo vā ekagrāmisāṅgatikayor anyataro 'py atithir nāsyābhukto gṛhe vaset | pratiṣiddho 'pīti | punar itaratra (?) vaiśvānaraśruteḥ | tasya hi sāyaṃ nibhṛteṣv āgacchan atithir vaiśvānaraḥ sampadyate tasmāt tenānanujñāto hīnapi (2 akṣ lacuna)na juhuyāt sa hy asya parādevateti | idānīṃ saty apy atithidāne gṛhasthasyobhayoḥ kālayor viṣamabhojanapratiṣedhārtham idam ārabhyate |
na vai svayaṃ tad aśnīyād atithiṃ yan na bhojayet |
asyārthasyānuṣṭhānaprarocanāya dṛṣṭādṛṣṭopakārasambandhenāyaṃ stutyarthavādaḥ padārthasvābhāvyāt kriyate |
dhanyaṃ yaśasyam āyuṣyaṃ svargyaṃ cātithibhojanam || 3.106 ||
yathoktaviśeṣaṇam | evam |
āsanāvasathau śayyām anuvrajyām upāsanam |
uttameṣūttamaṃ kuryād dhīne hīnaṃ same samam || 3.107 ||
prakaraṇād atithīnām eva hīnottamānām ātmanaḥ syād anuvṛttiguṇānurūpam ucyate bhojanātirekiṇaḥ |
vaiśvadeve tu nirvṛtte yady anyo 'tithir āvrajet |
tasyāpy annaṃ yathāśakti pradadyān na baliṃ haret || 3.108 ||
evaṃ ca baliharaṇaṃ vaiśvadevākhyaṃ na pākasaṃskārāḥ | kiṃ tarhi | ātmasaṃskārārthaḥ | itarathā hi prativāde kam āvarteta (?) śāstraprāptasya pratiṣedhaḥ | adhunā bhojanapratigrahārtham atitheḥ svaguṇasaṃkīrtanam asya nivāryate |
na bhojanārthaṃ sve vipraḥ kulagotre nivedayet |
bhojanārthaṃ hi te śaṃsan vāntāśīty ucyate budhaiḥ || 3.109 ||
evaṃ ca bhojanagrahaṇaṃ sarvapratigrahapradarśanārtham, kulagotragrahaṇaṃ ca sarvātmaguṇānāṃ na kuryād iti sāmānyād avagantavyam |
na brāhmaṇasya tv atithir gṛhe rājanya ucyate |
vaiśyaśūdrau sakhā caiva jñātayo gurur eva ca || 3.110 ||
[128]
tuśabdo 'vadhāraṇārthaḥ | brāhmaṇasyānatithayo rājanyavaiśyaśūdrāḥ | kṣatriyādīnāṃ tu savarṇotkṛṣṭavarṇābhāvaṃ (?) te vātithayaḥ | sakhā cātra brāhmaṇa evāsyārambhasāmarthyād vijñeyaḥ | evaṃ kṣatriyādiṣv api jñātigurūṇāṃ ca yatnasiddhaṃ brāhmaṇyaṃ tv anatithitvavacanāt | na ca tulyakāraṇam eṣām anatithitvam, rājanyādīnāṃ tāvad dhīnajātitvāt, sakhijñātyor āptatvāt, putrādivat | gurau ca gṛhiṇaḥ parasaktayānatithitvam (?) | tasya –
yadi tv atithidharmeṇa kṣatriyo gṛham āvrajet |
bhuktavatsu brāhmaṇeṣu kāmaṃ tam api bhojayet || 3.111 ||
atithidharmeṇa tv evam āgato bhavati yadi dūrād aparijñātaḥ apatyadhanaḥ (?) kālopagataś ca tathā bhuktavatsu brāhmaṇeṣu taṃ bhojayet | pūrvāpavādo 'yam |
vaiśyaśūdrāv api prāptau kuṭumbe 'tithidharmiṇau |
bhojayet saha bhṛtyais tāv ānṛśaṃsyaṃ prayojayan || 3.112 ||
atithikarmaṇātithidharmeṇa | sa ca vyākhyātaḥ | ṛjv anyat | brāhmaṇasyaiva dātur itaravarṇānāṃ bhojanakālaḥ ānṛśaṃsyakāraṇopadeśatvād ayam api parvāpavāda eva kāraṇataḥ |
itarān api sakhyādīn saṃprītyā gṛham āgatān |
prakṛtyānnaṃ yathāśakti bhojayet saha bhāryayā || 3.113 ||
saṃprītyā kāraṇenāpi jñātīnāṃ bhāryābhojanakālopadeśas tad api vidyāguros tu nocyate | bhojanakālasvātantryāt tasyottamatvāc ca uttamasya | yathoktam (?) "uttameṣūttamaṃ kuryāt" (MDh 3.107) iti |
suhāsinīḥ kumārīś ca rogiṇo garbhiṇīs tathā |
atithibhyo 'nvag evainān bhojayed avicārayan || 3.114 ||
saty arthitve teṣāṃ suvāsinyādīnām anvag eva bhojanam | asaty arthitve teṣām asaṃbhave vā kumārādayo yathākāmam aśnīyuḥ |
adatvā tu ya etebhyaḥ pūrvaṃ bhuṅkte 'vicakṣaṇaḥ |
tasyāyaṃ niyamavyatikramadoṣaḥ smaryate | tathā ca nindāvacanam idam |
sa bhuñjāno na jānāti śvagṛdhrair jagdhim ātmanaḥ || 3.115 ||
yathā kathaṃcit pratiṣiddhārthaṃ nindeyam, na nivṛttyarthā | sa ca puruṣārtha ity ato yukto 'yam upadeśas tatsiddhaye |
bhuktavatsv atha vipreṣu sveṣu bhṛtyeṣu caiva hi |
bhuñjīyātāṃ tataḥ paścād avaśiṣṭaṃ tu dampatī || 3.116 ||
sve garbhadāsādayaḥ | bhṛtyavetaninaḥ codyante | kasya punar hetoḥ | gṛhasthasya iyān ātmasaṃyamopadeśo mahatā prakaraṇena kriyata ity ucyate | katham | nāyam evātmasaṃyamajñaḥ prayatnena |
devān ṛṣīn manuṣyāṃś ca pitṝn gṛhyāś ca devatāḥ |
pūjayitvā tataḥ paścād gṛhasthaḥ śeṣabhug bhavet || 3.117 ||
pūvaślokoktam apy etat gṛhasthasya paścād bhojanaṃ punar ucyate | kathaṃ nāmāyaṃ proṣito 'pi san tasmin kāle bhuñjāno luptapañcamahāyajñaḥ syāt | atha vā prakaraṇopasaṃhārārtho 'yaṃ ślokaḥ | pūrvas tv avaśiṣṭabhojanārthaḥ | evaṃ ca saty etaṃ nindāvacanam aśeṣaṃ prakaraṇopasaṃhārāpekṣaṃ nyāyyam |
aghaṃ sa kevalaṃ bhuṅkte yaḥ pacaty ātmakāraṇāt |
yajñaśiṣṭāśanaṃ hy etat satām annaṃ vidhīyate || 3.118 ||
atithipakṣaprakaraṇe cedam atyantaviśiṣṭapūjāsādhanaṃ keṣāṃcid apūjyānām abhidhīyate |
rājartviksnātakagurūn priyaśvaśuramātulān |
arhayen madhuparkeṇa parisaṃvatsarāt punaḥ || 3.119 ||
rājaśabdaḥ kṣatriyamātre dṛṣṭaḥ | "rājño balārthinaḥ ṣaṣṭhe" (MDh 2.37) iti vacanāt | kṣatriyasya sataḥ abhiṣekarakṣādhipatyaprabhāvaguṇavataḥ "rājāśvamedhena yajeta, rājā rājasūyean yajeta" iti vacanād akṣatriyo 'pi kriyāguṇābhyāṃ rājaśabdo dṛṣṭaḥ | "na śūdrarājye nivaset" (MDh 4.61) | tat kasyedaṃ grahaṇam | kṣatriyasyābhiṣekādiguṇasaṃbandhinaḥ, prakaraṇasāmarthyāt | ataḥ kramāgatam "arhayen madhuparkeṇa" prakṛto gṛhītasya punar hānakālaṃ vidadhāti parisaṃvatsarāt punaḥ parigatāt | saṃvatsarād ūrdhvam ity arthaḥ | kecit tu "parisaṃvatsarān" iti paṭhanti | teṣām ayaṃ tāvad arthaḥ | parigataḥ saṃvatsaro yeṣāṃ te iti parivatsarā jādayas (?) tān evam ṛtvijaṃ vakṣyamāṇam | "arhayen madhuparkeṇa" iti samānaṃ pūrvena | snātakaṃ cācāryaś (?) śiṣyaṃ samāvṛtttyanantaraṃ "taṃ pratītaṃ svadharmeṇa" (MDh 3.3)ityevamādi | anyathā tu sarveṣām evaiteṣām ṛtvigādīnāṃ snātakatvāt punarvacanam ayuktam ṛtvigādīnām | guruṃ ca śiśyaḥ priyaṃ ca jāmātaraṃ śvaśuro duhitur udvāhāgatam | anye priyaśabdaṃ śvaśuramātulaviśeṣaṇam icchanti, śvaśuramātulān priyān iti | teṣām atra pramāṇābhāvaḥ | [130] jāmātuś ca noktā syān madhuparkapūjā sarvasmṛtiśāstraprasiddhā | gautamas tu priyaṃ na paṭhati, pitṛvyaṃ cādhikam adhīte: "ṛtvigācāryaśvaśurapitṛvyamātulānām upasthāne (?) madhuparkaḥ" (GDh 5.27) iti | gṛhye tu priyaśabdena jāmātur eva grahaṇam | vivāhyaśabdena ca śvaśurasya | evaṃ ca śvaśuraṃ jāmātārhayet, mātulaṃ bhāgineyaḥ | pitṛvyasya tu grahaṇaṃ anuktasyāpi mātulasya grahaṇāt siddham, saṃbandhasāmānyāt prakaraṇāc ca | dvijātīnām evāyaṃ parasparo madhuparkadānapratigrahasaṃbandhaḥ | anye tu sarvavarṇādhikāram icchanti | vyāsavacanaṃ cātodāharanti "tadarham āsanaṃ tasmai yathā rāpat (?) pratyetadayat (pūjayat?) | madhuparkaṃ ca gāṃ caiva tasmai bhagavate svayam" vāsudevāya vidura ity arthaḥ | tat punar amantrakam | dvijātimadhuparkam āhur mantrayuktam ayuktaṃ veti bhagavanto bhūmidevā jñātum arhanti |
Editor's note:
[[mahābhārate sabhāvarvaṇi nāradāya yudhiṣṭhireṇa madhuparkasamarpaṇam itthaṃ varṇitam:
abhyavādayata prītyā vinayāvanatas tadā |
tadarham āsanaṃ tasmai saṃpradāya yathāvidhi ||
gāṃ caiva madhuparkaṃ ca saṃpradāyārghyam eva ca |
arcayāmāsa ratnaiś ca sarvakāmaiś ca dharmavit ||
pañcamādhyāyagatau ślokāv etau | etayoḥ saṃkhye nīlakaṇṭhīyavyākhyānusāraṃ 14–15, kṛṣṇamācāryasaṃskarāṇānusāraṃ tu 17–18 | viduraḥ kṛṣṇāya yadi madhuparkam adāsyat udyogaparvaṇi tatprasaṃgo bāḍhabhaviṣyat | paraṃ tu tatra kutrāpi ślokāv imau na staḥ | katham etat saṃgamanīyam ity atra doṣajñā eva pramāṇam ]]
rājā ca śrotriyaś caiva yajñakarmaṇy upasthite |
madhuparkeṇa saṃpūjyau na tv ayajña iti sthitiḥ || 3.120 ||
tato yajña ity ayam aparo (apapāṭho?) dīkṣitadānapratiṣedhāt | tena "rājā ca śrotriyaś caiva yajñe prāptau vidhānataaḥ" ity ayam api pāṭhaḥ pratyukto veditavyaḥ | evaṃ "yajñakarmaṇy upasthitau" ayaṃ ca | kas tarhy atra pāṭhaḥ | "rājā ca śrotriyaś caiva yajñakarmaṇy upasthite | madhuparkeṇa saṃpūjyau na tva ajajña iti sthitiḥ" iti | ṛtvig arvāg api saṃvatsarasyārambhasāmarthyād ārambhe ca sati | śrotriyaśabdena ṛtviṅmātraṃ gṛhyate 'tas tadapavādo rājñaś ca | tathā coktam – yady apy asakṛt saṃvatsarasya "somena yajeta kṛtārghya eva, na (?) yājayen nākṛtārghyaḥ" iti śruteḥ ity arthaḥ | śrotriyaśabdena sarva evartvigādayo gṛhyante | tato yajño nimittena sarveṣām evārvāg api saṃvatsarasya punar madhuparkaḥ syād ity evaṃ ca sarvāpavādaḥ syāt | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "ṛtvigādīnām upasthāne madhuparkaḥ | saṃvatsare punaḥ | yajñavivāhayor arvāk (?)" iti | sāyaṃ prātar baliharaṇam asyoktam | idānīṃ tadviśeṣārtham idam ārabhyate (?) |
sāyaṃ tv annasya siddhasya patny amantraṃ baliṃ haret |
vaiśvdevaṃ hi nāmaitat sāyaṃ prātar vidhīyate || 3.121 ||
[131]
vaiśvadevaṃ homopalakṣitaṃ sāyaṃ prātar vidhīyate | gṛhasthasya balaharaṇaṃ tu patny eva sāyaṃ kuryād anagnikam amantraṃ ca | loke evaṃ baliharaṇaṃ dṛṣṭam | aparas tasyārambhaprayojanam āha | kaiścid gṛhakāraiḥ sāyaṃ prātar vaiśvadevam āmnātaṃ yata idaṃ vidhīyata ity evaṃ ca pañcamahāyajñoktāc chrāddhād tasya māsikasya upadidikṣayedam adhunāha |
pitṛyajñaṃ tu nirvartya vipraś candrakṣaye 'gnimān |
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryān māsānumāsikam || 3.122 ||
sapiṇḍānām asaṃbhūtaṃ māsikaśrāddhaṃ vijñeyam | kuto nāmadheyādadhyasyād (?) idam anvāhāryam ity ucyate | katham āhāryam (?) | anu paścād ity arthaḥ | kasya | piṇḍānām | yenāntaṃ (hriyate?) tad anvāhāryam ity ucyate | evaṃ ca tadaṅgatvāt piṇḍavat pratimāsam idam api tad yat siddham (?) | āmamiṣasy (?) praśastasya niyamārtha upadeśaḥ |
tatra ye bhojanīyāḥ syur ye ca varjyā dvijottamāḥ |
yāvantaś caiva yaiś cānnais tāṇ pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ || 3.124 ||
upanyāsārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
dvau daive pitṛkārye trīn ekaikam ubhayatra vā |
bhojayet susamṛddho 'pi na prasajyeta (?) vistare || 3.125 ||
dvau daive pitraṃśabhūtasya, prakaraṇāt | pitrye trīn bhojayet | iti saṃkhyāviśiṣṭabrāhmaṇabhojanavidhiḥ | "ekaikam ubhayatra vā" ity evam arthavādaḥ | "susamṛddho 'pi na prasajyeta vistare" ity asya pratiṣedhavidhis tathā ca vakṣyati "nimantrayīta tryavarān" (MDh 3.187) iti | gautamaś ca "bhojayed ūrdhvaṃ tribhyaḥ" (GDh 15.21) iti | smṛtyantare 'py uktam "na tv ekaikaṃ sarveṣām" iti | evaṃ ca saty asyārthavādatvād adhisaṃkīrtane 'pi stutir upapadyate | kāmaṃ saṃkhyāgatavidhim api kuryāt "ekaikam ubhayatra vā" iti | na vistare prasajyeta na caivam ekaikasya bhojanam ubhayatrānujñātaṃ bhavati | tathā ca loke "kāmaṃ viṣam api bhakṣaya mā cāsya gṛhe bhuṅkṣā" iti | nānena viṣabhakṣaṇam upadiṣṭaṃ bhavati | etena"'ekaikam api vidvāṃsam" (MDh 3.129) ity etad [132] vyākhyātam | vidvatstutiparatvād asya na saṃkhyā vidhīyate | tasmāt tryavarā eva pitṛkarmaṇi brāhmaṇā vijñeyāḥ | etac caivam arthavādārthaṃ prakalpya brāhmaṇān bhojayanti vaikalpikatryavaravidher idam anyavidhyantaraṃ manyamānāḥ kecit | aparaḥ punar āha: nātra brāhmaṇānāṃ saṃkhyā vidhīyate | kva tarhi | "nimantrayīta tryavarān" (MDh 187) ity atra | tad ayuktam, bhojayed ity upadeśāt | "susamṛddho 'pi na prasajyeta vistare" iti vacanād ayam eva saṃkhyāviśiṣṭabrāhmaṇavidhir arthavādo mantavyaḥ | āmantraṇārthaṃ ca tad vākyaṃ na saṃkhyāvidhyartham | tasmād ayam avaratvāt "susamṛddho 'pi na prasjyeta vistare" ity atra kāraṇaṃ vakti |
satkriyāṃ deśakālau ca śaucaṃ brāhmaṇasaṃpadam |
pañcaitān vistaro hanti tasmān neheta vistaraḥ || 3.126 ||
satkriyām āmantribrāhmaṇānuvṛttyādipūjām | deśakālau ca śrāddhāṅgabhūtau | śaucaṃ saguṇasyānnasya | brāhmaṇasaṃpadaṃ yathāśāstrodiṣṭam | vistaro hanti yasmāt sarvagṛhasthānām ekakālopadiṣṭaṃ śrāddhaṃ "tasmān neheta vistaraḥ" ity arthavādaḥ | guṇavāṃs tasmin kāle bahavo durlabhā ity evaṃparam | etat kriyādiguṇakathanaṃ na svārtham, aprakaraṇāt | kāraṇataś ca vistarapratiṣedhaṃ kurvan sati sāmarthye vistaram anujānāti | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "ayujo yathotsāham" (GDh 15.78) iti | "kuryān māsānumāsikam" (MDh 3.122) ity asya vidheḥ stutyartham idam ārabhyate |
prathitā pretakṛtyaiṣā pitryaṃ nām vidhukṣaye |
prathitā sarvagṛhiṇāṃ pretakartavyateṣṭistutiparam etad yat pitryam ity eva prakhyāto vidhiḥ karmakṣaye gṛheṇānyatrāśrame (?)
tasmin yuktasya
yathākālaṃ pitṛbhaktyā ta eva,
eti nityam
kiṃ tat |
pretakṛtaiva
lokāntaragatasya bahuputrapautratvena śrāddhakarmatayā pitṛpūjayā tāṃ viśiniṣṭi | pretakṛtyā
laukikī || 3.127 ||
tasmāt smārtaivety arthaḥ | evaṃ cānayā praśaṃsayā etad vijñāyate [t] sāmarthyāc chrāddhakarmakṛtaḥ pramītaḥ smārtakarmavaśād yatra tatrotpannāḥ putrapautrair vidhyupasaṃhṛtasya kavyasya nimittābhāvenāvicchinnasukhopabhogā [133] tatrāsate | itarathā hīyam ākasmikī karmapraśaṃsā na syāt | aniṣṭaṃ caitat pramāṇaśāstropadiṣṭatvād asya "tatra ye bhojanīyāḥ syuḥ" (MDh 3.124) ity uktam | yatas ta ima ucyante |
śrotriyāyaiva deyāni havyakavyāni dātṛbhiḥ |
arhattamāya viprāya tasmai dattaṃ mahāphalam || 3.128 ||
atra śrotriyaśabdena na (?) chandomātrādhyāyiniyamaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | mantrabrāhmaṇādhyāpakaniyamaḥ | evaśabdaś ca a?śrotriyapratiṣedhakaḥ | saty api brāhmaṇye yena prathamam idaṃ brāhmaṇasya śrāddhabhojanādhikārakāraṇam, sati śrotriyatve 'rhattamāya | arhattamaśabdena ca vijñātaguṇasaṃpadam asyācaṣṭte | tathā caitad darśayati |
ekaikam api vidvāṃsaṃ daiva pitrye ca bhojayet |
puṣkalaṃ phalam āpnoti nāmantrajñān bahūn api || 3.129 ||
vidvajjanastutiparam etan na vidhyartham | tathā ca kṛtavyākhyāno 'yaṃ ślokaḥ |
dūrād eva parīkṣeta brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam |
tīrthaṃ tad dhavyakavyānāṃ pradāne so 'tithiḥ smṛtaḥ || 3.130 ||
abhijanmaguṇasaṃprāptyartho 'yaṃ ślokaḥ |
sahasraṃ hi sahasrāṇām anṛcāṃ ye tu (yatra) bhuñjate |
ekas tān mantravit prājñaḥ (prītaḥ) sarvān arhati dharmataḥ || 3.131 ||
iyam aparārthajñapraśaṃsā | evaṃ ca saty anṛcāṃ mā bhūt | ṛgvidām iti draṣṭavyam | mantravicchabdaś ca vedārthajānivacanaḥ | evaṃ ca sati –
jñānotkṛṣṭāya deyāni kavyāni ca havīṃṣi ca |
asya dṛṣṭānto 'rthavādaḥ |
na hi hastāv asṛgdigdhau rudhireṇaiva śudhyataḥ || 3.132 ||
yathā prakṣālanavidhijña eva hastāv asṛgdigdhau samarthaḥ kṣālayituṃ nānyaḥ, evam arthañja evam ātmānaṃ paraṃ ca tārayituṃ samartho netaraḥ |
yāvato grasate piṇḍān havyakavyeṣv amantravit |
tāvato grasate pretya dīptāṃ (?) śūlarṣṭyayoguḍān || 3.133 ||
grāsayitur atrāparādho na grasataḥ | tasya śrāddhadānopadeśaśāstrādhikārāt | evaṃ [134] ca sati "yāvato grasate piṇḍāṃś śrāddhakarmaṇi piṇḍadaḥ | havyakavyeṣv amantravit" | evam evam idaṃ vākyasāmarthyād draṣṭavyam | atha vā grasitur amantro vijñeyo 'parādhaḥ | raṅkuśaḥ (kleśaḥ?) kīdṛśaḥ punar grāsayituḥ ity evam | param etā (ete?) |
jñānaniṣṭhā dvijāḥ kecit taponiṣṭhā tathāpare |
tapaḥsvādhyāyaniṣṭhāś ca karmaniṣṭhās tathāpare || 3.134 ||
havyakavyatayor (?) dānavibhāgārtham | tathā cāha |
jñānaniṣṭheṣu kavyāni pratiṣṭāpyāni yatnataḥ |
havyāni tu yathānyāyaṃ sarveṣv eva caturṣv api || 3.135 ||
aśrotriyo pitā yasya putraḥ syād vedapāragaḥ |
aśrotriyo vā putraḥ syāt pitā syād vedapāragaḥ || 3.136 ||
atrācāryavipratipattau manur bhagavān āha |
jyāyāṃsam anayor vidyād yasya syāc chrotriyaḥ pitā |
mantrasaṃpūjanārthaṃ tu satkāram itaro 'rhati || 3.137 ||
śrotriyaputrasya pitā saṃskṛtatvāt parigrahaḥ saṃskārastutiparo draṣṭavyaḥ | ācārasaṃpad bhojanapradarśanārthā (?) | "śrotriyāyaiva devyāni" (MDh 3.128) iti vacanād itarasya nindaiva tatparaiva vijñeyā | tathā ca mantraḥ (?) –
kiṃ brāhmaṇasya pitaraṃ kim u pṛcchasi mātaram |
śrutaṃ ced asti vaśyatve (vṛddhatve?) tau mātāpitarau smṛtau ||
apare bahirjanastutim imām āhuḥ | tad ayuktam, uktatvāt "dūrād eva parīkṣeta" (MDh 3.130) iti | tasmād yathokta evam | uktaṃ cātra – sati śrotriyatve "arhatamāya" (MDh 3.128) iti vacanam | sarvaguṇopasaṃhārārtham iti hetumadbhāvo 'tra yojyaḥ | jyāyāṃś cec chrotriyaputras tasmād dūrād eva parīkṣeteti | phalam idam |
na śrāddhe bhojayan mitraṃ dhanaiḥ kāryo 'sya saṃgrahaḥ |
nāriṃ na mitraṃ yaṃ vidyāt taṃ śrāddhe bhojayed dvijam || 3.138 ||
arimitrayoḥ śrāddhadānapratiṣedhārtham idam | śāstrāt prāptayor ayaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | śrotriyatvādiguṇasaṃpadasyārthavādo bhavati |
yasya mitrapradhānāni śrāddhāni ca havīṃṣi ca |
tasya pretya phalaṃ nāsti śrāddheṣu ca hiviḥṣu ca || 3.139 ||
ayaṃ ca anayor ubhayor yatra pratiṣedhaḥ | tathā ca pūrvaślokena viśeṣavacanam | [135] "nāriṃ na mitraṃ yaṃ vidyāt taṃ śrāddhe bhojayed dvijam" (MDh 3.138) iti tam tadgrahaṇaṃ aripradarśanārtham atra prakṛtatvād areḥ | pūrvaśloke nindārthavādopacayam apekṣeṇa (?) cedam anyad ārabhyate (?) |
yaḥ saṃgatāni kurute mohāc chrāddhena mānavaḥ |
sa svargāc cyavate lokāc chrāddhamitro dvijādhamaḥ || 3.140 ||
saṃbhojanī sābhihitā paiśācī dakṣiṇā dvijaiḥ |
ihaivāste tu sā loke gaur andhevaikaveśmani || 3.141 ||
yatheriṇe bījamuptvā na vaptā labhate phalam |
tathānṛce havir datvā na dātā labhate phalam || 3.142 ||
ayaṃ guṇavanmitrapratiprasavārthaḥ ślokaḥ | anyasya guṇavato 'bhāve syāt tathā cānantarasloke darśayati |
dātṝn pratigrahītṝṃś ca kurute phalabhāginaḥ |
viduṣe dakṣiṇā datvā vidhivat pretya ceha ca || 3.143 ||
prakaraṇān mitrāyaiva viduṣe 'nyasya viduṣo vihitatvād ihāsaṃbhavaḥ | evaṃ ca saty asya śrotriyatvādiguṇavato 'bhāve guṇavantaṃ |
kāmaṃ śrāddhe 'rcayen mitraṃ nābhirūpam api tv arim |
guṇavadbrāhmaṇābhāve ityarthaḥ (?) | asyārthavādaḥ |
dviṣatā hi havir bhuktaṃ bhavati pretya niṣphalam || 3.144 ||
"nāriṃ na mitraṃ yaṃ vidyāt" (MDh 3.138) ity arimitrayoḥ saṃpratiṣiddhayor mitravad arer api guṇavataḥ pratiprasavo mā bhūd guṇavadbrāhmaṇābhāve iti tatpratiṣedhārtham ārabhyata iti "śrotriyāyaiva deyāni havyakavyāni" (MDh 3.128) ity uktam | yatas tān viśinaṣṭi chandomātrādhyāyipratiṣedhārtham |
yatnena bhojayec chrāddhe bahvṛcaṃ vedapāragam |
śākhāntagam athādhvaryuṃ chandogaṃ vā samāptikam || 3.145 ||
sarve evaite parisamāptavedādhyayanā nānāprakāraiḥ śabdair abhidhīyante | evaṃ ca sati yathā bahvṛco vedapāṭhagaḥ evam adhvaryuḥ śākhāntaragrahaṇena tathaivocyate | chandogaṃ ca samāptikam | trisahasravidyāṃ jānīyād yajñe 'pi sādhyātmavidyālakṣaṇavidye te tāḥ sahasravartmanā sahasreṇopalakṣyante sahasravartmanaḥ | sāmavedasya sahasrasyemāḥ sāhasras trisraḥ sahasro vidyā yasya sa trisahasravidyaḥ |
[136]
eṣām anyatamo yasya bhuñjīta śrāddham arcitam |
pitṝṇāṃ tatra tṛptiḥ syāc chāśvatī sāptapauruṣī || 3.146 ||
nedaṃ bahvṛcādīnāṃ grahaṇaṃ kramārtham abhāvavikalpāśrayeṇa | kiṃ tarhi | śrotriyāṇām eṣāṃ satataṃ krameṇopadeśaḥ | ṛgvedaprakramatvād vedānāṃ mantrārthavādeṣu | tathā coktam "eṣām anyatamo yasya bhuñjīta śrāddham arcitam" iti |
eṣa vai prathamaḥ kalpaḥ pradāne havyakavyayoḥ |
adhikṛtabrāhmaṇākṣepam idaṃ praśaṃsāvacanam | tadabhāve tu,
anukalpas tv ayaṃ jñeyaḥ sadā sadbhir anuṣṭhitaḥ || 3.147 ||
anukalpo vakṣyamāṇaḥ | anukalpe vicārayan praśasta eva yasmāt sadā sadbhir anuṣṭhitaḥ | so 'yam ucyate |
mātāmahaṃ mātulaṃ ca svasrīyaṃ śvaśuraṃ gurum |
dauhitraṃ viṭpatiṃ bandhum ṛtvig yājyau ca bhojayet || 3.148 ||
atra gurugrahaṇaṃ vidyāgurūṇām arthāya | kecit tu [āhuḥ?] priyam asmin sthāne pareti mitrapratiprasavārtham [iti?] | tad ayuktam | uktatvān mitrapratiprasavasya "kāmaṃ śrāddhe 'rcayen mitram" (MDh 3.144) iti | viṭpatir jāmātā viṭśabdasya prajāvācitvād, viśāṃ patir dauhitrasaṃnidhau cāsya śravaṇāt | saṃbandhibāndhavamadhye (?) corvadedhādato (?) nātithir evaṃ ca yat | kaiścid uktaṃ viśāṃ patir aveśeṣeṇa sarvaviśāmpatitvād atithis tadāsannam uktatvāc ca tasya prathamakalpa iti | mātāhahādīnāṃ cānukalpopadeśaḥ sampat (?) so 'thakāragandhenānyathā hi jñeyaḥ |
na brāhmaṇaṃ parīkṣeta daiva karmaṇi dharmavit |
pitrye karmaṇi tu prāpte parīkṣeta prayatnataḥ (?) || 3.149 ||
niṣidhyate yathainaṃ loko veda, tathā prativaktavyam | pitrye tu vṛttaḥ svādhyāyāvagame yatna āstheyaḥ | atha vā kāṇaślipadyādīnāṃ saty api durācāraṇapāṭhe durācāratā naiṣāṃ vidyata iti daive teṣām anujñānārthaṃ parīkṣāpratiṣedhaḥ | parapāṭhe na satāṃ vigarhitācārān iti tatra garhitācāravacanārthavad bhavati pratiṣiddhabrāhmaṇaprakaraṇād yatra tu garhitācārās tān ubhayatrāpi varjayet | ye hy atra kāṇaślīpadyādayas tāṃ pitryaṃ taveti | tathā ca "brāhmaṇo hy anadhīyānas tṛṇāgnir iva śāmyati" (MDh 3.168) iti vakṣyamāṇo havye pratiṣedhārthavāda upapadyate | anadhīyāna iti | brāhmaṇas tyāgam arhati kiṃ durācāro vādhīyāno 'yam api na tu [137] ślīpadyādayaḥ | atha vā "pitrye karmaṇi tu prāpte parīkṣeta prayatnataḥ" ity uttarataḥ prakaraṇārtham idaṃ parīkṣāprayatnavacanam | tathā cāha |
ye stenapatitaklībā ye ca nāstikavṛttayaḥ |
tān havyakavyayor viprān anarhān manur abravīt || 3.150 ||
suvarnādyasyāpahartā stenaḥ pṛthagupadeśāt | pratītānāṃ klībaḥ apumān apahataśuklo vā | sati puṃstve nāsti tebhyo vṛttir yasya anāstikasya sataḥ nāstikatvena vā nāstikatvaṃ vā vṛttidharmo yasya sa nāstikavṛttir aśraddadhānaḥ | ete tāvad ubhayatra pratiṣiddhāḥ | śāstraproktānāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | evam uttareṣām api |
jaṭilaṃ cānadhīyānaṃ durbalaṃ kitavaṃ tathā |
yājayanti ca ye pugāṃs tāṃś ca śrāddhe no bhojayet || 3.151 ||
jaṭābhir brahmacāry upalakṣyate | anadhīyānasya prāptir eva nāstīti yataḥ sāmarthyāt prakārāntareṇa vedādhyayanam aparisamāptam asamāptavedo labhyate, asvīkṛtavedo vā | anena ca pratiṣedho 'nujñāyate | īṣadadhyayanasya prāptir astīti daive | na tu pitrye | atha vā cālpādhyayanasya tapasi sthitasya pratiṣedhaḥ | tathā coktam taponiṣṭhā tathāpara iti | upanītamātrasya (?) vānādivedavratatvād anadhīyānasya "vratastham api dauhitram" (MDh 3.234) ity anena prāptasya pratiṣedhaḥ | *durbalaṃ khalatiṃ lohitakeśaṃ vikalendriyam iti trīṇi padāni astavyastaṃ patitānīvātra* pātasyebhyāvāsase(??) pūrvāś śādvalo nāpy alaṃ bhavatīti | vakṣyati ca "prāpnuvanti durātmāno narā rūpaviparyayam" (MDh 11.48) iti | kitavo dyūtakāraḥ | kitavaṃ tathety atra kecit tathāśabdasya sthāne "striyaṃ" paṭhitvā vyākhyāṃ khyānayanti (?) | striyaṃ na bhojayet | striyaṃ śrāddhaprasaṃgāt prāptāṃ vārayati | mātrādibhyaḥ kila tāni yujyante tarāṃ mūlanamambha[1 akṣ unclear]tvā śākapiṇḍibhiḥ brāhmaṇīś cāritratūpasaṃpannā mātṛbhyo bhojayed ity atra brūmaḥ | anena strīśrāddhadarśanena tatrāpratiṣedho manuṣyaṃ śrāddhe cāprāptatvād ayuktastrīpratiṣedha pāthā(?) yatas tathāśabda eva paritarpyadṛṣṭārambhārthavādārthaṃ vā striyaḥ | iti (?) pāṭhaḥ | anye tu punar brahmavādinyā śrotriyatvāt prāptapratiṣedham āhus tat punar nyāyyam anyāyyaṃ veti vicāraṇīyam | "yājayanti ca ye pūgān" gaṇād arthaṃ prāpya yājakāḥ | vrātyastomaiś ca vakṣyamāṇaiḥ | pṛthag agnīn ādāheti vacanād evaṃ teṣāṃ gaṇasya yañjaśrutes tac caitad akāryaṃ prāyaścittena jñāyate | vakṣyati hi "vrātyānāṃ yājanaṃ kṛtvā" (MDh 11.198) iti | eṣāṃ śrāddhe pratiṣedhād deve bhogābhyanujñānam | ataś cānujñānan naite 'tyantam akāryakāriṇo gamyante |
[138]
cikitsakā devalakā māṃsavikrayiṇas tathā |
vipaṇena ca jīvanto varjyāḥ syur havyakavyayoḥ || 3.152 ||
cikitsakadevalakayoḥ pṛthakpratiṣedhasyārthavattvāya jīvikāsaṃbandhena (?) pratiṣedhaḥ sāmarthyād vijñāyate | māṃsavikrayiṇaś ca sakṛd api ye bhavanti "sadyaḥ patanti māṃsena" (MDh 10.92) iti vacanāt | tasmād anyena paṇyena laghunāpy abhyastavikrayeṇa jīvanto varjyāḥ syur ubhayatra |
preṣyo grāmasya rājñaś ca kunakhī śyāvadantakaḥ |
pratiroddhā guroś caiva tyaktāgnir vārdhuṣis tathā || 3.153 ||
grāmarājapreṣyayoḥ karmasaṃbandhamātrāt pratiṣedhaḥ | kunakhīśyāvadatoḥ pūrvakarmāvaśeṣeṇa | pratiroddhā guror yatra tad rājasaṃ pratikulam | pratiraddheti pāṭhāntaram | guror ābhimukhyena hiṃsakaḥ | tyaktāgnir aviśeṣāt | vārdhuṣiś ca pratiṣiddhavṛddhijīvanaḥ |
yakṣmī ca paśupālaś ca parivettā nirākṛtiḥ |
brahmadviṭ parivittiś ca gaṇābhyantara eva ca || 3.154 ||
yakṣmī kṣayī | paśupālasyājīvasaṃbandhena pratiṣedhaḥ | āpuras (aparas?) tatra pātho (pāṭho?) gorakṣaḥ paśupālaś ceti | sa ca gobalīvardanyāyena vyākhyeyaḥ | parivettāraṃ parivittiṃ ca vakṣyati | nirākṛtir nirākartā devarṣimanuṣyāṇām | anaddhā puruṣaḥ ananujīvya ity arthaḥ | tathā ca vājasaneyināṃ śrutiḥ: "addhābhavā yeṣāṃ puruṣo ye devan na (?) namati pitṝn na manuṣyān" | gautamaś ca prāyaścittam asyāha: "agnyutsādinirākṛtyupapātakeṣu (?) caivam" (GDh 22.34) iti | brahmadviṭ brāhmaṇadviṭ (?) brahmabrāhmaṇaśabdayor ekarthatvāt | tathā ca śiṣṭaprayogaḥ: "kuto na paṭhasi brāhmaṇa" iti | śabdadviṣas tūttaratra vidhānād "vedanindaka eva ca" (MDh 3.161) iti | gaṇābhyantaras tu gaṇasamayamadhyagaḥ | atha vā nānājātīyā niyamavṛttayaḥ utsedhajīvinaḥ saṅghā gaṇās tadabhyantaro vā |
kuśīlavo 'vakīrṇī ca vṛṣalīpatir eva ca |
paunarbhavaś ca kāṇaś ca yasya copapatir gṛhe || 3.155 ||
vṛṣalīpatiḥ prāsādavāsī yena kevalo jñeyaḥ | sa coktaḥ punas tad ete nauravimāhiṣakau (?) vyākhyātau | paunarbhavo vakṣyamāṇaḥ | upapatir jāro guṇāpekṣayā |
bhṛtakādhyāpako yaś ca bhṛtakādhyāpitas tathā |
śūdraśiṣyo guruś caiva vāgduṣṭaḥ kuṇḍagolakau || 3.156 ||
utpādyādhyāpakaḥ bhṛtakādhyāpakaḥ gurur yadi yateti (?) bhṛtaiḥ prasiddhatvāt | [139] yadi vai tad evaṃ bhavati tataḥ "adhyāpyā daśa dharmataḥ"(MDh 2.109), "dharmarthau yatra na syātāṃ" (MDh 2.112), "snāsyaṃstu guruṇājñāptaḥ śaktyā gurvartham āharet" (MDh 2.245), "trīṇi karmāṇi jīvikā" (MDh 10.76) ityevamādīni śāstrāṇy arthavanti nānyathā | kuṇḍagolakau vakṣyamāṇau |
akāraṇe parityaktā mātāpitror guros tathā |
brāhmair yaunaiś ca saṃbandhaiḥ saṃyogaṃ patitair saha || 3.157 ||
mātrādīnāṃ parityāge kāraṇaṃ vakṣyati | tena vinā yas teṣāṃ parityaktā sa cābhojanīyaḥ | ṛjv anyat |
agāradāhī garadaḥ kuṇḍāśī somavikrayī |
samudrayāyī bandī ca tailakaḥ kūṭakārakaḥ || 3.158 ||
agāradāhī veśvoddīpakaḥ | prasiddho garadaḥ | kuṇḍāśī vakṣyamāṇaḥ | somavikrayī latā somasya jyotiṣṭomadaivandī (?) pratyūṣasī pītasaṃyuktābhiḥ stutibhiḥ sthāpako rājñām (?) | tailikakūṭakārakau aṅgāradāhābhāve | śmodrī prasiddhau (?) |
pitrā vivadamānaś ca kitavo madyapas tathā |
pāparogy abhiśastaś ca dāmbhiko rasavikrayī || 3.159 ||
pitrā vivadamāno 'dhikasmārtalaukikeṣu padārtheṣu "jaṭilaṃ ca" (MDh 3.151) ityādiśloke cābhihitaḥ kitavaḥ | punar iha kasmād ucyate | pūrvo hi sākṣāt kartā dyūtasyāto mukhye (?) śrāvaṇaṃ (?) puanas tatprayojakaḥ gauṇamukhyayoś ca mukhye saṃpratyayāt | ayam api gauṇo 'yaṃ cāturāṇām abhidhānīyaḥ (?) | avaśyavaktavyatvād ayaṃ punaḥ padārthāntarāt pādasyopadeśe paraśāstrakṣīraṃ savikārām iti (?) | yathā gautamasya (?) madyapagrahaṇasya vā kitavaviśeṣaṇārthatvād ayaṃ vā na kitavaḥ | pūrvas tv akṣititapaḥ (kitavaḥ?) kasmāt punar iha madyapa eva kevalo nocyate | kiṃ te viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvenaivaṃ kalpite | heti pādapūraṇārtham | padyagranthasya | apare tu punaruktibhayāt kekaram atra paṭhanti: "pitrā vivadamānaś ca kekaro madyapas tathā" iti | arthas tu kekaro hy arthadṛṣṭasya krūro bhavty ato (?) heyaḥ | madyapas tv ariṣṭādīnām anyānāṃ pātā | surāpas tu paṭhita (?) eva | yato 'yaṃ (?) śāstrāntare 'nyadyasurāpaḥ (?) paritaṃ [surātaḥ patiatiṃ?] surāmadyapāne cāndrāyaṇam abhyasyed ity ayam api ca vakṣyati | "surābhāṅḍasthtā apaḥ" ity evamādi | [[apaḥ surābhājanasthā madyabhāṇḍasthitās tathā | pañcarātraṃ pibet pītvā śaṅkhapuṣpīśṛtaṃ payaḥ" iti (MDh 11.147) saṃkhyāṅkitaṃ padyam ]] madyaprāyaścittaviśeṣaṃ ca sa ca tatyodādyuktaḥ (?) | pāparogī [140] kuṣṭhī | abhiśastaḥ prasiddhaḥ | ḍāmbhikaḥ dhopadme (?) dharmacaraṇe rasavikrayī viṣavikretā | syahme (?) tadabhidhānam arthavinyāyāṃ dṛṣṭam | tathā copaṃśuvadhe rasapyaindriprayoga (??) ucyate |
dhanuḥ śarāṇām kartā ca yaś cāgredidhiṣūpatiḥ |
mitradhrug dyūtavṛttiś ca putrācāryas tathaiva ca || 3.160 ||
agredidhiṣūpatir ity atra dvayaṃ vāryate | agredidhiṣūr didhiṣūpatiś ca | tatrāgredidhiṣur nāmānūḍhaḥ kanyāgāmī didhiṣūpatis tadudvoḍhaḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ dvayam idaṃ paṭhaty agre "didhiṣur didhiṣūpatiḥ" (GDh 15.16) iti | uttaratas (?) tu kanyādūṣakaṃ vā vidyāt treṇety anyasyāpi madohaḥ (?) pratyavāyāyāyaṃ tv iti śayārtho mitraduhaḥ dyūtavṛttiḥ karayitā etasmād eva ca tadakāryam ity anumīyate | etena dyūtasya kartā vyākhyātaḥ | putrācāryo yena putrād adhītam |
bhrāmarī gaṇḍamālī ca śvitry atho piśunas tathā |
unmatto 'ndhaś ca varjyāḥ syur vedanindaka eva ca || 3.161 ||
bhrāmary apasmārī | gaṇḍamālyādayas tu prasiddhā eva |
hastigo'śvoṣṭradamako nakṣatrair yaś ca jīvati |
pakṣiṇāṃ poṣakaś caiva yuddhācāryas tathaiva ca || 3.162 ||
yuddhācāryaś cāstrakuśalaḥ (?) |
srotasāṃ bhedakaś caiva teṣāṃ cāvaraṇe sthitaḥ |
gṛhasaṃveśako dūto vṛkṣāropaka eva ca || 3.163 ||
dūtaś c rājapreṣyo 'pi kadācid bhavati, rājādhīno 'pi madhyasthatayā jānapado 'pi | vṛkṣāropakas tu mūlyena ropayitā pratiṣidhyate itarasyopadeśasāmarthyāt |
śvakṛīḍī śyenajīvī ca kanyādūṣaka eva ca |
hiṃsro vṛalaputraś ca gaṇānāṃ caiva yājakaḥ || 3.164 ||
kanyādūṣako vā cety uktam, itaras tv agredidhiṣur vyākhyātaḥ | hiṃsraḥ kṣudrajantuvadhārabhya(?) yena dūṣyate | anyas tūkta eva | vṛṣalaputraḥ anutpannetarabhāryāputraḥ śūdraputratayocyate | gaṇānāṃ yājako bahuyājyayājakaḥ laukikagaṇasya vācāṃ (?) ityādikarmaṇā | "yājayanti ca ye pūgān" (MDh 3.151) ity atra vrātyayājako 'bhihitaḥ | laukikālaukikaguṇavibhāgena vā dvirvacanam asya | anye [141] tu punaruktibhayād anyathā paṭhanti | "nṛṇāṃ caiva vyeṣakaḥ (?)" iti | parigrahabhṛtsāmadīkṣām udīkṣaka ity arthaḥ | (?)
ācārahīnaḥ klībaś ca nityaṃ yācanakas tathā |
kṛṣijīvī ślīpadī ca sadbhir nindita eva ca || 3.165 ||
ācāreṇa saṃdhyopāsanādinā hīnaḥ | apacārakṛd (?) api jñeyaḥ (?) | klības tu adhītavedo 'bhihitaḥ | vijñānabhāryo 'pi (?) gṛhakarmānuṣṭhāne bhagnotsāhaḥ | adhikṛtaḥ sann ālasyot (?) klība ucyate | yathārjuno yuddhe bhagnotsāho vāsudevenoktaḥ "klaibyaṃ mā sma gamaḥ pārtha" (BhG 2.3) iti | atha vaikatra napuṃsakaḥ | klībo 'nyatropahatajīvaḥ, saty api puṃstve punarvacanasāmarthyāt | nityaṃ yācanako vidyamānasvaḥ vināpi nimittena yācamāna ucyate | kṛṣijīvī tu yaḥ svayaṃ kartṛtva nānāvadyavyarthakāmopabhogaparo jīvanāyaiva na dharmāya tāṃ karoti sa tv evaṃ (?) nindyate | ślīpadī prasiddhaḥ | siddhaś ca yo nindyate īṣadvyatikrame 'pi sa tadapadhyānāt (?) garhyaḥ |
aurabhriko māhiṣikaḥ parapūrvāpati tathā |
pretaniryāsakaś caiva varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ || 3.166 ||
aurabhramāhiṣakau vyākhyātau || pretaniryāsasakaḥ śāpasya karmaṇaḥ karteti kecit | tad ayuktaṃ śiṣyajñātyādīnām antyakarmapratiṣedhāt | ajñātiṃ tarhi padakāruṇyena (?) dharmārtham | evaṃ ca śrutiḥ "etad vai paramaṃ tapaḥ yat pretam araṇyaṃ nayanti" |
etān vigarhitācārān apāṅkteyān dvijādhamān |
dvijātipravaro vidvān ubhayatra vivarjayet || 3.167 ||
garhitācāravacanād (?) ye tu kāṇaślipadyādayaḥ agarhitācārāḥ syus teṣāṃ yukto havyāvakāśo 'nena śāstreṇa | atha tv agarhitācārāṇām api teṣāṃ tatrānavakāśaḥ | evaṃ tarhi garhitācāravacanam anarthakaṃ bhavati | aśrotriyās te syur ato vā teṣāṃ tatrānavakāśo yuktaḥ syāt tat kāraṇam |
brāhmaṇo hy anadhīyānas tṛṇāgnir iva śāmyati |
tasmai havyaṃ na dātavyaṃ na hi bhasmani hūyate || 3.168 ||
anadhīyāno hi yat brāhmaṇas tyāgam arhatīty uktaṃ śāstrāt | adhīyāno 'pi durācāraḥ [142] yas tu kāṇaślīpadyādiḥ tasya yukto bhojanāvakāśo daive evaṃ kāṇādīnāṃ durācārapāṭhe kavyabhojanapratiṣedhaś cārthavān bhavati | kāṇatvādinā nimittena durācāraśrotriyatvābhyāṃ caiṣāṃ havyabhojanāvakāśaḥ siddho bhavati, viparyāsasāmarthyād iti |
apāṅktyadāne yo dātur bhavaty ūrdhvaṃ phalodayaḥ |
daive karmaṇi pitrye vā taṃ pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ || 3.169 ||
asya pratiṣedhasyāyaṃ nindārthavādaprakaraṇopanyāsārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
avratair yad dvijair bhuktaṃ parivettrādibhis tathā |
apāṅkteyair yad anyaiś ca tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate || 3.170 ||
yāvān kaścid akāryakārī sarvo 'sāv apāṅktyaḥ | eṣāṃ bhojane tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjata ity arthavādo nindārthaḥ | kathaṃ nāma sarvān apāṅktyān (?) varjayed ity evaṃ ca sati nānena rakṣobhyo dāne nindye 'pāṅktyebhyo dānam abhyanujñāyate  | rakṣobhyo dānasyāśiṣṭatvāc chāstre aprakaraṇāc ca tasya sāmānyena yat sarveṣām apāṅktyānāṃ bhojanapratiṣedhārthavādaḥ |
dārāgnihotrasaṃyogaṃ kurute yo 'graje sthite |
parivettā sa vijñeyaḥ parivittis tu pūrvajaḥ || 3.171 ||
atrāginotraśabdena āvasatho 'pi gṛhyate sāmarthāt karmāṅgatvād ubhayoḥ | tathā ca svāgnihotraśabdena grahaṇaṃ kariṣyate | "naiva kanyā na yuvatir nālpavidyo na bāliśaḥ | hotā syād agnihotrasya" (MDh 11.36) ity ubhayoḥ saṃjñāyāś ca śāstreṇaivārthābhidhānam asya vyabhicārasya nindārthavādo varjanārthaḥ |
parivittiḥ parivettā yayā ca parividyate |
sarve te narakaṃ yānti dātṛyājakapañcamāḥ || 3.172 ||
parivettā tāvac chāstrātikrmāt parivittir vighnahetutvāt | kanīyasaḥ karmānuṣṭhāne kanyā ca pratyavāyahetur yena pated evaṃ yo dātṛyājakau ca pratiṣiddhārthānuṣṭhānasahakāritayā pañcaśa[1 akṣ unclear; pañcamau?] dātṛyājakagrahaṇāt sarvakāryeṣu sāhityapratiṣedho 'numīyate | tathā cen nibandhana evāyaṃ śiṣṭaprativādaḥ | puruṣasya bhavanti ye sabhāyāḥ kaluṣe karmaṇi dharmasaṃcaye vā avagacchati tāṃs tadantarātmā niyataṃ te 'pi narās tadaṃśabhāja iti | (?)
bhrātur mṛtasya bhāryāyāṃ yo 'nurajyeta kāmataḥ |
dharmeṇāpi niyuktāyāṃ sa jñeyo didhiṣūpatiḥ || 3.173 ||
[143]
āmnāye apaṭhitaṃ kecic caitaṃ paṭhanti | didhiṣūpatiś cāyam uktenāgredidhiṣuś cātra smṛtyantrasāmarthyena vyākhyātaḥ | didhiṣūpatiś cāyaṃ yaś cāgredidhiṣūpatir ity atra pratiṣedhapare vākye kuṇḍagolakāv idānīṃ nirdiśati |
paradāreṣu yāyete dvau sutau kuṇḍagolakau |
patyau jīvati kuṇḍaḥ syān mṛte bhatari golakaḥ || 3.174 ||
evaṃ ca kuṇḍaḥ syān na mannat (?) kuṇḍāśīti prasiddhaḥ | kuṇḍagrahaṇaṃ golakārtham api syāt, pratiṣedhe sāhacaryopadeśāt | tatra
tau tu jātau parakṣetre prāṇinau pretya ceha ca |
dattāni havyakavyāni nāśayanti pradāyinām || 3.175 ||
ayaṃ cānyo viśeṣārthavādaḥ |
apāṅktyo yāvataḥ paṅktyān bhuñjānān anupaśyati |
tāvatāṃ na phalaṃ tatra dātā prāpnoti bāliśaḥ || 3.176 ||
vīkṣyāndho navateḥ kāṇaḥ ṣaṣṭeḥ śvitrī śatasya tu |
pāparogī sahasrasya dātur nāśayate phalam || 3.177 ||
paṅktigatā ete 'pi paśyanto 'pīty arthaḥ | evaṃ ca "vīkṣyāndho navateḥ kāṇaḥ" ity etad arthavad bhavati, tasya darśanābhāvāt ṣaṣṭyādinirdeśāc ca | yathā kathaṃcin nindārthavādaḥ avivakṣitasaṃkhyāṅkaḥ (?) | adhikasya saṃkhyāviśeṣasyābhyanujñārthaḥ | eṣu satsu,
yāvataḥ saṃspṛśed aṅgair brāhmaṇaṃ śūdrayājakaḥ |
tāvatān na bhaved dātuḥ phalaṃ dānasya paurttikam || 3.178 ||
atrāṅgaśabdo dṛ[1 akṣ unclear]to veditavyaḥ, prakaraṇāt | atha vā paṅktigataḥ evaite apaṅktiyogāt parasparataḥ saṃspṛṣṭā iva bhavantīti | etasmāt sāmānyād ayaṃ saṃsparśa ucyate |
vedavic cāpi vipro 'sya lobhāt kṛtvā pratigraham |
vināśaṃ vrajati kṣipram āmapātram ivāmbhasi || 3.179 ||
vedavid apīti draṣṭavyam asatpratigrahasaṃyogāc cāyaṃ pratiṣedho veditavyaḥ liṅgataḥ |
somavikrayiṇo viṣṭā bhiṣaje pūyaśoṇitam |
apratiṣṭhaṃ vārdhuṣike naṣṭaṃ devalake bhavet || 3.180 ||
[144]
yathā kathaṃcid deyāni vṛttyarthāni (?) [editor thinks this may be from a different verse]
yat tu vāṇijake dattaṃ neha nāmutra tad bhavet |
bhasmanīva hutaṃ havyaṃ tathā paunarbhave dvije || 3.181 ||
itareṣu tv apaṅktyeṣu yathoddiṣṭeṣv asādhuṣu |
medo'sṛṅmāṃsamajjāsthi vadanty annaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ || 3.182 ||
aviśeṣeṇa sarveṣāṃ nindārthavādaḥ | ayam abhiprāyaḥ | na kevalaṃ ninditebhyo dattaṃ niṣphalaṃ bhavati, aparam anartham utpādayati | yuktā cānarthotpattiḥ pratiṣiddhāparaṇā (?) ślokapatsasyām (?) anarthotpattau | yathāśrutakalpanā nyāyavirodhinī muṇḍitvāsāmarthyāt pratyavāyārthataivaiṣā parikalpanīyā |
apāṅktyopahatā paṅktiḥ pāvyate yair dvijottamaiḥ |
tān nibodhata kārtsnyena dvijāgryān paṅktipāvanān || 3.183 ||
nanv apāṅktyānāṃ pratiṣedhāc chrāddhaniyogāsaṃbhave paṅktipāvanopanyāsavacanam anarthakam ity evaṃ tarhy abhijanādhyayanavijñānavatām apy avijñātadoṣāṇāṃ yadṛcchayā niyogapade 'rthād bhavati | kathaṃ paṅktipāvanābhiprayatnataḥ parīkṣya niyuktāḥ "pitrye karmaṇi tu prāpte parīkṣeta prayatnataḥ" (MDh 3.149) ity anena tatrāpaṅktyabhojanadoṣaṃ pāṭhayanti svamahimnā, śrāddhasya vā vaiguṇyaṃ ca kalpayanti, kuta etat | paṅktipāvananiyogopadeśasāmarthyāt | itarathā hi tadupadeśo 'narthakaḥ syāt | atha vā vakṣyamāṇabrāhmaṇastutyartham idaṃ paṅktipāvanam | katham |
agryāḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu sarvapravacaneṣu ca |
śrotriyānvayajāś caiva vijñeyāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ || 3.184 ||
pūrvoktā yathāmī brāhmaṇā abhijātādiguṇasampadā prathamakalpānukalpajā paṅktipāvanā bhavanti, evam amī vakṣyamāṇās triṇāciketādayo 'pi | kasmāt punar ayaṃ ślokaḥ dṛṣṭāntārtha evam ucyate | parārthaṃ na svārtham uktatvāt | eteṣāṃ punarvacanam ihānarthakaṃ syād dṛṣṭāntatve triṇāciketādayas (?) tv anuktā ayathoktaguṇatvād atas teṣāṃ stutyarthapūrvoktānām ayam upadeśo 'nena śloke (1 akṣ lacuna) na dravyanānārtho varṇyate |
triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgnis trisuparṇaḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit |
brahmadeyānusaṃtāno jyeṣṭhasāmika eva ca || 3.185 ||
triṇāciketam adhyayanaṃ vrataṃ sāhacaryāt tac cānūcyate | brāhmaṇaḥ [145] pañcāgnir bahuvrīhiṇoktaḥ | savyāpasavyārthābhyāṃ sahate tayā vānaprasthasya [1 akṣ unclear] prāptiḥ "śīrṣe pañcatapās tu syāt" ity anena trisuparṇabahvṛcavrataṃ tatsāhacaryāt tadvānūcyata ity uktam | aparas tu taddhitapāṭhatvāt (?) sauparṇas tatra bhavatvād ucyate | eteṣām alpādhyayanānām api triṇāciketādibhiḥ kāraṇair iha prāptir asyārambhāsāmarthāt ṣaḍaṅgam idam | sampūrṇādhayano 'pi brahmadeyānusantānadaḥ brāhmavivāhotpannaḥ | jyeṣṭhasāma tacchandogānāṃ tad asyāstīti jyeṣṭhasāmikaḥ | apare tu jyeṣṭhasāmaga iti paṭhanti | teṣām api vyutpattir guṇato vacanīyā |
vedārthavit pravaktā ca brahmacārī sahasradaḥ |
śatāyuś caiva vijñeyā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ || 3.186 ||
vedārthād antareṇāpy aṅgāny athāgamataḥ (?) | tathā ca nairuktāḥ: "na hy eṣu pratyakṣam asty anṛṣeḥ (?) | apāṅkteyā (?) vā" iti | pravaktā śāstrāntarāṇāṃ (?) smṛtiprabhṛtīnām, na vedabāhyānām | vakṣyati hi "asacchāstrādhigamanam" (MDh 11.66), "yā vedabāhyāḥ śrutayaḥ" (MDh 12.95) iti ca | evaṃ "ṣaḍaṅgavit"padāt pravaktṛpadaṃ bhinnārthaṃ vyākhyeyam | brahmacāri ca pūrvavad vacanīyaḥ | śrāddhabhojanapratiṣedhaṃ cāsya samādhāsyāmaḥ | sahasrado vidheyād gavāṃ śatāyuṣo(?) 'napekṣyaitān gaṇān paripakvadoṣaḥ kālena samādhipralambhāt paṅktipāvanaḥ apaṅktyopahatapaṅktir iti (?) vikalpāl liṅgād ebhiḥ (?) sahaikapaṅktibhojane pratyavāyo 'numīyate | evaṃ hi paṅktyupaghātavacanam atrārthavad bhavati | yato 'nyatrāpy ebhiḥ saha na bhuñjīta na caikatra samāsīta | paṅktipāvanānāṃ tu sādhutvam anenābhyupāyenocyate | atha vā taiḥ sahaikapaṅktyāṃ bhojanād abhyudayaṃ pratyavāyānutpattimātraṃ vā yena paṅktipāvanā na paṅktidūṣakās taiḥ sahaikapaṅktyām bhojanenābhyudahaḥ na pratyavāyaḥ | parvāś caivaṃ (?) brāhmaṇaparīkṣā śrāddhabhojane 'py ucyamānā tulyatvāt kāraṇasya, samācārād anyatrāpi draṣṭavyā | yac cāpi daivasaṃkīrtanaṃ na tat pitṛgatam eva kevaladaive 'pi vijñeyam | kāryāviśeṣād anyatra ca daivārthaṃ "vedavidyāvratasnātān" (MDh 4.31) iti sāmānyanirdeśāt | sarvadevārthaṃ draṣṭavyam ity ukto brāhmaṇaparīkṣāvidhiḥ | karmavivakṣayā tv idam ucyate |
pūrvedyur aparedyur vā śrāddhakarmaṇy upasthite |
nimantrayīta tryavarān samyag viprān yathoditān || 3.187 ||
pūrvedyuḥ śrāddhāt pūrvasmin ahani maithunaśaṅkayedam anāgatakālopanimantraṇam | parvapratītau ca maithunasya māsikaśrāddham adinyataitat (?) kṛtārthaṃ durlabhatvād vā tad ahar eva brāhmaṇānām aparedyur apy ekasminn ahani | etatkāraṇadvayābhāve sati tryavarān ity etat kṛtavicāram | ṛjv anyat |
[146]
nimantrito dvijaḥ pitrye niyatātmā bhavet sadā |
na ca chandāṃsy adhīyīta yasya śrāddhaṃ ca tad bhavet || 3.188 ||
ataś ca yuktam anāgatanimantraṇaṃ śrāddhabhujāṃ tadahorātraṃ brahmacaryopadeśārtham idaṃ pitrye niyamo brahmacaryasya | daivatvavacanāc chando'nadhyayanaṃ ca pitryarthaṃ nimantritasya prakaraṇāt tadavasthānaliṅgāc ca chandograhaṇaṃ brāhmaṇārtham api sadā dātary api caitat samānasyārthavādaḥ |
nimantritān hi pitara upatiṣṭhanti tān dvijān |
vāyubhūtānugacchanti tathāsīnān upāsate || 3.189 ||
anatikramastutyarthaḥ ślokaḥ |
nimantrito yathānyāyaṃ havye kavye dvijottamaḥ |
kathaṃcid apy atikrāman pāpaḥ sūkaratāṃ vrajet || 3.190 ||
nimantrite bhokṣya iti pratijñāya yo na bhuṅkte so 'syātikramaḥ |
āmantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe vṛṣalyā saha modate |
dātur yad duṣkṛtaṃ kiṃcit tat sarvaṃ pratipadyate || 3.191 ||
vṛṣalīśabdena brāhmaṇy eva nindyate | yasmād vṛṣasyantī bhartāraṃ calayati brahmacaryād ubhayor doṣe 'pi tat sarvaṃ dhanatvād vratabhaṅgasya yuktā tannindā | abhuktavataś cāyaṃ niyamavyatikramadoṣaḥ | bhuktavatas tu vakṣyati "śrāddhabhug vṛṣalītalpam" (MDh 3.250) iti |
akrodhanāḥ śaucaparāḥ satataṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ |
nyastaśastrā mahābhāgāḥ pitaraḥ pūrvadevatāḥ || 3.192 ||
upanyāsārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
yasmād utpattir eteṣāṃ sarveṣām apy aśeṣataḥ |
ye ca yair upacaryāḥ syur niyamais tān nibodhata || 3.193 ||
manor hairaṇyagarbhasya ye marīcyādayaḥ sutāḥ |
teṣām ṛṣīṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ putrāḥ pitṛgaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || 3.194 ||
virāṭsutāḥ somasadaḥ sādhyānāṃ pitaraḥ smṛtāḥ |
agniṣvāttāś ca devānāṃ mārīcā lokaviśrutāḥ || 3.195 ||
daityadānavayakṣāṇāṃ gandharmoragarakṣasām |
suparṇakinnarāṇāṃ ca smṛtā barhiṣado 'trijāḥ || 3.196 ||
[147]        somapā nāma viprāṇāṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ havirbhujaḥ |
vaiśyānām ājyapā nāma śūdrāṇāṃ tu sukālinaḥ || 3.197 ||
anenaiṣāṃ brāhmaṇādipitṝṇāṃ pitara ucyante |
somapās tu kaveḥ putrā haviṣmanto 'ṅgirasaḥ smṛtāḥ |
pulastyasyājyapāḥ putrā vasiṣṭhasya sukālinaḥ || 3.198 ||
agnidagdhānagnidagdhān kavyabarhiṣado 'pahān |
agniṣvāttāṃś ca saumyāṃś ca viprāṇām eva nirdiśet || 3.199 ||
ya ete tu gaṇā mukhyāḥ pitṝṇāṃ parikīrtitāḥ |
teṣām apīha vijñeyaṃ putrapautram anantakam || 3.200 ||
ṛṣibhyaḥ pitaro jātāḥ pitṛbhyo devadānavāḥ |
devebhyaś ca jagat sarvaṃ caraṃ sthāṇv anupūrvaśaḥ || 3.201 ||
aṣṭau ślokāḥ pitṝṇām utpattyarthāḥ stutitvam āpadyante prakaraṇasāmarthāt nyāyāvirodhena vyākhyeyāḥ | anadhikṛtānāṃ ca daityādīnāṃ parikīrtanāt | na cet somapādīnām api viprādigrahaṇena grahaṇam arthavādo gamyate | evaṃnāmānaś ca pitaro vedeṣu paṭhyanta ity ata eteṣāṃ vividhair nāmabhiḥ parikīrtitam | na caite pitaraḥ kadāpi tadutpannasthānādhikāranityatvāt | karmasiddhaye tu sādhanabhāvena nityaṃ vyavasthitānām iyam utpattis tatra kathaṃ tāvat stutyarthā | vidhiprarocanāyocyate | katham | devādīnām eva tāvat pitaraḥ śrāddhena pūjanīyāḥ | kiṃ punar manuṣyāṇām iti | tatpūjāyai tv adhunā karmaṇo guṇavidhir ucyate |
rājatair bhājanair eṣām api vā rājatānvitaiḥ |
vāry api śraddhayā dattam akṣayāyopakalpate || 3.202 ||
niyamo rājatānāṃ tu rajatānvitānāṃ cātha vākṣayyakāmasya rājatāni syur apara evaṃ ca naimittikatvaṃ rājatānāṃ prāpnoti | na caitad iṣṭaṃ saṃstutiparatvād akṣayyaśabdasya | apare tu daṇḍāpūpikayā sauvarṇety api paṭhanti, tāny adhikataraphalānīti | tad ayuktam | nyāyasya vyavasthitatvād anyatra doṣaḥ syād ato yathāśrutakalpanaiva jyāyasī | paras tu guṇavidhiḥ |
daivakāryād dvijātīnāṃ pitṛkāryaṃ viśiṣyate |
daivaṃ hi pitṛkāryasya pūrvam āpyāyanaṃ smṛtam || 3.203 ||
[148]
yataś caitad evam ataḥ |
teṣām ārakṣabhūtaṃ tu pūrvaṃ daivaṃ niyojayet |
rakṣāṃsi hi vilumpanti śrāddham ārakṣavarjitam || 3.204 ||
iṣṭan daivatam uddiśya daivaṃ pūrvaṃ niyoktavyam aviśeṣād vā sarvadaivatam ārakṣabhūtaṃ cet pitryasya tat | evam evaṃ (?) ca sati,
daivādyantaṃ tad īheta pitryādyantaṃ na tad bhavet |
pitrādhyantaṃ tv īhamānaḥ kṣipraṃ naśyati sānvayaḥ || 3.205 ||
"pūrvaṃ daivaṃ niyojayed" (MDh 3.204) ity asya vidheḥ stutyarthaṃ ta(1 akṣ lacuna) t | vyutkramaṃ ca śrāddhe sarvam anyat syād iti kramārthaṃ pūrvam eva cāsmāt –
śuciṃ deśaṃ viviktaṃ ca gomayenopalepayet |
dakṣināpravaṇāṃ caiva prayatnenopapādayet || 3.206 ||
yasmāt,
avakāśeṣu cokṣeṣu jalatīreṣu caiva hi |
vivikteṣu ca tuṣyanti dattena pitaraḥ sadā || 3.207 ||
agomayenā(o?)palipteṣv api svabhāvaśuciṣu | tatra ca –
āsaneṣūpakḷpteṣu barhiṣmatsu pṛthak pṛthak |
upasṛṣṭodakān samyag dvijāṃs tān upaveśayet || 3.208 ||
evam –
upaveśya tu tān sarvān āsaneṣv ajugupsitān |
gandhamālyaiḥ surabhibhir arcayed devapūrvakam || 3.209 ||
teṣām udakam ānīya sapavitrāṃs tilān api |
agnau kuryād anujñāto brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇaiḥ sadā || 3.210 ||
agneḥ somayamābhyāṃ ca kṛtvāpyāyanam āditaḥ |
havirdānena vidhivat paścāt saṃtarpayet pitṝn || 3.211 ||
agnyābhāve tu viprasya pāṇāv evopapādayet |
yo hy agniḥ sa dvijo vipraiḥ mantradarśibhir ucyate || 3.212 ||
[149]        akrodhanān suprasādān vadanty etān purātanān |
lokasyāpyāyane yuktāñ śrāddhe devān dvijottamān || 3.213 ||
tasmād ete cākuddhāḥ (?) paricaraṇīyāḥ | yasmād ete suprasannāḥ |
apasavyaṃ tu kṛtvāgnau sarvam āvṛtparikramam |
apasavyena hastena nirvaped udakaṃ bhuvi || 3.214 ||
trīṃs tu tasmād dhaviḥśeṣān piṇḍān kṛtvā samāhitaḥ |
audakenaiva vidhinā nirvaped dakṣināmukhaḥ || 3.215 ||
nyupya piṇḍāṃs tatas tāṃs tu prayato vidhipūrvakam |
teṣu darbheṣu taṃ hastaṃ nimṛjyāl lepabhāginām || 3.216 ||
dvau ca lepabhāginau parateḥ (?) | apare tu trīn āhuḥ |
ācamyodakparāvṛtya trir āyamya śanair asūn |
ṣaḍ apy ṛtūn namaskuryāt pitṝn eva ca mantravit || 3.217 ||
"namo vaḥ pitara" ity etena |
udakcheṣaṃ ca ninayet śanaiḥ piṇḍāntikaṃ punaḥ |
avajighrec ca tān piṇḍān yathānyuptān samāhitaḥ || 3.218 ||
piṇḍebhyas tv alpikāṃ mātrāṃ samādāyānupūrvaśaḥ |
tān eva viprān āsīnān vidhivat pūrvam āśayet || 3.219 ||
caturdaśaślokāḥ sphuṭārthatvān na vyākhyātāḥ | atra ca śrautasya karmaṇaḥ piṇḍapitṛyajñasya śrāddhakarmaṇo gatva(?) vidhyarthaṃ grahaṇam āhitāgner api vāstri(?) piṇḍapitṛyajñaḥ āvasathye | tathā ca sūtrakāraḥ: "āhitāgner apy evam āhuḥ" iti |
dhriyamāṇe tu pitari pūrveṣām eva nirvapet |
satyām apy aviśeṣeṇa dānacodanāyāṃ liṅgaprasiddhyā jīvati pitari yajamānasya pretebhyaḥ | pitur ūrdhvaṃ tribhyo yukto nirvāpaḥ | evaṃ hi nityasya karmaṇaḥ uparilokaḥ kalpito bhaviṣyati | ā saptamāc ca samānapiṇḍatā sā caivam arthavatī bhaviṣyati | tathā hi, kevalāśaucavidheḥ samayārthatā syād yady api na caturthaṃ piṇḍo gacchatīti | naivātra caturthasya piṇḍadānaṃ pitur ūrdhvaṃ tathā ca vakṣyati: "trayāṇām udakaṃ kāryaṃ triṣu piṇḍaḥ pravartate" (MDh 9.186) | evaṃ ca śāstrāntaram: [150] "pitāputrau cedānīṃ sāgnī syāyām  | yebhya eva pitā dadyāt tebhyaḥ putro dadāti hi" |
vipravad vāpi taṃ śrāddhaṃ svakaṃ pitaram āśrayet (?) || 3.220 ||
parataś ca dvābhyām eva pretābhyāṃ dadyāt | tathā ca jātūkarṇiḥ: "na jīvantam atidadātīti śruteḥ" ity āha | "caturthaṃ piṇḍo na gacchati" iti | evaṃ ca satyāṃ pitṛprītau kartavyatāyāṃ ko jīvati pitari bhaved yataḥ | sākṣād evāsau āśayitavyaḥ | aparaḥ pakṣo jīvatpitṛkasya homāntaṃ karmanāgraṃbho (?) vā karmaṇaḥ |
pitā yasya tu vṛttaḥ syāj jīvec cāpi pitāmahaḥ |
pituḥ sa nāma saṃkīrtya kīratyet prapitāmaham || 3.221 ||
pitāmahasya nāmagrāhapratiṣedho 'tra piṇḍanivṛttyarthaḥ | yasmāt pretānāṃ pitṛpūjādipitṛpūjādhikāraḥ śāstre pretebhya udāharatīti vacanāt | tasmāt jīvatpitāmahaḥ pretayor eva dadyāt | na jīvate dānam upapadyate | na caturthasya parasya dātavyaṃ "na caturthaṃ piṇḍo gacchati" iti vacanāt | tasmād dvābhyām eva dātavyam iti | evaṃ ca idānīṃ jīvatpitṛko 'pi dvābhyām eva dadyāt tulyatvāt kāraṇe 'syeti punar brūmaḥ | kutaḥ | vyākhyātam asmābhir na caturthaṃ piṇḍo gacchatīti, piṇḍatānugrahasāmarthāc ca tribhya eva dānaṃ vijñeyam | tathā coktam "triṣu piṇḍaḥ pravartate" (MDh 9.186) iti |
pitāmaho vā tacchrāddhaṃ bhuñjītety abravīn manuḥ  |
ity etat pūrvaślokārdhena samānam |
kāmaṃ vā samanujñātaḥ svayam eva samācaret || 3.222 ||
kenānujñātaḥ | prakṛtatvāt pitāmahena | svayam eva yady uktaṃ tat samācaret | kiṃ punar yuktam | vāmapakṣāt tadvivakṣāśrayaṇaṃ katham | trayāṇām adhikṛtatvāt, pretabhāvaṃ ca gatānāṃ pūjārhatvāt | śrāddhaṃ pratyakṣārcanam ayuktam | pitāmahaḥ syād atha vā jīvato (?) 'narcanīyatvāt trayāṇāṃ yathā na niyamād anadhikāra evaṃ, jīvatpitāmahasya śrāddhadāneneti homāntaṃ vety uktam | etena jīvatprapitāmaho 'pi vyākhyāto vijñeyaḥ | samānatvāt kārantasyeti (?) |
teṣāṃ datvā tu hasteṣu sapavitraṃ tilodakam |
tatpiṇḍāgraṃ prayacchetu svadhaiṣām astv iti bruvan || 3.223 ||
pāṇibhyāṃ tūpasaṃgṛhya svayam annasya vardhitam |
[151]        viprāntike pitṝn dhyāyan śanakair upanikṣipet || 3.224 ||
ubhayor hastayor muktaṃ yad annam upanīyate |
tad vipralumpanty asurāḥ sahasā duṣṭacetasaḥ || 3.225 ||
guṇāṃś ca sūpaśākādyān payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu |
vinyaset prayataḥ pūrvaṃ svayam eva samāhitaḥ || 3.226 ||
bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca vividhaṃ mūlāni ca phalāni ca |
hṛdyāni caiva māṃsāni pānāni surabhīṇi ca || 3.227 ||
upanīya samaṃ vaitac chanakaiḥ susamāhitaḥ |
pariveṣayet prayato guṇān sarvāṇi vedayan || 3.228 ||
nāsram āpātayej jātu na kupyān nānṛtaṃ vadet |
na pādena spṛśed annaṃ na vaitad avadhūnayet || 3.229 ||
āsraṅ gamayati pretān kopo 'rīn anṛtaṃ sunaḥ |
pādasparśas tu rakṣāṃsi duṣkṛtīn avadhūnanam || 3.230 ||
yad yad roceta viprebhyas tat tad dadyād amatsaraḥ |
brahmodyāś ca kathāḥ kuryāt pitṝṇām etad īpsitam || 3.231 ||
svādhyāyaṃ śrāvayet pitrye dharmaśāstrāṇi caiva hi |
ākyānānītihāsāṃś ca purāṇāni khilāni ca || 3.232 ||
harṣayed brāhmaṇāṃs tuṣṭo bhojayec ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
annādyenāsakṛc cainān guṇaiś ca paricodayet || 3.233 ||
vratastham api dauhitraṃ śrāddhe yatnena bhojayet |
kutapaṃ cāsanaṃ dadyāt tilaiś ca vikiren mahīm || 3.234 ||
vratastham api dauhitraṃ pitrya eva prakaraṇāt yatnavacanam | tad evaṃ tu tṛcā niyama (?) eva vratasthagrahaṇam | dauhitrastutyartham | "vratavad devadaivatye" (MDh 2.189) ityādi | yatas tad adoṣaḥ (?) "māsikārthaṃ tu yo 'śnīyāt" (MDh 11.158) ityatra tatraiva parihāraṃ vakṣyāmaḥ | dauhitrasya cānuktaproktatvād (?) ānukalpikebhya eva mātāmahādibhyaḥ tasya jyāyastvam ucyate | kutapasya ca | dauhitrasaṃniyoge vidhānam atha vā āsanadānaprakaraṇe [152] aparimitatvāt kutapasyānukalpike taddauhitreṇa sahopadeśād asyānukalpika etāmahamyateti (?) | kasmāt punar etad evam | yasmāt –
trīṇi śrāddhe pavitrāṇi dauhitraḥ kutapas tilāḥ |
trīṇi cātra praśaṃsanti śaucam akrodham atvarām || 3.235 ||
śaucādīnāṃ praśaṃsāvacanaṃ vidhyartham | apare tu satyam | pare tu satyam akrodham atvarām iti |
atyuṣṇaṃ sarvam annaṃ syād bhuñjīraṃs te ca vāgyatāḥ |
na ca dvijātayo brūyur dātrā pṛṣṭā (?) havirguṇān || 3.236 ||
abhinayenāpi | niyamavidhyarthaḥ ślokaḥ | kāraṇaṃ vakti |
yāvad uṣmā bhavaty annaṃ yāvad aśnanti vāgyatāḥ |
pitaras tāvad aśnanti yāvan noktā havirguṇāḥ || 3.237 ||
vidhyarthā stutir ūṣmādīnām |
yad veṣṭitaśirā bhuṅkte yad bhuṅkte dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
sopānatkaś ca yad bhuṅkte tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate || 3.238 ||
akṛtānnaśrāddhe tu prāptānām eṣāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | vakṣyati "prāṇi vā yadi vāprāṇi" (MDh 4.117) iti | bhuṅkta iti | grahītur hastena draṣṭavyasambandha ucyate | "pāṇyāsyo hi dvijaḥ smṛtaḥ" (MDh 4.117) iti vacanāt | bhojane 'pi cārthaprāptir astv eva | ekadā yathāsambhavam arthapratiṣedho 'yam adhikṛtaniyamastutyarthaḥ | sarvaprakāre ca śrāddhe,
caṇḍālaś ca varāhaś ca kukkuṭaḥ śvā tathaiva ca |
rajasvalā ca ṣaṇḍaś ca nekṣerann aśnato dvijān || 3.239 ||
tasmāt parīkṣite dadyāj jalatīrapulinādiṣu | aparīkṣitadeśeṣu prāptānāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | yasmāt,
home pradāne bhojye ca yad ebhir abhivīkṣyate |
daive karmaṇi pitrye ca tad gacchaty ayathātatham || 3.240 ||
ete 'pi vidhayo daive pitrye vacanāt syuḥ | viśeṣāt tv idaṃ bravīti –
ghrāṇena sūkaro hanti pakṣavātena kukkuṭaḥ |
śvā tu dṛṣṭinipātena sparśenāvaravarṇajaḥ || 3.241||
[153]
nekṣetann (3.239) ity asya vidheḥ sarva ete 'rthavādāḥ | aśucayaḥ sarva ete paśyanto 'py upaghnantīty ato nekṣeran na ca saṃnikṛṣṭā dṛṣṭtinipāte (?) vā śrāddhasy syuḥ | aparavarṇajaṃ ca caṇḍālaṃ kecid āhuḥ | tad ayuktaṃ tatsargasya sāmānyaśāstreṇaiva pratiṣiddhatvāt | tena śūdro 'varavarṇajaḥ dvijātiśrāddheṣu sparśanopaghātakṛd veditavyaḥ, nātmīyaśrāddhe tasya tatrānuśiṣṭatvāt | nṛṇām (?) avaravarṇajaś caṇḍāla eva prakṛtaḥ | "caṇḍālaś ca varāhaś ca" (MDh 3.239) iti īkṣaṇapratiṣedhaś ca | tata iha sparśenāvaravarṇaja iti sparśapratiṣedhaḥ | yataś caṇḍālasyāprāptisparśe śūdrasya svasti (?) |
khañjo vā yadi vā kāṇo dātuḥ preṣyo 'pi vā bhavet |
hīnātiriktagātro vā tam apy apanayet tataḥ || 3.242 ||
khañjādayaḥ śūdrā veditavyāḥ śāstrārambhasāmarthyāt | preṣyāś cāpreṣyāś ca |
brāhmaṇaṃ bhikṣukaṃ vāpi bhojanārtham upasthitam |
brāhmaṇair apy anujñātaḥ śaktitaḥ pratipūjayet || 3.243 ||
anyam āmantritebhyas tatkāla āgatam atithiṃ bhikṣukaṃ vā tair abhyanujñāta iti yuktam idaṃ tadarthatvāt proktasya |
sārvavarṇikam annādyaṃ saṃnīyāplāvya vāriṇā |
samutsṛjed bhuktavatām agrato vikiran bhuvi || 3.244 ||
saṃskṛtapramītānāṃ tyāgināṃ kulayoṣitām |
ucchiṣṭaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ syād darbheṣu vikirañ ca yaḥ || 3.245 ||
uccheṣaṇam bhūmigatam ajihmasyāśaṭhasya ca |
dāsavargasya tat pitrye bhāgadheyaṃ pracakṣate || 3.246 ||
ṛjvarthās trayaḥ ślokāḥ | "darbheṣu vikirañ ca yaḥ" iti | na ca nādarbheṣv eva vāso deya iti gamyate, na bhūmau | itarathā hi darbhagrahaṇam arthakaraṃ syāt | evaṃ tāvat sapitrordhvam (?) | idaṃ cānyat –
ā sapiṇḍakriyākarma dvijāteḥ saṃsthitasya tu |
atraivaṃ bhojayec chrāddhaṃ piṇḍam ekaṃ ca nirvapet || 3.247 ||
navaśrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍāḥ saha kuryuḥ | piṇḍam ekaṃ ca nirvapet | ity ekasyaiva śrāddhakarma syāt sā ceyam asapiṇḍakriyā saṃvatsarāt bhavati | dadyād ekasmai piṇḍaṃ caikavac ca mantrāntaṃ hetutvāt (?) taṃ ca śāstrāntaraprasiddhā tatraikatā tad evaṃ ca na kuryāt | bhuvi caikam eva piṇḍaṃ nirvaped yadā tu "sannayaṃ [154] tu tvā pitaraḥ" iti mantravarṇāt piṇḍasaṃsargo bhavati tadā –
sahapiṇḍakriyāyāṃ tu kṛtāyām asya dharmataḥ |
anayaivāvṛtā kāryaṃ piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ sutaiḥ || 3.248 ||
uktayaivāvṛtā tadūrdhvaṃ tasya putrāḥ strībhyo dadyuḥ |
śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā ya ucchiṣṭaṃ vṛṣalāya prayacchati |
sa mūḍho narakaṃ yāti kālasūtram avākśirāḥ || 3.249 ||
viśeṣapratiṣedho 'yam |
śrāddhabhig vṛṣalītalpaṃ tad ahar yo 'dhigacchati |
tasyāḥ purīṣe taṃ māsaṃ pitaras tasya śerate || 3.250 ||
vyatikramanindā anatikramastuyarthā | vṛṣalī ca vyākhyātā |
pṛṣṭvā svaditam ity evaṃ tṛptān ācāmayet tataḥ |
ācāntāṃś cānujānīyād abhito ramyatām iti || 3.251 ||
svadhāstv ity eva taṃ brūyur brāhmaṇās tadanantaram |
svadhākāraḥ parā hy āśīḥ sarveṣu pitṛkarmasu || 3.252 ||
tato bhuktavatāṃ teṣām annaśeṣaṃ nivedayet |
yathā brūyus tathā kuryād anujñātas tair dvijaiḥ || 3.253 ||
ṛjvarthāḥ pañcaślokāḥ |
pitrye svaditam ity evaṃ vācyaṃ goṣṭheṣu tu suśṛtam |
saṃpannam ity abhyudaye daive rucitam ity api || 3.254 ||
bhojanakāla etaiḥ śabdaiḥ sambhāvayitavyā iti niyama ucyate 'tha vābhyanujñānaṃ bhojana ebhiḥ śabdaiḥ kriyate | tathā ca sati svadatām ity evamādiḥ pāṭho vijñeyaḥ |
aparāhṇas tathā darbhā vāstusaṃpādanaṃ tilāḥ |
sṛṣṭir mṛṣṭir dvijāś cāgryāḥ śrāddhakarmasu saṃpadaḥ || 3.255 ||
aparāhṇādīnāṃ stutyarthaṃ punar grahaṇam | tato 'nyeṣāṃ vā vikalpārtham |
darbhāḥ pavitraṃ pūrvāhṇo haviṣyāṇi ca sarvaśaḥ |
pavitraṃ yac ca pūrvoktaṃ vijñeyā havyasaṃpadaḥ || 3.256 ||
[155]
agradarbhādayā eva karma saṃpadaṃ te daive karmaṇi kāryāḥ | sampacchabdāc ca vikalpo 'numīyate |
munyannāni payaḥ somo māṃsaṃ yac cānupaskṛtam |
akṣāralavaṇaṃ caiva prakṛtyā havir ucyate || 3.257 ||
haviṣyāṇīty uktvemāni haviṣyāṇi nidarśayati | payograhaṇaṃ sarvapayovikārārtham |
visṛjya brāhmaṇāṃs tāṃs tu niyato vāgyataḥ śuciḥ |
dakṣiṇāṃ diśam ākāṅkṣan yācetemān varān pitṝn || 3.258 ||
dātāro no 'bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ saṃtatir eva ca |
śraddhā ca no mā vyagamad bahudeyaṃ ca no 'stv iti || 3.259 ||
evaṃ nirvapaṇaṃ kṛtvā piṇḍāṃs tāṃs tadanantaram |
gāṃ vipram ajam agnīṃś ca prāśayed apsu vā kṣipet || 3.260 ||
evaṃ pitṛvarayācanānantaraṃ yajamānaḥ piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kṛtvā pitṛpūjitaprajāyai tān pitṛyajñaviddhāṃs tadanantaraṃ gavādīni prāśayet | kecit tu vikirapiṇḍānām etāṃ pratipattim āhuḥ | tad ayuktam | tatra piṇḍānāṃ tāvat purastān nirvāpo virudhyeta | anantaraślokopadiṣṭaḥ pañyāś ca madhyamapiṇḍavirodhaḥ syāt tadabhāvāt | pitṛyajñapiṇḍeṣu tu sarvam etad avirodhāt saṃbhavati –
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kecit purastād eva kurvati |
kuru tu śrāddhabhojanāt (?) |
