vayobhiḥ khādayanty etān prakṣipanty anale 'psu vā || 3.261 ||
ṛjvarthāḥ ślokāḥ (?) | na tu sarvān eva vayaḥprabhṛtibhyo dadyāt | kiṃ tarhi |
pativratā dharmapatnī pitṛpūjanatatparā |
madhyamaṃ tu tataḥ piṇḍam adyāt samyak sutārthinī || 3.262 ||
āyuṣmantaṃ sutaṃ vinded yaśomedhāsamanvitam |
dhanavantaṃ prajāvantaṃ dhārmikaṃ sāttvikaṃ tathā || 3.263 ||
madhyamapiṇḍaprāśanārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
prakṣālya hastau vācamya jñātiprāyaṃ prakalpayet |
[156]        jñātibhyaḥ satkṛtaṃ datvā bāndhāvān api pūjayet || 3.264 ||
śrāddhaṃ (ddhe?) pariṣicya niyamanam idam ucyate | jñātyādibhyo dārañca (?) (dātavyaṃ?)
uccheṣaṇaṃ ca tat tiṣṭhed yāvad viprā visarjitāḥ |
tato gṛhabaliṃ kuryād iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ || 3.265 ||
havir yac cirakālāya yac cānantyāya kalpate |
pitṛbhyo vidhivad dattaṃ tat pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ || 3.266 ||
upanyāsārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
tilavrīhiyavair māṣair adbhir mūlaphalena vā |
dattena māsaṃ tṛpyanti (?) vidhivat pitaro nṛṇām || 3.267 ||
yāvat kiṃcid apratiṣiddham ādyaṃ tatsarvaṃ śravaṇāt, jñāpakāt gṛhyate |
dvau mātsyena tu māṃsena trīn māsān hāriṇena tu |
arubhreṇātha caturaḥ śākunenātha pañca vai || 3.268 ||
ṣaṇmāsāṃś chāgamāṃsena pārṣatena ca sapta vai |
aṣṭāv eṇasya māṃsena rauraveṇa navaiva tu || 3.269 ||
daśamāsāṃs tu tṛpyanti varāhamahiṣāmiṣaiḥ |
śaśakūrmayos tu māṃsena māsān ekādaśaiva tu || 3.270 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyena payasā pāyasena vā |
vārdhrīṇasasya māṃsena tṛptir dvādaśavārṣikī || 3.271 ||
kālaśākaṃ mahāśaṃkhaṃ (?) khaṅgalohām iṣaṃ madhu |
ānantyāyaiva kalpante munyannāni ca sarvaśaḥ || 3.272 ||
yat kiṃcin madhunā miśraṃ pradadyāt tat trayodaśīm |
tad apy akṣayam eva syād varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca || 3.273 ||
api naḥ sa kule jāyād yo no dadyāt trayodaśīm |
pāyasaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ prākcchāye kuñjarasya tu || 3.274 ||
"dvau māsau (mātsyena?)" itya ata ārabhya saptaślokāḥ phalaviśeṣāpekṣayā stutiviśeṣaṇārthāḥ sākṣād atithitṛptivat pitṛtṛpter anupalabdheḥ stutyarthā evaite kalpe tu nyāyyāḥ (?) [157] yena kuryān māsānumāsi kim ity aviśeṣeṇātra pitṛbhyo dānaṃ codyate | na ca chāgādimāṃsadāne sati dvau na mātsyena (?) dāhatavyam iti kiṃcid atra kāraṇam abhidhātuṃ śakyam, pratimāsaṃ coditatvāt | śrāddhasya śakyante cāmī na nyāyena stutyarthāḥ kalpayituṃ śrutibādhitatvāt tṛptānām api vaiśdyād (?) vidhisāmarthād vā punas tṛptisāmartham anumānasya te yadi tṛpyase (?) ṣandānaṃ (?) syād bahūnāṃ bhrātṝṇām ekena tarpitatvād itareṣām apravṛttiḥ syāt | api ca devatābhogena viśiṣṭaḥ pitṛbhogaḥ | katham | yathaiva hi daivatānāṃ sampradānatvam avagacchantīnāṃ tṛptivādaḥ | evaṃ pitṝṇām api draṣṭavyam |
yad yad dadāti vidhivat samyak śraddhāsamanvitaḥ |
tat tat pitṝṇāṃ bhavati paratrānantam akṣayam || 3.275 ||
śrādhāṅgīkaraṇārtho 'yaṃ yatnaḥ | evaṃ sati yad yad dadātīty anenādaravacanena pūrvaśāstrāvirodhaḥ śrāddhastutiparatvād asyopadeśasāmarthyād vijñeyaḥ |
kṛṣṇapakṣe daśamyādau varjayitvā caturdaśīm |
śrāddhe praśastās tithayo yathaitā na tathetarāḥ || 3.276 ||
tasyāṃ śaktau yajamānasya pūrvatithyapekṣayā daśamyādiviśeṣasya dānasyābhyudayaviśeṣārtha ārambhaḥ |
yathā caivāparaḥ pakṣaḥ pūrvapakṣād viśiṣyate |
tathā śrāddhasya pūrvāhṇād aparāhṇo viśiṣyate || [MDh 3.278]
iti vacanāt | pūrvāhṇaprāptir asti śrāddhe | nanu cedam aprasiddhodāharaṇam | "yathā caivāparaḥ pakṣaḥ pūrvapakṣād viśiṣyate" [MDh 3.278] iti śuklapakṣāt kṛṣṇa iti tathetarā iti nedam asya prāpakaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣasaṃnidhāv asyopadeśāc chuklapakṣaprasiddhir nāsmāt | evaṃ tarhy agranthasmaraṇānuvāde (?) 'yam asya dṛṣṭāntārthaḥ | athaitasmād evobhayasiddhiḥ sāmarthyād vijñāyate | gautamīye ca sagranthakam evedaṃ smaraṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | daśabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhāne vety evaṃ ca | tadapekṣam idaṃ bhaviṣyati |
yukṣu kurvan dinarkṣeṣu sarvān kāmān samaśnute |
ayukṣu tu pitṝn arcan prajāṃ prāpnoti puṣkalām || 3.277 ||
kṛṣṇadaśamīprabhṛtiṣu yukṣu nakṣatreṣu cāhuḥ | arcan (?) prajām avāpnotīty abhipretam | kāmyo 'yaṃ vidhiḥ | atha vā stutyarthatvād etayor abhyudayaviśeṣārtha ārambhaḥ | kecit tv etasmāc chrādhāt kṛṣṇapakṣaprabhṛtiyugāyugmopadeśaṃ dinarkṣayoś ca nayanti kāmyam | tad ayuktam | daśamyādāv ity adhikṛtatvāt | ity anupadṛṣṭārtham idam | śrāddhāṅgam adhunocyate |
[158]
yathācaivāparah pakṣaḥ pūrvapakṣād viśiṣyate |
tathā śrāddhasya pūrvāhnād aparāhṇo viśiṣyate || 3.278 ||
prācīnāvītinā samyag apasavyam atandriṇā |
pitryam ā nidhanāt kāryaṃ vidhivad darbhapāṇinā || 3.279 ||
śrāddhāṅgaguṇavidhyarthaḥ ślokaḥ |
rātrau śrāddhaṃ na kurvīta rākṣasī kīrtitā hi sā |
saṃdhyayor ubhayoś caiva sūrye caivācirodite (?) || 3.280 ||
aprāptānāṃ rātryādīnāṃ śrāddhāṅgatvena pratiṣedhād aparāhṇād anyatrāpi śrāddhakarma bhavatīti gamyate | itarathā syād anarthako 'syārambhaḥ prasajyate | etasmiṃś ca sati pūrvāhṇaprāptyarthaṃ śrāddhaṃ yujyate | tad uktam "tathā śrāddhasya puṛvāhṇād aparāhṇo viśiṣyate" [MDh 3.278] ity ataḥ kṛtānnaśrāddhapakṣe caitat syāt |
anena vidhinā nityaṃ trir abdasyeha nirvapet |
hemantagrīṣmavarṣāsu pāñcayajñikam anvaham || 3.281 ||
nirvacanam idam anāhitāgner iti vakṣyaty anantaram eva |
na paitṛyajñiko homo (?) laukike 'gnau vidhīyate |
na darśena vinā śrāddham āhitāgner dvijanmanaḥ || 3.282 ||
na paitṛyajñiko homo (?) laukike 'gnau yasmād vidhīyate, tasmād anāhitāgner māsānumāsikaṃ niyamato na bhavati | śrāddhaṃ na ca darśena vinā śrāddham anāhitāgner yasmād vidhīyate | tasmād āhitāgner api triḥ saṃvatsaravidhiḥ saṃbhavati | kiṃ tarhi | niyamato māsānumāsikaṃ tadaṅgatvād bhavati |
yad eva tarpayaty adbhiḥ pitṝn snātvā dvijottamaḥ |
tenaiva sarvam āpnoti pitṛyajñakriyāphalam || 3.283 ||
anena caivodakatarpaṇena nityam aharahaḥ kriyamāṇena śrāddham anuṣṭhitaṃ bhavati | kiṃ punas trisaṃvatsarasyeti | atha vā viśeṣaśrāddhastutiparam etad vijñeyam |
vasūn vadanti tu pitṝn rudrān eva pitāmahān |
prapitāmahāṃs tathādityāñ chrutir eṣā purātanī || 3.284 ||
[159]
yasmād vasvādayaḥ pitrādayaḥ smaryante | tasmāt pitrādīn anupekṣyaiva śrāddhaṃ deyaṃ nityārtham idam | na pitṛdveṣibhir na vartayitavyam | yasmād "devā hy ete pitṛnāmānaḥ" iti smaryante | atha vā avijñātapitrādīnām abhiṣyajamānair (abhidhīyamānair?) vasvādiśabdena pitryam anuṣṭheyam udakadānādi |
vighasāśī bhaven nityaṃ nityaṃ vāmṛtabhojanaḥ |
vighaso bhuktaśeṣaṃ (?) tu yajñaśeṣaṃ tathāmṛtam || 3.285 ||
adhikṛtakarmānuṣṭhānastutiparam etan nāpūrvavidhyartham |
etad vo 'bhihitaṃ sarvaṃ vidhānaṃ pāñcayajñikam |
dvijātimukhyavṛttīnāṃ vidhānaṃ śrūyatām idam || 3.286 ||
upasaṃhāro 'yam | upanyāsārthaḥ ślokaḥ | ṛtādīnāṃ ca vṛttīnāṃ saṃparikīrtanād dvijātimukhyaśabdo brāhmaṇārtha eveti |
iti ślaghyasmṛtisāyujyācāryaguror anartīyasya kṛtau manuśāstravivaraṇe tṛtīyo 'dhyāyaḥ |
śrī śrī om (nandināgarīlipyā likhitam etat)
manuśāstravivaraṇam
caturtho 'dhyāyaḥ
āśramāṇāṃ samuccayapakṣam āśrityāśramiṇaḥ puruṣasya yadāyustac (?) caturdhā vibhajyedam āha |
caturthām āyuṣo bhāgam uṣitvādyaṃ gurau dvijaḥ |
dvitīyam āyuṣo bhāgaṃ krtadāro gṛhe vaset || 4.1 ||
brahmacaryakālasya vihitatvāt "caturtham āyuṣo bhāgam" ity etad uttaravivakṣārtham uddiṣṭasaṃkīrtanam | etena dvitīyabhāgo vyākhyātaḥ | "gṛhe vased dvijaḥ" ity anena tṛtīyādhyāyavihitasya pañcamahāyajñādeḥ karmaṇo 'nuṣṭhānam ucyate | tac ca punar anuṣṭhānaṃ karmaṇo vittasādhanaṃ yasmād arthavṛttiniyamaśāstram idaṃ prakriyate |
adroheṇaiva bhūtānām alpadroheṇa vā punaḥ |
yā vṛttis tāṃ samāsthāya vipro jīved anāpadi || 4.2 ||
"maitro brāhmaṇa ucyate" (MDh 11.35) iti etasmād alpadrohābhyanujñānam apy ayuktam | yato 'smāt sāmarthād iyān droha uparodhārthaṃ kalpate ca hiṃsāyām | evaṃ ca saty anuparodhena bhūtānāṃ prathamaṃ pravartitavyaṃ sarvavṛttiṣu vakṣyamāṇāsu sarvārthatvād asya vidher udāharaṇam ayācitalābhas tadabhāvolpāparādhenāyācitalābhenaiva sarvavṛttiṣv etad vijñeyam | evaṃ ca (?) putradārāc ca tyayaprāptiḥ (??) dharmasaṃgrahaś ca | tadviparyayas tv anāpattiḥ syād anāpadīty etad viseṣaṇam asmin vṛttiprakaraṇe 'dhikārārthaṃ jīvann api caivam |
yātrāmātraprasiddhyarthaṃ svaiḥ karmabhir agarhitaiḥ |
akleśena śarīrasya kurvīta dhanasaṃcayam || 4.3 ||
akleśena śarīrasya yātrāpramāṇo dhanasaṃcayaḥ kāryo nādhikṛtaniyama ucyate | śāstroktayātrāpramāṇatvaṃ ca kartavyaṃ yāvān nityakarmāvacchityai putradārasthityai dhanasaṃcayaḥ kalpyate, tāvān evāsya syān na tato 'dhikaḥ | kena | svaiḥ karmabhir agarhitaiḥ | svaiḥ śāstravadbhir (?) ity arthaḥ | yasmād ucyate –
ṛtāmṛtābhyāṃ jīvat tu mṛtena pramṛtena vā |
[161]        satyāmṛtābhyām api vā na śvavṛttyā kathaṃcana || 4.4 ||
anāpadi vartate nānṛtādīn aprasiddhāṇ svayam eva ślokadvayena vyākaroti |
ṛtam uñchaśilaṃ jñeyam amṛtaṃ syād ayācitam |
mṛtaṃ tu yācitaṃ bhaikṣaṃ pramṛtaṃ karṣaṇaṃ smṛtam || 4.5 ||
satyānṛtaṃ tu vāṇijyaṃ tena caivāpi jīvyate |
sevā śvavṛttir vyākhyātā tasmāt tāṃ parivarjayet || 4.6 ||
uñchaśilam iti dvandvaikavadbhāvaḥ padārthadvayaviṣayaḥ | amṛtādiśabdāś cāyācitalābhādiṣv artheṣu kenacit sāmānyena vyākaraṇīyāḥ | yācitapratigrahavacanāt tatsahacarite yājanādhyāpane 'py atra gṛhyete | tathā ca vakṣyati "caturthas tu brahmasatreṇa jīvati" iti | "sevā śvavṛttir vyākhyātā" iti brāhmaṇāpekṣam etat prakaraṇād vijñeyam | uktam hi "dvijātimukhyavṛttīnāṃ vidhānaṃ śrūyatām" (MDh 3.286) iti | yato netaravarṇaviṣayam | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "sarve cottarottaraṃ paricareyuḥ" (GDh 10.65) ity āha | prakṛtyapekṣārthaś cāyaṃ brāhmaṇasya sevāpratiṣedhaḥ | anāpadīti | tathā ca prakaraṇam uktam anāpadīti | ebhiś ca vṛttipadārthair yathā yogaśāstravihitasya karmaṇaḥ kartavyāpekṣayā |
kusūladhānyako vā syāt kumbhīdhānyaka eva vā |
tryahaihiko vāpi bhaved aśvastanika eva vā || 4.7 ||
ābhir vṛttibhiḥ saṃcayavikalpo 'yam upadiśyate | jñānavairāgyavato hi gṛhasthasya kṛtārthatvāpekṣayā kusūlādigrahaṇaṃ saṃcayaparimāṇopalakṣaṇaparaṃ na niyamārthaṃ saṃcayādhikaraṇasya | tryahaikikapadaṃ tu vyutpādyate | iha bhavam aihikam | kiṃ tat | vṛttikarma prakṛtam | tryahaihikaṃ yasya so 'yaṃ tryahaihiko gṛhasthaḥ | aśvastanikas tu saṃprakṣālanako vijñeyaḥ | apare tūktaślokārdham evaṃ paṭhanti: "tryahaihiko hy aśvastanika eva vā" iti | teṣām uttaraślokavirodhaḥ | yenāsmin pāṭhe pañca ity eke paṭhanti na catvāraḥ | "caturṇām api caiteṣām" (MDh 4.8) iti vakṣyati | yatra mātrā prasiddhyartham ity atra | nanv idam uktam eva yato nārabhyamātroktaṃ parihāraḥ | jñānavairāgyato gṛhasthasya kṛtārthatvāpakṣāyāḥ saṃcayavikalpo 'yam upadiśyata iti | atha vā pūrvasthityapekṣo dhasaṃcaya uktaḥ | tathā ca yātrāgrahaṇam | tatra yātrāgrahaṇāt tithir (?) iti prasiddhā | iha tu vihitaḥ sukarmaṇaḥ kṛtakartavyāpekṣayātha vārthavādārthā | punaḥ śrutyuktam (?) āha –
caturṇām api vaiteṣāṃ dvijānāṃ gṛhamedhinām |
[162]        jyāyān paraḥ paro jñeyo dharmato lokajittamaḥ || 4.8 ||
vṛttisaṅkocastutiḥ jñānavairāgyavivekaprācuryāpekṣayā | evaṃ ca saty akṛtārthakarmāṅgasaṃcayabhūyastvavyapekṣayā kaścit,
ṣaṭkarmaiko bhavaty eṣāṃ tribhir anyaḥ pravartate |
dvādhyām ekaś caturthas tu brahmasattreṇa jīvati || 4.9 ||
vṛttiprakaraṇāt tāvat yājanādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇām iha grahaṇam | ye hy āhur yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāt trīṇi karmāṇy āpadviṣayāṇi, trīṇy asaptīti (?) grahaṇaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyavṛttaya iti, teṣām adhyāpanād iti vacanāt prakṛtārthoparodhaḥ | sadasatpratigrahaikatvāc ca paścāt kutadṛṣatvaṃ (?) | ye 'pi ca ṛtaśabdālābena kalpanā na(?) yāvan nayanti, teṣām apīmāni pañca bhavanti kusīdagorakṣāgrahaṇe ca sapta | yata idaṃ karmaṇāṃ ṣaṣṭham ucyate | śiloñche dve karmaṇi yā pitā yācate dve eva | tathā ca kṛṣivāṇijye evaṃ ṣaṭ karmāṇi bhavanti, prakṛtatvād eteṣām | atha vācyatvād ekaṃ śiloñcham arthaprayāsattyocyate | evaṃ yācitayā cittapratigraho nimittabhede hy ekaḥ yājanādhyāpane tu pratigrahodāharaṇasāmarthād arthato 'dhikṛtasāhacaryeṇātra kṛṣivāṇijye ca dve eva | yato 'nayā kalpanayā ṣaṭkarmātha voktanyāyopacāreṇa śiloñchaikatvād yājanādhyāpanapratigrahakṛṣivāṇijyānāṃ ca pañcatvāt ṣaṭkarma śiloñchapratigrahayor vā dvijaikatvaviparyayeṇa etad vācyaṃ ṣaṭ karmatvam | aparā tu vyākhyā | yājanādhyāpanapratigrahās trayaḥ kṛṣivāṇijyakusīdāni ca trīṇi evaṃ vā ṣaṭkarmāṇi | tathā ca gautamo 'nāpady evāha "kṛṣivāṇijye cāsvayaṃkṛte, kusīdaṃ ca" (GDh 10.5–6) iti | "tribhir anyaḥ pravartate" iti prasiddhāni trīṇi yājanādhyāpanapratigrahā vakṣyamāṇāḥ brahmasatrasāhacaryāt gṛhyante | tathā ca "pratigrahaḥ pratyavaraḥ" (MDh 10.109) iti pratigrahasyācoditatvād dvābhyām eka iti | yājanādhyāpane gṛhyete | caturthas tu "brahmasatreṇa jīvati" (MDh 4.9) ity adhyāpanaṃ brahmasatraṃ jīvatīti liṅgāt | tasya ca nutyarthatvāt | eteṣāṃ ca karmaṇāṃ parasya jyāyastvaṃ śāstranyāyasiddham | "nivṛttis tu mahāphalā" (MDh 5.56) ityātmasaṃyamaḥ | saṅkoceneti śiloñchāyācitānāṃ ca brāhmaṇavṛttīnāṃ darśanāt | sarvo 'yaṃ kṛṣivāṇijyādivṛttiprapañco brāhmaṇaviṣaya  evānāpadi, prakaraṇāt svayaṃkṛto draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca kṛtvoktaṃ "dvijātimukhyavṛttīnāṃ vidhānaṃ śrūyatām iti" (MDh 3.286) |
vartayaṃś ca śiloñchābhyām agnihotraparāyaṇaḥ |
iṣṭīḥ pārvāyaṇāntīyāḥ kevalā nirvapet sadā || 4.10 ||
atha vā kṛtārtho viraktaś ca sarvā evānyāyyavṛttīḥ parityajya śiloñchābhyām [163] eva vartayet | yathā kathaṃcid yāpayan vṛttyātmanāgnihotraparāyaṇa eva syād iṣṭīś ca kuryān na kāmyāḥ | kiṃ tarhi | pārvāyaṇāntīyāḥ | parva cāyanaṃ ca parvāyaṇe | tayor antaḥ parvāyaṇāntaḥ | tatra bhavāḥ pārvāyaṇāntīyāḥ darśapūrṇamāsanavayajñāgrāyaṇāḥ (?) | evaṃ ca sati sarvakāmyapratiṣedho 'syām avasthāyāṃ (?) na kevalam iṣṭīnām eva | tathā ca saty ayam |
na lokavṛttaṃ varteta vṛttihetoḥ kathaṃcana |
ajihmām aśaṭhāṃ śuddhāṃ jīvad brāhmaṇajīvikām || 4.11 ||
lokavṛttaṃ nāma vicitrakathāparihāsādibhiḥ ślokāvarjanam | tat nānutiṣṭhet kadācid api vṛttihetoḥ | na tu parānugrahārthaṃ karuṇayā | kathaṃ tarhi | ajihmām akuṭilāṃ kenacid vyājenāśaṭhāṃ śraddadhānas tathā śuddhāṃ sarvopadhāparityāgenātha vā ajihmārthavivaraṇam idam | aśaṭhām ity evaṃ śuddhāṃ jīved brāhmaṇajīvikām | jīvanārthā vṛttir jīvikāśabdenocyate, guṇatas tatprakaraṇāt | evaṃ ca saty āṅgam aśaṭhāṃ śuddhām | ity etāni tadveśeṣaṇāni draṣṭavyāni | sarvasyaivāsya vṛttiprakarṣasya vairāgyāvasthānasya kāraṇam adhunocyate |
saṃtoṣaṃ param āsthāya sukhārthī saṃyato bhavet |
saṃtoṣamūlaṃ hi sukhaṃ duḥkhamūlaṃ viparyayaḥ || 4.12 ||
niratiśayādṛṣṭasukhārthī dṛṣṭaśamasukhārthī caivaṃ saṃyato bhavet yathāvocāma | yasmād iha loke dṛṣṭaṃ "saṃtoṣamūlaṃ hi sukhaṃ duḥkhamūlaṃ viparyayaḥ" iti | dṛṣṭāc ca dṛṣṭam api anumeyam evaṃ ca na sukhārtham asatpratigraheṣṭe prasajyeta satpratigrahādiṣu yāvatātirekeṇa |
ato 'nyatamayā vṛttyā jīvaṃs tu snātako dvijaḥ |
svargyāyuṣyayaśasyāni vratānīmāni dhārayet || 4.13 ||
pūrvoktaprakaraṇopasaṃhāropanyāsārthaḥ ślokaḥ | atra kecid anyatamayeti vacanād vṛttisamuccayaṃ necchanti | tad ayuktam "ṣaṭkarmaiko bhavaty eṣām" (MDh 4.9) ity evamādinātra sākṣād eva samuccayaśravaṇāt | tasmād yā etā vṛttayo 'bhihitās tā sāmanyatamayā loke yathāpadeśaṃ samastayā vyastayā vā jīvan ity uktānuvādamātram etat | apare punaḥ "anyarayā" (?) iti paṭhanti | tad ayuktam | bahutvād vṛttīnām asampado hālpadohāpeṣayaitad yuktam | sarvavṛttiviśeṣaṇatvād etayos tathā | kṛtaṃ tayos tadviśeṣaṇavyākhyānam anekavṛttyupadeśaṃ ca sākṣān na śrutaṃ tvayā (?) | nūnaṃ ṣaṭkarmaiko bhavati ity evamādi | yenaivam ācakṣveti "vratānīmāni dhārayet" ity asya vidheḥ stutyupacayapakṣeṇedam ārabhyate |
[164]
vedoditaṃ svakaṃ karma nityaṃ kuryād atandritaḥ |
tad dhi kurvan yathāśakti prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim || 4.14 ||
vedoditāni hotrāder anuvādo 'yaṃ dṛṣṭānto 'rthavādārthaḥ | yathaiva hi vedoditam agnihotrādyatyantokṛṣṭasādhanānuṣṭhānair evaṃ vakṣyamāṇavratadhāraṇam ity eva stutir evam eva tat | tathā ca "svargyāṇīmāni"(MDh 4.13) ity uktam atha vopalambhaprasaṅgāt smaraṇasya lokavad vodiditam evedaṃ vratadhāraṇam | utsannasmaraṇapakṣe vedaliṅgaprabhavatve 'pi vā smaraṇasyānumānapakṣam āśritya pūrvavad asya stutyarthatā vacanīyā | svātantryapakṣe 'pi smṛter vedaliṅgatāyāḥ prāmāṇyāddyuktāpūrvā (?) (prāmāṇyam uktapūrvam?) | yad asya stutir aprayuktopamā vaitat | katham | vedoditam iva vedoditam | kiṃ punas tat | idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ vratadhāraṇaṃ svakaṃ karma | kasya | snātakasya puruṣadharmākhyam | yasmād ato nityaṃ kuryād etad atandritaḥ | asya phalavidhis tad dhi kurvan yathāśakti prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim iti yataś caitad evam atas tānīmānīy ucyante |
nehetārthaprasaṅgena (?)
śītādinā dharmārtham api
na viruddhena karmaṇā |
na kalpamāneṣv artheṣu nārtyām api yatas tataḥ || 4.15 ||
ayājayājanādinā | dharmārtham apīti vartate | nākalpyam ātmārthe 'py aprārthito 'vanatān api sataḥ nārtyām api putradārātyayadharmatantravivakṣāyāṃ (?) yatas tata ity asatpratigrahapratiṣedhaḥ | evaṃ ca sahasaiva kāryo 'yam asatpratigrahaḥ tat tv atyantapratiṣedhaḥ | yasmād āpadyetaṃ vakṣyati |
indriyārtheṣu sarveṣu na prasajyeta kāmataḥ |
na kevalaṃ jihvopasthaviṣaya eva prasaṅgo vāryate | kiṃ tarhy anyeṣv api śītādiṣu śrotrādiviṣayeṣu | ataḥ sarvagrahaṇam |
atiprasaktiṃ caiteṣāṃ manasā saṃnivartayet || 4.16 ||
apratiṣiddheṣv api svadāragamanādiṣu saṃnivartayed iti paramārthaprasaṃkhyāne nādhyātmavidyānuśāsanākhyo neti | kasmāt punar etat | "evaṃ yatnataḥ" ity atrocyate | kathaṃ nāmāyam evaṃ tat (?) |
sarvān parityajed arthān svādhyāyasya virodhinaḥ |
viṣayasaṅgākhyān yathā tathā yāpayantaḥ sthitimātrasaṃtuṣṭaḥ svādhyāyaparaḥ [165] syād yena,
yathā tathādhyāpayaṃs tu sā hy asya kṛtakṛtyatā || 4.17 ||
yaṃ (?) nityasvādhyāyatvam | tathā ca brāhmaṇam "yaṃ yaṃ kratum adhīte tena tena hāsya kratuneṣṭaṃ bhavati" iti |
vayasaḥ karmaṇo 'rthasya śrutasyābhijanasya ca |
veṣavācbuddhisārūpyam ācaran vicared iha || 4.18 ||
yeṣāṃ śuśrūṣākalpanādir vākparihasanaṃ narmakathā (?) buddhir dharmārthakāmeṣu prasaktiḥ ete veṣavāgbuddhayaḥ ācārāvirodhinyo lokaprasiddhyā vayaādīnāṃ puruṣadharmāṇām ānurūpyeṇānuṣṭheyā iti niyama ucyate | śāstreṇa ca ślokasya sarvo 'yaṃ lokavyavahāro viṣayaḥ prasamīkṣya yathā yogaṃ sarvataḥ prayoktavyaḥ |
buddhivṛddhikarāṇy āśu dhanyāni ca hitāni ca |
nityaṃ śāstrāṇy avekṣeta nigamāṃś caiva vaidikān || 4.19 ||
śāstrāṇy aṅgāni gṛhyante | vedaśāstrāvirodhīni ca | tadupasarjanāni nyāyaśāstrāṇi | nigamās tu prasiddhā evānavagataśabdārthaparyāyopadeśārthāḥ | atha vā nigamyata ebhir vedārtha iti nigamāḥ tadaṅgāni śāstrāṇi na tv aṅgavyatiriktāni vedaśeṣāṇy eva gṛhyante nyāyaśāstrādīni |
yathā yathā hi puruṣaḥ śāstraṃ samadhigacchati |
tathā tathā vijānāti vijñānaṃ cāsya rocate || 4.20 ||
"buddhivṛddhikarāṇy āśu dhanyāni ca hitāni ca" (MDh 4.19) iti pūrvaśokotārthavādasyāsyopacayapakṣeṇeyam aparā śāstrādhigamastutiḥ, "nityaṃ śāstrāṇy aveketa" (MDh 4.19) ity asya pūrvaślokoktasya vidheḥ |
ṛṣiyajñaṃ devayajñaṃ bhūtayajñaṃ ca sarvadā |
nṛyajñaṃ pitṛyajñaṃ ca yathāśakti na hāpayet || 4.21 ||
vaiśvadevādikānāṃ mahāyajñānām abhyāsārtha ārambha iti kecit | tat punar yuktam ayuktaṃ veti vicāraṇīyam | vayaṃ punar brūmaḥ | śeṣavidhyartha eṣāṃ punar upanyāsastutyartho vā | tathā ca pradarśayiṣyāmaḥ |
etān eke mahāyajñān yajñaśāstravido janāḥ |
anīhamānāḥ satatam indriyeṣv eva juhvati || 4.22 ||
[166]
ye sarvaprakārasya yajñasya śāstrajāś te nādhikṛtatvād anīhamānās tān | mahāyajñāt satataṃ yāvajjīvam "indriyāgniṣu (?) juhvati" | kiṃ punas tadāstikyaśrāddhābhiniveśo vā graheṇa tanmayām āhutimayaṃ cāsya prapañco vṛttisthāne bhavati \
vācy eke juhvati prāṇaṃ prāṇe vācaṃ ca sarvadā |
vāci prāṇe ca paśyanto yajñanirvṛttiṃ akṣayām || 4.23 ||
vāyuvāgindriyayoḥ (?) parasparavirodhena vṛttir arthalakṣaṇā | tāṃ darśayati | yena vāci pravartamānāyām adhyayanādiṣu prāṇasya tadupayogo dṛṣṭas tat | guṇatvād ahuteḥ | āhutiś cātmaopamardenāgnyābiś (?) ca vā na dṛṣṭaḥ | yata idaṃ guṇavṛttyocyate | "vācy eke juhvati prāṇam" evaṃ viparyayeṇa "prāṇe vācaṃ ca sarvadā" iti vaktavyam | atra kāraṇam āha "vāci prāṇe ca paśyantaḥ" | anayā śāstropāsanayādhikṛtayajñāni vṛttim akṣayāṃ śāstrasyānatiśaṅkyatvād upāsanāvidher vāyam upadeśastutiparaṃ caitat | "indriyāgniṣv juhvati" (MDh 4.22) ity asya pūrvaślokaṃ vidher nidarśanaṃ caitat sarvendriyaprāṇavṛttyor vijñeyaṃ pakṣadvaye 'pi | tathā ca kauṣītakibrāhmaṇam "yāvad vai puruṣo na bhāṣyate na tāvat prāṇituṃ śaknoti prāṇaṃ tadā vāci juhoti yāvad vai puruṣaḥ prāṇiti na tāvat bhaṣītuṃ śaknoti vācaṃ tadā prāṇeṣu juhoti ete anante amṛte juhoti jāgrac ca svapaṃś ca satataṃ juhoty atha yā anyā āhutayontavatyas tāḥ karmayo (?) bhavanty evaṃ evaṃ ha smaitat pūrve vidvāṃso 'gnihotraṃ na juhuvāṃ cakruḥ" | śrutiparam etat katham evaṃ nāmaite nityāḥ | yenānadhikṛtā eva karmabhir etair anyena prakāreṇa yajante naiṣām ucchitir astīti manyamānaḥ | saiṣānuṣṭhānastutir eva | teṣām upāsanāvidhir vyānadhikṛtapuruṣaviṣayā (?) | yuktā caivam iyaṃ smṛtyarthakalpanā, yasmāc chrutāv api –
jñānenaivāpare viprā
adhikṛtebhyo 'nadhikṛtā ity arthaḥ |
yajante tena tair makhaiḥ |
yān vakṣyāmo 'nantaraśloke sadā yāvajjīvam atra kāraṇam āha –
jñānamūlāṃ kriyām etāṃ paśyantaḥ
kena prakāreṇa |
jñānacakṣuṣā || 4.24 ||
[167]
jñāyate 'neneti jñānaṃ śāstram | karaṇe lyuṭ | tad eva cakṣus tena jñānacakṣuṣā śāstreṇa tad idam ucyate | vidvān yajata ity evaṃ ca sati yady adhikāriśrutir itīyam athāpi phalaviśeṣaprayojanety ubhayathāpi śrutivijñānasaṃyuktaivam anyadāpy udāharaṇīyam | "yo vā etad akṣaraṃ gārgy aviditvā yajate evam ete viduḥ" ity evamādi codāharaṇīyam | "yaṃ yaṃ kratum adhīte tena tena hāsya kratuneṣṭaṃ bhavati," "ya evaṃ vidvān svādhyāyam adhīte" na hy akasmāt stutiḥ pravartata iti kṛtvāitad udāhṛtam | vikalpavac ca śrutaṃ jñānaṃ "yo ha vaivaṃ vidvān etat karma kurute yo vaitad evaṃ veda" ity atrāpi "saiva yo janā idaṃ cāparaṃ yad eva vidyayā karoti śraddhayopaniṣadā tad evaṃ vīryavattaraṃ bhavat" iti | tathā ca vyāsabhagavān gītāsv āha |
śreyān dravyamayād yajñād jñānayajñaḥ parṃtapa |
sarvaṃ karmākhilaṃ pārtha jñāne parisamāpyate || [BhG 4.33]
tasmād evam upapannam uktam | jñānamūlāṃ kriyām ity asyodāharaṇam |
agnihotraṃ ca juhuyād ādyante dyuniśoḥ sadā |
darśena pakṣamāsānte paurṇamāsena caiva ha || 4.25 ||
sasyānte navasasyeṣṭyā tathartvante dvijo 'dhvaraiḥ |
paśunā hy ayanasyānte samānte saumikair makhaiḥ || 4.26 ||
agnihotre yajñaśabdo juhotiśabdena pāṭhaḥ | darśapūrṇamāsādiṣu tu śruto 'pi yajatiśabdaḥ śāstrād vijñeyaḥ | katham | "dvijo 'dhvaraiḥ" iti saumikeṣu cāturmāseṣv ayam adhvaraśabdo veditavyaḥ, evaṃ ca saty anadhikṛto 'py atra yathokto vāsanayā pariprāpayaty evāṃs tayā (?) yathoktayā evaṃ mahāyajñān api pariprāpayed iti stutyartham idam anadhikṛtaṃ vā pratyayam upadeśaḥ | evaṃ kṛtaśāstranyāyavirodhasāmarthāinayasyeṣṭipaśuprasaṅgena vedam aparaṃ ślokadvayaṃ prakṛtārthānugrahāya dṛṣṭāntatvenocyate |
nāniṣṭvā navasasyeṣṭyā paśunā vāgnimān dvijaḥ |
navānnam adyān māṃsaṃ vā dīrgham āyur jijīviṣuḥ || 4.27 ||
navenānarcitā hy asya paśuhavyena cāgnayaḥ |
prāṇān evāttum icchanti navānnāmiṣagardhinaḥ || 4.28 ||
idam apy anenānaupāsanayaiva pariprāpaṇīyam iti stutiparam etad upadeśaparaṃ vety etad uktam | anye tu "agnihotraṃ ca juhuyāt" (MDh 4.25) ityevamādima(?) ślokānām artham āhuḥ | agnihotrādīnāṃ śrutau nityatvasya pratyakṣato 'nupraveśād iha mānusa(?)navānnādanena saha caturbhiḥ ślokair [168] nityataiṣām ucyate | tat tv etaṃ nyāyyam anyāyyam eveti vicāraṇīyam | mahāyajñaprasaṅgena nṛyajñasya śeṣavaktavyatayedam ārabhyate |
āsanāśanaśayyābhir adbhir mūlaphalena vā |
nāsya kaścid vased gehe śaktito 'narcito 'tithiḥ || 4.29 ||
pāṣaṇḍāpavādārtham atītārthasya punarvacanam | atas tatpratiṣedhārtham idam ārabhyate |
pāṣaṇḍino vikarmathān baiḍālavratikāñ chaṭān |
haitukān bakavṛttīṃś ca vāṅmātreṇāpi nārcayet || 3.30 ||
pāṣaṇḍaśabdo hi yadā nindārthaḥ kalpyate | yathānyatra na pāṣaṇḍajanā kānta ity evamādi tadā vikarmasthādiśabdās tatparyāyāḥ santaḥ nindātiśayārthā lokavad vedaṃ ca prayojanatvād anuvādanakalpanāyām eṣām apunaruktavibhaktanyāyaṃ caitad ubhayam | pūrvo gobalīvardanyāyapakṣo 'pi (m?) aprataṣedhādarārtho vijñeyāḥ | yadā punarāya (?) pāṣaṇḍiśabdo liṅgiparyāyas tadā tasyobhayathābhāvāt tadviśeṣaṇaṃ yuktam idam | ye ye vikarmasthāḥ pāṣaṇḍino liṅginas tān vāṅmātreṇāpi nārcayet | nitarām etasyāṃ ca vyākhyāmā (a. va.) yām uttaro 'pi karmasthaprapañcaḥ pratiṣedhādarārthaḥ pūrvavad gamanīyaḥ | kavikarmasthatvahetvaprapañco vā | kathaṃ baiḍālavratikān (tsv? unclear) vat (?) parātisaṃdhānārtham āśramadvāreṇa pracchannopadhā mārjāraliṅginaḥ narā (?) dhiyā | yady api cedaṃ śṛṇvantaś ca tadarthahetukāni | tatstutyai (?) | āgamalokāyatikādayaḥ (?) bakavṛttayaḥ ca bhannabhannkāras (?) tadekadhā kāraṇe bhedopavarṇanārtham uttaraḥ prapañco vijñeyaḥ | tatprayojanaṃ ca vijitavikarmasthabhedasya prayatnatas tatparihāraḥ | yadi caite aśrotriyās tata eṣām aprāptapratiṣedhānayuktā (?) yataḥ śrotriyatvena prāptātithyānām anena karmaṇā yaḥ pratiṣedho yuktārambhaḥ | kim ayam atyantapratiṣedha eva tebhyo dānasya nety āhātyantapratiṣedhe niḥśaktito vacamānebhya (?) ity etad virudhyeta | tathā ca pradarśayiṣyāmaḥ | prakṛtasya pratiṣedho yuktaḥ | prakṛtaṃ ca sāyam atithibhyāṃ tadāgamanaṃ pūrvaślokenāsya (āha?) kaścit | "gṛhe vasec chaktito 'narcito 'tithiḥ" iti vacanāt prātardānaṃ | tarhi nanu prāptam eṣām apratiṣedhāt sarvatreti yatnata idam ārabhyate | vratasnāto 'pi (??)
vedavidyāvratasnātān śrotriyān gṛhamedhinaḥ (gṛham āgatān) |
[169]        pūjayed dhavyakavyābhyāṃ viparītāṃs tu varjayet || 4.31 ||
veda eva vidyā vedavidyā | vedavidyā ca vratāni ca (?) vedavidyāvratāni | taiḥ snāto vedavidyāvrasnātās triprakārā api snātakā gṛhyante | tatra ca vratsnātāpekṣā śrotriyānītyevadam (?) arthavat bhavati yena vratasnātakaḥ | parisamāpya vedavidyāṃ yaḥsnāti sa ucyate | itarayor api va pakṣayor idam ucyate | vedavidyāvratasnātānīti vacanāt | satyaṃ śrotriyatvaṃ siddhaṃ tadvacanaṃ tu punar atiśayārthaṃ yathābhirūpāya kanyā dīyatām ity ukte 'bhirūpatarāyeti gamyate | yasmād anabhirūpāya kanyādānam eva nāsti, evam ihāpi siddhe sati pūrveṇaiva śrotriyatve sāṅgavedavijñānārthaḥ śrotriyaśabdaḥ punar vaktavya eva | tathā ca sati havyakavyayor anyatraiṣāṃ divānnadānam apratiṣiddhaṃ veditavyam | pāṣaṇḍino bhojanābhyanujñātasandhena (jñānavacanena?) ca vihitasyāpi śrotriyātithidānasya punar upadeśo yukta iti | tasmād asti tebhyo 'nnadānaṃ divaiva havyakavyābhyām anyatra ceti patitādibhyo 'pi cānnadānopadeśād vijñāyate | viśeṣapratiṣedho 'yaṃ nātyantapratiṣedhaḥ | itaś caiṣāṃ nātyantapratiṣedho, yad ayam āha –
śaktito 'pacamānebhyo dātavyaṃ gṛhamedhinā |
saṃvibhāgaś ca bhūtebhyaḥ kartavyo 'nuparodhataḥ || 4.32 ||
tac caitad (?) atyantapratiṣedhe virudhyeta yasmād ihāpacamānāḥ pāṣaṇḍina eva prakṛtāḥ | bhūtagrahaṇaṃ ca sammānaliṅgaṃ tena ca vijñāyate | vedavidyāvratasnātakebhyo 'nyatrāyaṃ dānacodanahavyakavyābhyāṃ cetarathā hy anārambhasamaṃ prasajyetoktatvāt tadannadānasyeti | "saṃvibhāgaś ca bhūtebhyaḥ" ity etac cānnadānavyatirekeṇānyad divyadānaṃ gamyate | itarathā hy anārambhasamaṃ prasajyeta | tat pūrveṇaiva siddhatvād iti |
rājato dhanam anvicchet saṃsīdan snātakaḥ kṣudhā |
yājyāntevāsinor vāpi na tv anyata iti sthitiḥ || 4.33 ||
"arājanyaprasūtitaḥ" (MDh 4.84) iti pratiṣedhāt kṣatriyād rājñaḥ pratilābhaṃ vakṣyati | kasyedaṃ saṃsīdann iti liṅgād āpaddharmaviṣayatocyate | sarvāśrameṣu ca kṣatriyād ityādy uktvā tatpratigrahasyāpaddharmaviṣayatātha vā dīkṣitāṃpratiṣedhavat (?) pratiṣedhaḥ | sīdat kavyam icchad dvir iti cāsyaivottaravidhitsayāpaddharmeṣu grahaṇam | yājyāntevāsinor upakārakatvād apratiṣedhaḥ | asmāc ca yatnāt satpratigrahapakṣe yaṃ prathamaḥ kalpaḥ | "na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt" (MDh 4.84) iti brāhmaṇavaiśyayos tu rājño 'satpratigrahapakṣe pratiṣedhaḥ | tathā "yo rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇīyāl lubdhasyocchāstravartinaḥ" (MDh 4.87) iti liṅgāt tayor [170] evāyam apavādaḥ | asya ślokasyaitam artham āhur upādhyāyaviṣṇusvāmipādāḥ | ahaṃ tu bruve – "rājato dhanam anvicchet" ity anena kṣatriyād rājño yathāśāstravartinaś ca sakāśād ayaṃ pratigraha ucyate | kiṃ punar atra kāraṇaṃ yasmād akṣatriyād rājño 'traiva snātakadharmaprakaraṇe pratigrahaṇapratiṣedhaṃ vakṣyati "na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇīyād arājanyaprasūtitaḥ" (MDh 4.84) iti, "yo rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇīyāl lubdhayocchāstravartinaḥ" (MDh 4.87) iti ca | anena kṣatriyasyāpi sata ucchāstravṛtteś ca pratigrahapratiṣedhāt | tasmād vijñāyate nāyam asatpratigrahapakṣaḥ | yathopādhyāya āha | yenānyat kṣatriyād rājñaḥ pratigrahaśāstram asti | snātakasyedam uktatvād yato nāpaddharmaviṣayatāsya | idaṃ tarhi kathaṃ "saṃsīdan snātakaḥ kṣudhā" ity ucyate | nāyam asmād asadrājapratigraho vijñātuṃ yuktaḥ | kasmāt | prakaraṇāt prāptiśāstratvāc cānsyety uktam ¦ etāvat tu śakyam asmād vijñātuṃ nānyasmin praśastadvijātipratigrahe sati nyāyyād api rājño grahītavyaṃ gurutaratvāt sadrājapratigrahasyāpi sataḥ itarebhyo dvijātipratigrahebhyaḥ | mahopamātvāt sādhor api rājñas tantrasya dīkṣitānnavad vā sādhukāratve 'pi sadrājñaḥ pratigrahasya īṣadgurutvaṃ vijñeyam uktahetutvād vā nāyam asatpratigrahaḥ sa vāyaṃ pratiṣiddhasyāsatpratigrahasya pratiprasavas tasyopadeśād vāpaddharmeṣu | vakṣyati hi "sarvataḥ pratigṛhaṇīyād brāhmaṇo vṛttikarṣitaḥ" (MDh 10.102), "sīdadbhiḥ kupyam icchadbhiḥ" (MDh 10.1113) ityevamādi | namadvipratiṣedhārthaḥ pratiprasavo yuktasnātakadharmeṣv āpaddharmeṣu ca paunaruktyaṃ hi | tathā syād vratakaraṇoparodhaś ca prakaraṇasnātakasameṣu yājyānte | vāsino sakaṃ (?) "rājato dhanam anvicchet" iti vartate pratigraheṇa yājanādhyāpanābhyāṃvā tatsaṃjñāṃ pratilābhahetutvād dhy etayoḥ | evaṃ ca sati rājño yājyāntevāsibhyāṃ (?) saha vikalpopadeśāt tadvat sādhutve sati nāyam asatpratigraho vijñāyate | na ca samānānāṃ tulyavikalpo yuktaḥ | nābhāvavikalpaḥ | yataś cāha – na tv anyata iti | sthitir iti apraśastadvijātibhya ity arthaḥ | dhanam icched ity anyenāsya saṃbandho 'tha vā saṃsīdann ity anena dharmatantrasaṅga ucyate | nātmādīnām avasādo yathā śrutiḥ uktasāmarthāt | tathā ca vṛttikarṣitasyāpaddharmaṃ vakṣyati |
avṛttikarṣitaḥ sīdann imaṃ dharmaṃ samācaret |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyād brāhmaṇas tva anayaṃ gataḥ || (MDh 10.101–02)
ityevamādi | yuktaṃ caitad eva kalpayitum | na hy asatpratigṛhṇātur dharmānuṣṭhānaṃ yuktam | uttarapratiṣedhāt "ye śūdrād adhigamyārtham" (MDh 12.42) ityevamādi | ātmakuṭumbadhāraṃ tu vacanasāmarthād uktaṃ na vacanasyātihāro (-cāro?) 'sti | tathā ca kṛtvedaṃ yuktarūpaṃ bhavati |
na sīdet snātako vipraḥ kṣudhā śaktaḥ kathaṃcana |
na jīrṇamalavadvāsā bhavec ca vibhave sati || 4.34 ||
[171]
anenātmādīnāṃ (?) kṣudavasādaḥ pratiṣiddho na pūrveṇa yas tatpratikārārtham imām avasthām āpannasya snātakasyāpaddharmān vakṣyati |
kḷptakeśanakhaśmaśrur dāntaḥ śuklāmbaradharaḥ śuciḥ |
svādhyāye caiva yuktaḥ syān nityam ātmahiteṣu ca || 4.35 ||
nakhaśmaśruprabhṛtīnāṃ kalpanena śucisnānād evaṃ ca nākasmāt śmaśruprabhṛtīni dhārayed aśuddhitvāt taddhāraṇasya liṅgopadeśo vāyam | tathā cedam aparaliṅgadhāraṇam āha snātakasya |
vaiṇavīṃ dhārayed yaṣṭiṃ sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum |
yajñopavītaṃ vedaṃ ca śubhaṃ raukme ca kuṇḍale || 4.36 ||
vaiṇavīṃ dhārayed yaṣṭim ity atra viśiṣṭajātisaṃniyogena tadvataḥ padārthasya dhāraṇaṃ niyamyate | sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum ity etat punaḥ kiṃ adṛṣṭāyaivāsya dhāraṇaṃ kuṇḍalādivad dṛṣṭāya śaucāya ca yajñopavītādivad ity atra āha kaścit | vyarthatvād vākyasya brahmacāriśāstrād yajñopavītasyopasparśanāṅgatā, nākasmāt kamaṇḍalor api | kiṃ tarhi | brahmacāriśāstrād eva śaucātmadharmasaṃskārasādhananiyamo vijñeyaḥ | anyathā hi śāstravad grahaṇair mekhalādibhir asya pratipattiniyamasāmyam ayuktam āpadyeta kamaṇḍalugrahaṇasyāśāstralakṣaṇatvād uktam:
mekhalām ajinaṃ daṇḍam upavītaṃ kamaṇḍalum |
apsu prāpya vinaṣṭāni gṛhītānyāni mantravat || [MDh 2.64]
atha punar dhāraṇamātrasyāpi brahmacāriṇo 'nena śāstreṇādṛṣṭaprayojanamekhalādivad eva kalpyate | ko doṣaḥ syāt | iha punarvacanam anarthakam asya prasajyeta | yat kāraṇam anuvṛttiśāstreṇaiva keṣāṃcid brahmacāridharmāṇām uttaratra paṭhitatvāt | tad uktaṃ hi:
agnīndhanaṃ baikṣacaryām adhaḥśayyāṃ guror hitam |
ā samāvartanāt kuryāt kṛtopanayano dvijaḥ || [MDh 2.108]
tathā ca smṛtyantaram: "uttareṣāṃ caitad avirodhi" (GDh 3.10) iti | nanv asya kamaṇḍalugrahaṇasya śaucārthatvād adṛṣṭārthatā saṃbhaved anupadeśārhatvaṃ prāpnoti pranāḍikayā syād adṛṣṭārthatvam asya niyamaśāstrasya yathopasparśanasyeha bhaved evaṃ cāhanyādīnāṃ (?) niyamaś cānyodakādhikaraṇasya samānasya nivṛttyarthaḥ | yathā lokaśāstrayor nāsamānasya | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "saṃnidhāyodakam" (GDh 2.41) ity āha | na cārthalakṣaṇatvād anyad udakādhikaraṇam upadeśārhaṃ brahmacāriṇo bhojanakāle 'nyad ataḥ kamaṇḍalor evaṃ ca naiṣṭhikabrahmacārivānaprasthabhikṣvāśramiṇāṃ kamaṇḍalugrahaṇanyāyād bhavati na ca satyāṃ viśiṣṭasmṛtāv eteṣām api saṃnidhānena kamaṇḍalunātmasaṃskārasādhanaṃ śauce evam upapadyate | tathā ca smṛtyantaram:
kamaṇḍaluṃ pariharet sarvāvastho 'py aśaucataḥ |
na tv enaṃ kutsayed vidvān na śaṅketāpi dūṣayet ||
yathaiva somasaṃyogāl lomaśaḥ śucir iṣyate |
apāṃ tathaiva saṃyogān nityamedhyaṃ [172] kamaṇḍaluḥ ||
brāhmaṇāḥ sarvato medhyās teṣāṃ caiva kamaṇḍaluḥ |
brāhmaṇāv iti taṃ pūrvam ity ayaṃ dharmaniścayaḥ (?) ||
vyāsaś ca dānadharmeṣu stutim āha taddānasya:
upasparśanaṣaḍbhāgaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ | iti |
triprakāraś ca dharmaḥ – śrautaḥ smārtaḥ śiṣṭācaritaś ca | yato 'yam eva śiṣṭācaritatvāt pramāṇam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram [Ed. atra granthalopa iva pratīyate kasyāpi padyasyānuddṛtatvāt]
śiṣṭācāraś ca śāstrāviruddhāh pramāṇam | tathā cāyam aviruddhaśāstreṇa | evaṃ ca sati śiṣṭācārapramāṇatvāt taddhāraṇasya dṛṣṭārthe taddhāraṇaṃ yuktam eva syād ātmasaṃskāraprayojanatvam api brahmacāriśāstrād yajñopavītavad iti sāmarthyaṃ cātraivam eva bahudhā vacanīyaṃ śāstranyāyāvirodheneti | vedo darbhavaṭuḥ vedena puroḍāśaṃ samūhatīti [Ed. pāṭho 'trātīva bhraṣṭa iva] mantrabrāhmaṇarāśes tu grahaṇe punaruktadoṣaḥ syād uktatvāt | tasya "śubhe raukme ca kuṇḍale" nākārataḥ, kiṃ tarhi | varṇataḥ śubhatvaṃ vijñeyam iti |
nekṣetodyantam ādityaṃ nāstaṃ yāntaṃ kadācana |
noparaktaṃ na vāristhaṃ na madhyaṃ nabhaso gatam || 4.37 ||
na laṅghayed vatsatantrīṃ na pradhāvec ca varṣati |
na codake nirīkṣeta svaṃ rūpam iti dhāraṇā || 4.38 ||
mṛdaṃ gāṃ daitaṃ vipraṃ ghṛtaṃ madhu catuṣpatham |
pradakṣiṇāni kurvīta prajñātaṃ ca vanaspatim || 4.39 ||
śāstre loke vā devatādhiṣṭhānena |
nopagacchet pramatto 'pi striyam ārtavadarśane |
samāne śayane caiva na śayīta tathā saha || 4.40 ||
rajasābhiplutāṃ nārīṃ dvijasyābhyupagacchataḥ |
prajā tejo yaśaś cakṣur āyuś caiva prahīyate || 4.41 ||
tāṃ vivarjayatas tasya rajasā samabhiplutām
prajñā tejo yaśaś cakṣur āyuś caiva pravardhate || 4.42 ||
ubhayathā vākyasvābhāvyād varjanastutiḥ |
nāśnīyāḍ bhāryayā sārdhaṃ naināṃ vīkṣeta vāśnatīm |
kṣuvatīṃ jṛmbhamāṇāṃ vā na cāsīnāṃ yathāsukham || 4.43 ||
"nityam āsyaṃ śuci strīṇām" (MDh 5.130) itīdam avaśiṣṭam apy anujñātam anena pratiṣedhena rativiṣayaṃ vijñāyate | ratyām (?) iha bhāryayā saha bhujyate | itarathā hi strīśūdrocchiṣṭapratiṣedhād eva [173] bhāryayā saha bhojanapratiṣedhe siddhe punaḥ pratiṣedho 'narthakaḥ syāt | na cānena haninyādi (?) strībhiḥ saha bhojanam anujñāyate nāpi bhojanād anyatra bhāryocciṣṭhasyānujñānam | evaṃ ca sati nyāyāt smṛtyācārāt trisaṃdhyaṃ ca prajñā naśyati me bhuktaḥ | tataś ca |
nāñjayantīṃ svake netre na cābhyaktām anāvṛtām |
na paśyet prasravantīṃ ca tejaskāmo dvijottamaḥ || 4.44 ||
nānnam adyād ekavāsā na nagnaḥ snānam ācaret |
na mūtraṃ pathi kurvīta na bhasmani na govraje || 4.45 ||
na phālakṛṣṭe na jale na cityāṃ na parvate |
na jīrṇadevāyatane na valmīke kadācana || 4.46 ||
na sasattveṣu garteṣu na gacchan nāpi ca sthitaḥ |
na nadītīram āsādya na ca parvatamastake || 4.47 ||
vāyvagnivipram ādityam apaḥ paśyaṃs tathaiva gāḥ |
na kadācana kurvīta viṇmūtrasya visarjanam || 4.48 ||
tiraskṛtyoccaret kāṣṭhaloṣṭhapatratṛṇādinā |
niyamya prayato vācaṃ saṃvītāṅgo 'vaguṣṭhitaḥ || 4.49 ||
mūtroccārasamutsargaṃ divā kuryād udaṅmukhaḥ |
dakṣiṇābhimukho rātrau saṃdhyayoś ca tathā divā || 4.50 ||
chāyāyām andhakāre vā rātrāv ahani vā dvijaḥ |
yathāsukhamukhaḥ kuryāt prāṇabādhābhayeṣu ca || 4.51 ||
pratyagniṃ pratisūryaṃ ca pratisomodakadvijān |
pratigāṃ prativātaṃ ca prajñā naśyati mehataḥ || 4.52 ||
nāgniṃ mukhenopadhamen nagnāṃnekṣeta ca striyam |
nāmedhyaṃ prakṣiped agnau na ca pādau pratāpayet || 4.53 ||
adhastān nopadadhyāc ca na caitnam abhilaṅghayet |
na cainaṃ pādataḥ kuryān na prāṇābādham ācaret || 4.54 ||
nāśnīyāt saṃdhivelāyāṃ na gacchen nāpi saṃviśet |
[174]        na caiva pralikhed bhūmiṃ nātmano 'paharet srajam || 4.55 ||
nāpsu mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā ṣṭhīvanaṃ vā samutsṛjet |
amedhyaliptam anyad vā lohitaṃ vā viṣāṇi vā || 4.56 ||
naikaḥ svapec chūnyagṛhe na śreyāṃsaṃ prabodhayet |
nodakyayābhibhāṣeta yajñaṃ gacchen na cāvṛtaḥ || 4.57 ||
[Ed. bhārucivyākhyātamanusmṛtipāṭhamātragocaraḥ ślokaḥ]
namaskartuṃ vrajet kāmaṃ didṛkṣur yajñakarma ca |
kulāt kulaṃ rajasyārthī saṃcaren nānimittataḥ ||
agnyagāre gavāṃ goṣṭhe brāhmaṇānāṃ ca saṃnidhau |
svādhyāye bhojane caiva dakṣiṇaṃ bāhum uddharet || 4.58 ||
na vārayed gāṃ (?) dhayantīṃ na cācakṣīta kasyacit |
na divīndrāyudhaṃ dṛṣṭvā kasyacid darśayed budhaḥ || 4.59 ||
nādharmike vased grāme na vyādhibahule tathā |
prapadyetāṭavīṃ naiko na ciraṃ parvate vaset || 4.60 ||
na śūdrarājye nivasen nādhārmikajanāvṛte |
na pāṣaṇḍijanākrānte nopasṛṣṭe 'ntyajair nṛbhiḥ || 4.61 ||
na bhuñjītoddhṛtasnehaṃ nātisauhityam ācaret |
nātiprage nātisāyaṃ na sāyaṃ prātarāśitaḥ || 4.62 ||
na kurvīta vṛthā ceṣṭāṃ na vāry añjalinā pibet |
notsaṅge bhakṣayed bhakṣyān na jātu syāt kutūhalī || 4.63 ||
na nṛtyed atha vā gāyen na vāditrāṇi vādayet |
nāsphoṭayen na ca kṣeḍen na ca rakto virāvayet || 4.64 ||
na pādau dhāvayet kāṃsye kacid api bhājane |
na bhinnabhāṇḍe bhuñjīta na bhāvapratidūṣite (?) || 4.65 ||
upānahau ca vāsaś ca ghṛtam anyar na dhārayet |
[175]        upavītam alaṃkāraṃ srajaṃ karakam eva ca || 4.66 ||
nāvinītair vrajed bhuryair na ca kṣudvyādhipīḍitaiḥ |
na bhinnākṣikṣurair vāpi na vāladhivrūpitaiḥ || 4.67 ||
vinītais tu vrajen nityam āsugair lakṣaṇānvitaiḥ |
varṇarūpopasaṃpannaiḥ pratodenātudan bhṛśam || 4.68 ||
bālātapaḥ pretadhūmo varjyaṃ bhinnaṃ tathāsanam |
na chindyān nakharomāṇi dantair notpāṭayen nakhān || 4.69 ||
na mṛlloṣṭaṃ vimṛdnīyān na chindyāt karajais tṛṇam ||
na karma niṣphalaṃ kuryān nāyatyām asukhodayam || 4.70 ||
loṣṭamardī tṛṇacchedī nakhakhādī ca yo naraḥ |
sa vināśaṃ prayāty āśu (?) sūtako 'śucir eva ca || 4.71 ||
na vigṛhyakathāṃ kuryāt bahirmālyaṃ na dhārayet |
gavāṃ ca yānaṃ pṛṣṭhena sarvaṃ caiva vigarhitam || 4.72 ||
advāreṇa ca nātīyād grāmaṃ vā veśma vāvṛtam |
[Ed. eṣa bhāgaḥ adhikamaṣīpātanād duṣpāṭhyaḥ]
rātrau ca vṛkṣamūlāni dūrataḥ parivarjayet || 4.73 ||
nākṣaiḥ krīḍet kadācit tu svayaṃ nopānahau haret |
śayanastho na bhuñjīta na pāṇisthaṃ na cāsane || 4.74 ||
sarvaṃ ca tilasambaddhaṃ nādyāḍ astamayaṃ prati |
na ca nagnaḥ śayīteha na cocchiṣṭaḥ kathaṃcana || 4.75 ||
ārdrapādas tu bhuñjīta saṃviśen na kadācana |
nārdrapādas (?) tu bhuñjāno dīrgham āyur avāpnuyāt || 4.76 ||
acakxurviṣayaṃ darśaṃ (?) na parpadyeta karhicit |
na viṇmūtram udīkṣeta na bāhubhyāṃ nadīṃ taret || 4.77 ||
adhitiṣṭhen na keśāṃs tu na bhasmāsthikapālikāḥ |
na kārpāsāsthi na tuṣān dīrgham āyur jijīviṣuḥ || 4.78 ||
[176]        na saṃvasec ca patitair na caṇḍālair na pulkasaiḥ |
na mūrkhair nāvaliptaiś ca nāntyair nāntyāvasāyibhiḥ (?) || 4.79 ||
yamaniyamārthāḥ ślokāḥ snātakasya ṛjūtvān na vyākhyātāḥ |
na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyān nocchiṣṭaṃ na haviṣkṛtam |
na cāsyopadiśed dharmaṃ na cāsya vratam ādiśet || 4.80 ||
"na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyāt" ity apravṛttasya dharme śūdrasya vṛttyartham upadeśam anicchato na kuryāt | "pṛcchatas tu kathayet | prāyaścittopadeśavat" iti kaścid āha | tad asat, śāstrāntarāsiddhatvāt | uktaṃ hi "nāpṛṣṭaḥ kasyacid brūyāt" (MDh 3.110) iti | kas tarhy asyārthaḥ | adṛṣṭārtham iti dānam anena sarvaprakāraṃ vāryate | na tu laukikaṃ kaṭakaraṇādi dharmopadeśapratiṣedhasāmarthyāt | nocchiṣṭam iti | bhuktam iti bhuktaśiṣṭaṃ bhuñjya(?)dhikaraṇasthaṃ pratiṣidhyate | bhuñjānasyaiva iti (?) kecit | tad ayuktam, anujñātatvād asya | vakṣyati hi "ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyam" (MDh 10.125) iti | tad anena virudhyate | ete pratiṣedhānujñānasmṛtī parasparavirodhinyau mā bhūtām iti, yato 'yam narthaḥ | api syāt – anujñānasmṛtir anyārthātithyādibhuktaśiṣṭāt pākāc chūdrāya deyam evaṃ cāvirodha iti, tad etad asat | pradeśāntarasiddhatvād asyārthays "vaiśyaśūdrāv api prāptau" (MDh 3.112) ityevamādinā | tathā ca tadavastho virodhaḥ | ājñāya cainaṃ smṛtyartham uktatve (?) "śūdrāṇāṃ māsikaṃ kāryaṃ vapanaṃ nyāyavartināṃ" (MDh 5.140) ity avacchedavikalpaś ca | "dvijocchiṣṭaṃ ca bhojanam" (MDh 5.140) ity ataḥ vakṣyati | ato 'nyatareṇedam arthena bhavitavyam idānīṃ cāparaṃ liṅgāt | śūdrāyocchiṣṭadānasya śrāddhaprakaraṇe hy uktam ucchoṣaṇaṃ bhūtam ajihmaasyāśaṭhasya (?) dāsavargasya tat pitre bhāgadheyaṃ pravakṣyata iti sarvasmṛtidharmeṣu cāvimānena śūdrādayaḥ brāhmaṇocchiṣṭam anujñātaṃ na śakyaṃ pratyakhyātum antareṇa pramāṇam | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "ucchiṣṭāśanaṃ śilpavṛttiś ca" (GDh 10.59–60) ity āha | kiṃ ca "śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā ya ucchiṣṭaṃ vṛṣalāya prayacchati ***?" (MDh 3.249) ity ayaṃ ca viśeṣapratiṣedhād darśayati | śrāddhād anyatrocchiṣṭadānaprāptir astīti | | itarathā hi "nocchiṣṭam" (MDh 2.56) ity anenaiva siddho 'nyatra ca sāmānyenaivocchiṣṭam ity ayam pratiṣedhād viśeṣapratiṣedhārtho nirviṣayatvā asyety abhyanujñāyavādena vedam ucyate | atha manyase, nityārtho 'yaṃ śrāddhapratiṣedho bhaviṣyaty evaṃ ca "nocchiṣṭam" ity ayaṃ pratiṣedhatvanityatvasiddhāv anyatra śrāddhāt pākṣikaṃ dānam anujānāti | evaṃ cobhayaṃ pratiṣedhasiddhiḥ | ted etad ayuktam, uktanyāyaśāstravirodhāt | asati tasmin etatsamañjasaṃ syād apy anujñāta caitad anityam ucyate | haviḥśeṣam apy ucchiṣṭaṃ śūdrāya dīyate tad dhi śrāddocchiṣṭād anyaṃ na dīyate | samācāravirodhāt | tasmād ayam uktasmṛtyarthaḥ | anye tu, devatābhyo dattam aśeṣaṃ puroḍāśādyucchiṣṭaṃ pratiṣidhyata [177] iti manyante | tad ayuktam | aprasiddhatvād rūḍhito vāyam arthaḥ prakalpyate, ucchiṣṭaśabdasya vyutpattito vā | na ca lokaśāstrayor ayaṃ śabdārthaḥ prasiddhaḥ | na ca prakṛtipratyayārthaprasaṅgena vyutpattitaḥ pākavan na ca samarthaḥ prakaraṇādy asti (?) kiṃcicchabdārthavyavasthāyāṃ na ca vācakībhūtatadvyavsthānvayā (?) apy anujñāya caitad ucyate | "na haviṣkṛtam" ity anenottarapadena na tv ayam artho yathopadiṣṭaḥ sampadyate kathaṃ haviṣkṛtaṃ cārthyate (?) tatra haviḥśeṣasya kutaḥ prāptiḥ | ato na kiṃcid etat | kas tasyārthaḥ | nocchiṣṭam arthaḥ syāt iti (?) brūmaḥ | na sarvam ucchiṣṭaṃ sāmānyato 'nena pratiṣidhyate | kiṃ tarhi | śiṣṭaṃ kiṃ punas tad dhutaśiṣṭaṃ brāhmaṇocchiṣṭaṃ śūdrāya na deyaṃ snātakena | kathaṃ gamyate | pariśeṣānumānasāmarthyāt | sāmānyena tāvat sarvocchiṣṭapratiṣedhe 'nujñānaśrāddhasmṛtī bādhyeyātāṃ yathoktena nyāyena tayor bādheṣyate | anyaś cocchiṣṭapadārthānāṃ striyopacādī (?) bhavet sāmānyasyocchiṣṭadānasya śiṣyate cety (?) ato haviḥśeṣasya puroḍāśāder brāhmaṇasyedaṃ grahaṇam | samācāraś caivam eva śiṣṭānāṃ virodhaś ca smṛtyantareṇāsya nāsti sāmarthyaṃ copapadyate | atha pratyanīkaṃ yato 'yam eva śāstrārthaḥ | kiṃ ca "śūdreṇa hi samas tāvad yāvad vedena jāyate" (MDh 2.172) iti śūdrasāmānyād anupanītebyo 'pi dvijātibhyo na dīyate | tasmād ayam evārthaḥ | haviṣkṛtam ity asya padāntasya saṃnidhisāmarthyād ayam evārthaḥ sutarāṃ vijñāyate | tac ca sāmarthyam asyaiva padasya vyākhyāna uttaratra pradarśayiṣyāmaḥ | syād etat | nanv evaṃ punaruktaṃ bhavati nocchiṣṭam ity uttareṇa padena | nanu haviṣkṛtam ity anena bhavati | kutaḥ | padāntaratvād asya haviḥśiṣṭā (?) tatsaṃsṛṣṭāc copadeśaparatvāc ca śāstrasya yathopadeśaṃ pravṛttir artheṣu śrotur ato na haviḥśeṣasaṃsṛṣṭāc copadeśaparatvāc ca śāstrasya yathopadeśaṃ pravṛttir artheṣu śrotuḥ [REPEATED ?] | ato na haviḥśeṣasaṃsṛṣṭāpratiṣedhena taducchiṣṭapratiṣedhaḥ śakyate vijñātum | evaṃ ca na haviṣkṛtam ity anenāpratiṣiddhaṃ tad idaṃ yuktam | padāntareṇa pratiṣeddhuṃ nocchiṣṭam ity anena | tathā ca sāmānyasmṛter apavādo 'yam | katham | "ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyam" (MDh 10.125) itīyaṃ smṛtiḥ sāmānyaviṣayā | utsargaḥ tadviśeṣeṇa yo vādo (?) nocchiṣṭhenocchiṣṭam iti | yathā śrāddhocchiṣṭam anyad ucchiṣṭaṃ deyam ihāpīti | tad evam utsargāpavādadharmeṇaite samañjase bhavata iti siddham | atha vā "ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyam (MDh 10.125) ity amuṣya brāhmaṇāśritasya śūdrasya prakaraṇe vidhānād etadviṣayam ucchiṣṭābhyanujñānaprati*pedysmṛtayaḥ*(?) sāmañjasyāyāyaṃ (?) kalpanīyaḥ | tathā hi (ca?) prakaraṇam anugṛhītaṃ bhavati | atha vā śūdrāyocchiṣṭaṃ viśiṣṭapratiṣiddhatvād vikalpaḥ | ubhayoḥ śāstrayor arthatvāyāśritān āśritaśūdravyavyasthayā sarvatra vikalpaprāpte idānīṃ śrāddhe pratiṣedho nityārthaḥ | vyākhyātaṃ padaṃ nocchiṣṭam ity etan na (?) | haviṣkṛtam ity etad adhunā vyākhyāyate | haviṣe kṛtaṃ vyākhyātam | na śūdrāya deyam iti kaścid āha | tad ayuktam | sāmānyenaiva sarveṣāṃ pratiṣiddhatvād abhakṣyaprakaraṇe | "devānnāni havīṃṣi ca" (MDh 5.7) ityevamādi ca viśeṣapratiṣedhaḥ [178] śūdrārho 'yaṃ na yujyate | kas tarhy asyārthaḥ | haviṣā haviḥśeṣeṇety arthaḥ | puroḍāśādinā saṃsṛṣṭaṃ yad haviṣkṛtaṃ tan na deyam (?) | śūdrāyaivaṃ cobhyam apratiṣidhyate sāmarthyād haviḥśeṣaṃ ca kevalaṃ tatsaṃsṛṣṭaṃ ca kiṃ punaḥ sāmarthyam idaṃ tad ucyate | yatra haviḥśeṣasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ vārayati tatra haviḥśeṣasya kṛte (?) dātṛprāptir iti yathā viṣasaṃsṛṣṭapratiṣedhe viṣapratiṣedhaḥ | tathā smṛtyantaram, tathācārāḥ | evaṃ ca sati trikam atra pratiṣidhyate | śūdrasya haviḥśeṣaṃ ca puroḍāśādi tatsaṃsṛṣṭaṃ ca haviḥśeṣam api tad eva dviḥ ṣaṭprakāram apy ucchiṣṭam iti | "na cāsyopadiśed dharmam" iti dharmaprayojanasya śāstrasya pravacanavyākaraṇe pratiṣiddhe (?) | tat tarhi kathaṃ "na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyāt" iti gobalīvardanyāyenāyaṃ (?) sāmānyaviśeṣadharmapratiṣedhād evārthaḥ | atha vā "na cāsyopadiśed dharmam" iti pravacanam atra vāryate, pūrvatra tadarthavyākaraṇāt | tathā ca matidānaṃ pūrvatra śrūyate | tac ca granthavyākhyānaliṅgaṃ śrutir vottaraṃ pūrvatra smṛtiḥ | tathā ca matidānam uktam | liṅgagranthato cottaratra granthoktaṃ (?) pūrvatra dharmavyākaraṇaṃ pratiṣiddhyate | matidānaṃ ca liṅgatvenoktam | śūdramatidānapratiṣedho vā strīmatidānābhyanujñānaparaḥ | kiṃ prāptam | tābhyo matidānaṃ prāptaṃ saha śiṣṭatvāt pratiṣedhasya | strīśūdraṃ tan nādhīyīteti yata evamarthaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | yathā cārthavādatvaliṅgam "ity uktvānuśāsanāsi maitreyi tāvad are khalu muktatvam iti he tvā yājñavalkyaḥ papraccha" (??) iti [Ed. pāṭho 'trātīva bhraṣṭaḥ | bṛhadāraṇyakopaniṣadgataṃ vākyam etat: "ity uktānuśāsanāsi maitreyi etāvad are khalv amṛtatvam iti hotkvā yājñavlkyo vijahāra (4.5.15) ityākārakam] nityārthaṃ vā "na cāsyopadiśed dharmam" iti nityaṃ dharmārthaṃ śāstraṃ "śudrāya nānuvaktavyam" na vyākaraṇīyam arthataḥ | evaṃ ca matidānapratiṣedhasyānityatvam agragranthoktasya (?) siddhaṃ bhavati | yathā jālapādapratiṣedhād eva haṃsacakravākapratiṣedhe siddhe haṃsacakravākagrahaṇaṃ punar nityārthaṃ syād anyeṣāṃ matsyādīnāṃ jālapādānāṃ pākṣikapravṛtteḥ kṣutparītasyāvasthitasya bhakṣaṇam anujānāti na anāpadi | evam asyāpi kṣudavasannātmakalatraputrasya parasmin daurgatyekavṛttimānasya vṛtter yuktyarthaṃ syād anugranthaokaṃ (?) śūdramatidānam ekadā taddharme | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "sarvathā tu śūdravṛttir aśaktā" (GDh 7.22) prītiyuktaṃ caitad evaṃ kartuṃ yena brāhmaṇasya vṛttkarśitena jīvitātyayam āpannātmakuṭumbasyāsatpratigraho viśiṣyate | śāstre "sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt brāhmaṇas tv anayaṃ gataḥ" (MDh 10.102) ity abhojyabhojanam api "jīvitātyayam āpanno yo 'nnam atti yatas tataḥ" (MDh 10.104) iti | cauryam api tathaiva | saptame bhakteti | kim utāvṛtte vṛttyarthaḥ (?) śūdramatidānam ācasthikaṃ (?) yathā sarvasvaṃ vā devadeveti brahmaghaprāyaścittārthavattvāya brāhmaṇasyānayaṃ gatasyāsatpratigrahasyādhikāraḥ (?) śāstra iṣyate upadeśasāmarthāt | brāhmaṇasyaivam [179] ihāpi nistīrṇodapapaś ca (?) tasyānivṛttiśāstram astīti varteta yaḥ śaktimān iti | yuktaṃ caitad evaṃ kalpayituṃ śāstre śūdrasya dharmopadeśasāmarthyāt | itarathā hy anarthaka upadeśas taddharmaḥ syāt | katham | śūdras tāvad anupadiṣṭaḥ svadharmaṃ na śaktaḥ pravaktum (?) | brāhmaṇasya ca tadupadeśaḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ ārṣasiddhadarśane cāsya svadharmo ('nantaḥ maharṣivanttācā) śaktaḥ maharṣivat tac cānyadharmapratipattyupāyaḥ kaḥ syād asyāḥ strīty anarthakaḥ tadupadeśasmṛtau prāpnoti | na caitad iṣṭam ato 'vaśyaṃ pratipādanīyo 'yam | yathāvyākhyātaḥ smṛtyarthaḥ | asmāt sāmarthād iti | yad caitad evaṃ bhavati, tata etat saṃgacchate | śūdraśāstradharme ca vastudharmajñā "yathā yathā hi sadvṛttam ātiṣṭhaty anasūyakaḥ" (MDh 10.128), "śūdrāṇāṃ māsikaṃ kāryaṃ vapanaṃ nyāyavartinām" (MDh 5,149), iti caivam anyāḥ śūdrasmṛtayaḥ śāstra upadiṣṭā yujyanta (?) iti | "na cāsya vratam ādiśet" ity atra kaścid āha | karmakriyopadiṣṭāṃ śūdrasya smṛtyadhyayanam arthāt prāpayati tad api ya (?) vidyāgrahaṇaṃ vratādeśanam iti | yataḥ evaṃ prāptasya pratiṣedho 'yaṃ "na cāsya vratam ādiśet" iti, tad etad ayuktam | tasmād vedādhyayanadharma eṣa na smṛteḥ | tathā ca nāmadheyaṃ vedavratāni vedagrahanārthāni vratāni vedavratāni | "na cānupanītasya vratādeśanam iṣyate" (2.173) iti vacanāt |upanayanaṃ cāsya paryudāsya pratiṣiddhaṃ pūrvatra | tac copodbalayati (?) ity (?) apare | vākye uttaratra "brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ" (MDh 10.4) iti | ato na kiṃcid etat | kas tarhy asyārthaḥ | nityārtha evāsyārambo veditavyaḥ | katham | sūkṣmavpratiṣedhaḥ syād anityatvāt snātakadharmavratādeśanam asya prāptaṃ tat pratiṣidhyate śūdrasya, prakaraṇasāmārthāt | tathā ca "dharmepsavas tu dharmajñāḥ" [MDh 10.127) ity evamādi | "yathā yathā hi sadvṛttam ātiṣṭhaty anasūyakaḥ" (MDh 10.128) iti cānena śāstrasāmarthyenotkṛṣyamāṇasyācārataḥ śūdrasya "vaiṇavīṃ dhārayed yaṣṭim" (MDh 4.36) ityevamādisnātakavratam asya nādiśet | sambhavantyām api prāptau yathāmayom (?) | tathā ca rājadharmeṣūpaviṣṭam idam eva daṇḍārtham "śūdrāṃs tu dvijaliṅginaḥ" (MDh 9.224) iti yato 'yam eva smṛtyartho veditavyaḥ | atha vā vrataṃ prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyati hi "etair vratair apoheta" (11.169) iti vacanāt atṃ nopadiśed ity arthaḥ | "yathā yathā hi sadvṛttam" (10128) ity anena śāstreṇa sadvṛttaprārethanāyāṃ tatrāsthitasy vyatikrame prāyaścittaṃ nopdiśed ity arthaḥ prakaraṇāc ca gamyate | eteṣām eva dharmāṇāṃ pratiṣiddhānāṃ vyatikrame abhojyādi bhojanādīnāṃ vyatikramaprāyaścittaṃ nopadiśet | na tu pātakopapātakādivyatikramaprakaraṇasāmarthyād ity uktam |
yo hy asya dharmam ācaṣṭe yaś copadiśati vratam |
so 'saṃvṛtaṃ nāma tamaḥ saha tenaiva gacchati || 4.81 ||
[180]
yadi śūdrasyānena nyāyenānujñāyate dharmopadeśa ekadāvasthiter astu nāyam apavādo yuktaḥ | "so 'saṃvṛtaṃ nāma tamaḥ saha tenaiva gacchati" ity adhikāravirodhād yuktam etan nindāvacanam | ato 'nyathopadeṣṭur yathādhikāraṃ dharmopadeśastutyarthaḥ |
na saṃhatābhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ kaṇḍūyetātmanaḥ śiraḥ |
na spṛśec caitad ucchiṣṭo na ca snāyād vinā tataḥ || 4.82 ||
saṃhatopadeśād asaṃhatayor apratiṣedhaḥ | kaṇḍūyateḥ pratiṣedhāt sparśanādikriyāntarāṇām apratiṣedhaḥ | ātmagrahaṇāt parasya saṃhatābhyām api hastābhyāṃ kaṇḍūyanāpratiṣedhaḥ | śirograhaṇāc cānyāvayavakaṇḍūyanāpratiṣedhaḥ | saṃhatābhyām apy ātmanaḥ na saṃspṛśed etad ucchiṣṭa upasaṃhatāsaṃhatābhyām api pāṇibhyāṃ "na ca snāyād vinā tataḥ" nityanaimittikayor na tu dṛṣṭārthenānena sāmarthyāc ca | sarvatraiṣa (?) gurulaghubhāvo darśayitavyaḥ |
keśagrahān prahārāṃś ca śirasy etān vivarjayet |
śiraḥsnātaś ca tailena nāṅgaṃ kiṃcid api spṛśet || 4.83 ||
na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇīyād arājanyaprasūtinaḥ |
sūnācakradhvajavatāṃ veśenaiva ca jīvatām || 4.84 ||
akṣatriyād rājñaḥ śūdrād anāpadi pratigrahapratiṣedhaḥ | yena rājño 'patyaṃ rājanyaḥ kṣatriya evocyate | tasya prasūtis tatprasūtis tajjātir evocyate | na rājanyaprasūtir arājanyaprasūtis tasmād arājanyaprasūtinaḥ śūdrād ity arthaḥ | anena rājyābhyutpattisāmarthyenocyate | evaṃ "rājato dhanam anvicchet" (MDh 4.33) ity anena śāstreṇa prāptasya pratiṣedhaḥ | ity anena śāstreṇa prāptasya pratiṣedhaḥ | sūnādīnāṃ cārthāt prāptaḥ pratiṣidhyate |
daśasūnāsamaṃ cakraṃ daśacakrasamo dhvajaḥ |
daśadhvajasamo veśo daśa veśasamo nṛpaḥ || 4.85 ||
daśasūnāsahasrāṇi yo vāhayati saunikaḥ |
tena tulyaḥ smṛto rājā ghoras tasya pratigrahaḥ || 4.86 ||
akṣatriyād rājñaḥ pratigrahanindaiṣā nivṛttyarthā | daśasūnāsamam iti parasparasya śuddhatā | tasmād yatnād vijñeyā rājanindārthā vā | sūnāgrahaṇaṃ daśaguṇottaravṛddhyāsminn api pakṣe teṣām api nindyatārthasiddhā | evaṃ sati
yo rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇāti lubdhasyocchāstravartinaḥ |
[181]        sa. paryāyeṇa yātīmān narakān ekaviṃśatim || 4.87 ||
tāmisram andhatāmisraṃ mahārauravarauravau |
narakaṃ kālasūtraṃ ca mahānarakam eva ca || 4.88 ||
saṃjīvanaṃ mahāvīciṃ tapanaṃ saṃpratāpanam |
saṃhātaṃ ca sakākolaṃ kuddālaṃ pūtimṛttikam || 4.89 ||
lohaśaṃkum ṛjīṣaṃ ca panthānaṃ śālmalīṃ nadīm |
asipatravanaṃ caiva lohadārakam eva ca || 4.90 ||
etad viditvā vidvāṃso brāhmaṇā brahmavādinaḥ |
na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇanti pretya śreyo 'bhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ || 4.91 ||
akṣatriyād rājño 'nāpadi pratigrahapratiṣedhaḥ prakaraṇād veditavyaḥ | kāraṇāc ca pratigrahapratiṣedhaṃ bruvan darśayati ya ucchāstravartī na bhavati tasmād anujānāti pratigrahaṃ śūdrād api yaś ca kṣatriyo 'py ucchāstravartī tasmāc ca pratigrahapratiṣedhas tulyatvāt kāraṇasya | ity evaṃ ca sati dharmādhikāraṇe ca jāte guṇabhāvo veditavyaḥ ||
brāhme muhūrte budhyeta dharmārthau cānucintayet |
kāyakleśāṃś ca tanmūlān vedatattvārtham eva ca || 4.92 ||
brāhmo muhūrtaḥ paścimayāmo rātreḥ prasiddho yogaśāstrādiṣu | pratibuddhaś ca tatra dharmārthāv anucintayet kleśānuṣṭhānatayā sādhyasādhanabhāvena parasparāvirodhena ceti kālakleśāṃś ca dharmānuṣṭhānamūlāṃś ca vedatattvārtham eva ca ṣaḍbhir aṅgaiḥ | ko 'tra vidhiḥ | ko 'rthavādapratiprasavaḥ | kimarthaś ca | ko 'nuvādaḥ | iyaṃ karmavidyā upaniṣadi hi karmaśeṣā | iyaṃ punar akarmaśeṣā svatantrā phalasaṃbandhinī | idaṃ jānam etena samuccīyate | karmaṇedaṃ vikalpyate | asyānenādhikāraḥ | karmaṇā nāsyedaṃ cāsyaṃ phalam abhyudayaṃ niśreyasaṃ ceti | karmāṅgadevatā cetthaṃbhūtā | kevalakarmakṛtkṣayiṇo (?) lokajñānasamuccayakāriṇas tu brahmatvam ity evamādipadārthāṃś cintayet | tasmin kāle prasannabuddhira anupasṛṣṭamohādibhiḥ (?) | evam
utthāyāvaśyakaṃ kṛtvā kṛtaśaucaḥ samāhitaḥ |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃs tiṣṭhet svakāle cāparāṃ ciram || 4.93 ||
[182]
asya stutiḥ |
ṛṣayo dīrghasaṃdhyatvād dīrgham āyur avāpnuyuḥ |
prajñāṃ yaśaś ca kīrtiṃ ca brahmavarcasam eva ca || 4.94 ||
agnihotrakāmasya saṃdhyopāsanakālasyoparodhāśaṅkayā punarvacanam asyāś cirakālavidhānārthaṃ brahmacāriṇo 'py anyaḥ saṃdhyopāsanakālaḥ | sa ca ṛkṣavibhāganād (?) iti | uditahomapakṣe ca naivaṃ saṃdhyoparodho 'gnihotrakāleneti |
śrāvaṇyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ vāpy upākṛtya yathāvidhi |
yuktaś chandāṃsy adhīyīta māsaṃ vipro 'rdhapañcamān || 4.95 ||
ayam upakaraṇavidhir yuktaḥ | prayatnavān vipragrahaṇāc ca kṣatriyavaiśyayor nivṛttimātraṃ darśayaty ardhapañcamān iti cobhayatra jñeyam | vyavasthitavikalpaś cāyam adhvaryūnāṃ śrāvaṇyām upākarma prauṣṭhapadyāṃ chandogānām |
puṣye tu chandasāṃ kuryād bahir utsarjanaṃ dvijaḥ |
māghaśuklasya vā prāpte pūrāhṇe prathame 'hani || 4.96 ||
utsargavidhyartho nigadārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
yathāśāstraṃ tu kṛtvaivam utsargaṃ chandasāṃ bahiḥ |
viramet pakṣiṇīṃ rātriṃ tad evaikam aharniśam || 4.97 ||
ubhayatrāhaḥ pakṣāpakṣiṇī ahorātraṃ ca (?) |
ata ūrdhvaṃ tu chandāṃsi śukleṣu niyataḥ paṭhet |
vedāṅgāni ca sarvāṇi kṛṣṇapakṣe tu saṃpaṭhet || 4.98 ||
utsargād ūrdhvam ayaṃ pravibhāgaḥ pṛthak brāhmaṇasyāvacanāt chandograhaṇaṃ brāhmaṇārtham api syāt | tasmin ślokatraye 'pi vibhaktamantrabrāhmaṇasāmarthāt | kvacid vede |
nāvispaṣṭam adhīyīta na śūdrajanasaṃnidhau |
na niśānte pariśrānto brahmādhītya punaḥ svapet || 4.99 ||
vṛṣalasaṃnidhau pratiṣedhād uttaratra, iha "strījanasaṃnidhau" iti yuktaḥ pāṭhaḥ | atha vā tv adhārmikas tatra gṛhyate vṛṣalavyutpattyā na jātivṛṣalaḥ | tato 'yam eva jyāyān pāṭhaḥ |
yathoditena vidhinā nityaṃ chandaskṛtaṃ paṭhet |
brahmachandaskṛtaṃ caiv dvijo yukto hy nāpadi || 4.100 ||
[183]
upasaṃhārārthaḥ ślokaḥ | brahmabrāhmaṇachandaskṛtaṃ mantrā gāyatrachando'nugataḥ ubhayatra chandasi brāhmaṇe ca |
imān nityam anādhyāyān adhīyāno vivarjayet |
adhyāpanaṃ ca kurvāṇaḥ śiṣyāṇāṃ vidhipūrvakam || 4.101 ||
ādarārthaṃ nityagrahaṇam | anadhyāyādhyayanavarjanaḥ prayatnato yathā syād iti | atha vā nityagrahaṇaṃ vāriṣikād adhyayanād anyatrāpi vakṣyamāṇaḥ anadhyāyaprakaraṇārthaṃ tatraiva –
karṇaśrave 'nile rātrau divā pāṃsusamūhane |
etau varṣāsv anadhyāyāv adhyāyajñāḥ pracakṣate || 4.102 ||
etena yā (?) yannimittam anadhyāyau varṣāsv eva |
vidyutstanitavarṣeṣu maholkānāṃ ca saṃplave |
ākālikam anadhyāyam eteṣu manur abravīt || 4.103 ||
na sarvasmin kāle | aśanisaṃnipāte |
etāṃs tv abhyuditān vidyād yadā prāduṣkṛtāgniṣu |
tadā vidyād anadhyāyam anṛtau cāha darśane (cābhra-) || 4.104 ||
etāṃs tu viduḥ dādan (?) | saṃdhyākāle yadā paśyet tadānadhyāyaḥ | adhikārād ākālikam evānarthakyāc cānyatra | varṣābhyo 'yam anadhyāyo gamyate | teṣu vidyādiṣu (?) saṃdhyākāle varṣāsv anyatra vākālika uktaḥ | ataḥ samdhyākāle punar vidhīyamāno 'narthakaḥ syāt tasmāt varṣāsv anadhikāro nivartate | ākālikādhikāra eṣv (?) anuvartate ity ucyate | atha vāsty evātrānṛtāv iti grahaṇād ubhayārthaṃ kariṣyāmas tathā ca sati | cakāra upariparikalpaḥ | anṛtāpradarśane ceti kecit | vayaṃ punaḥ pūrvaślokaśeṣatayā makaṃ(?) kalpayāmaḥ anuvādādiṣṭakālaviśeṣaṇārtham (?) |
nirghāte bhūmicalane jyotiṣāṃ copasarjane |
etān ākālikān dvidyād anādhyāyān ṛtāv api || 4.105 ||
nirghātādīnāṃ varṣāsu prāyeṇa darśanād ṛtāv apīty ucyate | apiśabdād anṛtāv api |
prāduṣkṛteṣv agniṣu tu vidytstanitaniḥsvane |
sajyotiḥ syād anādhyāyaḥ śeṣaṃ rātrau yathā divā || 4.106 ||
aparas tu kalpaḥ | udyateṣv agniṣu juhuyād api vidyutstanitaśabdayor anyataranimittopanipāte 'pi sajyotiḥ syād anadhyāyaḥ | divaivety arthaḥ | śeṣaṃ trayasya prakṛtasya [184] vidyutstanitavarṣeṣu | atra śloke vidyutstanitayor varṣaṃ tasmin upajāte rātrāv api divānadhyāyaḥ syād atha vā śeṣe dṛśyamāne rātrāv apīti pūrvasaṃdhyāto vā paścimasaṃdhyāśeṣaṃ tatra bhavatvād vidyutstanitayor api śeṣaśabda evaṃ ca kṛtvā taccheṣam api gṛhyamāṇaṃ rātrāv anadhyāyaṃ karoti yathā divā pūrvasaṃdhyābhavaṃ vidyutstanitam idaṃ ca vidhyantarakramāṇām ity ayaṃ vikalpaḥ sarvasmin adhyāyaprakaraṇe 'nadhikṛto veditavyaḥ | vikalpaliṅgāni cātraiva pratiślokaṃ darśayiṣyāmaḥ |
nityānadhyāya eva syād grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca |
dharmanaipuṇyakāmānāṃ pūtigandhe ca sarvaśaḥ || 4.107 ||
antaḥśavagate grāme vṛṣalasya ca saṃnidhau |
anadhyāyo rudyamāne samavāye janasya ca || 4.108 ||
udake madhyarātre ca viṇmūtrasya visarjane |
ucchiṣṭaḥ śrāddhabhuk caiva manasāpi na cintayet || 4.109 ||
aśūdrāṇām api | [Ed. atra granthapāta iva] vṛṣalaś ca vyākhyātaḥ pūrvatra | udake madhyarātrau ca visarjayann ucchiṣṭaḥ (?) śrāddhaṃ bhuktvaiva manasāpi na cintayet | avikalpārtham idam ādaravacanam | na tv anenānyeṣv anadhyāyeṣu manovṛttyādhyayanam anujñāyate |
pratigṛhya dvijo vidvān ekoddiṣṭasya ketanam |
tryahaṃ ca kīrtayed brahma rājño rāhoś ca sūtake || 4.110 ||
ekoddiṣṭaṃ navaśrāddhaṃ yatraikavan mantrāṇām ūhas tadā mantraṇaṃ pratigṛhya vidvān apīti śabdalopaḥ | kecit tv āhuḥ: na tryaham | kiṃ tarhi |
yāvad ekānudiṣṭasya sneho lepaś ca tiṣṭhati |
viprasya viduṣo dehe tāvat brahma na kīrtayet || 4.111 ||
ekānuddiṣṭasya tryahaṃ vānadhyāyaḥ | snehāvagamo vā tāvat |
śayānaḥ pauḍhapādaś ca kṛtvā caivāvaskthikām |
nādhīyītāmiṣaṃ jagdhvā sūtkānnādyam eva ca || 4.112 ||
aśrāddhārtham amiṣasya grahaṇam | sūtakānnādanenānadhyāyavacanād anyeṣv abhojyabhojaneṣu nāsty anādhyāyaḥ |
nīhāre bāṇaśabde ca saṃdhyayor eva cobhayoḥ |
amāvāsyācaturdaśyoḥ paurṇamāsyaṣṭakāsu ca || 4.113 ||
[185]
bāṇo vīṇā, "bāṇaṃ vitanvati (vādayati?)" iti prayogadarśanāc chāstrāntare 'pi cānyātodyasāhacaryād bāṇo viṇā | bherīmṛdaṅgagarttārttaśabdeṣv (??) iti vacanād ātodyaśabdopalakṣaṇaparaṃ vaitad bāṇagrahaṇam | na svārtham etat tṛcāṣṭakāgrahaṇam aṣṭamīnavamyartham amāvāsyādisāhacaryāt | evaṃ ca saty aṣṭakādiṣv ahorātram ity etad arthavad bhavati |
amāvāsyā guruṃ hanti śiṣyaṃ hanti caturdaśī |
brahmāṣṭkapaurṇamāsyau (?) tasmāt tāḥ parivarjayet || 4.114 ||
viśeṣavacanād avikalpo 'nadhyayasya caturdaśyādiṣu gamyate | tathā śakhalikhitau nityānadhāyaṃ parvasv āhatuḥ (?) |
pāṃsuvarṣe diśāṃ dāhe gomāyuvirute tathā |
śvakharoṣṭre ruvati ca paṅktau ca na paṭhed dvijaḥ || 4.115 ||
nādhīyīta śmaśānānte grāmānte govraje tathā |
vasitvā maithunaṃ vāsaḥ śrāddhikaṃ pratigṛhya ca || 4.116 ||
prāṇi vā yadi vāprāṇi yat kiṃcic chrāddikaṃ bhavet |
tad ālabhyāpy anadhyāyaḥ pāṇyāsyo hi dvijaḥ smṛtaḥ || 4.117 ||
corair upaplute grāme saṃbhrame cāgnikārite |
ākālikam anadhyāyaṃ vidyāt sarvādbhuteṣu ca || 4.118 ||
adbhutāni divyantarikṣabhaumāny atharvavedād upalabdhavyāni yajurvede ca keṣāṃcid etāni sūtrakārair upadiṣṭāni |
upākarmaṇi sotsarge trirātraṃ kṣepaṇaṃ smṛtam |
aṣṭakāsu tv ahorātram ṛtvantāsu ca rātriṣu || 4.119 ||
aṣṭakās tu catasraś "caturaṣṭako hemantaḥ" iti vacanāt | yatyaṣṭaka ity apare | ṛtvantyāsu ca rātriṣv anadhyāyaḥ |
nādhīyītāśvam ārūḍho na vṛkṣaṃ na ca hastinam |
na nāvaṃ na kharaṃ noṣṭraṃ neriṇastho na yānagaḥ || 4.120 ||
aśvādiyānopadeśāt tebhyo 'nyad yānaṃ gantryādy upadiśyate sāmarthyāt | [186]
na vivāde na kalahe na senāyāṃ na saṅgare |
na bhuktamātre nājīrṇe na samitvā na śuktake || 4.121 ||
vivādakalahayor ātmagatayor anadhyāyaḥ | asainiko 'pi senāyām alakṣito (?) nādhīyīta | tathā saṅgare | kaḥ punar anayor viśeṣaḥ | apravṛttayuddhā senā | pravṛttayuddhāh saṅgaraḥ | gobalīvardanyāyena vyākhyeyau |
atithiṃ cānujñāpya mārute vāti vā bhṛśam |
rudhire ca śrute gātrāc chastreṇa ca parikṣate || 4.122 ||
sāmadhvanāu cargyajuṣī nādhīyīta kadācana |
vedasyādhītya cāpy antam āraṇyakam adhītya ca || 4.123 ||
kadācid apy anadhyāyāpeṣur vidvān naiva ca "varjayen nityam" (MDh 4.127) ity evamādi | āraṇyakaṃ caikākināpy adhītyānārabdhavedādhyayno 'py atra kada(?) kāraṇābhāsam arthavādam āha |
ṛgvedo devadaivatyo yajurvedas tu mānuṣaḥ |
sāmavedaḥ smṛtaḥ pitryas tasmāt tasyāśucir dhvaniḥ || 4.124 ||
arthavādebhyaḥ kenacit sāmānyenedam ucyate | tulyabalānāṃ virodhaḥ | yato 'tra smṛtiḥ śrutyā bādhyate, balīyastvāc chruteḥ | ity evaṃ ca sati –
etad viditvā vidvāṃsas trayīniṣkarṣam anvaham |
kramaśaḥ pūrvam abhyasya paścād vedam adhīyate || 4.125 ||
etad eva pitṛmanuṣyātmakatvantaryā (?) evaṃ viditvā trayīniṣkarṣaṃ praṇavavyāhṛtisāvitryākhyam anvahaṃ kramaśaḥ pūrvaṃ vedādhyayanaṃ trir abhyasya tato yaṃ kaṃcid vedam adhīyate | vidyāsaṃśayakāraṇam evādhikriyamāṇe devapitṛmānuṣyātmakaṃ brahmalokatrayāvajayāya (?) tasyādhyayanenaivaṃ guṇavataḥ kalpitaṃ bhavati | anyā saṃpādaśiṣyavau (?) | ācāryaprakaraṇe evāyam artho vaktavyaḥ sann arthavādopajijīviṣayā pūrvasya pratiṣedho 'pīhokta iti |
paśumaṇḍūkamārjārāśvasarpanakuleṣu ca |
antarāgamane vidyād anadhyāyam aharniśam || 4.126 ||
paśuśabdāc ca kecid viśeṣaṇa (?) sapta grāmyān paśūn icchanti | go'vyājāśvāśvaataragardabhapuruṣān sapta cāraṇyānuṣṭhādīn (?) | [187]
[Ed. bhārucivyākhyātamanusmṛtipāṭhamātragocaraḥ ślokaḥ]
apare tu paśuyāgam eva "paśunā yajeta" ity atra sāmānyacodanāyāḥ vibhāgasya ca pāyāmeso 'nubrūhi (?) ity asmān mantravaṇād yathā chāgaḥ pratīyate, evam ihāpi kutascit sāmarthyāt chāgas tāvat pratipādanīya iti | pūrvas tv aviśeṣacodanārtho jyāyān atra dṛśyate netaraḥ sāmarthadarśanābhāvāt | sarvānadhyāyavikalpārthaṃ tv idam adhunocyate |
dvāv eva varjayen nityam anadhyāyau prayatnataḥ |
svādhyāyabhūmiṃ cāśuddhām ātmanaś cāśuciṃ dvijaḥ || 4.127 ||
nityagrahaṇād atra sarvāya pūrvānadhyāye vidhiḥ vikalpeneti gamyate | sati hi proyojane ādarārthaṃ nityagrahaṇam nopapadyate | kiṃ tarhi | pūrvānadhyāyān nityārtham eva tathā coktāni tatra tatrānityatvaliṅgānīti kṛtārthatvāpekṣayā cāyam adhyetṝṇāṃ vikalpānadhyāyānāṃ draṣṭavyaḥ | nātmecchayā tu vedādhyayananiyamaḥ, smṛtyupadeśasāmarthyād iti | śruteś ca "tasya ha vā ye tv anyabrahmasatrasya dvāvesāna syād (?) yena yad ātmāśucir yaddeśa" iti | yadi brahmasatraviṣayam etat kalpyate smṛtivacanam anadhyāyārthaṃ tataḥ pūrvair liṅgair evānadhyāyānindyatā boddhavyā | nānena "dvāv eva varjayen nityam" ity evamādinācāraprasaṃgāt tam anadhyāyaprakaraṇaṃ parisamāpya tadupadeśa eva bhūyo 'yam ucyate |
amāvāsyām aṣṭamīṃ ca paurṇamāsīṃ caturdaśīm (?) |
brahmacārī bhaven nityam apy ṛtau snātako dvijaḥ || 4.128 ||
parvakālena tu kālātikramād yuktaḥ parvasu parihārārthaḥ | pāṭho yam api – "ṛtau snātako dvijaḥ" iti | tathā coktam – "parvavajaṃ vrajec cainām" (MDh 3.45) ity ataś ca | "anṛtau" ity ayam anyaḥ pāṭhaḥ utsṛjyate, vyarthānarthasamānārtham, ārṣaṃ na vaco yata iti |
na snānam ācared bhuktvā nāturo na mahāniśi |
na vāsobhiḥ sahājasraṃ nāvijñāte jalāśaye || 4.129 ||
adṛṣṭārthasya snātasyāyaṃ vidhiḥ | śaucārthasya snānasya naimittikatvān nimittānāṃ ca niyatakālatvāt sarvadā pratiṣedhaḥ | āturavacanam aprāyaścittārtham etasmād eva yato 'nyatrāturasyāsati vacane niyamātikramāt | prāyaścittīy[te?]r siddhā (?) na mahān ity ardharātre naimittikam api nivāso (nirvāsāḥ?) hi nityaṃ turvāsa (bahuvāsāḥ?) smṛtīyaṃ snātakasya nityāgagata paśyan pratiśedhati (?) |
[188]
devatānāṃ guro rājñaḥ snātakācāryayos tathā |
nākrāmet kāmataś chāyāṃ babhruṇo dīkṣitasya ca || 4.130 ||
amūrtatvād devatānāṃ tatprakṛtaye dṛḍhaṃ te 'nenaiva darśanena cchata(?)prakṛtīḥ śiṣṭāḥ pūjayati babhrur iti yataḥ somasya karmāṅgabhūtasyeyam ākhyā tadāpy ayaṃ babhruśabdo na śrūyate | kapilā vā gaur babhruśabdenocyate | "sā babhuṃ liṅgākṣī" iti śruteḥ |  brahmacāriṇam apare babhruśabdārtham āhuḥ | kapilaśiroruhatvād iti kāmata iti ca | akāmataḥ prāyaścittanivṛttyartham idaṃ vacanaṃ sāmarthāt gamyate |
madhyandine 'rdharātre ca śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā ca sāmiṣam |
saṃdhyayor ubhayoś caiva na seveta catuṣpatham || 4.131 ||
udvartanam apasnānaṃ viṇmūtre raktam eva ca |
śleśmaniṣṭhūtavāntāni nādhitiṣṭhet tu kāmataḥ || 4.132 ||
vairiṇaṃ nopaseveta sahāyaṃ caiva vairiṇaḥ |
adhārmikaṃ taskaraṃ ca parasyaiva ca yoṣitam || 4.133 ||
na hīdṛśam anāyuṣyaṃ loke kiṃcana dṛśyate |
yādṛśaṃ puruṣasyeha paradāropasevanam || 4.134 ||
pratītārthāḥ ślokāḥ | kāmata iti vyākhyātam |
kṣatriyaṃ caiva sarpaṃ ca brāhmaṇaṃ ca bahuśrutam |
nāvamanyeta vai bhūṣṇuḥ kṛśān api kadācana || 4.135 ||
kṣatriyasarpayor dṛṣṭāntārtham upādānaṃ ca guṇavadbrāhmaṇaparivārasya (?) |
etat trayaṃ hi puruṣaṃ nirdahed avamānitam |
dṛṣṭādṛṣṭasaṃbandhe nedam ucyate |
tasmād etat trayaṃ nityaṃ nāvamanyet buddhimān || 4.136 ||
anye 'py āhuḥ | trayāṇām apy adṛṣṭārthaṃ sati kṣatriyasarpayoḥ sāmarthyād vyavasthitānām abahuśrutānām apy ayaṃ pratiṣedho gamyate | hīnāṅgādīnām ākṣepapratiṣedhād uttaratra daṇḍāpūpikayā kṣatriyādīnām anākṣepaḥ siddhaḥ | utkṛṣṭatvāt tu viśeṣapratiṣedho 'yaṃ prāyaścittaviśeṣanārtho vā kṣatriyādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ |
[189]
nātmānam avamanyeta pūrvābhir asamṛddhibhiḥ |
ā mṛtyoḥ śriyam anvicchan naināṃ manyeta durlabhām || 4.137 ||
ātmā nāvamantavya ity adṛṣṭārtho 'yaṃ niyamaḥ | ātmāvamānāc ca kadācid apy ayaṃ viṣabhakṣaṇādiṣv apy ākāryeṣu pravartate |
satyaṃ brūyāt priyaṃ brūyān na brūyāt satyam apriyam |
priyaṃ ca nānṛtaṃ brūyād eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ || 4.138 ||
tatra satyaṃ brūyād ity etad uktam | satyavac cedam anyapadārthāntaravaktavyatayā tadviśeṣārthaṃ vidhīyate, "priyaṃ brūyāt" iti | so 'yam adhunocyate viseṣaḥ | na brūyāt satyam apriyam | yathā "brāhmaṇa, kanyā te garbhiṇī" iti | ayaṃ tāvat satyavacanāpavādād apriyavacanam idānīm apohyate | "priyaṃ ca nānṛtaṃ brūyāt" yathā kanyāyāṃ jātāyām "brāhmaṇa, putras te jātaḥ" iti | tatrānena satyāṃ gatau ajñānam āśrayitavyam | asatyaṃ tu parapīḍātaḥ | atra vīddhaḥ (?) param iti yataḥ kāruṇyāt paraduḥkhotpattiparihārārtham anṛtam āśrayitavyam | evaṃ sarvāpriyeṣu bandhumaraṇādiṣu veditavyam | sākṣivacane tu vyavahārata ekasya pīḍāparam iti syād (?) anugraho 'vaśyaṃ bhāvyam ubhayatrāpi | tatra yuktaṃ śakyam eva vaktuṃ nānṛtam atra doṣād vadanānuṣaṅgād iti viṣayavivekātmakam evaṃ cedaṃ satyam priyayor uktam | nāpūrvavipyatmiḥ (nānṛtam ityādibhiḥ?) satyasya (?) |
bhradraṃ bhadram iti brūyāt bhadram ity eva vā vadet |
gṛhavairaṃ vivādaṃ ca na kuryāt kenacit saha || 4.139 ||
abhadraṃ bhadram ity uktam ādarārtham | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "abhadraṃ bhadram ity āhuḥ" (GDh 9.20) | evaṃ ca saty atra na śabdaniyamaḥ bhadram iti ced dvitīyaṃ bhadraśabdagrahaṇaṃ śabdaniyamārthaṃ tathā tv evakāragrahaṇam | bhadram ity eva vā vaded ity atra idam aśabda evaṃ niyamyate |
nātikalyaṃ nātisāyaṃ nātimadhyaṃdine sthite |
nājñātena samaṃ gacchen naiko na vṛṣalaiḥ saha || 4.140 ||
hīnāṅgān atiriktāṅgān vidyāhīnān vayo'tigān |
rūpadraviṇahīnāṃś ca jātihīnāṃś ca nākṣipet || 4.141 ||
na spṛśet pāṇinocchiṣṭo vipro gobrāhmaṇānalān |
na cāpi paśyed aśuciḥ svastho jyotirgaṇān divin || 4.142 ||
[190]        spṛṣṭvāitān aśucir nityam adbhiḥ prāṇān upaspṛśet |
gātrāṇi caiva sarvāṇi nābhiṃ pāṇitalena tu || 4.143 ||
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā jyotīṃṣy aśucir etad eva kuryād ubhayasya pratiṣiddhatvāt tulyavyatikramaprāyaścittam adhikārasāmarthāt |
anāturaḥ svāni khāni na spṛśed animittataḥ |
romāṇi ca rahasyāni sarvāṇy eva vivarjayet || 4.144 ||
svanigadavyākhyātaḥ ślokaḥ |
maṅgalācārayuktaḥ syāt prayatātmā jitendriyaḥ |
japec ca juhuyāc caiva nityam agnim atandritaḥ || 4.145 ||
duḥkhasvapnadarśanādiṣu yat kriyate tadupaghātāya tanmaṅgalam atha vā kriyamāṇam abhyudayāya rocatām iti | saṃsparśaḥ śiraādīnām akriyamāṇo vā kriyamāṇo vā pratyavāya | yathā tad eva maṅgalācāraḥ satyavacanādiḥ | yadi uktau maṅgalācāras tato 'sya punaḥśrutir arthavādārthā | atha yuktas tato yuktam evāsya vacanam apūrvavidhyartham | tathā ca vakṣyati | atha vā sādanābhāvapakṣam āśritya gṛhasthasyaitad ucyate | ātmaikasādhanasādhyam aparādhīnaṃ kramety asya stutiḥ (?) |
maṅgalācārayuktānāṃ nityāṃ ca niyatātmanām |
japatāṃ juhvatāṃ caiva vinipāto na vidyate || 4.146 ||
tad idam ācāramukhyatvapradarśanāyocyate tasya |
vedam eva japen (?) nityaṃ yathākālam atandritaḥ |
taṃ hy asyāhuḥ paraṃ dharmam upadharmo 'nya ucyate || 4.147 ||
anyācāranindā vedādhyayanastutyarthā |
vedābhyāsena satataṃ śaucena tapasaiva ca |
adroheṇaiva bhūtānāṃ jātiṃ smarati paurvikīm || 4.148 ||
paurvikīṃ saṃsmaran jātiṃ brahma cābhyasyati dvijaḥ |
brahmābhyāsena cājasram anantaṃ sukham aśnute || 4.149||
vedābhyāsasy punarvacanaṃ stutyartham |
sāvitrāñ chāntihomāṃś ca kuryāt parvasu nityaśaḥ |
pitṝṃś caivāṣṭakāsv arcen nityam anvaṣṭakāsu ca || 4.150 ||
[191]
sāvitrāñ chāntihomāṃś ceti (?) vacanād ājyena kartavyād amukhyasyānyasya
homasādhanasya |
dūrād āvasathaṃ mūtraṃ dūrāt pādāvanejanam |
ucchiṣṭānnaniṣekaṃ ca dūrād eva samācaret || 4.151 ||
prasiddhārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
maitraṃ prasādhanaṃ snānaṃ dantadhāvanam añjanam |
pūrvāhṇ eva kurvīta devatānāṃ ca pūjanam || 4.152 ||
niyamārthaḥ śāstrārambhaḥ |
devatāny abhigaccheta dhārmikāṃś ca dvijottamān |
īśvaraṃ caiva rakṣārthaṃ gurūn eva ca parvasu || 4.153 ||
abhivādayeta vṛddhāṃś ca dadyāc caivāsanaṃ svakam |
kṛtāñjalir upāsīta gacchataḥ pṛṣṭhato 'nviyāt || 4.154 ||
uttarārtham ācārasya punarvacanam |
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ dharmaṃ nibandhaṃ sveṣu karmasu |
dharmamūlaṃ niṣeveta sadācāram atandritaḥ || 4.155 ||
sarvasyāsya phalasiddhiḥ |
ācārāl labhate hy āyur ācārād īpsitāḥ prajāḥ |
ācārād dhanam akṣayyam ācāro hanty alakṣaṇam || 4.156 ||
durācāro hy puruṣo loke bhavati ninditaḥ |
duḥkhabhāgī hi stataṃ vyādhito 'lpāyur eva ca || 4.157 ||
sarvalakṣaṇahīno 'pi yaḥ sadācāravān naraḥ |
śraddadhāno 'nasūyaś ca śatavarṣāṇi jīvati || 4.158 ||
ācārānuṣṭhānapraśaṃsanārthāś catvāraḥ ślokāḥ ubhayathā vākyasvābhāvyād vijñeyāḥ | prathame 'dhyāye ācārastutiḥ śāstrastutyarthoktā "ācāraḥ paramo dharmaḥ" (MDh 1.108) ityevamādi | iha tv ācāravidhiprakaraṇe stutiḥ svārthaprakaraṇaprāptā veditavyā |
yad yat paravaśaṃ karma tat tad yatnena varjayet |
[192]
kaḥ kṛtakaraṇīyaḥ viśeṣato yakṣyo dāsya ity evam (?) abhiprāyaḥ | pratigrahādiṣu pravarteta |
yad yad ātmavaśaṃ tu syāt tat tat seveta yatnataḥ || 4.159 ||
loke 'pi caitad dṛṣṭam |
sarvaṃ paravaśaṃ duḥkhaṃ sarvam ātmavaśaṃ sukham |
etad vidyāt samāsena lakṣaṇaṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ || 4.160 ||
upary eva svaviṣayopabhogārthaṃ dravyādānaṃ pratigrahādibhir ātmavaśam indriyasaṃyamaḥ (?) saṃtoṣaparatayā so 'yam apareṇa prakāreṇendriyasaṃyama ukto 'pi punaḥ ślokadvayenānūdyate | adhikṛtācārānuṣṭhānasutisaṃbandhenācārabahutve 'pi sati –
yat karma kurvato 'sya syāt paritoṣo 'ntarātmanaḥ |
tat prayatnena kurvīta viparītaṃ tu varjayet || 4.161 ||
vihitakarmaviśeṣam etad viparītavan manaś cānuṣṭhīyamānaṃ yad apratyavāyāya | tadviṣayastutyarthaṃ bhāvaprasādasya |
ācāryaṃ ca pravaktāraṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ tathā |
na hiṃsyād brāhmaṇān gāṃś ca sarvāṃś caiva tapasvinaḥ || 4.162 ||
sarvatrānapakāriṣu hiṃsā neṣyate | ācāryādīn hy anyāpakāriṇo 'pi sato na hiṃsyāt | pratikūlācaraṇaṃ cācāryādīnāṃ hiṃsety ucyate na vadhaḥ | tathā coktam "pratiroddhā guroś caiva" (MDh 3.153) ityarthaḥ (ityādinā?) prāptasya pratiṣedhaḥ | tathā ca bhārate: pratikūlācaraṇam evārjunena yudhiṣṭhirasya hanti (??) |
nāstikyaṃ vedanindāṃ ca devatānāṃ ca kutsanam |
dveṣaṃ stambhaṃ ca mānaṃ ca krodhaṃ baikṣyaṃ ca varjayet || 4.163||
dveṣaviśeṣapradarśanārthaṃ tat | parivarjanārtham ācāryādibhyaś cānyatrāpi |
parasya daṇḍaṃ nodyacchet kruddho nainaṃ nipātayet |
anyatra putrāc chiṣyād vā śiṣṭyarthaṃ tāḍayec ca tau || 4.164 ||
brāhmaṇāyāvaguryaiva dvijātir vadhakāṅkṣayā |
śataṃ varṣāṇi tāmisre narake parivartate || 4.165 ||
[193]        tāḍayitvā tṛṇenāpi saṃrambhāt matipūrvakam |
ekaviṃśatim ājātiṃ pāpayoniṣ jāyat || 4.166 ||
ayudhyamānasyotpādya brāhmaṇasyāsṛg aṅgataḥ |
duḥkhaṃ sumahad āpnoti pretyāprājñatayā naraḥ || 4.167 ||
yāvataḥ śoṇitaṃ pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇāti mahītalāt |
tāvato 'bdān amutrānyaiḥ śoṇitotpādako 'dyate || 4.168 ||
anyair iti vyāgrāṭādibhir (?) ity arthaḥ |
na kadācid dvijo viprāṃs tasmād avagured api |
na tāḍayet tṛṇenāpi na gātrāt srāvayed asṛk || 4.169 ||
sarvaprāṇipīḍāparihārārthaṃ cedaṃ brāhmaṇanidarśanād draṣṭavyam | tathā ca prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyati | rājadaṇḍaṃ ca na sarvaprāṇihiṃsāyāṃ na kevalaṃ brāhmaṇasyaiveti | prakarṣārthaṃ tu vyatikramasya brāhmaṇagrahaṇam | tathā ca loke dṛṣṭam |
adhārmiko naro yo hi yasya cāpy anṛtaṃ dhanam |
hiṃsāratiś ca yo nityaṃ nehāsau sukham edhate || 4.170 ||
evaṃ ca sati –
na sīdann api dharmeṇa
vyavasthārūpeṇātmasaṃkocākhyena
mano 'dharme niveśayet |
śāstravyavasthābhaṅgarūpe | kasya hetoḥ –
adhārmikāṇāṃ pāpānām āśu paśyan viparyayam || 4.171 ||
dṛṣṭaṃ ced adam (?) adṛṣṭaṃ śāstrataḥ | kiṃ kāraṇam | yena –
nādharmaś carito loke sadyaḥ phalati gaur iva |
gaur viṣāṇīva pṛthivī | sādharmyavaidharmyābhyāṃ dṛṣṭāntena vacanīyau (?) prakṛtārthasiddhaye |
śanair āvartamānas tu
kāryam ārabhyamāṇaṃ deśakālaprāye(kāra?)ṇādyapekṣayā
kartur mūlāni kṛntati || 4.172 ||
[194]
mūlakartanaṃ guṇavādo 'tiśayārthaḥ | kartāram apunarbhāvaṃ nayati | evaṃ ca sati –
yadi nātmani putreṣu na cet putreṣu naptṛṣu |
na tv eva tu kṛto 'dharmaḥ kartur bhavati niṣphalaḥ || 4.173 ||
iha phaladarśanān nānarthakam (?) aniṣṭakarma pratyetavyam | kiṃ tarhi | evaṃ putrapautrān iṣṭaphalabhogena śanair vipacyate 'niṣṭaṃ karmārthavādaś cāyam akṛtābhyāgamataḥ vipraṇāśadoṣaprasaṅgāc chāstravirodhāc caiva putrādīnām aniṣṭaphalasaṃbandhapradarśanena vibhīṣikaiṣā akāryanivṛttyarthā | tathā ca na ceha (?) śāstram aparigaṇayyaivaṃ vyavasthābhaṅgena vartitavyaṃ yena dharmeṇa dhṛte tāvac chāstravyavasthābhṅgākhyena (?) tv ātmasamavāyinā mukhyena kṛtaṃ tanmukhasya vṛddhikāryāsambhavāt | tāvācchabdaś cākāṅkṣayā śiraḥkampopagṛhītayā vyākhyānīyaḥ |
[adharmeṇaidhate kascit]? tato bhadrāṇi paśyati |
yathārtham aniruddhātmavṛttaḥ padārthasvābhāvyāt |
tataḥ sapatnāñ jayati
dvitīyaṃ puruṣārtham abhyudayād anarthoparamaṃ prāpnoti tat svābhāvadarśanāt pratyakṣataḥ | prāyaṇottarkālam ayam,
samūlas tu vinaśyati || 4.174 ||
dharmādharmavyavasthāprāmāṇyavacchāstrasya tatprāmāṇyāt | tathā ca lokavādaḥ – anumānaṃ cāyurvedaśāstrataḥ lokataś ca vacanīyaḥ | mūlavināśaś ca vyākhyātaḥ pūrvaśloke |
satyadharmāryavṛtteṣu śauce caivāramet sadā |
śiṣyāṃś ca śiṣyād dharmeṇa vāgbāhūdarasaṃyutaḥ || 4.175 ||
sarvasnātakakarmaṇām upahārārtha uktānuvādaḥ | "na sīdann api dharmeṇa mano 'dharme niveśayet" (MDh 4.171) ity atra ślokopadiṣṭaḥ "trivarga iti tu sthitḥ" (MDh 2.224) iti yatas tadapekṣayyedam ucyate |
parityajed arthakāmau yau syātāṃ dharmavarjitau |
dharmaṃ cāpy asukhodarkaṃ lokasaṃkruṣṭam eva ca || 4.176 ||
arthakāmau dharmānubandhinau syātām | tena (tau?) saty api trivargaparigrahadharmaṃ cāpy asukhodarkaṃ varjayed asukhodarkam asamarthasya svaśarīroparodhinī neṣyate yena [195] triyātmayogyasya dhanam apekṣyate | dṛṣṭārthavad adṛṣṭārthe 'pi śāstraśiṣṭatvāc ca ātmasaṃrakṣaṇasya lokasaṅkruṣṭo dharmas tatsādṛśyavādārthaṃ yajña[ā]caryate 'tha vā sarvam apy etat siddham | lokasaṅkruṣṭārthaṃ tu punar ārabhyate | sa ced jyāyān govadho yathā yajñāṅgapratirodhe vā somavikrayiṇaṃ brahmacaryapuṃścalyākrośas tu pratiṣidhyate | vidhānasāmarthyāt tasya śukla(?)vacanapratiṣedhād vā na kartavyaḥ | apare tu paradāram udāharanti satyavacane vā prāṇivināśaṃ prāyaścittopadeśād "vāgdevatyais tu carubhiḥ" (MDh 8.105) ityevamādi | evaṃ ca sati –
na pāṇipādacapalo na netracapalo 'nṛjuḥ |
na syād vākcapalaś caiva na paradrohakarmadhīḥ || 4.177 ||
nigadavyākhyātaḥ ślokaḥ | idam anyat saddharmapratipattaye kāraṇam andhunocyate |
yenāsya pitaro yātā yena yātāḥ pitāmahāḥ |
tena yāyāt satāṃ mārgaṃ tena gacchan na riṣyati || 4.178 ||
satāṃ mārgam iti liṅgāt santo vedasmṛtiśāstravidaḥ pitṛpitāmahā ucyante | tair yajñācāra ācaritas tam anutiṣṭhet | katham | yady api svadharmapratipattaye svayaṃ vivecanāyām asamarthas tathāpi tair ācarita it kṛtvā tadbhaktyāpi tāvat pratipadyate | upadeśam antareṇa pūrvaprayogānuśiṣṭa ity apareṇa dvāreṇa saddharmapratipattikāraṇam ucyate |
ṛtvikpurohitācāryair mātulātithisaṃśrayaiḥ |
bālavṛddhāturair vaidyair jñātisaṃbandhibāndavaiḥ || 4.179 ||
mātāpitṛbhyāṃ jāmībhir bhrātrā putreṇa bhāryayā |
duhitrā dāsavargeṇa vivādaṃ na samācaret || 4.180 ||
etair vivādān saṃtyajya sarpāpaiḥ pramucyate |
etān jitvā ca jayati sarvalokān imān gṛhī || 4.181 ||
yatas tata ime ucyate |
ācāryo brahmalokeśaḥ prājāpatye pitā prabhuḥ |
atithis tv indralokeśo devalokasya cartvijaḥ || 4.182 ||
jāmayo 'psarasām loke vaiśvadevasya bāndhavāḥ |
saṃbandhino 'pāṃ lokasya pṛthivyāṃ mātṛmātulau || 4.183 ||
ākāśeśās tu vijñeyā bālavṛddhakṛśāturāḥ |
bhrātā jyeṣṭhaḥ samaḥ pitrā bhāryā putraḥ svakā tanuḥ || 4.184||
[196]        chāyā svā dāsavargas tu duhitā kṛpaṇaṃ param |
tasmād etair adhikṣiptaḥ sahetāsaṃjvaraḥ sadā || 4.185 ||
ṛtvigādīnāṃ grahaṇaṃ saptabhiḥ ślokaiḥ saṃbandhamātrajñāpanārthaḥ |
pratigrahasamartho 'pi prasaṅgaṃ tatra varjayet |
pratigraheṇa hy asyāśu brahmatejaḥ praśāmyati || 4.186 ||
"yātrāmātraprasiddhyartham" (MDh 4.3) ity atroktam api tad etat punar ucyate | kṛtakaraṇīyasya viśeṣapratiṣedhārthaṃ viśeṣato vakṣye | dāsya (?) ity evaṃ ca sati satpratigrahād api vṛttiniyamasyābhyudayaviśeṣārthā uttarārthaṃ vā pratigrahaviśeṣābhidhitsayā | tathā ca darśayati |
na dravyāṇām avijñāya vidhiṃ dharmyaṃ parigrahe |
prājñaḥ pratigrahaṃ kuryād avasīdann api kṣudhā || 4.187 ||
dharmyo vidhir yajñārthatvam | dhanasya etad vijñāya dravyadevatām vā "varuṇāyāśvaṃ rudrāya gām" ity evam ārabhya dravyāṇāṃ devatābhihitā śāstrāntare | pratigraham antaṃ vā sāmānyaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ vā | "avasīdann api kṣudā" ity arthavādo 'yaṃ pratigrahavidhistutyarthaḥ | evaṃ ca kṣudhāvasāde abuddhyāpi gṛhyamāṇo (?) na pratyavāyāya | etad api śabdāt | tad idānīṃ pratigrahadravyaṃ darśayati |
hiraṇyaṃ bhūmim aśvaṃ gām annaṃ vāsas tilān ghṛtam |
pratigṛhṇann avidvāṃs tu bhasmībhavati dāruvat || 4.188 ||
etad dravyaviṣayo vā | ayaṃ vidhir utpāte teṣāṃ dravyāṇāṃ nidarśanārthatvād vai teṣāṃ grahaṇasya sāmānyeneva stutyarthaḥ | prakṛtasya pratigrahavidheḥ | tathā ca hiraṇyādigrahaṇaṃ vivakṣitam asya viśeṣapratiṣedhārthād ādarārtham ayam ucyate |
hiraṇyam āyur annaṃ ca bhūr gauś cāpy oṣatas tanam |
aśvaś cakṣus tvacaṃ vāso ghṛtaṃ tejas tilāḥ prajāḥ || 4.189 ||
yataś caitad evam |
atapās tv anadhīyānaḥ pratigraharucir dvijaḥ |
ambhasy aśmaplaveneva saha tenaiva majjati || 4.190 ||
evaṃ ca sati tapovijñānasahāyam atropadeśād vijñeyam | pūrvavidhivad adhyanagrahaṇaṃ ca vijñānādhikārasāmarthyāt | tadarthaṃ vijñeyaṃ svārthaṃ vā tapaso viśeṣaṇārthaṃ vā | atapasvī yo 'nadhīyāna iti prakṛtārthopasaṃhāram idānīṃ karoti |
tasmād avidvān bibhiyād yasmāt tasmāt pratigrahāt |
svalpakenāpy avidvān hi paṅke gaur iva sīdati || 4.191 ||
na kevalam abhato (?) 'śvādipratigrahād alpake nāpy avidvān iti ca | bruvann adhītavedaś ca viduṣaś ca tapasvinaś ca gurudravyapratigraham api tasya (?) pratiṣedhayati | vedārthaviñāne saty api ca |
na vāry api prayaccheta baiḍālavratike dvije |
na bakavratike pāpe nāvedavidi dharmavit || 4.192 ||
"baiḍālavratikān viprān vāṅmātreṇāpi nārcayet" (MDh 4.30) ity atra siddham apy etat punar ucyate yadarthaṃ tad vakṣyāmaḥ | na vāry apīty atrāpiśabdād vāriṇaḥ sarvārthatvaṃ darśayati | tathā coktam "tṛṇāni bhūmir udakam" (MDh 3.101) ity atra | evaṃ ca pratiprasiddhasya sarvārthasya vāriṇo dānapratiṣedhenānyadravyadānapratiṣedhaṃ karotinā vāriṇo 'py uktanyāyasāmarthyāt baiḍālavratikabakavratikaulakṣatotā (?) vakṣyati | "navedavidi" ity anenānadhigatavedavedārtham āha | nādhīte vedam anadhigatavedārtham | yenāviduṣo 'pi śrotriyasya dānam uktam | śāstreṇa cādhītavedasya sataḥ aśrutaśāstrāthasya | tābhyāṃ sāmyam ayuktam | śāstranyāyavirodhāt tadaśrutam [1 akṣ unclear] tvāsāmarthyāt yathokta evārtho grahītavyaḥ | dharmavic cātra pratiṣedhaśāstrajño netaro 'sya pratiṣedhasya vidveṣaṇārthavādo nivṛttyarthaḥ |
triṣv apy eteṣu dattaṃ hi vidhināpy arjitaṃ dhanam |
dātur bhavaty anarthāya paratrādātur eva ca || 4.193 ||
