na kevalaṃ ninditebhyo tān dharmeṇotpādayati tebhyo dharmam aparaṃ pratiṣiddhācaraṇāt karotīti evaṃ ca dhanavacanād atra baiḍāla (?) udvratikādibhyo na pūrvābhyanujñātasya pratiṣedhaḥ | pāṣaṇḍiślokoktaś ca viśeṣapratiṣedhanyāyaḥ | itarathā hy atra sarvapratiṣedhe pūrvoktanyāyoparodhād ity atha vā vihitapratiṣiddhānāṃ vikalpe sati niyamadānaṃ pūrvoktasya bhojanasya nāsty atithivad ity etad darśayati | anyas tv āha – tatroktaṃ baiḍālavratikādibhyo na deyam itīha tu baiḍālavratikādibhyaḥ pratigraho na kartavya ity evaṃpara ārambhaḥ | yata etad vidhis tayokto 'rthaḥ punar anūdyate | na punar (?) eṣaṇārthavāde hi pracchannapāpatā durvijñānāyatas tallakṣaṇavivakṣayedaṃ punar uktaṃ pūrvaśloke idam abhihitam | "vidhināpy arjitaṃ dhanam" ebhyo dīyamānaṃ nābhyudayāya, anarthāya bhavatīty atra dṛṣṭārthaṃ tadarthaṃ vibhāvamāna (?) [198]
yathā plavenaupalena nimajjaty udake taran |
tathā nimajjato 'dhastād ajñau dātṛpratīcchakau || 4.194 ||
kaḥ atra (?) upamānārthaḥ | yathā plava ātmanā nimajjati tadārūḍhaṃ dhārayitum aśaktaḥ nyāyāgatam ebhyā (?) dīyamānaṃ sa namannadho (?) gacchati dātāraṃ dhārayitum asamartham ity adhunā pracchannabhāvadoṣatayaiṣā durvijñānatvāt tadvibhāvam idaṃ lakṣaṇam ārabhyate parihārārtham |
dharmadhvajī sadā lubdhaś chādmiko lokaḍambhikaḥ (?) |
baiḍālavratiko jñeyo hiṃsraḥ sarvātisandhakaḥ|| 4.195 ||
tatkarmatadguṇayogād vā kenacid vṛttisāmānyenāyaṃ tathocyate nindārtham | apare tu dvitīyaślokaṃ tadartham eva paṭhanty asmin deśe:
yasya dharmadhvajo nityaṃ śakradhvaja ivocchritaḥ |
pracchannāni ca pāpāni baiḍālaṃ nāma tad vratam || iti (?) |
tad ayuktaṃ pūrvaślokenaivārthasyoktatvāt | punas tadarthena ślokena nārthaḥ | na ca samāmnāye 'pi śrūyata iti |
adhodṛṣṭir naiṣkṛtikaḥ svārthasādhanatatparaḥ |
śaṭho mithyāvinītaś ca bakavratacaro dvijaḥ || 4.196 ||
asadbrāhmaṇanindārtham asyopānā (?) yady api kiṃcid atra pūrvaślokena punar uktaṃ syāt tathāpi tad avivakṣitaṃ vijñeyam | yasmān na hy anena kiṃcid vidhīyate pratiṣidhyate vety evaṃ ca sati |
ye bakavratino viprā ye ca mārjāraliṅginaḥ |
te patanty andhatāmisre tena pāpena karmaṇā || 4.197 ||
parātisandhānaphalārthena pātaś caitad evam atas tatsamam anyad api sopadhaṃ karma parihārārthaṃ nidyate |
na dharmasyāpadeśena pāpaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ caret |
vratena pāpaṃ pracchādya kurvan strīśūdradambhanam || 4.198 ||
yasmāt
pretyeha cedṛśā viprā garhyante brahmavādibhiḥ |
chadmanācaritaṃ yac ca vrataṃ rakṣāṃsi gacchati || 4.199 ||
tasmān na dharmacaraṇāpadeśena prāyaścittaṃ caritavyam itīdaṃ cānyat tatsamam |
aliṅgī liṅgiveṣeṇa yo vṛttim upajīvati |
[199]        sa liṅgināṃ haraty enas tiryagyonau prajāyate || 4.200 ||
etad api tādṛg eva yatas tathaiva vyākhyeyam atra pratiṣedhābhāvādinārthavāde vipratiṣedha utprekṣyaḥ | sāhacaryād etayoḥ pūrvaṃ snānam uktam | "maitraṃ prasādhanaṃ snānam" (MDh 4.152) ity atra śloke yatas tatsādhananiyamārtham adhunā cārabhyate |
parakīyanipāneṣu snāyān naiva kadācana |
nipānakartuḥ snātvā hi duṣkṛtāṃśena lipyate || 4.201 ||
nipīyate (?) 'sminnāpāna iti nipānaṃ puṣkariṇyādi | tasmin parakīye na snāyāt kadācaneti kadāpīty arthaḥ | dṛṣṭārtham apy ātmano mṛjāyai (?) caṇḍālādispṛṣṭo na tacchuddaye kiṃ punar dharmāya yo nātra snātakas tatkartur doṣāṃśena lipayate | atrāṃśaśabdaś caturbhāgavacana uttaratra tṛtīyaśravaṇād iti kecit | tad ayuktam, arthavādatvād asya | evaṃ ca saty avivakṣitam atra caturthāṃśatvam | pratyavāyas tu śāstravyatikramakāriṇaḥ pratīyate | atra dṛṣṭāntaṃ vakti |
yānaśayyāsanāny asya kūpodyānagṛhāṇi ca |
adattāny upayuñjānas tṛtīyaṃ harate hi saḥ || 4.202 ||
yathā hi svabhogārthaṃ kḷptāni yānaśayyāsanādīny adattāny upayuñjānaḥ paraṃ pratyavāya evaṃ (?) parakīyanipānāny adattānīti vacanān nipāneṣu sarvārthatayotsṛṣṭeṣu ekadā(tad?) anujānāti snānam anutsṛṣṭeṣv api cānujñāteṣu tathāpi (?) śuryā(?)divat tathā ca paraśabdasāmarthyamātreṣv anutsṛṣṭeṣv api kalpitaṃ bhavaty evaṃ ca puṣkariṇīdānāni sarvatra lokaprasiddhāni yujyante | etasmin smṛtyarthe sati | itarathā py asāstralakṣaṇāny etāni syuḥ | na caitad iṣṭam | tasmād yathokta eva smṛtyarthaḥ | idaṃ tv adhunānyat tadviśeṣaṇārthaṃ snānasādhanam ucyate |
nadīṣu devakhāteṣu taṭākeṣu saraḥsu ca |
snānaṃ samācaren nityaṃ gartaprasravaṇeṣu ca || 4.203 ||
atra yadi nadya ubhayathā bhavanti tato 'yam eva (?) cātra śabdas tadviśeṣaṇatvād yuktas triliṅgo 'dhyetum itaratra ca sāmarthyād vijñāyate | liṅgam asyāśrutam api bhavatu (?) | naivaṃ tatastaśākatvādiviśeṣaṇatvād (?) ayaṃ talliṅga evādhyeyaḥ | (Ed. vākyayor anayoḥ samuccito 'bhiprāyo 'spaṣṭaḥ) | taṭākādīni cobhayathā prasiddhāny eva nityaśabdaś ca dharmasnānaviśeṣaṇārtho niyataḥ | naimittikadṛṣṭārthayos tv evam aniyamo vijñāyate | atha vā nityagrahaṇam ādarārthaṃ vijñeyam | dharmasnānasya prathamakalpāśrayaṃ kathaṃ tāvad yatitavyam | prathamaṃ nirupadhāntaḥ karaṇena yathā nadyādiṣu snānaṃ bhavaty adyacchatas (?) tv eva utsṛṣṭānujñātātmanipāneṣv api dharmasnānaṃ syāt | itarayos tu naimittikadṛṣṭārthayor eva [200] bhāvavikalpaniyamo nāsti | parakīyeṣu punas trayāṇām apy atyantapratiṣedhaḥ pūrvaślokasyārambhasāmarthyād vijñāyate | ayaṃ ca ślokatrayārthaḥ prapañcaḥ śāstrasāmarthyād vijñāyate, na pratijñāmātreṇa | katham | nadyādiṣu snānavacanāt | parakīyanipānapratiṣedhe sati punas tatpratiṣedhād vijñāyate | utsṛṣṭānujñāyate nipāneṣv asti snānatrayaniyamena nyāyena | itarathāpy anārambhaḥ pūrvasya syād dṛṣṭāntaślokasyet | uktam api caitat | pratiślokaṃ punar uktaṃ prakaraṇānte tadarthavaispaṣṭyāya mohāt | uktaḥ snātakasya yamaniyamāḥ | teṣāṃ gurulāghavapradarśanārtham adhunocyate |
yamān seveta satataṃ na nityaṃ niymān budhaḥ |
yamān pataty akurvāṇo niyamān kevalān yajet (bhajan?) || 4.204 ||
atra nivṛttyātmakā yamā ahiṃsādayaḥ | pravṛttyātmakās tu niyamās saṃdhyopāsanādayaḥ | evaṃ ca brahmahatyādau yamalope sati tattvān niyamaiḥ saṃdhyopānanādibhiḥ puruṣadharmair nādhikriyate karmabhiḥ śrautasmārtair niyamalope | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "dvijātikarmabhyo hāniḥ patanam" (GDh 21.4) ity āha | evaṃ ca yamabalīyastvād idam ucyate | tadanuṣṭānādarārthaṃ yamān seveta satataṃ niyamāl lāghavāc cedaṃ tadanuṣṭhānānām anityatvam eva | na nityaṃ niyamān budha iti | na tv idaṃ niyamānuṣṭhānapratyākhyānm ubhayopadeśasāmarthyād avikalpakaṃ (?) śāstram | tathā ca śiṣṭasmaraṇaṃ patati | niyamavān yameṣv asakto na tu yamapālananiyamālaso 'pi sīdatīti yamaniyamau samīkṣya buddhyā yamabahule 'dhvani sandadhīta buddhiṃ yeṣām api pāribhāṣikā yamaniyamāḥ |
ahiṃsā satyavacanaṃ brahmacaryam akalkatā |
asteyam iti pañcaite yamāś caiva vratāni ca ||
akrodho guruśuśrūṣā śaucam āhāralādhvam |
apramādaś ca niyamāḥ pañcaivopavratāni ca ||
teṣām apy ayam eva nyāyo (nānyo?) veditavya itīdānīm uktvā nāyadu(?) niyamānām anuṣṭhānasyāhāraśuddhimūlatvād idaṃ snātakasyāhāraśuddhaye prakaraṇam ārabhyate |
nāśrotriyatate yajñe grāmayājikṛte tathā |
striyā klībena ca hute bhuñjīta brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit || 4.205 ||
aśrotriyeṇānadhītavedena tate prārabdhe yajñe | kuta etat | sāmarthyāt | kalpyamātrādhyayanaprāpte ca saty ayaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | na bhuñjīta brāhmaṇaḥ kadācid ity abhyudayaśrāddhe py atra vā grāmayājena bahuyājena aśrotriyeṇāpy ārambhasāmarthyād vijñāyate | striyeti ca patnyā kumāryā ca | keṣāṃ keṣāṃcid dhoma ucyate | strīsāyam iti vacanāt (??) | pākayajñexu chandogānāṃ yatas tat pratiṣedhārtham [201] idam ucyate | striyā huta iti strīprādhānyena ca yatra yajñās trāyate yajamānena pratinidhiviṣayam evaitat strīgrahaṇam aśrotriyatvāt | evaṃ vā strīpratiniṣedhe siddhe tadgrahaṇam | dṛṣṭāntārthavādārtham | etena klībagrahaṇaṃ vyākhyātaṃ śrotriyatvāt klībasya pratiṣedhaḥ | aśrotriyatvādigrahaṇaṃ ca sarvadācārapradarśanārthaṃ vijñeyaṃ asau vākyaśeṣaḥ |
aśrīkam etat sādhūnāṃ yatra juhvaty amī haviḥ |
pratīpam etad devānāṃ tasmāt tat parivarjayet || 4.206 ||
aśrīkaṃ aśrīghram (aślāghjyam?) eteṣāṃ bhojanam | kena prakāreṇa | sādhūnāṃ śiṣṭānāṃ (?) matena | yatra hi juhvaty amī haviḥ | grāmayājakāḥ paryudastāḥ mantas taddhome | atra ye tva aślīlaṃ paṭhanti teṣāṃ jugupsitam aślīlam | tathā cocyate | "aślīlaṃ paruṣavāditā śiṣṭaiḥ śiṣṭānāṃ pratīpam etat pratīkūlam aśiṣṭam iti arthaḥ | kasya | devānām | arthavādaḥ pūrvasya (?) | "tasmāt tat parivarjayet" ity etad dhi vākyārambhaphalaprasaṅgāc ca dharmam evābhojyaprakaraṇe darśayati | tat caturvidhaṃ parigrahasvabhāvasaṃsargakālakrtam |
mattakruddhāturāṇāṃ ca na bhuñjīta kadācana |
keśakīṭāvasannaṃ ca pādaspṛṣṭaṃ ca kāmataḥ || 4.207 ||
mattakruddhāturānām ity atas tātparyaṃ gṛhyate | mattādayo vā yāvat | keśagrahaṇaṃ cātra romanakhamālādipradarśanārtham | kīṭagrahaṇaṃ tu tatsadṛśānāṃ kṣudrajantūnāṃ samācārād vijñeyam | kāmata iti caitat pādaspṛṣṭaviśeṣaṇam akāmato bhojanasyānujñānārtham |
bhrūṇaghnāvekṣitaṃ caiva saṃspṛṣṭaṃcāpy udakyayā |
patatriṇāvalīḍhaṃ ca śunā saṃspṛṣṭam eva ca || 4.208 ||
brahmaghno(bhrūṇagha?)grahaṇaṃ ca tatsamapradarśanārtham īkṣaṇapratiṣedhāc ca tatsparśanam apy aśucikaraṃ sutarām ity etad arthasiddham | udakyāgrahaṇaṃ ca sarvadāśucīnāṃ sūtikādyānāṃ rajasvalāsaṃspṛṣṭapratiṣedhāc ca taddṛṣṭasya pratiṣedhaḥ | patatrigrahaṇaṃ ca kākādyaśucipakṣigrāhakam sannidhānād vijñeyam |
gavā cānnam upaghrātaṃ ghuṣṭānnaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca viduṣā ca jugupsitam || 4.209 ||
viduṣā copaghātakahetusambandhajñena itareṇa vā prasiddhena |
stenagāyanayoś caiva takṣṇo vārdhuṣikasya ca |
dīkṣitasya kadaryasya baddhasya nigaḍasya ca || 4.210 ||
[202]
stenaḥ suvarṇād anyadravyāpahārakaḥ | pātakinas tūpariṣād vakṣyante | nigaḍo vārdhuṣikaḥ |
[abhiśastasya ṣaṇḍhasya puṃścalyā dāmbhikasya ca |
śuktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ caiva śūdrasyocchiṣṭam eva ca] || 4.211 ||
puṃścalī vyabhicāriṇī, na dāsī | dāsyāḥ pṛthak pratiṣedhāt "gaṇikānnaṃ ca" [MDh 4.209] iti | śuktaparyuṣitayoḥ prāyaścittārthaṃ punar iha grahaṇaṃ viśeṣārthaṃ vā | abhojyaprakaraṇoktayor api satoḥ pākaḥ śūdrabhuktaśiṣṭaḥ śūdrocchiṣṭaḥ | "ucchiṣṭam aguros tathā' ity apare paṭhanti |
cikitsakasya mṛgayoḥ krūrasyocchiṣṭabhojinaḥ |
ugrānnaṃ sūtikānnaṃ ca paryācāntam anirdaśam || 4.212 ||
a(ā)jīvasambandhau (?) cikitsakapratiṣedhaḥ | mṛgayo rājñas tasya pṛthagvacanād uttaratra | krūrasvabhāvatva ucchiṣṭabhojinaḥ śūdrasyevety arthaḥ | śāstrasāmarthyād ugro rājā | "ugro madhyaśarī vā" iti liṅgāt | tathā ca vakṣyati: "rājānaṃ teja ādatte" (MDh 4.218) ity arthavādaḥ | na cāsmin prakaraṇe śakyaṃ rājānaṃ nirdeṣṭum anyato 'smāṃ na śarī ca tat (?) | bhṛtya ity evaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ | "nagare nagare caivaṃ kuryāt sarvārthacintanam" [MDh 7.121] iti coktam | rājadharmeṣu kecid yajamānam āhur ugram | tad ayuktaṃ dīkṣitasyeti vacanāt | sūtakānnaṃ mātāpitro tsvāgau (?) ca pakṣe ca | mṛte patyau prasūtāyā strīdhanabhojanapratiṣedho vā | paryācāntam ācamanavyāpṛtam | anirdiśam gavādīnāṃ payaḥ |
anarcitaṃ vṛthāmāṃsam avīrāyāś ca yoṣitaḥ |
dviṣadannaṃ nagaryannaṃ (?) patitānnam avakṣutam || 4.213 ||
anarcitaṃ dāturaścada(nava?)dhānatayā śiṣṭabhojanīyam api tasyātithyādīnām arcanānarhāṇāṃ (?) na tamisrādīnāṃ (?) mitatvādibhir eva kāraṇair vṛthā māṃsaṃ ca yad ātmaprītyarthaṃ ca tarpitaṃ ca gṛhasthena tac cābhojyam ātithyādīnām evānarcitam api san na kalpayitur  anarcitābhojyasya sannidhāv asyopadeśāt | tathā cottaratra pradarśayiṣyati | "vṛthā kṛsarasaṃyāvam" (MDh 5.7) iti śloke anupākṛtamāṃsaśabdena vṛthāmāṃsapratiṣedhaṃ kalpayitur evaṃ cāpunaruktatvam uttaratra siddhaṃ bhavati, "prokṣitaṃ bhakṣayet [māṃsam]" (MDh 5.27) ity atra | avirayoṣid aputrā apatikā vā | nagarī vakṣyamāṇā | rājabhṛtyaḥ | vakṣyati hi "nagare nagare caikaṃ" (MDh 7.121] ityādi | ṛjv anyat |
[203]
piśunānṛtinoś cānnaṃ kratuvikrayakasya ca |
śailūṣatunnavāyānnaṃ kṛtaghnasyānnam eva ca || 4.214 ||
śailūṣo 'narthaprayogadarśanād bhārate: "śaulūṣa iva rājan" iti | evaṃ ca sati brāhmaṇādayo 'pi tatkarmāṇaḥ tathocyante guṇataḥ | bhāryāpaṇyam apare śailūṣam ācakṣate |
karmārasya niṣādasya raṅgāvatārakasya ca |
suvarṇakartur veṇasya śāstravikrayiṇas tathā || 4.214 ||
sarva ete tatkarmāṇas tacchabdair brāhmaṇādaya evaṃ santaḥ pratiṣidhyante, prāptapratiṣedhasāmarthyāt | pūrvatra raṅgoparakto 'pi śaulūṣakarmā tacchabdenoktaḥ | iha punar asau puruṣo 'pi raṅgāvatārakarmā nindyate yāyakaś ca | nartakaḥ pūrvam uktaḥ | iha punar nartakādir gṛhyate |
śvavatāṃ śauṇdikānāṃ ca cailanirṇejakasya ca |
rajakasya nṛśaṃsasya yasya copapatir gṛhe || 4.216 ||
kṛtavyākhyāna eṣa ślokaḥ |
mṛṣyanti ye copapatiṃ strījitānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ |
anirdaśaṃ ca pretānnam atuṣṭikaram eva ca || 4.217 ||
mṛṣyanti ye copapatim ity asya śaulūṣasya cāyam viśeṣaḥ | bhāryāpaṇyo hi lobhāt striyaṃ preṣayati | itaras tu vakṣyate 'tha vā śailūṣo naṭa ity uktaḥ | asmiṃś ca pakṣe punaruktadoṣo nāsti |
rājānnaṃ teja ādatte śūdrānnaṃ brahmavarcasam |
āyuḥ suvarṇakārānnaṃ yaśaś carmāvakartinaḥ || 4.218 ||
kārukānnaṃ prajā hanti balaṃ nirṇejakasya ca |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca lokebhyaḥ parikṛntati || 4.219 ||
pūyaṃ cikitsakasyānnaṃ puṃścalyāś cānnam indriyam |
viṣṭhā vārdhuṣikasyānnaṃ śastravikrayiṇo malam || 4.220 ||
ya ebhyo 'nye tv abhojyānnaḥ kramaśaḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
teṣāṃ tvagasthiromāṇi vadanty annaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ || 4.221 ||
dharmavyatikramāt tiryakṣutvam (?) | nānāphalārthavādo 'yaṃ nivṛttyarthaḥ | "rājānnaṃ teja ādatte" ity evamādi | kathaṃ nāmāyam "ninditaṃ na samācaret" iti [204] bhojyānnānām api caiṣām ebhir nimittar abhojyānnānām api caiṣām ebhir nimittair abhojyānnatocyate | evaṃ ca saty eṣāṃ nimittānāṃ kratuvikryādīnām akāryatocyate | apratiṣiddhānāṃ vinindayā | nanu dharmāvikrayāsambhavād ayam ukto vyatikramaḥ bhavatu vā dharmavikrayo mā vā bhūt, tatkrāntyāsambhavād amūrtitve sati sarvathaiva na kartavyam iti śāstrārthaḥ | yac cāsmin pradeśe sāmānyanirdeśaḥ gobalīvardanyāyena vyākhyeyaḥ |
bhuktvāto 'nyatamasyānnaṃ amatyā kṣapaṇaṃ tryaham |
upavased (?) ity arthaḥ | kṣapaṇam abhojanaṃ "bhuktvāto 'nyatamasyānnam" iti vacanāt | yady api parigrahāparigrahāt tūcy(?) upadeśo 'yam | tathāpi saṃsargāśuciprabhṛtīnām api grahaṇam asya nidarśanārthatvād vijñeyam | ekam ekaprakaraṇopadeśasāmarthyād eteṣāṃ tadviśiṣṭābhidhānāc ca |
matyā bhuktvācaret kṛcchraṃ
taptaṃ kṛcchraṃ na prājāpatyam | tathā ca gautamasmṛtimātrodāhariṣyāmaḥ |
retoviṇmūtram eva ca || 4.222 ||
ity atra tad amatipānena sambadhyate tenāvyavahitena matipānena smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt | evaṃ hi gautamaḥ: "surāyā amatyā pāne payoghṛtam udakaṃ vāyuṃ pratyahaṃ taptāni sakṛcchraḥ, tato 'sya saṃskāraḥ" (GDh 23.2) ity uktvā idam āha "mūtrapurīṣakuṇaparetasāṃ ca prāśane" etad evaṃ taptakṛcchrakaṃ saṃskāraṃ yato 'yam eva śāstrārthaḥ | manur api ca vakṣyati: "ajñānād vāruṇīṃ pītvā saṃskareṇaiva śudhyati" (MDh 11.147) | taptakṛcchrasahitenety ataḥ vyaktaṃ tat smṛtyantarasāmārthyād evaṃ vyaktīkaraṇīyam, na tu yathāśrutam | ubhayatrāsmin śāstre aprakaraṇopadiṣṭam api caitat prāyaścittam arthavādārtham iha pratyavagantavyam, pradhānato na svārtham | adhunā sādhoḥ śūdrasya gṛhiṇo bhojanapratiprasavārtham idam ārabhyate, sāmānyena śūdrapratiṣedhe sati |
nādyāc chūdrasya pakvānnaṃ vidvān aśrāddino dvijaḥ |
āmam evādadītāsmād avṛttāv ekarātrikam || 4.223 ||
śrāddhagrahaṇam atra satyavacanādīnāṃ sarvatra śūdradharmāṇāṃ pradarśanārthaṃ sāmarthyāt gamyate | yena nānyadharmaparivarjitasya śūdrasya śrāddham ekabhojyānnatāṃ kartum utsahate yatas tadgrahaṇaṃ sacchūdropalakṣaṇārtham evaṃ vijñeyam itarasmād asacchūdrād āgamam evādadīta na pakvānnam ekarātrikam eva ca tan na bāhyavṛtau ca na vidyamānasya balābalaparīkṣā prati kenādhunedaṃ (?) śraddāstutyartham āha –
[205]
śrotriyasya kadaryasya vadānyasya ca vārdhuṣeḥ |
mīmāṃsitobhayaṃ devāḥ samam annam akalpayan || 4.224 ||
tān prajāpatir āhaivaṃ mā kṛdhvaṃ viṣamaṃ samam |
śraddhāpūtaṃ vadānyasya hatam aśraddhayetaram || 4.225 ||
ubhayor api pratiṣiddhatvād anādyaṃ śraddāstutyarthatvān nāvadānyapratiprasavaḥ | atha tv arthavādaliṅgād veti (?) tadanumātraṃ śakyaṃ tato bhavati arthavādān na pratiprasavaḥ | yasmāc ca śraddhāvirahitam annaṃ śrotriyasyāpy aśucir bhavati tasmāt |
śraddhayeṣṭaṃ ca pūrtaṃ ca nityaṃ kuryāt prayatnataḥ |
śraddhākṛte hy akṣaye te bhavataḥ svāgatair dhanaiḥ || 4.226 ||
yataś caitad evam ataḥ |
dānadharmaṃ niṣeveta nityam aiṣṭikapaurtikam |
parituṣṭena bhāvena pātram āsādya śaktitaḥ || 4.227 ||
nityam ābhyudayikaṃ vā dānaṃ śraddhayā dātavyam tiy ārambhārthaṃ etat syāt ca śrāddhārthakalpanāyām |
yat kiṃcid api dātavyaṃ yācitenānasūyatā |
utpatsyate hi tat pātraṃ yat tārayati sarvataḥ || 4.228 ||
yācitenānapekṣya (?) pātratāṃ svalpam api dātavyam | utpatsyate hi tat pātram iti liṅgād apātradānam apy astīti strayati (?) dadāti sādhanadeyadravyasvarūpam idānīṃ darśayati śārthavādakam |
vāridas tṛptim āpnoti sukham akxayam annadaḥ |
tilapradaḥ prajām iṣṭāṃ dīpadaś cakṣur uttamam || 4.229 ||
bhūmido bhūmiṃ āpnoti dīrgham āyur hiraṇyadaḥ |
vṛhado 'gryāṇi veśmāni rūpado rūpam uttaamam || 4.230 ||
vāsadaś candrasālokyam aśvisālokyam āsvadaḥ |
anaḍuddaḥ śriyaṃ puṣṭāṃ godo bradhnasya viṣṭapam || 4.231 ||
yānaśayyāprado bhāryām aiśvaryam abhayapradaḥ |
[206]        dhānyadaḥ śāsvataṃ saukhyaṃ brahmado brahmasārṣṭitām || 4.232 ||
sarveṣām eva dānānāṃ brahmadānaṃ viśiṣyate |
vāryannagomahīvāsastilakāñcanasarpiṣām || 4.233 ||
pañcabhiḥ ślokaiḥ sarva ete phalārthavādāḥ | phalopadeśo vā anumeyatvāt svargasya nyāyaśāstravirodhino dṛṣṭavādadṛṣṭārthatā veditavyā | vāryādayaś ca sarvadeyadravyāṇāṃ śraddhāprakārārcitadānapradarśanārthāḥ | evaṃ coktebhyo 'nuktāni na parisaṃkhyāyante | tathā ca sati –
yena yena tu bhāvena yad yad dānaṃ prayacchati |
tat tat tenaiva bhāvena prāpnoti pratipūjitaḥ || 4.234 ||
śraddhāstutiprakaraṇe hīmam api dṛṣṭaṃ "dānadharmaṃ niṣeveta nityam aiṣṭikapaurtikam parituṣṭena bhāvena" (MDh 4.227) iti yato asya prabhāvaprasādasya śraddhākāryasya śrāddhākhyasya vā stutyartham idaṃ prakriyate | tad anuṣṭhāya dānakāle yena bhāvenety evamādibhāvaṃ śraddhāpūrvaṃ pratigahītṛpūjārthaḥ cittaprasādo na tu śraddhaiva vā pratigrahītṛpūjārthābhāvaḥ | tasyāś cādhamamadhyamottamatvāt tadanurūpaphalasambandheneyaṃ stutiḥ | apare tu sattvarajastamaḥsambandhenedaṃ sannayanti | evaṃ ca sati antaḥkaraṇasādhanaṃ karmapradhānakarmabāhyāder dravyopasarjanam ity uktaṃ bhavati | tathā ca saty ayaṃ vākyārthaḥ | parayā śraddhayā cittaṃ deyaṃ yena |
yo 'rcitaṃ pratigṛhṇāti dadāty arcitam eva ca |
tāv ubhau gacchataḥ svargaṃ narakaṃ tu viparyaye || 4.235 ||
arcitaṃ pratigṛhṇataḥ svargaḥ namanaḥ (?) yuktam, "ṣaṇṇāṃ tu karmaṇām asya trīṇi karmāṇi jīvikā" (MDh 10.76) iti vacanāt pratigrahasya dṛṣṭārthatvam | evaṃ ca "narakaṃ tu viparyaye" ity etad api nānyatra bruve 'rcitadānapraśaṃsārtho 'yam arthavādaḥ | katham evaṃ nāmedaṃ śraddhāmātraprasādopagṛhītam arcitadānaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ yenātra śakyam evaṃ vaktum arcitapratigrahītur api svargaḥ kiṃ punar dātur ity evam ādānasya pratiṣedhārtham idam – evaṃ ca vijñātuṃ śakyam | "narakaṃ tu viparyaye" iti yadā cāsvārthanirapeṅaṇapratigṛhītadātur  anugrahāyaiva tadadhyeṣaṇayā pratigrahaḥ kriyate | śrāddhabhojanād itaras tv ayaṃ tapasvīmāṃ prati pātrāviśeṣaṃ prāpya tad evaṃ yuktam agasthyādivad agṛhyamāṇakāraṇatvāt pratigrahasyātha vā saviśeṣāt pratigrahād yo niyamadharmad tadapekṣam atra stutivacanaṃ prakaraṇāc ca dānasaṃkīrtanapratiṣedhasaṃbandhenānyad api pratiṣedhyaṃ darśayati |
na vismayeta tapasā vaded iṣṭvā ca nānṛtam |
[207]        nārto 'py apavaded viprān na datvā parikīrtayet || 4.236 ||
vaded iṣṭvā ca jyotiṣṭomādibhir yajñair nānṛtam ity asya viśeṣārthaḥ punar ārambhaḥ | tathā ca prāyaścittānyatvasiddhir atra vijñeyā | snātakavratalopasya prāyaścittāsiddhiḥ | asya vyatikrame nindārthavādaḥ |
yajño 'nṛtena kṣarati tapaḥ kṣarati vismayāt |
āyur viprāpavādena dānaṃ tu parikīrtanāt || 4.237 ||
yataś caitad evam ataḥ pratiṣiddhaṃ na samācaret | dānaparikīrtinādiprakaraṇāc ca dānadharmaviśeṣaviṣayānyadbhaktiśraddadhānatayā snātakaḥ pratyahaṃ svalpam api yat kiṃcid arthibhyaḥ prayacchan –
dharmaṃ śanaiḥ saṃcinuyād valmīkam iva varmrikāḥ (?) |
paralokasahāyārthaṃ sarvalokān apīḍayan || 4.238 ||
kāraṇaṃ tv atra prasaṃkhyānaṃ vakti |
nāmutra hi sahāyārthaṃ pitā mātā ca tiṣṭhataḥ |
na putradāraṃ na jñātir dharmas tiṣṭhati kevalaḥ || 4.239 ||
atas tadapekṣayā dhanasaṃcayaṃ na hi (?) kuryat | kiṃ tarhi | dadyād eva śanaiḥ pratyahaṃ yat kiṃcit | yataś ca –
[Here verse order is changed]
mṛtaṃ śarīram utsṛjya kāṣṭhaloṣṭasamaṃ kṣitau |
vimukhā bāndhavā yānti dharmas tam anugacchati || 4.240 ||
tasmād dharmaṃ sahāyārthaṃ nityaṃ saṃcinuyāc chanaiḥ |
dharmeṇa hi sahāyena tamas tarati dustaram || 4.241 ||
yataś ca | ato na mātrādyarthaṃ –
ekaḥ prajāyate jantur eka eva pramīyate |
eko 'nubhuṅkte sukṛtam ekaś cāpnoti duṣkṛtam || 4.242 ||
ato na mātrādyarthaṃ dhanaṃ sthāpayed yenāmutrāsahāyās te |
[here repeats verse 240, 241]
yataś ca |
dharmapradhānaṃ puruṣaṃ tapasā hatakilbiṣam |
paralokaṃ nayaty āśu bhāsvantaṃ khaśarīriṇam || 4.243 ||
kaḥ | dharmaḥ | ataś ca – tam apy atra sahāyaṃ sarvāpatsu pratyahaṃ yat kilbi[1 akṣ unclear] dānenārthibhyaḥ dharmaḥ śanaiḥ saṃcinuyād ity atraiva sthitaṃ dharmasambandhaviśeṣaṇārtham adhunedam āha –
uttamair uttamair nityaṃ sambandhān ācaret saha |
ninīṣuḥ kulam utkarṣam adhamān adhamāṃs tyajet || 4.244 ||
na kevalaṃ viśiṣṭapratigrahītṛbhir eva puruṣaiḥ snātakasya sambandho 'pekṣitavyaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | sarvasambandhā yaunādayaḥ sarveṣām evaiteṣām utkarṣānimittatvād aviśeṣavacaneṣv eha (?) ekajātena caitad abhijanādiguṇasampadottamārthaṃ vijñeyam | tadabhāve tu samair api syān na tv āpady api hīnaiḥ | tathā ca satīdaṃ yuktam atra praśaṃsāvacanam |
uttamān uttamāṃś caiva gacched hīnāṃś ca varjayet |
brāhmaṇaḥ śreṣṭhatām eti pratyavāye tu śūdratām || 4.245 ||
evaṃ ca saṃsargāśritatayā –
dṛḍhakārī mṛdur dāntaḥ krūrācārair asaṃvasan |
ahiṃsro damadānābhyāṃ jayet svargaṃ tathā dvijaḥ || 4.246 ||
snātakasya saṃsargābhyāṃ dṛdhakāritvaṃ viśiṣṭaphalasambandham ucyate | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
karmaṇā manasā vācā yad abhīkṣṇaṃ viṣevate |
tasya nyāso (?) bharaty enaṃ tasmāt kalyāṇam ācaret ||
śiṣṭakaraṇaśīlo vā diṣṭakārīti pāṭhāntaravyākhyā | idānīṃ sadbhiḥ saha sarvasambandhopadeśād anāpady asatpratigraho na prāptaḥ iṣyate | yaḥ (?) syād ity ārambhaḥ |
[209]
edhokaṃ mūlaphalam annam abhyudyataṃ ca yat |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyān madhv athābhayadakṣiṇām || 4.247 ||
ajīvataḥ pratigraho yebhyo 'satkāribhyaḥ śūdraś (?) ceṣyate tebhya evam edhodakādīny anāpady apy abhyanujñāyānta anāpatprakaraṇopadeśasāmarthyād asya kṛtānnasya cābhyudyatasya niyamād edhodakāni yācitvā vipratigṛhṇīyād ity etad arthād āpadyate | anyathā hy annasambandho 'bhyuditaśabdaḥ prayojanābhāvād avaktavyaḥ syāt | daṇḍaœupikayā caidhodakādīnām ayācitopapannānām apraṇodaḥ (?) siddhaḥ | ayācitalābhasya laghutarasāmarthāt | atha vāyam abhyudyataśabdaḥ sarveṣām evaidhodakādīnāṃ viśeṣaṇamātraḥ syāt | tato 'yam ayācitalābhaḥ sarvadravyaviṣaya eva, nānnamātrasya | tathā ca gautamaḥ praśastaṃ dvijātibhyaḥ pratigraham uktvā "edhodakādīny aprāṇodyāni sarvebhyaḥ" (GDh 17.2–3) ity āha | asyārthavādaḥ |
āhṛṭābhyuditāṃ bhikṣāṃ purastād apracoditām |
grāhyāṃ prajāpatir mene api duṣkṛtakarmaṇaḥ || 4.248 ||
āhṛtā cāsau taṃ deśam ānītā yatra pratigrahītābhyuditā ca taddeyatvena kalpitā | āhṛtābhyuditātha vā hṛtā gavādiprāṇibhikṣāṃ mahārhābhyudyatāṃ hiraṇyādyaparāṇibhikṣāṃ evaṃ caitad dehavataḥ pūrvam apracoditāṃ svayaṃ tatprayuktena cānyena cānyena vidyānnā (?) liṅgena grāhyam | etām evaṃ viśiṣṭāṃ bhikṣāṃ prajāpatir mene (?) | prajāpatigrahaṇaṃ cāyācitalābhastutyartham | "api duṣkṛtakarmaṇaḥ" varṇāpaśadāt | kiṃ punaḥ praśastadvijātiśūdrebhyaḥ (?) apis tatra sambhāvanāyāṃ na samuccaye | asattvād dārūṇām apratigrāhyā śaṅkayeyam ayācitalābhastutiprarocanā yācyate | nirdoṣatayā ayācitalābhasyālpadoṣatayā na hy asmāt stutiḥ pravartata iti kṛtvā stutyāyam artho 'pi jñātuṃ śakyaḥ | ātmārthaḥ tāvad ayācitaṃ na niyamato grāhyakatayaivam uddiśyate | gurvādiprayojane tv etasmin niyamayiṣyate grahaṇasya | yena tatpratyākhyānam āha –
nāśnanti pitaras tasya daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca |
na ca havyaṃ vahaty agnir yas tām abhyavamanyate || 4.249 ||
atrāpi na hy akasmād agrahaṇanindā yuktety ato 'syāgrahaṇaniyamo vijñāyate | uktaś cātra niyamo gurubhṛtyātithidevanimittasyāyācitagrahaṇasya sadupayogena mahāprayojanatvād asādhanasya | itarathā hi tadagrahaṇe tasya dharmas tatsaṃgrahapratyavāyāya syāt | evaṃ ca satīdaṃ tadarthagrahaṇanindāvacanam [210] arthavad bhavatīty ātmasaṃyamaś cātra śrūyate | ity uktes tv ayaṃ tatra iti udūrdhvaś cāyam ātmā saṃnihitadravyopabhogāt | ato yathokta evārtho yuktaḥ | yataś caitad evam ataḥ –
śayyāṃ gṛhān kuśān gandhān apaḥ puṣpaṃ maṇīn dadhi |
dhānā matsyān payo māṃsaṃ śākaṃ caiva na nirṇudet || 4.250 ||
udakagrahaṇād atra sarvadravyopasaṃhāra evāyaṃ na vākhyāntaram idam anyathā hy edhodakādiślokapaṭhitasyodakasya punaḥ śayyādiṣu pāṭho 'narthakaḥ syāt | tathā ca gautamaḥ edhodakāni śayyādibhir ekasūtra eva paṭhati: "edhodakayavasamūlaphale"(?) (GDh 17.3) ityevamādi | payograhaṇāc ca gṛtādi praṇudyate | dhānāgrahaṇāc cānyāt kṛtānnaṃ matsyamāṃsayos tu nānāśrayatvāt pṛthaggrahanam | śākunikādibhyo māṃsaṃ kaivartikebhyo matsyāḥ | ślokavac chabdavṛttyā vā loke 'pi kiṃ māṃsam uta matyā iti pṛthaṅmatsyagrahaṇaṃ gobalīvardavac ca ayaṃ tu vākyabhedam āśrityedam āha | ubhayeṣām api tv edhodakādīnāṃ śayyādīnāṃ ca saty arthitve pratigrahaḥ | ataś cāyam apraṇodaḥ śayyādīnām ayācitapratigraha eva praṇodaśabdenābhidhīyate | na tv anena niyamenaiṣāṃ pratigraha ucyate | yena tad agṛhṇan pratyavayād evaṃ ca sati tasya dṛṣṭārthatve 'pi sati aśāsanīyatvaṃ tat tv etan (?) nyāyyam anyāyyaṃ veti vicāraṇīyam | yataś caitad evam ataḥ –
gurūn bhṛtyāṃś cojjihīrṣann arciṣyan devatātithīn |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyān na tu tṛpyet svayaṃ tataḥ || 4.251 ||
guravaḥ pitrādayaḥ putradāraprabhṛtayo bhṛtyāḥ tān kṣudyogapaṅkalagnān uddhartum icchann arciṣyaṃś ca devatāḥ tad apy asādhanayecchayā devatānāṃ ca kṣudyogāsambhavād atrāvyanā(?)śabdaḥ prayuktaḥ | yena guruvat kartā saṃbharaṇaṃ nāsty evam atithiṣv api vaktavyam | yata ete tatpūjāyai sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyād apaśastadvijātiśūdrbhyo 'pītīty uktam | na tv āpatkalpavihitebhyo 'tyantāsadbhiḥ prakaraṇabhedād gurvādiprayojanatvāc cāsyāvṛttikarśitasyāpatkalpopadeśaḥ | tathā ca vakṣyati – "avṛttikarśitaḥ sīdann imaṃ dharmaṃ samācaret" (MDh 10.101) iti | gurvādyarthas tu pratigrahācchādanavṛttenāvṛttikarśitasya | "na tu tṛpyet svayaṃ tataḥ" ity etat kṛtavyākhyānam |
guruṣu tv abhyatīteṣu vinā vā tair gṛhe vasan |
ātmano vṛttim anvicchan gṛhṇīyāt sādhutaḥ sadā || 4.252 ||
[211]
etasmāc ca gamyate nāyam āpatkalpapratigraho 'tyantāsadbhyo 'pi gurvādipūjāprayojana iti | karmaṇā śūdrasyābhojyānnatvam ity uktam, netarasya | "nādyāc chūdrasya pakvānnam" (MDh 4.223) ity atra śloke | iṣyate cānanyasyāpi bhojyānnatvam, yata idam ārabhyate aprāptaprāpaṇārtham –
ārdhikaḥ kulamitraṃ ca gopālo dāsanāpitau |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā yāś cātmānaṃ nivedayet || 4.253 ||
ārdhiko 'ṃśaharaḥ | kaś cāsau | pāraśavaḥ tatkulīnānāṃ snātakānām asau bhojyānnaḥ | apare tu kṛṣyādivyavahāreṣu saṃbhūyakāriṇam āhuḥ | pitṛpitāmahānuvṛttaṃ kulamitram | gopāladāsanāpitā ātmīyā eva | śāke tv aśrāddhino 'pi santaḥ śūdrā bhojyānnāḥ yataś cātmānaṃ nivedayed ebhyo 'nye 'pi tadātmanivedanaṃ darśayati –
yādṛśo 'sya bhaved ātmā yādṛśaṃ ca cikīrṣitam |
yathā copacared enaṃ tathātmānaṃ nivedayet || 4.254 ||
yādṛśo 'sya bhaved ātmā navavṛttādibhir yādṛśaṃ ca cikīrṣitam ātmano vā rakṣādiprayojanaṃ dṛṣṭārtham adṛṣṭārthaṃ vā brāhmaṇārirādhayiṣayā guṇapradhānabhāvena cobhayaṃ yathopacared enaṃ śilpakṛṣivāṇijyādibhir na dāsaḥ punar uktadoṣaprasaṅgāt | uktaṃ hi "gopālo dāsanāpitau" (MDh 4.253) iti | tathātmānaṃ nivṛttaṃ kṛtvā satyena nivedayen nānyathā yena –
yo 'nyathā santam atmānam dnyathā satsu bhāṣate |
sa pāpakṛttamo loke stena ātmāpahārakaḥ || 4.255 ||
arthavādaḥ | yasmāt –
vācy arthā niyatāḥ sarve vaṅmūlā vāgviniḥsṛtāḥ |
tāṃs tu yaḥ stenayed vācaṃ sa sarvasteyakṛt naraḥ || 4.256 ||
vāg indriyam uktam | arthaśabdo vāg ucyate | guṇatas tasyāṃ vāci tadabhidheyāḥ sarvārthā āśritāḥ yena vāṅmūlā vākpratītayaḥ śabdā na pratīyante | tatpratītidvāreṇa cārthasaṃvyavahārapravṛtter vāgviniḥsṛtāḥ | tadartha ucyate guṇataḥ sasyaparjanyavad evaṃ ca sati – "tāṃs tu yaḥ stenayed vacam" ātamnivedanārtham anṛtābhidhānena sarvasteyakṛd ucyate puruṣaḥ | so 'yam ātmani vedanārthāya vāco 'nṛtanivṛttyarthas tannindārthavādaḥ | evaṃ ca "yādṛśo 'sya bhaved ātmā" (MDh 4.254) ityevamādi sthitam | śūdrasya caitad ātmā nivedanārtham ucyamānaṃ brahmacaryādīnām api kāryasāmānyād vijñeyam | tad evaṃ samyag anuśiṣṭo gṛhasthaḥ |
[212]
maharṣipitṛdevānāṃ gatvānṛṇyaṃ yathāvidhi |
putre sarvaṃ samāsajya vasen mādhyasthyam āsritaḥ || 4.257 ||
ṛṣyādīnām adhyayanāt pretya pitṛkarmajyotiṣṭomadiyajñānuṣṭhānenānṛṇyaṃ gatvā kṛtakaraṇīyaḥ sa dvitīyam āyurbhāgaṃ paripakvavijñānatayā putrāsaktavyāpāro gṛhasthaḥ kaścit kālaṃ mādhyasthyam āśritya grāme eva vaset | āśrayam asamuccayapakṣye caitad evaṃ vijñeyam | na tv anyayoḥ pakṣayor iti | evaṃ ca paramavibhāgāpannaḥ san |
ekākī cintayen nityaṃ vivikte hitam ātmanaḥ |
ekākī cintayan nityaṃ paraṃ śreyo 'dhigacchati || 4.258 ||
tarhy asya brāhmaṇopadiṣṭavidhyanuṣṭhānaṃ śreyaḥ prāptyupāyabhūtam idam āha –
eṣoditā gṛhasthasya vṛttir viprasya śāsvatī |
snātakavratakalpaś ca sattvaśuddhikaraḥ paraḥ || 4.259 ||
vipragrahaṇāc ca vṛttivat snātakadharmo 'pi tasyaiva syād viśeṣeṇa vā tasya | na tathā kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ |
anena virp vṛttena vartayan vedaśāstravit |
vyapetakaluṣo nityaṃ brahmaloke mahīyate ||| 4.260 ||
anena vipro vṛtteneti snātakaprakaraṇopadiṣṭān yamaniyamān gṛhṇāti | vedaśāstravidgrahaṇena jñānakarmaṇī | etaṃ caitad arthād uktaṃ bhavati – ete jñānakarmaṇī eva sarmarthe bhavataḥ | yady ebhiḥ snātakadharmaḥ (?) sambadhyate nānyatheti stutivākyam etat snātakadharmānuṣṭhānasya paramātmavijñānānapekṣaṃ vaitat | katham | antareṇāpi karmāṇi paramātmajñānaṃ snātakakarmasahitaṃ brahmaprāptaye samarthaṃ tathā tasya śrutatvāt | evam api caitat stutiparatvam eṣām iti |
iti caturtho 'dhyāyaḥ \
manuśāstravivaraṇam
pañcamo 'dhyāyaḥ
śrutvaitān ṛṣayo dharmān snātakasya yathoditān |
idam ūcur māhātmānam analaprabhavaṃ bhṛgum || 5.1 ||
manuprabhavaḥ prathame 'dhyāye bhṛgur uktaḥ, "ahaṃ prajāḥ sisṛkṣus tu" (MDh 1.34) iti | atra śloke "analaprabhava" ity uktam | atra samādhiḥ – sarvadharmatattvaprakāśenājñānatamonivartanād analaprabhava ivāyam upacaryate | guṇataḥ sattvabāhulyād vā tatkāryakāraṇasya purāṇadṛṣṭyā vā tattvabhyasya (?) bhṛgor analaprabhavatvaṃ śrūyate varuṇayajñe (?) | adhunā yad uktam jñānavanto maharṣayas tad darśayati –
evaṃ yathoktaṃ viprāṇāṃ svadharmam anutiṣṭhatām |
kathaṃ mṛtyuḥ prabhavati vedaśāstravidāṃ prabho || 5.2 ||
kathaṃśabdaḥ kṣepeṇa mṛtyunā bhavitavyam | evam ācāratāṃ teṣāṃ yasmād atroktam "ācārāl labhate hy āyuḥ" (MDh 4.156) ityevamādīti | kathaṃ (?) punar ajarāmarā vedaśāstravido, yata evaṃ vakti | nety ucyate | dṛṣṭavirodhāc chāva (?) karmopadeśāt śāstravirodhāc ca | kiṃ tarhi | vedaśāstropadiṣṭād āyuṣaḥ prāg eva dharmamaraṇam ayuktam akālamṛtyusaṃjñakam | tathā ca ye mṛtyava ekaśatam itīdam atharvavedaliṅgam akālamṛtyūnām | evaṃ ca kathaṃ mṛtyuḥ prabhavatīti pūrṇāyuṣaḥ | evam āyurnimittasāmagrya iti |
sa tān uvāca dharmātmā maharṣīn mānavo bhṛguḥ |
śrūyatāṃ yena doṣeṇa mṛtyur viprāñ jighāṃsati || 5.3 ||
ādaropadeśārham asya prativacanaṃ manyamānaḥ praśnasya tān ṛṣīn abhimukhīkṛtyaivam ādareṇedaṃ pratijñāvacanam āha |
anabhyāsena vedānām ācārasya ca varjanāt |
ālasyād annadoṣāc mṛtyur viprān jighāṃsati || 5.4 ||
evaṃ yathoktaviprāṇāṃ svadharmam anutiṣṭhatām (MDh 5.2) iti praśnaḥ | "anabhāsena vedānām" ityevamādiprativacanaṃ tac ca nopapadyate, "svadharmam anutiṣṭhatām" iti pṛṣṭatvāt | tasya samādhir ayam (?) | pūrvokto vedābhyāsādipadārtho naikāntena (?) svadharmaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | ayam aparas taddharmaḥ (?) [214] taddoṣarihākhyo (?) vakṣyamāṇaḥ | tadgauravopadeśaparam idaṃ vākovākyaṃ praśnaprativacanākhyam | evaṃ ca kṛtvā pṛthakkaraṇam asya | bhakṣyasya laśunāder guruṇaḥ pūrvoktadravyājyās (?) tathā ca prāyaścittaviśeṣaśravaṇamātrāt –
chatrākaṃ vaḍvarāhaṃ ca laśunaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
palāṇḍuṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva matyā jagdhvā pated dvijaḥ ||
amatyaitāni ṣaḍ jagdhvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret |
yaticāndrāyaṇam vāpi śeṣeṣūpavased ahaḥ || iti | (MDh 5.19–20)
evaṃ ca sati yathā vedānabhyāsācāravarjanālasyāni pūrṇāyuṣo maraṇahetava evam (?) ayaṃ viśeṣeṇa doṣo vakṣyamānās tathā ca laśunādigurutvapradarśanaṃ tatparivarjanādarārthaṃ vijñeyam | so 'yam annadoṣo 'dhunocyate |
laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva palāṇḍuṃ kavakāni ca |
abhakṣyāṇi dvijātīnām amedhyaprabhavāṇi ca || 5.5 ||
kavaka–katyuḍuka (?) abhakṣyāṇi dvijātīnām iti | śūdraparyudāsārthaṃ dvijātigrahaṇam | upasaṃhāraṃ cāsya prakaraṇasyaivam eva kariṣyann ity etad uktam | dvijātīnāṃ bhakṣyābhakṣyam aśeṣata iti pūrvābhojyapratikaraṇe ca caurthādhyāyopadiṣṭe brāhmaṇagrahaṇam,
nāśrotriyatate yajñe grāmayājihuta tathā |
striyā klībena ca hute bhuñjīta brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit || [MDh 4.205]
iti vacanāt "dvijātimukhyavṛttīnām" [MDh 3.286] iti vacanāt brāhmaṇādhikāra eva | pūrvasminn abhojyānnaprakaraṇe viśeṣato vā brāhmaṇānām | iha tu dvijānām iti vacanād varṇatrayādhikāra evaṃ ca prakaraṇe bhedo yukta iti | amedhyaprabhavāni ca māṣādīni |
lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān vṛścanaprabhavāṃs tathā |
seluṃ gavyaṃ pīyūṣaṃ prayatnena vivarjayet || 5.6 ||
lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān sallakīprabhṛtīnāṃ (?) vṛścanaprabhavāṃś cālohitānām api kapitthādīnāṃ seluḥ śrīta(śṛta)kṣīrasya sānānikād pīyūṣasāhacaryād vijñāyate | gavyaṃ ca pīyūṣam anirdaśāyāḥ goḥ kṣīram iti vakṣyati | nirgatadaśāhāyā api pīyūṣapratiṣedhārtho 'yam ārambhaḥ | yadi kadācit sambhavaty asau kasyacid api pīyūṣam asya vā nirgatadaśāhasya tatra pratiṣedho vijñeyaḥ | anye tv āhuḥ seluḥ śleṣmāntakaḥ |
vṛthā kṛsarasaṃyāvaṃ pāyasāpūpam eva ca |
anupākṛtamāṃsāni devānnāni havīṃṣi ca || 5.7 ||
vṛthā yad anuddiśya devādīn svārthaṃ kṛsarādi kriyate | samācārāc ca kṛsarādīni modakādīnām api pradarśanārthāni sarvasyaiva ca pātakasya devādyarthasyābhojyatvāt prāyaścittaviśeṣārthaḥ | kṛsarādīnām upadeśaḥ ārambhasāmarthād vijñāyate | nanu cākṛtamāṃsaṃ vṛtā māṃsaṃ vijñeyaṃ kṛsarādivat | tathā ca samānatvād asya [215] kṛsarādibhiḥ mamekatropadeśo (?) yujyate | evaṃ tarhi vṛthāśabdasyādhikṛtatvād anupākṛtaśabdo 'tra māsaviśeṣaṇatvenāvaktavyo veti pādapūraṇārthas tasyopadeśo yukta iha padyagranthe | evaṃ ca sati nānenānupākṛtamāṃsagovyajanam (?) ucyate | yathā kecid āhus tathā yad vakṣyati "prokṣitaṃ bhakṣayed māṃsam" (MDh 5.27) ity anenāsya paunaruktyaṃ parihṛtaṃ bhavati | nanu vā na dvitvam "vṛthāmāṃsam" (MDh 4.213) ity uktatvād eva caturthādhyāyabhojyaprakaraṇe | ucyate – bhoktus tatropadeśaḥ prakaraṇasāmarthyād ity uktam, na kalpayituḥ | tathā cānena kāraṇenāsya dvirupadeśaḥ siddho bhavati | devānnāni nivedakāni | anye tv āhuḥ | yat prakṛtyaiva balavatpuroḍāśasadṛśaṃ (??) śraddhayā kriyate tac cābhojyaṃ saṃparigṛhād dhaviḥśeṣasya paryuṣitasyāpi bhakṣyatāṃ vakṣyati | ato havīṃṣy apramattadevatā bahir grāhyante 'tha vā havīṃṣyatve (haviṣṭve)? nopakḷptānām ayaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ |
anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣīram auṣṭram aikaśaphaṃ tathā |
āvikaṃ saṃdhinīkṣīraṃ vivatsāyāś ca ghoḥ payaḥ || 5.8 ||
uṣṭraikaśabdāvikānāṃ vacaso nityapratiṣedhaḥ prāyaścittasmṛtyantarasamācārād vijñeyaḥ | saṃdhinīkṣīraṃ yā sāyam adugdhā prātas tad ucyate | garbhiṇī yā saṃdhinī | ye (?) garbhasambandhād dhetoḥ (?) saṃdhinīkṣīraṃ paṭhanti teṣām atiśayo vivakṣitaḥ | yady ayam asti pāṭhaḥ savatsāyā api (?) | vivatsāyāś ca vinākṛtavatsāyā (?) mṛtavatsāyā vā | gograhaṇāc cājāmahiṣyor apratiṣedhaḥ |
āraṇyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ māhiṣaṃ vinā |
strīkṣīraṃ caiva varjyāni sarvaśuktāni caiva hi || 5.9 ||
[Ed. atra pūrvārdhe "sarveṣāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ mahiṣam" ity atra "sarvāsāṃ gṛgīṇāṃ mahiṣīr" iti vyākhyoddhṛtaṃ pāṭhāntaram]
pulliṅganirdeśe 'pi ca sati mṛgamahiṣaśabdayoḥ payaḥpānapratiṣedhe prakaraṇāt strīviṣayatvaṃ vijñeyam | jñātinirdeśo vā liṅgasaṃkhyayor avivakṣā | pāṭhāntare cāsmiṃś codyānavakāśa eva, "āraṇyānāṃ ca sarvāsāṃ mṛgīṇāṃ mahiṣīr vinā" iti | "strīkṣiraṃ caiva varjyāni" ity atra evaśabdāt sārvatriko 'sya pratiṣedhaḥ | ajñānādiṣv apīti sarvaśuktāni cety ayam upa(t?)sargo 'syāpavādaḥ |
dadhi bhakṣyaṃ ca śukteṣu sarvaṃ ca dadhisaṃbhavam |
yāni caivābhiṣūyante puṣpamūlaphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 5.10 ||
[216]
atra śuktapratiṣedhe piṣṭāktaśuktapratiṣedhaḥ | puṣpamūlaphalaiḥ śubhair iti | tathājātīyasya pratiprasavād ato yāni svabhāvataḥ śuktāni puṣpamūlaphalāni tāni na pratiṣidhyante | prāyeṇa ca puṣpaphalair abhiṣecanādikāya puṣpādigrahaṇaṃ vijñeyam | na tv anenekṣuguḍādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | evaṃ ca saty ācāravirodhī smṛtyarthaḥ kalpayitavyaḥ | śubhānāṃ cābhyanujñānād aśubhādiṣu śīdhuśubhasa (?) pratiṣedhaḥ |
kravyādaḥ śakunīn sarvān tathā grāmanivāsinaḥ |
anirdiṣṭāṃś caikaśaphāṃs ṭiṭṭibhāṃś ca vivarjayet || 5.11 ||
kravyādo māṃsabhakṣakān eva gṛhṇāti (?), naivaṃ cānyeṣām ubhabhakṣaṇādīnāṃ mayūrādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | tathā grāmanivāsinaḥ akravyādo 'pi pārāvatādīn anirdiṣṭāṃś caikaśaphān iti śāstrāntare ekaśaphā (?) eva pratiṣidhyante | astrādayo (?) nirdiśyante ca | ekaśaphaśrutau bhakṣyatvena tv āśu baḍavām ālabheta gauramahat (?) gardabhaṃ prajākāmatas tasya māṃsam aśnīyād ity ādhvaryavaśrutis tatsiddhatvād evāsyānārambha iti cen na | taddarśanena pravṛttiprasaṅgāt pratiṣedhaḥ | ye tvacān āmnāya itivat ṭiṭṭibhaṃ caiva varjayec chuci bhakṣyam api santaḥ –
kalaviṅkaṃ plavaṃ haṃsaṃ cakrāhvaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
sārasaṃ rajjudālaṃ ca dātyūhaṃ śukasārike || 5.12 ||
kalaviṅko grāmacaṭako nigameṣūktaḥ | grāmaikasāṃ (?) pratiṣedhe kalaviṅkasya ubhayanivāsāḥ pratiṣidhyante | te hi varṣāsu vanavāsino bhavanti | grāmakukkuṭasyāraṇyapratiprasavārthaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ | yady api cānayor vivasthito nivāsaḥ | tathāpy ekajātitvāt saṃdihyate | bhakṣya ekārohiṇa iti ced bhavādīnām (?) api deśāntarādiṣv ākāro bhidyate | ato 'syā jāter ubhayatra nivāsāt kalaviṅkavad eva yuktam | rajjudālo 'rajjudālo vā | śakunis tanmadhyapāṭhāc chākunikebhya upalabdhavyaḥ | anye tu rajjudālaṃ śleṣmātakam āmananti | tasya ca prāṇimadhye grahaṇan tacchedasthimatprāyaścittasāmyaṃ tad ayuktaṃ ca kiṃ madhyopadeśāt | uktaṃ ca śleṣmātakaseluśabdena "seluṃ gavyaṃ ca pīyūṣam" (MDh 5.6) ity atra deśāntaraprayogadarśanāt | prasiddham anyat |
pratudān jālapādāṃś ca koyaṣṭinakhaviṣkirān |
nimajjataś ca matsyādān saunaṃ vallūram eva ca || 5.13 ||
pratudya ye bhakṣayanti te pratudāḥ | jālapādagrahaṇāc ca plavabhāsacakrapādānāṃ siddhe pratiṣedhe nityārthaṃ tadgrahaṇam | evaṃ ca (pratudāṃ?) jālapādānāṃ vikalpaḥ pratiṣedhasyānumīyate | [217] koyaṣṭiḥ śākanikebhya upalabdhavyaḥ | [viṣkiro] nakhair viṣkīrya bhakṣaṇāt | na ca viṣkirāḥ kukkuṭādayaḥ | evaṃ ca grāmakukkuṭapratiṣedhād āraṇyasyābhyanujñā | mayūralāvakādayas tarhi kahaṃ bhakṣyante (?) āpastambamatena kukkuṭo na ca viṣkirāṇām iti | evaṃ ca mayūrādīnām abhyanujñā | kukkuṭasyāraṇyasyāpratiṣedhaḥ | tulyabalavirodhe vikalpa āśrayaṇīyaḥ | saunaṃ vallūraṃ bhakṣāṇām api |
bakaṃ caiva balākāṃ ca kākolaṃ khañjarīṭakam |
matsyādān viḍvarāhāṃś ca matsyān eva ca sarvaśaḥ || 5.14 ||
bakabalākakākolanimajjamatsyādānāṃ pratiṣedhe siddhe matsyāgrahaṇāt tadanyeṣāṃ vikalpaḥ pratiṣedhasyānumīyate | matsyādā apakṣiṇo 'pi | matsyādagrahaṇenābhakṣyā vijñeyā menakādattaḥ (maukalyādayaḥ?) kriyānimittatvāt matsyādaśabdasya | kākolaśyenaḥ deśāntarasiddhaḥ | ayaṃ sābdhīkeṣv evam ucyata iti la(?) siddhaṃ viḍvarahapratiṣedhāc cāraṇyabhavābhyanujñā (?) | grāmabhavādipratiṣedhaś (?) ca pūrvatra prakaraṇāc chakuniviśeṣārtho vijñeyaḥ | evaṃ ceha viḍvarāhagrahaṇam arthavad bhavati | "matsyān eva ca sarvaśaḥ" ity ayam utsargaḥ sāmānyaviṣayaḥ matsyanindārthavādaḥ |
yo yasya māṃsam aśnāti sa tanmāṃśada ucyate |
matsyādaḥ sarvamāṃsādas tasmān matsyān vivarjayet || 5.15 ||
yathā puruṣādaḥ sarpādaḥ vṛṣadaṃśa evam ihāpīti | adhunā tajjātiviśeṣāśrayeṇa pratiṣiddhasya māṃsasya bhakṣaṇaṃ pratiprasūyate |
pāṭhīnarohitāv ādyau niyuktau havyakavyayoḥ |
rājīvāḥ siṃhatuṇḍāś ca saśalkāś caiva sarvaśaḥ || 5.16 ||
pāṭhīnarohitau havyakavyayor anenānujñātau | rājīvāḥ siṃhatuṇāś ca | cakārakaraṇena samuccyayārthena niyogata eveti vijñāyate | saśalkās tu sarvaśa eveti (?) vacanād aniyoget 'pi ca rohitagrahaṇena ca saśalkānāṃ vikalpena bhakṣaṇam anumīyate |
na bhakṣayed ekacarān ajñātāṃś ca mṛgadvijān |
bhakṣyeṣv api samuddiṣṭān sarvān pañcanakhāṃs tathā || 5.17 ||
ekacarāḥ sarpādayaḥ ajñātāś ca tajjātīnām ato mṛgadvijāḥ śiṣṭair anyair vā vāhagocaraiḥ (?) bhakṣyeṣv apratiṣiddheṣv apīty arthaḥ | samuddiṣṭā iva samuddiṣṭāḥ | pratiṣedhabhakṣaṇād aśanacihnena parihartavyatayāvijñātā saṃdigdhā tathā pañcanakhāś cety evaṃ pañcanakhe cāpi bhakṣaṇapratiprasavārtham idam āha –
[218]
śvāvidhaṃ śalyakaṃ godhāṃ khaḍgakūrmaśaśāṃs tathā |
bhakṣyān pañcanakheṣv āhur anuṣṭrāṃś caikatodataḥ || 5.18 ||
go'vyajamṛgāṃs tatra niyamaṃ vakṣyati śāstradvayena | go'vyajasya mṛgāṇāṃ ca adhunāprasiddhaparivarjanārtham aprakaraṇatve 'pi prāyaścittam idam ucyate | arthavāyāha –
[Here Ed. inserts after go'vyajasya: goḥ aveḥ ajasya cety arthaḥ pratīyate | samastapadasyāsya prayoge prāya ekavacanaṃ prayuktaṃ bhārucinā | 27tamasya ślokasya vyākhyāyām ekatra bahuvacanaprayogo 'pi dṛṣṭaḥ | samāhāradvandva iti parigaṇayya ekavacanaprayogaḥ nirvāhyo vety atra doṣajñāḥ pramāṇam]
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca laśunaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
palāṇḍuṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva matyā jagdhvā pated dvijaḥ || 5.19 ||
amatyaitāni ṣaḍ jagdhvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret |
yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ vāpi śeṣeṣūpavased ahaḥ || 5.20 ||
matipūrvabhakṣaṇaṃ pātakaṃ surāpānaprāyaścittaṃ tat sāmānyād vakṣyati – "garhitānnādayor jagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ" (MDh 11.57) iti | bhojyānnaṃ śūdrād bhuñjataś ca brāhmaṇasyāvijñātasaṃsargadoṣaśuddhyartham idam āha –
saṃvatsarasyaikam api caret kṛcchraṃ dvijottamaḥ |
ajñātajagdhaśuddhyarthaṃ jñātasya tu viśeṣataḥ || 5.21 ||
sarvābhojyasya parigrahasvabhāvasaṃsargakālapariṇāmas tasmāc chreyaskāmenātmanaḥ | abhakṣyabhojyapeyāni prayatnataḥ pariharaṇīyāni | hiṃsā śāstre lakṣaṇā | yasmād ayam āha –
yajñārthaṃ brāhmaṇair vadhyāḥ praśastā mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ |
bhṛtyānāṃ caiva vṛttyartham agastyo hy ācarat tathā (purā?) || 5.22 ||
śruticoditā mṛgapakṣiṇo yajñe vadhyā iti | śrutisiddham apīdāhṛtya [anīmāhṛtya?] haraṇasyaitena sāmyopadeśanārtham idam ucyate | evaṃ ca dṛṣṭānto 'yaṃ yathā yajñārtham ete vadhyās tathā bhṛtyāharaṇārtham apīty atra kāraṇopacāram āhāgastyo hy ācarat tathā prokṣaṇam antareṇāgastyas tān upakalpya yathā bhṛtyebhyo daivam anyat syād dhyācāravato na doṣaḥ syāt | brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ cātra pradarśaṇārtham | tathā yudhiṣṭhirādayaḥ (?) atithyādipūjāyai dhanāyāṃ pratipedire | evaṃ ca sati yudhiṣṭhirādidarśanena svārtham apīdam "yajñārthaṃ brāhmaṇair vadhyāḥ praśastā mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ" iti graṣucanna(?) bhṛtyabharaṇadṛṣṭāntāyaiva | tathā ca brāhmaṇam – "tathaivādo manuṣyasya rājaka āgate 'yasminnyā (?) abhatyukṣāṇi [219] (ahastyṛkṣāṇi?)taṃ vā vehataṃ vā kṣadantam evam evāsmā etat kṣadante yad agniṃ mathanyāgnivid devānāṃ paśu" ityāṣaṭtriśatsaṃvatsare mṛgapakṣivadho 'tra dyatigyeṣṭibrāhmaṇam idaṃ bahvṛcāṃ draṣṭavyam | bhavati ca tanmāse na devatāyāgaḥ śrutidarśanād ity etad āha –
babhūvur hi puroḍāśā bhakṣyāṇāṃ mṛgapakṣiṇām |
purāṇeṣv ṛṣiyajñeṣu brahmakṣatrasaveṣu ca || 5.23 ||
ṣaṭtriśasaṃvatsare mṛgapakṣivadho 'nūdyate 'nayā smṛtyoktā | tatraikeṣāṃ mṛgayā jīvitātyaye ca bhṛtyānāṃ bharaṇārtha, smṛtyavirodhāt |
yat kiṃcit snehasaṃyuktaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhojyam agarhitam |
tat paryuṣitam apy ādyaṃ haviḥśeṣaṃ ca yad bhavet || 5.24 ||
paryuṣitasya bhakṣyabhojyasya pratiṣiddhatvād ayaṃ pratiprasavo ghṛtādisnehasaṃbandhe tasya kriyate | agarhitaṃ ca yad anyenopaghātasaṃbandhena bhavati haviḥśeṣaṃ cāsnehasaṃyuktam apy ārambhasāmarthād vijñāyate |
cirasthitam api tv ādyam asnehāktaṃ dvijātibhiḥ |
yavagodhūmajaṃ sarvaṃ payasaś caiva vikriyā || 5.25 ||
ciraṃ paryuṣitānnam api yavagodhūmayor vikārāṇām asnehāktānām ayaṃ bhakṣaṇapratiprasavaḥ | pūrveṇāprāptatvād ārabhyate |
etad uktaṃ dvijātīnāṃ bhakṣyābhhakṣyam aśeṣataḥ |
māṃsasyātaḥ pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ bhakṣaṇavarjane || 5.26 ||
pūrvoktaprakaraṇayor ayam upasaṃhāropanyāsārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
prokṣitaṃ bhakṣayen māṃsaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca kāmyayā |
yathāvidhi niyuktaś ca prāṇānām eva cātyaye || 5.27 ||
tatra prokṣitaṃ nāma śrutikarmāṅgatvāt go'vyajānāṃ prokṣaṇasaṃskāravatā yan māṃsam avadānahomaśiṣṭam | puroḍāśaṃ ca tāvat prokṣitaṃ paśuprabhavam | prokṣitaśabdo guṇavṛttyocyate | tad bhakṣayet | na cāyaṃ vidhir arthaprāptatvāt bhakṣaṇasya | na ca tadbhakṣaṇe pratyavāyaḥ śrūyate | yathottara tv avihitārthātikrame | ata eva niyamārthatvam asya  neṣyate | yathottaratra pariśeṣād anyopāyabhakṣaṇapratiṣedhārthe 'yaṃ parisaṃkhyā | katham | yathā gavādīnām apratiṣiddhānāṃ māṃsasya bhakṣaṇam arthād evaṃ go'vyajasyāpi prāptaḥ san pratiṣidhyate 'nena | na ca gavādimāṃsavat [220] kayādinopāyenopādāyātithyādibhuktaśiṣṭaṃ bhakṣayet | chāgādīnāṃ māṃsaṃ kathaṃ punaḥ prokṣitaśabdena (?) go'vyajasyaiva grahaṇaṃ nābhyeti yena go'vyajānām eva prokṣaṇārhaṃ yajñaśāstra upadiṣṭam | nātiśayād idaṃ kathaṃ tarhi | "ṣaṇmāsāc chāgamāṃsena" (MDh 3.269) ityevamādi prokṣitāpekṣam eva tat syād vacanam eva nādvedo (?) keṣā (?) | nāsty atra madhuparkaḥ | kiṃ tarhi pākṣike govadhe tanmāṃsabhakṣaṇam aprokṣitatvān na prāpnoti | prāpnoty eva, yena tasmāt kālāpasyedaṃ naṃaproṇam (pramāṇam?) | evaṃ tv asati, tadbhakṣaṇaṃ yuktam | atha votsargapakṣe gaur asya viṣayaḥ | "na tvo vāmāṅghra syāt" ity etasmād vijātaye vacanād vā doṣo na syād ity uktaṃ yathā meṣacchāgamāṃsayoḥ śrāddha iti | kecit tv avaśabhakṣaṇāniyamam idaṃ kalpayanti yathottaratra | tat punar yuktam ayuktaṃ veti vicāraṇīyam | niyamabhakṣaṇārthavādāsaṃbhavād yathokatvāt | na ca māṃsābhakṣaṇavidhānam atra yuktam | yat tu vedaśāstrapramāṇatvāt tasya karmāṅgabhakṣaṇasya vidhānepi (?) tadanuvādam āhur uttaravidhistutyartham, teṣām ahutaśiṣṭasyāhutaśiṣṭasya ca (?) chāgādīnāṃ māṃsasya bhakṣaṇam asaty asmin parisaṃkhyāśāstre prāptaṃ bhavatu, evaṃ ko doṣaḥ | nanv ayaṃ vakṣyamāṇaḥ "asaṃskṛtān paśūn mantrair nādyād vipraḥ kathaṃcana" (MDh 5.36) iti, evaṃ tarhy asmād eva śāstrād asya go'vyajabhakṣaṇapratiṣedhasiddhiḥ iti | idaṃ na vaktavyam abhidhānena cāsya paunaruktyam āpadyate | tatredam ucyate | vidheḥ pratiṣedhasya vā śeṣaprarocanārtho vidveṣaṇārtho vārthavādo yukto 'bhidhātuṃ yathoktaṃ "niyuktas tu yathānyāyam" (MDh 5.35) ityādi | yat kāraṇam eva hi puruṣārthāyopadeśavākyād dviprakāram api samarthaṃ bhavati, pravṛttau nivṛttau vā tatsvābhāvyād vā | yathā hi sarvārthavādānām ānarthakyād abhāva eva prasajyeta | dṛṣṭaś ca vihitārthānuṣṭhānābhimukhyajanakaś cārthaḥ kalpyeta | tathāpi tanmāṃsaṃ na bhakṣayet | yasmād āha |
asaṃskṛtān paśūn mantrair nādyād vipraḥ kadācana |
mantrais tu saṃskṛtān adyāc chāśvataṃ vidhim āsthitaḥ || [MDh 5.36]
"madhuparke ca" (MDh 5.41) ity evam ayaṃ stutiparo 'rthavādo vijñeyaḥ vihitārthaveśeṣakaḥ | evaṃ ca saty anuvādakṛtaḥ vṛthāmāṃsaśabdo yady ekārtho yadi bhinnārthaḥ tathāpi tadarthaviśeṣapratītir nāntareṇa prokṣitabhakṣitaśāstrād eva adyaṃ ca | na śāstraṃ ca yenātas taccheṣatayedaṃ śāstradvayam abhidhānārham | anyathā hi tadabhāvaḥ | apratiṣiddhamāṃsānāṃ go'vyajādīnām asya niyamakāriṇaḥ śāstrasyābhāvān niyamena tanmāṃsabhakṣaṇam āpadyeta na ca dahyasa(?) gantuṃ śakyaṃ śeṣapratipādana iti | "brāhmaṇānāṃ ca kāmyayā" bhakṣayed iti vartate | kaḥ punar asau | madhuparkātithir arghyābhiḥ goḥ paśor vadhapakṣe utsargapakṣe ca na tv eva māso 'rghaḥ (?)syād ity anena nyāyenādyād api goḥ paśor  niyamato bhakṣayed brāhmaṇakāmyayānimittena dātṛprārthanayā śāstralakṣaṇasya (?) dadātes tādarthyānugrahāya bhakṣayed gṛhyasmṛtyā manuśāstrasmṛtyā [221] vānayā | katham | yena hy apratiṣiddham arthaprāptam utsṛṣṭamāṃsāśanaṃ "nivṛttis tu mahāphalā" (MDh 5.56) ity anena śāstreṇa tasyehādhikāraḥ | aniṣṭapaśor vāhitāgneḥ pratiṣiddhamāṃsāśanasya sāmarthyād grāmyateyo(?) 'bhyanutsṛṣṭapratiṣiddhamāṃsāśano 'tithir arthaprāptamāṃsabhakṣaḥ taṃ praty anartham idaṃ niyogaśāstraṃ bhavati | tathā cārthavad idaṃ bhavati tathā yuktaṃ kalpayitum | evaṃ cārthavad yathāvocāma | parisaṃkhaiṣā na pūrvavat | kiṃ tarhi | niyamaḥ | tathā cāvaśyabhakṣaṇacodite tatparivarjanādyatikramapratyavāyaḥ yathāvidhi niyogavat | tathā ca vakṣyati: "madhuparke ca yajñe ca" (MDh 5.41) ityevamādi, vaiśeṣikam arthavādaṃ sāmānyaṃ ca "niyuktas tu yathānyāyam" (MDh 5.35) iti | evaṃ ca sati nedaṃ bhakṣayata upāyāntaravidhānam upadeśasāmarthyād ity uktam | na ca nivṭttapratiṣiddhamāṃsāśanayor brāhmaṇakāmyayā (?) nimittena māṃsaṃ bhakṣayataḥ pratyavāyotpattiḥ, parārthatvād bhakṣaṇasya | ihoktaśāstranigṛhītātmanā dātranugrahārtham asyā svārthaṃ yenāto na dharmavyatikramo 'yaṃ māṃse dhā(?) bhakṣaṇavat brahmacāry ekannabhojanavac cāvirodhaḥ | yathā ca brahmacāriṇa ekānnamāṃsi kārthabhojanapratiṣedhe sati "kāmam abhyarthito 'śnīyād vratam asya na lupyate" (MDh 2.189) ity etasmāc chāstropadeśasāmarthyād abhyarthitasyaikānnāśanā māsikāśanaṃ ca na vratavirodhe vartate evam ihāpīti | viṣama upanyāsaḥ | yatkāraṇaṃ brahmacāriṇo hi yuktaṃ praveśa ity upadeśād vratāviruddhamāṃsavarjitaṃ bhojanam | iha tu nivṛttapratiṣiddhamāṃsāśanayoḥ śāstracayena brāhmaṇakāmyayā māṃsaṃ bhakṣayato nivṛttiśāstravyatikramadoṣaḥ | atrocyate | na hy ataḥ māṃsāśanamudā haraṇīyam |kiṃ tarhi | ekānna(ha?)bhojanaṃ māsikārthaṃ bhojanaṃ caivaṃ kṛtvā yathā dātur brāhmaṇaprārthanayā viśiṣṭaviṣaye bhojane praiprasavaṃ śāstraṃ sāmarthyād avirodhaḥ evam ihāpīti | tasmād upapannam etad evaṃ bhakṣayator na māṃsaśāstravyatikramadoṣaḥ | apare tv ācakṣate – yathā dātur brāhmaṇakāmyayātithir aśnīyad iti niyamas tathātithir brāhmaṇakāmyayā dātur ayaṃ niyamabhakṣaṇopadeśa iti | anye yajñavivāhādiṣu apare darśanamaitryām | bhojanagoṣṭhyām ity anye | sarva evaite niyamaparyāya iti kecit | tad etāni vyākhyānāni brāhmaṇakāmyaśabdasya yuktarūpāṇīti vicāraṇīyam | yathāvidhi yuktaś ca daive pitrye vā śrāddhe yathopadiṣṭo brāhmaṇo 'śnīyān niyamataḥ sāmarthyād ity uktam | nyāyyam etat prāṇānām evātyaye viprakāre tannimitte vyādhinimitte cāntareṇāpi pūrvopadiṣṭanimittadvayam anyat samānam etat syāc cāvasthāyām anena śāstreṇaivam adhikṛtamāṃsabhakṣaṇasya tadabhakṣayataḥ pratyavāyasya jīvino 'pekṣaṇe | tathā ca manuṣyajīvitaprayatnopadeśa evaṃ śrūyate | "kurvann eveha karmāṇi jijīviṣec chataṃ samāḥ" (ĪśUp 2) | evaṃ ca sati asyām avasthāyāṃ bhakṣayann ātmahā sampadyate | ātmavadhaś ca śāstraviruddhaḥ | tathā ca śāstram "idaṃ tam u ha vai na punar āyuṣaḥ smarakāmapreyād alokikyaṃ hi tad bhavati" iti śruteḥ | evaṃ ca manto(?) [222] varaḥ
"asūryā nāma te lokāḥ andhena tamasāvṛtāḥ |
tāṃs te pretyābhigacchanti ye kecātmahano janāḥ || [IśUp 3]
gautamādayaś ca sarve dharmaśāstrakārā evam āhuḥ: "sarvata evātmahanaṃ gopāyet" (GDh 9.34) ity ataḥ sthitam etat brāhmaṇakāmyayādiṣv avaśyaṃ bhakṣaṇam iti | brāhmacāriṇo 'pi tarhi bāhmaṇakāmyādibhir nimittair māṃsabhakṣaṇam āpannaṃ samānatvāt kāraṇasya yathotsṛṣṭpratiṣiddhamāṃśaśanayor iti | na yuktam etat, pratiprasavaśāstrapratiṣedhāt | tasya hi māsi māsikārtham aśano 'pi "kāmam abhyarthito 'śnīyād vratam asya na lupyate" (MDh 2.189) iti | anena śāstreṇa vratavad annam upadiṣtaṃ madhumāṃsavarjitam | tathā ca māṃsabhakṣaṇe prāyaścittam asya "vratacārī tu yo 'śnīyān madhumāṃsau kathaṃcana" (MDh 12.159) ityevamādi brāhmaṇakāmyayā | tarhy ārtvijyādau prāṇātyaye ca katham | ucyate – samānakaraṇatvād itarayor api nimittayor an yuktam asya māṃsabhakṣaṇaṃ tad evam apratiṣiddhānāṃ mṛgapakṣigavādiniyamena bhakṣaṇe prāpte niyamakāriṇaḥ śāstrād anyopāyabhakṣaṇapratiṣedhe sati teṣām arthaprāptaṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ yat tasya yathoktena dviprakāreṇa nivṛttiśāstreṇa pratiṣedhe sati pratiprasavaśāstram idaṃ pratiṣedhaviṣaye punar māṃsabhakṣaṇasya niyamārtham ārabhyate | evaṃ ca śāstratrayasya pratiprasavabhakṣaṇenābhyudayaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | pratyavāyādyutpattimātram | abhakṣaṇe tu pratyavāyaḥ, śāstrārambhasāmarthāt | evaṃ ca vakṣyati: "niyuktas tu yathānyāyam" (MDh 5.35) iti | anye tv āhuḥ – abhakṣayato yathā pratyavāya evaṃ bhakṣayato 'bhyudayena bhavitavyam | saṃdhyopāsanavattvaṃ punar asya yuktasya vicāraṇīyavidhitvāt saṃdhyopāsanasya cārthaprāptasya niyamabhāvād iti "prāṇānām eva cātyaye" ity arthavādaḥ |
prāṇasyānnam idaṃ sarvaṃ prajāpatir akapayat |
jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ caiva sarvaṃ prāṇasya bhojanam || 5.28 ||
tatra pañcavṛtter vāyoḥ śarīrendriyasthitihetubhūtasyedaṃ nāma prāṇa iti | apare tv indriyavṛttiviśeṣasamudāyaṃ hṛdayāyatanaṃ prāṇam ācakṣate | taṃ ca kecit tārkikā nānymanyante | sthitihetutvaṃ cāsya tadbhāvabhāvitatvāc chārīrātvādikalpasyānnam idam | na kevalaṃ vrīhyādivikārabhūtam | kiṃ tarhi | mṛgakāṣṭhātṛṇaparṇādisarvaṃ śārīrasya prāṇasya kathaṃcana tad annaṃ vyapadiśyate | tasya sthitihetutvāt | tathā ca tenaiva tan nigīryate | bhoktur asya satas tasyedam ucyate bhojyam, yena kṣetrajñasya bhogādhikāraḥ | evaṃ ca sati na bhojyabhoktṛsambandhāpekṣayā ṣaṣṭhī | kiṃ tarhi | yathoktārthe ca prakaraṇasāmarthyābhyāṃ veditavyā padārthagauravārtham | asyādhunā kāraṇaṃ darśayati | evaṃ yathoktaṃ prajāpatir akalpayat | na svābhāvikam asya kāraṇaṃ ca | prāṇasaṃvādopaniṣadi ceyam arthakalpanā prajāpater ucyate | "yad idaṃ kiṃcid ārabhya ākīṭapataṅgebhyas tat te naram" iti vacanāt sarvam ity enad adhunā vivṛṇoti | jaṅgamaṃ prāṇyanugataṃ sthāvaraṃ ca tadviparītam | taṃ sarvaṃ prāṇasya bhojanam | evaṃ na [223] kevalaṃ lokaprasiddham eva | asya sūtrasthānasyedaṃ bhāṣyam ucyate |
carāṇām annam acarā daṃśṭriṇām apy adaṃṣṭriṇaḥ |
ahastāś ca sahastānāṃ śūrāṇāṃ caiva bhīravaḥ || 5.29 ||
āpekṣikam etad evaṃ ca loke dṛṣṛṭam | carāṇāṃ vegavattarāṇāṃ śyenādīnām īṣadvegā acarāḥ kapotādaya ucyante, daṃṣṭriṇāṃ ca vyāghrādīnāṃ gṛgādayo 'daṃṣṭriṇaḥ, evam eva ahastāḥ sarpādayaḥ sahastānāṃ pāṇipādavatāṃ nakulādīnām abhisaraṇāpasaraṇakriyākṣamāṇāṃ nidarśaṇārthatvād evaṃ śūrāṇāṃ ca bhīravaḥ sarvadā śaktisambandhavyāpāraviśeṣavatām annam | evam anye 'pi tadviparītā vijñeyāḥ | evaṃ ca prajāpativyavasthāyāṃ satyām |
nāttā duṣyaty adann ādyān prāṇino 'hany ahany api |
dhātraiva sṛṣṭā hy ādyāś ca prāṇino 'ttāra eva ca || 5.30 ||
prāṇānntyamāṃsabhakṣaṇaniyamastutyartho 'yam arthavādaḥ | tadanuṣṭhānaprarocanārtho vijñeyaḥ | sa punar yathāśrutavāda evāyam ity atra ko hetuḥ | sarvaprāṇyapaghātapratiṣedhopadeśasāmarthāt tadvyatikramopadeśāc ca na vikalpaḥ | yadi ca hetura ayam asya syān nārthavādaḥ tata eva śāstravyavasthābhaṅga āpadyeta samānatvāt kāraṇasya na caiva dṛṣtam | (??) mama tu yathoktatva evāsyārthaḥ |
yajñāya jagdhir māṃsasyety eṣa daivo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
tasyāyam arthavādaḥ madhuparkātithyaśrāddhayor māṃsabhakṣaṇaniyamaprarocanārtheyaṃ smṛtiḥ atha vā dṛṣṭāntārthavādo 'yam | katham | yathā hi yāgatantre ṛtvijā tadaṅgam avaśyaṃmāṃsabhakṣaṇam anuṣṭhīyate brāhmaṇakāmyayā śrāddhe māṃsabhakṣaṇam avaśyānuṣṭheyam apratyavāyāya | tathā ca vakṣyati "nyuktas tu yathānyāyam" (MDh 5.35) iti | yeṣāṃ ca prokṣitabhakṣaṇam api niyamatas teṣām ayaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ trayāṇām apy arthavādaḥ |
ato 'nyathā pravṛttis tu rākṣaso vidhir ucyate || 5.31 ||
ity etat punar uttaraślokārtham aprokṣitabhakṣaṇanindāyaitannivṛttyarthatvāt parisaṃkhyāśeṣa evaṃ ca saty aprokṣitabhakṣaṇapratiṣedhārthe kā smṛtir niyamabhakṣaṇārthā cāpārā brāhmaṇakāmyāya śrāddhaprāṇātyayeṣv iyaṃ tṛtīyocyate 'nyopāyapratiṣedharthā |
krītvā svyaṃ vāpy utpādya paropakṛtam eva vā |
devān pitṝṃś cārcayitvā khādan māṃsaṃ na duṣyati || 5.32 ||
evaṃ ca kriyādinopāyenopādāya bhakṣayed evārcanaśiṣṭaṃ yad anyat go'vyajamṛgamāṃsān mṛgādīnām [224] atra vā svayam utpādanasyānādarakīrtanān nābhyanujñānaṃ yena nātra māṃsotpādananiyamāḥ śiṣyante 'rthalakṣaṇatvād eteṣām | bhavati cārthaviddhātor (?)avidhitsaya vacanam | yathā mṛtvā jartvedaṃ (?) kartavyam ity athātāvirodhena (?) prayojanaṃ tato yajñāya svayam utpādyeti bhaviṣyati evaṃ ca yathā saṃbhavam avirodho 'vatkeṣyā (?)śrito bhavati | asya māṃsotpādanahetor devān pitṝṃś cārghyapūrvam iti gṛhasthadharmānuvado 'yam | evaṃ ca nedaṃ gṛhasthadharmād bhidyante | tathā ca sarva eva pāko gṛhasthasyaivaṃrūpaḥ | gṛhasthadharmānuvādaprayojanaṃ cāsyedam ucyate | gurupādasya snātakavartalopaprāyaścittaviśeṣo yaś cittaviśeṣo yathā syāt | anyathā tadbhakaṣayataḥ kṛsarādivad ity uktaṃ purastāt | ity evaṃ ca sati  –
nādyād avidhinā māṃsaṃ vidhijño 'nāpadi dvijaḥ |
jagdhvā hy avidhinā māṃsaṃ pretas tair adyate 'vaśaḥ || 5.33 ||
etadvākyaśeṣasāmānyārtho 'yam aparaḥ kenacid viśeṣaṇaślokaḥ [prakṣiptaḥ?] |
na tādṛśaṃ bhavaty eno mṛgahantur dhanārthinaḥ |
yādṛśaṃ bhavati pretya vṛthāmāṃsāni khādataḥ || 5.34 ||
ślokadvayam idaṃ krītārthaprayogasya nindārtham | nāyaṃ vidhiḥ | kiṃ tarhi | prokṣitādipūrvabhakṣaṇam eva pūrvavidhir ity evam ity ayam api pūrvaśāstrārthastutir atha vānantaratvāt krītādividhistutyartham evedam | ato 'nyathā na bhakṣayitavyaṃ māṃsam iti niyuktabhakṣaṇastutyartham | idaṃ adhunocyate |
niyuktas tu yathānyāyaṃ yo māṃsaṃ nātti vai dvijaḥ |
sa pretya paśutāṃ yāti saṃbhavān ekaviṃśatim || 5.35 ||
evaṃ tāvad ayaṃ niyogārthaprasiddhyā vaiśeṣikaśrāddhamāṃsabhakṣaṇārthavādaḥ | adhyāhāreṇa tv ayaṃ sarvaṃ vidhiśea eva kriyate śāstreṇa "niyuktas tu yathānyāyam" iti |
asaṃskṛtān paśūn mantrair nādyād vipraḥ kadācana |
mantrais tu saṃskṛtān dadyāc chāśvataṃ dharmam āsthitaḥ || 5.36 ||
"anuṣṭrāṃś caikatodataḥ" (MDh 5.18) ity anena go'vyajānām ekatodatvāt bhakṣyatve prāpte krītādiśāstreṇa tatpratiṣedhārtham | yad uktam "prokṣitaṃ bhakṣayen māṃsam" (MDh 5.27) iti, etat parisaṃkhyāśeṣatayāyam artho vijñeyaḥ, sāmarthyāt | tathā coktam asya punaruktatvaṃ yenārthavādārtham asyopadeśenāpūrvavidhyarthaḥ | evaṃ ca prakaraṇam atha vā viṣayāntarakalpanayoktam | asya punaruktatvam uktaṃ purastāt | sītāyajñādiṣu chāgādīnāṃ mantrānvitā yo 'vaśaḥ tatpratiṣedhartham iti | yadi [225] tu paśuvadhād vinā dhānyādidṛṣṭo na saṃpadyata ity evaṃ mithyābuddhir asya syāt | tataḥ kāmāt –
kuryād ghṛtapaśuṃ saṅge kuryāt piṣṭapaśuṃ tathā |
na tv eva tu vṛthā hantuṃ paśum icchet kathaṃcana || 5.37 ||
saṅge karmaṇaḥ paśusthāne chāgasyāgeyatvāt "tejo vai ghṛtam" iti śruteḥ ghṛtapaśuṃ kuryān mukhyapaśusthāne ghṛtādānam | medorūpāṇāṃ (?) vā | tatkāraṇena piṣṭena piṣṭamayaṃ paśuṃ na tu śāstraviruddhena [vidhinā]? vṛthā paśūnāṃ yasmād āha –
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvat kṛtvo hi māraṇam |
vṛthā paśughnaḥ prāpnoti pretya janmani janmani || 5.38 ||
na tv ayaṃ doṣaś chāgādīnāṃ yajñavadhe (?) yasmād –
yajñārthaṃ paśavaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayam eva svayaṃbhuvā |
yajñaś ca bhūtyai sarvasya tasmād yajñe vadho 'vadhaḥ || 5.39 ||
na kevalaṃ hiṃsādoṣakāryābhāvād evam ucyate, kiṃ tarhi | tadanugrahakriyākaś ca | tathā ca darśayati –
oṣadhyaḥ paśavo vṛkṣās tiryañcaḥ pakṣiṇas tathā |
yajñārthaṃ nidhanaṃ prāptāḥ prāpnuvanty ucchritīḥ punaḥ || 5.40 ||
manuṣyatvādhikāralakṣaṇād dvīpavarṣāntarajanmarūpā ca śāstrasāmarthyād veditavyā | tathā cāyaṃ brāhmaṇakāmyārthavādārthaḥ –
madhuparke ca yajñe ca pitṛdaivatakarmaṇi |
atraiva paśavo hiṃsyā nānyatrety abravīn manuḥ || 5.41 ||
evaṃ ca sati na kevalam apratyavāyakaras tadvadhaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | bhṛ(ha)tyaiva pradhānakriyāṅgabhāvenobhayeṣāṃ yajamānasya paśūnāṃ ca yathā yajña evaṃ madhuparke ceti stutiparam etad arghyapaśumāṃsabhakṣaṇasyaivaṃ sāmarthyād vijñeyam | yathā ca tadvadhatayānugrāhī (?) yajñādiṣu tathedam aparaṃ śāstram –
eṣv artheṣu paśūn hiṃsan vedatattvārthavid dvijaḥ |
ātmānaṃ ca paśuṃ caiva gamayaty uttamāṃ gatim || 5.42 ||
devapitṛmanuṣyānugrahakriyāsādhanabhāve vartamānaḥ paśavo yad utkṛṣyante tad yuktaṃyean dharmādharmaprāptiparihārasya vyavasthāpakād vipāko (?) śāstrapramāṇa eva na ca vedasmṛtiśāstravirodhaḥ kaścit | etasmin yathā śūdratvādeḥ (?) yenaitad evaṃ na pratipadyemahi | yathānyatra "sarvaṃ [226] svaṃ brāhmaṇasya" (MDh 1.100) ity evamādi | yaś ca manyeta yajamānārthān karmaṇas tatsādhanabhūtānāṃ paśvādīnām utkarṣo yathāśruto na prāpnoti yathartvijām iti saṃnivartya evaṃ yathā paśvādaya ātmopamardena yāgasādhanāṅgabhāvaṃ pratipadyante nartvija evaṃ tasmād utkṛṣyeran karmaṇo netarathā vyarthād api ca karmaṇo yady artvijām ānuṣaṅgikaphlam aihikaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ karmano dakṣiṇeyaṃ paśvādīnām atadarthād api tasmāt karmaṇo 'nuṣaṅgaphalam adṛṣṭāntaḥ syād ity adhikṛtatvāt teṣāṃ tatphalenādhikāra iti ced vacanād adhikāras teṣām aśva(?) syād ānuṣaṅgikaphalābhisandhau yathartvijām iti dṛṣṭaṃ ca | parārthād api bhārodvahanādikarmaṇas tatsādhanabhūtānāṃ karmakārāṇāṃ pradhānaphalānuṣaktam anuṣaṅgakāryam | yathaivam ihāpi syād ity evaṃ ca sati –
gṛhe gurāv araṇye vā nivasan ātmavān dvijaḥ |
nāvedavihitāṃ hiṃsām āpady api samācaret || 5.43 ||
atra ca hetum āha –
yā vedavihitā hiṃsā niyatāsmiṃś carācare |
ahiṃsām eva tāṃ vidyād
hiṃsādoṣakāryābhāvāt lokavandyāt (?)
vedād dharmo hi nirbabhau || 5.44 ||
na pramāṇāntarataḥ | tathā ca vedasmṛtyāgamagamyo hiṃsānuṣṭhānaparibhāṣayor viṣayavibhāgena tartaśilāḥ (?) pramāṇaṃ syād iha na paraduḥkhotpādamātraṃ hiṃsā na paraduḥkhotpādanamātre ca nāgraha (?) ubhayatra vyabhicāradarśanāt (?) parahitānuṣṭhānapunarduḥkhānuṣaktam apy ekāntataḥ satyam ahiṃety evaṃ prativaktavyam | anyathā hi sarvadṛṣṭipravādeṣu śāstrāṇām asāmarthyaṃ prasajyeta na caitad iṣṭam (?) | tasmād yuktam idam "vedād dharmo hi nirbabhau" iti | ayam aparo hiṃsāpratiṣedhārthaḥ ślokaḥ | prakaraṇasāmarthyād avidhimāṃsabhakṣaṇapratiṣedhārthavādaḥ |
yo 'hiṃsakāni bhūtāni hinasty ātmasukhecchayā |
sa jīvaṃś ca mṛtaś caiva na kvacit sukham edhate || 5.45 ||
evaṃ ca vyāghrātatāyiprabhṛtīnām analpapratyavāyo 'vadha ity etad darśitaṃ bhavati | hiṃsratvāt (?) teṣām ahiṃsrakānām iti copadeśāt | tathā ca –
yo bandhanavadhakleśān prāṇināṃ ca cikīrṣati |
sa sarvasya sukhaprepsuḥ sukham atyantam aśnute || 5.46 ||
[227]
pūrvavad ayaṃ ca | aparas (?) tu mānārtha evam –
yad dhyāyati yat kurute ratiṃ badhnāti yatra ca |
tad avāpnoty ayatnena yo hinasti na kiṃcana || 5.47 ||
vyāghrādīty api (?) sarva ete hiṃsānivṛttyarthā api santaḥ ślokāḥ prakaraṇasāmarthyād avidhimāṃsabhakṣaṇapratiṣedhārthā veditavyāḥ | yo hy avidhinā māṃsaṃ bhakṣayati tena paśuvadha evaṃ kṛto bhavati | sa hi māṃsārthaṃ kevalaṃ hanyata ity evaṃ ca sati avidhimāṃsabhakṣaṇanindā vidhibhakṣaṇastutyarthā sampadyate 'to yataś ca –
nākṛtvā prāṇināṃ hiṃsāṃ māṃam utpadyate kvacit |
ataś cānena nimitte naimittikasambandhe nānāprakaraṇaṇe (?) 'pi hiṃsāpratiṣedhopanyāsaḥ idam artho 'pi jñeyaḥ |
na ca prāṇivadhaḥ
hiṃsākaratvāt (?)
svargyas
(a)vidhinā (?) yasmāt
tasmān māṃsaṃ vivarjayet || 5.48 ||
avidhinā bhakṣayitur idaṃ cāparavidhibhakṣaṇaparihārārthaṃ prasaṃkhyānam arthavādarūpam adhunocyate |
samutpattiṃ ca māṃsasya vadhabandhau ca dehinām |
prasamīkṣya nivarteta sarvamāṃsasya bhakṣaṇāt || 5.49 ||
samutpattir māṃsasya mātāpitroḥ śuklaśoṇitābhyām atyantāśucibhyāṃ vijñāya kukṣāv aśucisthāne vadhabandhau ca dehinām api (?) krūtau māṃsotpattyupāyabhūtau buddhvā nivarteta sarvamāṃsasya bhakṣaṇād ity arthavādo 'yam, na hetuḥ | itarathādyavidhibhakṣaṇam apy asya hetoḥ samānatvād virudhyeteti | tathā ca sarvagrahaṇam anugṛhītaṃ bhavaty atra | evaṃ ca sati –
na bhakṣayati yo māṃsaṃ vidhiṃ hitvā piśācavat |
sa loke priyatāṃ yāti vyādhibhiś ca na pīḍyate || 5.50 ||
dṛṣṭārthasambandheneyam avidhimāṃsabhakṣaṇanindātatparivarjanāvarjanastutir iyaṃ cāparārthamāṃsabhakṣaṇād bhavati –
[228]
anumantā viśasitā nihantā krayavikrayī |
saṃskartā copahartā ca khādakaś ceti ghātakāḥ || 5.51 ||
[Ed. atra caturthe pāde "śceti" ity atra "ścāṣṭa" iti pāṭhāntaraṃ vyākhyāyāṃ sūcitaṃ tatrāruciś ca pradarśitā, kravikrayiṇa ekatvaṃ ca taddhetutvenopanyastam ]
katham | yathaiva vihantur anumantuś ca śāstrāntarasiddham eva bādhakasyāsya vidhibhakṣaṇanindārthavādo 'yam | evaṃ saty api pratiṣiddhamāṃsabhakṣaṇe prāṇivadhaprāyaścittaṃ ghātakasya na bhavati | yac cānyā prakārāntareṇa nindā | viśasitṛkrayavikrayisaṃskartrapahartṝṇām eva tāvad piṣṭapaśubandhaprayogānugatānāṃ ghātakatvam asati partiṣedhaśāstre 'nyasmin kiṃ punaḥ khādakasya yadarthaḥ paśuvadha iti | tad evam eteṣām anyatamavaiklavye (?) 'py upayogābhāvāt khādakārtho 'yaṃ vidhiḥ māṃsabhakṣaṇanindārthavāda ubhayathā vyākaraṇīyaḥ | pūrvavad ayam api vyākaraṇīyaḥ | krayavikrayiṇaś caiktvād aṣṭatvapāṭho 'tra nāsti | tathā ca saty ayam anyaḥ pāṭha āśraṇīyaḥ: "khādaśaceti ghātakāḥ" iti | na cāsmin pāṭhe kaścic chāstroparodho na cāṣṭatvapāṭhe 'nugraho 'rthavādatvād asyeti | yataś caitad evam ataḥ –
svamāṃsaṃ paramāṃsena yo vardhayitum icchati |
anabhyarcya pitṝn devān na tato 'nyo 'sty apuṇyakṛt || 5.52 ||
iyam anyā prakṛtārthastutir apratiṣiddhamāṃsanivṛttistutiḥ |
varṣe varṣe 'śvamedhena yo yajeta śataṃ samāḥ |
māṃsāni ca na khāded yas tayoḥ puṇyaphalaṃ samam || 5.53 ||
phalamūlāśanair medhyair munyannānāṃ ca bhojanaiḥ |
na tat phalam avāpnoti yan māṃsaparivarjanāt || 5.54 ||
māṃsanivṛttyarthāv imau ślokau | katarasya māṃsasya | pratiṣidhyanti svati (?) | prakaraṇāt tāvat pratiṣiddhasyeti gamyate | yataḥ "tayoḥ puṇyaphalaṃ samam", "na tat phalam avāpnoti yan māṃsaparivarjanāt" iti yato vijñayate | atithyādibhuktaśiṣṭasyāpratiṣiddhasya tyāga idaṃ praśaṃsāvacanam | yasmād iha pratiṣiddhavarjanaṃ pratyavāyānutpattimātraphalaṃ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaṃ dṭṣṭāc cādṛṣṭam apy anumeyaṃ tathā rūpam evātha tasmād apy abhayaṃ kathaṃcit kalpayituṃ śakyata etad evaṃ yujyata ity adhunā nāmadheyanirvacanena punar arthavādīkaroti pratiṣiddhavarjanārthaṃ –
māṃ sa bhakṣayitāmutra yasya māṃsam ihādmy aham |
etan māṃsasya māṃsatvaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ || 5.55 ||
[229]
idānīṃ māṃsanivṛttidharmāya vaktavyeti yat tu idaṃ tatsambandhenocyate |
na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣo na made na ca maithune |
pravṛttir eṣā bhūtānāṃ nivṛttis tu mahāphalā || 5.56 ||
pratiṣedhaviṣaye tāvad idam amartham "na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣaḥ" iti doṣavataḥ pratiṣedhadarśanāt | pratiṣedhaśāstrānārthakyaprasaṅge 'py avidhiviṣeye 'pi doṣāśaṅkābhāvāt kaḥ prasaṅgas tasya | evaṃ ca satyāṃ śiṣṭaṃ pratiṣiddhaviṣayam idaṃ vacanam, sāmarthyāt "na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣaḥ" iti – tac ca punar atithyādibhuktaśiṣṭaṃ prokṣitaśeṣaṃ ca śiṣṭāpratiṣiddhaṃ veditavyam | yat tv avaśyaṃ bhakṣaṇīyaṃ śrāddhādiṣu, tasyotsarge doṣaśravaṇam | tathā coktam "niyuktas tu yathānyāyam" (YDh 5.35) iti | "pravṛttir eṣā bhūtānām" ity ayam arthavādo nivṛttistutyarthaḥ | evaṃ ca saty aśiṣṭasya vā pratiṣiddhasya vā sato nivṛttir ātmecchayā mahāphalā | na pratiṣedhaśāstreṇa | tathā cāśvamedhāvṛttyanuṣṭhānaṃ (?) kandamūlaphalatulyaṃ (?) na bhojanābhyudayasampadādṛto 'syānivṛtteḥ saṃstavaḥ nidarśanārthaṃ cedaṃ sarvanivṛttīnām itthaṃbhūtānāṃ vijñeyam | tathā ca gudalavaṇādityāgena śiṣṭasamācāraḥ samyag asya dṛṣṭāntaḥ | yathādhikāram aśiṣṭāpratiṣiddhaviṣayā madyamaithunanivṛttir vyākhyātā jñātavyā | adhunā –
pretaśuddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi dravyaśuddhiṃ tathaiva ca |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ || 5.57 ||
"etad uktaṃ dvijātīnāṃ bhakṣyābhakṣyam aśeṣataḥ" (MDh 5.26) ity etasmāt pūrvoktād abhakṣyaprakaraṇopasaṃhārān māṃsaprakaraṇe yady api sarvavarṇādhikāras tathāpīdaṃ punar vacanam ādarārthaṃ "caturṇām api varṇānām" iti | tathā ca pradarśayiṣyaty ādaram | adhunā yathānirdeśaṃ pratiśuddhim eva brūmaḥ |
dantajāte 'nujāte ca kṛtacaule ca saṃsthite |
aśuddhā bāndhavāḥ sarve sūtake ca tathocyate || 5.58 ||
dantajātānujāte ca [anujāto] dantajātād bālatara ity arthaḥ | kṛtacaule | caśabdāt kṛtopanayanādau ca saṃsthite aśuddhiśāstralakṣaṇād alaukikāḥ (?) bāndhavā ity anena sapiṇḍā evocyante | tathā cottaraśloke viśeṣayiṣyati "sūtake ca tathocyate" ity asya samānatvād aprakṛtasyāpy upanyāsaḥ | tad idānīm aśuddhiṃ darśayati –
daśāhaṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate |
arvāk saṃcayanād asthnāṃ tyaham ekāham eva vā || 5.59 ||
[230]
sapiṇḍeṣu saṃsthiteṣu ayaṃ catuḥprakāro vidhiḥ samānodakeṣv anyaṃ vidhiṃ vakṣyati | vyavasthitavikalpaś cāyam | vṛttaṃ svādhyāyāpekṣayā | tathā ca vakṣyati "na vardhayed aghāni" (MDh 5.84) ityevamādi | gautamo 'py āha: "sadyaḥ śaucam," "rājñāṃ ca kāryāvighātārtham (virodhāt?)," "brāhmaṇasya ca svādhyāyānivṛttyartham" (GDh 14.44–46) iti | bālyād iva yo vyavasthāviśeṣāpekṣayā nābrāhmaṇaviṣaya eva kṣatriyādīnām uttaratropadeśād vā "saṃcayanād asthnām" ity etac cāsthypalakṣaṇam (?) "saṃcayanād asthnām" iti vacanāt āhitāgniviṣayam eva caitad vijñeyam | sapiṇḍatām idānīṃ darśayati –
sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate |
samānodakabhāvas tu janmanāmnor avedane || 5.60 ||
sapiṇḍatā ṣaṭpuruṣān atītya saptame prāpte nivartate  | yena triṣu pitrādiṣu jīvatsu parebhyas tribhyaḥ piṇḍadānam iṣyate | evaṃ ca sati "ṣāṭpuruṣī sapiṇḍatā" iti samānodakatā tu janmanāmnor apodane (avedane?) | yadā tu pūrvapuruṣasya nāma vijñāyate 'muṣyānvayas tāvad dhyāmaha iti janmā cāyam asmatkukulīna iti | tad aniṣṭodakaṃ deyam iti samānodakānām eṣā śāvam āśaucasūtakaṃ ca vakṣyate |
janane 'py evam eva syān nipuṇaṃ śuddhim icchatām |
sarveṣāṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ mātāpitros tu sūtakam || 5.61 ||
adhikārāt sapiṇḍānām eva daśāha ekoddiṣṭa uddiśyate | ācāravirodhāt saṃcayanakalpena ca vyavadhānād idam evam ucyate avikārasya ceṣṭataḥ prakṛter daśāha eva netare | ayaṃ janane mukhyakalpaḥ yaḥ sarveṣāṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ sūtakāśaucaṃ syāt | ayaṃ ca paraḥ vikalpaḥ mātāpitror veti yadi ca jajane 'py evam eva syād iti savikalpāḥ grāhyāḥ | mātur api ca sūtakam ācāravirodhi ekāhādikalpeṣu syān na caitad iṣṭam | ata eka eva daśāhādikalp 'tidiśyate | tathā ca gṛhyaśāstraṃ "navamyām utthāpya brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā pitā nāma karoti" iti |
sūtakaṃ mātur eva syād upaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ || 5.62 ||
nirasya tu pumān śukram upaspṛśyaiva śudhyati |
baijikād abhisambandhād anurundhyād aghaṃ tryaham || 5.63 ||
pitur vikalpārtham idam ucyate | yatra śuklanirasanamātreṇa snānaṃ viśiṣṭam upadiśati | nityād adhikam | "snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam" (MDh 5.144) ity anena śāstreṇa tatrāpatyajanmānurodhena sūtakaṃ tryahaṃ kathaṃ na syāt | pitus tryahagrahaṇaṃ ca sarvavikalpapradarśanārthaṃ vikalpaprasiddhaye pitur ity anekeṣāṃ cātra bahavaḥ pāṭhavikalpās tu granthagauravabhayān noktāḥ |
rajanyāhnaivam ekena trirārair eva ca tribhiḥ |
śavaspṛśo viśudhyanti tryahād udakadāyinaḥ || 5.64 ||
śavaspṛśaḥ pretahārāḥ, na tatspṛṣṭāḥ | teṣāṃ sacelasnānaṃ vakṣyati | divākīrtiśloke (MDh 5.85) ślokabandhānuvidhānena cāpayaśo (?) daśarātram idam upadiṣṭaṃ śavaspṛśāṃ vijñeyam | tathā cottaraśloke vakṣyati "pretahāraiḥ samaṃ tatra daśarātreṇa śudhyati" (MDh 5.65) | "tryahāt tūdakadāyinaḥ" iti varṣeṣūdakadāyiṣu satsu tatra kevalodakadāyina eva sasamānodakā gṛhyante, nānye sāmarthyāt |
guroḥ pretasya śiṣyas tu pitṛmedhaṃ samācaran |
pretahāraiḥ samaṃ tatra daśarātreṇa śudhyati || 5.65 ||
[Ed. padyam idaṃ 11.197 saṃkhyākasya padyasya vyākhyām uddhṛtam | tatra "samācaran" ity atra "samācaaret" iti pāṭhaḥ]
ayaṃ cāsapiṇḍasāmarthyād vijñāyate | yathā śavaspṛśaḥ pūrvaśloke yas tv apitṛmedhakṛc chiṣyas tasya "trirātram āhur āśaucam ācārye saṃsthite sati" (MDh 5.80) iti vakṣyati |
rātribhir māsatulyābhir garbhasrāve viśudhyati |
rajasy uparate sādhvī snānena strī rajasvalā || 5.66 ||
nigadavyākhyātaḥ ślokaḥ |
nṛṇām akṛtacaulānāṃ viśuddhir naiśikī smṛtā |
nrvṛttacaulakānāṃ (?) tu trirātrāc chuddhir iṣyate || 5.67 ||
viśeṣavidhir ayam asya vijñeyaḥ | ekāha eva na tryaham | varṣābhāve saṃnivṛttacaulakānāṃ tryahavidhir eva netare daśāhādayaḥ | prāg evopanayanān(?) nirvṛttacaulakā gṛhyante | yeṣāṃ ca saṃskārāntaraprāptir upanayanalakṣaṇā teṣāṃ vyapadeśāntaraṃ jāyate, "upanītā" iti |
[232]
ūnadvivārṣikaṃ pretaṃ nidadhyur bāndhavā bahiḥ |
alaṃkṛtya śucau bhūmāv asthisaṃcayanād ṛte || 5.68 ||
sāṃvatsarikasya caulakaraṇaṃ tṛtīye vā | tatraivaṃ sati tṛtīye saṃvatsare kalpam āśritya smṛtyantarād akṛtacaulasyedaṃ grahaṇaṃ vijñeyam |
nāsya kāryo 'gnisaṃskāro na ca kāryodakakriyā |
araṇye kāṣṭhavat tyaktvā kṣapayet tryaham eva tu || 5.69 ||
asmiṃc chloke ca tattryahavidhir āmnāyate akṛtacaulānām | evaṃ ca sati prāk cūḍākaraṇād ekāhas tryaho vā | ūrdhvaṃ tu cūḍākaraṇāt tryaha eva |
nātrivarṣasya kartavyā bāndhavair udakakriyā |
jātadantasya vā kuryur nāmni vāpi kṛte sati || 5.70 ||
jātadantakṛtanāmnor udakābhyanujñānād agnisaṃskāro 'pi tena saha pratiṣiddhaḥ pūrvaśloke, sāmarthyāt | adhikāro 'pīhābhyanujñāyate |
sabrahmacāriṇy ekāham atīte kṣapaṇaṃ smṛtam |
janmany ekodakānāṃ tu trirātrāc chuddhir iṣyate || 5.71 ||
sabrahmacārī pratyāsanno gṛhyate ekopādhyāyatayā | apare tu sabrahmacārimātra evam icchanti | kṣaṇaṃ [kṣapaṇaṃ?] prakaraṇād āśaucaṃ anadhyāyaḥ | sarvasyaiva śrotriyasya trirātraṃ vakṣyati | ato 'yam aśrotriyārtha ārambhaḥ pratyāsattyapekṣaḥ (?) | yā janmani (?) sūtake ekodakānām asapiṇḍnāṃ trirātrāc chuddhiḥ | sapiṇḍānāṃ tu yathoktena vidhinā | etac cāsapiṇḍasūtakāśaucaṃ sarvasapiṇḍasūtakapakṣe veditavyam, nānyatra |
strīṇām asaṃskṛtānāṃ tu tryahāc chuddyanti bāndhavāḥ |
yathoktenaiva kalpena śudhyanti tu sanābhayaḥ || 5.72 ||
asaṃkṛtānām acūḍānām avratānām ūrdhvaṃ cūḍākaraṇāt tryahāc chudhyanti ca bāndhavāḥ sambandhinaḥ sāmarthyāt | ayaṃ tu nānāmaprabhānāṃ (nāsanābhīnāṃ?) yathoktenaiva śudhyanti na tu sanābhayaḥ | sodaryā daśarātreṇeti kṛtavivāhānāṃ punas tāsāṃ bhāryatvam anubhavantīnāṃ yathokto vidhir jñeyaḥ | tathā ca gṛhyaśāstram udakānām itare kurvīraṃs tām ca teṣām iti gautamo 'py āha "udakadānaṃ sapiṇḍaiḥ kṛtajaṭasya | tat strīṇāṃ ca | eke prattānām" (GDh 14.34–36) iti |
[233]
akṣāralavaṇānnāḥ syur na nimajjeyuś ca te tryaham |
māṃsāśanaṃ ca nāśnīyuḥ śayīraṃś ca pṛthak kṣitau || 5.73 ||
yāvac chāvam āśaucaṃ tāvad ity arthaḥ |
saṃnidhāv eṣa vai kalpaḥ śāvāśaucasya kīrtitaḥ |
asaṃnidhāv ayaṃ jñeyo vidhiḥ saṃbandhibāndhavaiḥ || 5.74 ||
saṃbandhinas te bāndhavā nijenotpattikena saṃbandhena bhāryeṇety atha vā sapiṇḍāḥ saṃbandhinaḥ sodakā bāndhavāḥ | bāndhavaśabdo hy anyatrāpi dṛśyate "mātur āptāṃś ca bāndhavān" (MDh 5.102) iti |
vigataṃ tu videśasthaṃ śṛṇuyād yo hy anidaśam |
yaccheṣaṃ daśarātrasya tāvad evāśucir bhavet || 5.75 ||
videśasthavidhir ayam ucyate daśarātrapakṣe | evam anyatrāpi |
atikrānte daśāhe tu trirātram aśucir bhavet |
daśarātrapakṣa evam itaratrāpi yathāsaṃbhavam |
saṃvatsare vyatīte tu spṛṣṭvaivāpi viśudhyati || 5.76 ||
daśāhātikrame ekasaṃvatsarāt trirātram ūrdhvaṃ tu saṃvatsarāt sadyaḥ śaucam |
nirdaśaṃ jñātimaraṇaṃ śrutvā putrasya janma ca |
savāsā jalam āplutya sadya eva viśudhyati || 5.77 ||
nirdaśe sadyaḥśaucam ucyate | pūrvoktaṃ vā trirātram |
bāle deśāntarasthe ca pṛthakpiṇḍe ca saṃsthite |
savāsā jalam āplutya sadya eva viśudhyati || 5.78 ||
abhāva adeśāntarastho gṛhyate | tasya pūrvam ekarātram aikarātrikī śuddhir uktā: "nṛṇām akṛtacauḷānām aśuddhir naiśikī smṛtā" iti (MDh 5.67) | iha tu tasya ca sadyaḥśaucam ucyate | vaikalpikaṃ bālaka(ta?)rasyedam | tathā coktam "dantajāte 'nujāte ca" (MDh 5.58) iti | deśāntarasthe ceti | abāle 'pi sadyaḥśaucam | kālabhedena saṃvatsarāt pūrvam iha tva anirdaśe 'pi deśāntarasthe sadyaḥśaucam ucyate | tryahākṛdakadāyinā (?) iti pṛthak piṇḍānāṃ sannimaraṇe tryaha uktaṃ keṣām evāyam aparaḥ sadyaḥśaucavidhir āmnāyate | vaikalpikovattas (?) sādhyāyāpekṣayā |
antardaśāhe syātāṃ cet punar maraṇajanmanī |
[234]        tāvat syād aśucir vipro yāvat tat syād anirdaśam || 5.79 ||
idam apy ayuktaṃ smṛtyantarād vyākaraṇīyam | na sūtakaṃ daśāhe samānajātīyam asamānajātīyaṃ copanipatitaṃ maraṇadaśāhe vā | kiṃ tarhi | samānajātīyam evaitan nirdiśyate | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "tac ced antaḥ punar āpatec cheṣeṇa śudhyeran" (GDh 14.6) iti | tadgrahaṇe 'dhikṛtāpekṣam atra vyākaraṇīyam | anye tūbhayam ubhayatra vyākhyāṃ nayanti (?) śaṅkhavacanād "atha ced antarā jāyeta mriyeta vā" iti |
trirātram āhur āśaucam ācārye saṃsthite sati |
tasya putre ca patynāṃ ca divārātram iti sthitiḥ || 5.80 ||
nigadavyākhyātaḥ ślokaḥ |
śrotriya tūpasaṃpanne trirātram aśucir bhavet |
mātule pakṣiṇīṃ rātriṃ śiṣyartvigbāndhaveṣu ca || 5.81 ||
upasaṃpanna āśritaḥ | kenacit kāraṇena mātule pakṣiṇīm rātrim ubhayata abhokṣyat | ahobhyāṃ saṃbandhād rātriḥ pakṣiṇīty etad aupamikaṃ śiṣyo granthārthābhyām ṛtvik smṛtikarmasaṃbandhenāpi bāndhavāś caivābhyādayaḥ (?) |
prete rājani sajyotir yasya syād viṣaye sthitaḥ |
aśrotriye tv ahaḥ kṛtsnam anūcāne tathā gurau || 5.82 ||
yasya rājño viṣaye vāsīta sthitaḥ jyotir āśaucam | rātrau tu tan na kāryam | aśrotriye tv anūcāne (?) kṛtsnam ahaḥ | śrotriye tu trirātram uktam | kecit tu rājaviśeṣaṇaṃ etat kalpayanti | tat punar vicaryam | gurau ca pūjye ayaṃ cāparo 'sapiṇḍānām ekāhakalpaḥ |
śudhyed vipro daśāhena dvādaśāhena bhūmipaḥ |
vaiśyaḥ pañcadaśāhena śūdro māsena śudhyati || 5.83 ||
brāhmaṇadaśāhasyetaravidhitsayā punarvacanam | asyārthaṃ kecid āhuḥ – vaikalpikam idaṃ yena pūrvo vidhiś caturṇām eva | tathā ca vakṣyati: "dharmepsavaś ca dharmajñāḥ" (MDh 10.127) ity evam | apare tu saṃnidhānābhyām etat kalpayanti | gautamas tv etan necchati | tac ca pradarśayitavyam |
na vardhayed aghāhāni pratyūhen nāgniṣu kriyāḥ |
na ca tat karma kurvāṇaḥ sanābhyo 'py aśucir bhavet || 5.84 ||
pūrvoktāhas tyāgārtho 'yaṃ (?) pratiṣedhaḥ | kasyacid evādhyayanavighāto (?) [235] [1 akṣ unclear] vārakarmasa[1 akṣ unclear]dā | tathā ca smṛtyantrāṇy udāharaṇīyāni | evaṃ ca saty ālasyāt "tiṣkurmāya rakhamātsniṣya (niṣkarmā sukham āsiṣye)" ity evaṃ matiḥ prāye 'dhikṛtapuruṣo na vardhayed aghayuktāny ahāni na ca pratyūhet kriyā vaiśvadevādyā na kuryād ity arthaḥ | atha vā pralayed ity arthaḥ (?) | nityatvāt tu tāḥ atipravṛttāḥ kuryād eva | ubhāv api cemau pakṣau gṛhyasmṛtipratigṛhītau | evaṃ hi tatrāha – "nityatvād ahāni na nivarteran vaikhānasaśāstragau caika (??)" iti | "na ca tatkarma kurvāṇaḥ sanābhyo 'śucir bhavet" | narasambandha ity anuṣṭhānastutiparam etat | tathā coktaṃ gṛhye "etāni ekāhni kuryuḥ" iti |
divākīrtim udakyāṃ ca patitāṃ sūtikāṃ tathā |
śavaṃ tatspṛṣṭinaṃ caiva spṛṣṭvā snānena śudhyati || 5.85 ||
divākīrtyaś caṇḍālaḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyā" ityevamādi (GDh 14.30) | anya āhuḥ: "divākīrtyasya yat śmaśrakarmaṇi sparśanaṃ (?) tatredaṃ snānaṃ netarasminn aṅgasaṃsparśa" iti | tat tv idaṃ smṛtyantarasāmarthyāt apavyākhyānam (?) ity ayuktam (?) | mārjāramūṣikasaṃvāde ca vedavyāsena divākīrtyaś caṇḍāla ukto yenātaḥ pūrvokta evārthaḥ |
ācamya prayato nityaṃ japed aśucidarśane |
saurān mantrān yathotsāhaṃ pāvamānīś ca śaktitaḥ || 5.86 ||
prakṛtānāṃ divākīrtyānām aśucipadārthānāṃ darśaṇaṃ kṛtaṃ syāt (cet?) | sparśane tūktam |
nāraṃ spṛṣṭvāsthi sasnehaṃ snātvā vipro viśudhyati |
ācamyaiva tu niḥsnehaṃ gām ālabhyārkam īkṣya vā || 5.87 ||
nigadavyākkhyāta evāyaṃ ślokaḥ |
ādiṣṭī nodakaṃ kuryād ā vratasya samāpanāt |
samāpter (?) udakaṃ kuryāt trirātreṇaiva śudhyati || 5.88 ||
vratinaḥ sato yo bandhuḥ praiti na tasmai dadyād udakam ā vratasamāpteḥ | pūrveṣāṃ ca pratiṣedhaḥ | yenoktaṃ "nityaṃ snātvā śuciḥ kuryād devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam" (MDh 8.176) iti | samāpter udakaṃ kṛtvā sarvapretebhyas trirātreṇaiva śudhyati | tatra vṛtyā |
vṛthā saṃkarajātānāṃ pravrajyāsu ca tiṣṭhatām |
ātmanas tyāgināṃ caiva nivartetodakakriyā || 5.89 ||
vṛthā jāto yo na devān arcati na pitṝn na manuṣyān | niyuktāḥ sutādayaḥ śāstrapratiṣedhāt | [236] saṃkarajātānāṃ pravrajyā | sā ca śāstralakṣaṇā | vyatikramasāhacaryād viṣādibhir ātmatyāgināṃ ca nivartetodakadānāt | śāstraprāptasyāyaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | āśaucam api codakavat sāmarthyād evam eva vijñeyam |
pāṣaṇḍam āśritānāṃ ca carantīnāṃ ca kāmataḥ |
garbhabhartṛdruhāṃ caiva surāpīnāṃ ca yoṣitām || 5.90 ||
atra ca vyatikramasāhacaryād udakadānapratiṣedho yasmād ataḥ dvijātistrīṇām etad yathāpratiṣedhaṃ kalpayitavyam | surāpānavyatikramaṇam ataś codakyāliṅgaṃ (?) patitatvād āsāṃ vijñeyam | brahmahatyā suvarṇasteyaṃ ca | tad aparas tv āha – saty api jātyarthāviśeṣe "devānām aśnatā haviḥ" (MDh 11.95) iti liṅgāt puṃsa eva brāhmaṇasya surāpānapratiṣedho gamyate niyamena | strīṇāṃ tu kṣatriyavaiśyavad aniṣedha ity anenābhiprāyeṇa kvacit brāhmaṇaśabdaṃ paṭhanti (?) | evaṃ niyamapakṣe tadvyatikramanindaiṣā sarvathā jñeyā | na tu patitatvena | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: "surāṃ mūlyena mohayitvā brāhmaṇīḥ kulaśīlacāritrasampannāḥ śatacaṇḍībhiḥ (?) saha pāyayen mātrartham" iti parisaṃkhyā ca | gautamīye: "bhrūṇahani hīnavaṇasevāyāṃ strī patati" (GDh 21.9) ity atra caitasyām vipratipattau nirṇaye bhagavanto bhūmidevāḥ pramāṇam iti |
ācāryaṃ svam upādhyāyaṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum |
nirhṛtya tu vratī pretaṃ na vratena viyujyate || 5.91 ||
anyaṃ nivṛtya tu viyujyata ity etad vratino 'nyapretanirharaṇapratiṣedhavākyam arthād vijñeyam | svagrahaṇāc ca "guror gurau saṃnihite guruvad vṛttim ācaret" (MDh 2.205) ity anenāpi | yad api vyavahite gurugurau prāptaṃ tad anena pratiṣiddhaṃ bhavati |
dakṣiṇena mṛtaṃ śūdraṃ puradvāreṇa nirharet |
paścimottarapūrvais tu yathāyogaṃ dvijanmanām || 5.92 ||
diṅniyamārtha ārambhaḥ | amaṅgalatvāc ca śavanirharaṇasya varṇaviparyayeṇopadeśaḥ sādhuḥ vijñeyaḥ |
na rājñām aghadoṣo 'sti vratināṃ satriṇāṃ tathā |
aindrasthānam upāsīnā brahmabhūtā hi te sadā || 5.93 ||
sarvalokavinayādhānārtham ādhipatyapradam aindrasthānaṃ rājñāṃ guṇāt sambandhanopāyena (?) tasya sadyaḥśaucam | devādevādhiṣṭhāne netarathā ceti stutyarthavādaḥ sadyaḥśaucavidhyarthaḥ | [237] evaṃ prakriyāṇāṃ satriṇāṃ ca brahmabhūtatvād iti pūrvavad vyākhyeyam | yataś caitad evam ataḥ –
rājño mahātmike sthāne sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
prajānāṃ parirakṣārtham āsanaṃ cātra kāraṇam || 5.94 ||
mahān ātmā yasya sthānasya yasmin vā tad idaṃ māhātmyakaṃ tatsthitasya rājñaḥ sadyaḥśaucaṃ yena tatsthānaṃ prajānāṃ parirakṣārtham | tathā caitad darśayati "āsanaṃ cātra kāraṇam" iti | ataś ca nakṣatramātrasyedaṃ grahaṇam | rājaśabdena abhiṣekādiguṇayuktasya prāṇānāṃ pālayituḥ | kiṃ ca –
ḍimbāhavahatānāṃ ca vidyutā pārthivena ca |
gobrāhmaṇasya caivārthe yasya cecchati bhūmipaḥ || 5.95 ||
[Ed. vyākhyāyām uddhṛte caturthe pāde "cecchati bhūmipaḥ" ity atra "vecchati pārthivaḥ" iti pāṭhāntaram]
