ḍimbo mahājano nipātasapiṇḍākulo janasamūhaḥ aśastrakalaho vā | āhavas tu śastrakalahaḥ | vidyud aśaniḥ | pārthivo rājā | tena cāyuddhe ca | tathā ca gautamaḥ: "rājakrodhāc yuddhe" sadyaḥ śaucam āha | gavārthe brāhmaṇārthe cānāhave 'pi sadyaḥśaucam | "yasya vecchati pārthivaḥ" amātyādeḥ svakāryāvighātārtham anyasya vā yasmād asya devatātvāt paśvālambhaśravaṇāt | tathā ca darśayati "purutajā yamark[ā adhvaraṃ viduḥ]" iti | yasmāc ca –
somāgnyarkānilendrāṇāṃ cittāppatyor yamasya ca |
aṣṭānāṃ lokapālānāṃ vapur dhārayate nṛpaḥ || 5.96 ||
evaṃ ca sati |
lokeśādhiṣṭhito rājā nāsyāśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
śaucāśaucaṃ hi martyānāṃ lokeśaprabhavāpyayam || 5.97 ||
na tu devānām anadhikārāt | tena teṣāṃ tadānīm āhavahatānām ity asyārthavādo 'yaṃ pratīyate |
udyatair āhave śastraiḥ kṣatradharmahatasya ca |
sadyaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate yajño nāsyāśaucaṃ vidhīyate || 5.98 ||
tatsaṃniyoge yajamānavad asyāśaucaṃ yajñasthitasyeti |
vipraḥ śudhyaty apaḥ spṛṣṭvā kṣatriyo vāhanāyudham |
[5.99]        vaiśyaḥ pratodaṃ yaṣṭiṃ vā yaṣṭiṃ śūdraḥ kṛtakriyaḥ || 5.99 ||
daśāhādīnāṃ kalpanāmanta idam udakopasparśanādikaṃ varaṃ śaucaṃ kartavyam |
etad vo 'bhihitaṃ śaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu dvijottamāḥ |
asapiṇḍeṣu sarveṣu pretaśuddhiṃ nibodhata || 5.100 ||
upasaṃhāropanyāsārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
asapiṇḍaṃ dvijaṃ pretaṃ vipro nirhṛtya bandhuvat |
viśudhyati trirātreṇa mātur āptāṃś ca bāndhavān || 5.101 ||
ṛjvarthaḥ ślokaḥ |
yady annam atti teṣāṃ tu daśāhenaiva śudhyati |
anadann annam ahnaiva na cet tasmin gṛhe vaset || 5.102 ||
anaśnataś cātrāvasatā cāśocaśuddhir vā sado (?) 'naśnatā cānantaraślokoktas tryahaḥ | aśnatas tu tadīyam annaṃ tataś ca daśāha eva |
anugamyecchayā pretaṃ jñātim ajñātim eva vā |
snātvā sacelaṃ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || 5.103 ||
snānam anis(?)payoghṛtapāśanaṃ ca sarvatrāpi kartavyam |
na vipraṃ sveṣu tiṣṭhatsu mṛtaṃ śūdreṇa nāyayet |
asvargyājyāhitiḥ sā syāc chūdrasaṃsparśadūṣitā || 5.104 ||
yo yatroṣṭe (?) tasyāyam upadeśo niyamārthaḥ | idānīṃ dṛṣṭāntārthaṃ śavaṃ tundhe(buddheḥ?) svārṣaṃ (?) vedam adhunocyate | śuddhiprakaraṇe –
jñānaṃ tapo 'gnir āhāro mṛn mano vāry upārjanam (?) |
vāyuḥ karmārkakālau ca śuddheḥ kartṝṇi dehinām || 5.105 ||
jñānena buddheḥ śuddhiṃ vakṣyati: "buddhir jñānena śudhyati" (MDh 5.109) | puruṣasya vā | "tapasā vedavittamāḥ" (MDh 5.107) tāṃ? | "punaḥpākena mṛmayam" (MDh 5.122) ity agninā | āhāreṇa haviṣyādibhojanena | mṛdā amdhyaliptasya | "manaḥ satyena śudhyati" (MDh 5.109) iti vacanād atrāpi viparyasya "manaḥpūtāṃ vaded vācam" (MDh 6.46?) iti vaktavyam | vāri prasiddhaṃ śaucārtham | na copārjanenopalepanena cen manaḥ | "mārjanopārjanaiḥ veśma" (MDh 5.122) iti vacanāt | vāyur anuraktaḥ (?) śaucārthatvena | etasmād eva yatnāt śiṣṭācārāvirodhena kvacic caṇḍālādispṛṣṭe [239] tṛṇakāṣṭhādau śuddhisādhanatvena draṣṭavyaḥ | etenārkakālau vyākhyātau | karmaṇāgniṣṭomādinā pāpakṛta iti prakaraṇād idam anayc chaucam ucyate pūrvavat |
sarveṣām eva śaucānām arthaśaucaṃ paraṃ smṛtam |
yo 'rthe śucir hi sa śucir na mṛdvāriśuciḥ śuciḥ || 5.106 ||
alobhārthavyavahāriṇāṃ śucir lokasaṃvyavahāreṣv āgateṣv adṛṣṭaśuddhyapekṣayā cedam abhidhīyamānaṃ dṛṣṭāntārthatayāpi saṃgacchate |
kṣāntyā śuddhyanti vidvāṃso dānenākāryakāriṇaḥ |
pracchannapāpā japyena tapasā vedavittamāḥ || 5.107 ||
prāyaścittaprakaraṇa etad darśayiṣyati | anye tu paṭhanti "kṣāntyā śudhyanti vidvāṃsaḥ" iti |
mṛttoyaiḥ śudhyate śodhyaṃ nadī vegena śudhyati |
rajasā strī manoduṣṭā saṃnyāsena dvijottamāḥ || 5.108 ||
toyagrahaṇaṃ dṛṣṭāntārtham | nadīvegādīnāṃ śuddhihetūnāṃ nayai(cet?) codakam itarathā sthāvarāc chucitaram iti yatas tena nadīpulinānām aśucibahulānāṃ (?) śuddhir ucyate | evaṃ "rajasā strī manoduṣṭā" | karmaduṣṭāyās tu prāyaścittaṃ vakṣyati | pratiṣiddhakarma saṃnyāsena cākāryam akurvato dvijottamasya śuddhir na darśanārthaṃ dvijottamagrahanam | atha vā prasiddhasaṃnyāsaviṣayaṃ cedaṃ śāstrasāmarthyād vijñeyam | idaṃ cāparam uktānāṃ jñānādīnāṃ śuddhisādhanānāṃ viṣayārtham upadiśyate |
adbhir gātrāṇi sudhyanti manaḥ satyena śudhyati |
vidyātapobhyāṃ bhūtātmā buddhir jñānena śudhyati || 5.109 ||
ayaṃ ca jñānādiśuddhiprapañcaḥ pūrvaśaucaprasaṅgenārthavādārtham anugṛhyate | yathemāni jñānādīnisvayam eva śuddheḥ kartṝṇi evam idam api śāvāśaucaṃ vijñeyaṃ sāmarthyāt hy evaiṣāṃ śuddhiḥ prakaraṇena prasaṅgena yathāsambandhanirdeśaṃ kalpanīyā (?) |
eṣa śaucasya vaḥ proktaḥ śarīrasya vinirṇayaḥ |
nānāvidhānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ śuddheḥ śṛṇuta nirṇayam || 5.110 ||
prasiddhārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
taijasānāṃ maṇīnāṃ ca sarvasyāśmamayasya ca |
[240]        bhasmanādbhir mṛdā caiva śuddhir uktā manīṣibhiḥ || 5.111 ||
atra mṛdbhasmanor vikalpitayor adbhiḥ saha samuccayaḥ |
nirlepaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhāṇḍam abhiṣicya viśudhyati (?) |
abjam aśmamayaṃ caiva rājataṃ cānupaskṛtam || 5.112 ||
evaṃ pūrvavidhiṃ salepānāṃ taijasānām anyatamaṃ ca nyastai (?) saviṣayor loha(?)kāñcanarājatayor nirlepayor adbhir eva | pūrvaśloke ca śaṅkyasyāprakṛtatvād ihābjagrahaṇe tasya pūrvavidhir yaḥ pūrvabhava(?)maṇir yas tasyāyam evārthavādaḥ evaṃ maṇer (?) abjasya pūrvaśloke 'bjānabjamaṇiviṣayatvād asyārthavādaḥ |
agneś cāpāṃ ca saṃyogād dhaimaṃ raupyaṃ ca sambabhau (?) |
tasmāt tayoḥ svayonyaiva nirṇeko guṇavattaraḥ || 5.113 ||
"agnir vai varuṇānīm abhyakāmayata" iti śruter atra cāgneḥ suvarṇamantriyamaḥ (?) śrūyate | varuṇānyā rajatam | tathā saty anena ca nirvṛttena bhasmanādbhiś ca tayoḥ kāñcanarājatayor nirneko guṇavattaraḥ | arthavādatvāc cāsya | bhasmanāṃ lohādīnām apy eṣa eva vidhir jñeyaḥ |
tāmrāyaskāṃsyarūpyāṇāṃ trapuṇaḥ sīsakasya ca |
śaucaṃ yathārhaṃ kartavyaṃ kṣārāmlodakavāribhiḥ || 5.114 ||
tāmrādīnāṃ rakṣādīny ucyante | śuddheḥ sādhanāni bhasma ca kṣāraśabdenocyante, nānyat, tathā bāhlīkeṣu prasiddheḥ | āmlodakaṃ yasya kasyacid āmladravyasya kāñcikaṃ vāri sarvatrānuktam api prasajyate | āmlodakaṃ tāmrasyaivārthatvān masyāpi (?) rūpyādes tāmraṃ ced upakaroti tatas tatrāpi grāhyam | kṣārasyāmlodakavidhitsayā punargrahaṇam | rūpyagrahaṇaṃ cāmlodakaprasiddhyartham | rūpyasuvarṇayor hi kevalam udakaṃ bhasma vā |  ato rūpyāṃ bhasmodakārtham idaṃ punar grahaṇam | prāyogikī veyaṃ śuddhir yāpyate | tathā ca smṛtyantare upaghātaviśeṣeṇa śuddhir viśeṣā uktāḥ | yathā "amedhyād upahatānāṃ taijasānām ākārādāhāvakulanāt" iti bodhāyanaḥ |
dravāṇāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ śuddhir utpavanena ca |
prokṣaṇaṃ saṃhatānāṃ ca dāravāṇāṃ ca takṣaṇam || 5.115 ||
utpavanavacanaṃ vedoktam | kuśābhyām udakādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ dravyāṇāṃ prāyogikaṃ kartavyam | na hy upaghāte svalpe 'pi pipīlikādīnām utpavanena śuddhim icchanti śiṣṭāḥ | evaṃ [241] saṃhatānām āsanaśayanādīnāṃ prokṣaṇaṃ prāyogikam | atyantopahatānāṃ tu takṣaṇaṃ prakṣālanādi ca |
mārjanaṃ yajñapātrāṇāṃ pāṇinā yajñakarmaṇi |
camasānāṃ grahānāṃ ca śuddhiḥ prakṣālanena tu || 5.116 ||
saṃmārjanaṃ pāṇinā vedāṅgair vā yajñakarmagrahāṇāṃ karmāṅgaṃ tad iti gamyate 'taś camasārthaṃ ca grahaṇīyam | ayaṃ camasādīnāṃ prāyogikaḥ śaucavidhiḥ syāt | prāśitṛharaṇānāṃ cānuktānām eṣa eva vidhiḥ | upahatānāṃ takṣaṇaṃ parityāgo vā samayācārikaḥ syāt |
carūṇāṃ sruksruvāṇāṃ ca śuddhir uṣṇena vāriṇā |
sphyaśūrpaśakaṭānāṃ ca musalolūkhalasya ca || 5.117 ||
nigadavyākhyātaḥ ślokaḥ |
adbhis tu prokṣaṇaṃ śaucaṃ bahūnāṃ dhānyavāsasām |
prakṣālanena tv alpānām adbhiḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate || 5.118 ||
abgrahaṇam anuvādārtham alpamahattve āpekṣike deśakālābhyāṃ vijñeye | ayam api prāyogika eva vidhiḥ |
cailavac carmaṇāṃ śuddhir vaidalānāṃ tathaiva ca |
śākamūlaphalānāṃ ca dhānyavac chuddhir iṣyate || 5.119 ||
yac cailānām (?) ucyate tat kāryasāmānyāt tālapatramayādīnām api syāc chākādīnām api cailavad iti siddhe dhānyagrahaṇāt vākyatām apekṣyat (?) tac chākādīnām ātmanas tuṣṭyāśaucam iti kalpyam | ayam api samācārāt prāyogika eva | upaghāte tu smṛtyantaraṃ samācāro vānveṣaṇīyaḥ |
kauśeyāvikayor ūṣaiḥ kutapānām ariṣṭakaiḥ |
śrīphalair aṃśupaṭṭānāṃ kṣaumāṇāṃ gaurasarṣapaiḥ || 5.120 ||
ṛjvarthaḥ ślokaḥ |
kṣaumavac chaṅkhaśṛṅgāṇām asthidantamayasya ca |
śuddhir vijānatā kāryā gomūtreṇodakena ca || 5.121 ||
saty api śaṅkhasya vijatva(?)viśeṣavidhānād ayam eva vidhiḥ syān na kvacin na pūrvaḥ |
[142]        prokṣaṇāt tṛṇakāṣṭhāni palālaṃ ca viśudhyati |
mārjanopāñjanair veśma punaḥpākena mṛnmayam || 5.122 ||
tṛṇādīnāṃ saṃhatānāṃ prokṣaṇasiddham uttaravivakṣārthaḥ punar ārambhaḥ | mārjanopāñjanena prathamapraveśopaghāte ca kvacit punaḥpākaś caiṣa upaghātāpekṣaḥ atyantopahatānām apunastyāge 'nupahatānāṃ prokṣaṇam |
[madyair mūtraiḥ purīṣair vā ṣṭhīvanaiḥ pūyaśoṇitaiḥ |
saṃspṛṣṭaṃ naiva śudhyeta punaḥpākena mṛnmayam || 5.123]
saṃmārjanenopāñjanena sekenollekhanena ca |
gavāṃ ca parivāsena bhūmiḥ śudhyati pañcabhiḥ || 5.124 ||
samastair vyastair vā yathāyogaṃ samācārāt bahubhiḥ śuddhiḥ |
pakṣijagdhaṃ gavāghrātam avadhūtam avakṣutam |
dūṣitaṃ keśakīṭaiś ca mṛtprakṣepeṇa śudhyati || 5.125 ||
pakṣiviśeṣāḥ kīṭaviśeṣāḥ smṛtyantarād vā vijñeyāḥ | keṣu tsrddhāḥ(?) keṣu mṛtprakṣepa iti |
yāvan nāpaity amedhyāktād gandho lepaś ca tatkṛtaḥ |
tāvan mṛdvāri vādeyaṃ sarvāsu dravyaśuddhiṣu || 5.126 ||
sarvadravyāṇāṃ śuddhivādārthopalakṣaṇārtham idam ucyate | sāmānyaśaucopadeśaparaṃ vā śūdraviṣaya evedaṃ prakalpyam | vihitaśaucālpavādo 'yaṃ vijñeyaḥ | idānīm avijñātaśuddhisamparkadravyahāriṇas tatsambandhapratyavāyapratiṣedhārtham idam ārabhyate |
trīṇi vedāḥ pavitrāṇi brāhmaṇānām akalpayan |
adṛṣṭam adbhir nirṇiktaṃ yac ca vācā praśasyate || 5.127 ||
veda(?)grahaṇam atrādarārthaṃ vijñeyam | brāhmaṇagrahaṇatve mukhyatvād vrataś ca (?) nārtham itaratrāpy adṛṣṭaḥ pratyakṣānumānāgamair apavādahelanāṃ sambandho yasya tad idam adṛṣṭopaghātahetusambandham adṛṣṭaṃ tat tūpahatam apy asaṃsargeṇa pavitraṃ devā akalpayan | ataś ca tadadṛṣṭayogādau pratyavāyābhāvaḥ śāstrasāmarthyād vijñeyaḥ | upahatasaṃdehe vādbhir nirṇiktaṃ pavitraṃ kṛtaśaucasaṃdehe ca vākpraśastaṃ śiṣṭair atha vā tādṛśavākpraśasto vidhīyate | adbhis tu nirṇiktaṃ dṛṣṭāntatvenādīyate (?) itarasaṃstutyatvam (?) uktatvād asya | evaṃ ca sati yathādbhir nirṇiktaṃ pavitram evam adṛṣṭavākpraśaste [243] avismṛtyabhojanakriyāsādhane dravye | idaṃ tarhi katham iva saṃvatsarasyaikam api caret kṛcchradvijottamagna (?) "ajñātajagdhaśuddhyarthaṃ jñātasya tu viśeṣataḥ" (MDh 5.21) iti | guṇasvabhāvāśucino dravyasyāvijñātasyopayoga etatprāyaścittasaṃspṛṣṭasya cāprakaranasāmarthyād vijñeyam | iyam tu saṃsargāśucino latyunaḥ (?) pakṣijagdhādeḥ | ataś ca yathāviṣayam ubhayam apīdaṃ pramāṇaṃ vijñeyam | vihitapratiṣiddhānāṃ vā vikalpaḥ prākṛtavad dharmaviṣayayor hy avasthita eva vijñeyaḥ | atha vāyaṃ sāmānyaṃ viśeṣa utsargas trīṇi devāḥ pavitrāṇi tasyāviśeṣāpavādaḥ saṃvatsarasyaikam api cared ity evamādi |
āpaḥ śuddhā bhūmigatā vaitṛṣṇyaṃ yāsu gor bhavet |
avyāptāś ced amedhyena gandhavarṇarasānvitāḥ || 5.128 ||
svabhāvaśuddhā eva hy āpaḥ | na yathā bhūmir ubhayathaivaṃ ca tāsām indriyapatitānāṃ bhūmisaṃspṛṣṭānām aśucyāśaṅkāyāṃ yāvatīnāṃ śucitvaṃ darśayati vaitṛṣṇyaṃ yāsu gor bhavet na tv alpānāṃ sāsnālpavane ca sati vaitṛṣṇyaṃ gavām bhaved goḥ snānam abhiplāvya vinyasyatīti gater evaṃ ca tad udakaṃ jānudaghnam ūrudaghnaṃ vāśritaṃ bhavati tathā ca tāsāṃ bhūyastvopalakṣaṇam idam | asyāpavādaḥ | avyāptās ced amedhyena | evaṃ ca saty api bhūyastve amedhyavyāptās tā aśucayo bhavanti | amedhyaprāptiḥ punas tāsāṃ kathaṃ vijñeyā | ye bhavanty amedhyadravyasya "gandhavarṇarasānvitāḥ" tadabhāvād avyāptāḥ śucayo bhavanti | evaṃ ca sati nedam apāṃ viśeṣaṇam | kiṃ tarhi | amedhyadravyasyeti |
nityaṃ śuddhaḥ kāruhastaḥ paṇye yac ca prasāritam |
brahmacārigataṃ bhaikṣaṃ nityaṃ medhyam iti sthitiḥ || 5.129 ||
śuddhyā (?) kāruhastaḥ śuddhaḥ na tūpaghāte sati | tasya śuddhir ucyate | śāvādyāśaucasambandhe cetīdaṃ nivṛttya grahaṇam evam | praṇyasyāpi saktutaṇḍulāder vikretur hastasparśād adoṣaḥ | brahmacārigataṃ bhaikṣaṃ mūtrapurīṣāśucideśāpakramaṇopaghāte 'pi sati śuci jñeyam |
nityam āsyaṃ śuci strīṇāṃ śakuniḥ phalapātane |
prasrave cā śucir vatsaḥ śvā mṛgagrahaṇe śuciḥ || 5.130 ||
bhāryāyāḥ saha bhojanapratiṣedhāt, "striyaś ca ratisaṃsarge" ity etasmāc ca smṛtyantare darśanād iti sabhogaviṣayatāsya gamyate, nānyatra | kecit tu nityagrahaṇa[ṇād?]bhojane 'py anujñānaṃ manyante | tad ayuktaṃ strīsahabhojanapratiṣedhāt taducchiṣṭabhojane prāyaścittasmaraṇāc ceti | sarvavarṇastrīviṣayaś cāyam upadeśaḥ | strīśūdrocchiṣṭabhojanam iti tulyatvād asyocchiṣṭapratiṣedhasya tatra śūdragrahaṇena [244] śūdrāyā api gṛhītatvāt | jātinirdeśatvena strīgrahaṇam āryastrīgrahaṇārthaṃ vijñeyam | evaṃ saty āryāyāś ca prati virodhaviśeṣo nāsty ataḥ sarve viṣayatvāt pratiṣedhasyānujñātenāpi sarvaviṣayeṇa bhavituṃ yuktaḥ | yataś caitad evam ataḥ |
śvabhir hatasya yan māṃsaṃ śuci tan manur abravīt |
kravyādbhiś ca hatasyānyaiś caṇḍālādyaiś ca dasyubhiḥ || 5.131 ||
āmasya māṃsasya śvādyucchiṣṭasya śucivacanād anyasyocchiṣṭasyotsarga evaṃ caṇḍālādisaṃspṛṣṭam api draṣṭavyam |
ūrdhvaṃ nābher yāni khāni tāni medhyāni sarvaśaḥ |
yāny adhastād amedhyāni deśāc caiva malāś cyutāḥ || 5.132 ||
yāny adhastān nābheḥ khāni teṣām aśucitvāt tatsaṃsparśaparihāraḥ | dehāc caiva malāś cyutāḥ | dvau pariśiṣṭebhyo 'py evāmedhyā vijñeyāḥ śleṣmādayaḥ |
makṣikā vipruṣaś chāyā gaur aśvaḥ sūryaraśmayaḥ |
rajo bhūr vāyur agniś ca sparśe medhyaāni nirdiśet || 5.133 ||
na tu lepa evaṃ ca makṣikādīnām alepānāṃ śucitā sparśamātre | vipruṣas tu saty apy aviśeṣavacane samācārān mūtrādivipruṣaḥ prayatne (?)
viṇmūtrotsargaśuddhyarthaṃ mṛdvāry ādeyam arthavat |
daihikānāṃ malānāṃ ca śuddhiṣu dvādaśasv api || 5.134 ||
arthavad iti | nātistokaṃ nātibahu kiṃ tu yāvatā mṛdvāriṇām śuddhyapanayanaṃ syād daihikamalapanuttyarthaṃ ceyam eva mṛdvāri syād iti niyamārtho 'yam ārambhaḥ | daihikāś ca malā iti kṛtvedam ārabhyate |
vasā śukram asṛṅ majjā mūtraviḍghrāṇakarṇaviṭ |
śleśmāśrudūṣikā svedo dvādaśaite nṛṇāṃ malāḥ || 5.135 ||
nṛgrahaṇam anyeṣāṃ vittatulyānāṃ pradarśanārtham |
ekā liṅge gude tisraḥ tathaikatra kare daśa (?) |
ubhayoḥ sapta dātavyā mṛdaḥ śuddhim abhīpasatā (?) || 5.136 ||
etacchaucaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām |
[245]        triguṇaṃ syād vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ tu caturguṇam || 5.137 ||
lepagandhayor amedhyadravyasyāpanītayor api saṃkhyā pūrayitavyā | pūrṇāyāṃ ca saṃkhyāyāṃ (?) lepagandhāpakarṣaṇam parataḥ kartavyam eva, "yāvan nāpaity amedhyāktāt" (MDh 5.126) iti vacanāt | evam ubhayor api vidhyor arthavādatvam aviruddhaṃ bhavati |
kṛtvā mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā khāny ācānta upaspṛśet |
vedam adhyṣyamānaś ca annam aśnaṃś ca sarvadā || 5.138 ||
trir ācāmed apaḥ pūrvaṃ dvir unmṛjyāt tato mukham |
śārīraṃ śaucam anvicchan strī śūdras tu sakṛt sakṛt || 5.139 ||
brahmacāriprakaraṇe "hṛdgābhiḥ pūyate vipraḥ" (MDh 2.62) ityevamādy uktam iha strīśūdrārthaṃ punar ārambhaḥ | evaṃ ca evottarayor (?) upasparśanayor vyavasthitavikalpaḥ sadasacchūdraviṣayo vijñeyaḥ | anenaiva sacchūdraprasaṅgenedam aparaṃ tasyodyate |
śūdrāṇāṃ māsikaṃ kāryaṃ vapanaṃ nyāyavartinām |
vaiśyavac chaucakalpaś ca dvijocchiṣṭaṃ ca bhojanam || 5.140 ||
sacchūdrāṇāṃ pākayajñādikarmavatāṃ brāhmaṇopāśrayatvān māsikaṃ kāryaṃ vapanaṃ dvijātivad "vaiśyavac chaucakapaś ca" ity āmanaḥśaucaṃ tadīyaṃ gṛhyate | ardhamāsaśāvaśuddhiś ca | itarasya tu māsenaivopasparśanaṃ ca prakṛtam "strīśūdras tu sakṛt sakṛt" (MDh 5.139) ity etad dvijocchiṣṭabhojanād iti vistaraśo vyākhyātam | "na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyān noccchiṣṭam" (MDh 4.80) ity atra tacchloke | tathā coktam – brāhmaṇāśritaśūdraprakaraṇe:
yatha yathā hi sadvṛttam ātiṣṭhaty anasūyakaḥ |
tathā tathemaṃ cāmuṃ ca lokaṃ prāpnoty aninditaḥ || (MDh 10.128)
iti |
nocchiṣṭaṃ kurvate mukhyā vipruṣo 'ṅge patanti yāḥ |
na śmaśrūṇi gatāny āsyaṃ na dantāntaradhiṣṭhitam || 5.141 ||
śāstraprāptasya pratiṣedhaḥ | agannayāntyayātāny āsyaṃ (aṅge patanti gatāny āsyam??) ity ayaṃ ca pratiṣedhapratiprasavaḥ | dantāntaradhiṣṭhitasya smṛtyantare jihvābhimarśanaṃ pracyavanaviśiraṇānvitāni ca pramāṇam |
spṛśanti bindavaḥ pādau ya ācāmayataḥ parān |
bhaumikais te samā jñeyā na tair aprayato bhavet || 5.142 ||
[246]
paravacanāt svayaṃ parihartavyā |
ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭo dravyahastaḥ kathaṃcana |
anidhāyaiva tad dravyam ācāntaḥ śucitām iyāt || 5.143 ||
gautamasya tu nidhānapakṣa eva | yena "dravyahasta ucchiṣṭo nidhāyācāmet" dravyahasta iti yady abhidhānasyeti sūtre nidhānārthaṃ vacanam adhikaṃ paṭhati | tena padādhikyena tulyatve 'pi saṃhitāyānidhānapakṣas tasya vijñāyate | tathā ca tulyabalatvād virodhe vikalpaḥ |
vānto viriktaḥ snātvā tu ghṛtaprāśanam ācaret |
ācāmed eva bhuktvānnaṃ snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam || 5.144 ||
ācāmed eva bhuktvānnam iti | yathā bhojanottarakālam ācamanāc chuddhir evaṃ vāntaviriktayor (?) api snānottarakālaṃ ghṛtaprāśanāc chuddhis tathā ced upasparśanam | dṛṣṭāntavādatvāt parārtham upādīyate na svārtham uktatvād asya | nidarśanaṃ caitat sarvopasparśanimittānāṃ dṛṣṭāntārthatayā sāmarthyād vijñeyam | maithuninaś ca nyāyyā (?) maithuni tūpagamanena vā parvavarjaṃ vrajet snānam | idaṃ guṇādhikyād viśiṣṭam upadiśyate nityād adhikam | anye tu āvṛttyaikam evecchanti |
suptvā kṣutvā ca bhuktvā ca niṣṭhīvyoktvānṛtāni ca |
pītvāpo 'dhyṣyamāṇaś ca ācāmet prayato 'pi san || 5.145 ||
apara ācamanahetavo 'tra vedādhyananasya punargrahaṇam ācamanasya svādhyāyāṅgatva ācamanārtham | pūrvatra tu śuddhyartham uktam | evaṃ ca sati dvāv imau niyamau sucīnām asaytasya ācāntau adhyetavyam iti | tena tv etena tv ekatiṣyakāleṣu bhojana ācamanam ucyate | brahmacāriṇo bhaikṣopayogakāla iha dvir ucyate | atra ca brahmacāriṇas tad bhojanāṅgaṃ yat punaḥ kṛtvā mūtraṃ purīṣam ity atra tatprayatārtham, iha tv itaravidhitsārtham |
eṣa śaucavidhiḥ kṛtsno dravaśuddhis tathaiva ca |
ukto vaḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ strīṇāṃ dharmān nibodhata || 5.146 ||
bālayā vā yuvatyā vā vṛddhayā vāpi yoṣitā |
na svātantryeṇa kartavyaṃ kiṃcit kāryaṃ gṛheṣv api || 5.147 ||
asvātantryopadeśo 'yam asyādharmeṇātmasaṃskārāt (?) prāg api cobhās tvābhāve (?)
bālye pitur vaśe tiṣṭhet pāṇigrāhasya yauvane |
putrāṇāṃ bhartari prete na bhajet strī svatantratām || 5.148 ||
[147]
kadācid apīty atyarthaḥ | evaṃ ca sati –
pitrā bhartrā sutair vāpi necched viraham ātmanaḥ |
eṣāṃ hi vraheṇa strī garhye kuryād ubhe kule || 5.149 ||
ayaṃ cāparas taddharmaḥ –
sadā prahṛṣṭayā bhāvyaṃ gṛhakārye ca dakṣayā |
susaṃskṛtopaskarayā vyaye cāmuktahastayā || 5.150 ||
kiṃ cānyat |
yasmai dadyāt pitā tv enāṃ bhrātā vānumate pituḥ |
taṃ śuśrūṣeta jīvantaṃ saṃsthitaṃ ca na laṅghayet || 5.151 ||
pitur abhāve bhrātrādibhā(hā?)dinvaludeyety (?) darśayati | "saṃsthitaṃ ca na laṅghayet" ity anenordhvaṃ patyur avyabhicāram āha | vāṃyānā(?) prabhṛty asyāḥ pāratantryaṃ patyau na vivāhāt (?) |
maṅgalārthaṃ svastyayanaṃ yajñaś cāsāṃ prajāpateḥ |
prayujyate vivāheṣu pradānaṃ svāmyakāraṇam || 5.152 ||
svastyayanaṃ vivāhas tatra yo mantraprayogaḥ sa maṅgalārthaṃ na svatvahetuḥ | yena sattve sati vāgdānena maṅgalaprayuktir iyaṃ yuktā (?) "daśāsyām putrān ā dhehi patim ekādaśaṃ kṛdhi" (RV 10.85.45) ityevamādiprajāpater mantrahomābhyāṃ vivāheṣu svīkṛtāyāṃ vāgdānena yujyate | itaś ca taṃ śuśrūṣeta jīvantaṃ "saṃsthitaṃ ca na laṅghayet" (MDh 5.151) yena –
anṛtāv ṛtukāle ca mantrasaṃskārakṛt patiḥ |
sukhasya nityaṃ dāteha paraloke ca yoṣitaḥ || 5.153 ||
viśīlaḥ kāmavṛtto vā guṇair vā parivarjitaḥ |
upacaryaḥ striyā sādhvyā satataṃ devavat patiḥ || 5.154 ||
nāsti strīṇāṃ pṛthag yajño na vrataṃ na hy (nāpy?) upoṣaṇam |
patiṃ śuśrūṣate yat tu (yena) tena svarge mahīyate || 5.155 ||
ayaṃ pūrvavidhistutyarthavādaḥ | tathā ca –
pāṇigrāhasya sādhvī strī jīvato vā mṛtasya vā |
patilokam abhīpsantī nācaret kiṃcid apriyam || 5.156 ||
[248]        kāmaṃ tu kṣapeyed dehaṃ puṣpamūlaphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
na ca (tu) nāmāpi gṛhṇīyāt patyau prete parasya tu || 5.157 ||
vṛttyasaṃbhave 'pi ca |
āsītā maraṇāt kṣāntā niyatā brahmacāriṇī |
yo dharma ekapatnīnāṃ kāṅkṣantī tam anuttamam || 5.158 ||
na syāt (?) putrārtham api na pravarteta (?) | anyatra saṃsthite patyau yasmāt |
anekāni sahasrāṇi kumārabrahmacāriṇām |
divaṃ gatāni viprāṇām akṛtvā kulasaṃtatim || 5.159 ||
mṛte bhartari sādhvī strī brahmacarye vyavasthitā |
svargaṃ gacchaty aputrāpi yathā te brahmacāriṇaḥ || 5.160 ||
evam eva pratiṣedhaṃ viditvāpi –
apatyalobhād yā tu strī bhartāram ativartate |
seha nindām avāpnoti paralokāc ca hīyate || 5.161 ||
yataś ca –
nānyotpannaprajāstīti (sīha?) nāpy anyasya parigrahe |
dvitīyaś ca na sādhvīnāṃ kvacid bhartopadiśyate || 5.162 ||
yataś ca –
patiṃ hitvāpakṛṣṭaṃ svam utkṛṣṭaṃ yā ca sevate [niṣevate?] |
utkṛṣṭasevane 'pi sā –
nindyaiva loke bhavati parapūrveti cocyate || 5.163 ||
na kevalam ayaṃ doṣo na vyabhicāro 'yaṃ cāparo nitarām –
vyabhicārāt tu bhartuḥ strī
na kevalam
loke prāpnoti nindyatām |
prayāṇād ūrdhvam –
sṛgālayoniṃ prāpnoti pāparogaiś ca pīḍyate || 5.164 ||
[249]
yenāto nāticared bhartāraṃ dṛṣṭaphalalobhena | tathā ca darśayati –
patiṃ yā nāticarati manovāgdehasaṃyatā |
sā bhartṛlokān āpnoti sadbhiḥ sādhvīti cocyate || 5.165 ||
anena nārī vṛttena manogāgdehasaṃyatā |
ihāgryāṃ kīrtim āpnoti patilokaṃ paratra ca || 5.166 ||
strīdharmopasaṃhārārthaḥ ślokaḥ | ṛjavaś strīdharmā iti | yato nātra vyākhyānādaraḥ kṛtaḥ | etāvat tv atropadeśārtham – yathā puṃso 'nyayā saha punaḥ pravṛttiḥ karmasv evam "saṃsthitaṃ ca na laṅghayet" (MDh 5.151) iti, yena nāsyā anyena saha punaḥ pravṛttir asti yathā ca "svargaṃ gacchaty aputrāpi" (MDh 5.160) ity anena apatyajananam āpadi pratiṣidhyate | niyogasmṛtyā tu tat punar abhyanujñāsyate | tadapatyotpādanam uktapratiṣiddhatvād vikalpyate | anayoḥ smṛtyoḥ katarā jyāyasīti na śakyā kartum atiśayāvadhāraṇā | yenaikatrāpatyam, anyatra saṃyamaḥ | ubhayor api stutir iti jānīmahi |
evaṃvṛttāṃ savarṇāṃ strīṃ dvijātiḥ pūrvamāriṇīm |
dāhayed agnihotreṇa yajñapātraiś ca dharmavit || 5.167 ||
nyāyaprāptānuvādaḥ ślokaḥ | evaṃ ca sa tasyāḥ svāmitvād yukta evāgnihotreṇa saṃskāraḥ tathaitat punar ādarārtham udāhriyate |
bhāryāyai pūrvamāriṇyai datvāgnīn antyakarmaṇi |
punar dārakriyāṃ kuryāt punar ādhānam eva ca || 5.168 ||
idam anyayā sahādhikārāntaram ucyate | na tv adhīnatvādhikārāntaraśeṣaḥ pratiprasavaḥ | yadā tv asamarthaḥ prajanane karmānuṣṭhāne vā tadāsya sahāyābhāvād vānaprasthe pārivrājye vādhikāraḥ syād vipratiṣedhaḥ | tathā ca śrutiḥ "jarayā ha vā etasmānm (?) ucyate" ity arthalopena | apare ty āhuḥ: atra yadeti kalpiṣyate | etena yāvajjīvahomaśruter avirodhaḥ śuddho bhaviṣyati |
anena vidhinā nityaṃ pñcayajñān na hāpayet |
dvitīyam āyuṣo bhāgaṃ gṛheṣu brāhmaṇo vasan || 5.169 ||
upasaṃhārārthaḥ ślokaḥ | pañcayajñagrahāṇāṃ ca sarvakarmaprasiddhyartham iti |
iti bhārucikṛtau manuśāstravivaraṇe pañcamo 'dhyāyaḥ ||
ṣaṣṭho ‘dhyāyaḥ
evaṃ gṛhāśrame sthitvā vidhivat snātako dvijaḥ |
vane vaset tu niyato yathāvad vijitendriyaḥ || 6.1 ||
upanyāsārthaḥ ślokaḥ |
gṛhasthas tu yadā paśyed yad valīpalitam ātmanaḥ |
apatyasyaiva cāpatyaṃ tadāraṇyaṃ samāśrayet || 6.2 ||
tṛtīyasyāyuṣo bhāgasyopalakṣaṇaṃ valīpalitagrahaṇaṃ putranivṛttyupalakṣaṇārtham | pautragrahaṇam ṛṇāpādānasambandhāt | avaśyam anena putranivṛttir anuṣṭheyā | etasmiṃś ca tṛtīye vānaprasthāśrame brahmacaryavidhānāt putrotpattivirodhaḥ | tasmāt tṛtīya āyuṣo bhāge prāpte jātaputraḥ araṇyam āśrayet |
santyajya grāmyam āhāraṃ sarvaṃ caiva paricchadam |
putre bhāryāṃ vinikṣipya vanaṃ gacchet sahaiva vā || 6.3 ||
nāyaṃ viśeṣaṇavikalpaḥ | kin tarhi bhāryāyā āsām arthāpekṣo vyavasthita vikalpo ‘yam |
agnihotraṃ samādāya gṛhyaṃ cāgniparicchadam |
grāmād araṇyaṃ niḥsṛtya nivasan niyatendriyaḥ || 6.4 ||
atra cānyārtho ’gnihotraśabdastutyarthaḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: ‘śrāmaṇikenāgnim ādhāya’ (GDh 3.27) tatryahasmāt (?) tam evāgniṃ niyatendriyagrahaṇaṃ ca | anuvādo viśeṣaṇārthaḥ |
munyannair vividhair medhyaiḥ śākamūlaphalena vā |
etān eva mahāyajñān nirvaped vidhipūrvakam || 6.5 ||
munyannam āraṇyaṃ vakṣyati |
vasīta carma cīraṃ vā snāyāt sāyaṃ prage tathā |
jaṭāś ca bibhṛyāt nityaṃ śmaśrulomanakhāni ca || 6.6 ||
idaṃ niyamena snānaṃ triṣavaṇam apy asya vikalpena vakṣyati |
yad bhakṣyaṃ syāt tato dadyād baliṃ bhikṣāṃ ca śaktitaḥ |
ammūlaphalabhikṣābhir arcayed āśramāgatān || 6.7 ||
svādhāye nityayuktaḥ syād dānto maitraḥ samāhitaḥ |
dātā nityam anādātā sarvabhūtānukampakaḥ || 6.8 ||
avikriyāgrahaṇārthaṃ svādhyāyagrahaṇam | dāntaṃ svaśāstreṇa vaikhānasena pratigrahādayaś cāsya pratiṣidhyante nityārthaṃ tu kadāśramānuṣṭhānasādhanam |
